śrīśivamahāpurāṇam

atha śrīśivamahāpurāṇaṃ vidyeśvarasaṃhitā prārabhyate   ||


śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ   ||
śrīgurubhyo namaḥ   ||
śrisarasvatyai namaḥ   ||
atha śivapuraṇe prathamā vidyeśvarasaṃhitāprārabhyate   ||

ādyantamaṃgalamajātasamānabhāvamāryaṃ tamīśamajarāmaramātmadevam  || 1,ma_
pañcānanaṃ prabalapañcavinodaśīlaṃ saṃbhāvaye manasiśaṃkaramambikeśam  || 1,ma_

adhyāya 1
vyāsa uvāca
dharmakṣetre mahākṣetre gaṃgākālindisaṃgame  || 1,1.1
prayāge parame puṇye brahmalokasya vartmani  || 1,1.1
munayaḥ śaṃsitātmanassatyavrataparāyaṇāḥ  || 1,1.2
mahaujaso mahābhāgā mahāsatraṃ vitenire  || 1,1.2
tatra satraṃ samākarṇya vyāsaśiṣyo mahāmuniḥ   || 1,1.3
ājagāma munīndraṣṭuṃ sūtaḥ paurāṇikottamaḥ  || 1,1.3
taṃ dṛṣṭvā sūtamāyāṃtaṃ harṣitā munayastadā  || 1,1.4
cetasā suprasannena pūjāṃ cakruryathāvidhi  || 1,1.4
tato vinayasaṃyuktā procuḥ sāṃjalayaścate  || 1,1.5
suprasannā mahātmānaḥ stutiṃ kṛtvāyathāvidhi  || 1,1.5
romaharṣaṇa sarvajña bhavān vai bhāgyagauravāt || 1,1.6
purāṇavidyāmakhilāṃ vyāsātpratyarthamīyivān  || 1,1.6
tasmādāścaryabhūtānāṃ kathānāṃ tvaṃ hi bhājanam  || 1,1.7
ratnānāmurusārāṇāṃ ratnākara ivārṇavaḥ  || 1,1.7
yacca bhūtaṃ ca bhavyaṃ ca yaccānyadvastu vartate  || 1,1.8
na tvayā 'viditaṃ kiṃcittriṣu lokeṣu vidyate  || 1,1.8
tvaṃ maddiṣṭavaśādasya darśanārthamihāgataḥ  || 1,1.9
kurvankimapi naḥ śreyo na vṛthā gaṃtumarhasi  || 1,1.9
tattvaṃ śrutaṃ sma naḥ sarvaṃ pūrvameva śubhāśubham  || 1,1.10
na tṛptimadhigacchāmaḥ śravaṇecchā muhurmuhuḥ  || 1,1.10
idānīmekamevāsti śrotavyaṃ sūta sanmate  || 1,1.11
tadrahasyamapi brūhi yadi te 'nugraho bhavet || 1,1.11
prāpte kaliyuge ghore narāḥ puṇyavivarjitāḥ  || 1,1.12
durācāraratāḥ sarve satyavārtāparāṅmukhāḥ  || 1,1.12
parāpavādaniratāḥ paradravyābhilāṣiṇaḥ  || 1,1.13
parastrīsaktamanasaḥ parahiṃsāparāyaṇāḥ  || 1,1.13
dehātmadṛṣṭayā mūḍhā nāstikāḥ paśubuddhayaḥ  || 1,1.14
mātṛpitṛkṛtadveṣāḥ strīdevāḥ kāmakiṃkarāḥ  || 1,1.14
viprā lobhagrahagrastā vedavikrayajīvinaḥ  || 1,1.15
dhanārjanārthamabhyastavidyā madavimohitāḥ  || 1,1.15
tyaktasvajātikarmāṇaḥ prāyhaśaḥparavaṃcakāḥ  || 1,1.16
trikālasaṃdhyayā hīnā brahmabodhavivarjitāḥ  || 1,1.16
adayāḥ paṃḍitaṃmanyāssvācāravratalopakāḥ  || 1,1.17
kṛṣyudyamaratāḥ krūrasvabhāvā malināśayāḥ  || 1,1.17
kṣatriyāśca tathā sarve svadharmatyāgaśīlinaḥ  || 1,1.18
asatsaṃgāḥ pāparatā vyabhicāraparāyaṇāḥ  || 1,1.18
aśūrā araṇaprītāḥ palāyanaparāyaṇāḥ  || 1,1.19
kucauravṛttayaḥ śūdrāḥ kāmakiṃkaracetasaḥ  || 1,1.19
śastrāstravidyayā hīnā dhenuviprāvanojjhitāḥ  || 1,1.20
śaraṇyāvanahīnāśca kāminyūtimṛgāssadā  || 1,1.20
prajāpālanasaddharmavihīnā bhogatatparāḥ  || 1,1.21
prajāsaṃhārakā duṣṭā jīvahiṃsākarā mudā  || 1,1.21
vaiśyāḥ saṃskārahīnāste svadharmatyāgaśīlinaḥ  || 1,1.22
kupathāḥ svārjanaratāstulākarmakuvṛttayaḥ  || 1,1.22
gurudevadvijātīnāṃ bhaktihīnāḥ kubuddhayaḥ  || 1,1.23
abhojitadvijāḥ prāyaḥ kṛpaṇā baddhamuṣṭayaḥ  || 1,1.23
kāminījārabhāveṣu suratā malināśayāḥ  || 1,1.24
lobhamohavicetaskāḥ pūrtādisuvṛṣojjhitāḥ  || 1,1.24
tadvacchūdrāśca ye kecidbrāhmaṇācāratatparāḥ  || 1,1.25
ujjvalākṛtayo mūḍhāḥ svadharmatyāgaśīlinaḥ  || 1,1.25
kartārastapasāṃ bhūyo dvijatejopahārakāḥ  || 1,1.26
śiśvalpamṛtyukārāśca maṃtroccāraparāyaṇāḥ  || 1,1.26
śīligrāmaśilādīnāṃ pūjakāhomatatparāḥ  || 1,1.27
pratikūlavicārāśca kuṭilā dvijadūṣakāḥ  || 1,1.27
dhanavaṃtaḥ kukarmāṇo vidyāvanto vivādinaḥ  || 1,1.28
ākhyāyopāsanā dharmavaktāro dharmalopakāḥ  || 1,1.28
subhūpākṛtayo daṃbhāḥ sudātāro mahāmadāḥ  || 1,1.29
viprādīnsevakānmatvā manyamānā nijaṃ prabhum  || 1,1.29
svadharmarahitā mūḍāḥ saṃkarāḥ krūrabuddhayaḥ  || 1,1.30
mahābhimānino nityaṃ caturvarṇavilopakāḥ  || 1,1.30
sukulīnānnijānmatvā caturvarṇairvivartanāḥ  || 1,1.31
sarvavarṇabhraṣṭakarā mūḍhāssatkarmakāriṇaḥ  || 1,1.31
striyaśca prāyaśo bhraṣṭā bhartravajñānakārikāḥ  || 1,1.32
śvaśuradrohakāriṇyo nirbhayā malināśanāḥ  || 1,1.32
kuhāvabhāvaniratāḥ kuśīlāssmaravihvalāḥ  || 1,1.33
jārasaṃgaratā nityaṃ svasvāmivimukhāstathā  || 1,1.33
tanayā mātṛpitrośca bhaktihīnā durāśayāḥ  || 1,1.34
avidyāpāṭhakā nityaṃ rogagrasitadehakāḥ  || 1,1.34
eteṣāṃ naṣṭabuddhīnāṃ svadharmatyāgaśīlinām  || 1,1.35
paralokepīha loke kathaṃ sūta gatirbhavet || 1,1.35
iti ciṃtākulaṃ cittaṃ jāyate satataṃ hi naḥ  || 1,1.36
paropakārasadṛśo nāsti dharmo paraḥ khalu  || 1,1.36
laghūpāyena yenaiṣāṃ bhavetsadyoghanāśanam  || 1,1.37
sarvasiddhāntavittvaṃ hi kṛpayā tadvadādhunā  || 1,1.37
vyāsa uvāca
ityākarṇya vacasteṣāṃ munīnāṃ bhāvitātmanām  || 1,1.38
manasā śaṃkaraṃ smṛtvā sūtaḥ provāca tānmunīn  || 1,1.38

iti śrīśaive mahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ munipraśnavarṇanonāmaprathamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 2
sūtauvāca
sādhupṛṣṭaṃ sādhavo vastrailokyahitakārakam  || 1,2.1
guruṃ smṛtvā bhavatsnehādvakṣye tacchṛṇutādarāt || 1,2.1
vedāṃtasārasarvasvaṃ purāṇaṃ śaivamuttamam  || 1,2.2
sarvāghaughoddhārakaraṃ paratra paramārthadam  || 1,2.2
kalikalmaṣavidhvaṃsi yasmiñcchivayaśaḥ param  || 1,2.3
vijṛmbhate sadā viprāścaturvargaphalapradam  || 1,2.3
tasyādhyayanamātreṇa purāṇasya dvijottamāḥ  || 1,2.4
sarvottamasya śaivasya te yāsyaṃti susadgatim  || 1,2.4
tāvadvijṛṃbhate pāpaṃ brahmahatyāpurassaram  || 1,2.5
yāvacchivapurāṇaṃ hi nodeṣyati jagatyaho  || 1,2.5
tāvatkalimahotpātāḥ saṃcariṣyaṃti nirbhayāḥ  || 1,2.6
yāvacchivapurāṇaṃ hi nodeṣyati jagatyaho  || 1,2.6
tāvatsarvāṇi śāstrāṇi vivadaṃti parasparam  || 1,2.7
yāvacchivapurāṇaṃ hi nodeṣyati jagatyaho  || 1,2.7
tāvatsvarūpaṃ durbodhaṃ śivasya mahatāmapi  || 1,2.8
yāvacchivapurāṇaṃ hi no deṣyati jagatyaho  || 1,2.8
tāvadyamabhaṭāḥ krūrāḥ saṃcariṣyaṃti nirbhayāḥ  || 1,2.9
yāvacchivapurāṇaṃ hi nodeṣyati jagatyaho  || 1,2.9
tāvatsarvapurāṇāni pragarjaṃti mahītale  || 1,2.10
yāvacchivapurāṇaṃ hi nodeṣyati jagatyaho  || 1,2.10
tāvatsarvāṇi tīrthāni vivadaṃti mahītale  || 1,2.11
yāvachivapurāṇaṃ hi nodeṣyati jagatyaho  || 1,2.11
tāvatsarvāṇi maṃtrāṇi vivadaṃti mahītale  || 1,2.12
yāvacchivapurāṇaṃ hi nodeṣyati mahītale  || 1,2.12
tāvatsarvāṇi kṣetrāṇi vivadaṃti mahītale  || 1,2.13
yāvacchivapurāṇaṃ hi nodeṣyati mahītale  || 1,2.13
tāvatsarvāṇi pīṭhāni vivadaṃti mahītale  || 1,2.14
yāvacchivapurāṇaṃ hi nodeṣyati mahītale  || 1,2.14
tāvatsarvāṇi dānāni vivadaṃti mahītale  || 1,2.15
yāvacchivapurāṇaṃ hi nodeṣyati mahītale  || 1,2.15
tāvatsarve ca te devā vivadaṃti mahītale  || 1,2.16
yāvacchivapurāṇaṃ hi nodeṣyati mahītale  || 1,2.16
tāvatsarve ca siddhāntā vivadaṃti mahītale  || 1,2.17
yāvacchivapurāṇaṃ hi nodeṣyati mahītale  || 1,2.17
asya śaivapurāṇasya kīrtanaśravaṇāddvijāḥ  || 1,2.18
phalaṃ vaktuṃ na śaknomi kārtsnyena munisattamāḥ  || 1,2.18
tathāpi tasya māhātmyaṃ vakṣye kiṃcittu vonaghāḥ  || 1,2.19
cittamādhāya śṛṇuta vyāsenoktaṃ purā mama  || 1,2.19
etacchivapurāṇaṃ hi ślokaṃ ślokārdhameva ca  || 1,2.20
yaḥ paṭhedbhaktisaṃyuktassa pāpānmucyate kṣaṇāt || 1,2.20
etacchivapurāṇaṃ hi yaḥ pratyahamataṃdritaḥ  || 1,2.21
yathāśakti paṭhedbhaktyā sa jīvanmukta ucyate  || 1,2.21
etacchivapurāṇaṃ hi yo bhaktyārcayate sadā  || 1,2.22
dine dine 'śvamedhasya phalaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam   || 1,2.22
etacchivapurāṇaṃ yassādhāraṇapadecchayā  || 1,2.23
anyataḥ śṛṇuyātso 'pi matto mucyeta pātakāt || 1,2.23
etacchivapurāṇaṃ yo namaskuryādadūrataḥ  || 1,2.24
sarvadevārcanaphalaṃ sa prāpnoti na saṃśayaḥ  || 1,2.24
etacchivapurāṇaṃ vai likhitvā pustakaṃ svayam  || 1,2.25
yo dadyācchivabhaktebhyastasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  || 1,2.25
adhīteṣu ca śāstreṣu vedeṣu vyākṛteṣu ca  || 1,2.26
yatphalaṃ durlabhaṃ loke tatphalaṃ tasya saṃbhavet || 1,2.26
etacchivapurāṇaṃ hi caturdaśyāmupoṣitaḥ  || 1,2.27
śivabhaktasabhāyāṃ yo vyākaroti sa uttamaḥ  || 1,2.27
pratyakṣaraṃ tu gāyatrīpuraścaryāphalaṃ labhet || 1,2.28
iha bhuktvākhilānkāmānaṃte nirvāṇatāṃ vrajet || 1,2.28
upoṣitaścaturdaśyāṃ rātrau jāgaraṇānvitaḥ  || 1,2.29
yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādvāpi tasya puṇyaṃ vadāmyaham  || 1,2.29
kurukṣetrādinikhilapuṇyatīrtheṣvanekaśaḥ  || 1,2.30
ātmatulyadhanaṃ sūryagrahaṇe sarvatomukhe  || 1,2.30
viprebhyo vyāsamukhyebhyo dattvāyatphalamaśnute  || 1,2.31
tatphalaṃ saṃbhavettasya satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 1,2.31
etacchivapurāṇaṃ hi gāyate yopyaharniśam  || 1,2.32
ājñāṃ tasya pratīkṣerandavā indrapurogamāḥ  || 1,2.32
etacchivapurāṇaṃ yaḥ paṭhañchṛṇvanhi nityaśaḥ  || 1,2.33
yadyatkaroti satkarma tatkoṭiguṇitaṃ bhavet || 1,2.33
samāhitaḥ paṭhedyastu tatra śrīrudrasaṃhitām  || 1,2.34
sa brahmaghno'pi pūtātmā tribhireva dinairbhavet || 1,2.34
tāṃ rudrasaṃhitāṃ yastu bhairavapratimāṃtike  || 1,2.35
triḥ paṭhetpratyahaṃ maunī sa kāmānakhilāṃllabhet || 1,2.35
tāṃ rudrasaṃhitāṃ yastu sapaṭhedvaṭabilvayoḥ  || 1,2.36
pradakṣiṇāṃ prakurvāṇo brahmahatyā nivartate  || 1,2.36
kailāsasaṃhitā tatra tato'pi paramasmṛtā  || 1,2.37
brahmasvarūpiṇī sākṣātpraṇavārthaprakāśikā  || 1,2.37
kailāsasaṃhitāyāstu māhātmyaṃ vetti śaṃkaraḥ  || 1,2.38
kṛtsnaṃ tadardhaṃ vyāsaśca tadardhaṃ vedmyahaṃ dvijāḥ  || 1,2.38
tatra kiṃcitpravakṣyāmi kṛtsnaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyate  || 1,2.39
yajjñātvā tatkṣaṇāllokaścittaśuddhimavāpnuyāt || 1,2.39
na nāśayati yatpāpaṃ sā raudrī saṃhitā dvijāḥ  || 1,2.40
tanna paśyāmyahaṃ loke mārgamāṇo'pi sarvadā  || 1,2.40
śivenopaniṣatsiṃdhumanthanotpāditāṃ mudā  || 1,2.41
kumārāyārpitāṃ tāṃ vai sudhāṃ pītvā 'maro bhavet || 1,2.41
brahmahatyādipāpānāṃ niṣkṛtiṃ kartumudyataḥ  || 1,2.42
māsamātraṃ saṃhitāṃ tāṃ paṭhitvā mucyate tataḥ  || 1,2.42
duṣpratigrahadurbhojyadurālāpādisaṃbhavam  || 1,2.43
pāpaṃ sakṛtkīrtanena saṃhitā sā vināśayet || 1,2.43
śivālaye vilvavane saṃhitāṃ tāṃ paṭhettu yaḥ  || 1,2.44
sa tatphalamavāpnoti yadvāco'pi na gocare  || 1,2.44
saṃhitāṃ tāṃ paṭhan bhaktyā yaḥ śrāddhe bhojayeddvijān  || 1,2.45
tasya ye pitaraḥ sarve yāṃti śaṃbhoḥ paraṃ padam  || 1,2.45
caturdaśyāṃ nirāhāro yaḥ paṭhetsaṃhitāṃ ca tām  || 1,2.46
bilvamūle śivaḥ sākṣātsadevaiśca prapūjyate  || 1,2.46
anyāpi saṃhitā tatra sarvakāmaphalapradā  || 1,2.47
ubhe viśiṣṭe vijñeye līlāvijñānapūrite  || 1,2.47
tadidaṃ śaivamākhyātaṃ purāṇaṃ vedasaṃmitam  || 1,2.48
nirmitaṃ tacchivenaiva prathamaṃ brahmasaṃmitam  || 1,2.48
vidyeśaṃca tathāraudraṃ vaināyakamathaumikam  || 1,2.49
mātraṃ rudraikādaśakaṃ kailāsaṃ śatarudrakam  || 1,2.49
koṭirudrasahasrādyaṃ koṭirudraṃ tathaiva ca  || 1,2.50
vāyavīyaṃ dharmasaṃjñaṃ purāṇamiti bhedataḥ  || 1,2.50
saṃhitā dvādaśamitā mahāpuṇyatarā matā  || 1,2.51
tāsāṃ saṃkhyāṃ bruve viprāḥ śṛṇutādaratokhilam  || 1,2.51
vidyeśaṃ daśāsāhasraṃ rudraṃ vaināyakaṃ tathā  || 1,2.52
aumaṃ mātṛpurāṇākhyaṃ pratyekāṣṭasahasrakam  || 1,2.52
trayodaśasahasraṃ hi rudraikādaśakaṃ dvijāḥ  || 1,2.53
ṣaṭsahasraṃ ca kailāsaṃ śatarudraṃ tadardhakam  || 1,2.53
koṭirudraṃ triguṇitamekādaśasahasrakam  || 1,2.54
sahasrakoṭirudrākhyamuditaṃ graṃthasaṃkhyayā  || 1,2.54
vāyavīyaṃ khābdhiśataṃ gharmaṃ ravisahasrakam  || 1,2.55
tadevaṃ lakṣasaṃkhyākaṃ śaivasaṃkhyāvibhedataḥ  || 1,2.55
vyāsena tattu saṃkṣiptaṃ caturviṃśatsahasrakam  || 1,2.56
śaivaṃ tatra caturthaṃ vai purāṇaṃ saptasaṃhitam  || 1,2.56
śive saṃkalpitaṃ pūrvaṃ purāṇaṃ granthasaṃkhyayā  || 1,2.57
śatakoṭipramāṇaṃ hi purā sṛṣṭau suvismṛtam  || 1,2.57
vyasteṣṭādaśadhā caiva purāṇe dvāparādiṣu  || 1,2.58
caturlakṣeṇa saṃkṣipte kṛte dvaipāyanādibhiḥ  || 1,2.58
proktaṃ śivapurāṇaṃ hi caturviṃśatsahasrakam  || 1,2.59
ślokānāṃ saṃkhyayā saptasaṃhitaṃ brahmasaṃmitam  || 1,2.59
vidyeśvarākhyā tatrādyā raudrī jñeyā dvitīyikā  || 1,2.60
tṛtīyā śatarudrākhyā koṭirudrā caturthikā  || 1,2.60
pañcamī caiva maumākhyā ṣaṣṭhī kailāsasaṃjñikā  || 1,2.61
saptamī vāyavīyākhyā saptaivaṃ saṃhitāmatāḥ  || 1,2.61
sasaptasaṃhitaṃ divyaṃ purāṇaṃ śivasaṃjñakam  || 1,2.62
varīvarti brahmatulyaṃ sarvopari gatipradam  || 1,2.62
etacchivapurāṇaṃ hi saptasaṃhitamādarāt || 1,2.63
paripūrṇaṃ paṭhedyastu sa jīvanmukta ucyate  || 1,2.63
śrutismṛtipurāṇetihāsāgamaśatāni ca  || 1,2.64
etacchivapurāṇasya nārhaṃtyalpāṃ kalāmapi  || 1,2.64
śaivaṃ purāṇamamalaṃ śivakīrtitaṃ tadvyāsena śaivapravaṇena na saṃgṛhītam  || 1,2.65
saṃkṣepataḥ sakalajīvaguṇopakāre tāpatrayaghnamatulaṃ śivadaṃ satāṃ hi  || 1,2.65
vikaitavo dharma iha pragīto vedāṃtavijñānamayaḥ pradhānaḥ  || 1,2.66
amatsarāṃtarbudhavedyavastu satkḷptamatraiva trivargayuktam  || 1,2.66
śaivaṃ purāṇatilakaṃ khalu satpurāṇaṃ vedāṃtavedavilasatparavastugītam  || 1,2.67
yo vai paṭhecca śṛṇuyātparamādareṇa śaṃbhupriyaḥ sa hi labhetparamāṃ gatiṃ vai  || 1,2.67

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ dvidīyo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 3
vyāsa uvāca
ityākarṇya vacaḥ sautaṃ procuste paramarṣayaḥ  || 1,3.1
vedāṃtasārasarvasvaṃ purāṇaṃ śrāvayādbhutam  || 1,3.1
iti śrutvā munīnāṃ sa vacanaṃ supraharṣitaḥ  || 1,3.2
saṃsmarañchaṃkaraṃ sūtaḥ provāca munisattamān  || 1,3.2
sūta uvāca
śṛṇvaṃtu ṛṣayaḥ sarve smṛtvā śivamanāmayam  || 1,3.3
purāṇapravaṇaṃ śaivaṃ purāṇaṃ vedasārajam  || 1,3.3
yatra gītaṃ trikaṃ prītyā bhaktijñānavirāgakam  || 1,3.4
vedāṃtavedyaṃ sadvastu viśeṣeṇa pravarṇitam  || 1,3.5
sūta uvāca
śṛṇvaṃtu ṛṣayaḥ sarve purāṇaṃ vedasārajam  || 1,3.6
purā kālena mahatā kalpe 'tīte punaḥpunaḥ  || 1,3.6
asminnupasthite kalpe pravṛtte sṛṣṭikarmaṇi  || 1,3.7
munīnāṃ ṣaṭkulīnānāṃ bruvatāmitaretaram  || 1,3.7
idaṃ paramidaṃ neti vivādaḥ sumahānabhūt || 1,3.8
te 'bhijagmurvidhātāraṃ brahmāṇaṃ praṣṭumavyayam  || 1,3.8
vāgbhirvinayagarbhābhiḥ sarve prāṃjalayo 'bruvan  || 1,3.9
tvaṃ hi sarvajagaddhātā sarvakāraṇakāraṇam  || 1,3.9
kaḥ pumānsarvatattvebhyaḥ purāṇaḥ parataḥ paraḥ  || 1,3.10
brahmovāca
yato vāco nivartaṃte aprāpya manasā saha  || 1,3.10
yasmātsarvamidaṃ brahmaviṣṇurudreṃdrapūrvakam  || 1,3.11
sahabhūteṃdriyaiḥ sarvaiḥ prathamaṃ saṃprasūyate  || 1,3.11
eṣa devo mahādevaḥ sarvajño jagadīśvaraḥ  || 1,3.12
ayaṃ tu parayā bhaktyā dṛśyate nā 'nyathā kvacit || 1,3.12
rudro harirharaścaiva tathānye ca sureśvarāḥ  || 1,3.13
bhaktyā paramayā tasya nityaṃ darśanakāṃkṣiṇaḥ  || 1,3.13
bahunātra kimuktena śive bhaktyā vimucyate  || 1,3.14
prasādāddevatābhaktiḥ prasādo bhaktisaṃbhavaḥ  || 1,3.14
yathehāṃkurato bījaṃ bījato vā yathāṃkuraḥ  || 1,3.14
tasmādīśaprasādārthaṃ yūyaṃ gatvā bhuvaṃ dvijāḥ  || 1,3.15
dīrghasatraṃ samākṛdhvaṃ yūyaṃ varṣasahasrakam  || 1,3.15
amuṣyaivādhvareśasya śivasyaiva prasādataḥ  || 1,3.16
vedoktavidyāsāraṃ tu jñāyate sādhyasādhanaṃ  || 1,3.16
munaya ūcuḥ
atha kiṃ paramaṃ sādhyaṃ kiṃvā tatsādhanaṃ param  || 1,3.17
sādhakaḥ kīdṛśastatra tadidaṃ brūhi tattvataḥ  || 1,3.17
brahmovāca
sādhyaṃ śivapadaprāptiḥ sādhanaṃ tasya sevanam  || 1,3.18
sādhakastatprasādādyo'nityādiphalaniḥspṛhaḥ  || 1,3.18
karma kṛtvā tu vedoktaṃ tadarpitamahāphalam  || 1,3.19
parameśapadaprāptaḥ sālokyādikramāttataḥ  || 1,3.19
tattadbhaktyanusāreṇa sarveṣāṃ paramaṃ phalam  || 1,3.20
tatsādhanaṃ bahuvidhaṃ sākṣādīśena bodhitam  || 1,3.20
saṃkṣipya tatra vaḥ sāraṃ sādhanaṃ prabravīmyaham  || 1,3.21
śrotreṇa śravaṇaṃ tasya vacasā kīrtanaṃ tathā  || 1,3.21
manasā mananaṃ tasya mahāsādhanamucyate  || 1,3.22
śrotavyaḥ kīrtitavyaśca mantavyaśca maheśvaraḥ  || 1,3.22
iti śrutipramāṇaṃ naḥ sādhanenā'munā param  || 1,3.23
sādhyaṃ vrajata sarvārthasādhanaikaparāyaṇāḥ  || 1,3.23
pratyakṣaṃ cakṣuṣā dṛṣṭvā tatra lokaḥ pravartate  || 1,3.24
apratyakṣaṃ hi sarvatra jñātvā śrotreṇa ceṣṭate  || 1,3.24
tasmācchravaṇamevādau śrutvā gurumukhādbudhaḥ  || 1,3.25
tataḥ saṃsādhayedanyatkīrtanaṃ mananaṃ sudhīḥ  || 1,3.25
kramānmananaparyaṃte sādhane 'sminsusādhite  || 1,3.26
śivayogo bhavettena sālokyādikramācchanaiḥ  || 1,3.26
sarvāṃgavyādhayaḥ paścātsarvānaṃdaśca līyate  || 1,3.27
abhyāsātkleśametadvai paścādādyaṃtamaṃgalam  || 1,3.27

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ sādhyasādhanakhaṇḍe tṛtīyo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 4
munaya ūcuḥ
mananaṃ kīdṛśaṃ brahmañchravaṇaṃ cāpi kīdṛśam  || 1,4.1
kīrtanaṃ vā kathaṃ tasya kīrtayaitadyathāyatham  || 1,4.1
brahmovaca
pūjājapeśaguṇarūpavilāsanāmnāṃ yuktipriyeṇa manasā pariśodhanaṃ yat || 1,4.2
tatsaṃtataṃ mananamīśvaradṛṣṭilabhyaṃ sarveṣu sādhanavareṣvapi mukhyamukhyam  || 1,4.2
gītātmanā śrutipadena ca bhāṣayā vā śaṃbhupratāpaguṇarūpavilāsanāmnām  || 1,4.3
vācā sphuṭaṃ tu rasavatstavanaṃ yadasya tatkīrtanaṃ bhavati sā dhanamatra madhyam  || 1,4.3
yenāpi kena karaṇena ca śabdapuṃjaṃ yatra kvacicchivaparaṃ śravaṇeṃdriyeṇa  || 1,4.4
strīkelivaddṛḍhataraṃ praṇidhīyate yattadvai budhāḥ śravaṇamatra jagatprasiddham  || 1,4.4
satsaṃgamena bhavati śravaṇaṃ purastātsaṃkīrtanaṃ paśupateratha taddṛḍhaṃ syāt || 1,4.5
sarvottamaṃ bhavati tanmananaṃ tadaṃte sarvaṃ hi saṃbhavati śaṃkaradṛṣṭipāte  || 1,4.5
sūta uvāca
asminsādhanamāhatmye purā vṛttaṃ munīśvarāḥ  || 1,4.6
yuṣmadarthaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇudhvamavadhānataḥ  || 1,4.6
purā mama gururvyāsaḥ parāśaramuneḥ sutaḥ  || 1,4.7
tapaścacāra saṃbhrāṃtaḥ sarasvatyāstaṭe śubhe  || 1,4.7
gacchanyadṛchayā tatra vimānenārkarociṣā  || 1,4.8
sanatkumāro bhagavāndadarśa mama deśikam  || 1,4.8
dhyānārūḍhaḥ prabuddho 'sau dadarśa tamajātmajam  || 1,4.9
praṇipatyāha saṃbhrāṃtaḥ paraṃ kautūhalaṃ muniḥ  || 1,4.9
dattvārghyamasmai pradadau devayogyaṃ ca viṣṭiram  || 1,4.10
prasannaḥ prāha taṃ prahvaṃ prabhurgaṃbhīrayā girā  || 1,4.10
sanatkumāra uvāca
satyaṃ vastu mune dadhyāḥ sākṣātkaraṇagocaraḥ  || 1,4.11
sa śivothāsahāyotra tapaścarasi kiṃ kṛte  || 1,4.11
evamuktaḥ kumāreṇa provāca svāśayaṃ muniḥ  || 1,4.12
dharmārthakāmamokṣāśca vedamārge kṛtādarāḥ  || 1,4.12
bahudhā sthāpitā loke mayā tvatkṛpayā tathā  || 1,4.13
evaṃ bhutasya mepyevaṃ gurubhūtasya sarvataḥ  || 1,4.13
muktisādhanakaṃ jñānaṃ nodeti paramādbhutam  || 1,4.14
tapaścarāmi muktyarthaṃ na jāne tatra kāraṇam  || 1,4.14
itthaṃ kumāro bhagavān vyāsena muninārthitaḥ  || 1,4.15
samarthaḥ prāha vipreṃdrā niścayaṃ muktikāraṇam  || 1,4.15
śravaṇaṃ kīrtanaṃ śaṃbhormananaṃ ca mahattaram  || 1,4.16
trayaṃ sādhanamuktaṃ ca vidyate vedasaṃmatam  || 1,4.16
purāhamatha saṃbhrāṃto hyanyasādhanasaṃbhramaḥ  || 1,4.17
acale maṃdare śaile tapaścaraṇamācaram  || 1,4.17
śivājñayā tataḥ prāpto bhagavānnandikeśvaraḥ  || 1,4.18
sa me dayālurbhagavānsarvasākṣī gaṇeśvaraḥ  || 1,4.18
uvāca mahyaṃ sasnehaṃ muktisādhanamuttamam  || 1,4.19
śravaṇaṃ kīrtanaṃ śaṃbhormananaṃ vedasaṃmatam  || 1,4.19
trikaṃ ca sādhanaṃ muktau śivena mama bhāṣitam  || 1,4.20
śravaṇādiṃ brahmankuruṣveti muhurmuhuḥ  || 1,4.20
evamuktvā tato vyāsaṃ sānugo vidhinaṃdanaḥ  || 1,4.21
jagāma svavimānena padaṃ paramaśobhanam  || 1,4.21
evamuktaṃ samāsena pūrvavṛttāṃtamuttamam  || 1,4.22
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
śravaṇāditrayaṃ sūta muktyopāyastvayeritaḥ  || 1,4.22
śravaṇāditrike 'śaktaḥ kiṃ kṛtvā mucyate janaḥ  || 1,4.23
ayatnenaiva muktiḥ syātkarmaṇā kena hetunā  || 1,4.23

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe prathamāyāṃ vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyaṃ sādhyasādhanakhaṇḍe caturtho 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 5
sūta uvāca
śravaṇāditrike 'śakto liṃgaṃ beraṃ ca śāṃkaram  || 1,5.1
saṃsthāpya nityamabhyarcya taretsaṃsārasāgaram  || 1,5.1
api dravyaṃ vahedeva yathābalamavaṃcayan  || 1,5.2
arpayelliṃgaberārthamarcayedapi saṃtatam  || 1,5.2
maṃḍapaṃ gopuraṃ tīrthaṃ maṭhaṃ kṣetraṃ tathotsavam  || 1,5.3
vastraṃ gaṃdhaṃ ca mālyaṃ ca dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ ca bhaktitaḥ  || 1,5.3
vividhānnaṃ ca naivedyamapūpavyaṃjanairyutam  || 1,5.4
chatraṃ dhvajaṃ ca vyajanaṃ cāmaraṃ cāpi sāṃgakam  || 1,5.4
rājopacāravatsarvaṃ dhārayelliṃgaberayoḥ  || 1,5.5
pradakṣiṇāṃ namaskāraṃ yathāśakti japaṃ tathā  || 1,5.5
āvāhanādisargāṃtaṃ nityaṃ kuryātsubhaktitaḥ  || 1,5.6
itthamabhyarcya yandevaṃ liṃgebere ca śāṃkare  || 1,5.6
siddhimeti śivaprītyā hitvāpi śravaṇādikam  || 1,5.7
liṃgaberārcanāmātrānmuktāḥ purve mahājanāḥ  || 1,5.7
manuya ūcuḥ
beramātre tu sarvatra pūjyaṃte devatāgaṇāḥ  || 1,5.8
liṃgebere ca sarvatra kathaṃ saṃpūjyate śivaḥ  || 1,5.8
sūta uvāca
aho munīśvarāḥ puṇyaṃ praśnametanmahādbhutam  || 1,5.9
atra vaktā mahādevo nānyo 'sti puruṣaḥ kvacit || 1,5.9
śivenoktaṃ pravakṣyāmi kramādgurumukhācchrutam  || 1,5.10
śivaiko brahmarūpatvānniṣkalaḥ parikīrtitaḥ  || 1,5.10
rūpitvātsakalastadvattasmātsakalaniṣkalaḥ  || 1,5.11
niṣkalatvānnirākāraṃ liṃgaṃ tasya samāgatam  || 1,5.11
sakalatvāttathā beraṃ sākāraṃ tasya saṃgatam  || 1,5.12
sakalākalarūpatvādbrahmaśabdābhidhaḥ paraḥ  || 1,5.12
api liṃge ca bere ca nityamabhyarcyate janaiḥ  || 1,5.13
abrahmatvāttadanyeṣāṃ niṣkalatvaṃ na hi kvacit || 1,5.13
tasmātte niṣkale liṃge nārādhyaṃte sureśvarāḥ  || 1,5.14
abrahmatvācca jīvatvāttathānye devatāgaṇāḥ  || 1,5.14
tūṣṇīṃ sakalamātratvādarcyaṃte beramātrake  || 1,5.15
jīvatvaṃ śaṃkarānyeṣāṃ brahmatvaṃ śaṃkarasya ca  || 1,5.15
vedāṃtasārasaṃsiddhaṃ praṇavārthe prakāśanāt || 1,5.16
evameva purā pṛṣṭo maṃdare nandikeśvaraḥ  || 1,5.16
sanatkumāramuninā brahmaputreṇa dhīmatā   || 1,5.17
sanatkumāra uvāca
śivānyadevavaśyānāṃ sarveṣāmapi sarvataḥ  || 1,5.17
beramātraṃ ca pūjārthaṃ śrutaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ ca bhūriśaḥ  || 1,5.18
śivamātrasya pūjāyāṃ liṃgaṃ beraṃ ca dṛśyate  || 1,5.18
atastadbrūhi kalyāṇa tattvaṃ me sādhubodhanam  || 1,5.19
nandikeśvara uvāca
anuttaramimaṃ praśnaṃ rahasyaṃ brahmalakṣaṇam  || 1,5.19
kathayāmi śivenoktaṃ bhaktiyuktasya te 'nagha  || 1,5.20
śivasya brahmarūpatvānniṣkalatvācca niṣkalam  || 1,5.20
liṃgaṃ tasyaiva pūjāyāṃ sarvavedeṣu saṃmatam  || 1,5.21
tasyaiva sakalatvācca tathā sakalaniṣkalam  || 1,5.21
sakalaṃ ca tathā beraṃ pūjāyāṃ lokasaṃmatam  || 1,5.22
śivānyeṣāṃ ca jīvatvātsakalatvācca sarvataḥ  || 1,5.22
beramātraṃ ca pūjāyāṃ saṃmataṃ vedanirṇaye  || 1,5.23
svāvirbhāve ca devānāṃ sakalaṃ rūpameva hi  || 1,5.23
śivasya liṃgaṃ beraṃ ca darśane dṛśyate khalu  || 1,5.24
sanatkumāra uvāca
uktaṃ tvayā mahābhāga liṃgaberapracāraṇam  || 1,5.24
śivasya ca tadanyeṣāṃ vibhajya paramārthataḥ  || 1,5.25
tasmāttadeva paramaṃ liṃgaberādisaṃbhavam  || 1,5.25
śrotumicchāmi yogīṃdra liṃgāvirbhāvalakṣaṇam  || 1,5.26
nandikeśvara uvāca
śṛṇu vatsa bhavatprītyā vakṣyāmi paramārthataḥ  || 1,5.26
purā kalpe mahākāle prapanne lokaviśrute  || 1,5.27
āyudhyetāṃ mahātmānau brahmaviṣṇū parasparam  || 1,5.27
tayormānaṃ nirākartuṃ tanmadhye parameśvaraḥ  || 1,5.28
niṣkalastaṃbharūpeṇa svarūpaṃ samadarśayat || 1,5.28
tataḥ svaliṃgacihnatvātstambhato niṣkalaṃ śivaḥ  || 1,5.29
svaliṃgaṃ darśayāmāsa jagatāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 1,5.29
tadāprabhṛti lokeṣu niṣkalaṃ liṃgamaiśvaram  || 1,5.30
sakalaṃ ca tathā beraṃ śivasyaiva prakalpitam  || 1,5.30
śivānyeṣaḥ tu devānāṃ beramātraṃ prakalpitam  || 1,5.31
tattadberaṃ tu devānāṃ tattadbhogapradaṃ śubham  || 1,5.31
śivasya liṃgaberatvaṃ bhogamokṣapradaṃ śubham  || 1,5.31

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ pañcamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 6
nandikeśvara uvāca
purā kadācidyogīṃdra viṣṇurviṣadharāsanaḥ  || 1,6.1
suṣvāpa parayā bhūtyā svānugairapi saṃvṛtaḥ  || 1,6.1
yadṛcchayā gatastatra brahmā brahmavidāṃvaraḥ  || 1,6.2
apṛcchatpuṃḍarīkākṣaṃ śayanaṃ sarvasundaram  || 1,6.2
kastvaṃ puruṣavaccheṣe dṛṣṭvā māmapi dṛptavat || 1,6.3
uttiṣṭha vatsa māṃ paśya tava nāthamihāgatam  || 1,6.3
āgataṃ gurumārādhyaṃ dṛṣṭvā yo dṛptavaccaret || 1,6.4
drohiṇastasya mūḍhasya prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate  || 1,6.4
iti śrutvā vacaḥ kruddho bahiḥ śāṃtavadācarat || 1,6.5
svasti te svāgataṃ vatsa tiṣṭha pīṭhamito viśa  || 1,6.5
kimu te vyāgravadvaktraṃ vibhāti viṣamekṣaṇam  || 1,6.6
brahmovāca
vatsa viṣṇo mahāmānamāgataṃ kālavegataḥ  || 1,6.6
pitāmahaśca jagataḥ pātā ca tava vatsaka  || 1,6.7
viṣṇuruvāca
matsthaṃ jagadidaṃ vatsa manuṣe tvaṃ hi coravat || 1,6.7
mannābhikamalājjātaḥ putrastvaṃ bhāṣase vṛthā  || 1,6.8
nandikeśvara uvāca
evaṃ hi vadatostatra mugdhayorajayostadā  || 1,6.8
ahameva baro na tvamahaṃ prabhurahaṃ prabhuḥ  || 1,6.9
parasparaṃ haṃtukāmau cakratuḥ samarodyamam  || 1,6.9
yuyudhāte 'marau vīrau haṃsapakṣīṃdravāhanau  || 1,6.10
vairaṃcyā vaiṣṇavāścaivaṃ mitho yuyudhire tadā  || 1,6.10
tāvadvimānagatayaḥ sarvā vai devajātayaḥ  || 1,6.11
didṛkṣavaḥ samājagmuḥ samaraṃ taṃ mahādbhutam  || 1,6.11
kṣipaṃtaḥ puṣpavarṣāṇi paśyaṃtaḥ svairamaṃbare  || 1,6.12
suparṇavāhanastatra kruddho vai brahmavakṣasi  || 1,6.12
mumoca bāṇānasahānastrāṃśca vividhānbahūn  || 1,6.13
mumocā'tha vidhiḥ kruddho viṣṇorurasi duḥsahān  || 1,6.13
bāṇānanalasaṃkāśānastrāṃśca bahuśastadā  || 1,6.14
tadāścaryamiti spaṣṭaṃ tayoḥ samaragocaram  || 1,6.14
samīkṣya daivatagaṇāḥ śaśaṃsurbhṛśamākulāḥ  || 1,6.15
tato viṣṇuḥ susaṃkruddhaḥ śvasanvyasanakarśitaḥ  || 1,6.15
māheśvarāstraṃ matimān saṃdadhe brahmaṇopari  || 1,6.16
tato brahmā bhṛśaṃ kruddhaḥ kaṃpayanviśvameva hi  || 1,6.16
astraṃ pāśupataṃ ghoraṃ saṃdadhe viṣṇuvakṣasi  || 1,6.17
tatastadutthitaṃ vyomni tapanāyutasannibham  || 1,6.17
sahasramukhamatyugraṃ caṃḍavātabhayaṃkaram  || 1,6.18
astradvayamidaṃ tatra brahmaviṣṇvorbhayaṃkaram  || 1,6.18
itthaṃ babhūva samaro brahmaviṣṇvoḥ parasparam  || 1,6.19
tato devagaṇāḥ sarve viṣaṇṇā bhṛśamākulāḥ  || 1,6.19
ūcuḥ parasparaṃ tāta rājakṣobhe yathā dvijāḥ  || 1,6.19
sṛṣṭiḥ sthitiśca saṃhārastiro bhāvopyanugrahaḥ  || 1,6.20
yasmātpravartate tasmai brahmaṇe ca triśūline  || 1,6.20
aśakyamanyairyadanugrahaṃ vinā tṛṇakṣayopyatra yadṛcchayā kvacit || 1,6.21
iti devābhayaṃ kṛtvā vicinvaṃtaḥ śivakṣayam  || 1,6.22
jagmuḥ kailāsaśikharaṃ yatrāste caṃdraśekharaḥ  || 1,6.22
dṛṣṭvaivamamarā hṛṣṭāḥ padaṃtatpārameśvaram  || 1,6.23
praṇemuḥ praṇavākāraṃ praviṣṭāstatra sadmani  || 1,6.23
tepi tatra sabhāmadhye maṃḍape maṇiviṣṭare  || 1,6.24
virājamānamumayā dadṛśurdevapuṃgavam  || 1,6.24
savyottaretarapadaṃ tadarhitakarāṃ bujam  || 1,6.25
svagaṇaiḥ sarvato juṣṭaṃ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitam  || 1,6.25
vījyamānaṃ viśoṣajaiḥ strījanaistīvrabhāvanaiḥ  || 1,6.26
śasyamānaṃ sadāvedairanugṛhṇaṃtamīśvaram  || 1,6.26
dṛṣṭvaivamīśamamarāḥ saṃtoṣasalilekṣaṇāḥ  || 1,6.27
daṃḍavaddūrato vatsa namaścakrurmahāgaṇāḥ  || 1,6.27
tānavekṣya patirdevānsamīpe cāhvayadgaṇaiḥ  || 1,6.28
atha saṃhlādayandevāndevo devaśikhāmaṇiḥ  || 1,6.28
avocadarthagaṃbhīraṃ vacanaṃ madhumaṃgalam  || 1,6.28

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ ṣaṣṭho 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 7
īśvara uvāca
vatsakāḥ svastivaḥ kaccidvartate mama śāsanāt || 1,7.1
jagacca devatāvaṃśaḥ svasvakarmaṇi kiṃ navā  || 1,7.1
prāgeva viditaṃ yuddhaṃ brahmaviṣṇvormayāsurāḥ  || 1,7.2
bhavatāmabhitāpena paunaruktyena bhāṣitam  || 1,7.2
iti sasmitayā mādhvyā kumāraparibhāṣayā  || 1,7.3
samatoṣayadaṃbāyāḥ sa patistatsuravrajam  || 1,7.3
atha yuddhāṃgaṇaṃ gaṃtuṃ haridhātroradhīśvaraḥ  || 1,7.4
ājñāpayadgaṇeśānāṃ śataṃ tatraiva saṃsadi  || 1,7.4
tato vādyaṃ bahuvidhaṃ prayāṇāya pareśituḥ  || 1,7.5
gaṇeśvarāśca saṃnaddhā nānāvāhanabhūṣaṇāḥ  || 1,7.5
praṇavākāramādyaṃtaṃ pañcamaṃḍalamaṃḍitam  || 1,7.6
āruroha rathaṃ bhadramaṃbikāpatirīśvaraḥ  || 1,7.6
sasūnugaṇamiṃdrādyāḥ sarvepyanuyayuḥ surāḥ  || 1,7.6
citradhvajavyajanacāmarapuṣpavarṣasaṃgatinṛtyanivahairaipi vādyavargaiḥ  || 1,7. 7
saṃmānitaḥ paśupatiḥ parayā ca devyā sākaṃ tayoḥ samarabhūmimagātsasainyaḥ  || 1,7.7
samīkṣyaṃ tu tayoryuddhaṃ nigūḍho 'bhraṃ samāsthitaḥ  || 1,7.8
samāptavādyanirghoṣaḥ śāṃtorugaṇaniḥsvanaḥ  || 1,7.8
atha brahmācyutau vīrau haṃtukāmau parasparam  || 1,7.9
māheśvareṇa cā'streṇa tathā pāśupatena ca  || 1,7.9
astrajvālairatho dagdhaṃ brahmaviṣṇvorjagattrayam  || 1,7.10
īśopi taṃ nirīkṣyātha hyakālapralayaṃ bhṛśam  || 1,7.10
mahānalastaṃbhavibhīṣaṇākṛtirbabhūva tanmadhyatale sa niṣkalaḥ  || 1,7.11
te astre cāpi sajvāle lokasaṃharaṇakṣame  || 1,7.12
nipatetuḥ kṣaṇe naiva hyāvirbhūte mahānale  || 1,7.12
dṛṣṭvā tadadbhutaṃ citramastraśāṃtikaraṃ śubham  || 1,7.13
kimetadadbhutākāramityūcuśca parasparam  || 1,7.13
atīṃdriyamidaṃ staṃbhamagnirūpaṃ kimutthitam  || 1,7.14
asyordhvamapi cādhaścāvayorlakṣyameva hi  || 1,7.14
iti vyavasitau vīrau militau vīramāninau  || 1,7.15
tatparau tatparīkṣārthaṃ pratasthāte 'tha satvaram  || 1,7.15
āvayormiśrayostatra kāryamekaṃ na saṃbhavet || 1,7.16
ityuktvā sūkaratanurviṣṇustasyādimīyivān  || 1,7.16
tathā brahmāhaṃ satanustadaṃtaṃ vīkṣituṃ yayau  || 1,7.17
bhittvā pātālanilayaṃ gatvā dūrataraṃ hariḥ  || 1,7.17
nā 'pi śyāttasya saṃsthānaṃ staṃbhasyānalavarcasaḥ  || 1,7.18
śrāṃtaḥ sa sūkaraharīḥ prāpa pūrvaṃ raṇāṃgaṇam  || 1,7.18
atha gacchaṃstu vyomnā ca vidhistāta pitā tava  || 1,7.19
dadarśa ketakī puṣpaṃ kiṃcidvicyutamadbhutam  || 1,7.19
atisaurabhyamamlānaṃ bahuvarṣacyutaṃ tathā  || 1,7.20
anvīkṣya ca tayoḥ kṛtyaṃ bhagavānparameśvaraḥ  || 1,7.20
parihāsaṃ tu kṛtavānkaṃpanāccalitaṃ śiraḥ  || 1,7.21
tasmāttāvanugṛhṇātuṃ cyutaṃ ketakamuttamam  || 1,7.21
kiṃ tvaṃ patasi puṣpeśa puṣparāṭkena vā dhṛtam  || 1,7.22
ādimasyāprameyasya staṃbhamadhyāccyutaściram  || 1,7.22
na saṃpaśyāmi tasmāttvaṃ jahyāśāmaṃtadarśane  || 1,7.23
asyāṃ tasya ca sevārthaṃ haṃsamūrtirihāgataḥ  || 1,7.23
itaḥ paraṃ sakhe me 'dya tvayā kartavyamīpsitam  || 1,7.24
mayā saha tvayā vācyametadviṣṇośca sannidhau  || 1,7.24
staṃbhāṃto vīkṣito dhātrā tatra sākṣyahamacyuta  || 1,7.25
ityuktvā ketakaṃ tatra praṇanāma punaḥ naḥ  || 1,7.25
asatyamapi śastaṃ syādāpadītyanuśāsanam  || 1,7.25
samīkṣya tatrā'cyutamāyataśramaṃ pranaṣṭaharṣaṃ tu nanarta harṣāt || 1,7.26
uvāca cainaṃ paramārthamacyutaṃ ṣaṃḍhāttavādaḥ sa vidhistato 'cyutam  || 1,7.26
staṃbhāgrametatsamudīkṣitaṃ hare tatraiva sākṣī nanu ketakaṃ tvidam  || 1,7.27
tato 'vadattatra hi ketakaṃ mṛṣā tatheti taddhātṛvacastadaṃtike  || 1,7.27
hariśca tatsatyamitīva ciṃtayaṃścakāra tasmai vidhaye namaḥ svayam  || 1,7.28
ṣoḍaśairupacāraiśca pūjayāmāsa taṃ vidhim  || 1,7.28
vidhiṃ prahartuṃ śaṭhamagniliṃgataḥ sa īśvarastatra babhūva sākṛtiḥ  || 1,7.29
samutthitaḥ svāmi vilokanātpunaḥ prakaṃpapāṇiḥ parigṛhya tatpadam  || 1,7.29
ādyaṃtahīnavapuṣi tvayi mohabuddhyā bhūyādvimarśa iha nāvati kāmanotthaḥ  || 1,7.30
sa tvaṃ prasīda karuṇākara kaśmalaṃ nau mṛṣṭaṃ kṣamasva vihitaṃ bhavataiva kelyā  || 1,7.30
īśvara uvāca
vatsaprasanno 'smi hare yatastvamīśatvamicchannapi satyavākyam  || 1,7.31
brūyāstataste bhavitā janeṣu sāmyaṃ mayā satkṛtirapyalapthāḥ  || 1,7.31
itaḥ paraṃ te pṛthagātmanaśca kṣetrapratiṣṭhotsavapūjanaṃ ca  || 1,7.32
iti devaḥ purā prītaḥ satyena haraye param  || 1,7.33
dadau svasāmyamatyarthaṃ devasaṃghe ca paśyati  || 1,7.33

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ saptamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 8
nandikeśvara uvāca
sasarjātha mahādevaḥ puruṣaṃ kaṃcidadbhutam  || 1,8.1
bhairavākhyaṃ bhruvormadhyādbrahmadarpajighāṃsayā  || 1,8.1
sa vai tadā tatra patiṃ praṇamya śivamaṃgaṇe  || 1,8.2
kiṃ kāryaṃ karavāṇyatra śīghramājñāpaya prabho  || 1,8.2
vatsayo 'yaṃ vidhiḥ sākṣājjagatāmādyadaivatam  || 1,8.3
nūnamarcaya khaḍgaṃ svaṃ tigmena javasā param  || 1,8.3
sa vai gṛhītvaikakareṇa keśaṃ tatpañcamaṃ dṛptamasatyabhāṣaṇam  || 1,8.4
chittvā śirāṃsyasya nihaṃtumudyataḥ prakaṃpayankhaḍgamatisphuṭaṃ karaiḥ  || 1,8.4
pitā tavotsṛṣṭavibhūṣaṇāṃbarasraguttarīyāmalakeśasaṃhatiḥ  || 1,8.5
pravātaraṃbheva lateva caṃcalaḥ papāta vai bhairavapādapaṃkaje  || 1,8.5
tāvadvidhiṃ tāta didṛkṣuracyutaḥ kṛpālurasmatpatipādapallavam  || 1,8.6
niṣicya bāṣpairavadatkṛtāṃjaliryathā śiśuḥ svaṃ pitaraṃ kalākṣaram  || 1,8.6
acyuta uvāca
tvayā prayatnena purā hi dattaṃ yadasya pañcānanamīśacihnam  || 1,8.7
tasmātkṣamasvādyamanugrahārhaṃ kuru prasādaṃ vidhaye hyamuṣmai  || 1,8.7
ityarthito 'cyuteneśastuṣṭaḥ suragaṇāṃgaṇe  || 1,8.8
nivartayāmāsa tadā bhairavaṃ brahmadaṃḍataḥ  || 1,8.8
athāha devaḥ kitavaṃ vidhiṃ vigatakaṃdharam  || 1,8.9
brahmaṃstvamarhaṇākāṃkṣī śaṭhamīśatvamāsthitaḥ  || 1,8.9
nātaste satkṛtirloke bhūyātsthānotsavādikam  || 1,8.10
brahmovaca
svāminprasīdādya mahāvibhūte manye varaṃ varada me śirasaḥ pramokṣam  || 1,8.10
namastubhyaṃ bhagavate baṃdhave viśvayonaye  || 1,8.11
sahiṣṇave sarvadoṣāṇāṃ śaṃbhave śailadhanvane  || 1,8.11
īśvara uvāca
arājabhayametadvai jagatsarvaṃ na śiṣyati  || 1,8.12
tatastvaṃ jahi daṃḍārhaṃ vaha lokadhuraṃ śiśo  || 1,8.12
varaṃ dadāmi te tatra gṛhāṇa durlabhaṃ param  || 1,8.13
vaitānikeṣu gṛhyeṣu yajñe ca bhavān guruḥ  || 1,8.13
niṣphalastvadṛte yajñaḥ sāṃgaśca sahadakṣiṇaḥ  || 1,8.14
athāha devaḥ kitavaṃ ketakaṃ kūṭasākṣiṇam  || 1,8.14
re re ketaka duṣṭastvaṃ śaṭha dūramito vraja  || 1,8.15
mamāpi prema te puṣpe mā bhūtpūjāsvitaḥ param  || 1,8.15
ityukte tatra devena ketakaṃ devajātayaḥ  || 1,8.16
sarvāni vārayāmāsustatpārśvādanyatastadā  || 1,8.16
ketaka uvāca
namaste nātha me janmaniṣphalaṃ bhavadājñayā  || 1,8.17
saphalaṃ kriyatāṃ tāta kṣamyatāṃ mama kilbiṣam  || 1,8.17
jñānājñānakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ nāśayatyeva te smṛtiḥ  || 1,8.18
tādṛśe tvayi dṛṣṭe me mithyādoṣaḥ kuto bhavet || 1,8.18
tathā stutastu bhagavānketakena sabhātale  || 1,8.19
na me tvaddhāraṇaṃ yogyaṃ satyavāgahamīśvaraḥ  || 1,8.19
madīyāstvāṃ dhariṣyaṃi janma te saphalaṃ tataḥ  || 1,8.20
tvaṃ vai vitānavyājena mamopari bhaviṣyasi  || 1,8.20
ityanugṛhya bhagavānketakaṃ vidhimādhavau  || 1,8.21
virarāja sabhāmadhye sarvadevairabhiṣṭutaḥ  || 1,8.21

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāmaṣṭamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 9
nandikeśvara uvāca
tatrāṃtare tau ca nāthaṃ praṇamya vidhimādhavau  || 1,9.1
baddhāṃjalipuṭau tūṣṇīṃ tasthaturdakṣavāmagau  || 1,9.1
tatra saṃsthāpya tau devaṃ sakuṭuṃbaṃ varāsane  || 1,9.2
pūjayāmāsatuḥ pūjyaṃ puṇyaiḥ puruṣavastubhiḥ  || 1,9.2
pauruṣaṃ prākṛtaṃ vastujñeyaṃ dīrghālpakālikam  || 1,9.3
hāranūpurakeyūrakirīṭamaṇikuṃḍalaiḥ  || 1,9.3
yajñasūtrottarīyasrakkṣaumamālyāṃgulīyakaiḥ  || 1,9.4
puṣpatāṃbūlakarpūracaṃdanāgurulepanaiḥ  || 1,9.4
dhūpadīpasitacchatravyajanadhvajacāmaraiḥ  || 1,9.5
anyairdivyopahāraiśca vāṅmanotītavaibhavaiḥ  || 1,9.5
patiyogyaiḥ paśvalabhyaistau samarcayatāṃ patim  || 1,9.6
yadyacchreṣṭhatamaṃ vastu patiyogyaṃ hitaddhvaje  || 1,9.6
tadvastvakhilamīśopi pāraṃ paryacikīrṣayā  || 1,9.7
sabhyānāṃ pradadau hṛṣṭaḥ pṛthaktatra yathākramam  || 1,9.7
kolāhalo mahānāsīttatra tadvastu gṛhṇatām  || 1,9.8
tatraiva brahmaviṣṇubhyāṃ cārcitaḥ śaṃkaraḥ purā  || 1,9.8
prasannaḥ prāha tau namrau sasmitaṃ bhaktivardhanaḥ  || 1,9.9
īśvara uvāca
tuṣṭo 'hamadya vāṃ vatsau pūjayā 'sminmahādine  || 1,9.9
dinametattataḥ puṇyaṃ bhaviṣyati mahattaram  || 1,9.10
śivarātririti khyātā tithireṣā mama priyā  || 1,9.10
etatkāle tu yaḥ kuryātpūjāṃ malliṃgaberayoḥ  || 1,9.11
kuryāttu jagataḥ kṛtyaṃ sthitisargādikaṃ pumān  || 1,9.11
śivarātrāvahorātraṃ nirāhāro jiteṃdriyaḥ   || 1,9.12
arcayedvā yathānyāyaṃ yathābalamavaṃcakaḥ  || 1,9.12
yatphalaṃ mama pūjāyāṃ varṣamekaṃ niraṃtaram  || 1,9.13
tatphalaṃ labhate sadyaḥ śivarātrau madarcanāt || 1,9.13
maddharmavṛddhikālo 'yaṃ caṃdrakāla ivāṃbudheḥ  || 1,9.14
pratiṣṭhādyutsavo yatra māmako maṃgalāyanaḥ  || 1,9.14
yatpunaḥ staṃbharūpeṇa svāvirāsamahaṃ purā  || 1,9.15
sa kālo mārgaśīrṣe tu syādārdrā ṛkṣamarbhakau  || 1,9.15
ārdrāyāṃ mārgaśīrṣe tu yaḥ paśyenmāmumāsakham  || 1,9.16
madberamapi vā liṃgaṃ sa guhādapi me priyaḥ  || 1,9.16
alaṃ darśanamātreṇa phalaṃ tasmindine śubhe  || 1,9.17
abhyarcanaṃ cedadhikaṃ phalaṃ vācāmagocaram  || 1,9.17
raṇaraṃgatale 'muṣminyadahaṃ liṃgavarṣmaṇā  || 1,9.18
jṛṃbhito liṃgavattasmālliṃgasthānamidaṃ bhavet || 1,9.18
anādyaṃtamidaṃ staṃbhamaṇumātraṃ bhaviṣyati  || 1,9.19
darśanārthaṃ hi jagatāṃ pūjanārthaṃ hi putrako  || 1,9.19
bhogāvahamidaṃ liṃgaṃ bhuktiṃ muktyekasādhanam  || 1,9.20
darśanasparśanadhyānājjaṃtūnāṃ janmamocanam  || 1,9.20
analācalasaṃkāśaṃ yadidaṃ liṃgamutthitam  || 1,9.21
aruṇācalamityeva tadidaṃ khyātimeṣyati  || 1,9.21
atra tīrthaṃ ca bahudhā bhaviṣyati mahattaram  || 1,9.22
muktirapyatra jaṃtūnāṃ vāsena maraṇena ca  || 1,9.22
sthotsavādikalyāṇaṃ janāvāsaṃ tu sarvataḥ  || 1,9.23
atra dattaṃ hutaṃ japtaṃ sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 1,9.23
matkṣetrādapi sarvasmātkṣetrametanmahattaram  || 1,9.24
atra saṃsmṛtimātreṇa muktirbhavati dehinām  || 1,9.24
tasmānmahattaramidaṃ kṣetramatyaṃtaśobhanam  || 1,9.25
sarvakalyāṇasaṃpūrṇaṃ sarvamuktikaraṃ śubham  || 1,9.25
arcayitvā 'tra māmeva liṃge liṃginamīśvaram  || 1,9.26
sālokyaṃ caiva sāmīpyaṃ sārūpyaṃ sārṣṭireva ca  || 1,9.26
sāyujyamiti pañcaite kriyādīnāṃ phalaṃ matam  || 1,9.27
sarvepi yūyaṃ sakalaṃ prāpsyathāśu manoratham  || 1,9.27
nandikeśvara uvāca
ityanugṛhya bhagavānvinītau vidhimādhavau  || 1,9.28
yatpūrvaṃ prahataṃ yuddhe tayoḥ sainyaṃ parasparam  || 1,9.28
tadutthāpayadatyarthaṃ svaśaktyāmṛtadhārayā  || 1,9.29
tayormāḍhyaṃ ca vairaṃ ca vyapanetumuvāca tau  || 1,9.29
sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ ceti svarūpadvayamasti me  || 1,9.30
nānyasya kasyacittasmādanyaḥ sarvopyanīśvaraḥ  || 1,9.30
purastātstaṃbharūpeṇa paścādrūpeṇa cārbhakau  || 1,9.31
brahmatvaṃ niṣkalaṃ proktamīśatvaṃ sakalaṃ tathā  || 1,9.31
dvayaṃ mamaiva saṃsiddhaṃ na madanyasya kasyacit || 1,9.32
tasmādīśatvamanyeṣāṃ yuvayorapi na kvacit || 1,9.32
tadajñānena vāṃ vṛttamīśamānaṃ mahādbhutam  || 1,9.33
tannirākartumatraivamutthito 'haṃ raṇakṣitau  || 1,9.33
tyajataṃ mānamātmīyaṃ mayīśe kurutaṃ matim  || 1,9.34
matprasādena lokeṣu sarvopyarthaḥ prakāśate  || 1,9.34
gurūktirvyaṃjakaṃ tatra pramāṇaṃ vā punaḥ punaḥ  || 1,9.35
brahmatattvamidaṃ gūḍhaṃ bhavatprītyā bhaṇāmyaham  || 1,9.35
ahameva paraṃ brahma matsvarūpaṃ kalākalam  || 1,9.36
brahmatvādīśvaraścāhaṃ kṛtyaṃ menugrahādikam  || 1,9.36
bṛhattvādbṛṃhaṇatvācca brahmāhaṃ brahmakeśavau  || 1,9.37
samatvādvyāpakatvācca tathaivātmāhamarbhakau  || 1,9.37
anātmānaḥ pare sarve jīvā eva na saṃśayaḥ  || 1,9.38
anugrahādyaṃ sargāṃgaṃ jagatkṛtyaṃ ca paṃkajam  || 1,9.38
īśatvādeva me nityaṃ na madanyasya kasyacit || 1,9.39
ādau brahmattvabuddhyarthaṃ niṣkalaṃ liṃgamutthitam  || 1,9.39
tasmādajñātamīśatvaṃ vyaktaṃ dyotayituṃ hi vām  || 1,9.40
sakalohamato jātaḥ sākṣādīśastu tatkṣaṇāt || 1,9.40
sakalatvamato jñeyamīśatvaṃ mayi satvaram  || 1,9.41
yadidaṃ niṣkalaṃ staṃbhaṃ mama brahmatvabodhakam  || 1,9.41
liṃgalakṣaṇayuktatvānmama liṃgaṃ bhavedidam  || 1,9.42
tadidaṃ nityamabhyarcyaṃ yuvābhyāmatra putrakau  || 1,9.42
madātmakamidaṃ nityaṃ mama sānnidhyakāraṇam  || 1,9.43
mahatpūjyamidaṃ nityamabhedālliṃgasiṃginoḥ  || 1,9.43
yatrapratiṣṭhitaṃ yena madīyaṃ liṃgamīdṛśam  || 1,9.44
tatra pratiṣṭhitaḥ sohamapratiṣṭhopi vatsakau  || 1,9.44
matsāmyamekaliṃgasya sthāpane phalamīritam  || 1,9.45
dvitīye sthāpite liṃge madaikyaṃ phalameva hi  || 1,9.45
liṃgaṃ prādhānyataḥ sthāpyaṃ tathāberaṃ tu gauṇakam  || 1,9.46
liṃgābhāvena tatkṣetraṃ saberamapi sarvataḥ  || 1,9.46

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ navamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 10
brahmaviṣṇū ūcatuḥ
sargādipañcakṛtyasya lakṣaṇaṃ brūhi nau prabho  || 1,10.1
śiva uvāca
matkṛtyabodhanaṃ guhyaṃ kṛpayā prabravīmi vām  || 1,10.1
sṛṣṭiḥ sthitiśca saṃhārastirobhāvo 'pyanugrahaḥ  || 1,10.2
pañcaiva me jagatkṛtyaṃ nityasiddhamajācyutau  || 1,10.2
sargaḥ saṃsārasaṃraṃbhastatpratiṣṭhā sthitirmatā  || 1,10.3
saṃhāro mardanaṃ tasya tirobhāvastadutkramaḥ  || 1,10.3
tanmokṣo 'nugrahastanme kṛtyamevaṃ hi pañcakam  || 1,10.4
kṛtyametadvahatyanyastūṣṇīṃ gopurabiṃbavat || 1,10.4
sargādi yaccatuṣkṛtyaṃ saṃsāraparijṛṃbhaṇam  || 1,10.5
pañcamaṃ muktiheturvai nityaṃ mayi ca susthiram  || 1,10.5
tadidaṃ pañcabhūteṣu dṛśyate māmakairjanaiḥ  || 1,10.6
sṛṣṭirbhūmau sthitistoye saṃhāraḥ pāvake tathā  || 1,10.6
tirobhāvo 'nile tadvadanugraha ihāmbare  || 1,10.7
sṛjyate dharayā sarvamadbhiḥ sarvaṃ pravardhate  || 1,10.7
ardyate tejasā sarvaṃ vāyunā cāpanīyate  || 1,10.8
vyomnānugṛhyate sarvaṃ jñeyamevaṃ hi sūribhiḥ  || 1,10.8
pañcakṛtyamidaṃ boḍhuṃ mamāsti mukhapañcakam  || 1,10.9
caturdikṣu caturvaktraṃ tanmadhye pañcamaṃ mukham  || 1,10.9
yuvābhyāṃ tapasā labdhametatkṛtyadvayaṃ sutau  || 1,10.10
sṛṣṭisthityabhīdhaṃ bhāgyaṃ mattaḥ prītādatipriyam  || 1,10.10
tathā rudramaheśābhyāmanyatkṛtyadvayaṃ param  || 1,10.11
anugrahākhyaṃ kenāpi labdhuṃ naiva hi śakyate  || 1,10.11
tatsarvaṃ paurvikaṃ karma yuvābhyāṃ kālavismṛtam  || 1,10.12
na tadrudra maheśābhyāṃ vismṛtaṃ karma tādṛśam  || 1,10.12
rūpe veśe ca kṛtye ca vāhane cāsane tathā  || 1,10.13
āyudhādau ca matsāmyamasmābhistatkṛte kṛtam  || 1,10.13
maddhyānavirahādvatsau mauḍhyaṃ vāmevamāgatam  || 1,10.14
majjñāne sati naivaṃ syānmānaṃ rūpe maheśavat || 1,10.14
tasmānmajjñānasiddhyarthaṃ maṃtramoṃkāranāmakam  || 1,10.15
itaḥ paraṃ prajapataṃ māmakaṃ mānabhaṃjanam  || 1,10.15
upādiśaṃ nijaṃ maṃtramoṃkāramurumaṃgalam  || 1,10.16
oṃkāro manmukhājjajñe prathamaṃ matprabodhakaḥ  || 1,10.16
vācako 'yamahaṃ vācyo maṃtro 'yaṃ hi madātmakaḥ  || 1,10.17
tadanusmaraṇaṃ nityaṃ mamānusmaraṇaṃ bhavet || 1,10.17
akārauttarātpūrvamukāraḥ paścimānanāt || 1,10.18
makāro dakṣiṇamukhādbiṃduḥ prāṅmukhatastathā  || 1,10.18
nādo madhyamukhādevaṃ pañcadhā 'sau vijṛṃbhitaḥ  || 1,10.19
ekībhūtaḥ punastadvadomityekākṣaro bhavet || 1,10.19
nāmarūpātmakaṃ sarvaṃ vedabhūtakuladvayam  || 1,10.20
vyāptametena maṃtreṇa śivaśaktyośca bodhakaḥ  || 1,10.20
asmātpañcākṣaraṃ jajñe bodhakaṃ sakalasyatat || 1,10.21
ākārādikrameṇaiva nakārādiyathākramam  || 1,10.21
asmātpañcākṣarājjātā mātṛkāḥ pañcabhedataḥ  || 1,10.22
tasmācchiraścaturvaktrāttripādgāya trireva hi  || 1,10.22
vedaḥ sarvastato jajñe tato vai maṃtrakoṭayaḥ  || 1,10.23
tattanmaṃtreṇa tatsiddhiḥ sarvasiddhirito bhavet || 1,10.23
anena maṃtrakaṃdena bhogo mokṣaśca siddhyati  || 1,10.24
sakalā maṃtrarājānaḥ sākṣādbhogapradāḥ śubhāḥ  || 1,10.24
naṃdikeśvara uvāca
punastayostatra tiraḥ paṭaṃ guruḥ prakalpya maṃtraṃ ca samādiśatparam  || 1,10.25
nidhāya tacchīrṣṇi karāṃbujaṃ śanairudaṅmukhaṃ saṃsthitayoḥ sahāṃbikaḥ  || 1,10.25
triruccāryāgrahīnmaṃtraṃ yaṃtrataṃtroktipūrvakam  || 1,10.26
śiṣyau ca tau dakṣiṇāyāmātmānaṃ ca samarpayat || 1,10.26
prabaddhahastau kila tau tadaṃtike tameva devaṃ jagaturjagadgurum  || 1,10.27
brahmācyutāvūcatuḥ
namo niṣkalarūpāya namo niṣkalatejase  || 1,10.28
namaḥ sakalanāthāya namaste sakalātmane  || 1,10.28
namaḥ
praṇavavācyāya namaḥ praṇavaliṃgine  || 1,10.29
namaḥ sṛṣṭyādikartre ca namaḥ pañcamukhāyate  || 1,10.29
pañcabrahmasvarūpāya pañca kṛtyāyate namaḥ  || 1,10.30
ātmane brahmaṇe tubhyamanaṃtaguṇaśaktaye  || 1,10.30
sakalākalarūpāya śaṃbhave gurave namaḥ  || 1,10.31
iti stutvā guruṃ padyairbrahmā viṣṇuśca nematuḥ  || 1,10.31
īśvara uvāca
vatsakau sarvatattvaṃ ca kathitaṃ darśitaṃ ca vām  || 1,10.32
japataṃ praṇavaṃ maṃtraṃ devīdiṣṭaṃ madātmakam  || 1,10.32
jñānaṃ ca susthiraṃ bhāgyaṃ sarvaṃ bhavati śāśvatam  || 1,10.33
ārdrāyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ tajjāpyaṃ tvakṣayaṃ bhavet || 1,10.33
sūryagatyā mahārdrāyāmekaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 1,10.34
mṛgaśīrṣāṃtimo bhāgaḥ punarvasvādimastathā  || 1,10.34
ārdrāsamaḥ sadā jñeyaḥ pūjāhomāditarpaṇe  || 1,10.35
darśanaṃ tu prabhāte ca prātaḥsaṃgavakālayoḥ  || 1,10.35
caturdaśī tathā grāhyā niśīthavyāpinī bhavet || 1,10.36
pradoṣavyāpinī caiva parayuktā praśasyate  || 1,10.36
liṃgaṃ beraṃ ca metulyaṃ yajatāṃ liṃgamuttamam  || 1,10.37
tasmālliṃgaṃ paraṃ pūjyaṃ berādapi mumukṣubhiḥ  || 1,10.37
liṃgamoṃkāramaṃtreṇa beraṃ pañcākṣareṇa tu  || 1,10.38
svayameva hi dravyaiḥ pratiṣṭhāpyaṃ parairapi  || 1,10.38
pūjayedupacāraiśca matpadaṃ sulabhaṃ bhavet || 1,10.39
iti śāsya tathā śiṣyau tatraivāṃ'tarhitaḥ śivaḥ  || 1,10.39

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ daśamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 11
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
kathaṃ liṃgaṃ pratiṣṭhāpyaṃ kathaṃ vātasya lakṣaṇam  || 1,11.1
kathaṃ vā tatsamabhyarcyaṃ deśe kāle ca kena hi  || 1,11.1
sūta uvāca
yuṣmadarthaṃ pravakṣyāmi buddhyatāmavadhānataḥ  || 1,11.2
anukūle śubhe kāle puṇye tīrthe taṭe tathā  || 1,11.2
yatheṣṭaṃ liṃgamāropyaṃ yatra syānnityamarcanam  || 1,11.3
pārthivena tathāpyenaṃ taijasena yathāruci  || 1,11.3
kalpalakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ liṃgaṃ pūjāphalaṃ labhet || 1,11.4
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ sadyaḥ pūjāphalapradam  || 1,11.4
care viśiṣyate sūkṣmaṃ sthāvare sthūlameva hi  || 1,11.5
salakṣaṇaṃ sapīṭhaṃ ca sthāpayecchivanirmitam  || 1,11.5
maṃḍalaṃ caturasraṃ vā trikoṇamathavā tathā  || 1,11.6
khaṭvāṃgavanmadhyasūkṣmaṃ liṃgapīṭhaṃ mahāphalaṃ  || 1,11.6
prathamaṃ mṛcchilādibhyo ligaṃ lohādibhiḥ kṛtam  || 1,11.7
yena liṃgaṃ tena pīṭhaṃ sthāvare hi viśiṣyate  || 1,11.7
liṃgaṃ pīṭhaṃ care tvekaṃ liṃgaṃ bāṇakṛtaṃ vinā  || 1,11.8
liṃgapramāṇaṃ kartḥṇāṃ dvādaśāṃgulamuttamam  || 1,11.8
nyūnaṃ cetphalamalpaṃ syādadhikaṃ naiva dūṣyate  || 1,11.9
karturekāṃgulanyūnaṃ carepi ca tathaiva hi  || 1,11.9
ādau vimānaṃ śilpena kāryaṃ devagaṇairyutam  || 1,11.10
tatra garbhagṛhe ramye dṛḍhe darpaṇasaṃnibho bhūṣite  || 1,11.10
navaratnaiśca digdvāre ca pradhānakaiḥ  || 1,11.11
nīlaṃ raktaṃ ca vai dūryaṃ śyāmaṃ mārakataṃ tathā  || 1,11.11
muktāpravālagomedavajrāṇi navaratnakam   || 1,11.12
madhye liṃgaṃ mahaddravyaṃ nikṣipetsahavaidike  || 1,11.12
saṃpūjya liṃgaṃ sadyādyaiḥ pañcasthāne yathākramam  || 1,11.13
agnau ca hutvā bahudhā haviṣāsa kalaṃ ca mām  || 1,11.13
abhyarcya gurumācāryamarthaiḥ kāmaiśca bāṃdhavam  || 1,11.14
dadyādaiśvaryamarthibhyo jaḍamapyajaḍaṃ tathā  || 1,11.14
sthāvaraṃ jaṃgamaṃ jīvaṃ sarvaṃ saṃtoṣya yatnataḥ  || 1,11.15
suvarṇapūrite śvabhre navaratnaiśca pūrite  || 1,11.15
sadyādi brahma coccārya dhyātvā devaṃ paraṃ śubham  || 1,11.16
udīrya ca mahāmaṃtramoṃkāraṃ nādaghoṣitam  || 1,11.16
liṃgaṃ tatra pratiṣṭhāpya ligaṃ pīṭhena yojayet || 1,11.17
liṃgaṃ sapīṭhaṃ nikṣipya nityalepena baṃdhayet || 1,11.17
evaṃ beraṃ ca saṃsthāpyaṃ tatraiva paramaṃ śubham  || 1,11.18
pañcākṣareṇa beraṃ tu utsavārthaṃ vahistathā  || 1,11.18
beraṃ gurubhyo gṛhṇīyātsādhubhiḥ pūjitaṃ tu vā  || 1,11.19
evaṃ liṃge ca bere ca pūjā śivapadapradā  || 1,11.19
punaśca dvividhaṃ proktaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṃgamaṃ tathā  || 1,11.20
sthāvaraṃ liṃgamityāhustarugulmādikaṃ tathā  || 1,11.20
jaṃgamaṃ liṃgamityāhuḥ kṛmikīṭādikaṃ tathā  || 1,11.21
sthāvarasya ca śuśrūṣā jaṃgamasya ca tarpaṇam  || 1,11.21
tattatsukhānurāgeṇa śivapūjāṃ vidurbudhāḥ  || 1,11.22
pīṭhamaṃbāmayaṃ sarvaṃ śivaliṃgaṃ ca cinmayam  || 1,11.22
yathā devīmumāmaṃke dhṛtvā tiṣṭhati śaṃkaraḥ  || 1,11.23
tathā liṃgamidaṃ pīṭhaṃ dhṛtvā tiṣṭhati saṃtatam  || 1,11.23
evaṃ sthāpya mahāliṃgaṃ pūjayedupacārakaiḥ  || 1,11.24
nityapūjā yathā śaktidhvajādikaraṇaṃ tathā  || 1,11.24
iti saṃsthāpayelliṃgaṃ sākṣācchivapadapradam  || 1,11.25
athavā caraliṃgaṃ tu ṣoḍaśairupacārakaiḥ  || 1,11.25
pūjayecca yathānyāyaṃ kramācchivapadapradam  || 1,11.26
āvāhanaṃ cāsanaṃ ca arghyaṃ pādyaṃ tathaiva ca  || 1,11.26
tadaṃgācamanaṃ caiva snānamabhyaṃgapūrvakam  || 1,11.27
vastraṃ gaṃdhaṃ tathā puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ nivedanam  || 1,11.27
nīrājanaṃ ca tāṃbūlaṃ namaskāro visarjanam  || 1,11.28
athavā 'rghyādikaṃ kṛtvā naivedyāṃ taṃ yathāvidhi  || 1,11.28
athābhiṣekaṃ naivedyaṃ namaskāraṃ ca tarpaṇam  || 1,11.29
yathāśakti sadākuryātkramācchivapadapradam  || 1,11.29
athavā mānuṣe liṃgepyārṣe daive svayaṃbhuvi  || 1,11.30
sthāpite 'pūrvake liṃge sopacāraṃ yathā tathā  || 1,11.30
pūjopakaraṇe datte yatkiṃcitphalamaśnute  || 1,11.31
pradakṣiṇānamaskāraiḥ kramācchivapadapradam  || 1,11.31
liṃgaṃ darśanamātraṃ vā niyamena śivapradam  || 1,11.32
mṛtpiṣṭagośakṛtpuṣpaiḥ karavīreṇa vā phalaiḥ  || 1,11.32
guḍena navanītena bhasmanānnairyathāruci  || 1,11.33
liṃgaṃ yatnena kṛtvāṃte yajettadanusārataḥ  || 1,11.33
aṃguṣṭhādāvapi tathā pūjāmicchaṃti kecana  || 1,11.34
liṃgakarmaṇi sarvatra niṣedhosti na karhicit || 1,11.34
sarvatra phaladātā hi prayāsānuguṇaṃ śivaḥ  || 1,11.35
athavā liṃgadānaṃ vā liṃgamaulyamathāpi vā  || 1,11.35
śraddhayā śivabhaktāya dattaṃ śivapadapradam  || 1,11.36
athavā praṇavaṃ nityaṃ japeddaśasahasrakam  || 1,11.36
saṃdhyayośca sahasraṃ vā jñeyaṃ śivapadapradam  || 1,11.37
japakāle makārāṃtaṃ manaḥśuddhikaraṃ bhajet || 1,11.37
samādhau mānasaṃ proktamupāṃśu sārvakālikam  || 1,11.38
samānapraṇavaṃ cemaṃ biṃdunādayutaṃ viduḥ  || 1,11.38
atha pañcākṣaraṃ nityaṃ japedayutamādarāt || 1,11.39
saṃdhyayośca sahasraṃ vā jñeyaṃ śivapadapradam  || 1,11.39
praṇavenādisaṃyuktaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ viśiṣyate  || 1,11.40
dīkṣāyuktaṃ gurorgrāhyaṃ maṃtraṃ hyatha phalāptaye  || 1,11.40
kuṃbhasnānaṃ maṃtradīkṣāṃ mātṛkānyāsameva ca  || 1,11.41
brāhmaṇaḥ satyapūtātmā gururjñānī viśiṣyate  || 1,11.41
dvijānāṃ ca namaḥpūrvamanyeṣāṃ ca namontakam   || 1,11.42
strīṇāṃ ca kvacidicchaṃti namo taṃ ca yathāvidhi  || 1,11.42
viprastrīṇāṃ namaḥ pūrvamidamicchaṃti kecana  || 1,11.43
pañcakoṭijapaṃ kṛtvā sadā śivasamo bhavet || 1,11.43
ekadvitricatuḥkoṭyābrahmādīnāṃ padaṃ vrajet || 1,11.44
japedakṣaralakṣaṃvā akṣarāṇāṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 1,11.44
athavākṣaralakṣaṃ vā jñeyaṃ śivapadapradam  || 1,11.45
sahasraṃ tu sahasrāṇāṃ sahasreṇa dinena hi  || 1,11.45
japenmaṃtrādiṣṭasiddhirnityaṃ brāhmaṇabhojanāt || 1,11.46
aṣṭottarasahasraṃ vai gāyatrīṃ prātareva hi  || 1,11.46
brāhmaṇastu japennityaṃ kramācchivapadapradān  || 1,11.47
vedamaṃtrāṃstu sūktāni japenniyamamāsthitaḥ  || 1,11.47
ekaṃ daśārṇaṃ maṃtraṃ ca śatonaṃ ca tadūrdhvakam  || 1,11.48
ayutaṃ ca sahasraṃ ca śatamekaṃ vinā bhavet || 1,11.48
vedapārāyaṇaṃ caiva jñeyaṃ śivapadapradam  || 1,11.49
anyānbahutarānmaṃtrāñjapedakṣaralakṣataḥ  || 1,11.49
ekākṣarāṃstathā maṃtrāñjapedakṣarakoṭitaḥ  || 1,11.50
tataḥ paraṃ japeccaiva sahasraṃ bhaktipūrvakam  || 1,11.50
evaṃ kuryādyathāśakti kramācchiva padaṃ labhet || 1,11.51
nityaṃ rucikaraṃ tvekaṃ maṃtramāmaraṇāṃtikam  || 1,11.51
japetsahasramomiti sarvābhīṣṭaṃ śivājñayā  || 1,11.52
puṣpārāmādikaṃ vāpi tathā saṃmārjanādikam  || 1,11.52
śivāya śivakāryāthe kṛtvā śivapadaṃ labhet || 1,11.53
śivakṣetre tathā vāsaṃ nityaṃ kuryācca bhaktitaḥ  || 1,11.53
jaḍānāmajaḍānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ bhuktimuktidam  || 1,11.54
tasmādvāsaṃ śivakṣetre kuryadāmaraṇaṃ budhaḥ  || 1,11.54
liṃgāddhastaśataṃ puṇyaṃ kṣetre mānuṣake viduḥ  || 1,11.55
sahasrāratnimātraṃ tu puṇyakṣetre tathārṣake  || 1,11.55
daivaliṃge tathā jñeyaṃ sahasrāratnimānataḥ  || 1,11.56
dhanuṣpramāṇasāhasraṃ puṇyaṃ kṣetre svayaṃ bhuvi  || 1,11.56
puṇyakṣetre sthitā vāpī kūpādyapuṣkarāṇi ca  || 1,11.57
śivagaṃgeti vijñeyaṃ śivasya vacanaṃ yathā  || 1,11.57
tatra snātvā tathā dattvā japitvā hi śivaṃ vrajet || 1,11.58
śivakṣetraṃ samāśritya vasedāmaraṇaṃ tathā  || 1,11.58
dāhaṃ daśāhaṃ māsyaṃ vā sapiṃḍīkaraṇaṃ tu vā  || 1,11.59
ābdikaṃ vā śivakṣetre kṣetre piṃḍamathāpi vā  || 1,11.59
sarvapāpa vinirmuktaḥ sadyaḥ śivapadaṃ labhet || 1,11.60
athavā saptarātraṃ vā vasedvā pañcarātrakam  || 1,11.60
trirātramekarātraṃ vā kramācchivapadaṃ labhet || 1,11.61
svavarṇānuguṇaṃ loke svācārātprāpnute naraḥ  || 1,11.61
varṇoddhāreṇa bhaktyā ca tatphalātiśayaṃ naraḥ  || 1,11.62
sarvaṃ kṛtaṃ kāmanayā sadyaḥ phalamavāpnuyāt || 1,11.62
sarvaṃ kṛtamakāmena sākṣācchivapadapradam  || 1,11.63
prātarmadhyāhnasāyāhnamahastriṣvekataḥ kramāt || 1,11.63
prātarvidhikaraṃ jñeyaṃ madhyāhnaṃ kāmikaṃ tathā  || 1,11.64
sāyāhnaṃ śāṃtikaṃ jñeyaṃ rātrāvapi tathaiva hi  || 1,11.64
kālo niśītho vai proktomadhyayāmadvayaṃ niśi  || 1,11.65
śivapūjā viśeṣeṇa tatkāle 'bhīṣṭasiddhidā  || 1,11.65
evaṃ jñātvā naraḥ kurvanyathoktaphalabhāgbhavet || 1,11.66
kalau yuge viśeṣeṇa phalasiddhistu karmaṇā  || 1,11.66
uktena kenacidvāpi adhikāravibhedataḥ  || 1,11.67
sadvṛttiḥ pāpabhīruścettatatphalamavāpnuyāt || 1,11.67
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
atha kṣetrāṇi puṇyāni samāsātkathayasva naḥ  || 1,11.68
sarvāḥ striyaśca puruṣā yānyāśritya padaṃ labhet || 1,11.68
sūta yogivaraśreṣṭha śivakṣetrāgamāṃstathā  || 1,11.69
sūta uvāca
śṛṇuta śraddhayā sarvakṣetrāṇi ca tadāgamān  || 1,11.69

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃekadaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 12
sūta uvāca
śṛṇudhvamṛṣayaḥ prājñāḥ śivakṣetraṃ vimuktidam  || 1,12.1
tadāgamāṃstato vakṣye lokarakṣārthameva hi  || 1,12.1
pañcāśatkoṭivistīrṇā saśailavanakānanā  || 1,12.2
śivājñayā hi pṛthivī lokaṃ dhṛtvā ca tiṣṭhati  || 1,12.2
tatra tatra śivakṣetraṃ tatra tatra nivāsinām  || 1,12.3
mokṣārthaṃ kṛpayā devaḥ kṣetraṃ kalpitavānprabhuḥ  || 1,12.3
parigrahādṛṣīṇāṃ ca devānāṃ parigrahāt || 1,12.4
svayaṃbhūtānyathānyāni lokarakṣārthameva hi  || 1,12.4
tīrthe kṣetre sadākāryaṃ snānadānajapādikam  || 1,12.5
anyathā rogadāridryamūkatvādyāpnuyānnaraḥ  || 1,12.5
athāsminbhārate varṣe prāpnoti maraṇaṃ naraḥ  || 1,12.6
svayaṃbhūsthānavāsena punarmānuṣyamāpnuyāt || 1,12.6
kṣetre pāpasya karaṇaṃ dṛḍhaṃ bhavati bhūsurāḥ  || 1,12.7
puṇyakṣetre nivāse hi pāpamaṇvapi nācaret || 1,12.7
yena kenāpyupāyena puṇyakṣetre vasennaraḥ  || 1,12.8
siṃdhoḥ śatanadītīre saṃti kṣetrāṇyanekaśaḥ  || 1,12.8
sarasvatī nadī puṇyā proktā ṣaṣṭimukhā tathā  || 1,12.9
tattattīre vasetprājñaḥ kramādbrahmapadaṃ labhet || 1,12.9
himavadgirijā gaṃgā puṇyā śatamukhā nadī  || 1,12.10
tattīre caiva kāśyādipuṇyakṣetrāṇyanekaśaḥ  || 1,12.10
tatra tīraṃ praśastaṃ hi mṛgabṛhaspatau  || 1,12.11
śoṇabhadro daśamukhaḥ puṇyobhīṣṭaphalapradaḥ  || 1,12.11
tatra snānopavāsena padaṃ vaināyakaṃ labhet || 1,12.12
caturvīṃśamukhā puṇyā narmadā ca mahānadī  || 1,12.12
tasyāṃ snānena vāsena padaṃ vaiṣṇavamāpnuyāt || 1,12.13
tamasā dvādaśamukhā revā daśamukhā nadī  || 1,12.13
godāvarī mahāpuṇyā brahmagovadhanāśinī  || 1,12.14
ekaviṃśamukhā proktā rudralokapradāyinī  || 1,12.14
kṛṣṇaveṇī puṇyanadī sarvapāpakṣayāvahā  || 1,12.15
sāṣṭādaśamukhāproktā viṣṇulokapradāyinī  || 1,12.15
tuṃgabhadrā daśamukhā brahmalokapradāyinī  || 1,12.16
suvarṇamukharī puṇyā proktā navamukhā tathā  || 1,12.16
tatraiva suprajāyaṃte brahmalokacyutāstathā  || 1,12.17
sarasvatī ca paṃpā ca kanyāśvetanadī śubhā  || 1,12.17
etāsāṃ tīravāsena iṃdralokamavāpnuyāt || 1,12.18
sahyādrijā mahāpuṇyā kāverīti mahānadī  || 1,12.18
saptaviṃśamukhā proktā sarvābhīṣṭaṃ pradāyinī  || 1,12.19
tattīrāḥ svargadāścaiva brahmaviṣṇupadapradāḥ  || 1,12.19
śivalokapradā śaivāstathā 'bhīṣṭaphalapradāḥ  || 1,12.20
naimiṣe badare snāyānmeṣage ca gurau ravau  || 1,12.20
brahmalokapradaṃ vidyāttataḥ pūjādikaṃ tathā  || 1,12.21
siṃdhunadyāṃ tathā snānaṃ siṃhe karkaṭage ravau  || 1,12.21
kedārodakapānaṃ ca snānaṃ ca jñānadaṃ viduḥ  || 1,12.22
godāvaryāṃ siṃhamāse snāyātsiṃhabṛhaspatau  || 1,12.22
śivalokapradamiti śivenoktaṃ tathā purā  || 1,12.23
yamunāśoṇayoḥ snāyādgurau kanyāgate ravau  || 1,12.23
dharmaloke daṃtiloke mahābhogapradaṃ viduḥ  || 1,12.24
kāveryāṃ ca tathāsnāyāttulāge tu ravau gurau  || 1,12.24
viṣṇorvacanamahātmyātsarvābhīṣṭapradaṃ viduḥ  || 1,12.25
vṛścike māsi saṃprāpte tathārke guruvṛścike  || 1,12.25
narmadāyāṃ nadīsnānādviṣṇulokamvāpnuyāt || 1,12.26
suvarṇamukharīsnānaṃ cāpage ca gurau ravau  || 1,12.26
śivalokapradamiti brāhmaṇo vacanaṃ yathā  || 1,12.27
mṛgamāsi tathā snāyājjāhnavyāṃ mṛgage gurau  || 1,12.27
śivalokapradamiti brahmaṇo vacanaṃ yathā  || 1,12.28
brahmaviṣṇvoḥ pade bhuktvā tadaṃte jñānamāpnuyāt || 1,12.28
gaṃgāyāṃ māghamāse tu tathākuṃbhagate ravau  || 1,12.29
śrāddhaṃ vā piṃḍadānaṃ vā tilodakamathāpivā  || 1,12.29
vaṃśadvayapitḥṇāṃ ca kulakoṭyuddharaṃ viduḥ  || 1,12.30
kṛṣṇaveṇyāṃ praśaṃsaṃti mīnage ca gurau ravau  || 1,12.30
tattattīrthe ca tanmāsi snānamiṃdrapadapradam  || 1,12.31
gaṃgāṃ vā sahyajāṃ vāpi samāśritya vasedbudhaḥ  || 1,12.31
tatkālakṛtapāpasya kṣayo bhavati niścitam  || 1,12.32
rudralokapradānyeva saṃti kṣetrāṇyanekaśaḥ  || 1,12.32
tāmraparṇī vegavatī brahmalokaphalaprade  || 1,12.33
tayostīre hi saṃtyeva kṣetrāṇi svargadāni ca  || 1,12.33
saṃti kṣetrāṇi tanmadhye puṇyadāni ca bhūriśaḥ  || 1,12.34
tatra tatra vasanprājñastādṛśaṃ ca phalaṃ labhet || 1,12.34
sadācāreṇa sadvṛttyā sadā bhāvanayāpi ca  || 1,12.35
vaseddayāluḥ prājño vai nānyathā tatphalaṃ labhet || 1,12.35
puṇyakṣetre kṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ bahudhā ṛddhimṛcchati  || 1,12.36
puṇyakṣetre kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ mahadaṇvapi jāyate  || 1,12.36
tatkālaṃ jīvanārthaścetpuṇyena kṣayameṣyati  || 1,12.37
puṇyamaiśvaryadaṃ prāhuḥ kāyikaṃ vācikaṃ tathā  || 1,12.37
mānasaṃ ca tathā pāpaṃ tādṛśaṃ nāśayeddvijāḥ  || 1,12.38
mānasaṃ vajralepaṃ tu kalpakalpānugaṃ tathā  || 1,12.38
dhyānādeva hi tannaśyennānyathā nāśamṛcchati  || 1,12.39
vācikaṃ japajālena kāyikaṃ kāyaśoṣaṇāt || 1,12.39
dānāddhanakṛtaṃ naśyennā 'nyathākalpakoṭibhiḥ  || 1,12.40
kvacitpāpena puṇyaṃ ca vṛddhipūrveṇa naśyati  || 1,12.40
bījāṃśaścaiva vṛddhyaṃśo bhogāṃśaḥ puṇyapāpayoḥ  || 1,12.41
jñānanāśyo hi bījāṃśo vṛddhiruktaprakārataḥ  || 1,12.41
bhojāṃśo bhoganāśyastu nānyathā puṇyakoṭibhiḥ  || 1,12.42
bījaprarohe naṣṭe tu śeṣo bhogāya kalpate  || 1,12.42
devānāṃ pūjayā caiva brahmaṇānāṃ ca dānataḥ  || 1,12.43
tapodhikyācca kālena bhogaḥ sahyo bhavennṛṇām  || 1,12.43
tasmātpāpamakṛtvaiva vastavyaṃssukhamicchatā  || 1,12.43

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ dvādaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 13
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
sadācāraṃ śrāvayāśu yena lokāñjayedbudhaḥ  || 1,13.1
dharmādharmamayānbrūhi svarganārakadāṃstathā  || 1,13.1
sūta uvāca
sadācārayuto vidvānbrāhmaṇo nāma nāmataḥ  || 1,13.2
vedācārayuto vipro hyetairekaikavāndvijaḥ  || 1,13.2
alpācārolpavedaśca kṣatriyo rājasevakaḥ  || 1,13.3
kiṃcidācāravānvaiśyaḥ kṛṣivāṇijyakṛttayā  || 1,13.3
śūdrabrāhmaṇa ityuktaḥ svayameva hi karṣakaḥ  || 1,13.4
asūyāluḥ paradrohī caṃḍāladvija ucyate  || 1,13.4
pṛthivīpālako rājā itarekṣatriyā matāḥ  || 1,13.5
dhānyādikrayavānvaiśya itaro vaṇigucyate  || 1,13.5
brahmakṣatriyavaiśyānāṃ śuśrūṣuḥ śūdra ucyate  || 1,13.6
karṣako vṛṣalo jñeya itare caiva dasyavaḥ  || 1,13.6
sarvo hyuṣaḥprācīmukhaścintayeddevapūrvakān  || 1,13.7
dharmānarthāṃśca tatkleśānāyaṃ ca vyayameva ca  || 1,13.7
āyurdveṣaśca maraṇaṃ pāpaṃ bhāgyaṃ tathaiva ca  || 1,13.8
vyādhiḥ puṣṭistathā śaktiḥ prātarutthānadikphalam  || 1,13.8
niśāṃtyāyāmoṣā jñeyā yāmārdhaṃ saṃdhirucyate  || 1,13.9
tatkāle tu samutthāya viṇmūtre visṛjeddvijaḥ  || 1,13.9
gṛhāddūraṃ tato gatvā bāhyataḥ pravṛtastathā  || 1,13.10
udaṅmukhaḥ samāviśya pratibaṃdhe 'nyadiṅmukhaḥ  || 1,13.10
jalāgnibrāhmaṇādīnāṃ devānāṃ nābhimukhyataḥ  || 1,13.11
liṃgaṃ pidhāya vāmena mukhamanyena pāṇinā  || 1,13.11
malamutsṛjya cotthāya na paśyeccaiva tanmalam  || 1,13.12
uddhṛtena jalenaiva śaucaṃ kuryājjalādbahiḥ  || 1,13.12
athavā devapitrārṣatīrthāvataraṇaṃ vinā  || 1,13.13
sapta vā pañca vā trīnvā gudaṃ saṃśodhayenmṛdā  || 1,13.13
liṃge karkoṭamātraṃ tu gude prasṛtiriṣyate  || 1,13.14
tata utthāya paddhastaśaucaṃ gaṇḍūṣamaṣṭakam  || 1,13.14
yena kena ca patreṇa kāṣṭhena ca jalādbahiḥ  || 1,13.15
kāryaṃ saṃtyajya tarjanīṃ daṃtadhāvanamīritam  || 1,13.15
jaladevānnamaskṛtya maṃtreṇa snānamācaret || 1,13.16
aśaktaḥ kaṃṭhadaghnaṃ vā kaṭidaghnamathāpi vā  || 1,13.16
ājānu jalamāviśya maṃtrasnānaṃ samācaret || 1,13.17
devādīṃstarpayedvidvāṃstatra tīrthajalena ca  || 1,13.17
dhautavastraṃ samādāya pañcakacchena dhārayet || 1,13.18
uttarīyaṃ ca kiṃ caiva dhāryaṃ sarveṣu karmasu  || 1,13.18
nadyāditīrthasnāne tu snānavastraṃ na śodhayet || 1,13.19
vāpīkūpagṛhādau tu snānādūrdhvaṃ nayedbudhaḥ  || 1,13.19
śilādārvādike vāpi jale vāpi sthalepi vā  || 1,13.20
saṃśodhya pīḍayedvastraṃ pitḥṇāṃ tṛptaye dvijāḥ  || 1,13.20
jābālakoktamaṃtreṇa bhasmanā ca tripuṃḍrakam  || 1,13.21
anyathā cejjale pāta itastannarakamṛcchati  || 1,13.21
āpohiṣṭheti śirasi prokṣayetpāpaśāṃtaye  || 1,13.22
yasyeti maṃtraṃ pāde tu saṃdhiprokṣaṇamucyate  || 1,13.22
pāde mūrdhni hṛdi caiva mūrdhni hṛtpāda eva ca  || 1,13.23
hṛtpādamūrdhni saṃprokṣya maṃtrasnānaṃ vidurbudhāḥ  || 1,13.23
īṣatsparśe ca dauḥ svāsthye rājarāṣṭrabhaye 'pi ca  || 1,13.24
atyāgatikāle ca maṃtrasnānaṃ samācaret || 1,13.24
prātaḥ sūryānuvākena sāyamagnyanuvākataḥ  || 1,13.25
apaḥ pītvā tathāmadhye punaḥ prokṣaṇamācaret || 1,13.25
gāyatryā japamaṃtrāṃte vīrūrdhvaṃ prāgvinikṣipet || 1,13.26
maṃtreṇa saha caikaṃ vai madhye 'rghyaṃ tu raverdvijā  || 1,13.26
atha jāte ca sāyāhne bhuvi paścimadiṅmukhaḥ  || 1,13.27
uddhṛtya dadyātprātastu madhyāhneṃgulibhistathā  || 1,13.27
aṃgulīnāṃ ca raṃdhreṇa laṃbaṃ paśyeddivākaram  || 1,13.28
ātmapradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā śuddhācamanamācaret || 1,13.28
sāyaṃ muhūrtādarvāktu kṛtā saṃdhyā vṛthā bhavet || 1,13.29
akālātkāla ityukto dine 'tīte yathākramam  || 1,13.29
divā 'tīte ca gāyatrīṃ śataṃ nitye kramājjapet || 1,13.30
ādarśāhātparā'tīte gāyatrīṃ lakṣamabhyaset || 1,13.30
māsātīte tu nitye hi punaścopanayaṃ caret || 1,13.31
īśo gaurīguho viṣṇurbrahmā ceṃdraśca vai yamaḥ  || 1,13.31
evaṃ rūpāṃśca vai devāṃstarpayedarthasiddhaye  || 1,13.32
brahmārpaṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā śuddhācamanamācaret || 1,13.32
tīrthadakṣiṇataḥ śaste maṭhe maṃtrālaye budhaḥ  || 1,13.33
tatra devālaye vāpi gṛhe vā niyatasthale  || 1,13.33
sarvāndevānnamaskṛtya sthirabuddhiḥ sthirāsanaḥ  || 1,13.34
praṇavaṃ pūrvamabhyasya gāyatrīmabhyasettataḥ  || 1,13.34
jīvabrahmaikyaviṣayaṃ buddhvā praṇavamabhyaset || 1,13.35
trailokyasṛṣṭikartāraṃ sthitikartāramacyutam  || 1,13.35
saṃhartāraṃ tathā rudraṃ svaprakāśamupāsmahe  || 1,13.36
jñānakarmeṃdriyāṇāṃ ca manovṛttīrdhiyastathā  || 1,13.36
bhogamokṣaprade dharme jñāne ca prerayetsadā  || 1,13.37
itthamarthaṃ dhiyādhyāyanbrahmaprāpnoti niścayaḥ  || 1,13.37
kevalaṃ vā japennityaṃ brāhmaṇyasya ca pūrtaye  || 1,13.38
sahasramabhyasennityaṃ prātarbrāhmaṇapuṃgavaḥ  || 1,13.38
anyeṣāṃ ca yathā śaktimadhyāhne ca śataṃ japet || 1,13.39
sāyaṃ dvidaśakaṃ jñeyaṃ śikhāṣṭakasamanvitam  || 1,13.39
mūlādhāraṃ samārabhya dvādaśāṃtasthitāṃstathā  || 1,13.40
vidyeśabrahmaviṣṇvīśajīvātmaparameśvarān  || 1,13.40
brahmabuddhyā tadaikyaṃ ca sohaṃ bhāvanayā japet || 1,13.41
tāneva brahmaraṃdhrādau kāyādbāhye ca bhāvayet || 1,13.41
mahattattvaṃ samārabhya śarīraṃ tu sahasrakam  || 1,13.42
ekaikasmājjapādekamatikramya śanaiḥ śanaiḥ  || 1,13.42
parasminyojayejjīvaṃ japatattvamudāhṛtam  || 1,13.43
śatadvidaśakaṃ dehaṃ śikhāṣṭakasamanvitam  || 1,13.43
maṃtrāṇāṃ japa evaṃ hi japamādikramādviduḥ  || 1,13.44
sahasraṃ brāhmadaṃ vidyācchatamaiṃdrapradaṃ viduḥ  || 1,13.44
itarattvātmarakṣārthaṃ brahmayoniṣu jāyate  || 1,13.45
divākaramupasthāya nityamitthaṃ samācaret || 1,13.45
lakṣadvādaśayuktastu pūrṇabrāhmaṇa īritaḥ  || 1,13.46
gāyatryā lakṣahīnaṃ tu vedakāryena yojayet || 1,13.46
āsaptatestu niyamaṃ paścātpravrājanaṃ caret || 1,13.47
prātardvādaśasāhasraṃ pravrājīpraṇavaṃ japet || 1,13.47
dine dine tvatikrāṃte nityamevaṃ kramājjapet || 1,13.48
māsādau kramaśo 'tīte sārdhalakṣajapena hi  || 1,13.48
ata ūrdhvamatikrāṃte punaḥ praiṣaṃ samācaret || 1,13.49
evaṃ kṛtvā doṣaśāṃtiranyathā rauravaṃ vrajet || 1,13.49
dharmārthayostato yatnaṃ kuryātkāmī na cetaraḥ  || 1,13.50
brāhmaṇo muktikāmaḥ syādbrahmajñānaṃ sadābhyaset || 1,13.50
dharmādartho 'rthato bhogo bhogādvairāgyasaṃbhavaḥ  || 1,13.51
dharmārjitārthabhogena vairāgyamupajāyate  || 1,13.51
viparītārthabhogena rāga eva prajāyate  || 1,13.52
dharmaśca dvividhaḥ prokto dravyadehadvayena ca  || 1,13.52
dravyamijyādirūpaṃ syāttīrthasnānādi daihikam  || 1,13.53
dhanena dhanamāpnoti tapasā divyarūpatām  || 1,13.53
niṣkāmaḥ śuddhimāpnoti śuddhyā jñānaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 1,13.54
kṛtādau hi tapaḥśloghyaṃ dravyadharmaḥ kalau yuge  || 1,13.54
kṛtedhyānājjñānasiddhistretāyāṃ tapasā tathā  || 1,13.55
dvāpare yajanājjñānaṃ pratimāpūjayā kalau  || 1,13.55
yādṛśaṃ puṇyaṃ pāpaṃ vā tādṛśaṃ phalameva hi  || 1,13.56
dravyadehāṃgabhedena nyūnavṛddhikṣayādikam  || 1,13.56
adharmo hiṃsikārūpo dharmastu sukharūpakaḥ  || 1,13.57
adharmādduḥkhamāpnoti dharmādvai sukhamedhate  || 1,13.57
vidyādurvṛttito duḥkhaṃ sukhaṃ vidyātsuvṛttitaḥ  || 1,13.58
dharmārjanamataḥ kuryādbhogamokṣaprasiddhaye  || 1,13.58
sakuṭuṃbasya viprasya caturjanayutasya ca  || 1,13.59
śatavarṣasya vṛttiṃ tu dadyāttadbrahmalokadam  || 1,13.59
cāṃdrāyaṇasahasraṃ tu brahmalokapradaṃ viduḥ  || 1,13.60
sahasrasya kuṭuṃbasya pratiṣṭhāṃ kṣatriyaścaret || 1,13.60
iṃdralokapradaṃ vidyādayutaṃ brahmalokadam  || 1,13.61
yāṃ devatāṃ puraskṛtya dānamācarate naraḥ  || 1,13.61
tattallokamavāpnoti iti vedavido viduḥ  || 1,13.62
arthahīnaḥ sadā kuryāttapasā mārjanaṃ tathā  || 1,13.62
tīrthācca tapasā prāpyaṃ sukhamakṣayyamaśnute  || 1,13.63
arthārjanamatho vakṣye nyāyataḥ susamāhitaḥ  || 1,13.63
kṛtātpratigrahāccaiva yājanācca viśuddhitaḥ  || 1,13.64
adainyādanatikleśādbrāhmaṇo dhanamarjayet || 1,13.64
kṣatriyo bāhuvīryeṇa kṛṣigorakṣaṇādviśaḥ  || 1,13.65
nyāyārjitasya vittasya dānātsiddhiṃ samaśnute  || 1,13.65
jñānasiddhyā mokṣasiddhiḥ sarveṣāṃ gurvanugrahāt || 1,13.66
mokṣātsvarūpasiddhiḥ syātparānandaṃ samaśnute  || 1,13.66
satsaṃgātsarvametadvai narāṇāṃ jāyate dvijāḥ  || 1,13.67
dhanadhānyādikaṃ sarvaṃ deyaṃ vai gṛhamedhinā  || 1,13.67
yadyatkāle vastujātaṃ phalaṃ vā dhānyameva ca  || 1,13.68
tattatsarvaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo deyaṃ vai hitamicchatā  || 1,13.68
jalaṃ caiva sadā deyamannaṃ kṣudvyādhiśāṃtaye  || 1,13.69
kṣetraṃ dhānyaṃ tathā''mānnamannamevaṃ caturvidham  || 1,13.69
yāvatkālaṃ yadannaṃ vai bhuktvā śravaṇamedhate  || 1,13.70
tāvatkṛtasya puṇyasya tvardhaṃ dāturna saṃśayaḥ  || 1,13.70
grahītāhigṛhītasya dānādvai tapasā tathā  || 1,13.71
pāpasaṃśodhanaṃ kuryādanyathā rauravaṃ vrajet || 1,13.71
ātmavittaṃ tridhā kuryāddharmavṛddhyātmabhogataḥ  || 1,13.72
nityaṃ naimittakaṃ kāmyaṃ karma kuryāttu dharmataḥ  || 1,13.72
vittasya vardhanaṃ kuryādvṛddhyaṃśena hi sādhakaḥ  || 1,13.73
hitena mitame dhyena bhogaṃ bhogāṃśataścaret || 1,13.73
kṛṣyarjite daśāṃśaṃ hi deyaṃ pāpasya śuddhaye  || 1,13.74
śeṣeṇa kuryāddharmādi anyathā rauravaṃ vrajet || 1,13.74
athavā pāpabuddhiḥ syātkṣayaṃ vā satyameṣyati  || 1,13.75
vṛddhivāṇijyake deyaṣṣaḍaṃśo hi vicakṣaṇaiḥ  || 1,13.75
śuddhapratigrahe deyaścaturthāṃśo dvijottamaiḥ  || 1,13.76
akasmādutthite 'rthe hi deyamardhaṃ dvijottamaiḥ  || 1,13.76
asatpratigrahasarvaṃ durdānaṃ sāgare kṣipet || 1,13.77
āhūya dānaṃ kartavyamātmabhogasamṛddhaye  || 1,13.77
pṛṣṭaṃ sarvaṃ sadā deyamātmaśaktyanusārataḥ   || 1,13.78
janmāṃtare ṛṇī hi syādadatte pṛṣṭavastuni  || 1,13.78
pareṣāṃ ca tathā doṣaṃ na praśaṃsedvicakṣaṇaḥ  || 1,13.79
viśeṣeṇa tathābrahmañchrutaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ ca no vadet || 1,13.79
na vadetsarvajaṃtūnāṃ hṛdi roṣakaraṃ budhaḥ  || 1,13.80
saṃdhyayoragnikāryaṃ ca kuryādaiśvaryasiddhaye  || 1,13.80
aśaktastvekakāle vā sūryāgnī ca yathāvidhi  || 1,13.81
taṃḍulaṃ dhānyamājyaṃ vā phalaṃ kaṃdaṃ havistathā  || 1,13.81
sthālīpākaṃ tathā kuryādyathānyāyaṃ yathāvidhi  || 1,13.82
pradhānahomamātraṃ vā havyābhāve samācaret || 1,13.82
nityasaṃdhānamityuktaṃ tamajasraṃ vidurbudhāḥ  || 1,13.83
athavā japamātraṃ vā sūryavaṃdanameva ca  || 1,13.83
evamātmārthinaḥ kuryurarthārthī ca yathāvidhi  || 1,13.84
brahmayajñaratā nityaṃ devapūjāratāstathā  || 1,13.84
agnipūjāparā nityaṃ gurupūjāratāstathā  || 1,13.85
brāhmaṇānāṃ tṛptikarāḥ sarve svargasya bhāginaḥ  || 1,13.85

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ trayodaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 14
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
agniyajñadevayajñaṃ brahmayajñaṃ tathaiva ca  || 1,14.1
gurupūjāṃ brahmatṛptiṃ krameṇa brūhi naḥ prabho  || 1,14.1
sūta uvāca
agnau juhoti yaddravyamagniyajñaḥ sa ucyate  || 1,14.2
brahmacaryāśramasthānāṃ samidādhānameva hi  || 1,14.2
samidagrau vratādyaṃ ca viśeṣayajanādikam  || 1,14.3
prathamāśramiṇāmevaṃ yāvadaupāsanaṃ dvijāḥ  || 1,14.3
ātmanyāropitāgnīnāṃ vanināṃ yatināṃ dvijāḥ  || 1,14.4
hitaṃ ca mitamedhyānnaṃ svakāle bhojanaṃ hutiḥ  || 1,14.4
aupāsanāgnisaṃdhānaṃ samārabhya surakṣitam  || 1,14.5
kuṃḍe vāpyatha bhāṃḍe vā tadajasraṃ samīritam  || 1,14.5
agnimātmanyaraṇyāṃ vā rājadaivavaśāddhruvam  || 1,14.6
agnityāgabhayāduktaṃ samāropitamucyate  || 1,14.6
saṃpatkarī tathā jñeyā sāyamagnyāhutirdvijāḥ  || 1,14.7
āyuṣkarīti vijñeyā prātaḥ sūryāhutistathā  || 1,14.7
agniyajño hyayaṃ prokto divā sūryaniveśanāt || 1,14.8
iṃdrādīnsakalāndevānuddiśyāgnau juhotiyat  || 1,14.8
devayajñaṃ hi taṃ vidyātsthālīpākādikānkratūn  || 1,14.9
caulādikaṃ tathā jñeyaṃ laukikāgnau pratiṣṭhitam  || 1,14.9
brahmayajñaṃ dvijaḥ kuryāddevānāṃ tṛptaye sakṛt || 1,14.10
brahmayajña iti prokto vedasyā'dhyayanaṃ bhavet || 1,14.10
nityānaṃtaramāsoyaṃ tatastu na vidhīyate  || 1,14.11
anagnau devayajanaṃ śṛṇuta śraddhayādarāt || 1,14.11
ādisṛṣṭau mahādevaḥ sarvajñaḥ karuṇākaraḥ  || 1,14.12
sarvalokopakārārthaṃ vārānkalpitavānprabhuḥ  || 1,14.12
saṃsāravaidyaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvabheṣajabheṣajam  || 1,14.13
ādāvārogyadaṃ vāraṃ svavāraṃ kṛtavānprabhuḥ  || 1,14.13
saṃpatkāraṃ svamāyāyā varaṃ ca kṛtavāṃstataḥ  || 1,14.14
janane durgatikrāṃte kumārasya tataḥ param  || 1,14.14
ālasyaduritakrāṃtyai vāraṃ kalpitavānprabhuḥ  || 1,14.15
rakṣakasya tathā viṣṇorlokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 1,14.15
puṣṭyarthaṃ caiva rakṣārthaṃ vāraṃ kalpitavānprabhuḥ  || 1,14.16
āyuṣkaraṃ tato vāramāyuṣāṃ kartureva hi  || 1,14.16
trailokyasṛṣṭikarturhi brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 1,14.17
jagadāyuṣyasiddhyarthaṃ vāraṃ kalpitavānprabhuḥ  || 1,14.17
ādau trailokyavṛddhyarthaṃ puṇyapāpe prakalpite  || 1,14.18
tayoḥ kartrostato vāramiṃdrasya ca yamasya ca  || 1,14.18
bhogapradaṃ mṛtyuharaṃ lokānāṃ ca prakalpitam  || 1,14.19
ādityādīnsvasvarūpānsukhaduḥkhasya sūcakān  || 1,14.19
vāreśānkalpayitvādau jyotiścakrepratiṣṭhitān  || 1,14.20
svasvavāre tu teṣāṃ tu pūjā svasvaphalapradā  || 1,14.20
ārogyaṃ saṃpadaścaiva vyādhīnāṃ śāṃtireva ca  || 1,14.21
puṣṭirāyustathā bhogo mṛterhāniryathākramam  || 1,14.21
vārakramaphalaṃ prāhurdevaprītipuraḥsaram  || 1,14.22
anyeṣāmapi devānāṃ pūjāyāḥ phaladaḥ śivaḥ  || 1,14.22
devānāṃ prītaye pūjāpañcadhaiva prakalpitā  || 1,14.23
tattanmaṃtrajapo homo dānaṃ caiva tapastathā  || 1,14.23
sthaṃḍile pratimāyāṃ ca hyagnau brāhmaṇavigrahe  || 1,14.24
samārādhanamityevaṃ ṣoḍaśairupacārakaiḥ  || 1,14.24
uttarottaravaiśiṣṭyātpūrvābhāve tathottaram  || 1,14.25
netrayoḥ śiraso roge tathā kuṣṭhasya śāṃtaye  || 1,14.25
ādityaṃ pūjayitvā tu brāhmaṇānbhojayettataḥ  || 1,14.26
dinaṃ māsaṃ tathā varṣaṃ varṣatrayamathavāpi vā  || 1,14.26
prārabdhaṃ prabalaṃ cetsyānnaśyedrogajarādikam  || 1,14.27
japādyamiṣṭadevasya vārādīnāṃ phalaṃ viduḥ  || 1,14.27
pāpaśāṃtirviśeṣeṇa hyādivāre nivedayet || 1,14.28
ādityasyaiva devānāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ viśiṣṭadam  || 1,14.28
somavāre ca lakṣmyādīnsaṃpadarthaṃ yajedbudhaḥ  || 1,14.29
ājyānnena tathā viprānsapatnīkāṃśca bhojayet || 1,14.29
kālyādīnbhauma vāre tu yajedrogapraśāṃtaye  || 1,14.30
māṣamudgāḍhakānnena brahmaṇāṃścaiva bhojayet || 1,14.30
saumyavāre tathā viṣṇuṃ dadhyannena yajedbudhaḥ  || 1,14.31
putramitrakalatrādipuṣṭirbhavati sarvadā  || 1,14.31
āyuṣkāmo gurorvāre devānāṃ puṣṭisiddhaye  || 1,14.32
upavītena vastreṇa kṣīrājyena yajedbudhaḥ  || 1,14.32
bhogārthaṃ bhṛgavāre tu yajeddevānsamāhitaḥ  || 1,14.33
ṣaḍrasopetamannaṃ ca dadyādbrāhmaṇatṛptaye  || 1,14.33
strīṇāṃ ca tṛptaye tadvaddeyaṃ vastrādikaṃ śubham  || 1,14.34
apamṛtyuhare maṃde rudrādrīṃśca yajedbudhaḥ  || 1,14.34
tilahomena dānena tilānnena ca bhojayet || 1,14.35
itthaṃ yajecca vibudhānārogyādiphalaṃ labhet || 1,14.35
devānāṃ nityayajane viśeṣayajanepi ca  || 1,14.36
snāne dāne jape home brāhmaṇānāṃ ca tarpaṇe  || 1,14.36
tithinakṣatrayoge ca tattaddevaprapūjane  || 1,14.37
ādivārādivāreṣu sarvajño jagadīśvaraḥ  || 1,14.37
tatadrūpeṇa sarveṣāmārogyādiphalapradaḥ  || 1,14.38
deśakālānusāreṇa tathā pātrānusārataḥ  || 1,14.38
dravyaśraddhānusāreṇa tathā lokānusārataḥ   || 1,14.39
tāratamyakramāddevastvārogyādīnprayacchati  || 1,14.39
śubhādāvaśubhāṃte ca janmarkṣeṣu gṛhe gṛhī  || 1,14.40
ārogyādisamṛddhyarthamādityādīngrahānyajet || 1,14.40
tasmādvai devayajanaṃ sarvābhīṣṭaphalapradam  || 1,14.41
samaṃtrakaṃ brāhmaṇānāmanyeṣāṃ caiva tāṃtrikam  || 1,14.41
yathāśaktyānurūpeṇa kartavyaṃ sarvadā naraiḥ  || 1,14.42
saptasvapi ca vāreṣu naraiḥ śubhaphalepsubhiḥ  || 1,14.42
daridrastapasā devānyajedāḍhyo dhanena hi  || 1,14.43
punaścaivaṃvidhaṃ dharmaṃ kurute śraddhayā saha  || 1,14.43
punaśca bhogānvividhānbhuktvā bhūmau prajāyate  || 1,14.44
chāyāṃ jalāśayaṃ brahmapratiṣṭhāṃ dharmasaṃcayam  || 1,14.44
sarvaṃ ca vittavānkuryātsadā bhogaprasiddhaye  || 1,14.45
kālācca puṇyapākena jñānasiddhiḥ prajāyate  || 1,14.45
ya imaṃ śṛṇute 'dhyāyaṃ paṭhate vā naro dvijāḥ  || 1,14.46
śravaṇasyopakartā ca devayajñaphalaṃ labhet || 1,14.46

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ caturdaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 15
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
deśādīnkramaśo brūhi sūta sarvārthavittam  || 1,15.1
sūta uvāca
śuddhaṃ gṛhaṃ samaphalaṃ devayajñādikarmasu  || 1,15.1
tato daśaguṇaṃ goṣṭhaṃ jalatīraṃ tato daśa  || 1,15.2
tato daśaguṇaṃ bilvatulasyaśvatthamūlakam  || 1,15.2
tato devālayaṃ vidyāttīrthatīraṃ tato daśa  || 1,15.3
tato daśaguṇaṃ nadyāstīrthanadyāstato daśa  || 1,15.3
saptagaṃgānadītīraṃ tasyā daśaguṇaṃ bhavet || 1,15.4
gaṃgā godāvarī caiva kāverī tāmraparṇikā  || 1,15.4
siṃdhuśca sarayū revā saptagaṃgāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  || 1,15.5
tato 'bdhitīraṃ daśa ca parvatāgre tato daśa  || 1,15.5
sarvasmādadhikaṃ jñeyaṃ yatra vā rocate manaḥ  || 1,15.6
kṛte pūrṇaphalaṃ jñeyaṃ yajñadānādikaṃ tathā  || 1,15.6
tretāyuge tripādaṃ ca dvāpare 'rdhaṃ sadā smṛtam  || 1,15.7
kalau pādaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ tatpādonaṃ tatordhake  || 1,15.7
śuddhātmanaḥ śuddhadinaṃ puṇyaṃ samaphalaṃ viduḥ  || 1,15.8
tasmāddaśaguṇaṃ jñeyaṃ ravisaṃkramaṇe budhāḥ  || 1,15.8
viṣuve taddaśaguṇamayane taddaśa smṛtam  || 1,15.9
taddaśa mṛgasaṃkrāṃtau taccaṃdragrahaṇe daśa  || 1,15.9
tataśca sūryagrahaṇe pūrṇakālottame viduḥ  || 1,15.10
jagadrūpasya sūryasya viṣayogācca rogadam  || 1,15.10
atastadviṣaśāṃtyarthaṃ snānadānajapāṃ caret || 1,15.11
viṣaśāṃtyarthakālatvātsa kālaḥ puṇyadaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 1,15.11
janmarkṣe ca vratāṃte ca sūryarāgopamaṃ viduḥ  || 1,15.12
mahatāṃ saṃgakālaśca koṭyarkagrahaṇaṃ viduḥ  || 1,15.12
taponiṣṭhā jñānaniṣṭhā yogino yatayastathā  || 1,15.13
pūjāyāḥ pātramete hi pāpasaṃkṣayakāraṇam  || 1,15.13
caturviṃśatilakṣaṃ vā gāyatryā japasaṃyutaḥ  || 1,15.14
brāhmaṇastu bhavetpātraṃ saṃpūrṇaphalabhogadam  || 1,15.14
patanāttrāyata iti pātraṃ śāstre prayujyate  || 1,15.15
dātuśca pātakāttrāṇātpātramityabhidhīyate  || 1,15.15
gāyakaṃ trāyate pātādgāyatrītyucyate hi sā  || 1,15.16
yathā'rthahino loke 'sminparasyārthaṃ na yacchati  || 1,15.16
arthavāniha yo loke parasyārthaṃ prayacchati  || 1,15.17
svayaṃ śuddho hi pūtātmā narānsatrātumarhati  || 1,15.17
gāyatrījapaśuddho hi śuddhabrāhmaṇa ucyate  || 1,15.18
tasmāddāne jape home pūjāyāṃ sarvakarmaṇi  || 1,15.18
dānaṃ kartuṃ tathā trātuṃ pātraṃ tu brāhmaṇorhati  || 1,15.19
annasya kṣudhitaṃ pātraṃ nārīnaramayātmakam  || 1,15.19
brāhmaṇaṃ śreṣṭhamāhūya yatkāle susamāhitam  || 1,15.20
tadarthaṃ śabdamarthaṃ vā sadbodhakamabhīṣṭadam  || 1,15.20
icchāvataḥ pradānaṃ ca saṃpūrṇaphaladaṃ viduḥ  || 1,15.21
yatpraśnānaṃtaraṃ dattaṃ tadarthaphaladaṃ viduḥ  || 1,15.21
yatsevakāya dattaṃ syāttatpādaphaladaṃ viduḥ  || 1,15.22
jātimātrasya viprasya dīnavṛtterdvijarṣabhāḥ  || 1,15.22
dattamarthaṃ hi bhogāya bhūrlokedaśavārṣikam  || 1,15.23
vedayuktasya viprasya svarge hi daśavārṣikam  || 1,15.23
gāyatrījapayuktasya satye hi daśavārṣikam  || 1,15.24
viṣṇubhaktasya viprasya dattaṃ vaikuṃṭhadaṃ viduḥ  || 1,15.24
śivabhaktasya viprasya dattaṃ kailāsadaṃ viduḥ  || 1,15.25
tattallokopabhogārthaṃ sarveṣāṃ dānamiṣyate  || 1,15.25
daśāṃgamannaṃ viprasya bhānuvāre dadannaraḥ  || 1,15.26
parajanmani cārogyaṃ daśavarṣaṃ samaśnute  || 1,15.26
bahumānamathāhvānamabhyaṃgaṃ pādasevanam  || 1,15.27
vāso gaṃdhādyarcanaṃ ca ghṛtāpūparasottaram  || 1,15.27
ṣaḍrasaṃ vyaṃjanaṃ caiva tāṃbūlaṃ dakṣiṇottaram  || 1,15.28
namaścānugamaścaiva svannadānaṃ daśāṃgakam  || 1,15.28
daśāṃgamannaṃ viprebhyo daśabhyo vai dadannaraḥ  || 1,15.29
arkavāre tathā''rogyaṃ śatavarṣaṃ samaśnute  || 1,15.29
somavārādivāreṣu tattadvāraguṇaṃ phalam  || 1,15.30
annadānasya vijñeyaṃ bhūrloke parajanmani  || 1,15.30
saptasvapi ca vāreṣu daśabhyaśca daśāṃgakam  || 1,15.31
annaṃ dattvā śataṃ varṣamārogyādikamaśnute  || 1,15.31
evaṃ śatebhyo viprebhyo bhānuvāre dadannaraḥ  || 1,15.32
sahasravarṣamārogyaṃ śarvaloke samaśnute  || 1,15.32
sahasrebhyastathā dattvā 'yutavarṣaṃ samaśnute  || 1,15.33
evaṃ somādivāreṣu vijñeyaṃ hi vipaścitā  || 1,15.33
bhānuvāre sahasrāṇāṃ gāyatrīpūtacetasām  || 1,15.34
annaṃ dattvā satyaloke hyārogyādi samaśnute  || 1,15.34
ayutānāṃ tathā dattvā viṣṇuloke samaśnute  || 1,15.35
annaṃ dattvā tu lakṣāṇāṃ rudraloke samaśnute  || 1,15.35
bālānāṃ brahmabuddhyā hi deyaṃ vidyārthibhirnaraiḥ  || 1,15.36
yūnāṃ ca viṣṇubuddhyā hi putrakāmārthibhirnaraiḥ  || 1,15.36
vṛddhānāṃ rudrabuddhyā hi deyaṃ jñānārthibhirnaraiḥ  || 1,15.37
bālastrībhāratībuddhyā buddhikāmairnarottamaiḥ  || 1,15.37
lakṣmībuddhyā yuvastrīṣu bhogakāmairnarottamaiḥ  || 1,15.38
vṛddhāsu pārvatībuddhyā deyamātmārthibhirjanaiḥ  || 1,15.38
śilavṛttyoñchavṛttyā ca gurudakṣiṇayārjitam  || 1,15.39
śuddhadravyamiti prāhustatpūrṇaphaladaṃ viduḥ  || 1,15.39
śuklapratigrahāddattaṃ madhyamaṃ dravyamucyate  || 1,15.40
kṛṣivāṇijyakopetamadhamaṃ dravyamucyate  || 1,15.40
kṣatriyāṇāṃ viśāṃ caiva śauryavāṇijyakārjitam  || 1,15.41
uttamaṃ dravyamityāhuḥ śūdrāṇāṃ bhṛtakārjitam  || 1,15.41
strīṇāṃ dharmārthināṃ dravyaṃ paitṛkaṃ bhartṛkaṃ tathā  || 1,15.42
gavādīnāṃ dvādaśīnāṃ caitrādiṣu yathākramam  || 1,15.42
saṃbhūya vā puṇyakāle dadyādiṣṭasamṛddhaye  || 1,15.43
gobhūtilahiraṇyājyavāsodhānyaguḍāni ca  || 1,15.43
raupyaṃ lavaṇakūṣmāṃḍe kanyādvādaśakaṃ tathā  || 1,15.44
godānāddattagavyena gomayenopakāriṇā  || 1,15.44
dhanadhānyādyāśritānāṃ duritānāṃ nivāraṇam  || 1,15.45
jalasnehādyāśritānāṃ duritānāṃ tu gojalaiḥ  || 1,15.45
kāyikāditrāṇāṃ tu kṣīradadhyājyakaistathā  || 1,15.46
tathā teṣāṃ ca puṣṭiśca vijñeyā hi vipaścitā  || 1,15.46
bhūdānaṃ tu pratiṣṭhārthamiha cā'mutra ca dvijāḥ  || 1,15.47
tiladānaṃ balārthaṃ hi sadā mṛtyujayaṃ viduḥ  || 1,15.47
hiraṇyaṃ jāṭharāgnestu vṛddhidaṃ vīryadaṃ tathā  || 1,15.48
ājyaṃ puṣṭikaraṃ vidyādvastramāyuṣkaraṃ viduḥ  || 1,15.48
dhānyamannaṃ samṛddhyarthaṃ madhurāhāradaṃ guḍam  || 1,15.49
raupyaṃ retobhivṛddhyarthaṃ ṣaḍrasārthaṃ tu lāvaṇam  || 1,15.49
sarvaṃ sarvasamṛddhyarthaṃ kūṣmāṃḍaṃ puṣṭidaṃ viduḥ  || 1,15.50
prāptidaṃ sarvabhogānāmiha cā'mutra ca dvijāḥ  || 1,15.50
yāvajjīvanamuktaṃ hi kanyādānaṃ tu bhogadam  || 1,15.51
panasāmrakapitthānāṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ phalameva ca  || 1,15.51
kadalyādyauṣadhīnāṃ ca phalaṃ gulmodbhavaṃ tathā  || 1,15.52
māṣādīnāṃ ca mudgānāṃ phalaṃ śākādikaṃ tathā  || 1,15.52
marīcisarṣapādyānāṃ śākopakaraṇaṃ tathā  || 1,15.53
yadṛtau yatphalaṃ siddhaṃ taddeyaṃ hi vipaścitā  || 1,15.53
śrotrādīṃdriyatṛptiśca sadā deyā vipaścitā  || 1,15.54
śabdādidaśabhogārthaṃ digādīnāṃ ca tuṣṭidā  || 1,15.54
vedaśāstraṃ samādāya buddhvā gurumukhātsvayam  || 1,15.55
karmaṇāṃ phalamastīti buddhirāstikyamucyate  || 1,15.55
baṃdhurājabhayādbuddhiśraddhā sā ca kanīyasī  || 1,15.56
sarvābhāve daridrastu vācā vā karmaṇā yajet || 1,15.56
vācikaṃ yajanaṃ vidyānmaṃtrastotrajapādikam  || 1,15.57
tīrthayātrāvratādyaṃ hi kāyikaṃ yajanaṃ viduḥ  || 1,15.57
yena kenāpyupāyena hyalpaṃ vā yadi vā bahu  || 1,15.58
devatārpaṇabuddhyā ca kṛtaṃ bhogāya kalpate  || 1,15.58
tapaścaryā ca dānaṃ ca kartavyamubhayaṃ sadā  || 1,15.59
pratiśrayaṃ pradātavyaṃ svavarṇaguṇaśobhitam  || 1,15.59
devānāṃ tṛptaye 'tyarthaṃ sarvabhogapradaṃ budhaiḥ  || 1,15.60
ihā'mutrottamaṃ janmasadābhogaṃ labhedbudhaḥ  || 1,15.60
īśvarārpaṇabuddhyā hi kṛtvā mokṣaphalaṃ labhet || 1,15.60
ya imaṃ paṭhate 'dhyāyaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti sadā naraḥ  || 1,15.61
tasya vaidharmabuddhiśca jñānasiddhiḥ prajāyate  || 1,15.61

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ pañcadaśodhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 16
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
pārthivapratimāpūjāvidhānaṃ brūhi sattama  || 1,16.1
yena pūjāvidhānena sarvābhiṣṭamavāpyate  || 1,16.1
sūta uvāca
susādhupṛṣṭaṃ yuṣmābhiḥ sadā sarvārthadāyakam  || 1,16.2
sadyo duḥkhasya śamanaṃ śṛṇuta prabravīmi vaḥ  || 1,16.2
apamṛtyuharaṃ kālamṛtyoścāpi vināśanam  || 1,16.3
sadyaḥ kalatraputrādidhanadhānyapradaṃ dvijāḥ  || 1,16.3
annādibhojyaṃ vastrādisarvamutpadyate yataḥ  || 1,16.4
tato mṛdādipratimāpūjābhīṣṭapradā bhuvi  || 1,16.4
puruṣāṇāṃ ca nārīṇāmadhikārotra niścitam  || 1,16.5
nadyāṃ taḍāge kūpe vā jalāṃtarmṛdamāharet || 1,16.5
saṃśodhya gaṃdhacūrṇena peṣayitvā sumaṃḍape  || 1,16.6
hastena pratimāṃ kuryātkṣīreṇa ca susaṃskṛtām  || 1,16.6
aṃgapratyaṃgakopetāmāyudhaiśca samanvitām  || 1,16.7
padmāsanasthitāṃ kṛtvā pūjayedādareṇa hi  || 1,16.7
vighneśādityaviṣṇūnāmaṃbāyāśca śivasya ca  || 1,16.8
śivasyaśivaliṃgaṃ ca sarvadā pūjayeddvija  || 1,16.8
ṣoḍaśairupacāraiśca kuryāttatphalasiddhaye  || 1,16.9
puṣpeṇa prokṣaṇaṃ kuryādabhiṣekaṃ samaṃtrakam  || 1,16.9
śālyannenaiva naivedyaṃ sarvaṃ kuḍavamānataḥ  || 1,16.10
gṛhe tu kuḍavaṃ jñeyaṃ mānuṣe prasthamiṣyate  || 1,16.10
daive prasthatrayaṃ yogyaṃ svayaṃbhoḥ prasthapañcakam  || 1,16.11
evaṃ pūrṇaphalaṃ vidyādadhikaṃ vai dvayaṃ trayam  || 1,16.11
sahasrapūjayā satyaṃ satyalokaṃ labheddvijaḥ  || 1,16.12
dvādaśāṃgulamāyāmaṃ dviguṇaṃ ca tato 'dhikam  || 1,16.12
pramāṇamaṃgulasyaikaṃ tadūrdhvaṃ pañcakatrayam  || 1,16.13
ayodārukṛtaṃ pātraṃ śivamityucyate budhaiḥ  || 1,16.13
tadaṣṭabhāgaḥ prasthaḥ syāttaccatuḥkuḍavaṃ matam  || 1,16.14
daśaprasthaṃ śataprasthaṃ sahasraprasthameva ca  || 1,16.14
jalatailādigaṃdhānāṃ yathāyogyaṃ ca mānataḥ  || 1,16.15
mānuṣārṣasvayaṃbhūnāṃ mahāpūjeti kathyate  || 1,16.15
abhiṣekādātmaśuddhirgaṃdhātpuṇyamavāpyate  || 1,16.16
āyustṛptiśca naivedyāddhūpādarthamavāpyate  || 1,16.16
dīpājjñānamavāpnoti tāṃbūlādbhogamāpnuyāt || 1,16.17
tasmātsnānādikaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ prayatnena prasādhayet || 1,16.17
namaskāro japaścaiva sarvābhīṣṭapradāvubhau  || 1,16.18
pūjānte ca sadākāryau bhogamokṣārthibhirnaraiḥ  || 1,16.18
saṃpūjya manasā pūrvaṃ kuryāttattatsadā naraḥ  || 1,16.19
devānāṃ pūjayā caiva tattallokamavāpnuyāt || 1,16.19
tadavāṃtaraloke ca yatheṣṭaṃ bhogyamāpyate  || 1,16.20
tadviśeṣānpravakṣyāmi śṛṇuta śraddhayā dvijāḥ  || 1,16.20
vighneśapūjayā samyagbhūrloke 'bhīṣṭamāpnuyāt || 1,16.21
śukravāre caturthyāṃ ca site śrāvaṇabhādrake  || 1,16.21
bhiṣagṛkṣe dhanurmāse vighneśaṃ vidhivadyajet || 1,16.22
śataṃ pūjāsahasraṃ vā tatsaṃkhyākadinairvrajet || 1,16.22
devāgniśraddhayā nityaṃ putradaṃ ceṣṭadaṃ nṛṇām  || 1,16.23
sarvapāpapraśamanaṃ tattadduritanāśanam  || 1,16.23
vārapūjāṃśivādīnāmātmaśuddhipradāṃ viduḥ  || 1,16.24
tīthinakṣatrayogānāmādhāraṃ sārvakāmikam  || 1,16.24
tathā bṛddhikṣayābhāvātpūrṇabrahmātmakaṃ viduḥ  || 1,16.25
udayādudayaṃ vāro brahmaprabhṛti karmaṇām  || 1,16.25
tithyādau devapūjā hi pūrṇabhogapradā nṛṇām  || 1,16.26
pūrvabhāgaḥ pitḥṇāṃ tu niśi yuktaḥ praśasyate  || 1,16.26
parabhāgastu devānāṃ divā yuktaḥ praśāsyate  || 1,16.27
udayavyāpinī grāhyā madhyāhne yadi sā tithiḥ  || 1,16.27
devakārye tathā grāhyāsthiti ṛkṣādikāḥ śubhāḥ  || 1,16.28
samyagvicārya vārādīnkuryātpūjājapādikam  || 1,16.28
pūjāryate hyaneneti vedeṣvarthasya yojanā  || 1,16.29
pūrṇabhogaphalasiddhiśca jāyate tena karmaṇā  || 1,16.29
