manobhāvāṃstathā jñānamiṣṭabhogārthayojanāt || 1,16.30
pūjāśabdartha evaṃ hi viśruto lokavedayoḥ  || 1,16.30
nityanaimittikaṃ kālātsadyaḥ kāmye svanuṣṭhite  || 1,16.31
nityaṃ māsaṃ ca pakṣaṃ ca varṣaṃ caiva yathākramam  || 1,16.31
tattatkarmaphalaprāptistādṛkpāpakṣayaḥ kramāt || 1,16.32
mahāgaṇapateḥ pūjā caturthyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣake  || 1,16.32
pakṣapāpakṣayakarī pakṣabhogaphalapradā  || 1,16.33
caitre caturthyāṃ pūjā ca kṛtā māsaphalapradā  || 1,16.33
varṣabhogapradā jñeyā kṛtā vai siṃhabhādrake  || 1,16.34
śravaṇyādityavāre ca saptamyāṃ hastabhe dine  || 1,16.34
māghaśukle ca saptamyāmādityayajanaṃ caret || 1,16.35
jyeṣṭhabhādrakasaumye ca dvādaśyāṃ śravarṇakṣake  || 1,16.35
dvādaśyāṃ viṣṇuyajanamiṣṭaṃsaṃpatkaraṃ viduḥ  || 1,16.36
śrāvaṇe viṣṇuyajanamiṣṭārogyapradaṃ bhavet || 1,16.36
gavādīndvādaśānarthānsāṃgāndatvā tu yatphalam  || 1,16.37
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti dvādaśyāṃ viṣṇutarpaṇāt || 1,16.37
dvādaśyāṃ dvādaśānviprānviṣṇordvādaśanāmataḥ  || 1,16.38
ṣoḍaśairupacāraiśca yajettatprītimāpnuyāt || 1,16.38
evaṃ ca sarvadevānāṃ tattaddvādaśanāmakaiḥ  || 1,16.39
dvādaśabrahmayajanaṃ tattatprītikaraṃ bhavet || 1,16.39
karkaṭe somavāre ca navamyāṃ mṛgaśīrṣake  || 1,16.40
aṃbāṃ yajedbhūtikāmaḥ sarvabhogaphalapradām  || 1,16.40
āśvayukchuklanavamī sarvābhīṣṭaphalapradā  || 1,16.41
ādivāre caturdaśyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe viśeṣataḥ  || 1,16.41
ārdrāyāṃ ca mahārdrāyāṃ śivapūjā viśiṣyate  || 1,16.42
māghakṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ sarvābhīṣṭaphalapradā  || 1,16.42
āyuṣkarī mṛtyuharā sarvasiddhikarī nṛṇām  || 1,16.43
jyeṣṭhamāse mahārdrāyāṃ caturdaśīdinepi ca  || 1,16.43
mārgaśīrṣārdrakāyāṃ vā ṣoḍaśairupacārakaiḥ  || 1,16.44
tattanmūrtiśivaṃ pūjya tasya vai pādadarśanam  || 1,16.44
śivasya yajanaṃ jñeyaṃ bhogamokṣapradaṃ nṛṇām  || 1,16.45
vārādidevayajanaṃ kārtike hi viśiṣyate  || 1,16.45
kārtike māsi saṃprāpte sarvāndevānyajedbudhaḥ  || 1,16.46
dānena tapasā homairjapena niyamena ca  || 1,16.46
ṣoḍaśairupacāraiśca pratimā vipramaṃtrakaiḥ  || 1,16.47
brāhmaṇānāṃ bhojanena niṣkāmārtikaro bhavet || 1,16.47
kārtike devayajanaṃ sarvabhogapradaṃ bhavet || 1,16.48
vyādhīnāṃ haraṇaṃ caiva bhavedbhūtagrahakṣayaḥ  || 1,16.48
kārtikādityavāreṣu nṛṇāmādityapūjanāt || 1,16.49
tailakārpāsadānāttu bhavetkuṣṭhādisaṃkṣayaḥ  || 1,16.49
harītakīmarīcīnāṃ vastrakṣīrādidānataḥ  || 1,16.50
brahmapratiṣṭhayā caiva kṣayarogakṣayo bhavet || 1,16.50
dīpasarṣapadānācca apasmārakṣayo bhavet || 1,16.51
kṛttikāsomavāreṣu śivasya yajanaṃ nṛṇām  || 1,16.51
mahādāridryaśamanaṃ sarvasaṃpatkaraṃ bhavet || 1,16.52
gṛhakṣetrādidānācca gṛhopakaraṇādinā  || 1,16.52
kṛttikābhaumavāreṣu skaṃdasya yajanānnṛṇām  || 1,16.53
dīpaghaṃṭādidānādvai vāksiddhiracirādbhavet || 1,16.53
kṛttikāsaumyavāreṣu viṣṇorvai yajanaṃ nṛṇām  || 1,16.54
dadhyodanasya dānaṃ ca satsaṃtānakaraṃ bhavet || 1,16.54
kṛtikāguruvāreṣu brahmaṇo yajanāddhanaiḥ  || 1,16.55
madhusvarṇājyadānena bhogavṛddhirbhavennṛṇām  || 1,16.55
kṛttikāśukravāreṣu gajakomeḍayājanāt1  || 1,16.56
gaṃdhapuṣpānnadānena bhogyavṛddhirbhavennṛṇām  || 1,16.56
vaṃdhyā suputraṃ labhate svarṇaraupyādidānataḥ  || 1,16.57
kṛttikāśanivāreṣu dikpālānāṃ ca vaṃdanam  || 1,16.57
diggajānāṃ ca nāgānāṃ setupānāṃ ca pūjanam  || 1,16.58
gajakomeḍo gajavaktraḥ
tryaṃbakasya ca rudrasya viṣṇoḥ pāpaharasya ca  || 1,16.58
jñānadaṃ brahmaṇaścaiva dhanvaṃtaryaśvinostathā  || 1,16.59
rogāpamṛtyuharaṇaṃ tatkālavyādhiśāṃtidam  || 1,16.59
lavaṇāyasatailānāṃ māṣādīnāṃ ca dānataḥ  || 1,16.60
trikaṭuphalagaṃdhānāṃ jalādīnāṃ ca dānataḥ  || 1,16.60
dravāṇāṃ kaṭhinānāṃ ca prasthena palamānataḥ  || 1,16.61
svargaprāptirdhanurmāse hyuṣaḥkāle ca pūjanam  || 1,16.61
śivādīnāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ kramādvai sarvasiddhaye  || 1,16.62
śālyannasya haviṣyasya naivedyaṃ śastamucyate  || 1,16.62
vividhānnasya naivedyaṃ dhanurmāse viśiṣyate  || 1,16.63
mārgaśīrṣe 'nnadasyaiva sarvamiṣṭaphalaṃ bhavet || 1,16.63
pāpakṣayaṃ ceṣṭasiddhiṃ cārogyaṃ dharmameva ca  || 1,16.64
samyagvedaparijñānaṃ sadanuṣṭhānameva ca  || 1,16.64
ihāmutra mahābhogānaṃte yogaṃ ca śāśvatam  || 1,16.65
vedāṃtajñānasiddhiṃ ca mārgaśīrṣānnado labhet || 1,16.65
mārgaśīrṣe hyuṣaḥkāle dinatrayamathāpi vā  || 1,16.66
yajeddevānbhogakāmo nādhanurmāsiko bhavet || 1,16.66
yāvatsaṃgavakālaṃ tu dhanurmāso vidhīyate  || 1,16.67
dhanurmāse nirāhāro māsamātraṃ jiteṃdriyaḥ  || 1,16.67
āmadhyāhnajapedvipro gāyatrīṃ vedamātaram  || 1,16.68
pañcākṣarādikānmaṃtrānpaścādāsaptikaṃ japet || 1,16.68
jñānaṃ labdhvā ca dehāṃte vipro muktimavāpnuyāt || 1,16.69
anyeṣāṃ naranārīṇāṃ triḥsnānena japena ca  || 1,16.69
sadā pañcākṣarasyaiva viśuddhaṃ jñānamāpyate  || 1,16.70
iṣṭamantrānsadājaptvā mahāpāpakṣayaṃ labhet || 1,16.70
dhanurmāse viśeṣeṇa mahānaivedyamācaret || 1,16.71
śālitaṃḍulabhāreṇa marīcaprasthakena ca  || 1,16.71
gaṇanāddvādaśaṃ sarvaṃ madhvājyakuḍavena hi  || 1,16.72
droṇayuktena mudgena dvādaśavyaṃjanena ca  || 1,16.72
ghṛtapakvairapūpaiśca modakaiḥ śālikādibhiḥ  || 1,16.73
dvādaśaiśca dadhikṣīrairdvādaśaprasthakena ca  || 1,16.73
nārikelaphalādīnāṃ tathā gaṇanayā saha  || 1,16.74
dvādaśakramukairyuktaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśatpatrakairyutam  || 1,16.74
karpūrakhuracūrṇena pañcasaugaṃdhikairyutam  || 1,16.75
tāṃbūlayuktaṃ tu yadā mahānaivedyalakṣaṇam  || 1,16.75
mahānaivedyametadvai devatārpaṇapūrvakam  || 1,16.76
varṇānukramapūrveṇa tadbhaktebhyaḥ pradāpayet || 1,16.76
evaṃ caudananaivedyādbhūmau rāṣṭrapatirbhavet || 1,16.77
mahānaivedyadānena naraḥ svargamavāpnuyāt || 1,16.77
mahānaivedyadānena sahasreṇa dvijarṣabhāḥ  || 1,16.78
satyaloke ca talloke pūrṇamāyuravāpnuyāt || 1,16.78
sahasrāṇāṃ ca triṃśatyā mahānaivedyadānataḥ  || 1,16.79
tadūrdhvalokamāpyaiva na punarjanmabhāgbhavet || 1,16.79
sahasrāṇāṃ ca ṣaṭtriṃśajjanma naivedyamīritam  || 1,16.80
tāvannaivedyadānaṃ tu mahāpūrṇaṃ taducyate  || 1,16.80
mahāpūrṇasya naivedyaṃ janmanaivedyamiṣyate  || 1,16.81
janmanaivedyadānena punarjanma na vidyate  || 1,16.81
ūrje māsi dine puṇye janma naivedyamācaret || 1,16.82
saṃkrāṃtipātajanmarkṣapaurṇamāsyādisaṃyute  || 1,16.82
abdajanmadine kuryājjanmanaivedyamuttamam  || 1,16.83
māsāṃtareṣu janmarkṣapūrṇayogadinepi ca  || 1,16.83
melanecaśanervāpitāvatsāhasramācaret || 1,16.84
janmanaivedyadānenajanmārpaṇaphalaṃlabhet || 1,16.84
janmārpaṇācchivaḥ prītiḥsvasāyujyaṃdadātihi  || 1,16.85
idaṃtajjanmanaivedyaṃśivasyaivapradāpayet || 1,16.85
yoniliṃgasvarūpeṇaśivojanmānirūpakaḥ  || 1,16.86
tasmājjanmanivṛttyarthaṃjanma pūjā śivasya hi  || 1,16.86
biṃdunādātmakaṃsarvaṃjagatsthāvarajaṃgamam  || 1,16.87
biṃduḥśaktiḥśivonādaḥśivaśaktyātmakaṃjagat || 1,16.87
nādādhāramidaṃbiṃdurbiṃdvādhāramidaṃ jagat || 1,16.88
jagadādhārabhūtauhibiṃdunādau vyavasthitau  || 1,16.88
vindunādayutaṃsarvaṃsakalīkaraṇaṃbhavet || 1,16.89
sakalīkaraṇājjanmajagatprāpnotyasaṃśayaḥ  || 1,16.89
biṃdunādātmakaṃliṃgaṃjagatkāraṇamucyate  || 1,16.90
biṃdurdervīśivonādaḥśivaliṃgaṃtukathyate  || 1,16.90
tasmājjanmanivṛttyarthaṃśivaliṃgaṃprapūjayet || 1,16.91
mātādevībiṃdurūpānādarūpaḥ śivaḥ pitā  || 1,16.91
pūjitābhyāṃpitṛbhyāṃtuparamānaṃda eva hi  || 1,16.92
paramānaṃdalābhārthaṃśivaliṃgaṃprapūjayet || 1,16.92
sādevījagatāṃmātāsaśivojagataḥ pitā   || 1,16.93
pitroḥ śuśrūṣakenityaṃkṛpādhikyaṃ hi vardhate  || 1,16.93
kṛpayāṃtargataiśvaryaṃpūjakasyadadātihi  || 1,16.94
tasmādaṃtargatānaṃdalābhārthaṃmunipuṃgavāḥ  || 1,16.94
pitṛmātṛsvarūpeṇaśivaliṃgaṃprapūjayet || 1,16.95
bhargaḥ puruṣarūpo hi bhargāprakṛtirucyate  || 1,16.95
avyaktāṃtaradhiṣṭhānaṃ garbhaḥ puruṣa ucyate  || 1,16.96
suvyaktāṃtaradhiṣṭhānaṃgarbhaḥ prakṛtirucyate  || 1,16.96
puruṣatvādigarbhohigarbhavāñjanakoyataḥ  || 1,16.97
puruṣātprakṛtoyuktaṃprathamaṃjanmakathyate  || 1,16.97
prakṛtervyaktatāṃyātaṃdvitīyaṃ janma kathyate  || 1,16.98
janmajaṃturmṛtyujanma puruṣātpratipadyate  || 1,16.98
anyatobhāvyate 'vaśyaṃmāyayājanmakathyate  || 1,16.99
jīryatejanmakālādyattasmājjīva iti smṛtaḥ  || 1,16.99
janmatetanyate pāśairjīvaśabdārtha eva hi  || 1,16.100
janmapāśanivṛttyarthaṃjanmaliṃgaṃprapūjayet || 1,16.100
bhaṃ vṛddhiṃ gacchatītyarthādbhagaḥprakṛtirucyate  || 1,16.101
prākṛtaiḥ śabdamātrādyaiḥ prākṛteṃdriyabhojanāt || 1,16.101
bhagasyedaṃ bhogamiti śabdārtho mukhyataḥ śrutaḥ  || 1,16.102
mukhyo bhagastu prakṛtirbhagavāñchiva ucyate  || 1,16.102
bhagavānbhogadātāhinā 'nyobhogapradāyakaḥ  || 1,16.103
bhagasvāmīcabhagavānbharga ityucyatebudhaiḥ  || 1,16.103
bhagenasahitaṃliṃgaṃ bhagaṃliṃgenasaṃyutam  || 1,16.104
ihāmutracabhogārthaṃnityabhogārthameva ca  || 1,16.104
bhagavaṃtaṃmahādevaṃ śivaliṃgaṃprapūjayet || 1,16.105
lokaprasavitāsūryastaccihnaṃ prasavādbhavet || 1,16.105
liṃgeprasūtikartāraṃliṃginaṃpuruṣoyajet || 1,16. 106
liṃgārthagamakaṃcihnaṃliṃgamityabhidhīyate  || 1,16.106
liṃgamarthaṃhipuruṣaṃ śivaṃgamayatītyadaḥ  || 1,16.107
śivaśaktyoścacihnasyamelanaṃliṃgamucyate  || 1,16.107
svacihnapūjanātprītaścihnakāryaṃ na vīyate  || 1,16.108
cihnakāryaṃtujanmādijanmādyaṃvinivartate  || 1,16.108
prākṛtaiḥ puruṣaiścāpibāhyābhyaṃtarasaṃbhavaiḥ  || 1,16.109
ṣoḍaśairupacāraiśca śivaliṃgaṃprapūjayet || 1,16.109
evamādityavārehipūjājanmanivartikā  || 1,16.110
ādivāremahāliṃgaṃpraṇavenaivapūjayet || 1,16.110
ādivāre pañcagavyairabhiṣeko viśiṣyate  || 1,16.111
gomayaṃgojalaṃkṣīraṃdadhyājyaṃ pañcagavyakam  || 1,16.111
kṣīrādyaṃcapṛthakccaivamadhunācekṣusārakaiḥ  || 1,16.112
gavyakṣīrānnanaivedyaṃpraṇavenaivakārayet || 1,16.112
praṇavaṃdhvaniliṃgaṃtunādaliṃgaṃsvayaṃbhuvaḥ  || 1,16.113
biṃduliṃgaṃ tu yaṃtraṃsyānmakāraṃtupratiṣṭhitam  || 1,16.113
ukāraṃcaraliṃgaṃsyādakāraṃguruvigraham  || 1,16.114
ṣaḍliṃgapūjayānityaṃjīvanmuktonasaṃśayaḥ  || 1,16.114
śivasyabhaktyāpūjā hi janmamuktikarīnṛṇām  || 1,16.115
rudrākṣadhāraṇātpādamardhaṃ vaibhūtidhāraṇāt || 1,16.115
tripādaṃ maṃtrajāpyācca pūjayā pūrṇabhaktimān  || 1,16.116
śivaliṃgaṃ ca bhaktaṃ ca pūjya mokṣaṃ labhennaraḥ  || 1,16.116
ya imaṃ paṭhate 'dhyāyaṃ śṛṇuyādvā samāhitaḥ  || 1,16.117
tasyaiva śivabhaktiśca vardhatesudṛḍhā dvijāḥ  || 1,16.117
iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ ṣoḍaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 17
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
praṇavasyacamāhātmyaṃṣaḍliṃgasyamahāmune  || 1,17.1
śivabhaktasyapūjāṃcakramaśobrūhinaḥprabho  || 1,17.1
sūta uvāca
tapodhanairbhavadbhiśca samyakpraśnastvayaṃ kṛtaḥ  || 1,17.2
asyottaraṃmahādevojānātismanacāparaḥ  || 1,17.2
athāpivakṣyetamahaṃśivasyakṛpayaivahi  || 1,17.3
śivo 'smākaṃcayuṣmākaṃrakṣāṃgṛhṇātubhūriśaḥ  || 1,17.3
prohiprakṛtijātasyasaṃsārasyamahodadheḥ  || 1,17.4
navaṃ nāvāṃtaramiti praṇavaṃ vai vidurbudhāḥ  || 1,17.4
praḥprapañconanāstivo yuṣmākaṃpraṇavaṃviduḥ  || 1,17.5
prakarṣeṇanayedyasmānmokṣaṃvaḥ praṇavaṃ viduḥ  || 1,17.5
svajāpakānāṃyogināṃsvamaṃtrapūjakasyaca  || 1,17.6
sarvakarmakṣayaṃkṛtvādivyajñānaṃtunūtanam  || 1,17.6
tameva māyārahitaṃ nūtanaṃ paricakṣate  || 1,17.7
prakarṣeṇa mahātmānaṃ navaṃ śuddhasvarūpakam  || 1,17.7
nūtanaṃ vai karotīti praṇavaṃ taṃ vidurbudhāḥ  || 1,17.8
praṇavaṃdvividhaṃproktaṃsūkṣmasthūlavibhedataḥ  || 1,17.8
sūkṣmamekākṣaraṃ vidyātsthūlaṃ pañcākṣaraṃ viduḥ  || 1,17.9
sūkṣmamavyaktapañcārṇaṃ suvyaktārṇaṃ tathetarat || 1,17.9
jīvanmuktasya sūkṣmaṃ hi sarvasāraṃ hi tasyahi  || 1,17.10
maṃtreṇārthānusaṃdhānaṃ svadehavilayāvadhi  || 1,17.10
svadehegalitepūrṇaṃśivaṃprāpnotiniścayaḥ  || 1,17.11
kevalaṃmaṃtrajāpītuyogaṃprāpnotiniścayaḥ  || 1,17.11

akāraśca ukāraścamakāraścatataḥparam  || 1,17.13
biṃdunādayutaṃ taddhi śabdakālakalānvitam  || 1,17.13
dīrghapraṇavamevaṃhiyogināmevahṛdgatam  || 1,17.14
makāraṃtaṃtritattvaṃ hi hrasvapraṇava ucyate  || 1,17.14
śivaḥśaktistayoraikyaṃmakāraṃtutrikātmakam  || 1,17.15
hrasvamevaṃhijāpyaṃsyātsarvapāpakṣayaiṣiṇām  || 1,17.15
bhūvāyukanakārṇodyoḥśabdādyāścatathādaśa  || 1,17.16
āśānvayedaśapunaḥ pravṛttā itikathyate  || 1,17.16
hrasvamevapravṛttānāṃnivṛttānāṃtudīrghakam  || 1,17.17
vyāhṛtyādaucamaṃtrādaukāmaṃśabdakalāyutam  || 1,17.17
vedādaucaprayojyaṃsyādvaṃdanesaṃdhyayorapi  || 1,17.18
navakauṭijapāñjaptvāsaṃśuddhaḥpuruṣobhavet || 1,17.18
punaścanavakoṭyātupṛthivījayamāpnuyāt || 1,17.19
punaścanavakoṭyātuhyapāṃjayamavāpnuyāt || 1,17.19
punaścanavakoṭyātutejasāṃjayamāpnuyāt || 1,17.20
punaścanavakoṭyā tu vāyorjayamavāpnuyāt || 1,17.20
ākāśajayamāpnoti navakoṭijapena vai  || 1,17.20
gaṃdhādīnāṃkrameṇaivanavakoṭijapeṇavai  || 1,17.21
ahaṃkārasya ca punarnava koṭijapena vai  || 1,17.21
sahasramaṃtrajaptena nityaśuddho bhavetpumān  || 1,17.22

evamaṣṭottaraśatakoṭijaptenavaipunaḥ  || 1,17.23
praṇavenaprabuddhastuśuddhayogamavāpnuyāt || 1,17.23
śuddhayogenasaṃyuktojīvanmuktonasaṃśayaḥ  || 1,17.24
sadājapansadādhyāyañchivaṃ praṇavarūpiṇam  || 1,17.24
samādhisthomahāyogīśiva eva nasaṃśayaḥ  || 1,17.25
ṛṣicchaṃdodevatādinyasyadehepunarjapet || 1,17.25
praṇavaṃ mātṛkāyuktaṃdehetyasya ṛṣirbhavet || 1,17.26
daśamātṛṣaḍadhvādi sarvaṃnyāsaphalaṃ labhet || 1,17.26
pravṛttānāṃ camiśrāṇāṃsthūlapraṇavamiṣyate  || 1,17.27
kriyātapojapairyuktāstrividhāḥ śivayoginaḥ  || 1,17.27
dhanādivibhavaiścaivakarādyaṃgairnamādibhiḥ  || 1,17.28
kriyayā pūjayāyuktaḥkriyāyogīti kathyate  || 1,17.28

paradrohādirahitastapoyogīti kathyate  || 1,17.29
etairyuktaḥsadākruddhaḥsarvakāmādivarjitaḥ  || 1,17.30
sadājapaparaḥśāṃtojapayogīti taṃviduḥ  || 1,17.30
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ pūjayā śivayoginām  || 1,17.31
sālokyādikrameṇaivaśuddhomuktiṃlabhennaraḥ  || 1,17.31
japayogamathovakṣyegadataḥ śṛṇutadvijāḥ  || 1,17.32
tapaḥkarturjapaḥ proktoyajjamanparimārjate  || 1,17.32
śivanāmanamaḥpūrvaṃcaturthyāṃpañcatattvakam  || 1,17.33
sthūlapraṇavarūpaṃ hi śivapañcākṣaraṃ dvijāḥ  || 1,17.33
pañcākṣarajapenaiva sarvasiddhiṃ labhennaraḥ  || 1,17.34
praṇavenādisaṃyuktaṃsadāpañcākṣaraṃjapet || 1,17.34
gurūpadeśaṃsaṃgamyasukhavāsesubhūtale  || 1,17.35
pūrvapakṣesamārabhyakṛṣṇabhūtāvadhidvijāḥ  || 1,17.35
māghaṃbhādraṃviśiṣṭaṃtusarvakālottamottamam  || 1,17.36
ekavāraṃmitāśītuvāgyatoniyateṃdriyaḥ  || 1,17.36

sahasrajapamātreṇabhavecchuddho 'nyathā ṛṇī  || 1,17.37
pañcākṣaraṃpañcalakṣaṃjapecchivamanusmaran  || 1,17.38
padmāsanasthaṃśivadaṃgaṃgācaṃdrakalānvitam  || 1,17.38
vāmorusthitaśaktyācavirājaṃtaṃmahāgaṇaiḥ  || 1,17.39
mṛgaṭaṃkadharaṃdevaṃvaradābhayapāṇikam  || 1,17.39
sadānugrahakartāraṃ sadāśivamanusmaran  || 1,17.40
saṃpūjyamanasā pūrvaṃhṛdivāsūryamaṃḍale  || 1,17.40
japetpañcākṣarīṃvidyāṃprāṅmukhaḥ śuddhakarmakṛt || 1,17.41
prātaḥkṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ nityakarmasamāpyaca  || 1,17.41
manorameśucaudeśeniyataḥ śuddhamānasaḥ  || 1,17.42
pañcākṣarasyamaṃtrasya sahasraṃdvādaśaṃjapet || 1,17.42
varayeccasapatnīkāñchaivānvaibrāhmaṇottamān  || 1,17.43
ekaṃguruvaraṃśiṣṭaṃvarayetsāṃbamūrtikam  || 1,17.43
īśānaṃcāthapuruṣamaghoraṃvāmameva ca  || 1,17.44
sadyojātaṃ capañcaivaśivabhaktāndvijottamān  || 1,17.44
pūjādravyāṇisaṃpādyaśivapūjāṃsamārabhet || 1,17.45
śivapūjāṃcavidhivatkṛtvāhomaṃsamārabhet || 1,17.45
mukhāṃtaṃ ca svasūtreṇakṛtyāhomaṃsamārabhet || 1,17.46
daśaikaṃvāśataikaṃvāsahasraikamathāpivā  || 1,17.46
kāpilenaghṛtenaivajuhuyātsvayamevahi  || 1,17.47
kārayecchivabhaktairvāpyaṣṭottaraśataṃbudhaḥ  || 1,17.47
homānte dakṣiṇādeyāgurorgomithunaṃ tathā  || 1,17.48
īśānādisvarūpāṃstānguruṃsāṃbaṃvibhāvya ca  || 1,17.48
teṣāṃ patsiktatoyenasvaśiraḥ snānamācaret || 1,17.49
ṣaṭtriṃśatkoṭitīrtheṣusadyaḥsnānaphalaṃlabhet || 1,17.49
daśāṃgamannaṃteṣāṃ vaidadyādvaibhaktipūrvakam  || 1,17.50
parābuddhyāguroḥ patnīmīśānādikrameṇa tu  || 1,17.50
paramānnenasaṃpūjyayathāvibhavavistaram  || 1,17.51
rudrākṣavastrapūrvaṃcavaṭakāpūpakairyutam  || 1,17.51
balidānaṃtataḥ kṛtvābhūribhojanamācaret || 1,17.52
tataḥ saṃprārthyadeveśaṃjapaṃtāvatsamāpayet || 1,17.52
puraścaraṇamevaṃtukṛtvāmantrībhavennaraḥ  || 1,17.53
punaścapañca lakṣeṇasarvapāpakṣayobhavet || 1,17.53
atalādisamārabhyasatyalokāvadhikramāt || 1,17.54

punaścapañcalakṣeṇa sārūpyaiśvaryamāpnuyāt || 1,17.56
āhatyaśatalakṣeṇasākṣādbrahmasamobhavet || 1,17.56
kāryabrahmaṇa evaṃ hi sāyujyaṃpratipadya vai  || 1,17.57
yatheṣṭaṃbhogamāpnotitadbrahmapralayāvadhi  || 1,17.57
punaḥ kalpāṃtarevṛtte brahmaputraḥ sajāyate  || 1,17.58
punaścatapasādīptaḥkramānmuktobhaviṣyati  || 1,17.58
pṛthvyādikāryabhūtebhyolokāvainirmitāḥkramāt || 1,17.59

śucauloketukailāserudrovaibhūtahṛtsthitaḥ  || 1,17.62
ṣaḍuttarāścapañcāśadahiṃsāṃtāstadūrdhvagāḥ  || 1,17.62
ahiṃsālokamāsthāyajñānakailāsakepure  || 1,17.63
kāryeśvarastirobhāvaṃsarvānkṛtvādhitiṣṭhati  || 1,17.63
tadaṃtekālacakraṃhikālātītastataḥparam  || 1,17.64
śivenādhiṣṭhitastatrakālaścakreśvarāhvayaḥ  || 1,17.64
māhiṣaṃ dharmamāsthāyasarvānkālena yuṃjati  || 1,17.65
asatyaścāśuciścaiva hiṃsācaivāthanirghṛṇā  || 1,17.65
asatyādicatuṣpādaḥsarvāṃśaḥ kāmarūpadhṛk || 1,17.66

krodhasaṃgaḥkṛṣṇavarṇomahāmahiṣaveṣavān  || 1,17.67

tadūrdhvaṃnityabhogohitadarvāṅnaśvaraṃviduḥ  || 1,17.70

tadarvākparivartaṃtebiṃdupūjāparāyaṇāḥ  || 1,17.72
tadūrdhvaṃhivrajaṃtyevaniṣkāmāliṃgapūjakāḥ  || 1,17.73
tadarvākparivartaṃtaṃśivānyasurapūjakāḥ  || 1,17.73
śivaikaniratāye ca tadūrdhvaṃsaṃprayāṃtite  || 1,17.74
tadarvāgjīvakoṭiḥsyāttadūrdhvaṃparakoṭikāḥ  || 1,17.74

tadarvāgāvṛtaṃ liṃgaṃ tadūrdhvaṃ hi nirāvṛti  || 1,17.77

tadarvākchaktilokā hi śataṃ vai dvādaśādhikam  || 1,17.78

janijaṃtirodhānaṃnāniṣiddhyātate iti  || 1,17.80
jñānaśabdārtha evaṃ hi tirodhānanivāraṇāt || 1,17.81
tadarvākparivartaṃtehyādhibhautikapūjakāḥ  || 1,17.81
ādhyātmikārcakā eva tadūrdhvaṃsaṃprayāṃtivai  || 1,17.82
tāvadvaivedibhāgaṃtanmahālokātmaliṃgake  || 1,17.82
prakṛtyādyaṣṭabaṃdhopivedyaṃtesaṃpratiṣṭhataḥ  || 1,17.83

adharmamahiṣārūḍhaṃkālacakraṃtaraṃtite  || 1,17.84

tadūrdhvaṃvṛṣabhodharmobrahmacaryasvarūpadhṛk || 1,17.85
satyādipādayuktastuśivalokāgrataḥsthitaḥ  || 1,17.85
kṣamāśṛṅgaḥ śamaśrotro vedadhvanivibhūṣitaḥ  || 1,17.86
āstikyacakṣurniśvāsagurubuddhimanā vṛṣaḥ  || 1,17.86
kriyādivṛṣabhā jñeyāḥ kāraṇādiṣu sarvadā  || 1,17.87
taṃ kriyāvṛṣabhaṃ dharmaṃ kālātītodhitiṣṭhati  || 1,17.87
brahmaviṣṇumaheśānāṃ svasvāyurdinamucyate  || 1,17.88
tadūrdhvaṃ na dinaṃ rātrirna janmamaraṇādikam  || 1,17. 88

pañcakṛtyapravīṇo 'sausaccidānaṃdavigrahaḥ  || 1,17.95

samādhyāsanamāsīnaḥ svātmārāmovirājate  || 1,17.96
tasyasaṃdarśanaṃsāṃdhyaṃkarmadhyānādibhiḥ kramāt || 1,17.97
nityādikarmayajanācchivakarmamatirbhavet || 1,17.97

punaśca śatalakṣeṇa aiśvaryaṃ padamāpnuyāt || 1,17.104
punaścaivaṃvidhenaiva japena susamāhitaḥ  || 1,17.105
śivalokādibhūtaṃ hi kālacakramavāpnuyāt || 1,17.105
kālacakraṃpañcacakramekaikenakramottare  || 1,17.106
sṛṣṭimohaubrahmacakraṃbhogamohautuvaiṣṇavam  || 1,17.106
kopamohauraudracakraṃbhramaṇaṃcaiśvaraṃviduḥ  || 1,17.107
śivacakraṃjñānamohaupañcacakraṃvidurbudhāḥ  || 1,17.107
punaścadaśakoṭyā hi kāraṇabrahmaṇaḥ padam  || 1,17.108
punaśca daśakoṭyāhitatpadaiśvaryamāpnuyāt || 1,17.108
evaṃ krameṇa viṣṇvādeḥ padaṃlabdhvāmahaujasaḥ  || 1,17.109
krameṇatatpadaiśvaryaṃ labdhvācaivamahātmanaḥ  || 1,17.109
śatakoṭimanuṃ japtvā pañcottaramataṃdritaḥ  || 1,17.110
śivalokamavāpnotipañcamāvaraṇādbahiḥ  || 1,17.110
rājasaṃmaṃḍapaṃtatranaṃdīsaṃsthānamuttamam  || 1,17.111
taporūpaścavṛṣabhastatraivaparidṛśyate  || 1,17.111
sadyojātasyatatsthānaṃpañcamāvaraṇaṃparam  || 1,17.112
vāmadevasyacasthānaṃcaturthāvaraṇaṃpunaḥ  || 1,17.112
aghoranilayaṃpaścāttṛtīyāvaraṇaṃparam  || 1,17.113
puruṣasyaivasāṃbasyadvitīyāvaraṇaṃśubham  || 1,17.113
īśānasyaparasyaivaprathamāvaraṇaṃtataḥ  || 1,17.114
dhyānadharmasya ca sthānaṃpañcamaṃmaṃḍapaṃtataḥ  || 1,17.114
balināthasyasaṃsthānaṃtatrapūrṇāmṛtapradam  || 1,17.115
caturthaṃmaṃḍapaṃpaścāccaṃdraśekharamūrtimat || 1,17.115
somaskaṃdasyacasthānaṃtṛtīyaṃmaṃḍapaṃparam  || 1,17.116
dvitīyaṃmaṃḍapaṃnṛtyamaṃḍapaṃprāhurāstikāḥ  || 1,17.116
prathamaṃmūlamāyāyāḥ sthānaṃtatraivaśobhanam  || 1,17.117
tataḥ paraṃ garbhagṛhaṃliṃgasthānaṃ paraṃ śubham  || 1,17.117
nandisaṃsthānataḥ paścānnaviduḥ śivavaibhavam  || 1,17.118
naṃdīśvarobahistiṣṭhanpañcākṣaramupāsate  || 1,17.118
evaṃ gurukramāllabdhaṃ naṃdīśācca mayāpunaḥ  || 1,17.119
tataḥ paraṃsvasaṃvedyaṃ śive naivānubhāvitam  || 1,17.119
śivasyakṛpayāsākṣācchivalokasyavaibhavam  || 1,17.120
vijñātuṃśakyate sarvairnānyathetyāhurāstikāḥ  || 1,17.120
evaṃkrameṇamuktāḥsyurbrāhmaṇāvaijiteṃdriyaḥ  || 1,17.121
anyeṣāṃ ca kramaṃvakṣye gadataḥ śṛṇutādarāt || 1,17.121
gurūpadeśājjāpyaṃvaibrāhmaṇānāṃnamo 'ṃtakam  || 1,17.122
pañcākṣaraṃ pañcalakṣamāyuṣyaṃprajapedvidhiḥ  || 1,17.122
strītvāpanayanārthaṃtupañcalakṣaṃjapetpunaḥ  || 1,17.123
maṃtreṇapuruṣobhūtvākramānmuktobhavedbudhaḥ  || 1,17.123
kṣatriyaḥpañcalakṣeṇakṣattratvamapaneṣyati  || 1,17.124
punaścapañcalakṣeṇakṣattriyobrāhmaṇobhavet || 1,17.124
maṃtrasiddhirjapāccaivakramānmuktobhavainnaraḥ  || 1,17.125
vaiśyastupañcalakṣeṇavaiśyatvamapaneṣyati  || 1,17.125
punaścapañcalakṣeṇamaṃtrakṣattriya ucyate  || 1,17.126
punaścapañcalakṣeṇa kṣattratvamapaneṣyati  || 1,17.126
punaścapañcalakṣeṇamaṃtrabrāhmaṇa ucyate  || 1,17.127
śūdraścaivanamoṃtena pañcaviṃśatilakṣataḥ  || 1,17.127
maṃtravipratvamāpadya paścācchuddho bhaveddvijaḥ  || 1,17.128
nārīvātha naro vātha brāhmaṇo vānya eva vā  || 1,17.128
namontaṃ vā namaḥ pūrvamāturaḥ sarvamāturaḥ sarvadā japet || 1,17.129
tataḥ strīṇāṃtathaivohyagururnirdarśayetkramāt || 1,17.129
sādhakaḥ pañcalakṣānte śivaprītyarthameva hi  || 1,17.130
mahābhiṣeka naivedyaṃ kṛtvā bhaktāṃśca pūjayet || 1,17.130
pūjayā śivabhaktasya śivaḥ prītataro bhavet || 1,17.131
śivasya śivabhaktasya bhedo nāsti śivo hi saḥ  || 1,17.131
śivasvarūpamaṃtrasyadhāraṇācchiva eva hi  || 1,17.132
śivabhaktaśarīre hi śivetatparamobhavet || 1,17.132
śivabhaktāḥ kriyāḥ sarvā vedasarvakriyāṃviduḥ  || 1,17.133
yāvadyāvacchivaṃmaṃtraṃyenajaptaṃbhavetkramāt || 1,17.133
tāvadvaiśivasānnidhyaṃ tasmindehe na saṃśayaḥ  || 1,17.134
devīliṃgaṃbhavedrūpaṃ śivabhaktastriyāstathā  || 1,17.134
yāvanmaṃtraṃjapeddevyāstāvatsānnidhyamasti hi  || 1,17.135
śivaṃsaṃpūjayeddhīmānsvayaṃvaiśabdarūpabhāk || 1,17.135
svayaṃcaivaśivobhūtvāparāṃśaktiṃprapūjayet || 1,17.136
śaktiṃberaṃcaliṃgaṃ ca hyālekhyāmāyayāyajet || 1,17.136
śivaliṃgaṃ śivaṃmatvāsvātmānaṃśaktirūpakam  || 1,17.137
śaktiliṃgaṃcadevīṃcamatvāsvaṃśivarūpakam  || 1,17.137
śivaliṃgaṃnādarūpabiṃdurūpaṃtuśaktikam  || 1,17.138
upapradhānabhāvena anyonyāsaktaliṃgakam  || 1,17.138
pūjayecca śivaṃśaktisaśivomūlabhāvanāt || 1,17.139
śivabhaktāñchivamatrarūpakāñchivarūpakān  || 1,17.139
ṣoḍaśairupacāraiścapūjayediṣṭamāpnuyāt || 1,17.140
yenaśuśrūṣaṇādyaiśca śivabhaktasya liṃginaḥ  || 1,17.140
ānaṃdaṃjanayedvidvāñchivaḥprītataro bhavet || 1,17.141
śivabhaktānsapatnīkānpatnyāsahasadaivatat || 1,17.141
pūjayedbhojanādyaiścapañcavādaśavāśatam  || 1,17.142
dhanedehecamaṃtrecabhāvanāyāmavaṃcakaḥ  || 1,17.142
śivaśaktisvarūpeṇanapunarjāyate bhuvi  || 1,17.143
nābheradho brahmabhāgamākaṃṭhaṃviṣṇubhāgakam  || 1,17.143
mukhaṃliṃgamitiproktaṃśivabhaktaśarīrakam  || 1,17.144
mṛtāndāhādiyuktānvādāhādirahitānmṛtān  || 1,17.144
uddiśyapūjayedādipitaraṃśivamevahi  || 1,17.145
pūjāṃkṛtvādimātuścaśivabhaktāṃśca pūjayet || 1,17.145
pitṛlokaṃsamāsādyakramānmuktobhavenmṛtaḥ  || 1,17.146
kriyāyuktadaśabhyaścatapoyuktoviśiṣyate  || 1,17.146
tapoyuktaśatebhyaścajapayuktoviśiṣyate  || 1,17.147
japayuktasahasrebhyaḥ śivajñānī viśiṣyate  || 1,17.147
śivajñāniṣulakṣeṣu dhyānayukto viśiṣyate  || 1,17.148
dhyānayukteṣu koṭibhyaḥ samādhistho viśiṣyate  || 1,17.148
uttarottara vai śiṣṭyātpūjāyāmuttarottaram  || 1,17.149
phalaṃvaiśiṣṭyarūpañcadurvijñeyaṃmanīṣibhiḥ  || 1,17.149
tasmādvaiśivabhaktasyamahimānaṃvettikonaraḥ  || 1,17.150
śivaśaktyoḥ pūjanaṃ ca śivabhaktasya pūjanam  || 1,17.150
kuruteyonarobhaktyāsaśivaḥ śivamedhate  || 1,17.151
ya imaṃ paṭhate 'dhyāyamarthavadvedasaṃmatam  || 1,17.151
śivajñānībhavedvipraḥśivena sahamodate  || 1,17.152
śrāvayecchivabhaktāṃścaviśeṣajño manīśvarāḥ  || 1,17.152
śivaprasādaśiddhiḥ syācchivasyakṛpayā budhāḥ  || 1,17.153

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ saptadaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 18
ṛṣya ūcuḥ
baṃdhamokṣasvarūpaṃ hi brūhi sarvārthavittama  || 1,18.1
sūta uvāca
baṃdhamokṣaṃtathopāyaṃvakṣye 'haṃśṛṇutādarāt || 1,18.1
prakṛtyādyaṣṭabaṃdhenabaddhojīvaḥ sa ucyate  || 1,18.2
prakṛtyādyaṣṭabaṃdhenanirmuktomukta ucyate  || 1,18.2
prakṛtyādivaśīkāromokṣa ityucyatesvataḥ  || 1,18.3
baddhajīvastunirmuktomuktajīvaḥ sa kathyate  || 1,18.3
prakṛtyagretatobuddhirahaṃkāroguṇātmakaḥ  || 1,18.4
pañcatanmātramityeteprakṛtyādyaṣṭakaṃviduḥ  || 1,18.4
prakṛṭyādyaṣṭajodehodehajaṃkarma ucyate  || 1,18.5
punaścakarmajodehojanmakarmapunaḥpunaḥ  || 1,18.5
śarīraṃtrividhaṃjñeyaṃsthūlaṃsūkṣmaṃcakāraṇam  || 1,18.6
sthūlaṃvyāpāradaṃproktaṃsūkṣmamiṃdriyabhogadam  || 1,18.6
kāraṇaṃtvātmabhogārthaṃjīvakarmānurūpataḥ  || 1,18.7
sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ puṇyapāpaiḥ karmabhiḥ phalamaśnute  || 1,18.7
tasmāddhikarmarajjvā hi baddho jīvaḥ punaḥ punaḥ  || 1,18.8
śarīratrayakarmabhyāṃ cakravadbhrāmyate sadā  || 1,18.8
cakrabhramanivṛtyarthaṃ cakrakartāramīḍayet || 1,18.9
prakṛtyādi mahācakraṃ prakṛteḥ parataḥ śivaḥ  || 1,18.9
cakrakartāmaheśo hi prakṛteḥ paratoyataḥ  || 1,18.10
pibativāthavamatijīvanbālojalaṃyathā  || 1,18.10
śivastathā prakṛtyādi vaśīkṛtyādhitiṣṭhati  || 1,18.11
sarvaṃvaśīkṛtaṃyasmāttasmācchiva iti smṛtaḥ  || 1,18.11
śiva eva hi sarvajñaḥ paripūrṇaśca niḥspṛhaḥ  || 1,18.11
sarvajñatātṛptiranādibodhaḥ svataṃtratā nityamaluptaśaktiḥ  || 1,18.12
anaṃtaśaktiścamaheśvarasya yanmānasaiśvaryamavaiti vedaḥ  || 1,18.12
ataḥ śivaprasādena prakṛtyādivaśaṃbhavet || 1,18.13
śivaprasādalābhārthaṃ śivamevaprapūjayatet || 1,18.13
niḥspṛhasya ca pūrṇasyatasya pūjākathaṃ bhavet || 1,18.14
śivoddeśakṛtaṃ karma prasādajanakaṃ bhavet || 1,18.14
liṃgeberebhaktajaneśivamuddiśyapūjayet || 1,18.15
kāyenamanasāvācādhanenāpiprapūjayet || 1,18.15
pujayā tu maheśo hi prakṛteḥ paramaḥ śivaḥ  || 1,18.16
prasādaṃkurutesatyaṃpūjakasyaviśeṣataḥ  || 1,18.16
śivaprasādātkarmādyaṃkrameṇasvavaśaṃbhavet || 1,18.17
karmārabhyaprakṛtyaṃtaṃyadāsarvaṃ vaśaṃbhavet || 1,18.17
tadāmukta itiproktaḥsvātmārāmovirājate  || 1,18.18
prasādātparameśasyakarma dehoyadāvaśaḥ  || 1,18.18
tadāvaiśivaloketuvāsaḥsālokyamucyate  || 1,18.19
sāmīpyaṃyātisāṃbasyatanmātrecavaśaṃgate  || 1,18.19
tadātuśivasāyujyamāyudhādyaiḥ kriyādibhiḥ  || 1,18.20
mahāprasādalābhecabuddhiścāpivaśābhavet || 1,18.20
buddhistukāryaṃprakṛtestatsṛṣṭiritikathyate  || 1,18.21
punarmahāprasādenaprakṛtirvaśameṣyati  || 1,18.21
śivasyamānasaiśvaryaṃtadā 'yatnaṃbhaviṣyati  || 1,18.22
sārvajñādyaṃśivaiśvaryaṃlabdhvāsvātmani rājate  || 1,18.22
tatsāyujyamitiprāhurvedāgamaparāyaṇāḥ  || 1,18.23
evaṃkrameṇamuktiḥsyālliṃgādaupūjayāsvataḥ  || 1,18.23
ataḥśivaprasādārthaṃkriyādyaiḥpūjayecchivam  || 1,18.24
śivakriyā śivatapaḥ śivamaṃtrajapaḥ sadā  || 1,18.24
śivajñānaṃśivadhyānamuttarottaramabhyaset || 1,18.25
āsupterāmṛteḥkālaṃnayedvaiśivaciṃtayā  || 1,18.25
sadyādibhiścakusumairarcayecchiṃvameṣyati  || 1,18. 26
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
liṃgādauśivapūjāyāvidhānaṃbrūhisarvataḥ  || 1,18.26
sūta uvāca
liṃgānāṃcakramaṃvakṣyeyathāvacchṛṇuta dvijāḥ  || 1,18.27
tadevaliṃgaṃ prathamaṃ praṇavaṃ sārvakāmikam  || 1,18.27
sūkṣmapraṇavarūpaṃhisūkṣmarūpaṃtuniṣphalam  || 1,18.28
sthūlaliṃgaṃ hi sakalaṃtatpañcākṣaramucyate  || 1,18.28
tayoḥ pūjātapaḥ proktaṃsākṣānmokṣaprade ubhe  || 1,18.29
pauruṣaprakṛtibhūtāniliṃgānisubahūnica  || 1,18.29
tāni vistaratovaktuṃ śivo vettinacāparaḥ  || 1,18.30
bhūvikārāṇiliṃgānijñātāniprabravīmivaḥ  || 1,18.30
svayaṃbhūliṃgaṃprathamaṃbiṃduliṃgaṃdvitīyakam  || 1,18.31
pratiṣṭhitaṃcaraṃcaivaguruliṃgaṃtu pañcamam  || 1,18.31
devarṣitapasā tuṣṭaḥ sānnidhyārthaṃtutatravai  || 1,18.32
pṛthivyantargataḥśarvobījaṃ vai nādarūpataḥ  || 1,18.32
sthāvarāṃkuravadbhūmimudbhidya vyakta eva saḥ  || 1,18.33
svayaṃbhūtaṃ jātamiti svayaṃbhūriti taṃ viduḥ  || 1,18.33
talliṃgapūjayā jñānaṃ svayameva pravardhate  || 1,18.34
suvarṇarajatādau vā pṛthivyāṃsthiṃḍilepivā  || 1,18.34
svahastāllikhitaṃ liṃgaṃ śuddhapraṇavamaṃtrakam  || 1,18.35
yaṃtraliṃgaṃ samālikhya pratiṣṭhāvāhanaṃ caret || 1,18.35
biṃdunādamayaṃliṃgaṃsthāvaraṃjaṃgamaṃcayat || 1,18.36
bhāvanāmayametaddhi śivadṛṣṭaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 1,18.36
yatra viśvasyate śaṃbhustatra tasmai phalapradaḥ  || 1,18.37
svahastāllikhyate yaṃtre sthāvarādāvakṛtrime  || 1,18.37
āvāhyapūjayecchaṃbhuṃṣoḍaśairupacārakaiḥ  || 1,18.38
svayamaisvaryamāpnotijñānamabhyāsatobhavet || 1,18.38
devaiśca ṛṣibhiścāpisvātmasiddhyarthamevahi  || 1,18.39
samaṃtreṇātmahastenakṛtaṃyacchuddhamaṃḍale  || 1,18.39
śuddhabhāvanayā caivasthāpitaṃ liṃgamuttamam  || 1,18.40
talliṃgaṃpauruṣaṃ prāhustatpratiṣṭhitamucyate  || 1,18.40
talliṃgapūjayānityaṃpauruṣaiśvaryamāpnuyāt || 1,18.41
mahadbhirbrāhmaṇaiścāpirājabhiścamahādhanaiḥ  || 1,18.41
śilpinākalpitaṃliṃgaṃmaṃtreṇasthāpitaṃcayat || 1,18.42
pratiṣṭhitaṃprākṛtaṃhiprākṛtaiśvaryabhogadam  || 1,18.42
yadūrjitaṃcanityaṃ ca taddhi pauruṣamucyate  || 1,18.43
yaddurbalamanityaṃ ca taddhi prākṛtamucyate  || 1,18.43
liṃgaṃnābhistathājihvānāsāgrañcaśikhākramāt || 1,18.44
kaṭyādiṣutrilokeṣu liṃgamādhyātmikaṃcaram  || 1,18.44
parvataṃpauruṣaṃ proktaṃbhūtalaṃ prākṛtaṃ viduḥ  || 1,18.45
vṛkṣādi pauruṣaṃ jñeyaṃ gulmādi prākṛtaṃ viduḥ  || 1,18.45
ṣāṣṭikaṃ prākṛtaṃ jñeyaṃ śāligodhūmapauruṣam  || 1,18.46
aiśvaryaṃ pauruṣaṃ vidyādaṇimādyaṣṭasiddhidam  || 1,18.46
sustrīdhanādiviṣayaṃ prākṛtaṃ prāhurāstikāḥ  || 1,18.47
prathamaṃ caraliṃgeṣu rasaliṃgaṃ prakathyate  || 1,18.47
rasaliṃgaṃbrāhmaṇānāṃsarvābhīṣṭapradaṃbhavet || 1,18.48
bāṇaliṃgaṃkṣatriyāṇāṃ mahārājyapradaṃśubham  || 1,18.48
svarṇaliṃgaṃ tu vaiśyānāṃmahādhanapatitvadam  || 1,18.49
śilāliṃgaṃtuśūdrāṇāṃmahāśuddhikaraṃśubham  || 1,18.49
sphāṭikaṃbāṇaliṃgaṃ ca sarveṣāṃsarvakāmadam  || 1,18.50
svīyābhāve 'nyadīyaṃtupūjāyāṃnaniṣiddhyate  || 1,18.50
strīṇāṃtupārthivaṃliṃgaṃsabhatḥṇāṃviśeṣataḥ  || 1,18.51
vidhavānāṃpravṛttānāṃsphāṭikaṃ parikīrtitam  || 1,18.51
vidhavānāṃ nivṛttānāṃ rasaliṃgaṃ viśiṣyate  || 1,18.52
bālyevāyauvanevāpivārdhakevāpisuvratāḥ  || 1,18.52
śuddhasphaṭikaliṃgaṃtustrīṇāṃtatsarvabhogadam  || 1,18.53
pravṛttānāṃpīṭhapūjāsarvābhīṣṭapradā bhuvi  || 1,18.53
pātreṇaivapravṛttastusarvapūjāṃsamācaret || 1,18.54
abhiṣekāṃtenaivedyaṃśālyannenasamācaret || 1,18.54
pūjāṃtesthāpayelliṃgaṃsaṃpuṭeṣupṛthaggṛhe  || 1,18.55
karapūjānivṛttānāṃsvabhojyaṃtunivedayet || 1,18.55
nivṛttānāṃparaṃsūkṣmaliṃgamevaviśiṣyate  || 1,18.56
vibhūtyabhyarcanaṃ kuryādvibhūtiṃcanivedayet || 1,18.56
pūjāṃkṛtvāthatalliṃgaṃśirasādhārayetsadā  || 1,18.57
vibhūtistrividhāproktālokavedaśivāgnibhiḥ  || 1,18.57
lokāgnijamatho bhasmadravyaśuddhyarthamāvahet || 1,18.58
mṛddāruloharūpāṇāṃdhānyānāṃcatathaivaca  || 1,18.58
tilādīnāṃ ca dravyāṇāṃvastrādīnāṃtathaiva ca  || 1,18.59
tathāparyuṣitānāṃcabhasmanāśiddhiriṣyate  || 1,18.59
śvādibhirdūṣitānāṃcabhasmanāśuddhiriṣyate  || 1,18.60
sajalaṃnirjalaṃbhasma yathāyogyaṃ tu yojayet || 1,18.60
vedāgnijaṃtathābhasmatatkarmāṃteṣu dhārayet || 1,18.61
maṃtreṇakriyayājanyaṃkarmāgnau bhasmarūpadhṛk || 1,18.61
tadbhasmadhāraṇātkarma svātmanyāropitaṃ bhavet || 1,18.62
aghoreṇātmaṃtreṇa bilvakāṣṭhaṃpradāhayet || 1,18.62
śivāgniritisaṃproktastenadagdhaṃśivāgnijam  || 1,18.63
kapilāgomayaṃ pūrvaṃ kevalaṃ gavyameva vā  || 1,18.63
śamyasvatthapalāśānvāvaṭāramvadhabilvakān  || 1,18.64

ṛṣaya ūcuḥ

yathāvrateṣusarveṣuśivarātrivrataṃparam  || 1,19.14
tathāsarveṣuliṃgeṣupārthivaṃśreṣthamucyate  || 1,19.14
yathādevīṣusarvāsuśaivīśaktiḥ parāsmṛtā  || 1,19.15
tathā sarveṣu liṃgeṣu pārthivaṃ śreṣṭhamucyate  || 1,19.15
prakṛtyapārthivaṃliṃgaṃyonyadevaṃprapūjayet || 1,19.16
vṛthā bhavati sā pūjā snānadānādikaṃvṛthā  || 1,19.16
pārthivārādhanaṃpuṇyaṃdhanyamāyurvivardhanam  || 1,19.17
tuṣṭidaṃpuṣṭidaṃśrīdaṃkāryaṃsādhakasattamaiḥ  || 1,19.17
yathālabdhopacāraiścabhaktyā śraddhāsamanvitaḥ  || 1,19.18
pūjayetpārthivaṃ liṃgaṃ sarvakāmārthasiddhidam  || 1,19.18
yaḥkṛtvāpārthivaṃliṃgepūjayecchubhavedikam  || 1,19.19
ihaivadhanavāñchrīmānaṃterudrobhijāyate  || 1,19.19
trisaṃdhyaṃyorcayaṃlliṃgaṃkṛtvābilvenapārthivam  || 1,19.20
daśaikādaśakaṃyāvattasyapuṇyaphalaṃśṛṇu  || 1,19.20
anenaiva svadehena rudralokemahīyate  || 1,19.21
pāpahaṃ sarvamartyānāṃ darśanātsparśanādapi  || 1,19.21
jīvanmuktaḥ savaijñānīśiva eva na saṃśayaḥ  || 1,19.22
tasyadarśanamātreṇabhuktirmuktiśca jāyate  || 1,19.22
śivaṃ yaḥ pūjayennityaṃ kṛtvāliṃgaṃ tu pārthivam  || 1,19.23
yāvajjīvanaparyaṃtaṃ sa yāti śivamandiram  || 1,19.23
mṛḍenāpramitānvarṣāñchivalokehi tiṣṭhati  || 1,19.24
sakāmaḥ punarāgatya rājendrobhāratebhavet || 1,19.24
niṣkāmaḥ pūjayennityaṃ pārthivaṃliṃgamuttamam  || 1,19.25
śivaloke sadā tiṣṭhettataḥ sāyujyamāpnuyāt || 1,19.25
pārthivaṃ śivaliṃgaṃ ca vipro yadi na pūjayet || 1,19.26
sayātinarakaṃghoraṃśūlaprotaṃ sudāruṇam  || 1,19.26
yathākathaṃcidvidhināramyaṃ liṃgaṃ prakārayet || 1,19.27
pañcasūtravidhānāṃ ca pārthivena vicārayet || 1,19.27
akhaṇḍaṃtaddhikartavyaṃnavikhaṇḍaṃprakārayet || 1,19.28
dvikhaṇḍaṃtuprakurvāṇonaivapūjāphalaṃlabhet || 1,19.28
ratnajaṃ hemajaṃ liṃgaṃ pāradaṃ sphāṭikaṃ tathā  || 1,19.29
pārthivaṃ puṣparāgotthamakhaṃḍaṃ tu prakārayet || 1,19.29
akhaṃḍaṃ tu caraṃ liṃgaṃdvikhaṃḍamacaraṃ smṛtam  || 1,19.30
khaṃḍākhaṃḍavicāroyaṃ sacarācarayoḥ smṛtaḥ  || 1,19.30
vedikātumahāvidyāliṃgaṃdevo maheśvaraḥ  || 1,19.31
ato hi sthāvareliṃge smṛtāśreṣṭhādikhaṃḍitā  || 1,19.31
dvikhaṃḍaṃsthāvaraṃliṃgaṃkartavyaṃhividhānataḥ  || 1,19.32
akhaṃḍaṃjaṃgamaṃproktaṃśaivasiddhāntavedibhiḥ  || 1,19.32
dvikhaṃḍaṃtucarāṃliṃgaṃkurvantyajñānamohitāḥ  || 1,19.33
naivasiddhāntavettāro munayaḥ śāstrakovidāḥ  || 1,19.33
akhaṃḍaṃsthāvaraṃliṃgaṃdvikhaṃḍaṃcaramevaca  || 1,19.34
yekurvantinarāmūḍhānapūjāphalabhāginaḥ  || 1,19.34
tasmācchāstroktavidhinā akhaṃḍaṃcarasaṃjñakam  || 1,19.35
dvikhaṃḍaṃ sthāvaraṃ liṃgaṃ kartavyaṃ parayā mudā  || 1,19.35
akhaṃḍe tu care pūjāsampūrṇaphaladāyinī  || 1,19.36
dvikhaṃḍe tu care pūjāmahāhānipradā smṛtā  || 1,19.36
akhaṃḍe sthāvare pūjā na kāmaphaladāyinī  || 1,19.37
pratyavāyakarīnityamityuktaṃśāstravedibhiḥ  || 1,19.37

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ sādhyasādhanakhaṃḍe pārthivaśivaliṃgapūjanamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonaviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 20
sūta uvāca
atha vaidikabhaktānāṃ pārthivārcāṃ nigadyate  || 1,20.1
vaidikenaivamārgeṇabhuktimuktipradāyinī  || 1,20.1
sūtroktavidhināsnātvāsaṃdhyāṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi  || 1,20.2
brahmayajñaṃvidhāyādautatastarpaṇamācaret || 1,20.2
naityikaṃ sakalaṃkāmaṃ vidhāyānaṃtaraṃpumān  || 1,20. 3
śivasmaraṇapūrvaṃ hi bhasmarudrākṣadhārakaḥ  || 1,20.3
vedoktāvidhinā samyaksaṃpūrṇaphalasiddhaye  || 1,20.4
pūjayetparayābhaktyāpārthivaṃliṃgamuttamam  || 1,20.4
nadītīre taḍāge ca parvatekānane 'pica  || 1,20.5
śivālaye śucau deśe pārthivārcā vidhīyate  || 1,20.5
śuddhapradeśasaṃbhūtāṃ mṛdamāhṛtya yatnataḥ  || 1,20.6
śivaliṃgaṃ prakalpeta sāvadhānatayā dvijāḥ  || 1,20.6
vipre gaurā smṛtā śoṇā bāhuje pītavarṇakā  || 1,20.7
vaiśyekṛṣṇā pādajāte hyathavā yatra yā bhavet || 1,20.7
saṃgṛhyamṛttikāṃliṃganirmāṇārthaṃprayatnataḥ  || 1,20.8
atīvaśubhadeśecasthāpayettāṃmṛdaṃśubhām  || 1,20.8
saṃśodhyacajalenāpipiṃḍīkṛtya śanaiḥ śanaiḥ  || 1,20.9
vidhīyetaśubhaṃliṃgaṃpārthivaṃvedamārgataḥ  || 1,20.9
tataḥ saṃpūjayedbhaktyābhuktimuktiphalāptaye  || 1,20.10
tatprakāramahaṃvacmiśṛṇudhvaṃsaṃvidhānataḥ  || 1,20.10
namaḥ śivāyamaṃtreṇārcanaṃdravyaṃcaprokṣayet || 1,20.11
bhūrasīticamaṃtreṇakṣetrasiddhiṃprakārayet || 1,20.11
āposmānitimaṃtreṇajalasaṃskāramācaret || 1,20.12
namaste rudramaṃtreṇa phāṭikābaṃdhamucyate  || 1,20.12
śaṃbhavāyetimaṃtreṇakṣetraśuddhiṃ prakārayet || 1,20.13
namaḥ pūrveṇa kuryātpañcāmṛtasyāpi prokṣaṇam  || 1,20.13
nīlagrīvāyamaṃtreṇanamaḥpūrveṇabhaktimān  || 1,20.14
carecchaṃkaraliṃgasyapratiṣṭhāpanamuttamam  || 1,20.14
bhaktitastata etatterudrāyeti ca maṃtrataḥ  || 1,20.15
āsanaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ vaidadyādvaidikamārgakṛt || 1,20.15
mānomahantamiticamaṃtreṇāvāhanaṃcaret || 1,20.16
yāterudreṇamaṃtreṇasaṃcaredupaveśanam  || 1,20.16
maṃtreṇayāmiṣumiti nyāsaṃkuryācchivasyaca  || 1,20.17
adhyavocaditi premṇādhivāsaṃmanunācaret || 1,20.17
manunāsaujīva itidevatānyāsamācaret || 1,20.18
asauyovasarpatīti cācaredapasarpaṇam  || 1,20.18
namostu nīlagrīvāyeti pādyaṃ manunāharet || 1,20. 19
arghyaṃ ca rudragāyatryā 'camanaṃ tryaṃbakeṇa ca  || 1,20.19
payaḥpṛthivyāmiti ca payasāsnānamācaret || 1,20.20
dadhikrāvṇetimaṃtreṇadadhisnānaṃ ca kārayet || 1,20.20
ghṛṭaṃsnānekhalughṛtaṃghṛtaṃyāvetimaṃtrataḥ  || 1,20. 21
madhuvātāmadhunaktaṃmadhumānna iti tryṛcā  || 1,20. 21
madhukhaṃḍasnapanaṃproktamitipañcāmṛtaṃ smṛtam  || 1,20.22
athavā pādyamaṃtreṇa snānaṃpañcāmṛtena ca  || 1,20.22
mānastoke iti premṇā maṃtreṇa kaṭibaṃdhanam  || 1,20.23
namo dhṛṣṇave iti vā uttarīyaṃ ca dhāpayet || 1,20.23
yā te hetiriti premṇā ṛkcatuṣkeṇa vaidikaḥ  || 1,20.24
śivāya vidhinā bhaktaścaredvastrasamarpaṇam  || 1,20.24
namaḥ śvabhya iti premṇā gaṃdhaṃ dadyādṛcā sudhīḥ  || 1,20.25
namastakṣabhya iti cākṣatānmaṃtreṇa cārpayet || 1,20. 25
namaḥ pāryāya iti vā puṣpa maṃtreṇa cārpayet || 1,20.26
namaḥ parṇyāya iti vā bilbapatrasamarpaṇam  || 1,20.26
namaḥkapardineceti dhūpaṃ dadyādyathāvidhi  || 1,20.27
dīpaṃ dadyādyathoktaṃ tu nama āśava ityṛcā  || 1,20.27
namojyeṣṭhāyamaṃtreṇa dadyānnaivedyamuttamam  || 1,20.28
manunātryambakamitipunarācamanaṃsmṛtam  || 1,20.28
imā rudrāyeti ṛcā kuryātphalasamarpaṇam  || 1,20.29
namo vrajyāyeti ṛcā sakalaṃ śaṃbhaverpayet || 1,20.29
mano mahāṃtamiti ca mānastoke iti tataḥ  || 1,20.30
maṃtredvayenaikadaśākṣatai rudrānprapūjayet || 1,20.30
hiraṇyagarbha iti tryṛcādakṣiṇāṃhisamarpayet || 1,20.31
devasyatvetimaṃtreṇahyabhiṣekaṃcaredbudhaḥ  || 1,20.31
dīpamaṃtreṇavāśaṃbhornīrājanavidhiṃcaret || 1,20.32
puṣpāṃjaliṃcaredbhaktyā imā rudrāyacatryṛcā  || 1,20.32
mānomahāntamiti ca caretprājñaḥ pradakṣiṇām  || 1,20.33
mānastoketi maṃtreṇa sāṣṭāṅgaṃpraṇametsudhīḥ  || 1,20.33
eṣate itimaṃtreṇaśivamudrāṃpradarśayet || 1,20. 34
yatoyata ityabhayāṃjñānākhyāṃ tryaṃbakeṇa ca  || 1,20.34
namaḥseneti maṃtreṇamahāmudrāṃpradarśayet || 1,20.35
darśayeddhenumudrāṃcanamogebhya ṛcānayā  || 1,20.35
pañcamudrāḥpradarśyātha śivamaṃtrajapaṃ caret || 1,20.36
śatarudriyamaṃtreṇa japedvedavicakṣaṇaḥ  || 1,20.36
tataḥ pañcāṅgapāṭhaṃcakuryādvedavicakṣaṇaḥ  || 1,20.37
devāgātvitimaṃtreṇakuryācchaṃbhorvisarjanam  || 1,20.37
ityuktaḥ śivapūjāyāvyāsatovaidikovidhiḥ  || 1,20.38
samāsataścaśṛṇutavaidikaṃvidhimuttamam  || 1,20.38
ṛcāsadyojātamitimṛdāharaṇamācaret || 1,20.39
vāmadevāya iti ca jalaprakṣepamācaret || 1,20.39
aghoreṇa ca maṃtreṇaliṃganirmāṇamācaret || 1,20.40
tatpuruṣāyamaṃtreṇāhvānaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi  || 1,20.40
saṃyojayedvedikāyāmīśānamanunāharam  || 1,20.41
anyatsarvaṃ vidhānaṃcakuryātsaṃkṣepataḥ sudhīḥ  || 1,20.41
pañcākṣareṇamaṃtreṇagurudattenavātathā  || 1,20.42
kuryātpūjāṃ ṣoḍaśopacāreṇa vidhivatsudhīḥ  || 1,20.42
bhavāyabhavanāśāyamahādevāyadhīmahi  || 1,20.43
ugrāya ugranāśāya śarvāya śaśimauline  || 1,20.43
anena manunā vāpi pūjayecchaṃkaraṃsudhīḥ  || 1,20.44
subhaktyā cabhramaṃtyaktvābhaktyaivaphaladaḥ śivaḥ  || 1,20.44
ityapi proktamādṛtya vaidikakramapūjanam  || 1,20.45
procyatenyavidhiḥ samyaksādhāraṇatayādvijaḥ  || 1,20.45
pūjāpārthivaliṃgasyasaṃproktāśivanāmabhiḥ  || 1,20.46
tāṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ muniśreṣṭhāḥ sarvakāmapradāyinīm  || 1,20. 46
haromaheśvaraḥ śaṃbhuḥśūlapāṇiḥ pinākadhṛk || 1,20.47
śivaḥ paśupatiścaivamahādeva iti kramāt || 1,20.47
mṛdāharaṇasaṃghaṭṭapratiṣṭhāhvānamevaca  || 1,20.48
snapanaṃpūjanaṃcaivakṣamasvetivisarjanam  || 1,20.48
aṃkārādicaturthyaṃtairnamontairnāmabhiḥ kramāt || 1,20.49
kartavyācakriyāsarvābhaktyāparamayāmudā  || 1,20.49
kṛtvā nyāsavidhiṃsamyakṣaḍaṅgakarayostathā  || 1,20.50
ṣaḍakṣareṇamaṃtreṇatatodhyānaṃsamācaret || 1,20.50
kailāsapīṭhāsanamadhyasaṃsthaṃ bhaktaiḥ sanaṃdādibhirarcyamānam  || 1,20.51
bhaktartidāvānalamaprameyaṃdhyāyedumāliṃgitaviśvabhūṣaṇam  || 1,20.51
dhyāyennityaṃmaheśaṃrajatagirinibhaṃcārucaṃdrāvataṃsaṃratnākalpojjvalāṃgaṃparaśumṛgavarābhītihastaṃprasannam  || 1,20.52
padmāsīnaṃsamaṃtātsthitamamaragaṇairvyāghrakṛttiṃvasānaṃviśvādyaṃviśvabījaṃnikhilabhayaharaṃpañcavaktraṃtrinetram  || 1,20.52
itidhyātvācasaṃpūjyapārthivaṃliṃgamuttamam  || 1,20.53
japetpañcākṣaraṃmaṃtraṃgurudattaṃyathāvidhi  || 1,20.53
stutibhiścaivadeveśaṃstuvītapraṇamansudhīḥ  || 1,20.54
nānābhidhābhirviprendrāḥ paṭhedvaiśatarudriyam  || 1,20.54
tataḥ sākṣatapuṣpāṇigṛhītvāṃjalināmudā  || 1,20.55
prārthayecchaṃkaraṃbhaktyāmaṃtrairebhiḥsubhaktitaḥ  || 1,20.55
tāvakastvadguṇaprāṇastvaccittohaṃsadāmṛḍa  || 1,20.56
kṛpānidha itijñātvābhūtanāthaprasīdame  || 1,20.56
ajñānādyadivājñānājjapa pūjādikaṃ mayā  || 1,20.57
kṛtaṃ tadastusaphalaṃkṛpayātavaśaṃkara  || 1,20.57
ahaṃpāpīmahānadyapāvanaścabhavānmahān  || 1,20.58
itivijñāyagaurīśayadicchasitathākuru  || 1,20.58
vedaiḥ purāṇaiḥ siddhāntairṛṣibhirvividhairapi  || 1,20.59
najñātosimahādevakutohaṃtvaṃmahāśiva  || 1,20.59
yathā tathā tvadīyosmi sarvabhāvairmaheśvara  || 1,20.60
rakṣaṇīyastvayāhaṃ vai prasīda parameśvara  || 1,20.60
ityevaṃ cākṣatānpuṣpānāropya ca śivopari  || 1,20.61
praṇamedbhaktitaśśaṃbhuṃ sāṣṭāṃgaṃ vidhivanmune  || 1,20.61
tataḥ pradakṣiṇāṃ kuryādyathoktavidhinā sudhīḥ  || 1,20.62
punaḥ stuvīta deveśaṃ stutibhiḥ śraddhayānvitaḥ  || 1,20.62
tato galaravaṃkṛtvā praṇamecchucinamradhīḥ  || 1,20.63
kuryādvijñaptimādṛtya visarjanamathācaret || 1,20.63
ityuktā muniśārdūlāḥ pārthivārcā vidhānataḥ  || 1,20.64
bhuktidāmuktidācaivaśivabhaktivivardhinī  || 1,20.64
ityadhyāyaṃsucittenayaḥpaṭhecchṛṇuyādapi  || 1,20.65
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmāsarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 1,20.65
āyurāyogyadaṃcaivayaśasyaṃsvargyamevaca  || 1,20.66
putrapautrādisukhadamākhyānamidamuttamam  || 1,20.66

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe pra vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ sādhyasādhanakhaṇḍe pārthivaśivaliṃgapūjāvidhivarṇanaṃ nāma viṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 21
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
sūtasūtamahābhāgavyāsaśiṣyanamostute  || 1,21. 1
samyaguktaṃtvayātātapārthivārcāvidhānakam  || 1,21.1
kāmanābhedamāśrityasaṃkhyāṃbrūhividhānataḥ  || 1,21.2
śivapārthivaliṃgānāṃ kṛpayādīnavatsala  || 1,21.2
sūta uvāca
śṛṇudhvamṛṣayaḥ sarvepārthivārcāvidhānakam  || 1,21.3
yasyānuṣṭhānamātreṇakṛtakṛtyobhavennaraḥ  || 1,21.3
akṛtvā pārthivaṃ liṃgaṃ yonyadevaṃprapūjayet || 1,21.4
vṛthābhavatisāpūjādamadānādikaṃvṛthā  || 1,21.4
saṃkhyāpārthivaliṃgānāṃyathākāmaṃnigadyate  || 1,21.5
saṃkhyāsadyomuniśreṣṭhaniścayenaphalapradā  || 1,21.5
prathamāvāhanaṃtatrapratiṣṭhāpūjanaṃpṛthak || 1,21.6
liṃgākāraṃsamaṃtatrasarvaṃjñeyaṃpṛthakpṛthak || 1,21.6
vidyārthīpuruṣaḥprītyāsahasramitapārthivam  || 1,21.7
pūjayecchivaliṃgaṃhiniścayāttatphalapradam  || 1,21.7
naraḥpārthivaliṃgānāṃdhanārthīcatadardhakam  || 1,21.8
putrārthīsārdhasāhasraṃvastrārthīśatapañcakram  || 1,21.8
mokṣārthīkoṭiguṇitaṃbhūkāmaścasahasrakam  || 1,21.9
dayārthīcatrisāhasraṃtīrthārthīdvisahasrakam  || 1,21.9
suhṛtkāmītrisāhasraṃvaśyārthīśatamaṣṭakam  || 1,21.10
māraṇārthīsaptaśataṃmohanārthīśatāṣṭakam  || 1,21.10
uccāṭanaparaścaivasahasraṃcayathoktataḥ  || 1,21.11
staṃbhanārthīsahasraṃtudveṣaṇārthī tadardhakam  || 1,21.11
nigaḍānmuktikāmastusahasraṃsardhamuttamam  || 1,21.12
mahārājabhayepañcaśataṃjñeyaṃvicakṣaṇaiḥ  || 1,21.12
caurādisaṃkaṭejñeyaṃpārthivānāṃśatadvayam  || 1,21.13
ḍākinyādibhayepañcaśatamuktaṃjapārthivam  || 1,21.13
dāridryepañcasāhasramayutaṃsarvakāmadam  || 1,21.14
athanityavidhiṃvakṣyeśṛṇudhvaṃmunisattamāḥ  || 1,21.14
ekaṃpāpaharaṃproktaṃdviliṃgaṃcārthasiddhidam  || 1,21.15
triliṃgaṃsarvakāmānāṃkāraṇaṃparamīritam  || 1,21.15
uttarottaramevaṃsyātpūrvoktagaṇānāvidhi  || 1,21.16
matāṃtaramathovakṣyesaṃkhyāyāṃmunibhedataḥ  || 1,21.16
liṃgānāmayutaṃkṛtvāpārthivānāṃsubuddhimān  || 1,21.17
nirbhayohibhavennūnaṃmahārājabhayaṃharet || 1,21.17
kārāgṛhādimuktyarthamayutaṃkārayedbudhaḥ  || 1,21.18
ḍākinyādibhayesaptasahasraṃkārayettathā  || 1,21.18
sahasrāṇipañcapañcāśadaputraḥprakārayet || 1,21.19
liṃgānāmayutenaivakanyakāsaṃtatiṃlabhet || 1,21.19
liṃgānāmayutenaivaviṣṇvādaiśvaryamāpnuyāt || 1,21.20
liṃgānāṃprayutenaivahyatulāṃśriyamāpnuyāt || 1,21.20
koṭimekāṃtuliṃgānāṃ yaḥ karotinarobhuvi  || 1,21.21
śiva evabhavetsopinātrakāryā vicāraṇā  || 1,21.21
arcā pārthivaliṃgānāṃ koṭiyajñaphalapradā  || 1,21.22
bhuktidāmuktidānityaṃtataḥkāmarthināṃnṛṇām  || 1,21.22
vināliṃgārcanaṃ yasya kālogacchati nityaśaḥ  || 1,21.23
mahāhānirbhavettasyadurvṛttasyadurātmanaḥ  || 1,21.23
ekataḥ sarvadānāni vratāni vividhāni ca  || 1,21.24
tīrthāniniyamāyajñāliṃgārcācaikataḥ smṛtā  || 1,21.24
kalauliṃgārcanaṃśreṣṭhaṃtathālokepradṛśyate  || 1,21.25
tathānāstīti śāstrāṇāmeṣa siddhāntaniścayaḥ  || 1,21.25
bhuktimuktipradaṃliṃgaṃ vividhāpannivāraṇam  || 1,21.26
pūjayitvānaronityaṃ śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt || 1,21.26
śivānāmamayaṃ liṃgaṃnityaṃ pūjyaṃ maharṣibhiḥ  || 1,21.27
yataśca sarvaliṃgeṣu tasmātpūjyaṃvidhānataḥ  || 1,21.27
uttamaṃ madhyamaṃ nīcaṃ trividhaṃ liṃgamīritam  || 1,21.28
mānato muniśārdūlāstacchṛṇudhvaṃ vadāmyaham  || 1,21.28
caturaṃgulamucchrāyaṃramyaṃvedikayāyutam  || 1,21.29
uttamaṃliṃgamākhyātaṃmunibhiḥśāstrakovidaiḥ  || 1,21.29
tadardhaṃmadhyamaṃproktaṃtadardhamaghamaṃsmṛtam  || 1,21.30
itthaṃtrividhamākhyātamuttarottarataḥparam  || 1,21.30
anekaliṃgaṃyonityaṃbhaktiśraddhāsamanvitaḥ  || 1,21.31
pūjayetsalabhetkāmānmanasā mānasepsitān  || 1,21.31
naliṃgārādhanādanyatpuṇyaṃvedacatuṣṭaye  || 1,21.32
vidyatesarvaśāstrāṇāmeṣa evaviniścayaḥ  || 1,21.32
sarvametatparityajyakarmajālamaśeṣataḥ  || 1,21.33
bhaktyāparamayā vidvāṃlliṃgamekaṃprapūjayet || 1,21.33
liṃgercitercitaṃsarvaṃjagatsthāvarajaṃgamam  || 1,21.34
saṃsārāṃbudhimagnānāṃnānyattāraṇasādhanam  || 1,21.34
ajñānatimirāṃdhānāṃviṣayāsaktacetasām  || 1,21.35
plavonānyostijagatiliṃgārādhanamaṃtarā  || 1,21.35
haribrahmādayodevāmunayoyakṣarākṣasāḥ  || 1,21.36
gaṃdharvāścaraṇāssiddhādaiteyādānavāstathā  || 1,21.36
nāgāḥśeṣaprabhṛtayogaruḍādyāḥkhagāstathā  || 1,21.37
saprajāpatayaścānyemanavaḥkinnarānarāḥ  || 1,21.37
pūjayitvāmahābhaktyāliṃgaṃsarvārthasiddhidam  || 1,21.38
prāptāḥkāmānabhīṣṭāṃścatāṃstānsarvānhṛdisthitān  || 1,21.38
brāhmaṇaḥkṣatriyovaiśyaḥśūdrovāpratilomajaḥ  || 1,21.39
pūjayetsatataṃliṃgaṃtattanmaṃtreṇasādaram  || 1,21.39
kiṃbahūktenamunayaḥstrīṇāmapitathānyataḥ  || 1,21.40
adhikārostisarveṣāṃśivaliṃgārcanedvijāḥ  || 1,21.40
dvijānāṃvaidikenāpimārgeṇārādhanaṃvaram  || 1,21.41
anyeṣāmapijaṃtūnāṃvaidikenanasaṃmatam  || 1,21.41
vaidikānāṃdvijānāṃcapūjāvaidikamārgataḥ  || 1,21.42
kartavyānānyamārgeṇa ityāhabhagavāñchivaḥ  || 1,21.42
dadhīca gautamādīnāṃśāpenādagdhacetasām  || 1,21.43
dvijānāṃ jāyateśraddhānaiva vaidikakarmaṇi  || 1,21.43
yovaidikamanādṛtyakarmasmārtamathāpivā  || 1,21.44
anyatsamācarenmartyonasaṃkalpaphalaṃlabhet || 1,21.44
itthaṃkṛtvārcanaṃśaṃbhornaivedyāṃtaṃvidhānataḥ  || 1,21.45
pūjayedaṣṭamūrtīścatatraivatrijaganmayīḥ  || 1,21.45
kṣitirāponalovāyurākāśaḥ sūryasomakau  || 1,21.46
yajamāna iti tvaṣṭaumūrtayaḥparikīrtitāḥ  || 1,21.46
śarvobhavaśca rudraśca ugrobhīma itīśvaraḥ  || 1,21.47
mahādevaḥ paśupatiretānmūrtibhirarcayet || 1,21.47
pūjayetparivāraṃ catataḥśaṃbhoḥsubhaktitaḥ  || 1,21.48
īśānādikramāttatracaṃdanākṣatapatrakaiḥ  || 1,21.48
īśānaṃnandinaṃcaṃḍaṃmahākālaṃcabhṛṃgiṇam  || 1,21.49
vṛṣaṃskaṃdaṃkapardīśaṃsomaṃśukraṃcatatkramāt || 1,21.49
agratovīrabhadraṃ ca pṛṣṭhekīrtimukhaṃ tathā  || 1,21.50
tata ekādaśānnudrānpūjayedvidhinātataḥ  || 1,21.50
tataḥpañcākṣaraṃ japtvā śatarudriyameva ca  || 1,21.51
stutīrnānāvidhāḥ kṛtvā pañcāṃgapaṭhanaṃ tathā  || 1,21.51
tataḥ pradakṣiṇāṃ kṛtvā natvā liṃgaṃ visarjayet || 1,21.52
itiproktamaśeṣaṃ ca śivapūjanamādarāt || 1,21.52
rātrāvudaṅmukhaḥ kuryāddevakāryaṃsadaivahi  || 1,21.53
śivārcanaṃ sadāpyevaṃśuciḥ kuryādudaṅmukhaḥ  || 1,21.53
na prācīmagrataḥ śaṃbhornodīcīṃ śaktisaṃhitān  || 1,21.54
napratīcīṃyataḥ pṛṣṭhamatogrāhyaṃ samāśrayet || 1,21.54
vinābhasmatripuṃḍreṇa vinā rudrākṣamālayā  || 1,21.55
bilvapatraṃ vinā naiva pūjayecchaṃkaraṃ budhaḥ  || 1,21.55
bhasmāprāptaumuniśreṣṭhāḥ pravṛtte śivapūjane  || 1,21.56
tasmānmṛdāpi kartavyaṃ lalāṭe ca tripuṇḍrakam  || 1,21.56

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe prathamāyāṃ vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ sādhyasādhanakhaṇḍe pārthivapūjanavarṇanaṃ nāmaikaviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 22
ṛṣya ūcuḥ
agrāhyaṃ śivanaivedyamiti pūrvaṃ śrutaṃ vacaḥ  || 1,22.1
brūhi tannirṇayaṃ bilvamāhātmyamapi sanmune  || 1,22.1
sūta uvāca
śṛṇudhvaṃmunayaḥsarvesāvadhānatayādhunā  || 1,22. 2
sarvaṃvadāmisaṃprītyā dhanyā yūyaṃ śivavratāḥ  || 1,22.2
śivabhaktaḥ śuciḥ śuddhaḥ sadvratīdṛḍhaniścayaḥ  || 1,22.3
bhakṣayecchivanaivedyaṃtyajedagrāhyabhāvanām  || 1,22.3
dṛṣṭvāpi śivanaivedye yāṃti pāpāni dūrataḥ  || 1,22.4
bhaktetuśivanaivedyepuṇyānyā yāṃtikoṭiśaḥ  || 1,22.4
alaṃ yāgasahasreṇāpyalaṃ yāgārbudairapi  || 1,22.5
bhakṣite śivanaivedye śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt || 1,22.5
yadgṛheśivanaivedyapracāropiprajāyate  || 1,22.6
tadgṛhaṃpāvanaṃsarvamanyapāvanakāraṇam  || 1,22.6
āgataṃ śivanaivedyaṃ gṛhītvā śirasāmudā  || 1,22.7
bhakṣaṇīyaṃprayatne na śivasmaraṇapūrvakam  || 1,22.7
āgataṃ śivanaivedyamanyadā grāhyamityapi  || 1,22.8
vilaṃbepāpasaṃbaṃdhobhavatyeva hi mānave  || 1,22.8
na yasya śivanaivedyagrahaṇecchāprajāyate  || 1,22.9
sapāpiṣṭhogariṣṭhaḥsyānnarakaṃyātyapidhruvam  || 1,22.9
hṛdaye candrakānte ca svarṇarūpyādinirmite  || 1,22.10
śivadīkṣāvatābhaktenedaṃbhakṣyamitīryate  || 1,22.10
śivadīkṣānvitobhakto mahāprasādasaṃjñakam  || 1,22.11
sarveṣāmapi liṃgānāṃ naivedyaṃ bhakṣayecchubham  || 1,22.11
anyadīkṣāyujāṃnḥṇāṃśivabhaktiratātmanām  || 1,22.12
śṛṇudhvaṃnirṇayaṃprītyāśivanaivedyabhakṣaṇe  || 1,22.12
śālagrāmodbhave liṃge rasaliṃgetathā dvijāḥ  || 1,22.13
pāṣāṇe rājatesvarṇesurasiddhapratiṣṭhite  || 1,22.13
kāśmīre sphāṭikerātne jyotirliṃgeṣu sarvaśaḥ  || 1,22.14
cāndrāyaṇasamaṃ proktaṃ śaṃbhornaivedyabhakṣaṇam  || 1,22.14
brahmahāpi śucirbhūtvā nirmālyaṃyastudhārayet || 1,22.15
bhakṣayitvādrutaṃtasyasarvapāpaṃ praṇaśyati  || 1,22.15
caṃḍādhikāroyatrāstitadbhāktavyaṃnamānavaiḥ  || 1,22.16
caṃḍādhikāronoyatrabhoktavyaṃtaccabhaktitaḥ  || 1,22.16
bāṇaliṃgecalauhecasiddheliṃgesvayaṃbhuvi  || 1,22.17
pratimāsucasarvāsunacaṃḍodhikṛtobhavet || 1,22.17
snāpayitvāvidhānenayoliṃgasnāpanodakam  || 1,22.18
triḥpibettrividhaṃpāpaṃtasyehāśu vinaśyati  || 1,22.18
agrāhyaṃśivanaivedyaṃ patraṃpuṣpaṃphalaṃjalam  || 1,22.19
śālagrāmaśilāsaṃgātsarvaṃ yāti pavitritām  || 1,22.19
loṃgopari ca yaddravyaṃtadagrāhyaṃ munīśvarāḥ  || 1,22.20
supavitraṃ ca tajjñeyaṃ yalliṃgasparśabāhyataḥ  || 1,22.20
naivedyanirṇayaḥprokta itthaṃvomunisattamāḥ  || 1,22.21
śṛṇudhvaṃ bilvamāhātmyaṃ sāvadhānatayā 'darāt || 1,22.21
mahādevasvarūpoyaṃ bilvodevairapistutiḥ  || 1,22.22
yathākathaṃcidetasyamahimājñāyatekatham  || 1,22.22
puṇyatīrthāniyāvaṃtilokeṣuprathitānyapi  || 1,22.23
tānisarvāṇitīrthānibilvamūlevasaṃtihi  || 1,22.23
bilvamūle mahādevaṃ liṃgarūpiṇamavyayam  || 1,22.24
yaḥ pūjayati puṇyātmā sa śivaṃ prāpnuyāddhruvam  || 1,22.24
bilvamūle jalairyastu mūrdhānamabhiṣiṃcati  || 1,22.25
sa sarvatīrthasnātaḥsyātsa eva bhuvi pāvanaḥ  || 1,22.25
etasyabilvamūlasyāthālavālamanuttamam  || 1,22.26
jalākulaṃmahādevodṛṣṭvātuṣṭobhavatyalam  || 1,22.26
pūjayedvilvamūlaṃ yo gaṃdhapuṣpādibhirnaraḥ  || 1,22.27
śivalokamavāpnotisaṃtatirvardhate sukham  || 1,22.27
bilvamūle dīpamālāṃyaḥ kalpayati sādaram  || 1,22.28
satattvajñānasaṃpannomaheśāṃtargatobhavet || 1,22.28
bilvaśākhāṃ samādāya hastena navapallavam  || 1,22.29
gṛhītvāpūjayedbilvaṃsacapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 1,22.29
bilvamūle śivarataṃ bhojayedyastu bhaktitaḥ  || 1,22.30
ekaṃvākoṭiguṇitaṃtasyapuṇyaṃprajāyate  || 1,22.30
bilvamūle kṣīramuktamannamājyena saṃyutam  || 1,22.31
yo dadyācchivabhaktāya sa daridro na jāyate  || 1,22.31
sāṃgopāṃgamiti proktaṃ śivaliṃgaprapūjanam  || 1,22.32
pravṛttānāṃ nivṛttānāṃ bhedato dvividhaṃ dvijāḥ  || 1,22.32
pravṛttānāṃ pīṭhapūjāṃ sarvapūjāṃ samācaret || 1,22.33
abhiṣekānte naivedyaṃ śālyannena samācaret || 1,22.34
pūjānte sthāpayelliṃgaṃ puṭe śuddhe pṛthaggṛhe  || 1,22.34
karapūjānivṛttānāṃ svabhojyaṃ tu nivedayet || 1,22.35
nivṛttānāṃ paraṃsūkṣmaṃ liṃgameva viśiṣyate  || 1,22.35
vibhūtyabhyarcanaṃ kuryādvibhūtiṃ ca nivedayet || 1,22.36
pūjāṃ kṛtvā tathā liṃgaṃśirasādhārayetsadā  || 1,22.36

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ sādhyasādhanakhaṇḍe śivanaivedyavarṇanonāmadvāviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 23
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
sūtasūtamahābhāgavyāsaśiṣyanamostute  || 1,23.1
tadevavyāsatobrūhibhasmamāhātmyamuttamam  || 1,23.1
tathā rudrākṣamāhātmyaṃ nāma māhātmyamuttamam  || 1,23.2
tritayaṃ brūhisuprītyāmamānaṃdayacetasam  || 1,23.2
sūta uvāca
sādhupṛṣṭaṃbhavadbhiścalokānāṃhitakārakam  || 1,23.3
bhavaṃtovaimahādhanyāḥ pavitrāḥ kulabhūṣaṇāḥ  || 1,23.3
yeṣāṃcaivaśivaḥsākṣāddaivataṃparamaṃśubham  || 1,23.4
sadāśivakathālokevallabhābhavatāṃ sadā  || 1,23.4
tedhanyāścakṛtārthāścasaphalaṃdehadhāraṇam  || 1,23.5
uddhṛtañcakulaṃteṣāṃyeśivaṃsamupāsate  || 1,23.5
mukheyasyaśivanāmasadāśivaśivetica  || 1,23.6
pāpāninaspṛśaṃtyevakhadirāṃgāraṃkayathā  || 1,23.6
śrīśivāyanamastubhyaṃmukhaṃvyāharateyadā  || 1,23.7
tanmukhaṃpāvanaṃtīrthaṃsarvapāpavināśanam  || 1,23.7
tanmukhañcatathāyovaipaśyatiprītimānnaraḥ  || 1,23.8
tīrthajanyaṃphalaṃtasyabhavatītisuniścitam  || 1,23.8
yatratrayaṃsadātiṣṭhedetacchubhataraṃdvijā  || 1,23.9
tasyadarśanamātreṇaveṇīsnānaphalaṃlabhet || 1,23.9
śivanāmavibhūtiścatathārudrākṣa eva ca  || 1,23.10
etattrayaṃmahāpuṇyaṃtriveṇīsadṛśaṃsmṛtam  || 1,23.10
etattrayaṃśarīrecayasyatiṣṭhatinityaśaḥ  || 1,23.11
tasyaivadarśanaṃlokedurlabhaṃpāpahārakam  || 1,23.11
taddarśanaṃyathāveṇīnobhayoraṃtaraṃmanāk || 1,23.12
evaṃyonavijānātisapāpiṣṭhonasaṃśayaḥ  || 1,23.12
vibhūtiryasyanobhālenāṃgerudrākṣadhāraṇam  || 1,23.13
nāsye śivamayīvāṇītaṃtyajedadhamaṃyathā  || 1,23.13
śaivaṃnāmayathāgaṃgāvibhūtiryamunāmatā  || 1,23.14
rudrākṣaṃ vidhijāproktāsarvapāpāvināśinī  || 1,23.14
śarīrecatrayaṃyasyatatphalaṃcaikataḥsthitam  || 1,23.15
ekatoveṇikāyāścasnānajaṃtuphalaṃbudhaiḥ  || 1,23.15
tadevaṃtulitaṃpūrvaṃbrahmaṇāhitakāriṇā  || 1,23.16
samānaṃ caivatajjātaṃtasmāddhāryaṃsadābudhaiḥ  || 1,23.16
taddinaṃhisamārabhyabrahmaviṣṇvādibhiḥsaraiḥ  || 1,23.17
dhāryate tritayaṃtaccadarśanātpāpahārakam  || 1,23.17
ṛṣya ūcuḥ
īdṛśaṃhiphalaṃproktaṃnāmāditritayodbhavam  || 1,23.18
tanmāhātmyaṃviśeṣeṇavaktumarhasisuvrata  || 1,23.18
sūta uvāca
ṛṣayohimahāprājñāḥ sacchaivājñānināṃmarāḥ  || 1,23.19
tanmāhātmyaṃhisadbhaktyāśṛṇutādaratodvijāḥ  || 1,23.19
sugūḍhamapi śāstreṣupurāṇeśrutiṣvapi  || 1,23.20
bhavatsnehānmayā viprāḥprakāśaḥ kriyate 'dhunā  || 1,23.20
kastattritayamāhātmyaṃsaṃjānātidvijottamāḥ  || 1,23.21
maheśvaraṃvināsarvaṃbrahmāṇḍesadasatparam  || 1,23.21
vacmyahaṃnāmamāhātmyaṃyathābhaktisamāsataḥ  || 1,23.22
śṛṇutaprītitoviprāḥ sarvapāpaharaṃparam  || 1,23.22
śivetināmadāvāgnermahāpātakaparvatāḥ  || 1,23.23
bhasmībhavaṃtyanāyāsātsatyaṃsatyaṃnasaṃśayaḥ  || 1,23.23
pāpamūlāni duḥkhānivividhānyapiśaunaka  || 1,23.24
śivanāmaikanaśyāninānyanaśyānisarvathā  || 1,23.24
savaidikaḥ sapuṇyātmāsadhanyassabudhomataḥ  || 1,23.25
śivanāmajapāsaktoyonityaṃbhuvimānava  || 1,23.25
bhavaṃtivividhādharmāsteṣāṃsadyaḥphalonmukhāḥ  || 1,23.26
yeṣāṃbhavativiśvāsaḥśivanāmajapemune  || 1,23.26
pātakānivinaśyaṃtiyāvaṃtiśivanāmataḥ  || 1,23.27
bhuvitāvaṃtipāpānikriyaṃtenanarairmune  || 1,23.27
brahmahatyādipāpānāṃrāśīnapramitānmune  || 1,23.28
śivanāmadrutaṃproktaṃnāśayatyakhilānnaraiḥ  || 1,23.28
śivanāmatarīṃprāpyasaṃsārābdhiṃtaraṃtiye  || 1,23.29
saṃsāramūlapāpānitāninaśyaṃtyasaṃśayam  || 1,23.29
saṃsāramūlabhūtānāṃpātakānāṃmahāmune  || 1,23.30
śivanāmakuṭhāreṇavināśojāyatedhruvam  || 1,23.30
śivanāmāmṛtaṃpeyaṃpāpadāvānalārditaiḥ  || 1,23.31
pāpadāvāgnitaptānāṃśāṃtistenavinānahi  || 1,23.31
śivetināmapīyūṣavarṣavarṣadhārāpariplutāḥ  || 1,23.32
saṃsāradavamadhyepinaśocaṃtikadācana  || 1,23.32
śivanāmnimahadbhaktirjātāyeṣāṃmahātmanām  || 1,23.33
tadvidhānāṃtusahasāmuktirbhavatisarvathā  || 1,23.33
anekajanmabhiryenatapastaptaṃmunīśvara  || 1,23.34
śivanāmnibhavedbhaktiḥsarvapāpāpahāriṇī  || 1,23.34
yasyā sādhāraṇaṃ śaṃbhunāmnibhaktirakhaṃḍitā  || 1,23.35
tasyaivamokṣaḥ sulabhonānyasyetimatirmama  || 1,23.35
kṛtvāpyanekapāpāniśivanāmajapādaraḥ  || 1,23.36
sarvapāpavinirmuktobhavatyevanasaṃśayaḥ  || 1,23.36
bhavaṃtibhasmasādvṛkṣādavadagdhāyathāvane  || 1,23.37
tathātāvaṃtidagdhānipāpāniśivanāmataḥ  || 1,23.37
yonityaṃbhasmapūtāṃgaḥ śivanāmajapādaraḥ  || 1,23.38
saṃtaratyevasaṃsāraṃsaghoramapiśaunaka  || 1,23.38
brahmasvaharaṇaṃkṛtvāhatvāpibrāhmaṇānbahūn  || 1,23.39
nalipyatenaraḥ pāpaiḥśivanāmajapādaraḥ  || 1,23.39
vilokyavedānakhilāñchivanāmajapaḥparam  || 1,23.40
saṃsāratāraṇopāya itipūrvairviniścitaḥ  || 1,23.40
kiṃbahūktyāmuniśreṣṭhāḥ ślokenaikenavacmyaham  || 1,23.41
śivanāmnomahimānaṃsarvapāpāpahāriṇam  || 1,23.41
pāpānāṃharaṇeśaṃbhornāmaḥ śaktirhipāvanī  || 1,23.42
śaknotipātakaṃtāvatkartuṃnāpinaraḥ kvacit || 1,23.42
śivanāmaprabhāveṇalebhesadgatimuttamām  || 1,23.43
indradyumnanṛpaḥpūrvaṃmahāpāpaḥ purāmune  || 1,23.43
tathākāciddvijāyoṣāsaumunebahupāpinī  || 1,23.44
śivanāmaprabhāveṇalebhesadgatimuttamām  || 1,23.44
ityuktaṃvodvijaśreṣṭhānāmamāhātmyamuttamam  || 1,23.45
śṛṇudhvaṃbhasmamāhātmyaṃsarvapāvanapāvanam  || 1,23.45

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ sādhyasādhanakhaṃḍeśivanamamāhātmyavarṇanonāmatrayoviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 24
sūta uvāca
dvividhaṃbhasmasaṃproktaṃsarvamaṃgaladaṃparam  || 1,24.1
tatprakāramahaṃvakṣyesāvadhānatayāśṛṇu  || 1,24.1
ekaṃjñeyaṃ mahābhasmadvitīyaṃsvalpasaṃjñakam  || 1,24.2
mahābhasma itiproktaṃbhasmanānāvidhaṃparam  || 1,24.2
tadbhasmatrividhaṃproktaṃśrotaṃsmārtaṃcalaukikam  || 1,24.3
bhasmaiva svalpasajñāhibahudhāparikīrtitam  || 1,24.3
śrautaṃbhasmatathāsmārtaṃdvijānāmevakīrtitam  || 1,24.4
anyeṣāmapisarveṣāmaparaṃbhasmalaukikam  || 1,24.4
dhāraṇaṃmaṃtrataḥ proktaṃdvijānāṃmunipuṃgavaiḥ  || 1,24.5
kevalaṃdhāraṇaṃjñeyamanyeṣāṃmaṃtravarjitam  || 1,24.5
āgneyamucyatebhasmadagdhagomayasaṃbhavam  || 1,24.6
tadāpidravyamityuktaṃtripuṃḍrasyamahāmune  || 1,24.6
agnihotrotthitaṃbhasmasaṃgrāhyaṃvāmanīṣibhiḥ  || 1,24.7
anyayajñotthitaṃvāpitripuṇḍrasyacadhāraṇe  || 1,24.7
agnirityādibhirmaṃtrairjābālopaniṣadgateḥ  || 1,24.8
saptabhidhūlanaṃkāryaṃbhasmanāsajalenaca  || 1,24.8
varṇānāmāśramāṇāṃcamaṃtratomaṃtratopi ca  || 1,24.9
tripuṃḍroddhūlanaṃproktajābālairādareṇaca  || 1,24.9
bhasmanoddhūlanaṃcaivayathātiryaktripuṃḍrakam  || 1,24.10
pramādādapimokṣārthīnatyajeditiviśrutiḥ  || 1,24.10
śivenaviṣṇunācaivatathātiryaktripuṃḍrakam  || 1,24.11
umādevīcalakṣmīṃścavācānyābhiścanityaśaḥ  || 1,24.11
brāhmaṇaiḥ kṣatriyairvaiśyaiḥ śūdrairapicasaṃskaraiḥ  || 1,24.12
apabhraṃśairdhṛtaṃbhasmatripuṃḍroddhūlanātmanā  || 1,24.12
uddhūlanaṃtripuṃḍraṃcaśraddhayānācaraṃtiye  || 1,24.13
teṣāṃnāstisamācārovarṇāśramasamanvitaḥ  || 1,24.13
uddhūlanaṃtripuṃḍraṃcaśraddhayānācaraṃtiye  || 1,24.14
teṣāṃnāstivinimuṃktissaṃsārājjanmakoṭibhiḥ  || 1,24.14
uddhūlanaṃtripuṃḍraṃcaśraddhayānācarantiye  || 1,24.15
teṣāṃnāstiśivajñānaṃkalpakoṭiśatairapi  || 1,24.15
uddhūlanaṃtripuṃḍraṃ ca śraddhayānācarantiye  || 1,24.16
temahāpātakairyuktā itiśāstrīyanirṇayaḥ  || 1,24.16
uddhūlanaṃtripuṃḍraṃcaśraddhayānācaranti ye  || 1,24.17
teṣāmācaritaṃ sarvaṃviparītaphalāya hi  || 1,24.17
mahāpātakayuktānāṃ jaṃtūnāṃśarvavidviṣām  || 1,24.18
tripuṃḍroddhūlanadveṣojāyatesudṛḍhaṃ mune  || 1,24.18
śivāgnikāryaṃ yaḥ kṛtvākuryāttriyāyuṣātmavit || 1,24.19
mucyatesarvapāpaistuspṛṣṭenabhasmanānaraḥ  || 1,24.19
sitenabhasmanākuryāttrisandhyaṃyastripuṇḍrakam  || 1,24.20
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ śivenasahamodate  || 1,24.20
sitenabhasmanā kuryālāṭe tu tripuṇḍrakam  || 1,24.21
yosāvanādibhūtānhilokānāpto mṛtobhavet || 1,24.21
akṛtvā bhasmanāsnānaṃ na japedvaiṣaḍakṣaram  || 1,24.22
tripuṃḍraṃ ca racitvā tu vidhinābhasmanājapet || 1,24.22
adayo vādhamo vāpi sarvapāpānvitopi vā  || 1,24.23
uṣaḥpāpānvito vāpimūrkho vā patitopi vā  || 1,24.23
yasmindeśevasennityaṃbhūtiśāsanasaṃyutaḥ  || 1,24.24
sarvatīrthaiścakratubhiḥsāṃnidhyaṃkriyatesadā  || 1,24.24
tripuṃḍrasahito jīvaḥ pūjyaḥ sarvaiḥ surāsuraiḥ  || 1,24.25
pāpānvitopi śuddhātmā kiṃ punaḥ śraddhayā yutaḥ  || 1,24.25
yasmindeśe śivajñānī bhūtiśāsanasaṃyutaḥ  || 1,24.26
gato yadṛcchayādyāpi tasmistīrthāḥ samāgatāḥ  || 1,24.26
bahunātrakimuktenadhāryaṃbhasmasadā budhaiḥ  || 1,24.27
liṃgārcanaṃsadākāryaṃjapyomaṃtraḥṣaḍakṣaraḥ  || 1,24.27
brahmaṇāviṣṇunāvāpi rudreṇamunibhiḥ suraiḥ  || 1,24.28
bhasmadhāraṇamāhātmyaṃnaśakyaṃparibhāṣitum  || 1,24.28
iti varṇāśramācāroluptavarṇakriyopi ca  || 1,24.29
pāpātsakṛttripuṃḍrasyadhāraṇātsopimucyate  || 1,24.29
yebhasmadhāriṇaṃtyaktvākarmakurvaṃtimānavāḥ  || 1,24.30
teṣāṃ nāstivinirmokṣaḥ saṃsārājjanmakoṭibhiḥ  || 1,24.30
te nādhītaṃguroḥsarvaṃtenasarvamanuṣṭhitam  || 1,24.31
yenavipreṇaśirasitripuṃḍraṃbhasmanākṛtam  || 1,24.31
ye bhasmadhāriṇaṃ dṛṣṭvānarāḥ kurvaṃtitāḍanam  || 1,24.32
teṣāṃcaṃḍālatojanmabrahmannūhyaṃvipaścitā  || 1,24.32
mānastokenamaṃtreṇamaṃtritaṃbhasmadhārayet || 1,24.33
brāhmaṇaḥkṣatriyaścaiva prokteṣvaṃgeṣubhaktimān  || 1,24.33
vaiśyastriyaṃbakenaivaśūdraḥpañcākṣareṇatu  || 1,24.34
anyeṣāṃ vidhavāstrīṇāṃ vidhiḥ proktaścaśūdravat || 1,24.34
pañcabrahmādimanubhirgṛhasthasyavidhīyate  || 1,24.35
triyaṃbakenamanunāvidhirvaibrahmacāriṇaḥ  || 1,24.35
aghoreṇāthamanunāvipinasthavidhiḥsmṛtaḥ  || 1,24.36
yatistupraṇavenaiva tripuṃḍrādīnikārayet || 1,24.36
ativarṇāśramīnityaṃśivohaṃbhāvanātparāt || 1,24.37
śivayogī ca niyatamīśānenāpi dhārayet || 1,24.37
na tyājyaṃ sarvavarṇaiśca bhasmadhāraṇamuttamam  || 1,24.38
anyairapi yathājīvaissadeti śivaśāsanam  || 1,24.38
bhasmasnānenayāvaṃtaḥ kaṇāḥ svāṅgepratiṣṭhitāḥ  || 1,24.39
tāvaṃti śivaliṃgāni tanaudhattehidhārakaḥ  || 1,24.39
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyāvaiśyāḥ śūdrāścāpi ca saṃkarāḥ  || 1,24.40
striyothavidhavābālāḥ prāptāḥ pākhaṃḍikāstathā  || 1,24.40
brahmacārīgṛhīvanyaḥsaṃnyāsīvāvratītathā  || 1,24.41
nāryobhasmatripuṃḍrāṃkāmuktā evanasaṃśayaḥ  || 1,24.41
jñānadhṛjñānatovāpivahnidāhasamoyathā  || 1,24.42
jñānājñānadhṛtaṃbhasmapāvayetsakalaṃnaram  || 1,24.42
nāśnīyājjalamannamalpamapivā bhasmākṣadhṛtyāvinā bhuktvāvāthagṛhīvanīpatiyatirvarṇītathāsaṃkaraḥ  || 1,24.43
enobhuṅnarakaṃprayātisatadāgāyatrijāpenatadvarṇānāṃ tu yatestu mukhyapraṇavājapena muktaṃbhavet || 1,24.43
tripuṃḍraṃ ye viniṃdaṃti nindantiśivamevate  || 1,24.44
dhārayaṃticayebhaktyā dhārayanti tamevate  || 1,24.44
dhigbhasmarahitaṃ bhālaṃdhiggrāmamaśivālayam  || 1,24.45
dhiganīśārcanaṃ janmadhigvidyāmaśivāśrayām  || 1,24.45
yeniṃdaṃtimaheśvaraṃtrijagatāmādhārabhūtaṃharaṃ ye nindaṃtitripuṃḍradhāraṇakaraṃ doṣastu taddarśane  || 1,24.46
tevaisaṃkarasūkarāsurakharaśvakroṣṭukīṭopamājātā eva bhavaṃtipāpaparamāstenārakāḥ kevalam  || 1,24.46
tedṛṣṭvā śaśibhāskarauniśi dinesvapnepinokevalaṃpaśyaṃtuśrutirudrasūktajapatomucyetatenādṛtāḥ  || 1,24.47
satsaṃbhāṣaṇatobhaveddhinarakaṃnistāravānāsthitaṃyebhasmādividhāraṇaṃhipuruṣaṃniṃdaṃtimaṃdāhite  || 1,24.47
natāṃtrikastvadhikṛtonordhvapuṃḍradharomune  || 1,24.48
saṃtaptacakracihnotraśivayajñebahiṣkṛtaḥ  || 1,24.48
tatraitebahavolokābṛhajjābālacoditāḥ  || 1,24.49
tevicāryāḥprayatnenatatobhasmaratobhavet || 1,24.49
yaccaṃdanaiścaṃdanakepimiśraṃdhāryaṃhibhasmaivatripuṃḍrabhasmanā  || 1,24.50
vibhūtibhāloparikiṃcanāpidhāryaṃsadānoyadisaṃtibuddhayaḥ  || 1,24.50
strībhistripuṇḍramalakāvadhi dhāraṇīyaṃ bhasmadvijādibhiratho vidhavābhirevam  || 1,24.51
tadvatsadāśramavatāṃ viśadāvibhūtirdhāryāpavargaphaladāsakalāghahantrī  || 1,24.51
tripuṇḍraṃkuruteyastu bhasmanā vidhipūrvakam  || 1,24.52
mahāpātakasaṃghātairmucyate copapātakaiḥ  || 1,24.52
brahmacārīgṛhasthovāvānaprasthothavāyatiḥ  || 1,24.53
brahmakṣattrāścaviṭśūdrāstathānye patitādhamāḥ  || 1,24.53
uddhūlanaṃtripuṃḍraṃ ca dhṛtvāśuddhābhavaṃtica  || 1,24.54
bhasmanovidhinā samyakpāparāśiṃvihāya ca  || 1,24.54
bhasmadhārī viśeṣeṇa strīgohatyādipātakaiḥ  || 1,24.55
vīrahatyāśvahatyābhyāṃ mucyatenātra saṃśayaḥ  || 1,24.55
paradravyāpaharaṇaṃ paradārābhimarśanam  || 1,24.56
paranindā parakṣetraharaṇaṃ parapīḍanam  || 1,24.56
sasyārāmādiharaṇaṃ gṛhadāhādikarma ca  || 1,24.57
gohiraṇyamahiṣyāditilakambalavāsasām  || 1,24.57
annadhānyajalādīnāṃ nīcebhyaścaparigrahaḥ  || 1,24.58
daśaveśyāmataṃgīṣu vṛṣalīṣu naṭīṣu ca  || 1,24.58
rajasvalāsu kanyāsu vidhavāsu ca maithunam  || 1,24.59
māṃsacarmarasādīnāṃ lavaṇasya ca vikrayaḥ  || 1,24.59
paiśunyaṃ kūṭavādaśca sākṣimithyābhilāṣiṇām  || 1,24.60
evamādīnyasaṃkhyāni pāpāni vidhāni ca  || 1,24.60
sadya eva vinaśyaṃti tripuṃḍrasya ca dhāraṇāt || 1,24.60
śivadravyāpaharaṇaṃśivaniṃdācakutracit || 1,24.61
niṃdācaśivabhaktānāṃprāyaścittairnaśuddhyati  || 1,24.61
rudrākṣaṃ yasya gātreṣu lalāṭe tu tripaṃḍrakam  || 1,24.62
sacāṃḍālopisaṃpūjyassarvavarṇottamottamaḥ  || 1,24.62
yānitīrthānilokesmingaṃgādyāssaritaścayoḥ  || 1,24.63
snātobhavatisarvatralalāṭeyastripuṃḍrakam  || 1,24.63
saptakoṭi mahāmaṃtrāḥ pañcākṣarapurassarāḥ  || 1,24.64
tathānye koṭiśo maṃtrāḥ śaivakaivalyahetavaḥ  || 1,24.64
anye maṃtrāśca devānāṃ sarvasaukhyakarāmune  || 1,24.65
te sarve tasya vaśyāḥ syuryo bibharti tripuṃḍrakam  || 1,24.65
sahasraṃ pūrvajātānāṃ sahasraṃ janayiṣyatām  || 1,24.66
svavaṃśajānāṃ jñātīnāmuddharedyastripuṇḍrakṛt || 1,24.66
iha bhuktvākhilānbhogāndīrghāyurvyādhivarjitaḥ  || 1,24.67
jīvitāṃte ca maraṇaṃ sukhenaiva prapadyate  || 1,24.67
aṣṭaiśvaryaguṇopetaṃprāpyadivyavapuḥ śivam  || 1,24.68
divyaṃvimānamāruhyadivyatridaśasevitam  || 1,24.68
vidyādharāṇāṃsarveṣāṃgaṃdharvāṇāṃmahaujasām  || 1,24.69
iṃdrādilokapālānāṃlokeṣucayathākramam  || 1,24.69
bhuktvā bhogānsuvipulānprajeśānāṃ padeṣu ca  || 1,24.70
brahmaṇaḥ padamāsādyatatrakanyāśataṃlamet || 1,24.70
tatra brahmāyuṣomānaṃbhuktvābhogānanekaśaḥ  || 1,24.71
viṣṇorlokelabhedbhogaṃyāvadbrahmaśatātyayaḥ  || 1,24.71
śivalokaṃ tataḥ prāpya labdhveṣṭaṃ kāmamakṣayam  || 1,24.72
śivasāyujyamāpnotisaṃśayonātrajāyate  || 1,24.72
sarvopaniṣadāṃsāraṃsamālokyamuhurmuhuḥ  || 1,24.73
idameva hi nirṇītaṃ paraṃ śreyastripuṃḍrakam  || 1,24.73
vibhūtiṃniṃdateyovaibrāhmaṇaḥ sonyajātakaḥ  || 1,24.74
yāticanarakeghoreyāvadbrahmā caturmukhaḥ  || 1,24.74
śrāddheyajñejapehomevaiśvadevesurārcane  || 1,24.75
dhṛtatripuṃḍraḥpūtātmāmṛtyuṃjayatimānavaḥ  || 1,24.75
jalasnānaṃ malatyāgebhasmasnānaṃsadāśuci  || 1,24.76
maṃtrasnānaṃharetpāpaṃjñānasnāneparaṃpadam  || 1,24.76
sarvatīrtheṣuyatpuṇyaṃsarvatīrtheṣuyatphalam  || 1,24.77
tatphalaṃsamavāpnotibhasmasnānakaronaraḥ  || 1,24.77
bhasmasnānaṃ paraṃ tīrthaṃgaṃgāsnānaṃdinedine  || 1,24.78
bhasmarūpīśivaḥsākṣādbhasma trailokyapāvanam  || 1,24.78
natadūnaṃnataddhyānaṃnataddānaṃjaponasaḥ  || 1,24.79
tripuṃḍreṇavināyena vipreṇa yadanuṣṭhitam  || 1,24.79
vānaprasthasyakanyānāṃdīkṣāhīnanṛṇāṃ tathā  || 1,24.80
madhyāhnātprāgjalairyuktaṃparatojalavarjitam  || 1,24.80
evaṃ tripuṃḍraṃ yaḥ kuryānnityaṃniyatamānasaḥ  || 1,24.81
śivabhaktaḥ savijñeyo bhuktimuktiṃ ca viṃdati  || 1,24.81
yasyāṃgenaivarudrākṣa ekopibahupuṇyadaḥ  || 1,24.82
tasyajanmanirarthaṃsyāttripuṃḍrarahito yadi  || 1,24.82
evaṃtripuṃḍramāhātmyaṃsamāsātkathitaṃmayā  || 1,24.83
rahasyaṃsarvajaṃtūnāṃ gopanīyamidaṃ tvayā  || 1,24.83
tisrorekhābhavaṃtyevasthāneṣumunipuṃgavāḥ  || 1,24.84
lalāṭādiṣusarveṣuyathokteṣubudhairmune  || 1,24.84
bhruvormadhyaṃsamārabhyayāvadaṃtobhavedbhruvoḥ  || 1,24.85
tāvatpramāṇaṃsaṃdhāryaṃ lalāṭe ca tripuṃḍrakam  || 1,24.85
madhyamānāmikāṃgulyāmadhyetupratilomataḥ  || 1,24.86
aṃguṣṭhena kṛtārekhā tripuṃḍrākhyā bhidhīyate  || 1,24.86
madhyeṃgulibhirādāya tisṛbhirbhasmayatnataḥ  || 1,24.87
tripuṇḍradhārayedbhaktyābhuktimuktipradaṃparam  || 1,24.87
tisṛṇāmapi rekhānāṃ pratyekaṃnavadevatāḥ  || 1,24.88
sarvatrāṃgeṣutāvakṣyesāvadhānatayā śṛṇu  || 1,24.88
akāro gārhapatyāgnirbhūdharmaśca rajoguṇaḥ  || 1,24.89
ṛgvedaśca kriyāśaktiḥ prātaḥsavanameva ca  || 1,24.89
mahadevaśca rekhāyāḥ prathamāyāśca devatā  || 1,24.90
vijñeyā muniśārdūlāḥ śivadīkṣāparāyaṇaiḥ  || 1,24.90
ukāro dakṣiṇāgniśca nabhastattvaṃ yajustathā  || 1,24.91
madhyaṃdinaṃ ca savanamicchāśaktyaṃtarātmakau  || 1,24.91
maheśvaraśca rekhāyā dvitīyāyāśca devatā  || 1,24.92
vijñeyāmuniśārdūla śivadīkṣāparāyaṇaiḥ  || 1,24.92
makārāhavanīyau ca paramātmā tamodivau  || 1,24.93
jñānaśaktiḥ sāmavedastṛtīyaṃ savanaṃ tathā  || 1,24.93
śivaścaiva ca rekhāyāstṛtiyāyāśca devatā  || 1,24.94
vijñeyāmuniśārdūla śivadīkṣāparāyaṇau  || 1,24.94
evaṃ nityaṃ namaskṛtya sadbhaktyā sthānadevatāḥ  || 1,24.95
tripuṃḍraṃ dhārayecchuddho bhuktiṃmuktiṃ ca viṃdati  || 1,24.95
ityuktāḥsthānadevāścasarvāṃgeṣumuniśvara  || 1,24.96
teṣāṃ saṃbaṃdhinobhaktyāsthānāniśṛṇusāṃpratam  || 1,24.96
dvātriṃśatsthānake vārdhaṣoḍaśasthānakepi ca  || 1,24.97
aṣṭasthāne tathā caiva pañcasthānepi nānyaset || 1,24.97
uttamāṃge lalāṭe ca karṇayornetrayostathā  || 1,24.98
nāsāvaktragaleṣvevaṃhastadvaya ataḥparam  || 1,24.98
kūrpare maṇibaṃdhecahṛdayepārśvayordvayoḥ  || 1,24.99
nābhaumuṣkadvayecaivamūrvorgulphecajānuni  || 1,24.99
jaṃghādvayepadadvandvedvātriṃśatsthānamuttamam  || 1,24.100
agnyabbhūvāyudigdeśadikpālānvasubhiḥ saha  || 1,24.100
dharādhruvaścasomaśca apaścevānilonalaḥ  || 1,24.101
pratyūṣaśca prabhāsaścavasavoṣṭaprakīrtitāḥ  || 1,24.101
eteṣāṃnāmamātreṇatripuṃḍraṃdhārayedbudhāḥ  || 1,24.102
kuryādvāṣoḍaśasthānetripuṇḍraṃtusamāhitaḥ  || 1,24.102
śīrṣakecalalāṭecakaṃṭhecāṃsadvayebhuje  || 1,24.103
kūrparemaṇibaṃdhecahṛdayenābhipārśvake  || 1,24.103
pṛṣṭhecaivaṃpratiṣṭhāyayajettatrāśvidaivate  || 1,24.104
śivaśaktiṃtathārudramīśaṃnāradamevaca  || 1,24.104
vāmādinavaśaktīśca etāḥ ṣoḍaśadevatāḥ  || 1,24.105
nāsatyodasrakaścaiva aśvinaudvauprakīrtitau  || 1,24.105
athavāmūrdhnikeśecakarmayorvadanetathā  || 1,24.106
bāhudvaye ca hṛdayenābhyāmūruyugetathā  || 1,24.106
jānudvayecapadayoḥ pṛṣṭhabhāge ca ṣoḍaśa  || 1,24.107
śivaścandraśca rudraḥko vighneśoviṣṇureva vā  || 1,24.107
śrīścaivahṛdayeśambhustathānābhauprajāpatiḥ  || 1,24.108
nāgaścanāgakanyāśca ubhayorṛṣikanyakāḥ  || 1,24.108
pādayoścasamudrāścatīrthāḥ pṛṣṭhe viśālataḥ  || 1,24.109
ityeva ṣoḍaśa sthānamathocyate  || 1,24.109
guhyasthānaṃ lalāṭaśca karṇadvayamanuttamam  || 1,24.110
aṃsayugmaṃ ca hṛdayaṃ nābhirityevamaṣṭakam  || 1,24.110
brahmā ca ṛṣayaḥ saptadevatāścaprakīrtitāḥ  || 1,24.111
ityevaṃ tu samuddiṣṭaṃ bhasmavidbhirmunīśvarāḥ  || 1,24.111
atha vā mastakaṃ bāhūhṛdayaṃ nābhireva ca  || 1,24.112
pañcasthānānyamūnyāhurdhāraṇe bhasmavijjanāḥ  || 1,24.112
yathāsaṃbhavanaṃ kuryāddeśakālādyapekṣayā  || 1,24.113
uddhūlanepyaśaktiścettripuṇḍrādīni kārayet || 1,24.113
trinetraṃtriguṇādhāraṃtrivedajanakaṃśivam  || 1,24.114
smarannamaḥ śivāyeti lalāṭe tu tripuṇḍrakam  || 1,24.114
īśābhyāṃnama ityuktvāpārśvayośca tripuṇḍrakam  || 1,24.115
bījābhyāṃ nama ityuktvādhārayettuprakoṣṭhayoḥ  || 1,24.115
kuryādadhaḥ pitṛbhyāṃ ca umeśābhyāṃtathopari  || 1,24.116
bhīmāyetitataḥpṛṣṭheśirasaḥpaścimetathā  || 1,24.116

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ bhasmadhāraṇavarṇanonāma caturviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 25
sūta uvāca
śainakarṣe mahāprājñaśivarūpamahāpate  || 1,25.1
śṛṇurudrākṣamāhātmyaṃ samāsātkathayāmyaham  || 1,25.1
śivapriyatamojñeyo rudrākṣaḥ parapāvanaḥ  || 1,25.2
darśanātsparśanājjāpyātsarvapāpaharaḥsmṛtaḥ  || 1,25.2
purārudrākṣamahimādevyagrekathitomune  || 1,25.3
lokopakaraṇārthāyaśivena paramātmanā  || 1,25.3
śiva uvāca
śṛṇudevimaheśānirudrākṣamahimāśive  || 1,25.4
kathayāmitavaprītyābhaktānāṃhitakāmyayā  || 1,25.4
divyavarṣasahasrāṇi maheśānipunaḥpurā  || 1,25.5
tapaḥprakurvatastrastaṃmanaḥ saṃyamyavai mama  || 1,25.5
svataṃtreṇa pareśena lokopakṛtikāriṇā  || 1,25.6
līlayāparameśāni cakṣurunmīlitaṃmayā  || 1,25.6
puṭābhyāṃcārucakṣurbhyāṃpatitājalabiṃdavaḥ  || 1,25.7
tatrāśrubindavojātāvṛkṣārudrākṣasaṃjñakāḥ  || 1,25.7
sthāvaratvamanuprāpyabhaktānugrahakāraṇāt || 1,25.8
te dattāviṣṇubhaktebhyaścaturvarṇebhya eva ca  || 1,25.8
bhūmaugauṃḍodbhavāṃścakrerudrākṣāñchivavallabhān  || 1,25.9
mathurāyāmayodhyāyālaṃkāyāṃmalaye tathā  || 1,25.9
sahyādraucatathākāśyāṃdaśeṣvanyeṣuvātathā  || 1,25.10
parānasahyapāpaughabhedanāñchrutinodanāt || 1,25.10
brāhmaṇāḥkṣatriyāvaiśyāḥśūdrājātāmamājñayā  || 1,25.11
rudrākṣāstepṛthivyāṃtutajjātīyāḥ śubhākṣakāḥ  || 1,25.11
śvetaraktāḥ pītakṛṣṇāvarṇājñeyāḥ kramādbudhaiḥ  || 1,25.12
svajātīyaṃnṛbhirdhāryaṃ rudrākṣaṃvarṇataḥ kramāt || 1,25.12
varṇaistutatphalaṃdhāryaṃbhuktimuktiphalepsubhiḥ  || 1,25.13
śivabhaktairviśeṣeṇaśivayoḥ prītayesadā  || 1,25.13
dhātrīphalapramāṇaṃyacchreṣṭhametadudāhṛtam  || 1,25.14
badarīphalamātraṃtumadhyamaṃsaṃprakīrtitam  || 1,25.14
adhamaṃcaṇamātraṃsyātprakriyaiṣāparocyate  || 1,25.15
śṛṇupārvatisuprītyābhaktānāṃhitakāmyayā  || 1,25.15
badarīphalamātraṃcayatsyātkilamaheśvari  || 1,25.16
tathāpiphaladaṃlokesukhasaubhāgyavardhanam  || 1,25.16
dhātrīphalasamaṃyatsyātsarvāriṣṭavināśanam  || 1,25.17
guṃjayāsadṛśaṃyatsyātsarvārthaphalasādhanam  || 1,25.17
yathāyathālaghuḥsyādvaitathādhikaphalapradam  || 1,25.18
ekaikataḥphalaṃproktaṃdaśāṃśairadhikaṃbudhaiḥ  || 1,25.18
rudrākṣadhāraṇaṃproktaṃpāpanāśanahetave  || 1,25.19
tasmāccadhāraṇīyovaisarvārthasādhanodhruvam  || 1,25.19
yathācadṛśyatelokerudrākṣaphaladaḥ śubhaḥ  || 1,25.20
natathādṛśyate 'nyācamālikāparameśvari  || 1,25.20
samāḥsnigdhādṛḍhāḥsthūlāḥkaṃṭakaiḥsaṃyutāḥśubhāḥ  || 1,25.21
rudrākṣāḥkāmadādevibhuktimuktipradāḥ sadā  || 1,25.21
krimiduṣṭaṃchinnabhinnaṃkaṃṭakairhīnamevaca  || 1,25.22
vraṇayuktamavṛttaṃcarudrākṣānṣaḍvivarjayet || 1,25.22
svayamevakṛtadvāraṃrudrākṣaṃsyadihottamam  || 1,25.23
yattupauruṣayatnenakṛtaṃtanmadhyamaṃbhavet || 1,25.23
rudrākṣadhāraṇaṃprāptaṃmahāpātakanāśanam  || 1,25.24
rudrasaṃkhyāśataṃdhṛtvarudrarūpobhavennaraḥ  || 1,25.24
ekādaśaśatānīhadhṛtvāyatphalamāpyate  || 1,25.25
tatphalaṃśakyatenaiva vaktuṃ varṣaśatairapi  || 1,25.25
śatārdhenayuteḥ pañcaśatairvai mukuṭaṃ matam  || 1,25.26
rudrākṣairviracetsamyagbhaktimānpuruṣo varaḥ  || 1,25.26
tribhiḥ śataiḥ ṣaṣṭiyuktaistrirāvṛttyā tathā punaḥ  || 1,25.27
rudrākṣairupavītaṃ va nirmīyādbhaktitatparaḥ  || 1,25.27
śikhāyāṃcatrayaṃproktaṃrudrakṣāṇāṃ maheśvari  || 1,25.28
karṇayoḥ ṣaṭca ṣaṭcaivavāmadakṣiṇayostathā  || 1,25.28
śatamekottaraṃkaṃṭhebāhvorvairudrasaṃkhyayā  || 1,25.29
kūrparadvārayostatramaṇibaṃdhetathāpunaḥ  || 1,25.29
upavītetrayaṃdhāryaṃśivabhaktiratairnaraiḥ  || 1,25.30
śeṣānurvaritānpañcasammitāndhārayetkaṭau  || 1,25.30
etatsaṃkhyādhṛtāyenarudrākṣāḥ parameśvari  || 1,25.31
tadrūpaṃ tu praṇamyaṃhistutyaṃsarvairmaheśavat || 1,25.31
evaṃbhūtaṃsthitaṃdhyāneyadākṛtvāsanairjanam  || 1,25.32
śivetivyāharaṃścaivadṛṣṭvāpāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 1,25.32
śatādikasahasrasyavidhireṣaprakīrtitaḥ  || 1,25.33
tadabhāveprakāronyaḥ śubhaḥ saṃprocyatemayā  || 1,25.33
śikhāyāmekarudrākṣaṃśirasātriṃśataṃvahet || 1,25.34
pañcāśaccagaledadhyādbāhvoḥ ṣoḍaśaṣoḍaśa  || 1,25.34
maṇibaṃdhe dvādaśadviskaṃdhe pañcaśataṃvahet || 1,25.35
aṣṭottaraśatairmālyamupavītaṃprakalpayet || 1,25.35
evaṃsahasrarudrākṣāndhārayedyodṛḍhavrataḥ  || 1,25.36
taṃnamaṃtisurāḥsarveyathārudrastathaiva saḥ  || 1,25.36
ekaṃśikhāyāṃrudrākṣaṃcatvāriṃśattumastake  || 1,25.37
dvātriṃśatkaṇṭhadeśetuvakṣasyaṣṭottaraṃśatam  || 1,25.37
ekaikaṃkarṇayoḥ ṣaṭṣaḍbāhvoḥṣoḍaśaṣoḍaśa  || 1,25.38
karayoravimānenadviguṇenamunīśvara  || 1,25.38
saṃkhyāprītirdhṛyāyenasopiśaivajanaḥparaḥ  || 1,25.39
śivavatpūjanīyohivaṃdyassarvairabhīkṣṇaśaḥ  || 1,25.39
śirasīśānamaṃtreṇakarṇetatpuruṣeṇa ca  || 1,25.40
aghoreṇagaledhāryaṃtenaivahṛdayepi ca  || 1,25.40
aghorabījamaṃtreṇa karayordhārayetsudhīḥ  || 1,25.41
pañcadaśākṣagrathitāṃvāmadevenacodare  || 1,25.41
pañcabrahmabhiraṃgaścatrimālāṃ pañcasapta ca  || 1,25.42
athavāmūlamaṃtreṇa sarvānakṣāṃstudhārayet || 1,25.42
madyaṃmāṃsaṃtulaśunaṃpalāṇḍuṃśigrumeva ca  || 1,25.43
śleṣmāṃtakaṃ viḍvarāhaṃbhakṣaṇe varjayettataḥ  || 1,25.43
valakṣaṃrudrākṣaṃdvijatanubhirevehavihitaṃsuraktaṃkṣatrāṇāṃpramuditamumepītamasakṛt || 1,25.44

chinnaṃ khaṃḍitaṃ bhinnaṃ vidīrṇa

tatovaiśyairdhāyapratidivasabhāvaśyakamahotathākṛṣṇaṃśūdraiḥśrutigaditamārgoyamagaje  || 1,25.44
varṇīvanīgṛhayatīrniyamenadadhyādetadrahasyaparamonahijātutiṣṭhet || 1,25.45
rudrākṣadhāraṇamidaṃsukṛtaiścalabhyaṃtyaktvedametadakhilānnarakānprayāṃti  || 1,25.45
ādāvāmalakātsvato laghutarā rugṇāstataḥkaṃṭakaiḥsaṃdaṣṭāḥkrimibhistanūpakaraṇacchidreṇa hīnāstathā  || 1,25.46
dhāryānaiva śubhepsubhiścaṇakavadrudrākṣamapyaṃtatorudrākṣomamaliṃgamaṃgalamumesūkṣmaṃpraśastaṃsadā  || 1,25.46
sarvāśramāṇāṃvarṇānāṃstrīśūdraṇāṃśivājñayā  || 1,25.47
dhāryāḥ sadaivarudrākṣāyatīnāṃ praṇavena hi  || 1,25.47
divābibhradrātrikṛtairātrauvibhraddivākṛtaiḥ  || 1,25.48
prātarmadhyāhnasāyāhnemucyatesarvapātakaiḥ  || 1,25.48
ye tripuṇḍra dharāloke jaṭādhāriṇa eva ye  || 1,25.49
ye rudrākṣadharāstevaiyamalokaṃprayāṃtina  || 1,25.49
rudrākṣamekaṃśirasābibhartitathātripuṇḍraṃcalalāṭamadhye  || 1,25.50
pañcākṣaraṃyehijapaṃtimaṃtraṃpūjyābhavadbhiḥ khalutehisādhavaḥ  || 1,25.50
yasyāṅgenāstirudrākṣastripuṇḍraṃbhālapaṭṭake  || 1,25.51
mukhepañcākṣaraṃnāstitamānayayamālayam  || 1,25.51
jñātvājñātvātatprabhāvaṃbhasmarudrākṣadhāriṇaḥ  || 1,25.52
tepūjyāḥ sarvadāsmākaṃnonetavyāḥ kadācana  || 1,25.52
evamājñāpayāmāsakālopinijakiṅkarān  || 1,25.53
tathetimattvātesarvetūṣṇīmāsansuvismitāḥ  || 1,25.53
ata evamahādevirudrākṣotyaghanāśanaḥ  || 1,25.54
taddharomatpriyaḥ śuddho 'tyaghavānapipārvati  || 1,25.54
hastebāhautathāmūrdhnirudrākṣaṃdhārayettuyaḥ  || 1,25.55
avadhyaḥsarvabhūtānāṃrudrarūpīcaredbhuvi  || 1,25.55
surāsurāṇāṃsarveṣāṃ vaṃdanīyaḥsadāsavai  || 1,25.56
pūjanīyo hi dṛṣṭasyapāpahācayathāśivaḥ  || 1,25.56
dhyānajñānāvamuktopirudrākṣaṃdhārayettuyaḥ  || 1,25.57
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥsayātiparamāṃgatim  || 1,25.57
rudrākṣeṇajapanmantraṃ puṇyaṃkoṭi guṇaṃ bhavet || 1,25.58
daśakoṭiguṇaṃpuṇyaṃdhāraṇāllabhate naraḥ  || 1,25.58
yāvatkālaṃhijīvasyaśarīrasthobhavetsavai  || 1,25.59
tāvatkālaṃsvalpamṛtyurnataṃdevibādhate  || 1,25.59
tripuṃḍreṇacasaṃyuktaṃrudrākṣāvilasāṃgakam  || 1,25.60
mṛtyuṃjayaṃjapaṃtaṃcadṛṣṭvārudraphalaṃlabhet || 1,25.60
pañcadevapriyaścaivasarvadevapriyastathā  || 1,25.61
sarvamantrāñjapedbhaktorudrākṣamālayāpriye  || 1,25.61
viṣṇvādidevabhaktāścadhārayeyurnasaṃśayaḥ  || 1,25.62
rudrabhakto viśeṣeṇarudrākṣāndhārayetsadā  || 1,25.62
rudrākṣāvividhāḥproktāsteṣāṃ bhedānvadāmyaham  || 1,25.63
śṛṇupārvati sadbhaktyā bhuktimuktiphalapradān  || 1,25.63
ekavaktraḥ śivaḥ sākṣādbhuktimuktiphalapradaḥ  || 1,25.64
tasyadarśanamātreṇabrahmahatyāvyapohati  || 1,25.64
yatrasaṃpūjitastatralakṣmīrdūratarā nahi  || 1,25.65
naśyaṃtyupadravāḥsarvesarvakāmābhavaṃtihi  || 1,25.65
dvivaktrodevadeveśassarvakāmaphalapradaḥ  || 1,25.66
viśeṣataḥ sarudrākṣogovadhaṃnāśayeddrutam  || 1,25.66
trivaktroyohirudrākṣaḥ sākṣātsādhanadassadā  || 1,25.67
tatprabhāvādbhaveyurvaividyāḥ sarvāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ  || 1,25.67
caturvaktraḥ svayaṃbrahmānarahatyāṃvyapohati  || 1,25.68
darśanātsparśanātsadyaścaturvargaphalapradaḥ  || 1,25.68
pañcavaktraḥ svayaṃrudraḥ kālāgnirnāmataḥ prabhuḥ  || 1,25.69
sarvamuktipradaścaivasarvakāmaphalapradaḥ  || 1,25.69
agamyāgamanaṃpāpamabhakṣyasya ca bhakṣaṇam  || 1,25.70
ityādi sarvapāpāni pañcavaktrovyapohati  || 1,25.70
ṣaḍvaktraḥkārtikeyastudhāraṇāddakṣiṇebhuje  || 1,25.71
brahmahatyādikaiḥ pāpairmucyatenātra saṃśayaḥ  || 1,25.71
saptavaktromaheśāni hyanaṃgonāmanāmataḥ  || 1,25.72
dhāraṇāttasya deveśidaridropīśvarobhavet || 1,25.72
rudrākṣaścāṣṭavaktraśca vasumūrtiśca bhairavaḥ  || 1,25.73
dhāraṇāttasya pūrṇāyurmṛto bhavati śūlabhṛt || 1,25.73
bhairavonavavaktraśca kapilaścamuniḥsmṛtaḥ  || 1,25.74
durgāvātadadhiṣṭhātrī navarūpāmaheśvarī  || 1,25.74
taṃ dhārayedvāmahaste rudrākṣaṃbhaktitatparaḥ  || 1,25.75
sarveśvarobhavennūnaṃmama tulyo na saṃśayaḥ  || 1,25.75
daśavaktro maheśāni svayaṃ devo janārdanaḥ  || 1,25.76
dhāraṇāttasya deveśi sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 1,25.76
ekādaśamukhoyasturudrākṣaḥparameśvari  || 1,25.77
sarudrodhāraṇāttasyasarvatravijayībhavet || 1,25.77
dvādaśāsyaṃ tu rudrākṣaṃ dhārayetkeśadeśake  || 1,25.78
ādityāścaiva te sarvedvādaśaiva sthitāstathā  || 1,25.78
trayodaśamukho viśvedevastaddhāraṇānnaraḥ  || 1,25.79
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti saubhāgyaṃ maṃgalaṃlabhet || 1,25.79
caturdaśamukho yo hi rudrākṣaḥ paramaḥśivaḥ  || 1,25.80
dhārayenmūrdhni taṃ bhaktyāsarvapāpaṃ praṇaśyati  || 1,25.80
itirudrākṣabhedā hi proktāvaimukhabhedataḥ  || 1,25.81
tattanmaṃtrāñchṛṇu prītyā kramācchailleśvarātmaje  || 1,25. 81
aṃ hrīnamaḥ 1 aṃnamaḥ 2 aṃklīṃnamaḥ 3 aṃhrīṃnamaḥ 4 aṃhrīṃnamaḥ 5 aṃhrīṃ huṃ namaḥ 6 aṃhuṃnamaḥ 7 aṃhuṃnamaḥ 8 aṃhrīṃhuṃnamaḥ 9 aṃhrīṃnamaḥ namaḥ 10 aṃhrīṃhuṃnamaḥ 11 aṃkroṃ kṣauṃ rauṃ namaḥ 12 aṃhrīṃnamaḥ 13 aṃnama 14
bhaktiśraddhāyutaścaiva sarvakāmārthasiddhaye  || 1,25.82
rudrākṣāndhārayenmaṃtrairdevanālasya varjitaḥ  || 1,25.82
vinā maṃtreṇa ho dhatte rudrākṣaṃ bhuvimānavaḥ  || 1,25.83
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ yāvadindrāścaturdaśa  || 1,25.83
rudrākṣamālinaṃdṛṣṭvā bhūtapretapiśācakāḥ  || 1,25.84
ḍākinīśākinī caiva ye cānye drohakārakāḥ  || 1,25.84
kṛtrimaṃ caiva yatkiṃcidabhicārādikaṃcayāt || 1,25.85
tatsarvaṃdūratoyātidṛṣṭvā śaṃkitavigraham  || 1,25.85
rudrākṣamālinaṃ dṛṣṭvā śivo viṣṇuḥ prasīdati  || 1,25.86
devīgaṇapatissūryaḥsurāścānyepipārvati  || 1,25.86
evaṃjñātvātumāhātmyaṃrudrākṣasyamaheśvari  || 1,25.87
samyagdhāryāssamaṃtrāścabhaktyādharmavivṛddhaye  || 1,25.87
ityuktaṃgirijāgrehiśivenaparamātmanā  || 1,25.88
bhasmarūdrākṣamāhātmyaṃ bhuktimuktiphalapradam  || 1,25.88
śivasyātipriyaujñeyau bhasmarudrākṣadhāriṇau  || 1,25.89
taddhāraṇaprabhāvaddhi bhuktirmuktirna saṃśayaḥ  || 1,25.89
bhasmarudrākṣadhārī yaḥ śivabhaktassa ucyate  || 1,25.90
pañcākṣarajapāsaktaḥ paripūrṇaścasanmukhe  || 1,25.90
vinābhasmatripuṃḍreṇavinārudrākṣamālayā  || 1,25.91
pūjitopimahādevonābhīṣṭaphaladāyakaḥ  || 1,25.91
tatsarvaṃ ca samākhyātaṃ yatpṛṣṭaṃhimunīśvara  || 1,25.92
bhasmarudrākṣamāhātmyaṃ sarvakāmasamṛddhidam  || 1,25.92
etadyaḥśṛṇuyānnityaṃmāhātmyaparamaṃśubham  || 1,25.93
rudrākṣabhasmanorbhaktyāsarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt || 1,25.93
iha sarvasukhaṃ bhuktvā putrapautrādisaṃyutaḥ  || 1,25.94
labhetparatra sanmokṣaṃ śivasyātipriyo bhavet || 1,25.94
vidyeśvarasaṃhiteyaṃ kathitā vo munīśvarāḥ  || 1,25.95
sarvasiddhipradā nityaṃmuktidā śivaśāsanāt || 1,25.95
