iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe prathamāyāṃ vidyeśvarasaṃhitāyāṃ sādhyasādhanakhaṇḍe rudrākṣamahātmyavarṇanonāma pañcaviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ









vāyavīyasaṃhitā

atha śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamī vāyavīyasaṃhitā prārabhyate   ||
śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ   ||
śrīgaurīśaṃkarābhyāṃ namaḥ   ||
atha saptamī vāyavīyasaṃhitā prārabhyate   ||

adhyāya 1
vyāsa uvāca
namaśśivāya somāya sagaṇāya sasūnave  || 7.1,1.1
pradhānapuruṣeśāya sargasthityaṃtahetave  || 7.1,1.1
śaktirapratimā yasya hyaiśvaryaṃ cāpi sarvagam  || 7.1,1.2
svāmitvaṃ ca vibhutvaṃ ca svabhāvaṃ saṃpracakṣate  || 7.1,1.2
tamajaṃ viśvakarmāṇaṃ śāśvataṃ śivamavyayam  || 7.1,1.3
mahādevaṃ mahātmānaṃ vrajāmi śaraṇaṃ śivam  || 7.1,1.3
dharmakṣetre mahātīrthe gaṃgākāliṃdisaṃgame  || 7.1,1.4
prayāge naimiṣāraṇye brahmalokasya vartmani  || 7.1,1.4
munayaśśaṃsitātmānaḥ satyavrataparāyaṇāḥ  || 7.1,1.5
mahaujaso mahābhāgā mahāsatraṃ vitenire  || 7.1,1.5
tatra satraṃ samākarṇya teṣāmakliṣṭakarmaṇām  || 7.1,1.6
sākṣātsatyavatīsūnorvedavyāsasya dhīmataḥ  || 7.1,1.6
śiṣyo mahātmā medhāvī triṣu lokeṣu viśrutaḥ  || 7.1,1.7
pañcāvayavayuktasya vākyasya guṇadoṣavit || 7.1,1.7
uttarottaravaktā ca bruvato 'pi bṛhaspateḥ  || 7.1,1.8
madhuraḥ śravaṇānāṃ ca manojñapadaparvaṇām  || 7.1,1.8
kathānāṃ nipuṇo vaktā kālavinnayavitkaviḥ  || 7.1,1.9
ājagāma sa taṃ deśaṃ sūtaḥ paurāṇikottamaḥ  || 7.1,1.9
taṃ dṛṣṭvā sūtamāyāṃtaṃ munayo hṛṣṭamānasāḥ  || 7.1,1.10
tasmai sāma ca pūjāṃ ca yathāvatpratyapādayan  || 7.1,1.10
pratigṛhya satāṃ pūjāṃ munibhiḥ pratipāditām  || 7.1,1.11
uddiṣṭamānasaṃ bheje niyukto yuktamātmanaḥ  || 7.1,1.11
tatastatsaṃgamādeva munīnāṃ bhāvitātmanām  || 7.1,1.12
sotkaṃṭhamabhavaccitaṃ śrotuṃ paurāṇikīṃ kathām  || 7.1,1.12
tadā tamanukūlābhirvāgbhiḥ pūjya 1 maharṣayaḥ  || 7.1,1.13
atīvābhimukhaṃ kṛtvā vacanaṃ cedamabruvan  || 7.1,1.13
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
romaharṣaṇa sarvajña bhavānno bhāgyagauravāt || 7.1,1.14
saṃprāptodya mahābhāga śaivarāja mahāmate  || 7.1,1.14
purāṇavidyāmakhilāṃ vyāsātpratyakṣamīyivān  || 7.1,1.15
tasmādāścaryabhūtānāṃ kathānāṃ tvaṃ hi bhājanam  || 7.1,1.15
ratnānāmurusārāṇāṃ ratnākara ivārṇavaḥ  || 7.1,1.16
yacca bhūtaṃ yacca bhavyaṃ yaccānyadvastu vartate  || 7.1,1.16
na tavāviditaṃ kiñcittriṣu lokeṣu vidyate  || 7.1,1.17
tvamadṛṣṭavaśādasmaddarśanārthamihāgataḥ  || 7.1,1.17
akurvankimapi śreyo na vṛthā gantumarhasi  || 7.1,1.17
tasmācchrāvyataraṃ puṇyaṃ satkathājñānasaṃhitam  || 7.1,1.18
vedāṃtasārasarvasvaṃ purāṇaṃ śrāvayāśu naḥ  || 7.1,1.18
evamabhyarthitassūto munibhirvedavādibhiḥ  || 7.1,1.19
ślakṣṇāṃ ca nyāyasaṃyuktāṃ pratyuvāca śubhāṃ giram  || 7.1,1.19

502

sūta uvāca
pūjito 'nugṛhītaśca bhavadbhiriti coditaḥ  || 7.1,1.20
kasmātsamyaṅna vibrūyāṃ purāṇamṛṣipūjitam  || 7.1,1.20
abhivaṃdya mahādevaṃ devīṃ skaṃdaṃ vināyakam  || 7.1,1.21
naṃdinaṃ ca tathā vyāsaṃ sākṣātsatyavatīsutam  || 7.1,1.21
vakṣyāmi paramaṃ puṇyaṃ purāṇaṃ vedasaṃmitam  || 7.1,1.22
śivajñānārṇavaṃ sākṣādbhaktimuktiphalapradam  || 7.1,1.22
śabdārthanyāyasaṃyuktai rāgamārthairvibhūṣitam  || 7.1,1.23
śvetakalpaprasaṃgena vāyunā kathitaṃ purā  || 7.1,1.23
vidyāsthānāni sarvāṇi purāṇānukramaṃ tathā  || 7.1,1.24
tatpurāṇasya cotpattiṃ bruvato me nibodhata  || 7.1,1.24
aṃgāni vedāścatvāro mīmāṃsānyāyavistaraḥ  || 7.1,1.25
purāṇaṃ dharmaśāstraṃ ca vidyāścetāścaturdaśa  || 7.1,1.25
āyurvedo dhanurvedo gāṃdharvaścetyanukramāt || 7.1,1.26
arthaśāstraṃ paraṃ tasmādvidyā hyaṣṭādaśa smṛtāḥ  || 7.1,1.26
aṣṭādaśānāṃ vidyānāmetāsāṃ bhinnavartmanām  || 7.1,1.27
ādikartā kavissākṣācchūlapāṇiriti śrutiḥ  || 7.1,1.27
sa hi sarvajagannāthaḥ sisṛkṣurakhilaṃ jagat || 7.1,1.28
brahmāṇaṃ vidadhe sākṣātputramagre sanātanam  || 7.1,1.28
tasmai prathamaputrāya brahmaṇe viśvayonaye  || 7.1,1.29
vidyāścemā dadau pūrvaṃ viśvasṛṣṭyarthamīśvaraḥ  || 7.1,1.29
pālanāya hariṃ devaṃ rakṣāśaktiṃ dadau tataḥ  || 7.1,1.30
madhyamaṃ tanayaṃ viṣṇuṃ pātāraṃ brahmaṇo 'pi hi  || 7.1,1.30
labdhavidyena vidhinā prajāsṛṣṭiṃ vitanvatā  || 7.1,1.31
prathamaṃ sarvaśāstrāṇāṃ purāṇaṃ brahmaṇā smṛtam  || 7.1,1.31
anaṃtaraṃ tu vaktrebhyo vedāstasya vinirgatāḥ  || 7.1,1.32
pravṛttissarvaśāstrāṇāṃ tanmukhādabhavattataḥ  || 7.1,1.32
yadāsya vistaraṃ śaktā nādhigaṃtuṃ prajā bhuvi  || 7.1,1.33
tadā vidyāsamāsārthaṃ viśveśvaraniyogataḥ  || 7.1,1.33
dvāparāṃteṣu viśvātmā viṣṇurviśvaṃbharaḥ prabhuḥ  || 7.1,1.34
vyāsanāmnā caratyasminnavatīrya mahītale  || 7.1,1.34
evaṃ vyastāśca vedāśca dvāparedvāpare dvijāḥ  || 7.1,1.35
nirmitāni purāṇāni anyāni ca tataḥ param  || 7.1,1.35
sa punardvāpare cāsminkṛṣṇadvaipāyanākhyayā  || 7.1,1.36
araṇyāmiva havyāśī satyavatyāmajāyata  || 7.1,1.36
saṃkṣipya sa punarvedāṃścaturdhā kṛtavānmuniḥ  || 7.1,1.37
vyastavedatayā loke vedavyāsa iti śrutaḥ  || 7.1,1.37
purāṇānāñca saṃkṣiptaṃ caturlakṣapramāṇataḥ  || 7.1,1.38
adyāpi devaloke tacchatakoṭipravistaram  || 7.1,1.38
yo vidyāccaturo vedān sāṃgopaṇiṣadāndvijaḥ  || 7.1,1.39
na cetpurāṇaṃ saṃvidyānnaiva sa syādvicakṣaṇaḥ  || 7.1,1.39
itihāsapurāṇābhyāṃ vedaṃ samupabṛṃhayet || 7.1,1.40
bibhetyalpaśrutādvedo māmayaṃ pratariṣyati  || 7.1,1.40
sargaśca pratisargaśca vaṃśo manvaṃtarāṇi ca  || 7.1,1.41
vaṃśānucaritaṃ caiva purāṇaṃ pañcalakṣaṇam  || 7.1,1.41
daśadhā cāṣṭadhā caitatpurāṇamupadiśyate  || 7.1,1.42
502

bṛhatsūkṣmaprabhedena munibhistattvavittamaiḥ  || 7.1,1.42
brāhmaṃ pādmaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ ca śaivaṃ bhāgavataṃ tathā  || 7.1,1.43
bhaviṣyaṃ nāradīyaṃ ca mārkaṃḍeyamataḥ param  || 7.1,1.43
āgneyaṃ brahmavaivartaṃ laiṃgaṃ vārāhameva ca  || 7.1,1.44
skāndaṃ ca vāmanaṃ caiva kaurmyaṃ mātsyaṃ ca gāruḍam  || 7.1,1.44
brahmāṃḍaṃ ceti puṇyo 'yaṃ purāṇānāmanukramaḥ  || 7.1,1.45
tatra śaivaṃ turīyaṃ yacchārvaṃ sarvārthasādhakam  || 7.1,1.45
graṃtho lakṣapramāṇaṃ tadvyastaṃ dvādaśasaṃhitam  || 7.1,1.46
nirmitaṃ tacchivenaiva tatra dharmaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ  || 7.1,1.46
taduktenaiva dharmeṇa śaivāstraivarṇikā narāḥ  || 7.1,1.47
tasmādvimukutimanvicchañcchivameva samāśrayet || 7.1,1.47
tamāśrityaiva devānāmapi muktirna cānyathā  || 7.1,1.48
yadidaṃ śaivamākhyātaṃ purāṇaṃ vedasaṃmitam  || 7.1,1.49
tasya bhedānsamāsena bruvato me nibodhata  || 7.1,1.49
vidyeśvaraṃ tathā raudraṃ vaināyakamanuttamam  || 7.1,1.50
aumaṃ mātṛpurāṇaṃ ca rudraikādaśakaṃ tathā  || 7.1,1.50
kailāsaṃ śatarudraṃ ca śatarudrākhyameva ca  || 7.1,1.51
sahasrakoṭirudrākhyaṃ vāyavīyaṃ tataḥparam  || 7.1,1.51
dharmasaṃjñaṃ purāṇaṃ cetyevaṃ dvādaśa saṃhitāḥ  || 7.1,1.52
vidyeśaṃ daśasāhasramuditaṃ graṃthasaṃkhyayā  || 7.1,1.52
raudraṃ vaināyakaṃ caumaṃ mātṛkākhyaṃ tataḥ param  || 7.1,1.53
pratyekamaṣṭasāhasraṃ trayodaśasahasrakam  || 7.1,1.53
raudrakādaśakākhyaṃ yatkailāsaṃ ṣaṭsahasrakam  || 7.1,1.54
śatarudraṃ trisāhasraṃ koṭirudraṃ tataḥ param  || 7.1,1.54
sahasrairnavabhiryuktaṃ sarvārthajñānasaṃyutam  || 7.1,1.55
sahasrakoṭirudrākhyamekādaśasahasrakam  || 7.1,1.55
catussahasrasaṃkhyeyaṃ vāyavīyamanuttamam  || 7.1,1.56
dharmasaṃjñaṃ purāṇaṃ yattaddvādaśasahasrakam  || 7.1,1.56
tadevaṃ lakṣamuddiṣṭaṃ śaivaṃ śākhāvibhedataḥ  || 7.1,1.57
purāṇaṃ vedasāraṃ tadbhuktimuktiphalapradam  || 7.1,1.57
vyāsena tattu saṃkṣiptaṃ caturviṃśatsahasrakam  || 7.1,1.58
śaivantatra purāṇaṃ vai caturthaṃ saptasaṃhitam  || 7.1,1.58
vidyeśvarākhyā tatrādyā dvitīyā rudrasaṃhitā  || 7.1,1.59
tṛtīyā śatarudrākhyā koṭirudrā caturthikā  || 7.1,1.59
pañcamī kathitā comā ṣaṣṭhī kailāsasaṃhitā  || 7.1,1.60
saptamī vāyavīyākhyā saptaivaṃ saṃhitā iha  || 7.1,1.60
vidyeśvaraṃ dvisāhasraṃ raudraṃ pañcaśatāyutam  || 7.1,1.61
triṃśattathā dvisāhasraṃ sārdhaikaśatamīritam  || 7.1,1.61
śatarudrantathā koṭirudraṃ vyomayugādhikam  || 7.1,1.62
dvisāhasraṃ ca dviśataṃ tathomaṃ bhūsahasrakam  || 7.1,1.62
catvāriṃśatsāṣṭaśataṃ kailāsaṃ bhūsahasrakam  || 7.1,1.63
catvāriṃśacca dviśataṃ vāyavīyamataḥ param  || 7.1,1.63
catussāhasrasaṃkhyākamevaṃ saṃkhyāvibhedataḥ  || 7.1,1.64
śrutamparamapuṇyantu purāṇaṃ śivasaṃjñakam  || 7.1,1.64
catuḥsāhasrakaṃ yattu vāyavīyamudīritam  || 7.1,1.65
tadidaṃ vartayiṣyāmi bhāgadvayasamanvitam  || 7.1,1.65
nāvedaviduṣe vācyamidaṃ śāstramanuttamam  || 7.1,1.66
na caivāśraddhadhānāya nāpurāṇavide tathā  || 7.1,1.66
parīkṣitāya śiṣyāya dhārmikāyānasūyave  || 7.1,1.67
pradeyaṃ śivabhaktāya śivadharmānusāriṇe  || 7.1,1.67
purāṇasaṃhitā yasya prasādānmayi vartate  || 7.1,1.68
namo bhagavate tasmai vyāsāyāmitatejase  || 7.1,1.68

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe vidyāvatārakathanaṃ nāmaprathamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 2
sūta uvāca
purā kālena mahatā kalpetīte punaḥpunaḥ  || 7.1,2.1
asminnupasthite kalpe pravṛtte sṛṣṭhikarmaṇi  || 7.1,2.1
pratiṣṭhitāyāṃ vārtāyāṃ prabuddhāsu prajāsu ca  || 7.1,2.2
munīnāṃ ṣaṭkulīyānāṃ bruvatāmitaretaram  || 7.1,2.2
idaṃ paramidaṃ neti vivādassumahānabhūt || 7.1,2.3
parasya durnirūpatvānna jātastatra niścayaḥ  || 7.1,2.3
te 'bhijagmurvidhātāraṃ draṣṭuṃ brahmāṇamavyayam  || 7.1,2.4
yatrāste bhagavān brahmā stūyamānassurāsuraiḥ  || 7.1,2.4
meruśṛṃge śubhe ramye devadānavasaṃkule  || 7.1,2.5
siddhacāraṇasaṃvāde yakṣagaṃdharvasevite  || 7.1,2.5
vihaṃgasaṃghasaṃghuṣṭe maṇividrumabhūṣite  || 7.1,2.6
nikuṃjakaṃdaradarīgṛhānirjharaśobhite  || 7.1,2.6
tatra brahmavanaṃ nāma nānāmṛgasamākulam  || 7.1,2.7
daśayojanavistīrṇaṃ śatayojanamāyatam  || 7.1,2.7
surasāmalapānīyapūrṇaramyasarovaram  || 7.1,2.8
mattabhramarasaṃchannaramyapuṣpitapādapam  || 7.1,2.8
taruṇādityasaṃkāśaṃ tatra cāru mahatpuram  || 7.1,2.9
durdharṣabaladṛptānāṃ daityadānavarakṣasām  || 7.1,2.9
taptajāṃbūnadamayaṃ prāṃśuprākāratoraṇam  || 7.1,2.10
nirvyūhavalabhīkūṭapratolīśatamaṃḍitam  || 7.1,2.10
mahārhamaṇicitrābhirlelihānamivāṃbaram  || 7.1,2.11
mahābhavanakoṭībhiranekābhiralaṃkṛtam  || 7.1,2.11
tasminnivasati brahmā sabhyaiḥ sārdhaṃ prajāpatiḥ  || 7.1,2.12
tatra gatvā mahātmānaṃ sākṣāllokapitāmaham  || 7.1,2.12
daddaśurmunayo devā devarṣigaṇasevitam  || 7.1,2.13
śuddhacāmīkaraprakhyaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam  || 7.1,2.13
prasannavadanaṃ saumyaṃ padmapatrāyatekṣaṇam  || 7.1,2.14
divyakāṃtisamāyuktaṃ divyagaṃdhānulepanam  || 7.1,2.14
divyaśuklāṃbaradharaṃ divyamālāvibhūṣitam  || 7.1,2.15
surāsurendrayogīṃdravaṃdyamānapadāṃbujam  || 7.1,2.15
sarvalakṣaṇayuktāṃgyā labdhacāmarahastayā  || 7.1,2.16
bhrājamānaṃ sarasvatyā prabhayeva divākaram  || 7.1,2.16
taṃ dṛṣṭvā munayassarve prasannavadanekṣaṇāḥ  || 7.1,2.17
śirasyaṃjalimādhāya tuṣṭuvussurapuṃgavam  || 7.1,2.17
munaya ūcuḥ
namastrimūrtaye tubhyaṃ sargasthityaṃtahetave  || 7.1,2.18
puruṣāya purāṇāya brahmaṇe paramātmane  || 7.1,2.18
namaḥ pradhānadehāya pradhānakṣobhakāriṇe  || 7.1,2.19
trayoviṃśatibhedena vikṛtāyāvikāriṇe  || 7.1,2.19
namo brahmāṇḍadehāya brahmāṃḍodaravartine  || 7.1,2.20
tatra saṃsiddhakāryāya saṃsiddhakaraṇāya ca  || 7.1,2.20
namostu sarvalokāya sarvalokavidhāyine  || 7.1,2.21
sarvātmadehasaṃyoga viyogavidhihetave  || 7.1,2.21
tvayaiva nikhilaṃ sṛṣṭaṃ saṃhṛtaṃ pālitaṃ jagat || 7.1,2.22
tathāpi māyayā nātha na vidmastvāṃ pitāmaha  || 7.1,2.22
sūta uvāca
evaṃ brahmā mahābhāgairmaharṣibhirabhiṣṭutaḥ  || 7.1,2.23
prāha gaṃbhīrayā vācā munīn prahlādayanniva  || 7.1,2.23
brahmovāca
ṛṣayo he mahābhāgā mahāsattvā mahaujasaḥ  || 7.1,2.24
kimarthaṃ sahitāssarve yūyamatra samāgatāḥ  || 7.1,2.24
tamevaṃvādinaṃ devaṃ brahmāṇaṃ brahmavittamāḥ  || 7.1,2.25
vāgbhirvinayagarbhābhissarve prāṃjalayo 'bruvan  || 7.1,2.25
munaya ūcuḥ
bhagavannaṃdhakāreṇa mahatā vayamāvṛtāḥ  || 7.1,2.26
khinnā vivadamānāśca na paśyāmo 'tra yatparam  || 7.1,2.26
tvaṃ hi sarvajagaddhātā sarvakāraṇakāraṇam  || 7.1,2.27
tvayā hyaviditaṃ nātha neha kiṃcana vidyate  || 7.1,2.27
kaḥ pumān sarvasattvebhyaḥ purāṇaḥ puruṣaḥ paraḥ  || 7.1,2.28
viśuddhaḥ paripūrṇaśca śāśvataḥ parameśvaraḥ  || 7.1,2.28
kenaiva citrakṛtyena prathamaṃ sṛjyate jagat || 7.1,2.29
tattvaṃ vada mahāprājña svasaṃdehāpanuttaye  || 7.1,2.29
evaṃ pṛṣṭastadā brahmā vismayasmeravīkṣaṇaḥ  || 7.1,2.30
devānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca munīnāmapi sannidhau  || 7.1,2.30
utthāya suciraṃ dhyātvā rudra ityuddharan girim  || 7.1,2.31
ānaṃdaklinnasarvāṃgaḥ kṛtāṃjalirabhāṣata  || 7.1,2.31

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe muniprastāvavarṇanaṃ nāma dvitīyo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 3
brahamovāca
yato vāco nivartaṃte aprāpya manasā saha  || 7.1,3.1
ānaṃdaṃ yasya vai vidvānna bibheti kutaścana  || 7.1,3.1
yasmātsarvamidaṃ brahmaviṣṇurudrendrapūrvakam  || 7.1,3.2
saha bhūtendriyaiḥ sarvaiḥ prathamaṃ saṃprasūyate  || 7.1,3.2
kāraṇānāṃ ca yo dhātā dhyātā paramakāraṇam  || 7.1,3.3
na saṃprasūyate 'nyasmātkutaścana kadācana  || 7.1,3.3
sarvaiśvaryeṇa saṃpanno nāmnā sarveśvaraḥ svayam  || 7.1,3.4
sarvairmumukṣubhirdhyeyaśśaṃbhurākāśamadhyagaḥ  || 7.1,3.4
yo 'gre māṃ vidadhe putraṃ jñānaṃ ca prahiṇoti me  || 7.1,3.5
tatprasādānmayālabdhaṃ prājāpatyamidaṃ padam  || 7.1,3.5
īśo vṛkṣa iva stabdho ya eko divi tiṣṭhati  || 7.1,3.6
yenedamakhilaṃ pūrṇaṃ puruṣeṇa mahātmanā  || 7.1,3.6
504

eko bahūnāṃ jaṃtūnāṃ niṣkriyāṇāṃ ca sakriyaḥ  || 7.1,3.7
ya eko bahudhā bījaṃ karoti sa maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,3.7
jīvairebhirimāṃllokānsarvānīśo ya īśate 1  || 7.1,3.8
ya eko bhāgavānrudro na dvitīyo 'sti kaścana  || 7.1,3.8
sadā janānāṃ hṛdaye saṃniviṣṭo 'pi yaḥ paraiḥ  || 7.1,3.9
alakṣyo lakṣayanviśvamadhitiṣṭhati sarvadā  || 7.1,3.9
yastu kālātpramuktāni kāraṇānyakhilānyapi  || 7.1,3.10
anantaśaktirevaiko bhagavānadhitiṣṭhati  || 7.1,3.10
na yasya divaso rātrirna samāno na cādhikaḥ  || 7.1,3.11
svabhāvikī parāśaktirnityā jñānakriye api  || 7.1,3.11
yadidaṃ kṣaramavyaktaṃ yadapyamṛtamakṣaram  || 7.1,3.12
tāvubhāvakṣarātmānāveko devaḥ svayaṃ haraḥ  || 7.1,3.12
īśate tadabhidhyānādyojanāsattvabhāvanaḥ  || 7.1,3.13
bhūyo hyasya paśorante viśvamāyā nivartate  || 7.1,3.13
yasminna bhāsate vidyunna sūryo na ca candramāḥ  || 7.1,3.14
yasya bhāsā vibhātīdamityeṣā śāśvatī śrutiḥ  || 7.1,3.14
eko devo mahādevo vijñeyastu maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,3.15
na tasya paramaṃ kiṃcitpadaṃ samadhigamyate  || 7.1,3.15
ayamādiranādyantassvabhāvādeva nirmalaḥ  || 7.1,3.16
svatantraḥ paripūrṇaśca svecchādhīnaścarācaraḥ  || 7.1,3.16
aprākṛtavapuḥ śrīmāṃllakṣyalakṣaṇavarjitaḥ  || 7.1,3.17
ayaṃ mukto mocakaśca hyakālaḥ kālacodakaḥ  || 7.1,3.17
sarvoparikṛtāvāsassarvāvāsaśca sarvavit || 7.1,3.18
ṣaḍvidhādhvamayasyāsya sarvasya jagataḥ patiḥ  || 7.1,3.18
uttarottarabhūtānāmuttaraśca niruttaraḥ  || 7.1,3.19
anantānantasandohamakaraṃdamadhuvrataḥ  || 7.1,3.19
akhaṃḍajagadaṃḍānāṃ piṃḍīkaraṇapaṃḍitaḥ  || 7.1,3.20
audāryavīryagāṃbhīryamādhuryamakarālayaḥ  || 7.1,3.20
naivāsya sadṛśaṃ vastu nādhikaṃ cāpi kiṃcana  || 7.1,3.21
atulaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ rājarājaśca tiṣṭhati  || 7.1,3.21
anena citrakṛtyena prathamaṃ sṛjyate jagat || 7.1,3.22
aṃtakāle punaścedaṃ tasminpralayameṣyate  || 7.1,3.22
asya bhūtāni vaśyāni ayaṃ sarvaniyojakaḥ  || 7.1,3.23
ayaṃ tu parayā bhaktyā dṛśyate nānyathā kvacit || 7.1,3.23
vratāni sarvadānāni tapāṃsi niyamāstathā  || 7.1,3.24
kathitāni purā sadbhirbhāvārthaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 7.1,3.24
hariścāhaṃ ca rudraśca tathānye ca surāsurāḥ  || 7.1,3.25
tapobhirugrairadyāpi tasya darśanakāṃkṣiṇaḥ  || 7.1,3.25
adṛśyaḥ patitairmūḍhairdurjanairapi kutsitaiḥ  || 7.1,3.26
bhaktairantarbahiścāpi pūjyaḥ saṃbhāṣya eva ca  || 7.1,3.26
tadidaṃ trividhaṃ rūpaṃ sthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ tataḥ param  || 7.1,3.27
asmadādyamarairdṛśyaṃ sthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ tu yogibhiḥ  || 7.1,3.27

1 śapo lugabhāva ārṣa
504

tataḥ paraṃ tu yannityaṃ jñānamānaṃdamavyayam  || 7.1,3.28
tanniṣṭhaistatparairbhaktairdṛśyaṃ tadvratamāśritaiḥ  || 7.1,3.28
bahunātra kimuktena guhyādguhyataraṃ param  || 7.1,3.29
śive bhaktirna sandehastayā yukto vimucyate  || 7.1,3.29
prasādādeva sā bhaktiḥ prasādo bhaktisaṃbhavaḥ  || 7.1,3.30
yathā cāṃkurato bījaṃ bījato vā yathāṃkuraḥ  || 7.1,3.30
prasādapūrvikā eva paśossarvatra siddhayaḥ  || 7.1,3.31
sa eva sādhanairante sarvairapi ca sādhyate  || 7.1,3.31
prasādasādhanaṃ dharmassa ca vedena darśitaḥ  || 7.1,3.32
tadabhyāsavaśātsāmyaṃ pūrvayoḥ puṇyapāpayoḥ  || 7.1,3.32
sāmyātprasādasaṃparko dharmasyātiśayastataḥ  || 7.1,3.33
dharmātiśayamāsādya paśoḥ pāpaparikṣayaḥ  || 7.1,3.33
evaṃ prakṣīṇapāpasya bahubhirjanmabhiḥ kramāt || 7.1,3.34
sāṃbe sarveśvare bhaktirjñānapūrvā prajāyate  || 7.1,3.34
bhāvānuguṇamīśasya prasādo vyatiricyate  || 7.1,3.35
prasādātkarmasaṃtyāgaḥ phalato na svarūpataḥ  || 7.1,3.35
tasmātkarmaphalatyāgācchivadharmānvayaḥ śubhaḥ  || 7.1,3.36
sa ca gurvanapekṣaśca tadapekṣa iti dvidhā  || 7.1,3.36
tatrānapekṣātsāpekṣo mukhyaḥ śataguṇādhikaḥ  || 7.1,3.37
śivadharmānvayasyāsya śivajñānasamanvayaḥ  || 7.1,3.37
jñanānvayavaśātpuṃsaḥ saṃsāre doṣadarśanam  || 7.1,3.38
tato viṣayavairāgyaṃ vairāgyādbhāvasādhanam  || 7.1,3.38
bhāvasiddhyupapannasya dhyāne niṣṭhā na karmaṇi  || 7.1,3.39
jñānadhyānābhiyuktasya puṃso yogaḥ pravartate  || 7.1,3.39
yogena tu parā bhaktiḥ prasādastadanaṃtaram  || 7.1,3.40
prasādānmucyate jaṃturmuktaḥ śivasamo bhavet || 7.1,3.40
anugrahaprakārasya kramo 'yamavivakṣitaḥ  || 7.1,3.41
yādṛśī yogyatā puṃsastasya tādṛganugrahaḥ  || 7.1,3.41
garbhastho mucyate kaścijjāyamānastathāparaḥ  || 7.1,3.42
bālo vā taruṇo vātha vṛddho vā mucyate paraḥ  || 7.1,3.42
tiryagyonigataḥ kaścinmucyate nārako 'paraḥ  || 7.1,3.43
aparastu padaṃ prāpto mucyate svapadakṣaye  || 7.1,3.43
kaścitkṣīṇapado bhūtvā punarāvartya mucyate  || 7.1,3.44
kaścidadhvagatastasmin sthitvāsthitvā vimucyate  || 7.1,3.44
tasmānnaikaprakāreṇa narāṇāṃ muktiriṣyate  || 7.1,3.45
jñānabhāvānurūpeṇa prasādenaiva nirvṛtiḥ  || 7.1,3.45
tasmādasya prasādārthaṃ vāṅmanodoṣavarjitāḥ  || 7.1,3.46
dhyāyaṃtaśśivamevaikaṃ sadāratanayāgnayaḥ  || 7.1,3.46
tanniṣṭhāstatparāssarve tadyuktāstadupāśrayāḥ  || 7.1,3.47
sarvakriyāḥ prakurvāṇāstameva manasāgatāḥ  || 7.1,3.47
dīrghasūtrasamārabdhaṃ divyavarṣasahasrakam  || 7.1,3.48
satrāṃte maṃtrayogena vāyustatra gamiṣyati  || 7.1,3.48
sa eva bhavataḥ śreyaḥ sopāyaṃ kathayiṣyati  || 7.1,3.49
505

tato vārāṇasī puṇyā purī paramaśobhanā  || 7.1,3.49
gaṃtavyā yatra viśveśo devyā saha pinākadhṛk || 7.1,3.50
sadā viharati śrīmān bhaktānugrahakāraṇāt || 7.1,3.50
tatrāścaryaṃ mahaddṛṣṭvā matsamīpaṃ gamiṣyatha  || 7.1,3.51
tato vaḥ kathayiṣyāmi mokṣopāya dvijottamāḥ  || 7.1,3.51
yenaikajanmanā muktiryuṣmatkaratale sthitā  || 7.1,3.52
anekajanmasaṃsārabaṃdhanirmokṣakāriṇī  || 7.1,3.52
etanmanomayaṃ cakraṃ mayā sṛṣṭaṃ visṛjyate  || 7.1,3.53
yatrāsya śīryate nemiḥ sa deśastapasaśśubhaḥ  || 7.1,3.53
ityuktvā sūryasaṃkāśaṃ cakraṃ dṛṣṭvā manomayam  || 7.1,3.54
praṇipatya mahādevaṃ visasarja pitāmahaḥ  || 7.1,3.54
te 'pi hṛṣṭatarā viprāḥ praṇamya jagatāṃ prabhum  || 7.1,3.55
prayayustasya cakrasya yatra nemiraśīryata  || 7.1,3.55
cakraṃ tadapi saṃkṣiptaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ cāruśilātale  || 7.1,3.56
vimalasvādupānīye nijapāta vane kvacit || 7.1,3.56
tadvanaṃ tena vikhyātaṃ naimiṣaṃ munipūjitam  || 7.1,3.57
anekayakṣagaṃdharvavidyādharasamākulam  || 7.1,3.57
aṣṭādaśa samudrasya dvīpānaśnanpurūravāḥ  || 7.1,3.58
vilāsavaśamurvaśyā yāto daivena coditaḥ  || 7.1,3.58
akrameṇa haranmohādyajñavāṭaṃ hiraṇmayam  || 7.1,3.59
munibhiryatra saṃkruddhaiḥ kuśavajrairnipātitaḥ  || 7.1,3.59
viśvaṃ sisṛkṣamāṇā vai yatra viśvasṛjaḥ purā  || 7.1,3.60
satramārebhire divyaṃ brahmajñā gārhapatyagāḥ  || 7.1,3.60
ṛṣibhiryatra vidvadbhiḥ śabdārthanyāyakovidaiḥ  || 7.1,3.61
śaktiprajñākriyāyogairvidhirāsīdanuṣṭhitaḥ  || 7.1,3.61
yatra vedavido nityaṃ vedavādabahiṣkṛtān  || 7.1,3.62
vādajalpabalairghnaṃti vacobhirativādinaḥ  || 7.1,3.62
sphaṭikamayamahībhṛtpādajābhyaśśilābhyaḥ prasaradamṛtakalpassvacchapānīyaramyam  || 7.1,3.63
atirasaphalavṛkṣaprāyamavyālasattvaṃ tapasa ucitamāsīnnaimiṣaṃ tanmunīnām  || 7.1,3.63

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe naimiṣopākhyānaṃ nāma tṛtīyo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 4
sūta uvāca
tasmindeśe mahābhāgā munayaśśaṃsitavratāḥ  || 7.1,4.1
arcayaṃto mahādevaṃ satramārebhire tadā  || 7.1,4.1
tacca satraṃ pravavṛte sarvāścaryaṃ maharṣiṇām 1  || 7.1,4.2
viśvaṃ sisṛkṣamāṇānāṃ purā viśvasṛjāmiva  || 7.1,4.2
atha kāle gate satre samāpte bhūridakṣiṇe  || 7.1,4.3
pitāmahaniyogena vāyustatrāgamatsvayam  || 7.1,4.3
śiṣyassvayaṃbhuvo devassarvapratyakṣadṛgvaśī  || 7.1,4.4
ājñāyāṃ maruto yasya saṃsthitāssaptasaptakāḥ  || 7.1,4.4

1 dīrghābhāva ārṣaḥ

505

prerayañchaśvadaṃgāni prāṇādyābhiḥ svavṛttibhiḥ  || 7.1,4.5
sarvabhūtaśarīrāṇāṃ kurute yaśca dhāraṇam  || 7.1,4.5
aṇimādibhiraṣṭābhiraiśvaryaiśca samanvitaḥ  || 7.1,4.6
tiryakkālādibhirmedhyairbhuvanāni bibharti yaḥ  || 7.1,4.6
ākāśayonirdviguṇaḥ sparśaśabdasamanvayāt || 7.1,4.7
tejasāṃ prakṛtiśceti yamāhustattvaciṃtakāḥ  || 7.1,4.7
tamāśramagataṃ dṛṣṭvā munayo dīrghasatriṇaḥ  || 7.1,4.8
pitāmahavacaḥ smṛtvā praharṣamatulaṃ yayuḥ  || 7.1,4.8
abhyutthāya tatassarve praṇamyāṃbarasaṃbhavam  || 7.1,4.9
cāmīkaramayaṃ tasmai viṣṭaraṃ samakalpayan  || 7.1,4.9
sopi tatra samāsīno munibhissamyagarcitaḥ  || 7.1,4.10
pratinaṃdya ca tān sarvān papraccha kuśalaṃ tataḥ  || 7.1,4.10
vāyuruvāca
atra vaḥ kuśalaṃ viprāḥ kaccidvṛtte mahākratau  || 7.1,4.11
kaccidyajñahano daityā na bādheransuradviṣaḥ  || 7.1,4.11
prāyaścittaṃ duriṣṭaṃ vā na kaccitsamajāyata  || 7.1,4.12
stotraśastragṛhairdevān pitḥn pitryaiśca karmabhiḥ  || 7.1,4.12
kaccidabhyarcya yuṣmābhirvidhirāsītsvanuṣṭhitaḥ  || 7.1,4.13
nivṛtte ca mahāsatre paścātkiṃ vaścikīrṣitam  || 7.1,4.13
ityuktā munayaḥ sarve vāyunā śivabhāvinā  || 7.1,4.14
prahṛṣṭamanasaḥ pūtāḥ pratyūcurvinayānvitāḥ  || 7.1,4.14
munaya ūcuḥ
adya naḥ kuśalaṃ sarvamadya sādhu bhavettapaḥ  || 7.1,4.15
asmacchreyobhivṛddhyarthaṃ bhavānatrāgato yataḥ  || 7.1,4.15
śṛṇu cedaṃ purāvṛttaṃ tamasākrāṃtamānasaiḥ  || 7.1,4.16
upāsitaḥ purāsmābhirvijñānārthaṃ prajāpatiḥ  || 7.1,4.16
sopyasmānanugṛhyāha śaraṇyaśśaraṇāgatān  || 7.1,4.17
sarvasmādadhiko rudro viprāḥ paramakāraṇam  || 7.1,4.17
tamapratarkyaṃ yāthātmyaṃ bhaktimāneva paśyati  || 7.1,4.18
bhaktiścāsya prasādena prasādādeva nirvṛtiḥ  || 7.1,4.18
tasmādasya prasādārthaṃ naimiṣe satrayogataḥ  || 7.1,4.19
yajadhvaṃ dīrghasatreṇa rudraṃ paramakāraṇam  || 7.1,4.19
tatprasādena satrāṃte vāyustatrāgamiṣyati  || 7.1,4.20
tanmukhājjñānalābho vastatra śreyo bhaviṣyati  || 7.1,4.20
ityādiśya vayaṃ sarve preṣitā parameṣṭhinā  || 7.1,4.21
asmindeśe mahābhāga tavāgamanakāṃkṣiṇaḥ  || 7.1,4.21
dīrghasatraṃ samāsīnā divyavarṣasahasrakam  || 7.1,4.22
atastavāgamādanyatprārthyaṃ no nāsti kiṃcana  || 7.1,4.22
ityākarṇya purāvṛttamṛṣīṇāṃ dīrghasatriṇām  || 7.1,4.23
vāyuḥ prītamanā bhūtvā tatrāsīnmunisaṃvṛtaḥ  || 7.1,4.23
tatastairmunibhiḥ pṛṣṭasteṣāṃ bhāvavivṛddhaye  || 7.1,4.24
sargādi śārvamaiśvaryaṃ samāsāda vadadvibhuḥ  || 7.1,4.24

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe vāyusamāgamo nāma caturtho 'dhyāyaḥ
506


adhyāya 5
sūta uvāca
tatra pūrvaṃ mahābhāgā naimiṣāraṇyavāsinaḥ  || 7.1,5.1
praṇipatya yathānyāyaṃ papracchuḥ pavanaṃ prabhum  || 7.1,5.1
naimiṣīyā ūcuḥ
bhavān kathamanuprāpto jñānamīśvaragocaram  || 7.1,5.2
kathaṃ ca śivabhāvaste brahmaṇo 'vyaktajanmanaḥ  || 7.1,5.2
vāyuruvāca
ekonaviṃśatiḥ kalpo vijñeyaḥ śvetalohitaḥ  || 7.1,5.3
tasminkalpe caturvaktrassraṣṭukāmo 'tapattapaḥ  || 7.1,5.3
tapasā tena tīvreṇa tuṣṭastasya pitā svayam  || 7.1,5.4
divyaṃ kaumāramāsthāya rūpaṃ rūpavatāṃ varaḥ  || 7.1,5.4
śveto nāma munirbhūtvā divyāṃ vācamudīrayan  || 7.1,5.5
darśanaṃ pradadau tasmai devadevo maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,5.5
taṃ dṛṣṭvā pitaraṃ brahmā brahmaṇo 'dhipatiṃ patim  || 7.1,5.6
praṇamya paramajñānaṃ gāyatryā saha labdhavān  || 7.1,5.6
tatassa labdhavijñāno viśvakarmā caturmukhaḥ  || 7.1,5.7
asṛjatsarvabhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca  || 7.1,5.7
yataśśrutvāmṛtaṃ labdhaṃ brahmaṇā parameśvarāt || 7.1,5.8
tatastadvadanādeva mayā labdhaṃ tapobalāt || 7.1,5.8
munaya ūcuḥ
kiṃ tajjñānaṃ tvayā labdhaṃ tathyāttathyaṃtaraṃ śubham  || 7.1,5.9
yatra kṛtvā parāṃ niṣṭhāṃ puruṣassukhamṛcchati  || 7.1,5.9
vayuruvāca
paśupāśapatijñānaṃ yallabdhaṃ tu mayā purā  || 7.1,5.10
tatra niṣṭhā parā kāryā puruṣeṇa sukhārthinā  || 7.1,5.10
ajñānaprabhavaṃ duḥkhaṃ jñānenaiva nivartate  || 7.1,5.11
jñānaṃ vastuparicchedo vastu ca dvividhaṃ smṛtam  || 7.1,5.11
ajaḍaṃ ca jaḍaṃ caiva niyaṃtṛ ca tayorapi  || 7.1,5.12
paśuḥ pāśaḥ patiśceti kathyate tattrayaṃ kramāt || 7.1,5.12
akṣaraṃ ca kṣaraṃ caiva kṣarākṣaraparaṃ tathā  || 7.1,5.13
tadetattritayaṃ bhūmnā kathyate tattvavedibhiḥ  || 7.1,5.13
akṣaraṃ paśurityuktaḥ kṣaraṃ pāśa udāhṛtaḥ  || 7.1,5.14
kṣarākṣaraparaṃ yattatpatirityabhidhīyate  || 7.1,5.14
munaya ūcuḥ
kiṃ tadakṣaramityuktaṃ kiṃ ca kṣaramudāhṛtam  || 7.1,5.15
tayośca paramaṃ kiṃ vā tadetadbrūhi māruta  || 7.1,5.15
vāyuruvāca
prakṛtiḥ kṣaramityuktaṃ puruṣo 'kṣara ucyate  || 7.1,5.16
tāvimau prerayatyanyassa parā parameśvaraḥ  || 7.1,5.16
munaya ūcuḥ
kaiṣā prakṛtirityuktā ka eṣa puruṣo mataḥ  || 7.1,5.17
anayoḥ kena sambandhaḥ koyaṃ preraka īśvaraḥ  || 7.1,5.17
vāyuruvāca
māyā prakṛtiruddiṣṭā puruṣo māyayā vṛtaḥ  || 7.1,5.18
saṃbandho mūlakarmabhyāṃ śivaḥ preraka īśvaraḥ  || 7.1,5.18
munaya ūcuḥ
keyaṃ māyā samā khyātā kiṃrūpo māyayā vṛtaḥ  || 7.1,5.19
mūlaṃ kīdṛkkuto vāsya kiṃ śivatvaṃ kutaśśivaḥ  || 7.1,5.19
vāyuruvāca
māyā māheśvarī śaktiścidrūpo māyayā vṛtaḥ  || 7.1,5.20
506

malaścicchādako naijo viśuddhiśśivatā svataḥ  || 7.1,5.20
munaya ūcuḥ
āvṛṇoti kathaṃ māyā vyāpinaṃ kena hetunā  || 7.1,5.21
kimarthaṃ cāvṛtiḥ puṃsaḥ kena vā vinivartate  || 7.1,5.21
vāyuruvāca
āvṛtirvyapino 'pi syādvyāpi yasmātkalādyapi  || 7.1,5.22
hetuḥ karmaiva bhogārthaṃ nivarteta malakṣayāt || 7.1,5.22
munaya ūcuḥ
kalādi kathyate kiṃ tatkarma vā kimudāhṛtam  || 7.1,5.23
tatkimādi kimantaṃ vā kiṃ phalaṃ vā kimāśrayam  || 7.1,5.23
kasya bhogena kiṃ bhogyaṃ kiṃ vā tadbhogasādhanam  || 7.1,5.24
malakṣayasya ko hetuḥ kīdṛkkṣīṇamalaḥ pumān  || 7.1,5.24
vāyuruvāca
kalā vidyā ca rāgaśca kālo niyatireva ca  || 7.1,5.25
kalādayassamākhyātā yo bhoktā puruṣo bhavet || 7.1,5.25
puṇyapāpātmakaṃ karma sukhaduḥkhaphalaṃ tu yat || 7.1,5.26
anādimalabhogāntamajñānātmasamāśrayam  || 7.1,5.26
bhogaḥ karmavināśāya bhogamavyaktamucyate  || 7.1,5.27
bāhyāṃtaḥkaraṇadvāraṃ śarīraṃ bhogasādhanam  || 7.1,5.27
bhāvātiśayalabdhena prasādena malakṣayaḥ  || 7.1,5.28
kṣīṇe cātmamale tasmin pumāñcchivasamo bhavet || 7.1,5.28
munaya ūcuḥ
kalādipañcatattvānāṃ kiṃ karma pṛthagucyate  || 7.1,5.29
bhokteti puruṣaśceti yenātmā vyapadiśyate  || 7.1,5.29
kimātmakaṃ tadavyaktaṃ kenākāreṇa bhujyate  || 7.1,5.30
kiṃ tasya śaraṇaṃ bhuktau śarīraṃ ca kimucyate  || 7.1,5.30
vāyuruvāca
dikkriyāvyaṃjakā vidyā kālo rāgaḥ pravartakaḥ  || 7.1,5.31
kālo 'vacchedakastatra niyatistu niyāmikā  || 7.1,5.31
avyaktaṃ kāraṇaṃ yattattriguṇaṃ prabhavāpyayam  || 7.1,5.32
pradhānaṃ prakṛtiśceti yadāhustattvaciṃtakāḥ  || 7.1,5.32
kalātastadabhivyaktamanabhivyaktalakṣaṇam  || 7.1,5.33
sukhaduḥkhavimohātmā bhujyate guṇavāṃstridhā  || 7.1,5.33
sattvaṃ rajastama iti guṇāḥ prakṛtisaṃbhavāḥ  || 7.1,5.34
prakṛtau sūkṣmarūpeṇa tile tailamiva sthitāḥ  || 7.1,5.34
sukhaṃ ca sukhahetuśca samāsātsāttvikaṃ smṛtam  || 7.1,5.35
rājasaṃ tadviparyāsātstaṃbhamohau tu tāmasau  || 7.1,5.35
sāttvikyūrdhvagatiḥ proktā tāmasī syādadhogatiḥ  || 7.1,5.36
madhyamā tu gatiryā sā rājasī paripaṭhyate  || 7.1,5.36
tanmātrāpañcakaṃ caiva bhūtapañcakameva ca  || 7.1,5.37
jñāneṃdriyāṇi pañcaikyaṃ pañca karmendriyāṇi ca  || 7.1,5.37
pradhānabuddhyahaṃkāramanāṃsi ca catuṣṭayam  || 7.1,5.38
samāsādevamavyaktaṃ savikāramudāhṛtam  || 7.1,5.38
tatkāraṇadaśāpannamavyaktamiti kathyate  || 7.1,5.39
vyaktaṃ kāryadaśāpannaṃ śarīrādighaṭādivat || 7.1,5.39
507

yathā ghaṭādikaṃ kāryaṃ mṛdādernātibhidyate  || 7.1,5.40
śarīrādi tathā vyaktamavyaktānnātibhidyate  || 7.1,5.40
tasmādavyaktamevaikyakāraṇaṃ karaṇāni ca  || 7.1,5.41
śarīraṃ ca tadādhāraṃ tadbhogyaṃ cāpi netarat || 7.1,5.41
munaya ūcuḥ
buddhīndriyaśarīrebhyo vyatirekasya kasyacit || 7.1,5.42
ātmaśabdābhidheyasya vastuto 'pi kutaḥ sthitiḥ  || 7.1,5.42
vāyuruvāca
buddhīndriyaśarīrebhyo vyatireko vibhordhruvam  || 7.1,5.43
astyeva kaścidātmeti hetustatra sudurgamaḥ  || 7.1,5.43
buddhīndriyaśarīrāṇāṃ nātmatā sadbhiriṣyate  || 7.1,5.44
smṛteraniyatajñānādayāvaddehavedanāt || 7.1,5.44
ataḥ smartānubhūtānāmaśeṣajñeyagocaraḥ  || 7.1,5.45
antaryāmīti vedeṣu vedāṃteṣu ca gīyate  || 7.1,5.45
sarvaṃ tatra sa sarvatra vyāpya tiṣṭhati śāśvataḥ  || 7.1,5.46
tathāpi kvāpi kenāpi vyaktameṣa na dṛśyate  || 7.1,5.46
naivāyaṃ cakṣuṣā grāhyo nāparairindriyairapi  || 7.1,5.47
manasaiva pradīptena mahānātmāvasīyate 1  || 7.1,5.47
na ca strī na pumāneṣa naiva cāpi napuṃsakaḥ  || 7.1,5.48
naivordhvaṃ nāpi tiryaknādhastānna kutaścana  || 7.1,5.48
aśarīraṃ śarīreṣu caleṣu sthāṇumavyayam  || 7.1,5.49
sadā paśyati taṃ dhīro naraḥ pratyavamarśanāt || 7.1,5.49
kimatra bahunoktena puruṣo dehataḥ pṛthak || 7.1,5.50
apṛthagye tu paśyaṃti hyasamyakteṣu darśanam  || 7.1,5.50
yaccharīramidaṃ proktaṃ puruṣasya tataḥ param  || 7.1,5.51
aśuddhamavaśaṃ duḥkhamadhruvaṃ na ca vidyate  || 7.1,5.51
vipadāṃ vījabhūtena puruṣastena saṃyutaḥ  || 7.1,5.52
sukhī duḥkhī ca mūḍhaśca bhavati svena karmaṇā  || 7.1,5.52
adbhirāplavitaṃ kṣetraṃ janayatyaṃkuraṃ yathā  || 7.1,5.53
ājñānātplāvitaṃ karma dehaṃ janayate tathā  || 7.1,5.53
atyaṃtamasukhāvāsāssmṛtāścaikāṃtamṛtyavaḥ  || 7.1,5.54
anāgatā atītāśca tanavo 'sya sahasraśaḥ  || 7.1,5.54
āgatyāgatya śīrṇeṣu śarīreṣu śarīriṇaḥ  || 7.1,5.55
atyaṃtavasatiḥ kvāpi na kenāpi ca labhyate  || 7.1,5.55
chāditaśca viyuktaśca śarīraireṣu lakṣyate  || 7.1,5.56
caṃdrabiṃbavadākāśe taralairabhrasaṃcayaiḥ  || 7.1,5.56
anekadehabhedena bhinnā vṛttirihātmanaḥ  || 7.1,5.57
aṣṭāpadaparikṣepe hyakṣamudreva lakṣyate  || 7.1,5.57
naivāsya bhavitā kaścinnāsau bhavati kasyacit || 7.1,5.58
pathi saṃgama evāyaṃ dāraiḥ putraiśca baṃdhubhiḥ  || 7.1,5.58
yathā kāṣṭhaṃ ca kāṣṭhaṃ ca sameyātāṃ mahodadhau  || 7.1,5.59
sametya ca vyapeyātāṃ tadvadbhūtasamāgamaḥ  || 7.1,5.59
sa paśyati śarīraṃ taccharīraṃ tanna paśyati  || 7.1,5.60
tau paśyati paraḥ kaścittāvubhau taṃ na paśyataḥ  || 7.1,5.60

1 niścīyate

507

brahmādyāḥ sthāvarāṃtaśca paśavaḥ parikīrtitāḥ  || 7.1,5.61
paśūnāmeva sarveṣāṃ proktametannidarśanam  || 7.1,5.61
sa eṣa badhyate pāśaiḥ sukhaduḥkhāśanaḥ paśuḥ  || 7.1,5.62
līlāsādhanabhūto ya īśvarasyeti sūrayaḥ  || 7.1,5.62
ajño jaṃturanīśo 'yamātmanassukhaduḥkhayoḥ  || 7.1,5.63
īśvaraprerito gacchetsvargaṃ vā śvabhrameva vā  || 7.1,5.63
sūta uvāca
ityākarṇyānilavaco munayaḥ prītamānasāḥ  || 7.1,5.64
procuḥ praṇamya taṃ vāyuṃ śaivāgamavicakṣaṇam  || 7.1,5.64

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe śivatattvajñānavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 6
munaya ūcuḥ
yo 'yaṃ paśuriti prokto yaśca pāśa udāhṛtaḥ  || 7.1,6.1
abhyāṃ vilakṣaṇaḥ kaścitkoyamasti tayoḥ patiḥ  || 7.1,6.1
vāyuruvāca
asti kaścidaparyaṃtaramaṇīyaguṇāśrayaḥ  || 7.1,6.2
patirviśvasya nirmātā paśupāśavimocanaḥ  || 7.1,6.2
abhāve tasya viśvasya sṛṣṭireṣā kathaṃ bhavet || 7.1,6.3
acetanatvādajñānādanayoḥ paśupāśayoḥ  || 7.1,6.3
pradhānaparamāṇvādi yāvatkiṃcidacetanam  || 7.1,6.4
tatkartṛkaṃ svayaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ buddhimatkāraṇaṃ vinā  || 7.1,6.4
jagacca kartṛsāpekṣaṃ kāryaṃ sāvayavaṃ yataḥ  || 7.1,6.5
tasmātkāryasya kartṛtvaṃ patyurna paśupāśayoḥ  || 7.1,6.5
paśorapi ca kartṛtvaṃ patyuḥ preraṇapūrvakam  || 7.1,6.6
ayathākaraṇajñānamaṃdhasya gamanaṃ yathā  || 7.1,6.6
ātmānaṃ ca pṛthaṅmatvā preritāraṃ tataḥ pṛthak || 7.1,6.7
asau juṣṭastatastena hyamṛtatvāya kalpate  || 7.1,6.7
paśoḥ pāśasya patyuśca tattvato 'sti padaṃ param  || 7.1,6.8
brahmavittadviditvaiva yonimukto bhaviṣyati  || 7.1,6.8
saṃyuktametaddvitayaṃ kṣaramakṣarameva ca  || 7.1,6.9
vyaktāvyaktaṃ bibhartīśo viśvaṃ viśvavimocakaḥ  || 7.1,6.9
bhoktā bhogyaṃ prerayitā maṃtavyaṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam  || 7.1,6.10
nātaḥ paraṃ vijānadbhirveditavyaṃ hi kiṃcanaḥ  || 7.1,6.10
tileṣu vā yathā tailaṃ dadhni vā sarpirarpitam  || 7.1,6.11
yathāpaḥ srotasi vyāptā yathāraṇyāṃ hutāśanaḥ  || 7.1,6.11
evameva mahātmānamātmanyātmavilakṣaṇam  || 7.1,6.12
satyena tapasā caiva nityayukto 'nupaśyati  || 7.1,6.12
ya eko jālavānīśa īśānībhissvaśaktibhiḥ  || 7.1,6.13
sarvāṃllokānimān kṛtvā eka eva sa īśate 1  || 7.1,6.13
eka eva tadā rudro na dvitīyo 'sti kaścana  || 7.1,6.14
saṃsṛjya viśvabhuvanaṃ goptā te saṃcukoca yaḥ  || 7.1,6.14

1 śablugabhāva ārṣa

508

viśvataścakṣurevāyamutāyaṃ viśvatomukhaḥ  || 7.1,6.15
tathaiva viśvatobāhuviśvataḥ pādasaṃyutaḥ  || 7.1,6.15
dyāvābhūmī ca janayan deva eko maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,6.16
sa eva sarvadevānāṃ prabhavaścodbhavastathā  || 7.1,6.16
hiraṇyagarbhaṃ devānāṃ prathamaṃ janayedayam  || 7.1,6.17
viśvasmādadhiko rudro maharṣiriti hi śrutiḥ  || 7.1,6.17
vedāhametaṃ puruṣaṃ mahāṃtamamṛtaṃ dhruvam  || 7.1,6.18
ādityavarṇaṃ tamasaḥ parastātsaṃsthitaṃ prabhum  || 7.1,6.18
asmānnāsti paraṃ kiṃcidaparaṃ paramātmanaḥ  || 7.1,6.19
nāṇīyo 'sti na ca jyāyastena pūrṇamidaṃ jagat || 7.1,6.19
sarvānanaśirogrīvaḥ sarvabhūtaguhāśayaḥ  || 7.1,6.20
sarvavyāpī ca bhagavāṃstasmātsarvagataśśivaḥ  || 7.1,6.20
sarvataḥ pāṇipādo 'yaṃ sarvato 'kṣiśiromukhaḥ  || 7.1,6.21
sarvataḥ śrutimāṃlloke sarvamāvṛtya tiṣṭhati  || 7.1,6.21
sarvendriyaguṇābhāsassarvendriyavivarjitaḥ  || 7.1,6.22
sarvasya prabhurīśānaḥ sarvasya śaraṇaṃ suhṛt || 7.1,6.22
acakṣurapi yaḥ paśyatyakarṇo 'pi śṛṇoti yaḥ  || 7.1,6.23
sarvaṃ vetti na vettāsya tamāhuḥ puruṣaṃ param  || 7.1,6.23
aṇoraṇīyānmahato mahīyānayamavyayaḥ  || 7.1,6.24
guhāyāṃ nihitaścāpi jaṃtorasya maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,6.24
tamakratuṃ kratuprāyaṃ mahimātiśayānvitam  || 7.1,6.25
dhātuḥ prasādādīśānaṃ vītaśokaḥ prapaśyati  || 7.1,6.25
vedāhamenamajaraṃ purāṇaṃ sarvagaṃ vibhum  || 7.1,6.26
nirodhaṃ janmano yasya vadaṃti brahmavādinaḥ  || 7.1,6.26
eko 'pi trīnimāṃllokān bahudhā śaktiyogataḥ  || 7.1,6.27
vidadhāti vicetyaṃte 1 viśvamādau maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,6.27
viśvadhātrītyajākhyā ca śaivī citrā kṛtiḥ parā  || 7.1,6.28
tāmajāṃ lohitāṃ śuklāṃ kṛṣṇāmekāṃ tvajaḥ prajām  || 7.1,6.28
janitrīmanuśete 'nyojuṣamāṇassvarūpiṇīm  || 7.1,6.29
tāmevājāmajo 'nyastu bhaktabhogā jahāti ca  || 7.1,6.29
dvau suparṇau ca sayujau samānaṃ vṛkṣamāsthitau  || 7.1,6.30
eko 'tti pippalaṃ svādu paro 'naśnan prapaśyati  || 7.1,6.30
vṛkṣesmin puruṣo magno guhyamānaśca śocati  || 7.1,6.31
juṣṭamanyaṃ yadā paśyedīśaṃ paramakāraṇam  || 7.1,6.31
tadāsya mahimānaṃ ca vītaśokassukhī bhavet || 7.1,6.32
chaṃdāṃsi yajñāḥ ṛtavo yadbhūtaṃ bhavyameva ca  || 7.1,6.32
māyī viśvaṃ sṛjatyasminniviṣṭo māyayā paraḥ  || 7.1,6.33
māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ vidyānmāyinaṃ tu maheśvaram  || 7.1,6.33
tasyāstvavayavaireva vyāptaṃ sarvamidaṃ jagat || 7.1,6.34
sūkṣmātisūkṣmamīśānaṃ kalalasyāpi madhyataḥ  || 7.1,6.34

1 vicetoti prayogastvārṣatvena, ’vyatyayo bahulam' iti smaraṇena sādhuḥ
508

sraṣṭāramapi viśvasya veṣṭitāraṃ ca tasya tu  || 7.1,6.35
śivameveśvaraṃ jñātvā śāṃtimatyaṃtamṛcchati  || 7.1,6.35
sa eva kālo goptā ca viśvasyādhipatiḥ prabhuḥ  || 7.1,6.36
taṃ viśvādhipatiṃ jñātvā mṛtyupāśātpramucyate  || 7.1,6.36
ghṛtātparaṃ maṃḍamiva sūkṣmaṃ jñātvā sthitaṃ prabhum  || 7.1,6.37
sarvabhūteṣu gūḍhaṃ ca sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 7.1,6.37
eṣa eva paro devo viśvakarmā maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,6.38
hṛdaye saṃniviṣṭaṃ taṃ jñātvaivāmṛtamaśnute  || 7.1,6.38
yadā samastaṃ na divā na rātrirna sadapyasat || 7.1,6.39
kevalaśśiva evaiko yataḥ prajñā purātanī  || 7.1,6.39
nainamūrdhvaṃ na tiryakca na madhyaṃ paryajigrahat || 7.1,6.40
na tasya pratimā cāsti yasya nāma mahadyaśaḥ  || 7.1,6.40
ajātamimamevaike buddhā janmani bhīravaḥ  || 7.1,6.41
rudrasyāsya prapadyaṃte rakṣārthaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ sukham  || 7.1,6.41
dve akṣare brahmapare tvanaṃte samudāhṛte  || 7.1,6.42
vidyāvidye samākhyāte nihite yatra gūḍhavat || 7.1,6.42
kṣaraṃ tvavidyā hyamṛtaṃ vidyeti parigīyate  || 7.1,6.43
te ubhe īśate yastu so 'nyaḥ khalu maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,6.43
ekaikaṃ bahudhā jālaṃ vikurvannekavacca yaḥ  || 7.1,6.44
sarvādhipatyaṃ kurute sṛṣṭvā sarvān pratāpavān  || 7.1,6.44
diśa ūrdhvamadhastiryakbhāsayan bhrājate svayam  || 7.1,6.45
yo niḥsvabhāvādapyeko vareṇyastvadhitiṣṭhati  || 7.1,6.45
svabhāvavācakān sarvān vācyāṃśca pariṇāmayan  || 7.1,6.46
guṇāṃśca bhogyabhoktṛtve tadviśvamadhitiṣṭhati  || 7.1,6.46
te vai guhyopaṇiṣadi gūḍhaṃ brahma parātparam  || 7.1,6.47
brahmayoniṃ jagatpūrvaṃ vidurdevā maharṣayaḥ  || 7.1,6.47
bhāvagrāhyamanīhākhyaṃ bhāvābhāvakaraṃ śivam  || 7.1,6.48
kalāsargakaraṃ devaṃ ye viduste jahustanum  || 7.1,6.48
svabhāvameke manyaṃte kālameke vimohitāḥ  || 7.1,6.49
devasya mahimā hyeṣa yenedaṃ bhrāmyate jagat || 7.1,6.49
yenedamāvṛtaṃ nityaṃ kālakālātmanā yataḥ  || 7.1,6.50
teneritamidaṃ karma bhūtaiḥ saha vivartate  || 7.1,6.50
tatkarma bhūyaśaḥ kṛtvā vinivṛtya ca bhūyaśaḥ  || 7.1,6.51
tattvasya saha tattvena yogaṃ cāpi sametya vai  || 7.1,6.51
aṣṭābhiśca tribhiścaivaṃ dvābhyāṃ caikena vā punaḥ  || 7.1,6.52
kālenātmaguṇaiścāpi kṛtsnameva jagatsvayam  || 7.1,6.52
guṇairārabhya karmāṇi svabhāvādīni yojayet || 7.1,6.53
teṣāmabhāve nāśaḥ syātkṛtasyāpi ca karmaṇaḥ  || 7.1,6.53
karmakṣaye punaścānyattato yāti sa tattvataḥ  || 7.1,6.54
sa evādissvayaṃ yoganimittaṃ bhoktṛbhogayoḥ  || 7.1,6.54
parastrikālādakalassa eva parameśvaraḥ  || 7.1,6.55
sarvavittriguṇādhīśo brahmasākṣātparātparaḥ  || 7.1,6.55
taṃ viśvarūpamabhavaṃ bhavamīḍyaṃ prajāpatim  || 7.1,6.56
509
devadevaṃ jagatpūjyaṃ svacittasthamupāsmahe  || 7.1,6.56
kālādibhiḥ paro yasmātprapañcaḥ parivartate  || 7.1,6.57
dharmāvahaṃ pāpanudaṃ bhogeśaṃ viśvadhāma ca  || 7.1,6.57
tamīśvarāṇāṃ paramaṃ maheśvaraṃ taṃ devatānāṃ paramaṃ ca daivatam  || 7.1,6.58
patiṃ patīnāṃ paramaṃ parastādvidāma devaṃ bhuvaneśvareśvaram  || 7.1,6.58
na tasya vidyeta kāryaṃ kāraṇaṃ ca na vidyate  || 7.1,6.59
na tatsamo 'dhikaścāpi kvacijjagati dṛśyate  || 7.1,6.59
parāsya vividhā śaktiḥ śrutau svābhāvikī śrutā  || 7.1,6.60
jñānaṃ balaṃ kriyā caiva yābhyo viśvamidaṃ kṛtam  || 7.1,6.60
tasyāsti patiḥ kaścinnaiva liṃgaṃ na ceśitā  || 7.1,6.61
kāraṇaṃ kāraṇānāṃ ca sa teṣāmadhipādhipaḥ  || 7.1,6.61
na cāsya janitā kaścinna ca janma kutaścana  || 7.1,6.62
na janmahetavastadvanmalamāyādisaṃjñakāḥ  || 7.1,6.62
sa ekassarvabhūteṣu gūḍho vyāptaśca viśvataḥ  || 7.1,6.63
sarvabhūtāṃtarātmā ca dharmādhyakṣassa kathyate  || 7.1,6.63
sarvabhūtādhivāsaśca sākṣī cetā ca nirguṇaḥ  || 7.1,6.64
eko vaśī niṣkriyāṇāṃ bahūnāṃ vivaśātmanām  || 7.1,6.64
nityānāmapyasau nityaścetanānāṃ ca cetanaḥ  || 7.1,6.65
eko bahūnāṃ cākāmaḥ kāmānīśaḥ prayacchati  || 7.1,6.65
sāṃkhyayogādhigamyaṃ yatkāraṇaṃ jagatāṃ patim  || 7.1,6.66
jñātvā devaṃ paśuḥ pāśaissarvaireva vimucyate  || 7.1,6.66
viśvakṛdviśvavitsvātmayonijñaḥ kālakṛdguṇī  || 7.1,6.67
pradhānaḥ kṣetrajñapatirguṇeśaḥ pāśamocakaḥ  || 7.1,6.67
brahmāṇaṃ vidadhe pūrvaṃ vedāṃścopādiśatsvayam  || 7.1,6.68
yo devastamahaṃ buddhvā svātmabuddhiprasādataḥ  || 7.1,6.68
mumukṣurasmātsaṃsārātprapadye śaraṇaṃ śivam  || 7.1,6.69
niṣphalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ śāṃtaṃ niravadyaṃ niraṃjanam  || 7.1,6.69
amṛtasya paraṃ setuṃ dagdheṃdhanamivānilam  || 7.1,6.70
yadā carmavadākāśaṃ veṣṭayiṣyaṃti mānavāḥ  || 7.1,6.70
tadā śivamavijñāya duḥkhasyāṃto bhaviṣyati  || 7.1,6.71
tapaḥprabhāvāddevasya prasādācca maharṣayaḥ  || 7.1,6.72
atyāśramocitajñānaṃ pavitraṃ pāpanāśanam  || 7.1,6.72
vedāṃte paramaṃ guhyaṃ purākalpapracoditam  || 7.1,6.73
brahmaṇo vadanāllabdhaṃ mayedaṃ bhāgyagauravāt || 7.1,6.73
nāpraśāṃtāya dātavyametajjñānamanuttamam  || 7.1,6.74
na putrāyāśuvṛttāya nāśiṣyāya ca sarvathā  || 7.1,6.74
yasya deve parābhaktiryathā deve tathā gurau  || 7.1,6.75
tasyaite kathitāhyarthāḥ prakāśaṃte mahātmanaḥ  || 7.1,6.75
ataśca saṃkṣepamidaṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ śivaḥ parastātprakṛteśca puṃsaḥ  || 7.1,6.76
sa sargakāle ca karoti sarvaṃ saṃhārakāle punarādadāti  || 7.1,6.76

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe śivatattvavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṣṭho 'dhyāyaḥ
509


adhyāya 7
munaya ūcuḥ
kālādutpadyate sarvaṃ kāladeva vipadyate  || 7.1,7.1
na kālanirapekṣaṃ hi kvacitkiṃcana vidyate  || 7.1,7.1
yadāsyāṃtargataṃ viśvaṃ śaśvatsaṃsāramaṇḍalam  || 7.1,7.2
sargasaṃhṛtimudrābhyāṃ cakravatparivartate  || 7.1,7.2
brahmā hariśca rudraśca tathānye ca surāsurāḥ  || 7.1,7.3
yatkṛtāṃ niyatiṃ prāpya prabhavo nātivartitum  || 7.1,7.3
bhūtabhavyabhaviṣyādyairvibhajya jarayan prajāḥ  || 7.1,7.4
atiprabhuriti svairaṃ vartate 'tibhayaṃkaraḥ  || 7.1,7.4
ka eṣa bhagavān kālaḥ kasya vā vaśavartyayam  || 7.1,7.5
ka evāsya vaśe na syātkathayaitadvicakṣaṇa  || 7.1,7.5
vāyuruvāca
kālakāṣṭhānimeṣādikalākalitavigraham  || 7.1,7.6
kālātmeti samākhyātaṃ tejo māheśvaraṃ param  || 7.1,7.6
yadalaṃghyamaśeṣasya sthāvarasya carasya ca  || 7.1,7.7
niyogarūpamīśasya balaṃ viśvaniyāmakam  || 7.1,7.7
tasyāṃśāṃśamayī muktiḥ kālātmani mahātmani  || 7.1,7.8
tato niṣkramya saṃkrāṃtā visṛṣṭāgrerivāyasī  || 7.1,7.8
tasmātkālavaśe viśvaṃ na sa viśvavaśe sthitaḥ  || 7.1,7.9
śivasya tu vaśe kālo na kālasya vaśe śivaḥ  || 7.1,7.9
yato 'pratihataṃ śārvaṃ tejaḥ kāle pratiṣṭhitam  || 7.1,7.10
mahatī tena kālasya maryādā hi duratyayā  || 7.1,7.10
kālaṃ prajñāviśeṣeṇa ko 'tivartitumarhati  || 7.1,7.11
kālena tu kṛtaṃ karma na kaścidativartate  || 7.1,7.11
ekacchatrāṃ mahīṃ kṛtsnāṃ ye parākramya śāsati  || 7.1,7.12
te 'pi naivātivartaṃte kālavelāmivābdhayaḥ  || 7.1,7.12
ye nigṛhyeṃdriyagrāmaṃ jayaṃti sakalaṃ jagat || 7.1,7.13
na jayaṃtyapi te kālaṃ kālo jayati tānapi  || 7.1,7.13
āyurvedavido vaidyāstvanuṣṭhitarasāyanāḥ  || 7.1,7.14
na mṛtyumativartaṃte kālo hi duratikramaḥ  || 7.1,7.14
śriyā rūpeṇa śīlena balena ca kulena ca  || 7.1,7.15
anyacciṃtayate jaṃtuḥ kālo 'nyatkurute balāt || 7.1,7.15
apriyaiśca priyaiścaiva hyaciṃtitagamāgamaiḥ  || 7.1,7.16
saṃyojayati bhūtāni viyojayati ceśvaraḥ  || 7.1,7.16
yadaiva duḥkhitaḥ kaścittadaiva sukhitaḥ paraḥ  || 7.1,7.17
durvijñeyasvabhāvasya kālāsyāho vicitratā  || 7.1,7.17
yo yuvā sa bhavedvṛddho yo balīyānsa durbalaḥ  || 7.1,7.18
yaḥ śrīmānso 'pi niḥśrīkaḥ kālaścitragatirdvijā  || 7.1,7.18
nābhijātyaṃ na vai śīlaṃ na balaṃ na ca naipuṇam  || 7.1,7.19
bhavetkāryāya paryāptaṃ kālaśca hyanirodhakaḥ  || 7.1,7.19
ye sanāthāśca dātāro gītavādyairupasthitāḥ  || 7.1,7.20
ye cānāthāḥ parānnādāḥ kālasteṣu samakriyaḥ  || 7.1,7.20
phalaṃtyakāle na rasāyanāni samyakprayuktānyapi cauṣadhāni  || 7.1,7.21
tānyeva kālena samāhṛtāni siddhiṃ prayāṃtyāśu sukhaṃ diśaṃti  || 7.1,7.21
510

nākālato 'yaṃ mriyate jāyate vā nākālataḥ puṣṭimagryāmupaiti  || 7.1,7.22
nākālataḥ sukhitaṃ duḥkhitaṃ vā nākālikaṃ vastu samasti kiṃcit || 7.1,7.22
kālena śītaḥ prativāti vātaḥkālena vṛṣṭirjaladānupaiti  || 7.1,7.23
kālena coṣmā praśamaṃ prayāti kālena sarvaṃ saphalatvameti  || 7.1,7.23
kālaśca sarvasya bhavasya hetuḥ kālena sasyāni bhavaṃti nityam  || 7.1,7.24
kālena sasyāni layaṃ prayāṃti kālena saṃjīvati jīvalokaḥ  || 7.1,7.24
itthaṃ kālātmanastattvaṃ yo vijānāti tattvataḥ  || 7.1,7.25
kālātmānamatikramya kālātītaṃ sa paśyati  || 7.1,7.25
na yasya kālo na ca baṃdhamuktī na yaḥ pumānna prakṛtirna viśvam  || 7.1,7.26
vicitrarūpāya śivāya tasmai namaḥparasmai parameśvarāya  || 7.1,7.26

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe kālamahimavarṇanaṃ nāma saptamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 8
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
kena mānena kālesminnāyussaṃkhyā prakalpyate  || 7.1,8.1
saṃkhyārūpasya kālasya kaḥ punaḥ paramo 'vadhiḥ  || 7.1,8.1
vāyuruvāca
āyuṣo 'tra nimeṣākhyamādyamānaṃ pracakṣate  || 7.1,8.2
saṃkhyārūpasya kālasya śāṃttvatītakalāvadhi  || 7.1,8.2
akṣipakṣmaparikṣepo nimeṣaḥ parikalpitaḥ  || 7.1,8.3
tādṛśānāṃ nimeṣāṇāṃ kāṣṭhā daśa ca pañca ca  || 7.1,8.3
kāṣṭhāṃstriṃśatkalā nāma kalāṃstriṃśanmuhūrtakaḥ  || 7.1,8.4
muhūrtānāmapi triṃśadahorātraṃ pracakṣate  || 7.1,8.4
triṃśatsaṃkhyairahorātrairmāsaḥ pakṣadvayātmakaḥ  || 7.1,8.5
jñeyaṃ pitryamahorātraṃ māsaḥ kṛṣṇasitātmakaḥ  || 7.1,8.6
māsaistairayanaṃ ṣaḍbhirvarṣaṃ dve cāyanaṃ matam  || 7.1,8.7
laukikenaiva mānena abdo yo mānuṣaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.1,8.7
etaddivyamahorātramiti śāstrasya niścayaḥ  || 7.1,8.8
dakṣiṇaṃ cāyanaṃ rātristathodagayanaṃ dinam  || 7.1,8.8
māsastriṃśadahorātrairdivyo mānuṣavatsmṛtaḥ  || 7.1,8.9
saṃvatsaro 'pi devānāṃ māsairdvādaśabhistathā  || 7.1,8.9
trīṇi varṣaśatānyeva ṣaṣṭivarṣayutānyapi  || 7.1,8.10
divyassaṃvatsaro jñeyo mānuṣeṇa prakīrtitaḥ  || 7.1,8.10
divyenaiva pramāṇena yugasaṃkhyā pravartate  || 7.1,8.11
catvāri bhārate varṣe yugāni kavayo viduḥ  || 7.1,8.11
pūrvaṃ kṛtayugaṃ nāma tatastretā vidhīyate  || 7.1,8.12
dvāparaṃ ca kaliścaiva yugānyetāni kṛtsnaśaḥ  || 7.1,8.12
catvāri tu sahasrāṇi varṣāṇāṃ tatkṛtaṃ yugam  || 7.1,8.13
tasya tāvacchatīsaṃdhyā saṃdhyāṃśaśca tathāvidhaḥ  || 7.1,8.13
itareṣu sasaṃdhyeṣu sasaṃdhyāṃśeṣu ca triṣu  || 7.1,8.14
ekāpāyena vartaṃte sahasrāṇi śatāni ca  || 7.1,8.14
etaddvādaśasāhasraṃ sādhikaṃ ca caturyugam  || 7.1,8.15
caturyugasahasraṃ yatsaṃkalpa iti kathyate  || 7.1,8.15
caturyugaikasaptatyā manoraṃtaramucyate  || 7.1,8.16
kalpe caturdaśaikasminmanūnāṃ parivṛttayaḥ  || 7.1,8.16
etena kramayogena kalpamanvaṃtarāṇi ca  || 7.1,8.17
saprajāni vyatītāni śataśo 'tha sahasraśaḥ  || 7.1,8.17
ajñeyatvācca sarveṣāmasaṃkhyeyatayā punaḥ  || 7.1,8.18
śakyo naivānupūrvyādvai teṣāṃ vaktuṃ suvistaraḥ  || 7.1,8.18
kalpo nāma divā prokto brahmaṇo 'vyaktajanmanaḥ  || 7.1,8.19
kalpānāṃ vai sahasraṃ ca brāhmaṃ varṣamihocyate  || 7.1,8.19
varṣāṇāmaṣṭasāhasraṃ yacca tadbrahmaṇo yugam  || 7.1,8.20
savanaṃ yugasāhasraṃ brahmaṇaḥ padmajanmanaḥ  || 7.1,8.20
savanānāṃ sahasraṃ ca triguṇaṃ trivṛtaṃ tathā  || 7.1,8.21
kalpyate sakalaḥ kālo brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 7.1,8.21
tasya vai divase yāṃti caturdaśa puraṃdarāḥ  || 7.1,8.22
śatāni māse catvāri viṃśatyā sahitāni ca  || 7.1,8.22
abde pañca sahasrāṇi catvāriṃśadyutāni ca  || 7.1,8.23
catvāriṃśatsahasrāṇi pañca lakṣāṇi cāyuṣi  || 7.1,8.23
brahmā viṣṇordine caiko viṣṇū rudradine tathā  || 7.1,8.24
īśvarasya dine rudrassadākhyasya tatheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,8.24
sākṣācchivasya tatsaṃkhyastathā so 'pi sadāśivaḥ  || 7.1,8.25
catvāriṃśatsahasrāṇi pañcalakṣāṇi cāyuṣi  || 7.1,8.25
tasminsākṣācchivenaiṣa kālātmā sampravartate  || 7.1,8.26*1
yattatsṛṣṭessamākhyātaṃ kālāntaramiha dvijāḥ  || 7.1,8.26*1
etatkālāntaraṃ jñeyamaharvai pārameśvaram  || 7.1,8.26*2
rātriśca tāvatī jñeyā parameśasya kṛtsnaśaḥ  || 7.1,8.26*2
ahastasya tu yā sṛṣṭī rātriśca pralayaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.1,8.27
aharna vidyate tasya na rātririti dhārayet || 7.1,8.27
eṣopacāraḥ kriyate lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 7.1,8.28
prajāḥ prajānāṃ patayo mūrtayaśca surāsurāḥ  || 7.1,8.28
indriyāṇīndriyārthāśca mahābhūtāni pañca ca  || 7.1,8.29
tanmātrāṇyatha bhūtādirbuddhiśca saha daivataḥ  || 7.1,8.29
ahastiṣṭhaṃti sarvāṇi pārameśasya dhīmataḥ  || 7.1,8.30
aharaṃte pralīyante rātryante viśvasaṃbhavaḥ  || 7.1,8.30
yo viśvātmā karmakālasvabhāvādyarthe śaktiryasya nollaṃghanīyā  || 7.1,8.31
yasyaivājñādhīnametatsamastaṃ namastasmai mahate śaṃkarāya  || 7.1,8.31

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvabhāge kālaprabhāve tridevāyurvarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 9
munaya ūcuḥ
kathaṃ jagadidaṃ kṛtsnaṃ vidhāya ca nidhāya ca  || 7.1,9.1
ājñayā paramāṃ krīḍāṃ karoti parameśvaraḥ  || 7.1,9.1
kiṃ tatprathamasaṃbhūtaṃ kenedamakhilaṃ tatam  || 7.1,9.2
kenā vā punarevedaṃ grasyate pṛthukukṣiṇā  || 7.1,9.2
vāyuruvāca
śaktiḥ prathamasambhūtā śāṃtyatītapadottarā  || 7.1,9.3
tato māyā tato 'vyaktaṃ śivācchaktimataḥ prabhoḥ  || 7.1,9.3
511

śāntyatītapadaṃ śaktestataḥ śāntipadakramāt || 7.1,9.4
tato vidyāpadaṃ tasmātpratiṣṭhāpadasaṃbhavaḥ  || 7.1,9.4
nivṛttipadamutpannaṃ pratiṣṭhāpadataḥ kramāt || 7.1,9.5
evamuktā samāsena sṛṣṭirīśvaracoditā  || 7.1,9.5
ānulomyāttathaiteṣāṃ pratilomyena saṃhṛtiḥ  || 7.1,9.6
asmātpañcapadoddiṣṭātparassraṣṭā samiṣyate  || 7.1,9.6
kalābhiḥ pañcabhirvyāptaṃ tasmādviśvamidaṃ jagat || 7.1,9.7
avyaktaṃ kāraṇaṃ yattadātmanā samanuṣṭhitam  || 7.1,9.7
mahadādiviśeṣāṃtaṃ sṛjatītyapi saṃmatam  || 7.1,9.8
kiṃ tu tatrāpi kartṛtvaṃ nāvyaktasya na cātmanaḥ  || 7.1,9.8
acetanatvātprakṛterajñatvātpuruṣasya ca  || 7.1,9.9
pradhānaparamāṇvādi yāvatkiñcidacetanam  || 7.1,9.9
tatkartṛkaṃ svayaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ buddhimatkāraṇaṃ vinā  || 7.1,9.10
jagacca kartṛsāpekṣaṃ kāryaṃ sāvayavaṃ yataḥ  || 7.1,9.10
tasmācchaktassvatantro yaḥ sarvaśaktiśca sarvavit || 7.1,9.11
anādinidhanaścāyaṃ mahadaiśvaryasaṃyutaḥ  || 7.1,9.11
sa eva jagataḥ kartā mahādevo maheśvarāḥ  || 7.1,9.12
pātā hartā ca sarvasya tataḥ pṛthagananvayaḥ  || 7.1,9.12
pariṇāmaḥ pradhānasya pravṛttiḥ puruṣasya ca  || 7.1,9.13
sarvaṃ satyavratasyaiva śāsanena pravartate  || 7.1,9.13
itīyaṃ śāśvatī niṣṭhā satāṃ manasi vartate  || 7.1,9.14
na cainaṃ pakṣamāśritya vartate svalpacetanaḥ  || 7.1,9.14
yāvadādisamāraṃbho yāvadyaḥ pralayo mahān  || 7.1,9.15
tāvadapyeti sakalaṃ brahmaṇaḥ śāradāṃ śatam  || 7.1,9.15
paramityāyuṣo nāma brahmaṇo 'vyaktajanmanaḥ  || 7.1,9.16
tatparākhyaṃ tadardhaṃ ca parārdhamabhidhīyate  || 7.1,9.16
parārdhadvayakālāṃte pralaye samupasthite  || 7.1,9.17
avyaktamātmanaḥ kāryamādāyātmani tiṣṭhati  || 7.1,9.17
ātmanyavasthite 'vyakte vikāre pratisaṃhṛte  || 7.1,9.18
sādharmyeṇādhitiṣṭhete pradhānapuruṣāvubhau  || 7.1,9.18
tamaḥ sattvaguṇāvetau samatvena vyavasthitau  || 7.1,9.19
anudriktāvanantau tāvotaprotau parasparam  || 7.1,9.19
guṇasāmye tadā tasminnavibhāge tamodaye  || 7.1,9.20
śāṃtavātaikanīre ca na prājñāyata kiṃcana  || 7.1,9.20
aprajñāte jagatyasminneka eva maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,9.21
upāsya rajanīṃ kṛtsnāṃ parāṃ māheśvarīṃ tataḥ  || 7.1,9.21
prabhātāyāṃ tu śarvaryāṃ pradhānapuruṣāvubhau  || 7.1,9.22
praviśya kṣobhayāmāsa māyāyogānmaheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,9.22
tataḥ punaraśeṣāṇāṃ bhūtānāṃ prabhavāpyayāt || 7.1,9.23
avyaktādabhavatsṛṣṭirājñayā parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 7.1,9.23
viśvottarottaravicitramanorathasya yasyaikaśaktiśakale sakalassamāptaḥ  || 7.1,9.24
ātmānamadhvapatimadhvavido vadaṃti tasmai namaḥ sakalalokavilakṣaṇāya  || 7.1,9.24


iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvabhāge sṛṣṭipālanapralayakartṛtvavarṇanaṃ nāma navamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 10
vāyuruvāca
puruṣādhiṣṭhitātpūrvamavyaktādīśvarājñayā  || 7.1,10.1
buddhyādayo viśeṣāṃtā vikārāścābhavan kramāt || 7.1,10.1
tatastebhyo vikārebhyo rudro viṣṇuḥ pitāmahaḥ  || 7.1,10.2
kāraṇatvena sarveṣāṃ trayo devāḥ prajajñire  || 7.1,10.2
sarvato bhuvanavyāptiśaktimavyāhatāṃ kvacit || 7.1,10.3
jñānamapratimaṃ śaśvadaiśvaryaṃ cāṇimādikam  || 7.1,10.3
sṛṣṭisthitilayākhyeṣu karmasu triṣu hetutām  || 7.1,10.4
prabhutvena sahaiteṣāṃ prasīdati maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,10.4
kalpāntare punasteṣāmaspardhā buddhimohinām  || 7.1,10.5
sargarakṣālayācāraṃ pratyekaṃ pradadau ca saḥ  || 7.1,10.5
ete parasparotpannā dhārayanti parasparam  || 7.1,10.6
paraspareṇa vardhaṃte parasparamanuvratāḥ  || 7.1,10.6
kvacidbrahmā kvacidviṣṇuḥ kvacidrudraḥ praśasyate  || 7.1,10.7
nānena teṣāmādhikyamaiśvaryaṃ cātiricyate  || 7.1,10.7
mūrkhā niṃdaṃti tānvāgbhiḥ saṃraṃbhābhiniveśinaḥ  || 7.1,10.8
yātudhānā bhavaṃtyeva piśācāśca na saṃśayaḥ  || 7.1,10.8
devo guṇatrayātītaścaturvyūho maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,10.9
sakalassakalādhāraśakterutpattikāraṇam  || 7.1,10.9
soyamātmā trayasyāsya prakṛteḥ puruṣasya ca  || 7.1,10.10
līlākṛtajagatsṛṣṭirīśvaratve vyavasthitaḥ  || 7.1,10.10
yassarvasmātparo nityo niṣkalaḥ parameśvaraḥ  || 7.1,10.11
sa eva ca tadādhārastadātmā tadadhiṣṭhitaḥ  || 7.1,10.11
tasmānmaheśvaraścaiva prakṛtiḥ puruṣastathā  || 7.1,10.12
sadāśivabhavo viṣṇurbrahmā sarvaśivātmakam  || 7.1,10.12
pradhānātprathamaṃ jajñe vṛddhiḥ khyātirmatirmahān  || 7.1,10.13
mahattattvasya saṃkṣobhādahaṃkārastridhā 'bhavat || 7.1,10.13
ahaṃkāraśca bhūtāni tanmātrānīṃdriyāṇi ca  || 7.1,10.14
vaikārikādahaṃkārātsattvodriktāttu sāttvikaḥ  || 7.1,10.14
vaikārikaḥ sa sargastu yugapatsaṃpravartate  || 7.1,10.15
buddhīndriyāṇi pañcaiva pañcakarmeṃdriyāṇi ca  || 7.1,10.15
ekādaśaṃ manastatra svaguṇenobhayātmakam  || 7.1,10.16
tamoyuktādahaṃkārādbhūtatanmātrasaṃbhavaḥ  || 7.1,10.16
bhūtānāmādibhūtatvādbhūtādiḥ kathyate tu saḥ  || 7.1,10.17
bhūtādeśśabdamātraṃ syāttatra cākāśasaṃbhavaḥ  || 7.1,10.17
ākāśātsparśa utpannaḥ sparśādvāyusamudbhavaḥ  || 7.1,10.18
vāyo rūpaṃ tatastejastejaso rasasaṃbhavaḥ  || 7.1,10.18
rasādāpassamutpannāstebhyo gandhasamudbhavaḥ  || 7.1,10.19
gandhācca pṛthivī jātā bhūtebhyonyaccarācaram  || 7.1,10.19
puruṣādhiṣṭhitatvācca avyaktānugraheṇa ca  || 7.1,10.20
mahadādiviśeṣāntā hyaṇḍamutpādayanti te  || 7.1,10.20
512
tatra kāryaṃ ca karaṇaṃ saṃsiddhaṃ brahmaṇo yadā  || 7.1,10.21
tadaṃḍe supravṛddho 'bhūtkṣetrajño brahmasaṃjñitaḥ  || 7.1,10.21
sa vai śarīrī prathamaḥ sa vai puruṣa ucyate  || 7.1,10.22
ādikartā sa bhūtānāṃ brahmāgre samavartata  || 7.1,10.22
tasyeśvarasya pratimā jñānavairāgyalakṣaṇā  || 7.1,10.23
dharmaiśvaryakarī buddhirbrāhmī yajñe 'bhimāninaḥ  || 7.1,10.23
avyaktājjāyate tasya manasā yadyadīpsitam  || 7.1,10.24
vaśī vikṛtvāttraiguṇyātsāpekṣatvātsvabhāvataḥ  || 7.1,10.24
tridhā vibhajya cātmānaṃ trailokye saṃpravartate  || 7.1,10.25
sṛjate grasate caiva vīkṣate ca tribhissvayam  || 7.1,10.25
caturmukhastu brahmatve kālatve cāṃtakassmṛtaḥ  || 7.1,10.26
sahasramūrdhā puruṣastisrovasthāssvayaṃbhuvaḥ  || 7.1,10.26
sattvaṃ rajaśca brahmā ca kālatve ca tamo rajaḥ  || 7.1,10.27
viṣṇutve kevalaṃ sattvaṃ guṇavṛddhistridhā vibhau  || 7.1,10.27
brahmatve sṛjate lokān kālatve saṃkṣipatyapi  || 7.1,10.28
puruṣatve 'tyudāsīnaḥ karma ca trividhaṃ vibhoḥ  || 7.1,10.28
evaṃ tridhā vibhinnatvādbrahmā triguṇa ucyate  || 7.1,10.29
caturdhā pravibhaktatvāccāturvyūhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  || 7.1,10.29
āditvādādidevo 'sāvajātatvādajaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.1,10.30
pāti yasmātprajāḥ sarvāḥ prajāpatiriti smṛtaḥ  || 7.1,10.30
hiraṇmayastu yo merustasyolbaṃ sumahātmanaḥ  || 7.1,10.31
garbhodakaṃ samudrāśca jarāyuścā'pi parvatāḥ  || 7.1,10.31
tasminnaṃḍe tvime lokā aṃtarviśvamidaṃ jagat || 7.1,10.32
caṃdrādityau sanakṣatrau sagrahau saha vāyunā  || 7.1,10.32
adbhirdaśaguṇābhistu bāhyatoṇḍaṃ samāvṛtam  || 7.1,10.33
āpo daśaguṇenaiva tejasā bahirāvṛtāḥ  || 7.1,10.33
tejo daśaguṇenaiva vāyunā bahirāvṛtam  || 7.1,10.34
ākāśenāvṛto vāyuḥ khaṃ ca bhūtādināvṛtam  || 7.1,10.34
bhūtādirmahatā tadvadavyaktenāvṛto mahān  || 7.1,10.35
etairāvaraṇairaṇḍaṃ saptabhirbahirāvṛtam  || 7.1,10.35
etadāvṛttya cānyonyamaṣṭau prakṛtayaḥ sthitāḥ  || 7.1,10.36
sṛṣṭipālanavidhvaṃsakarmakartryo dvijottamāḥ  || 7.1,10.36
evaṃ parasparotpannā dhārayaṃti parasparam  || 7.1,10.37
ādhārādheyabhāvena vikārāstu vikāriṣu  || 7.1,10.37
kūrmoṃgāni yathā pūrvaṃ prasārya viniyacchati  || 7.1,10.38
vikārāṃśca tathā 'vyaktaṃ sṛṣṭvā bhūyo niyacchati  || 7.1,10.38
avyaktaprabhavaṃ sarvamānulomyena jāyate  || 7.1,10.39
prāpte pralayakāle tu pratilomyenulīyate  || 7.1,10.39
guṇāḥ kālavaśādeva bhavaṃti viṣamāḥ samāḥ  || 7.1,10.40
guṇasāmye layo jñeyo vaiṣamye sṛṣṭirucyate  || 7.1,10.40
tadidaṃ brahmaṇo yoniretadaṃḍaṃ ghanaṃ mahat || 7.1,10.41
brahmaṇaḥ kṣetramuddiṣṭaṃ brahmā kṣetrajña ucyate  || 7.1,10.41
itīdṛśānāmaṇḍānāṃ koṭyo jñeyāḥ sahasraśaḥ  || 7.1,10.42
sarvagatvātpradhānasya tiryagūrdhvamadhaḥ sthitāḥ  || 7.1,10.42
512

tatra tatra caturvaktrā brahmāṇo harayo bhavāḥ  || 7.1,10.43
sṛṣṭā pradhānena tathā labdhvā śaṃbhostu sannidhim  || 7.1,10.43
maheśvaraḥ parovyaktādaṃḍamavyaktasaṃbhavam  || 7.1,10.44
aṇḍājjajñe vibhurbrahmā lokāstena kṛtāstvime  || 7.1,10.44
abuddhipūrvaḥ kathito mayaiṣa pradhānasargaḥ prathamaḥ pravṛtaḥ  || 7.1,10.45
ātyaṃtikaśca pralayontakāle līlākṛtaḥ kevalamīśvarasya  || 7.1,10.45
yattatsmṛtaṃ kāraṇamaprameyaṃ brahmā pradhānaṃ prakṛteḥ prasūtiḥ  || 7.1,10.46
anādimadhyāntamanantavīryaṃ śuklaṃ suraktaṃ puruṣeṇa yuktam  || 7.1,10.46
utpādakatvādrajasotirekāllokasya saṃtānavivṛddhihetūn  || 7.1,10.47
aṣṭau vikārānapi cādikāle sṛṣṭvā samaśnāti tathāṃtakāle  || 7.1,10.47
prakṛtyavasthāpitakāraṇānāṃ yā ca sthitiryā ca punaḥ pravṛttiḥ  || 7.1,10.48
tatsarvamaprākṛtavaibhavasya saṃkalpamātreṇa maheśvarasya  || 7.1,10.48

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ brahmāṃḍasthitivarṇanaṃ nāma daśamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 11
munaya ūcuḥ
manvaṃtarāṇi sarvāṇi kalpabhedāṃśca sarvaśaḥ  || 7.1,11.1
teṣvevāṃtarasargaṃ ca pratisargaṃ ca no vada  || 7.1,11.1
vāyuruvāca
kālasaṃkhyāvivṛttasya parārdho brahmaṇassmṛtaḥ  || 7.1,11.2
tāvāṃścaivāsya kālonyastasyāṃte pratisṛjyate  || 7.1,11.2
divase divase tasya brahmaṇaḥ pūrvajanmanaḥ  || 7.1,11.3
caturdaśamahābhāgā manūnāṃ parivṛttayaḥ  || 7.1,11.3
anāditvādanaṃtatvādajñeyatvācca kṛtsnaśaḥ  || 7.1,11.4
manvaṃtarāṇi kalpāśca na śakyā vacanātpṛthak || 7.1,11.4
ukteṣvapi ca sarveṣu śṛṇvatāṃ vo vaco mama  || 7.1,11.5
kimihāsti phalaṃ tasmānna pṛthakvaktumutsahe  || 7.1,11.5
ya eva khalu kalpeṣu kalpaḥ saṃprati vartate  || 7.1,11.6
tatra saṃkṣipya vartaṃte sṛṣṭayaḥ pratisṛṣṭayaḥ  || 7.1,11.6
yastvayaṃ vartate kalpo vārāho nāma nāmataḥ  || 7.1,11.7
asminnapi dvijaśreṣṭhā manavastu caturdaśa  || 7.1,11.7
svāyaṃbhuvādayassapta sapta sāvarṇikādayaḥ  || 7.1,11.8
teṣu vaivasvato nāma saptamo vartate manuḥ  || 7.1,11.8
manvaṃtareṣu sarveṣu sargasaṃhāravṛttayaḥ  || 7.1,11.9
prāyaḥ samābhavaṃtīti tarkaḥ kāryo vijānatā  || 7.1,11.9
pūrvakalpe parāvṛtte pravṛtte kālamārute  || 7.1,11.10
samunmūlitamūleṣu vṛkṣeṣu ca vaneṣu ca  || 7.1,11.10
jagaṃti tṛṇavaktrīṇi deve dahati pāvake  || 7.1,11.11
vṛṣṭyā bhuvi niṣiktāyāṃ viveleṣvarṇaveṣu ca  || 7.1,11.11
dikṣu sarvāsu magnāsu vāripūre mahīyasi  || 7.1,11.12
tadadbhiścaṭulākṣepaistaraṃgabhujamaṇḍalaiḥ  || 7.1,11.12
prārabdhacaṇḍanṛtyeṣu tataḥ pralayavāriṣu  || 7.1,11.13
brahmā nārāyaṇo bhūtvā suṣvāpa salile sukham  || 7.1,11.13
513

imaṃ codāharanmaṃtraṃ ślokaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ prati  || 7.1,11.14
taṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ muniśreṣṭhāstadarthaṃ cākṣarāśrayam  || 7.1,11.14
āpo nārā iti proktā āpo vai narasūnavaḥ  || 7.1,11.15
ayanaṃ tasya tā yasmāttena nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.1,11.15
śivayogamayīṃ nidrāṃ kurvantaṃ tridaśeśvaram  || 7.1,11.16
baddhāṃjali puṭāssiddhā janalokanivāsinaḥ  || 7.1,11.16
stotraiḥ prabodhayāmāsuḥ prabhātasamaye surāḥ  || 7.1,11.17
yathā sṛṣṭyādisamaye īśvaraṃ śrutayaḥ purā  || 7.1,11.17
tataḥ prabuddha utthāya śayanāttoyamadhyagāt || 7.1,11.18
udaikṣata diśaḥ sarvā yoganidrālasekṣaṇaḥ  || 7.1,11.18
nāpaśyatsa tadā kiṃcitsvātmano vyatireki yat || 7.1,11.19
savismaya ivāsīnaḥ parāṃ ciṃtāmupāgamat || 7.1,11.19
kva sā bhagavatī yā tu manojñā mahatī mahī  || 7.1,11.20
nānāvidhamahāśailanadīnagarakānanā  || 7.1,11.20
evaṃ saṃciṃtayanbrahmā bubudhe naiva bhūsthitim  || 7.1,11.21
tadā sasmāra pitaraṃ bhagavaṃtaṃ trilocanam  || 7.1,11.21
smaraṇāddevadevasya bhavasyāmitatejasaḥ  || 7.1,11.22
jñātavānsalile magnāṃ dharaṇīṃ dharaṇīpatiḥ  || 7.1,11.22
tato bhūmessamuddhāraṃ kartukāmaḥ prajāpatiḥ  || 7.1,11.23
jalakrīḍocitaṃ divyaṃ vārāhaṃ rūpamasmarat || 7.1,11.23
mahāparvatavarṣmāṇaṃ mahājaladaniḥsvanam  || 7.1,11.24
nīlameghapratīkāśaṃ dīptaśabdaṃ bhayānakam  || 7.1,11.24
pīnavṛttaghanaskaṃdhapīnonnatakaṭītaṭam  || 7.1,11.25
hrasvavṛttorujaṃghāgraṃ sutīkṣṇapuramaṇḍalam  || 7.1,11.25
padmarāgamaṇiprakhyaṃ vṛttabhīṣaṇalocanam  || 7.1,11.26
vṛttadīrghamahāgātraṃ stabdhakarṇasthalojjvalam  || 7.1,11.26
udīrṇocchvāsaniśvāsaghūrṇitapralayārṇavam  || 7.1,11.27
visphuratsusaṭācchannakapolaskaṃdhabaṃdhuram  || 7.1,11.27
maṇibhirbhūṣaṇaiścitrairmahāratnaiḥpariṣkṛtam  || 7.1,11.28
virājamānaṃ vidyudbhirmeghasaṃghamivonnatam  || 7.1,11.28
āsthāya vipulaṃ rūpaṃ vārāhamamitaṃ vidhiḥ  || 7.1,11.29
pṛthivyuddharaṇārthāya praviveśa rasātalam  || 7.1,11.29
sa tadā śuśubhe 'tīva sūkaro girisaṃnibhaḥ  || 7.1,11.30
liṃgākṛtermaheśasya pādamūlaṃ gato yathā  || 7.1,11.30
tatassa salile magnāṃ pṛthivīṃ pṛthivīṃdharaḥ  || 7.1,11.31
uddhṛtyāliṃgya daṃṣṭrābhyāmunmamajja rasātalāt || 7.1,11.31
taṃ dṛṣṭvā munayassiddhā janalokanivāsinaḥ  || 7.1,11.32
mumudurnanṛturmūrdhni tasya puṣpairavākiran  || 7.1,11.32
vapurmahāvarāhasya śuśubhe puṣpasaṃvṛtam  || 7.1,11.33
patadbhiriva khadyotaiḥ prāśuraṃjanaparvataḥ  || 7.1,11.33
tataḥ saṃsthānamānīya varāho mahatīṃ mahīm  || 7.1,11.34
svameva rūpamāsthāya sthāpayāmāsa vai vibhuḥ  || 7.1,11.34
pṛthivīṃ ca samīkṛtya pṛthivyāṃ sthāpayangirīn  || 7.1,11.35
513

bhūrādyāṃścaturo lokān kalpayāmāsa pūrvavat || 7.1,11.35
iti saha mahatīṃ mahīṃ mahīdhraiḥ pralayamahājaladheradhaḥsthamadhyāt || 7.1,11.36
upari ca viniveśya viśvakarmā caramacaraṃ ca jagatsasarja bhūyaḥ  || 7.1,11.36

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vā pū sṛṣṭyādivarṇanaṃ nāmaikādaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 12
vāyuruvāca
sargaṃ ciṃtayatastasya tadā vai buddhipūrvakam  || 7.1,12.1
pradhyānakāle mohastu prādurbhūtastamomayaḥ  || 7.1,12.1
tamomoho mahāmohastāmisraścāndhasaṃjñitaḥ  || 7.1,12.2
avidyā pañcamī caiṣā prādurbhūtā mahātmanaḥ  || 7.1,12.2
pañcadhā 'vasthitaḥ sargo dhyāyatastvabhimāninaḥ  || 7.1,12.3
sarvatastamasātīva bījakumbhavadāvṛtaḥ  || 7.1,12.3
bahirantaścāprakāśaḥ stabdho niḥsaṃjña eva ca  || 7.1,12.4
tasmātteṣāṃ vṛtā buddhirmukhāni karaṇāni ca  || 7.1,12.4
tasmātte saṃvṛtātmāno nagā mukhyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  || 7.1,12.5
taṃ dṛṣṭvā'sādhakaṃ brahmā prathamaṃ sargamīdṛśam  || 7.1,12.5
aprasannamanā bhūtvā dvitīyaṃ so 'bhyamanyata  || 7.1,12.6
tasyābhidhāyataḥ sargaṃ tiryaksroto 'bhyavartata  || 7.1,12.6
antaḥprakāśāstiryaṃca āvṛtāśca bahiḥ punaḥ  || 7.1,12.7
paśvātmānastato jātā utpathagrāhiṇaśca te  || 7.1,12.7
tamapyasādhakaṃ jñātvā sargamanyamamanyata  || 7.1,12.8
tadordhvasrotaso vṛtto devasargastu sāttvikaḥ  || 7.1,12.8
te sukhaprītibahulā bahirantaśca nāvṛtāḥ  || 7.1,12.9
prakāśā bahirantaścasvabhāvādeva saṃjñitāḥ  || 7.1,12.9
tato 'bhidhyāyatovyaktādarvāksrotastu sādhakaḥ  || 7.1,12.10
manuṣyanāmā sañjātaḥ sargo duḥkhasamutkaṭaḥ  || 7.1,12.10
prakāśābahirantaste tamodriktā rajo 'dhikāḥ  || 7.1,12.11
pañcamonugrahaḥ sargaścaturdhā saṃvyavasthitaḥ  || 7.1,12.11
viparyayeṇa śaktyā ca tuṣṭyāsiddhyā tathaiva ca  || 7.1,12.12
te 'parigrāhiṇaḥ sarve saṃvibhāgaratāḥ punaḥ  || 7.1,12.12
khādanāścāpyaśīlāśca bhūtādyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ  || 7.1,12.13
prathamo mahataḥ sargo brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 7.1,12.13
tanmātrāṇāṃ dvitīyastu bhūtasargaḥ sa ucyate  || 7.1,12.14
vaikārikastṛtīyastu sarga aindriyakaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.1,12.14
ityeṣa prakṛteḥ sargaḥ sambhṛto 'buddhipūrvakaḥ  || 7.1,12.15
mukhyasargaścaturthastu mukhyā vai sthāvarāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 7.1,12.15
tiryaksrotastu yaḥ proktastiryagyoniḥ sa pañcamaḥ  || 7.1,12.16
tadūrdhvasrotasaḥ ṣaṣṭho devasargastu sa smṛtaḥ  || 7.1,12.16
tato 'rvāksrotasāṃ sargaḥ saptamaḥ sa tu mānuṣaḥ  || 7.1,12.17
aṣṭamo 'nugrahaḥ sargaḥ kaumāro navamaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.1,12.17
prākṛtāśca trayaḥ pūrve sargāste 'buddhipūrvakāḥ  || 7.1,12.18
514

buddhipūrvaṃ pravartante mukhyādyāḥ pañca vaikṛtāḥ  || 7.1,12.18
agre sasarja vai brahmā mānasānātmanaḥ samān  || 7.1,12.19
sanandaṃ sanakañcaiva vidvāṃsañca sanātanam  || 7.1,12.19
ṛbhuṃ sanatkumārañca pūrvameva prajāpatiḥ  || 7.1,12.20
sarve te yogino jñeyā vītarāgā vimatsarāḥ  || 7.1,12.20
iśvarāsaktamanaso na cakruḥ sṛṣṭaye matim  || 7.1,12.21
teṣu sṛṣṭyanapekṣeṣu gateṣu sanakādiṣu  || 7.1,12.21
sraṣṭukāmaḥ punarbrahmā tatāpa paramaṃ tapaḥ  || 7.1,12.22
tasyaivaṃ tapyamānasya na kiṃcitsamavartata  || 7.1,12.22
tato dīrgheṇa kālena duḥkhātkrodho vyajāyata  || 7.1,12.23
krodhāviṣṭasya netrābhyāṃ prāpatannaśrubindavaḥ  || 7.1,12.23
tatastebhyo 'śrubindubhyo bhūtāḥ pretāstadābhavan  || 7.1,12.24
sarvāṃstānaśrujāndṛṣṭvā brahmātmānamaniṃdata  || 7.1,12.24
tasya tīvrā 'bhavanmūrchā krodhāmarṣasamudbhavā  || 7.1,12.25
mūrchitastu jahau prāṇānkrodhāviṣṭaḥ prajāpatiḥ  || 7.1,12.25
tataḥ prāṇeśvaro rudro bhagavānnīlalohitaḥ  || 7.1,12.26
prasādamatulaṃ kartuṃ prādurāsītprabhormukhāt || 7.1,12.26
daśadhā caikadhā cakre svātmānaṃ prabhurīśvaraḥ  || 7.1,12.27
te tenoktā mahātmāno daśadhā caikadhā kṛtāḥ  || 7.1,12.27
yūyaṃ sṛṣṭā mayā vatsā lokānugrahakāraṇāt || 7.1,12.28
tasmātsarvasya lokasya sthāpanāya hitāya ca  || 7.1,12.28
prajāsantānahetośca prayatadhvamatandritāḥ  || 7.1,12.29
evamuktāśca rurudurdudruvuśca samantataḥ  || 7.1,12.29
rodanāddrāvaṇāccaiva te rudrā nāmataḥ smṛtāḥ  || 7.1,12.30
ye rudrāste khalu prāṇā ye prāṇāste mahātmakāḥ  || 7.1,12.30
tato mṛtasya devasya brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 7.1,12.31
ghṛṇī dadau punaḥ prāṇānbrahmaputro maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,12.31
prahṛṣṭavadano rudraḥ prāṇapratyāgamādvibhoḥ  || 7.1,12.32
abhyabhāṣata viśveśo brahmāṇaṃ paramaṃ vacaḥ  || 7.1,12.32
mābhairmābhairmahābhāga viriṃca jagatāṃ guro  || 7.1,12.33
mayā te prāṇitāḥ prāṇāḥ sukhamuttiṣṭha suvrata  || 7.1,12.33
svapnānubhūtamiva tacchrutvā vākyaṃ manoharam  || 7.1,12.34
haraṃ nirīkṣya śanakairnetraiḥ phullāmbujaprabhaiḥ  || 7.1,12.34
tathā pratyāgataprāṇaḥ snigdhagambhīrayā girā  || 7.1,12.35
uvāca vacanaṃ brahmā tamuddiśya kṛtāñjaliḥ  || 7.1,12.35
tvaṃ hi darśanamātreṇa cānandayasi me manaḥ  || 7.1,12.36
ko bhavān viśvamūrtyā vā sthita ekādaśātmakaḥ  || 7.1,12.36
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā vyājahāra maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,12.37
spṛśan kārābhyāṃ brahmāṇaṃ susukhābhyāṃ sureśvaraḥ  || 7.1,12.37
māṃ viddhi paramātmānaṃ tava putratvamāgatam  || 7.1,12.38
ete caikādaśa rudrāstvāṃ surakṣitumāgatāḥ  || 7.1,12.38
tasmāttīvrāmimāmmūrchāṃ vidhūya madanugrahāt || 7.1,12.39
prabuddhasva yathāpūrvaṃ prajā vai sraṣṭumarhasi  || 7.1,12.39
514

evaṃ bhagavatā prokto brahmā prītamanā hyabhūt || 7.1,12.40
nānāṣṭakena viśvātmā tuṣṭāva parameśvaram  || 7.1,12.40
brahmovāca
namaste bhagavan rudra bhāskarāmitatejase  || 7.1,12.41
namo bhavāya devāya rasāyāmbumayātmane  || 7.1,12.41
śarvāya kṣitirūpāya nandīsurabhaye namaḥ  || 7.1,12.41
īśāya vasave tubhyaṃ namassparśamayātmane  || 7.1,12.42
paśūnāṃ pataye caiva pāvakāyātitejase  || 7.1,12.42
bhīmāya vyomarūpāya śabdamātrāya te namaḥ  || 7.1,12.42
ugrāyograsvarūpāya yajamānātmane namaḥ  || 7.1,12.43
mahādevāya somāya namostvamṛtamūrtaye  || 7.1,12.43
evaṃ stutvā mahādevaṃ brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  || 7.1,12.44
prārthayāmāsa viśveśaṃ girā praṇatipūrvayā  || 7.1,12.44
bhagavan bhūtabhavyeśa mama putra maheśvara  || 7.1,12.45
sṛṣṭihetostvamutpanno mamāṃge 'naṃganāśanaḥ  || 7.1,12.45
tasmānmahati kāryesmin vyāpṛtasya jagatprabho  || 7.1,12.46
sahāyaṃ kuru sarvatra sraṣṭumarhasi sa prajāḥ  || 7.1,12.46
tenaiṣāṃ pāvito devo rudrastripuramardanaḥ  || 7.1,12.47
bāḍhamityeva tāṃ vāṇīṃ pratijagrāha śaṃkaraḥ  || 7.1,12.47
tatassa bhagavān brahmā hṛṣṭaṃ tamabhinaṃdya ca  || 7.1,12.48
sraṣṭuṃ tenābhyanujñātastathānyāścāsṛjatprajāḥ  || 7.1,12.48
marīcibhṛgvaṃgirasaḥ pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ kratum  || 7.1,12.49
dakṣamatriṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ ca so 'sṛjanmanasaiva ca  || 7.1,12.49
purastādasṛjadbrahmā dharmaṃ saṃkalpameva ca  || 7.1,12.49
ityete brahmaṇaḥ putrā dvādaśādau prakīrtitāḥ  || 7.1,12.50
saha rudreṇa saṃbhūtāḥ purāṇā gṛhamedhinaḥ  || 7.1,12.50
teṣāṃ dvādaśa vaṃśāḥ syurdivyā devagaṇānvitāḥ  || 7.1,12.51
prajāvantaḥ kriyāvanto maharṣibhiralaṃkṛtāḥ  || 7.1,12.51
atha devāsurapitḥnmanuṣyāṃśca catuṣṭayam  || 7.1,12.52
saha rudreṇa sisṛkṣuraṃbhasyetāni vai vidhiḥ  || 7.1,12.52
sa sṛṣṭyarthaṃ samādhāya brahmātmānamayūyujat || 7.1,12.53
mukhādajanayaddevān pitḥṃścaivopapakṣataḥ  || 7.1,12.53
jaghanādasurān sarvān prajanādapi mānuṣān  || 7.1,12.54
avaskare kṣudhāviṣṭā rākṣasāstasya jajñire  || 7.1,12.54
putrāstamorajaḥprāyā balinaste niśācarāḥ  || 7.1,12.55
sarpā yakṣāstathā bhūtā gaṃdharvāḥ saṃprajajñire  || 7.1,12.55
vayāṃsi pakṣataḥ sṛṣṭāḥ pakṣiṇo vakṣaso 'sṛjat || 7.1,12.56
mukhatojāṃstathā pārśvāduragāṃśca vinirmame  || 7.1,12.56
padbhyāṃ cāśvānsamātaṃgān śarabhān gavayānmṛgān  || 7.1,12.57
uṣṭrānaśvatarāṃścaiva nyaṃkūnanyāśca jātayaḥ 1  || 7.1,12.57
auṣadhyaḥ phalamūlāni romabhyastasya jajñire  || 7.1,12.58
gāyatrīṃ ca ṛcaṃ caiva trivṛtsāma rathaṃtaram  || 7.1,12.58
agniṣṭomaṃ ca yajñānāṃ nirmame prathamānmukhāt || 7.1,12.59
yajūṃṣi traiṣṭubhaṃ chaṃdaḥstomaṃ pañcadaśaṃ tathā  || 7.1,12.59

1 prathamārṣo

515

bṛhatsāma tathokthaṃ ca dakṣiṇādasṛjanmukhāt || 7.1,12.60
sāmāni jagatīchaṃdaḥ stomaṃ saptadaśaṃ tathā  || 7.1,12.60
vairūpyamatirātraṃ ca paścimādasṛjanmukhāt || 7.1,12.61
ekaviṃśamatharvāṇamāptoryāmāṇameva ca  || 7.1,12.61
anuṣṭubhaṃ sa vairājamuttarādasṛjanmukhāt || 7.1,12.62
uccāvacāni bhūtāni gātrebhyastasya jajñire  || 7.1,12.62
yakṣāḥ piśācā gaṃdharvāstathaivāpsarasāṃ gaṇāḥ  || 7.1,12.63
narakinnararakṣāṃsi vayaḥpaśumṛgoragāḥ  || 7.1,12.63
avyayaṃ caiva yadidaṃ sthāṇusthāvarajaṃgamam  || 7.1,12.64
teṣāṃ vai yāni karmāṇi prāksṛṣṭāni prapedire  || 7.1,12.64
tānyeva te prapadyaṃte sṛjyamānāḥ punaḥ punaḥ  || 7.1,12.65
hiṃsrāhiṃsre mṛdukrūre dharmādharmāvṛtānṛte  || 7.1,12.65
tadbhāvitāḥ prapadyaṃte tasmāttattasya rocate  || 7.1,12.66
mahābhūteṣu nānātvamiṃdriyārtheṣu muktiṣu  || 7.1,12.66
viniyogaṃ ca bhūtānāṃ dhātaiva vyadadhatsvayam  || 7.1,12.67
nāma rūpaṃ ca bhūtānāṃ prākṛtānāṃ prapañcanam  || 7.1,12.67
vedaśabdebhya evādau nirmame 'sau pitāmahaḥ  || 7.1,12.68
ārṣāṇi caiva nāmāni yāśca vedeṣu vṛttayaḥ  || 7.1,12.68
śarvaryaṃte prasūtānāṃ tānyevaibhyo dadāvajaḥ  || 7.1,12.69
yathartāvṛtuliṃgāni nānārūpāṇi paryaye  || 7.1,12.69
dṛśyaṃte tāni tānyeva tathā bhāvā yugādiṣu  || 7.1,12.70
ityeṣa karaṇodbhūto lokasargassvayaṃbhuvaḥ  || 7.1,12.70
mahadādyoviśeṣāṃto vikāraḥ prakṛteḥ svayam  || 7.1,12.71
caṃdrasūryaprabhājuṣṭo grahanakṣatramaṃḍitaḥ  || 7.1,12.71
nadībhiśca samudraiśca parvataiśca sa maṃḍitaḥ  || 7.1,12.72
paraiśca vividhairamyaissphītairjanapadaistathā  || 7.1,12.72
tasmin brahmavane 'vyakto brahmā carati sarvavit || 7.1,12.73
avyaktabījaprabhava īśvarānugrahe sthitaḥ  || 7.1,12.73
buddhiskaṃdhamahāśākha indriyāṃtarakoṭaraḥ  || 7.1,12.74
mahābhūtapramāṇaśca viśeṣāmalapallavaḥ  || 7.1,12.74
dharmādharmasupuṣpāḍhyaḥ sukhaduḥkhaphalodayaḥ  || 7.1,12.75
ājīvyaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ brahmavṛkṣaḥ sanātanaḥ  || 7.1,12.75
dyāṃ mūrdhānaṃ tasya viprā vadaṃti khaṃ vai nābhiṃ caṃdrasūryau ca netre  || 7.1,12.76
diśaḥ śrotre caraṇau ca kṣitiṃ ca so 'cintyātmā sarvabhūtapraṇetā  || 7.1,12.76
vaktrāttasya brahmaṇāssaṃprasūtāstadvakṣasaḥ kṣatriyāḥ pūrvabhāgāt || 7.1,12.77
vaiśyā urubhyāṃ tasya padbhyāṃ ca śūdrāḥ sarve varṇā gātrataḥ saṃprasūtāḥ  || 7.1,12.77

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe sṛṣṭivarṇanaṃ nāma dvādaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 13
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
bhavatā kathitā sṛṣṭirbhavasya paramātmanaḥ  || 7.1,13.1
caturmukhamukhāttasya saṃśayo naḥ prajāyate  || 7.1,13.1
devaśreṣṭho virūpākṣo dīptaśśūladharo haraḥ  || 7.1,13.2
kālātmā bhagavān rudraḥ kapardī nīlalohitaḥ  || 7.1,13.2
sabrahmakamimaṃ lokaṃ saviṣṇumapi pāvakam  || 7.1,13.3
515

yaḥ saṃharati saṃkruddho yugāṃte samupasthite  || 7.1,13.3
yasya brahmā ca viṣṇuśca praṇāmaṃ kuruto bhayāt || 7.1,13.4
lokasaṃkocakasyāsya yasya tau vaśavartinau  || 7.1,13.4
yo 'yaṃ devaḥ svakādaṃgādbrahmaviṣṇū purāsṛjat || 7.1,13.5
sa eva hi tayornityaṃ yogakṣemakaraḥ prabhuḥ  || 7.1,13.5
sa kathaṃ bhagavān rudra ādidevaḥ purātanaḥ  || 7.1,13.6
putratvamagamacchaṃbhurbrahmaṇo 'vyaktajanmanaḥ  || 7.1,13.6
prajāpatiśca viṣṇuśca rudrasyaitau parasparam  || 7.1,13.7
sṛṣṭau parasparasyāṃgāditi prāgapi śuśruma  || 7.1,13.7
kathaṃ punaraśeṣāṇāṃ bhūtānāṃ hetubhūtayoḥ  || 7.1,13.8
guṇapradhānabhāvena prādurbhāvaḥ parasparāt || 7.1,13.8
nāpṛṣṭaṃ bhavatā kiṃcinnāśrutaṃ ca kathaṃcana  || 7.1,13.9
bhagavacchiṣyabhūtena bhavatā sakalaṃ smṛtam  || 7.1,13.9
tattvaṃ vada yathā brahmā munīnāmavadadvibhuḥ  || 7.1,13.10
vayaṃ śraddhālavastāta śrotumīśvarasadyaśaḥ  || 7.1,13.10
vāyuruvāca
sthāne pṛṣṭamidaṃ viprā bhavadbhiḥ praśnakovidaiḥ  || 7.1,13.11
idameva purā pṛṣṭo mama prāha pitāmahaḥ  || 7.1,13.11
tadahaṃ sampravakṣyāmi yathā rudrasamudbhavaḥ  || 7.1,13.12
yathā ca punarutpattirbrahmaviṣṇvoḥ parasparam  || 7.1,13.12
trayaste kāraṇātmāno jatāssākṣānmaheśvarāt || 7.1,13.13
carācarasya viśvasya sargasthityaṃtahetavaḥ  || 7.1,13.13
paramaiśvaryasaṃyuktāḥ parameśvarabhāvitāḥ  || 7.1,13.14
tacchaktyādhiṣṭhitā nityaṃ tatkāryakaraṇakṣamāḥ  || 7.1,13.14
pitrā niyamitāḥ pūrvaṃ trayopi triṣu karmasu  || 7.1,13.15
brahmā sarge haristrāṇe rudraḥ saṃharaṇe tathā  || 7.1,13.15
tathāpyanyonyamātsaryādanyonyātiśayāśinaḥ  || 7.1,13.16
tapasā toṣayitvā svaṃ pitaraṃ parameśvaram  || 7.1,13.16
labdhvā sarvātmanā tasya prasādātparameṣṭhinaḥ  || 7.1,13.17
brahmanārāyaṇau pūrvaṃ rudraḥ kalpāntare 'sṛjat || 7.1,13.17
kalpāntare punarbrahmā rudraviṣṇū jaganmayaḥ  || 7.1,13.18
viṣṇuśca bhagavānrudraṃ brahmāṇamasṛjatpunaḥ  || 7.1,13.18
nārāyaṇaṃ punarbrahmā brahmāṇamasṛjatpunaḥ  || 7.1,13.19
evaṃ kalpeṣu kalpeṣu brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  || 7.1,13.19
paraspareṇa jāyaṃte parasparahitaiṣiṇaḥ  || 7.1,13.20
tattatkalpāntavṛttāntamadhikṛtya maharṣibhiḥ  || 7.1,13.20
prabhāvaḥ kathyate teṣāṃ parasparasamudbhavāt || 7.1,13.21
śṛṇu teṣāṃ kathāṃ citrāṃ puṇyāṃ pāpapramocinīm  || 7.1,13.21
kalpe tatpuruṣe vṛttāṃ brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 7.1,13.22
purā nārāyaṇo nāma kalpe vai meghavāhane  || 7.1,13.22
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu megho bhūtvāvahaddharām  || 7.1,13.23
tasya bhāvaṃ samālakṣya viṣṇorviśvajagadguruḥ  || 7.1,13.23
sarvassarvātmabhāvena pradadau śaktimavyayām  || 7.1,13.24
śaktiṃ labdhvā tu sarvātmā śivātsarveśvarāttadā  || 7.1,13.24
sasarja bhagāvan viṣṇurviśvaṃ viśvasṛjā saha  || 7.1,13.25
516

viṣṇostadvaibhavaṃ dṛṣṭvā sṛṣṭastena pitāmahaḥ  || 7.1,13.25
īrṣyayā parayā grastaḥ prahasannidamabravīt || 7.1,13.26
gaccha viṣṇo mayā jñātaṃ tava sargasya kāraṇam  || 7.1,13.26
āvayoradhikaścāsti sa rudro nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 7.1,13.26
tasya devādhidevasya prasādātparameṣṭhinaḥ  || 7.1,13.27
sraṣṭā tvaṃ bhagavānādyaḥ pālakaḥ paramārthataḥ  || 7.1,13.27
ahaṃ ca tapasārādhya rudraṃ tridaśanāyakam  || 7.1,13.28
tvayā saha jagatsarvaṃ srakṣyāmyatra na saṃśayaḥ  || 7.1,13.28
evaṃ viṣṇumupālabhya bhagavānabjasambhavaḥ  || 7.1,13.29
evaṃ vijñāpayāmāsa tapasā prāpya śaṃkaram  || 7.1,13.29
bhagavan devadeveśa viśveśvara maheśvara  || 7.1,13.30
tava vāmāṃgajo viṣṇurdakṣiṇāṃgabhavo hyaham  || 7.1,13.30
mayā saha jagatsarvaṃ tathāpyasṛjadacyutaḥ  || 7.1,13.31
sa matsarādupālabdhastvadāśrayabalānmayā  || 7.1,13.31
madbhāvānnādhikasteti bhāvastvayi maheśvare  || 7.1,13.32
tvatta eva samutpattirāvayossadṛśī yataḥ  || 7.1,13.32
tasya bhaktyā yathāpūrvaṃ prasādaṃ kṛtavānasi  || 7.1,13.33
tathā mamāpi tatsarvaṃ dātumarhasi śaṃkara  || 7.1,13.33
iti vijñāpitastena bhagavān bhaganetrahā  || 7.1,13.34
nyāyena vai dadau sarvaṃ tasyāpi sa ghṛṇānidhiḥ  || 7.1,13.34
labdhvaivamīśvarādeva brahmā sarvātmatāṃ kṣaṇāt || 7.1,13.35
tvaramāṇotha saṃgamya dadarśa puruṣottamam  || 7.1,13.35
kṣīrārṇavālaye śubhre vimāne sūryasaṃnibhe  || 7.1,13.36
hemaratnānvite divye manasā tena nirmite  || 7.1,13.36
anaṃtabhogaśayyāyāṃ śayānaṃ paṃkajekṣaṇam  || 7.1,13.37
caturbhujamudārāṃgaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam  || 7.1,13.37
śaṃkhacakradharaṃ saumyaṃ candrabiṃbasamānanam  || 7.1,13.38
śrīvatsavakṣasaṃ devaṃ prasannamadhurasmitam  || 7.1,13.38
dharāmṛdukarāṃbhojasparśaraktapadāṃbujam  || 7.1,13.39
kṣīrārṇavāmṛtamiva śayānaṃ yoganidrayā  || 7.1,13.39
tamasā kālarudrākhyaṃ rajasā kanakāṃḍajam  || 7.1,13.40
sattvena sarvagaṃ viṣṇuṃ nirguṇatve maheśvaram  || 7.1,13.40
taṃ dṛṣṭvā puruṣaṃ brahmā pragalbhamidamabravīt || 7.1,13.41
grasāmi tvāmahaṃ viṣṇo tvamātmānaṃ yathā purā  || 7.1,13.41
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā pratibuddhya pitāmaham  || 7.1,13.42
udaikṣata mahābāhussmitamīṣaccakāra ca  || 7.1,13.42
tasminnavasare viṣṇurgrastastena mahātmanā  || 7.1,13.43
sṛṣṭaśca brahmaṇā sadyo bhruvormadhyādayatnataḥ  || 7.1,13.43
tasminnavasare sākṣādbhagavānindubhūṣaṇaḥ  || 7.1,13.44
śaktiṃ tayorapi draṣṭumarūpo rūpamāsthitaḥ  || 7.1,13.44
prasādamatulaṃ kartuṃ purā dattavarastayoḥ  || 7.1,13.45
āgacchattatra yatremau brahmanārāyaṇau sthitau  || 7.1,13.45
atha tuṣṭuvaturdevaṃ prītau bhītau ca kautukāt || 7.1,13.46
praṇematuśca bahuśo bahumānena dūrataḥ  || 7.1,13.46
516

bhavopi bhagavānetāvanugṛhya pinākadhṛk || 7.1,13.47
sādaraṃ paśyatoreva tayoraṃtaradhīyata  || 7.1,13.47

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe brahmaviṣṇusṛṣṭikathanaṃ nāma trayodaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 14
vāyuruvāca
pratikalpaṃ pravakṣyāmi rudrāvirbhāvakāraṇam  || 7.1,14.1
yato vicchinnasaṃtānā brahmasṛṣṭiḥ pravartate  || 7.1,14.1
kalpekalpe prajāḥ sṛṣṭvā brahmā brahmāṃḍasaṃbhavaḥ  || 7.1,14.2
avṛddhihetorbhūtānāṃ mumoha bhṛśaduḥkhitaḥ  || 7.1,14.2
tasya duḥkhapraśāṃtyarthaṃ prajānāṃ ca vivṛddhaye  || 7.1,14.3
tattatkalpeṣu kālātmā rudro rudragaṇādhipaḥ  || 7.1,14.3
nirdiṣṭaḥ pamameśena maheśo nīlalohitaḥ  || 7.1,14.4
putro bhūtvānugṛhṇāti brahmāṇaṃ brahmaṇonujaḥ  || 7.1,14.4
sa eva bhagavānīśastejorāśiranāmayaḥ  || 7.1,14.5
anādinidhanodhātā bhūtasaṃkocako vibhuḥ  || 7.1,14.5
paramaiśvaryasaṃyuktaḥ parameśvarabhāvitaḥ  || 7.1,14.6
tacchaktyādhiṣṭhitaśśaśvattaccihnairapi cihnitaḥ  || 7.1,14.6
tannāmanāmā tadrūpastatkāryakaraṇakṣamaḥ  || 7.1,14.7
tattulyavyavahāraśca tadājñāparipālakaḥ  || 7.1,14.7
sahasrādityasaṃkāśaścandrāvayavabhūṣaṇaḥ  || 7.1,14.8
bhujaṃgahārakeyūravalayo muṃjamekhalaḥ  || 7.1,14.8
jalaṃdharaviriṃcendrakapālaśakalojjvalaḥ  || 7.1,14.9
gaṅgātuṃgataraṃgārdhapiṃgalānanamūrdhajaḥ  || 7.1,14.9
bhagnadaṃṣṭrāṃkurākrāntaprāntakāntadharādharaḥ  || 7.1,14.10
savyaśravaṇapārśvāṃtamaṃḍalīkṛtakuṇḍalaḥ  || 7.1,14.10
mahāvṛṣabhaniryāṇo mahājaladaniḥsvanaḥ  || 7.1,14.11
mahānalasamaprakhyo mahābalaparākramaḥ  || 7.1,14.11
evaṃ ghoramahārūpo brahmaputrīṃ maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,14.12
vijñānaṃ brahmaṇe dattvā sarge sahakaroti ca  || 7.1,14.12
tasmādrudraprasādena pratikalpaṃ prajāpateḥ  || 7.1,14.13
pravāharūpato nityā prajāsṛṣṭiḥ pravartate  || 7.1,14.13
kadācitprārthitaḥ sraṣṭuṃ brahmaṇā nīlalohitaḥ  || 7.1,14.14
svātmanā sadṛśān sarvān sasarja manasā vibhuḥ  || 7.1,14.14
kapardino nirātaṃkānnīlagrīvāṃstrilocanān  || 7.1,14.15
jarāmaraṇanirmuktān dīptaśūlavarāyudhān  || 7.1,14.15
taistu saṃcchāditaṃ sarvaṃ caturdaśavidhaṃ jagat || 7.1,14.16
tāndṛṣṭā vividhānrudrān rudramāha pitāmahaḥ  || 7.1,14.16
namaste devadeveśa māsrākṣīrīdṛśīḥ prajāḥ  || 7.1,14.17
anyāḥ sṛja tvaṃ bhadraṃ te prajā mṛtyusamanvitāḥ  || 7.1,14.17
ityuktaḥ prahasanprāha brahmāṇaṃ parameśvaraḥ  || 7.1,14.18
nāsti me tādṛśassargassṛja tvamaśubhāḥ prajāḥ  || 7.1,14.18
ye tvime manasā sṛṣṭā mahātmāno mahābalāḥ  || 7.1,14.19
cariṣyaṃti mayā sārdhaṃ sarva eva hi yājñikāḥ  || 7.1,14.19
ityuktvā viśvakarmāṇaṃ viśvabhūteśvaro haraḥ  || 7.1,14.20
saha rudraiḥ prajāsargānnivṛttātmā vyatiṣṭhata  || 7.1,14.20
tataḥ prabhṛti devo 'sau na prasūte prajāḥ śubhāḥ  || 7.1,14.21
517

ūrdhvaretāḥ sthitaḥ sthāṇuryāvadābhūtasaṃplavam  || 7.1,14.21

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe rudrāvirbhāvavarṇanaṃ nāma caturdaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 15
vāyuruvāca
yadā punaḥ prajāḥ sṛṣṭā na vyavardhanta vedhasaḥ  || 7.1,15.1
tadā maithunajāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ brahmā kartumamanyata  || 7.1,15.1
na nirgataṃ purā yasmānnārīṇāṃ kulamīśvarāt || 7.1,15.2
tena maithunajāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ na śaśāka pitāmahaḥ  || 7.1,15.2
tatassa vidadhe buddhimarthaniścayagāminīm  || 7.1,15.3
prajānameva vṛddhyarthaṃ praṣṭavyaḥ parameśvara  || 7.1,15.3
prasādena vinā tasya na vardherannimāḥ prajāḥ  || 7.1,15.4
evaṃ saṃcintya viśvātmā tapaḥ kartuṃ pracakrame  || 7.1,15.4
tadādyā paramā śaktiranaṃtā lokabhāvinī  || 7.1,15.5
ādyā sūkṣmatarā śuddhā bhāvagamyā manoharā  || 7.1,15.5
nirguṇā niṣprapañcā ca niṣkalā nirupaplavā  || 7.1,15.6
niraṃtaratarā nityā nityamīśvarapārśvagā  || 7.1,15.6
tayā paramayā śaktyā bhagavaṃtaṃ triyambakam  || 7.1,15.7
saṃcintya hṛdaye brahmā tatāpa paramaṃ tapaḥ  || 7.1,15.7
tīvreṇa tapasā tasya yuktasya parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 7.1,15.8
acireṇaiva kālena pitā saṃpratutoṣa ha  || 7.1,15.8
tataḥ kenacidaṃśena mūrtimāviśya kāmapi  || 7.1,15.9
ardhanārīśvaro bhūtvā yayau devassvayaṃ haraḥ  || 7.1,15.9
taṃ dṛṣṭvā paramaṃ devaṃ tamasaḥ paramavyayam  || 7.1,15.10
advitīyamanirdeśyamadṛśyamakṛtātmabhiḥ  || 7.1,15.10
sarvalokavidhātāraṃ sarvalokeśvareśvaram  || 7.1,15.11
sarvalokavidhāyinyā śaktyā paramayā yutam  || 7.1,15.11
apratarkyamanābhāsamameyamajaraṃ dhruvam  || 7.1,15.12
acalaṃ nirguṇaṃ śāṃtamanaṃtamahimāspadam  || 7.1,15.12
sarvagaṃ sarvadaṃ sarvasadasadvyaktivarjitam  || 7.1,15.13
sarvopamānanirmuktaṃ śaraṇyaṃ śāśvataṃ śivam  || 7.1,15.13
praṇamya daṃḍavadbrahmā samutthāya kṛtāṃjaliḥ  || 7.1,15.14
śraddhāvinayasaṃpannaiḥ śrāvyaiḥ saṃskarasaṃyutaiḥ  || 7.1,15.14
yathārthayuktasarvārthairvedārthaparibṛṃhitaiḥ  || 7.1,15.15
tuṣṭāva devaṃ devīṃ ca sūktaiḥ sūkṣmārthagocaraiḥ  || 7.1,15.15
brahmovāca
jaya deva mahādeva jayeśvara maheśvara  || 7.1,15.16
jaya sarvaguṇa śreṣṭha jaya sarvasurādhipa  || 7.1,15.16
jaya prakṛti kalyāṇi jaya prakṛtināyike  || 7.1,15.17
jaya prakṛtidūre tvaṃ jaya prakṛtisundari  || 7.1,15.17
jayāmoghamahāmāya jayāmogha manoratha  || 7.1,15.18
jayāmoghamahālīla jayāmoghamahābala  || 7.1,15.18
jaya viśvajaganmātarjaya viśvajaganmaye  || 7.1,15.19
jaya viśvajagaddhātri jaya viśvajagatsakhi  || 7.1,15.19
jaya śāśvatikaiśvarye jaya śāśvatikālaya  || 7.1,15.20
jaya śāśvatikākāra jaya śāśvatikānuga  || 7.1,15.20
jayātmatrayanirmātri jayātmatrayapālini  || 7.1,15.21
517

jayātmatrayasaṃhartri jayātmatrayanāyike  || 7.1,15.21
jayāvalokanāyattajagatkāraṇabṛṃhaṇa  || 7.1,15.22
jayopekṣākaṭākṣotthahutabhugbhuktabhautika  || 7.1,15.22
jaya devādyavijñeye svātmasūkṣmadṛśojjvale  || 7.1,15.23
jaya sthūlātmaśaktyeśejaya vyāptacarācare  || 7.1,15.23
jaya nāmaikavinyastaviśvatattvasamuccaya  || 7.1,15.24
jayāsuraśironiṣṭhaśreṣṭhānugakadaṃbaka  || 7.1,15.24
jayopāśritasaṃrakṣāsaṃvidhānapaṭīyasi  || 7.1,15.25
jayonmūlitasaṃsāraviṣavṛkṣāṃkurodgame  || 7.1,15.25
jaya prādeśikaiśvaryavīryaśauryavijṛṃbhaṇa  || 7.1,15.26
jaya viśvabahirbhūta nirastaparavaibhava  || 7.1,15.26
jaya praṇītapañcārthaprayogaparamāmṛta  || 7.1,15.27
jaya pañcārthavijñānasudhāstotrasvarūpiṇi  || 7.1,15.27
jayati ghorasaṃsāramahārogabhiṣagvara  || 7.1,15.28
jayānādimalājñānatamaḥpaṭalacaṃdrike  || 7.1,15.28
jaya tripurakālāgne jaya tripurabhairavi  || 7.1,15.29
jaya triguṇanirmukte jaya triguṇamardini  || 7.1,15.29
jaya prathamasarvajña jaya sarvaprabodhika  || 7.1,15.30
jaya pracuradivyāṃga jaya prārthitadāyini  || 7.1,15.30
kva deva te paraṃ dhāma kva ca tucchaṃ ca no vacaḥ  || 7.1,15.31
tathāpi bhagavan bhaktyā pralapaṃtaṃ kṣamasva mām  || 7.1,15.31
