vijñāpyaivaṃvidhaiḥ sūktairviśvakarmā caturmukhaḥ  || 7.1,15.32
namaścakāra rudrāya radrāṇyai ca muhurmuhuḥ  || 7.1,15.32
idaṃ stotravaraṃ puṇyaṃ brahmaṇā samudīritam  || 7.1,15.33
ardhanārīśvaraṃ nāma śivayorharṣavardhanam  || 7.1,15.33
ya idaṃ kīrtayedbhaktyā yasya kasyāpi śikṣayā  || 7.1,15.34
sa tatphalamavāpnoti śivayoḥ prītikāraṇāt || 7.1,15.34
sakalabhuvanabhūtabhāvanābhyāṃ jananavināśavihīnavigrahābhyām  || 7.1,15.35
naravarayuvatīvapurdharābhyāṃ satatamahaṃ praṇatosmi śaṃkarābhyām  || 7.1,15.35

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pū śivaśivāstutivarṇanaṃ nāma pañcadaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 16
vāyuruvāca
atha devo mahādevo mahājaladanādayā  || 7.1,16.1
vācā madhuragaṃbhīraśivadaślakṣṇavarṇayā  || 7.1,16.1
arthasaṃpannapadayā rājalakṣaṇayuktayā  || 7.1,16.2
aśeṣaviṣayāraṃbharakṣāvimaladakṣayā  || 7.1,16.2
manoharatarodāramadhurasmitapūrvayā  || 7.1,16.3
saṃbabhāṣe susaṃpīto viśvakarmāṇamīśvaraḥ  || 7.1,16.3
īśvara uvāca
vatsa vatsa mahābhāga mama putra pitāmaha  || 7.1,16.4
jñātameva mayā sarvaṃ tava vākyasya gauravam  || 7.1,16.4
prajānāmeva bṛddhyarthaṃ tapastaptaṃ tvayādhunā  || 7.1,16.5
tapasā 'nena tuṣṭosmi dadāmi ca tavepsitam  || 7.1,16.5
ityuktvā paramodāraṃ svabhāvamadhuraṃ vacaḥ  || 7.1,16.6
sasarja vapuṣo bhāgāddevīṃ devavaro haraḥ  || 7.1,16.6
yāmāhurbrahmavidvāṃso devīṃ divyaguṇānvitām  || 7.1,16.7
parasya paramāṃ śaktiṃ bhavasya paramātmanaḥ  || 7.1,16.7
518

yasyāṃ na khalu vidyaṃte janma mṛtyujarādayaḥ  || 7.1,16.8
yā bhavānī bhavasyāṃgātsamāvirabhavatkila  || 7.1,16.8
yasyā vāco nivartante manasā ceṃdriyaiḥ saha  || 7.1,16.9
sā bharturvapuṣo bhāgājjāteva samadṛśyata  || 7.1,16.9
yā sā jagadidaṃ kṛtsnaṃ mahimnā vyāpya tiṣṭhati  || 7.1,16.10
śarīriṇīva sa devī vicitraṃ samalakṣyata  || 7.1,16.10
sarvaṃ jagadidaṃ caiṣā saṃmohayati māyayā  || 7.1,16.11
īśvarātsaiva jātābhūdajātā paramārthataḥ  || 7.1,16.11
na yasyā paramo bhāvaḥ surāṇāmapi gocaraḥ  || 7.1,16.12
viśvāmareśvarī caiva vibhaktā bharturaṃgataḥ  || 7.1,16.12
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā parameśānīṃ sarvalokamaheśvarīm  || 7.1,16.13
sarvajñāṃ sarvagāṃ sūkṣmāṃ sadasadvyaktivarjitām  || 7.1,16.13
paramāṃ nikhilaṃ bhāsā bhāsayantīmidaṃ jagat || 7.1,16.14
praṇipatya mahādevīṃ prārthayāmāsa vai virāṭ || 7.1,16.14
brahmovāca
devi devena sṛṣṭo 'hamādau sarvajaganmayi  || 7.1,16.15
prajāsarge niyuktaśca sṛjāmi sakalaṃ jagat || 7.1,16.15
manasā nirmitāḥ sarve devi devādayo mayā  || 7.1,16.16
na vṛddhimupagacchanti sṛjyamānāḥ punaḥ punaḥ  || 7.1,16.16
mithunaprabhavāmeva kṛtvā sṛṣṭimataḥ param  || 7.1,16.17
saṃvardhayitumicchāmi sarvā eva mama prajāḥ  || 7.1,16.17
na nirgataṃ purā tvatto nārīṇāṃ kulamavyayam  || 7.1,16.18
tena nārīkulaṃ sraṣṭuṃ śaktirmama na vidyate  || 7.1,16.18
sarvāsāmeva śaktīnāṃ tvattaḥ khalu samudbhavaḥ  || 7.1,16.19
tasmātsarvatra sarveṣāṃ sarvaśaktipradāyinīm  || 7.1,16.19
tvāmeva varadāṃ māyāṃ prārthayāmi sureśvarīm  || 7.1,16.20
carācaravivṛddhyarthamaṃśenaikena sarvage  || 7.1,16.20
dakṣasya mama putrasya putrī bhava bhavārdini  || 7.1,16.21
evaṃ sā yācitā devī brahmaṇā brahmayoninā  || 7.1,16.21
śaktimekāṃ bhruvormadhyātsasarjātmasamaprabhām  || 7.1,16.22
tāmāha prahasanprekṣya devadevavaro haraḥ  || 7.1,16.22
brahmāṇaṃ tapasārādhya kuru tasya yathepsitam  || 7.1,16.23
tāmājñāṃ parameśasya śirasā pratigṛhya sā  || 7.1,16.23
brahmaṇo vacanāddevī dakṣasya duhitābhavat || 7.1,16.24
dattvaivamatulāṃ śaktiṃ brahmaṇe brahmarūpiṇīm  || 7.1,16.24
viveśa dehaṃ devasya devaścāṃtaradhīyata  || 7.1,16.25
tadā prabhṛti loke 'smin striyāṃ bhogaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ  || 7.1,16.25
prajāsṛṣṭiśca vipreṃdrā maithunena pravartate  || 7.1,16.26
brahmāpi prāpa sānandaṃ santoṣaṃ munipuṃgavāḥ  || 7.1,16.26
etadvassarvamākhyātaṃ devyāḥ śaktisamudbhavam  || 7.1,16.27
puṇyavṛddhikaraṃ śrāvyaṃ bhūtasargānupaṃgataḥ  || 7.1,16.27
ya idaṃ kīrtayennityaṃ devyāḥ śaktisamudbhavam  || 7.1,16.28
puṇyaṃ sarvamavāpnoti putrāṃśca labhate śubhān  || 7.1,16.28

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe devīśaktyudbhavo nāma ṣoḍaśo 'dhyāyaḥ
518


adhyāya 17
vāyuruvāca
evaṃ labdhvā parāṃ śaktimīśvarādeva śāśvatīm  || 7.1,17.1
maithunaprabhavāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ kartṛkāmaḥ prajāpatiḥ  || 7.1,17.1
svayamapyadbhuto nārī cārdhena puruṣo 'bhavat || 7.1,17.2
yārdhena nārī sā tasmācchatarūpā vyajāyata  || 7.1,17.2
virājamasṛjadbrahmā so 'rdhana puruṣo 'bhavat || 7.1,17.3
sa vai svāyaṃbhuvaḥ pūrvaṃ puruṣo manurucyate  || 7.1,17.3
sā devī śatarūpā tu tapaḥ kṛtvā suduścaram  || 7.1,17.4
bhartāraṃ dīptayaśasaṃ manumevānvapadyata  || 7.1,17.4
tasmāttu śatarūpā sā putradvayamasūyata  || 7.1,17.5
priyavratottānapādau putrau putravatāṃ varau  || 7.1,17.5
kanye dve ca mahābhāge yābhyāṃ jātāstvimāḥ prajāḥ  || 7.1,17.6
ākūtirekā vijñeyā prasūtiraparā smṛtā  || 7.1,17.6
svāyaṃbhuvaḥ prasūtiṃ ca dadau dakṣāya tāṃ prabhuḥ  || 7.1,17.7
ruceḥ prajāpatiścaiva cākūtiṃ samapādayat || 7.1,17.7
ākūtyāṃ mithunaṃ jajñe mānasasya ruceḥ śubham  || 7.1,17.8
yajñaśca dakṣiṇā caiva yābhyāṃ saṃvartitaṃ jagat || 7.1,17.8
svāyaṃbhuvasutāyāṃ tu prasūtyāṃ lokamātaraḥ 1  || 7.1,17.9
catasro viṃśatiḥ kanyā dakṣastvajanayatprabhuḥ  || 7.1,17.9
śraddhā lakṣmīrdhṛtiḥ puṣṭistuṣṭirmedhā kriyā tathā  || 7.1,17.10
buddhirlajjā vapuḥ śāṃtissiddhiḥ kīrtistrayodaśī  || 7.1,17.10
patnyarthaṃ pratijagrāha dharmo dākṣāyaṇīḥ prabhuḥ  || 7.1,17.11
tābhyaḥ śiṣṭā yavīyasya ekādaśa sulocanāḥ  || 7.1,17.11
khyātiḥ satyarthasaṃbhūtiḥ smṛtiḥ prītiḥ kṣamā tathā  || 7.1,17.12
sannatiścānasūyā ca ūrjā svāhā svadhā tathā  || 7.1,17.12
bhṛguśśarvo marīciśca aṃgirāḥ pulahaḥ kratuḥ  || 7.1,17.13
pulastyo 'trirviśiṣṭhaśca pāvakaḥ pitarastathā  || 7.1,17.13
khyātyādyā jagṛhuḥ kanyāmunayo munisattamāḥ  || 7.1,17.14
kāmādyāstu yaśoṃtā ye te trayodaśa sūnavaḥ  || 7.1,17.14
dharmasya jajñire tāstu śraddhādyāssusukhottarāḥ  || 7.1,17.15
duḥkhottarāśca hiṃsāyāmadharmasya ca saṃtatau  || 7.1,17.15
nikṛtyādaya utpannāḥputrāśca dharmalakṣaṇāḥ  || 7.1,17.16
naiṣāṃ bhāryāśca putrā vā sarve tvaniyamāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 7.1,17.16
sa eṣa tāmasassargo jajñe dharmaniyāmakaḥ  || 7.1,17.17
yā sā dakṣasya duhitā rudrasya dayitā satī  || 7.1,17.17
bhartṛnindāprasaṃgena tyaktvā dākṣāyiṇīṃ tanum  || 7.1,17.18
dakṣaṃ ca dakṣabhāryāṃ ca viniṃdya saha bandhubhiḥ  || 7.1,17.18
sā menāyāmāvirabhūtputrī himavato gireḥ  || 7.1,17.19

1 prathamārṣī

519

rudrastu tāṃ satīṃ dṛṣṭvā rudrāṃstvātmasamaprabhān  || 7.1,17.19
yathāsṛjadasaṃkhyātāṃstathā kathitameva ca  || 7.1,17.20
bhṛgoḥ khyātyāṃ samutpannā lakṣmīrnārāyaṇapriyā  || 7.1,17.20
devau dhātṛvidhātārau manvaṃtaravidhāriṇau  || 7.1,17.21
tayorvai putrapautrādyāśśataśo 'tha sahasraśaḥ  || 7.1,17.21
svāyaṃbhuve 'ṃtare nītāḥ sarve te bhārgavā matāḥ  || 7.1,17.22
marīcerapi saṃbhūtiḥ paurṇamāsamasūyata  || 7.1,17.22
kanyācatuṣṭayaṃ caiva mahīyāṃsastadanvayāḥ  || 7.1,17.23
yeṣāṃ vaṃśe samutpanno bahuputrasya kaśyapaḥ  || 7.1,17.23
smṛtiścāṃgirasaḥ patnī janayāmāsa vai sutau  || 7.1,17.24
āgnīdhraṃ śarabhañcaiva tathā kanyācatuṣṭayam  || 7.1,17.24
tadīyāḥ putrapautrāśca yetītāste sahasraśaḥ  || 7.1,17.25
prītyāṃ pulastyabhāryāyāṃ dantognirabhavatsutaḥ  || 7.1,17.25
pūrvajanmani yogastyassmṛtaḥ svāyaṃbhuve 'ṃtare  || 7.1,17.25
tatsaṃtatīyā bahavaḥ paulastyā iti viśrutāḥ  || 7.1,17.26
kṣamā tu suṣuve putrānpulahasya prajāpateḥ  || 7.1,17.26
kardamaśca suriścaiva sahiṣṇuśceti te trayaḥ  || 7.1,17.27
tretāgnivarcasassarve yeṣāṃ vaṃśaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ  || 7.1,17.27
kratoḥ kratusamānbhāryā sannatissuṣuve sutān  || 7.1,17.28
naiṣāṃ bhāryāśca putrāśca sarve te hyūrdhvaretasaḥ  || 7.1,17.28
ṣaṣṭistāni sahasrāṇi vālakhilyā iti smṛtāḥ  || 7.1,17.29
anūroragrato yāṃti parivārya divākaram  || 7.1,17.29
atrerbhāryānusūyā ca pañcātreyānasūyata  || 7.1,17.30
kanyakāṃ ca śrutiṃ nāma mātā śaṃkhapadasya ca  || 7.1,17.30
satyanetraśca havyaśca āpomūrtiśśanaiścaraḥ  || 7.1,17.31
somaśca pañcamastvete pañcātreyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  || 7.1,17.31
teṣāṃ putrāśca pautrāśca hyātreyāṇāṃ mahātmanām  || 7.1,17.32
svāyaṃbhuve 'ṃtare 'tītāḥ śataśo 'tha sahasraśaḥ  || 7.1,17.32
ūrjāyāṃ tu vasiṣṭhasya putrā vai sapta jajñire  || 7.1,17.33
jyāyasī ca svasā teṣāṃ puṃḍarīkā sumadhyamā  || 7.1,17.33
rajo gātrordhvabāhū ca savanaścānayaśca yaḥ  || 7.1,17.34
sutapāśśukra ityete sapta saptarṣayaḥ smṛtāḥ  || 7.1,17.34
gotrāṇi nāmabhisteṣāṃ vāsiṣṭhānāṃ mahātmanām  || 7.1,17.35
svāyaṃbhuve 'ṃtare 'tītānyarbudāni śatāni ca  || 7.1,17.35
ityeṣa ṛṣisargastu sānubaṃdhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  || 7.1,17.36
samāsādvistarādvaktumaśakyo 'yamiti dvijāḥ  || 7.1,17.36
yo 'sau rudrātmako bahnibrahmaṇo mānasassutaḥ  || 7.1,17.37
svāhā tasya priyā lebhe putrāṃstrīnamitaujasaḥ  || 7.1,17.37
pāvakaḥ pavamānaśca śucirityeṣa te trayaḥ  || 7.1,17.38
nirmaṃthyaḥ pavamānassyādvaidyutaḥ pāvakassmṛtaḥ  || 7.1,17.38
sūrye tapati yaścāsau śuciḥ saura udāhṛtaḥ  || 7.1,17.39
havyavāhaḥ kavyavāhaḥ saharakṣā iti trayaḥ  || 7.1,17.39
trayāṇāṃ kramaśaḥ putrā devapitṛsurāśca te  || 7.1,17.40
519

eteṣāṃ putrapautrāśca catvāriṃśannavaiva te  || 7.1,17.40
kāmyanaimittikājasrakarmasu triṣu saṃsthitāḥ  || 7.1,17.41
sarve tapasvino jñeyāḥ sarve vratabhṛtastathā  || 7.1,17.41
sarve rudrātmakaścaiva sarve rudraparāyaṇāḥ  || 7.1,17.42
tasmādagnimukhe yattaddhutaṃ syādeva kenacit || 7.1,17.42
tatsarvaṃ rudramuddiśya dattaṃ syānnātra saṃśayaḥ  || 7.1,17.43
ityevaṃ niścayognīnāmanukrāṃto yathātatham  || 7.1,17.43
nātivistarato viprāḥ pitḥnvakṣyāmyataḥ param  || 7.1,17.44
yasmātṣaḍṛtavasteṣāṃ sthānaṃ sthānābhimāninām  || 7.1,17.44
ṛtavaḥ pitarastasmādityeṣā vaidikī śrutiḥ  || 7.1,17.45
yuṣmādṛtuṣu sarve hi jāyaṃte sthāsnujaṃgamā  || 7.1,17.45
tasmādete pitara ārtavā iti ca śrutam  || 7.1,17.46
evaṃ pitḥṇāmeteṣāmṛtukālābhimāninām  || 7.1,17.46
ātmaiśvaryā mahātmānastiṣṭhaṃtīhābbhrasaṃgamāt || 7.1,17.47
āgniṣvāttā barhiṣadaḥ pitaro dvividhāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 7.1,17.47
ayajvānaśca yajvānaḥ kramātte mṛhamedhinaḥ  || 7.1,17.48
svadhāsūta pitṛbhyaśca dve kanye lokaviśrute  || 7.1,17.48
menāṃ ca dharaṇīṃ caiva yābhyāṃ viśvamidaṃ dhṛtam  || 7.1,17.49
agniṣvāttasutā menā dharaṇī barhiṣatsutā  || 7.1,17.49
menā himavataḥ patnī mainākaṃ krauṃcameva ca  || 7.1,17.50
gaurīṃ gaṃgāṃ ca suṣuve bhavāṃgāśleṣapāvanīm  || 7.1,17.50
merostu dharaṇī patnī divyauṣadhisamanvitam  || 7.1,17.51
maṃdaraṃ suṣuve putraṃ citrisundarakandharam  || 7.1,17.51
sa eva maṃdaraḥ śrīmānmeruputrastapobalāt || 7.1,17.52
sākṣācchrīkaṃṭhanāthasya śivasyāvasathaṃ gataḥ  || 7.1,17.52
sāsūtā dharaṇī bhūyastriṃśatkanyāśca viśrutāḥ  || 7.1,17.53
velāṃ ca niyatiṃ caiva tṛtīyāmapi cāyatim  || 7.1,17.53
āyatirniyatiścaiva patnyau dve bhṛguputrayoḥ  || 7.1,17.54
svāyaṃbhuve 'ṃtare pūrvaṃ kathitaste tadanvayaḥ  || 7.1,17.54
suṣuve sāgarādvelā kanyāmekāmaniṃditām  || 7.1,17.55
savarṇāṃ nāma sāmudrīṃ patnīṃ prācīnabarhiṣaḥ  || 7.1,17.55
sāmudrī suṣuve putrāndaśa prācīnabarhiṣaḥ  || 7.1,17.56
sarve prācetasā nāma dhanurvedasya pāragāḥ  || 7.1,17.56
yeṣāṃ svāyaṃbhuve dakṣaḥ putratvamagamatpurā  || 7.1,17.57
triyambakasya śāpena cākṣuṣasyāṃtare manoḥ  || 7.1,17.57
ityete brahmaputrāṇāṃ dharmādīnāmmahātmanām  || 7.1,17.58
nātisaṃkṣepato viprā nāti vistarataḥ kramāt || 7.1,17.58
varṇitā vai mayā vaṃśā divyā devagaṇānvitāḥ  || 7.1,17.59
kriyāvaṃtaḥ prajāvaṃto mahardhibhiralaṃkṛtāḥ  || 7.1,17.59
prajānāṃ saṃniveśo 'yaṃ prajāpatisamudbhavaḥ  || 7.1,17.60
na hi śakyaḥ prasaṃkhyātuṃ varṣakoṭiśatairapi  || 7.1,17.60
rājñāmapi ca yo vaṃśo dvidhā so 'pi pravartate  || 7.1,17.61
sūryavaṃśassomavaṃśa iti puṇyatamaḥ kṣitau  || 7.1,17.61
520

ikṣvākurambarīṣaśca yayātirnāhuṣādayaḥ  || 7.1,17.62
puṇyaślokāḥ śrutā ye 'tra te pi tadvaṃśasaṃbhavāḥ  || 7.1,17.62
anye ca rājaṛṣayo nānāvīryasamanvitā  || 7.1,17.63
kiṃ taiḥ phalamanutkrāṃtairuktapūrvaiḥ purātanaiḥ  || 7.1,17.63
kiṃ ceśvarakathā vṛttā yatra tatrānyakīrtanam  || 7.1,17.64
na sadbhiḥ saṃmataṃ matvā notsahe bahubhāṣitum  || 7.1,17.64
prasaṃgādīśvarasyaiva prabhāvadyotanādapi  || 7.1,17.65
sargādayo 'pi kathitā ityatra tatpravistaraiḥ  || 7.1,17.65

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe sṛṣṭikathanaṃ nāma saptadaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 18
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
devī dakṣasya tanayā tyaktvā dākṣāyaṇī tanum  || 7.1,18.1
kathaṃ himavataḥ putrī menāyāmabhavatpurā  || 7.1,18.1
kathaṃ ca nindito rudro dakṣeṇa ca mahātmanā  || 7.1,18.2
nimittamapi kiṃ tatra yena syānniṃdito bhavaḥ  || 7.1,18.2
utpannaśca kathaṃ dakṣo abhiśāpādbhavasya tu  || 7.1,18.3
cākṣuṣasyāṃtare pūrvaṃ manoḥ prabrūhi māruta  || 7.1,18.3
vāyuruvāva
śṛṇvaṃtu kathayiṣyāmi dakṣasya laghucetasaḥ  || 7.1,18.4
vṛttaṃ pāpātpramādācca viśvāmaravidūṣaṇam  || 7.1,18.4
purā surāsurāḥ sarve siddhāśca paramarṣayaḥ  || 7.1,18.5
kadāciddraṣṭumīśānaṃ himavacchikharaṃ yayuḥ  || 7.1,18.5
tadā devaśca devī ca divyāsanagatāvubhau  || 7.1,18.6
darśanaṃ dadatusteṣāṃ devādīnāṃ dvijottamāḥ  || 7.1,18.6
tadānīmeva dakṣo 'pi gatastatra sahāmaraiḥ  || 7.1,18.7
jāmātaraṃ haraṃ draṣṭuṃ draṣṭuṃ cātmasutāṃ satīm  || 7.1,18.7
tadātmagauravāddevo devyā dakṣe samāgate  || 7.1,18.8
devādibhyo viśeṣeṇa na kadācidabhūtsmṛtiḥ  || 7.1,18.8
tasya tasyāḥ paraṃ bhāvamajñātuścāpi kevalam  || 7.1,18.9
putrītyevaṃ vimūḍhasya tasyāṃ vairamajāyata  || 7.1,18.9
tatastenaiva vaireṇa vidhinā ca pracoditaḥ  || 7.1,18.10
nājuvāha bhavaṃ dakṣo dīkṣitastāmapi dviṣan  || 7.1,18.10
anyāñ1 āmātarassarvānāhūya sa yathākramam  || 7.1,18.11
śataśaḥ puṣkalāmarcāñcakāra ca pṛthakpṛthak || 7.1,18.11
tathā tānsaṃgatāñchrutvā nāradasya mukhāttadā  || 7.1,18.12
yayau rudrāya rudrāṇī vijñāpya bhavanaṃ pituḥ  || 7.1,18.12
atha saṃnihitaṃ divyaṃ vimānaṃ viśvatomukham  || 7.1,18.13
lakṣaṇāḍhyaṃ sukhārohamatimātramanoharam  || 7.1,18.13
taptajāṃbūnadaprakhyaṃ citraratnapariṣkṛtam  || 7.1,18.14
muktāmayavitānāgnyaṃ sragdāmasamalaṃkṛtam  || 7.1,18.14
taptakaṃcananirvyūhaṃ ratnastaṃbhaśatāvṛtam  || 7.1,18.15
vajrakalpitasopānaṃ vidrumastaṃbhatoraṇam  || 7.1,18.15

1 prathamāntaḥ prayoga ārṣaḥ

520

puṣpapaṭṭaparistīrṇaṃ citraratnamahāsanam  || 7.1,18.16
vajrajālakiracchidramacchidramaṇikuṭṭimam  || 7.1,18.16
maṇidaṃḍamanojñena mahāvṛṣabhalakṣmaṇā  || 7.1,18.17
alaṃkṛtapurobhāgamabbhraśubbhreṇa ketunā  || 7.1,18.17
ratnakaṃcukaguptāṃgaiścitravetrakapāṇibhiḥ  || 7.1,18.18
adhiṣṭhitamahādvāramapradhṛṣyairguṇeśvaraiḥ  || 7.1,18.18
mṛdaṃgatālagītādiveṇuvīṇāviśāradaiḥ  || 7.1,18.19
vidagdhaveṣabhāṣaiśca bahubhiḥ strījanairvṛtam  || 7.1,18.19
āruroha mahādevī saha priyasakhījanaiḥ  || 7.1,18.20
cāmāravyañjanaṃ tasyā vajradaṃḍamanohare  || 7.1,18.20
gṛhītvā rudrakanye dve vivījaturubhe śubhe  || 7.1,18.21
tadācāmarayormadhye devyā vadanamābabhau  || 7.1,18.21
anyonyaṃ yudhyatormadhye haṃsayoriva paṃkajam  || 7.1,18.22
chatraṃ śaśinibhaṃ tasyāścūḍopari sumālinī  || 7.1,18.22
dhṛtamuktāparikṣiptaṃ babhāra premanirbharā  || 7.1,18.23
tacchatramujjvalaṃ devyā ruruce vadanopari  || 7.1,18.23
uparyamṛtabhāṃḍasya maṃḍalaṃ śaśino yathā  || 7.1,18.24
atha cāgre samāsīnā susmitāsyā śubhāvatī  || 7.1,18.24
akṣadyūtavinodena ramayāmāsa vai satīm  || 7.1,18.25
suyaśāḥ pāduke devyāśśubhe ratnapariṣkṛte  || 7.1,18.25
stanayoraṃtare kṛtvā tadā devīmasevataḥ  || 7.1,18.26
anyā kāṃcanacārvaṃgī dīptaṃ jagrāha darpaṇam  || 7.1,18.26
aparā tālavṛntaṃ ca parā tāṃbūlapeṭikām  || 7.1,18.27
kācitkrīḍāśukaṃ cāru kare 'kuruta bhāminī  || 7.1,18.27
kācittu sumanojñāni puṣpāṇi surabhīṇi ca  || 7.1,18.28
kācidābharaṇādhāraṃ babhāra kamalekṣaṇā  || 7.1,18.28
kācicca punarālepaṃ suprasūtaṃ śubhāṃjanam  || 7.1,18.29
anyāśca sadṛśāstāstā yathāsvamucitakriyāḥ  || 7.1,18.29
āvṛttyā tāṃ mahādevīmasevaṃta samaṃtataḥ  || 7.1,18.30
atīva śuśubhe tāsāmaṃtare parameśvarī  || 7.1,18.30
tārāpariṣado madhye caṃdralekheva śāradī  || 7.1,18.31
tataḥ śaṃkhasamutthasya nādasya samanaṃtaram  || 7.1,18.31
prāsthāniko mahānādaḥ paṭahaḥ samatāḍyata  || 7.1,18.32
tato madhuravādyāni saha tālodyataissvanaiḥ  || 7.1,18.32
anāhatāni sanneduḥ kāhalānāṃ śatāni ca  || 7.1,18.33
sāyudhānāṃ gaṇeśānāṃ maheśasamatejasām  || 7.1,18.33
sahasrāṇi śatānyaṣṭau tadānīṃ purato yayuḥ  || 7.1,18.34
teṣāṃ madhye vṛṣārūḍho gajārūḍho yathā guruḥ  || 7.1,18.34
jagāma gaṇapaḥ śrīmān somanaṃdīśvarārcitaḥ  || 7.1,18.35
devaduṃdubhayo nedurdivi divyasukhā ghanāḥ  || 7.1,18.35
nanṛturmunayassarve mumuduḥ siddhayoginaḥ  || 7.1,18.36
sasṛjuḥ puṣpavṛṣṭiṃ ca vitānopari vāridāḥ  || 7.1,18.36
tadā devagaṇaiścānyaiḥ pathi sarvatra saṃgatā  || 7.1,18.37
kṣaṇādiva piturgehaṃ praviveśa maheśvarī  || 7.1,18.37
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā kupito dakṣaścātmanaḥ kṣayakāraṇāt || 7.1,18.38
tasyā yavīyasībhyo 'pi cakre pūjāma satkṛtām  || 7.1,18.38
521

tadā śaśimukhī devī pitaraṃ sadasi sthitam  || 7.1,18.39
aṃbikā yuktamavyagramuvācākṛpaṇaṃ vacaḥ  || 7.1,18.39
devyuvāca
brahmādayaḥ piśācāṃtā yasyājñāvaśavartinaḥ  || 7.1,18.40
sa devassāṃprataṃ tāta vidhinā nārcitaḥ kila  || 7.1,18.40
tadāstāṃ mama jyāyasyāḥ putryāḥ pūjāṃ kimīdṛśīm  || 7.1,18.41
asatkṛtāmavajñāya kṛtavānasi garhitam  || 7.1,18.41
evamukto 'bravīdenāṃ dakṣaḥ krodhādamarṣitaḥ  || 7.1,18.42
tvattaḥ śreṣṭhā viśiṣṭāśca pūjyā bālāḥ sutā mama  || 7.1,18.42
tāsāṃ tu ye ca bhartāraste me bahumatā mudā  || 7.1,18.43
gunaiścāpyadhikāssarvairbhartuste tryaṃbakādapi  || 7.1,18.43
stabdhātmā tāmasaśśarvastvamimaṃ samupāśritā  || 7.1,18.44
tena tvāmavamanye 'haṃ pratikūlo hi me bhavaḥ  || 7.1,18.44
tathoktā pitaraṃ dakṣaṃ kruddhā devī tamabravīt || 7.1,18.45
śṛṇvatāmeva sarveṣāṃ ye yajñasadasi sthitāḥ  || 7.1,18.45
akasmānmama bhartāramajātāśeṣadūṣaṇam  || 7.1,18.46
vācā dūṣayase dakṣa sākṣāllokamaheśvaram  || 7.1,18.46
vidyācauro gurudrohī vedeśvaravidūṣakaḥ  || 7.1,18.47
ta ete bahupāpmānassarve daṃḍyā iti śrutiḥ  || 7.1,18.47
tasmādatyutkaṭasyāsya pāpasya sadṛśo bhṛśam  || 7.1,18.48
sahasā dāruṇo daṃḍastava daivādbhaviṣyati  || 7.1,18.48
tvayā na pūjito yasmāddevadevastriyaṃbakaḥ  || 7.1,18.49
tasmāttava kulaṃ duṣṭaṃ naṣṭamityavadhāraya  || 7.1,18.49
ityuktvā pitaraṃ ruṣṭā satī saṃtyaktasādhvasā  || 7.1,18.50
tadīyāṃ ca tanuṃ tyaktvā himavaṃtaṃ yayau girim  || 7.1,18.50
sa parvataparaḥ śrīmāṃllabdhapuṇyaphalodayaḥ  || 7.1,18.51
tadarthameva kṛtavān suciraṃ duścaraṃ tapaḥ  || 7.1,18.51
tasmāttamanugṛhṇāti bhūdhareśvaramīśvarī  || 7.1,18.52
svecchayā pitaraṃ cakre svātmano yogamāyayā  || 7.1,18.52
yadā gatā satī dakṣaṃ viniṃdya bhayavihvalā  || 7.1,18.53
tadā tirohitā maṃtrā vihataśca tato 'dhvaraḥ  || 7.1,18.53
tadupaśrutya gamanaṃ devyāstripurumardanaḥ  || 7.1,18.54
dakṣāya ca ṛṣibhyaśca cukopa ca śaśāpa tān  || 7.1,18.54
yasmādavamatā dakṣamatkṛte 'nāgasā satī  || 7.1,18.55
pūjitāścetarāḥ sarvāḥ svasutā bhartṛbhiḥ saha  || 7.1,18.55
vaivasvate 'ṃtare tasmāttava jāmātarastvamī  || 7.1,18.56
utpatsyaṃte samaṃ sarve brahmayajñeṣvayonijāḥ  || 7.1,18.56
bhavitā mānuṣo rājā cākṣuṣasya tvamanvaye  || 7.1,18.57
prācīnabarhiṣaḥ pautraḥ putraścāpi pracetasaḥ  || 7.1,18.57
ahaṃ tatrāpi te vighnamācariṣyāmi durmate  || 7.1,18.58
dharmārthakāmayukteṣu karmasvapi punaḥ punaḥ  || 7.1,18.58
tenaivaṃ vyāhṛto dakṣo rudreṇāmitatejasā  || 7.1,18.59
svāyaṃbhuvīṃ tanuṃ tyaktvā papāta bhuvi duḥkhitaḥ  || 7.1,18.59
tataḥ prācetaso dakṣo jajñe vai cākṣuṣe 'ntare  || 7.1,18.60
prācīnabarhiṣaḥ pautraḥ putraścaiva pracetasām  || 7.1,18.60
521

bhṛgvādayo 'pi jātā vai manorvaivasvatasya tu  || 7.1,18.61
aṃtare brahmaṇo yajñe vāruṇīṃ bibhratastanum  || 7.1,18.61
tadā dakṣasya dharmārthaṃ yajñe tasya durātmanaḥ  || 7.1,18.62
maheśaḥ kṛtavānvighnaṃ manā vavasvate sati  || 7.1,18.62

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe satīdehatyāgo nāmāṣṭādaśo 'dhyāyaḥ
adhyāya 19
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
kathaṃ dakṣasya dharmārthaṃ pravṛttasya durātmanaḥ  || 7.1,19.1
maheśaḥ kṛtavān vighnametadicchāma veditum  || 7.1,19.1
vāyuruvāca
viśvasya jagato māturapi devyāstapobalāt || 7.1,19.2
pitṛbhāvamupāgamya mudite himavadgirau  || 7.1,19.2
deve 'pi tatkṛtodvāhe himavacchikharālaye  || 7.1,19.3
saṃkīḍati tayā sārdhaṃ kāle bahutare gate  || 7.1,19.3
vaivasvate 'ṃtare prāpte dakṣaḥ prācetasaḥ svayam  || 7.1,19.4
aśvamedhena yajñena yakṣyamāṇo 'nvapadyata  || 7.1,19.4
tato himavataḥ pṛṣṭhe dakṣo vai yajñamāharat || 7.1,19.5
gaṃgādvāre śubhe deśe ṛṣisiddhaniṣevite  || 7.1,19.5
tasya tasminmakhedevāḥ sarve śakra purogamāḥ  || 7.1,19.6
gamanāya samāgamya buddhimāpedire tadā  || 7.1,19.6
ādityā vasavo rudrāssādhyāssaha marudgaṇaiḥ  || 7.1,19.7
ūṣmapāḥ somapāścaiva ājyapā dhūmapāstathā  || 7.1,19.7
aśvinau pitaraścaiva tathā cānye maharṣayaḥ  || 7.1,19.8
viṣṇunā sahitāḥ sarve svāgatā yajñabhāginaḥ  || 7.1,19.8
dṛṣṭvā devakulaṃ sarvamīśvareṇa vināgatam  || 7.1,19.9
dadhīco manyunāviṣṭo dakṣamevamabhāṣata  || 7.1,19.9
dadhīca uvāca
aprapūjye caiva pūjā pūjyānāṃ cāpya pūjane  || 7.1,19.10
naraḥ pāpamavāpnoti mahadvai nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 7.1,19.10
asatāṃ saṃmatiryatra satāmavamatistathā  || 7.1,19.11
daṃḍo devakṛtastatra sadyaḥ patati dāruṇaḥ  || 7.1,19.11
evamuktvā tu viprarṣiḥ punardakṣamabhāṣata  || 7.1,19.12
pūjyaṃ tu paśubhartāraṃ kasmānnārcayase prabhum  || 7.1,19.12
dakṣa uvāca
saṃti me bahavo rudrāḥ śūlahastāḥ kapardinaḥ  || 7.1,19.13
ekādaśāvasthitā ye nānyaṃ vedmi maheśvaram  || 7.1,19.13
dadhīca uvāca
kimebhiramarairanyaiḥ pūjitairadhvare phalam  || 7.1,19.14
rājā cedadhvarasyāsya na rudraḥ pūjyate tvayā  || 7.1,19.14
brahmaviṣṇumaheśānāṃ sraṣṭā yaḥ prabhuravyayaḥ  || 7.1,19.15
brahmādayaḥ piśācāṃtā yasya kaiṃkaryavādinaḥ  || 7.1,19.15
prakṛtīnāṃ paraścaiva puruṣasya ca yaḥ paraḥ  || 7.1,19.16
ciṃtyate yogavidvadbhi ṛṣibhistattvadarśibhiḥ  || 7.1,19.16
akṣaraṃ paramaṃ brahma hyasacca sadasacca yat || 7.1,19.17
anādimadhyanidhanamapratarkyaṃ sanātanam  || 7.1,19.17
yaḥ sraṣṭā caiva saṃhartā bhartā caiva maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,19.18
522

tasmādanyaṃ na paśyāmi śaṃkarātmānamadhvare  || 7.1,19.18
dakṣa uvāca
etanmakheśasya suvarṇapātre haviḥ samastaṃ vidhimaṃtrapūtam  || 7.1,19.19
viṣṇornayāmyapratimasya bhāgaṃ prabhorvibhajyāvahanīyamadya  || 7.1,19.19
dadhīca uvāca
yasmānnārādhito rudrassarvadeveśvareśvaraḥ  || 7.1,19.20
tasmāddakṣa tavāśeṣo yajño 'yaṃ na bhaviṣyati  || 7.1,19.20
ityuktvā vacanaṃ kruddho dadhīco munisattamaḥ  || 7.1,19.21
nirgamya ca tato deśājjagāma svakamāśramam  || 7.1,19.21
nirgate 'pi munau tasmindevā dakṣaṃ na tatyajuḥ  || 7.1,19.22
avaśyamanubhāvitvādanarthasya tu bhāvinaḥ  || 7.1,19.22
etasminneva kāle tu jñātvaitatsarvamīśvarāt || 7.1,19.23
dagdhuṃ dakṣādhvaraṃ viprā devī devamacodayat || 7.1,19.23
devyā saṃcodito devo dakṣādhvarajighāṃsayā  || 7.1,19.24
sasarja sahasā vīraṃ vīrabhadraṃ gaṇeśvaram  || 7.1,19.24
sahasravadanaṃ devaṃ sahasrakamalekṣaṇam  || 7.1,19.25
sahasramudgaradharaṃ sahasraśarapāṇikam  || 7.1,19.25
śūlaṭaṃkagadāhastaṃ dīptakārmukadhāriṇam  || 7.1,19.26
cakravajradharaṃ ghoraṃ caṃdrārdhakṛtaśekharam  || 7.1,19.26
kuliśodyotitakaraṃ taḍijjvalitamūrdhajam  || 7.1,19.27
daṃṣṭrākarālaṃ bibhrāṇaṃ mahāvaktraṃ mahodaram  || 7.1,19.27
vidyujjihvaṃ pralaṃboṣṭhaṃ meghasāgaraniḥsvanam  || 7.1,19.28
vasānaṃ carma vaiyāghraṃ mahadrudhiranisravam  || 7.1,19.28
gaṇḍadvitayasaṃsṛṣṭamaṇḍalīkṛtakuṇḍalam  || 7.1,19.29
varāmaraśiromālāvalīkalitaśekharam  || 7.1,19.29
raṇannūpurakeyūramahākanakabhūṣitam  || 7.1,19.30
ratnasaṃcayasaṃdīptaṃ tārahārāvṛtorasam  || 7.1,19.30
mahāśarabhaśārdūlasiṃhaiḥ sadṛśavikramam  || 7.1,19.31
praśastamattamātaṃgasamānagamanālasam  || 7.1,19.31
śaṃkhacāmarakuṃdendumṛṇālasadṛśaprabham  || 7.1,19.32
satuṣāramivādrīndraṃ sākṣājjaṃgamatāṃ gatam  || 7.1,19.32
jvālāmālāparikṣiptaṃ dīptamauktikabhūṣaṇam  || 7.1,19.33
tejasā caiva dīvyaṃtaṃ yugāṃta iva pāvakam  || 7.1,19.33
sa jānubhyāṃ mahīṃ gatvā praṇataḥ prāṃjalistataḥ  || 7.1,19.34
pārśvato devadevasya paryatiṣṭhadgaṇeśvaraḥ  || 7.1,19.34
manyunā cāsṛjadbhadrāṃ bhadrakālīṃ maheśvarīm  || 7.1,19.35
ātmanaḥ karmasākṣitve tena gaṃtuṃ sahaiva tu  || 7.1,19.35
taṃ dṛṣṭvāvasthitaṃ vīrabhadraṃ kālāgnisannibham  || 7.1,19.36
bhadrayā sahitaṃ prāha bhadramastviti śaṃkaraḥ  || 7.1,19.36
sa ca vijñāpayāmāsa saha devyā maheśvaram  || 7.1,19.37
ājñāpaya mahādeva kiṃ kāryaṃ karavāṇyaham  || 7.1,19.37
tatastripurahā prāha haimavatyāḥ priyecchayā  || 7.1,19.38
vīrabhadraṃ mahābāhuṃ vācā vipulanādayā  || 7.1,19.38
devadeva uvāca
prācetasasya dakṣasya yajñaṃ sadyo vināśaya  || 7.1,19.39
bhadrakālyā sahāsi tvametatkṛtyaṃ gaṇeśvara  || 7.1,19.39
522

ahamapyanayā sārdhaṃ raibhyāśramasapīpataḥ  || 7.1,19.40
sthitvā vīkṣe gaṇeśāna vikramaṃ tava duḥsaham  || 7.1,19.40
vṛkṣā kanakhale ye tu gaṃgādvārasamīpagāḥ  || 7.1,19.41
suvarṇaśṛṃgasya girermerumaṃdarasaṃnibhāḥ  || 7.1,19.41
tasminpradeśe dakṣasya yujñaḥ saṃprati vartate  || 7.1,19.42
sahasā tasya yajñasya vighātaṃ kuru mā ciram  || 7.1,19.42
ityukte sati devena devī himagirīndrajā  || 7.1,19.43
bhadraṃ bhadraṃ ca saṃprekṣya vatsaṃ dhenurivaurasam  || 7.1,19.43
āliṃgya ca samāghrāya mūrdhni ṣaḍvadanaṃ yathā  || 7.1,19.44
sasmitā vacanaṃ prāha madhuraṃ madhuraṃ svayam  || 7.1,19.44
devyuvāca
vatsa bhadra mahābhāga mahābalaparākrama  || 7.1,19.45
matpriyārthaṃ tvamutpanno mama manyuṃ pramārjaka  || 7.1,19.45
yajñeśvaramanāhūya yajñakarmarato 'bhavat || 7.1,19.46
dakṣaṃ vaireṇa taṃ tasmādbhiṃdhi yajñaṃ gaṇeśvara  || 7.1,19.46
yajñalakṣmīmalakṣmīṃ tvaṃ bhadra kṛtvā mamājñayā  || 7.1,19.47
yajamānaṃ ca taṃ hatvā vatsa hiṃsaya bhadrayā  || 7.1,19.47
aśeṣāmiva tāmājñāṃ śivayościtrakṛtyayoḥ  || 7.1,19.48
mūrdhni kṛtvā namaskṛtya bhadro gaṃtuṃ pracakrame  || 7.1,19.48
athaiṣa bhagavānkruddhaḥ pretāvāsakṛtālayaḥ  || 7.1,19.49
vīrabhadro mahādevo devyā manyupramārjakaḥ  || 7.1,19.49
sasarja romakūpebhyo romajākhyāngaṇeśvarān  || 7.1,19.50
dakṣiṇādbhujadeśāttu śatakoṭigaviśvarān  || 7.1,19.50
pādāttathorudeśācca pṛṣṭhātpārśvānmukhādgalāt || 7.1,19.51
guhyādgulphācchiromadhyātkaṃṭhādāsyāttathodarāt || 7.1,19.51
tadā gaṇeśvarairbhadrairbhadratulyaparākramaiḥ  || 7.1,19.52
saṃchāditamabhūtsarvaṃ sākāśavivaraṃ jagat || 7.1,19.52
sarve sahasrahastāste sahasrāyudhapāṇayaḥ  || 7.1,19.53
rudrasyānucarāssarve sarve rudrasamaprabhāḥ  || 7.1,19.53
śūlaśaktigadāhastāṣṭaṃkopalaśilādharāḥ  || 7.1,19.54
kālāgnirudrasadṛśāstrinetrāśca jaṭādharāḥ  || 7.1,19.54
nipeturbhṛśamākāśe śataśassiṃhavāhanāḥ  || 7.1,19.55
vineduśca mahānādāñjaladā iva bhadrajāḥ  || 7.1,19.55
tairbhadrairbhagavānmadrastathā parivṛto babhau  || 7.1,19.56
kālānalaśatairyukto yathāṃte kālabhairavaḥ  || 7.1,19.56
teṣāṃ madhye samāruhya vṛṣeṃdraṃ vṛṣabhadhvajaḥ  || 7.1,19.57
jagāma bhagavānbhadraśśubhamabhraṃ yathā bhavaḥ  || 7.1,19.57
tasminvṛṣabhamārūḍhe bhadre tu bhasitaprabhaḥ  || 7.1,19.58
babhāra mauktikaṃ chatraṃ gṛhītasitacāmaraḥ  || 7.1,19.58
sa tadā śuśubhe pārśve bhadrasya bhasitaprabhaḥ  || 7.1,19.59
bhagavāniva śailendraḥ pārśve viśvajagadguroḥ  || 7.1,19.59
so 'pi tena babhau bhadraḥ śvetacāmarapāṇinā  || 7.1,19.60
bālasomena saumyena yathā śūlavarāyudhaḥ  || 7.1,19.60
dadhmau śaṃkhaṃ sitaṃ bhadraṃ bhadrasya purataḥ śubham  || 7.1,19.61
bhānukaṃpo mahātejā hemaratnairalaṃkṛtaḥ  || 7.1,19.61
devaduṃdubhayo nedurdivyasaṃkulaniḥsvanāḥ  || 7.1,19.62
523

vavṛṣuśśataśo mūrdhni puṣpavarṣaṃ balāhakāḥ  || 7.1,19.62
phullānāṃ madhugarbhāṇāṃ puṣpāṇāṃ gaṃdhabaṃdhavaḥ  || 7.1,19.63
mārgānukūlasaṃvāhā vabuśca pathi mārutāḥ  || 7.1,19.63
tato gaṇeśvarāḥ sarve mattā yuddhabaloddhatāḥ  || 7.1,19.64
nanṛturmumudur1 edurjahasurjagadurjaguḥ  || 7.1,19.64
tadā bhadragaṇāṃtaḥstho babhau bhadraḥ sa bhadrayā  || 7.1,19.65
yathā rudragaṇāṃtaḥ sthastryambakoṃbikayā saha  || 7.1,19.65
tatkṣaṇādeva dakṣasya yajñavāṭaṃ raṇmayam  || 7.1,19.66
praviveśa mahābāhurvīrabhadro mahānugaḥ  || 7.1,19.66
tatastu dakṣapratipāditasya kratupradhānasya gaṇapradhānaḥ  || 7.1,19.67
prayogabhūmiṃ praviveśa bhadro rudro yathāṃte bhuvanaṃ didhakṣuḥ  || 7.1,19.67
iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe vīrabhadrotpattivarṇanaṃ nāmaikonaviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 20
vāyuruvāca
tato viṣṇupradhānānāṃ surāṇāmamitaujasām  || 7.1,20.1
dadarśa ca mahatsatraṃ citradhvajaparicchadam  || 7.1,20.1
sudarbhaṛtusaṃstīrṇaṃ susamiddhahutāśanam  || 7.1,20.2
kāṃcanairyajñabhāṃḍaiśca bhrājiṣṇubhiralaṃkṛtam  || 7.1,20.2
ṛṣibhiryajñapaṭubhiryathāvatkarmakartṛbhiḥ  || 7.1,20.3
vidhinā vedadṛṣṭena svanuṣṭhitabahukramam  || 7.1,20.3
devāṃganāsahasrāḍhyamapsarogaṇasevitam  || 7.1,20.4
veṇuvīṇāravairjuṣṭaṃ vedaghoṣaiśca bṛṃhitam  || 7.1,20.4
dṛṣṭvā dakṣādhvare vīro vīrabhadraḥ pratāpavān  || 7.1,20.5
siṃhanādaṃ tadā cakre gaṃbhīro jalado yathā  || 7.1,20.5
tataḥ kilakilāśabda ākāśaṃ pūrayanniva  || 7.1,20.6
gaṇeśvaraiḥ kṛto jajñe mahānnyakkṛtasāgaraḥ  || 7.1,20.6
tena śabdena mahatāḥ grastā sarvedivaukasaḥ  || 7.1,20.7
dudruvuḥ parito bhītāḥ srastavastravibhūṣaṇāḥ  || 7.1,20.7
kiṃsvidbhagno mahāmeruḥ kiṃsvitsaṃdīryate mahī  || 7.1,20.8
kimidaṃ kimidaṃ veti jajalpustridaśā bhṛśam  || 7.1,20.8
mṛgendrāṇāṃ yathā nādaṃ gajeṃdrā gahane vane  || 7.1,20.9
śrutvā tathāvidhaṃ kecittatyajurjīvitaṃ bhayāt || 7.1,20.9
parvatāśca vyaśīryaṃta cakampe ca vasuṃdharā  || 7.1,20.10
marutaśca vyaghūrṇaṃta cukṣubhe makarālayaḥ  || 7.1,20.10
agnayo naiva dīpyaṃte na ca dīpyati bhāskaraḥ  || 7.1,20.11
grahāśca na prakāśaṃte nakṣatrāṇi ca tārakāḥ  || 7.1,20.11
etasminneva kāle tu yajñavāṭaṃ tadujjvalam  || 7.1,20.12
saṃprāpa bhagavānbhadro bhadraiśca saha bhadrayā  || 7.1,20.12
taṃ dṛṣṭvā bhītabhīto 'pi dakṣo dṛḍha iva sthitaḥ  || 7.1,20.13
kruddhavadvacanaṃ prāha ko bhavān kimihecchasi  || 7.1,20.13
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā dakṣasya ca durātmanaḥ  || 7.1,20.14
vīrabhadro mahātejā meghasaṃbhīranissvanaḥ  || 7.1,20.14

1 parasmaipadamārṣam

523

smayanniva tamālokya dakṣaṃ devāśca ṛtvijaḥ  || 7.1,20.15
arthagarbhamasaṃbhrāntamavocaducitaṃ vacaḥ  || 7.1,20.15
vīrabhadra uvāca
vayaṃ hyanucarāḥ sarve śarvasyāmitatejasaḥ  || 7.1,20.16
bhāgābhilipsayā prāptā bhāgo nassaṃpradīyatām  || 7.1,20.16
atha cedadhvare 'smākaṃ na bhāgaḥ parikalpitaḥ  || 7.1,20.17
kathyatāṃ kāraṇaṃ tatra yudhyatāṃ vā mayāmaraiḥ  || 7.1,20.17
ityuktāste gaṇeṃdreṇa devā dakṣapurogamāḥ  || 7.1,20.18
ūcurmantrāḥ pramāṇaṃ no na vayaṃ prabhavastviti  || 7.1,20.18
mantrā ūcussurā yūyaṃ mohopahatacetasaḥ  || 7.1,20.19
yena prathamabhāgārhaṃ na yajadhvaṃ maheśvaram  || 7.1,20.19
maṃtroktā api te devāḥ sarve saṃmūḍhacetasaḥ  || 7.1,20.20
bhadrāya na dadurbhāgaṃ tatprahāṇamabhīpsavaḥ  || 7.1,20.20
yadā tathyaṃ ca pathyaṃ ca svavākyaṃ tadvṛthā 'bhavat || 7.1,20.21
tadā tato yayurmaṃdā brahmalokaṃ sanātanam  || 7.1,20.21
athovāca gaṇādhyakṣo devānviṣṇupurogamān  || 7.1,20.22
mantrāḥ pramāṇaṃ na kṛtā yuṣmābhirbalagarvitaiḥ  || 7.1,20.22
yasmādasminmakhe devairitthaṃ vayamasatkṛtāḥ  || 7.1,20.23
tasmādvo jīvitaissārdhamapaneṣyāmi garvitam  || 7.1,20.23
ityuktvā bhagavān kruddho vyadahannetravahninā  || 7.1,20.24
yakṣavāṭaṃ mahākūṭaṃ yathātisraḥ puro haraḥ  || 7.1,20.24
tato gaṇeśvarāssarve parvatodagravigrahāḥ  || 7.1,20.25
yūpānutpāṭya hotḥṇāṃ kaṃṭheṣvābadhya rajjubhiḥ  || 7.1,20.25
yajñapātrāṇi citrāṇi bhittvā saṃcūrṇya vāriṇi  || 7.1,20.26
gṛhītvā caiva yajñāṃgaṃ gaṃgāsrotasi cikṣipuḥ  || 7.1,20.26
tatra divyānnapānānāṃ rāśayaḥ parvatopamāḥ  || 7.1,20.27
kṣīranadyo 'mṛtasrāvāḥ susnigdhadadhikardamāḥ  || 7.1,20.27
uccāvacāni māṃsāni bhakṣyāṇi surabhīṇi ca  || 7.1,20.28
rasavanti ca pānāni lehyacoṣyāṇi tāni vai  || 7.1,20.28
vīrāstadbhujate vaktrairviluṃpaṃti kṣipaṃti ca  || 7.1,20.29
vajraiścakrairmahāśūlaiśśaktibhiḥ pāśapaṭṭiśaiḥ  || 7.1,20.29
musalairasibhiṣṭaṃkairbhidhipālaiḥ paraśvadhaiḥ  || 7.1,20.30
uddhatāṃstridaśānsarvāṃllokapālapurassarān  || 7.1,20.30
bibhidurbalino vīrā vīrabhadrāṃgasaṃbhavāḥ  || 7.1,20.31
chiṃdhi bhiṃdhi kṣipa kṣipraṃ māryatāṃ dāryatāmiti  || 7.1,20.31
harasva praharasveti pāṭayotpāṭayeti ca  || 7.1,20.32
saṃraṃbhaprabhavāḥ krūrāśśabdāḥ śravaṇaśaṃkavaḥ  || 7.1,20.32
yatratatra gaṇeśānāṃ jajñire samarocitāḥ  || 7.1,20.33
vivṛttanayanāḥ keciddaṣṭadaṃṣṭroṣṭhatālavaḥ  || 7.1,20.33
āśramasthānsamākṛṣya mārayanti tapodhanāt || 7.1,20.34
sruvānapaharantaśca kṣipantogniṃ jaleṣu ca  || 7.1,20.34
kalaśānapi bhindaṃtaśchiṃdaṃto maṇivedikāḥ  || 7.1,20.35
gāyaṃtaśca nadantaśca hasantaśca muhurmuhuḥ  || 7.1,20.35
raktāsavaṃ pibantaśca nanṛturgaṇapuṃgavāḥ  || 7.1,20.36
524

nirmathya seṃdrānamarān gaṇendrānvṛṣendranāgendramṛgendrasārāḥ  || 7.1,20.36
cakrurbahūnyapratimabhāvāḥ saharṣaromāṇi viceṣṭitāni  || 7.1,20.37
nandaṃti kecitpraharanti keciddhāvanti kecitpralapanti kecit || 7.1,20.37
nṛtyanti kecidvihasanti kecidvalganti kecitpramathā balena  || 7.1,20.38
kecijjighṛkṣaṃti ghanānsa toyānkecidgrahītuṃ ravimutpataṃti  || 7.1,20.39
kecitprasartuṃ pavanena sārdhamicchaṃti bhīmāḥ pramathā viyatsthāḥ  || 7.1,20.39
ākṣipya kecicca varāyudhāni mahā bhujaṃgāniva vainateyāḥ  || 7.1,20.40
bhramaṃti devānapi vidravaṃtaḥ khamaṃḍale parvatakūṭakalpāḥ  || 7.1,20.40
utpāṭya cotpāṭyagṛhāṇi kecitsajālavātāyanavedikāni  || 7.1,20.41
vikṣipya vikṣipya jalasya madhye kālāṃbudābhāḥ pramathā nineduḥ  || 7.1,20.41
udvartitadvārakapāṭakuḍyaṃ vidhvastaśālāvalabhīgavākṣam  || 7.1,20.42
aho batābhajyata yajñavāṭamanāthavadvākyamivāyathārtham  || 7.1,20.42
hā nātha tāteti pituḥ suteti bhratarmamāmbeti ca mātuleti  || 7.1,20.43
utpāṭyamāneṣu gṛheṣu nāryo hyānāthaśabdānbahuśaḥ pracakruḥ  || 7.1,20.43

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe yajñavidhvaṃsano nāma viṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 21
vāyuruvāca
tatastridaśamukhyāste viṣṇuśakrapurogamāḥ  || 7.1,21.1
sarve bhayaparitrastādudruvurbhayavihvalāḥ  || 7.1,21.1
nijairadūṣitairaṃgairdṛṣṭvā devānupadrutān  || 7.1,21.2
daṃḍyānadaṃḍitānmatvā cukopa gaṇapuṃgavaḥ  || 7.1,21.2
tatastriśūlamādāya śarvaśaktinibarhaṇam  || 7.1,21.3
ūrdhvadṛṣṭirmahābāhurmukhājjvālāḥ samutsṛjan  || 7.1,21.3
amarānapi dudrāva dviradāniva kesarī  || 7.1,21.4
tānabhidravatastasya gamanaṃ sumanoharam  || 7.1,21.4
vārāṇasyeva mattasya jagāma prekṣaṇīyatām  || 7.1,21.5
tatastatkṣobhayāmāsa mahatsurabalaṃ balī  || 7.1,21.5
mahāsarovaraṃ yadvanmatto vāraṇayūthapaḥ  || 7.1,21.6
vikurvanbahudhāvarṇānnīlapāṃḍuralohitān  || 7.1,21.6
vibhradvyāghrājinaṃ vāso hemapravaratārakam  || 7.1,21.7
chindanbhindannuda 1 lindandārayanpramathannapi  || 7.1,21.7
vyacaraddevasaṃgheṣu bhadro 'gniriva kakṣagaḥ  || 7.1,21.8
tatra tatra mahāvegāccaraṃtaṃ śūladhāriṇam  || 7.1,21.8
tamekaṃ tridaśāḥ sarve sahasramiva menire  || 7.1,21.9
bhadrakālī ca saṃkruddhā yuddhavṛddhamadoddhatā  || 7.1,21.9
muktajvālena śūlena nirbibheda raṇe surān  || 7.1,21.10
sa tayā ruruce bhadro rudrakopasamudbhavaḥ  || 7.1,21.10
prabhayeva yugāṃtāgniścalayā dhūmadhūmrayā  || 7.1,21.11
bhadrakālī tadāyuddhe vidrutatridaśābabhau  || 7.1,21.11

1 nalopa ārṣaḥ

524

kalpe śeṣānalajvālādagdhāviśvajagadyathā  || 7.1,21.12
tadā savājinaṃ sūryaṃ rudrānrudragaṇāgraṇīḥ  || 7.1,21.12
bhadro mūrdhni jaghānāśu vāmapādena līlayā  || 7.1,21.13
asibhiḥ pāvakaṃ bhadraḥ paṭṭiśaistu yamaṃ yamī  || 7.1,21.13
rudrāndṛḍhena śūlena mudgarairvaruṇaṃ dṛḍhaiḥ  || 7.1,21.14
parighairnirṛtiṃ vāyuṃ ṭaṃkaiṣṭaṃkadharaḥ svayam  || 7.1,21.14
nirbibheda raṇe vīro līlayaiva gaṇeśvaraḥ  || 7.1,21.15
sarvāndevagaṇānsadyo munīñchaṃbhorvirodhinaḥ  || 7.1,21.15
tato devaḥ sarasvatyā nāsikāgraṃ suśobhanam  || 7.1,21.16
ciccheda karajāgreṇa devamātustathaiva ca  || 7.1,21.16
ciccheda ca kuṭhāreṇa bāhudaṃḍaṃ vibhāvasoḥ  || 7.1,21.17
agrato dvyaṃgulāṃ jihvāṃ māturdevyā lulāva ca  || 7.1,21.17
svāhādevyāstathā devo dakṣiṇaṃ nāsikāpuṭam  || 7.1,21.18
cakarta karajāgreṇa vāmaṃ ca stanacūcukam  || 7.1,21.18
bhagasya vipule netre śatapatrasamaprabhe  || 7.1,21.19
prasahyotpāṭayāmāsa bhadraḥ paramavegavān  || 7.1,21.19
pūṣṇo daśanarekhāṃ ca dīptāṃ muktāvalīmiva  || 7.1,21.20
jaghāna dhanuṣaḥ koṭyā sa tenāspaṣṭavāgabhūt || 7.1,21.20
tataścaṃdramasaṃ devaḥ pādāṃguṣṭhena līlayā  || 7.1,21.21
kṣaṇaṃ kṛmivadākramya gharṣayāmāsa bhūtale  || 7.1,21.21
śiraściccheda dakṣasya bhadraḥ paramakopataḥ  || 7.1,21.22
krośaṃtyāmeva vairiṇyāṃ bhadrakālyai dadau ca tat || 7.1,21.22
tatprahṛṣṭā samādāya śirastālaphalopamam  || 7.1,21.23
sā devī kaṃḍukakrīḍāṃ cakāra samarāṃgaṇe  || 7.1,21.23
tato dakṣasya yajñastrī kuśīlā bhartṛbhiryathā  || 7.1,21.24
pādābhyāṃ caiva hastābhyāṃ hanyate sma gaṇeśvaraiḥ  || 7.1,21.24
ariṣṭanemine somaṃ dharmaṃ caiva prajāpatim  || 7.1,21.25
bahuputraṃ cāṃgirasaṃ kṛśāśvaṃ kaśyapaṃ tathā  || 7.1,21.25
gale pragṛhya balino gaṇapāḥ siṃhavikramāḥ  || 7.1,21.26
bhartsayaṃto bhṛśaṃ vāgbhirnirjaghnurmūrdhni muṣṭibhiḥ  || 7.1,21.26
dharṣitā bhūtavetālairdārāssutaparigrahāḥ  || 7.1,21.27
yathā kaliyuge jārairbalena kulayoṣitaḥ  || 7.1,21.27
tacca vidhvastakalaśaṃ bhagnayūpaṃ gatotsavam  || 7.1,21.28
pradīpitamahāśālaṃ prabhinnadvāratoraṇam  || 7.1,21.28
utpāṭitasurānīkaṃ hanyamānaṃ tapodhanam  || 7.1,21.29
praśāntabrahmanirghoṣaṃ prakṣīṇajanasaṃcayam  || 7.1,21.29
krandamānāturastrīkaṃ hatāśeṣaparicchadam  || 7.1,21.30
śūnyāraṇyanibhaṃ jajñe yajñavāṭaṃ tadārditam  || 7.1,21.30
śūlavegaprarugṇāśca bhinnabāhūruvakṣasaḥ  || 7.1,21.31
vinikṛttottamāṃgāśca petururvyāṃ surottamāḥ  || 7.1,21.31
hateṣu teṣu deveṣu patiteṣuḥ sahasraśaḥ  || 7.1,21.32
praviveśa gaṇeśānaḥ kṣaṇādāhavanīyakam  || 7.1,21.32
praviṣṭamatha taṃ dṛṣṭvā bhadraṃ kālāgnisaṃnibham  || 7.1,21.33
dudrāva maraṇādbhīto yajño mṛgavapurdharaḥ  || 7.1,21.33
sa visphārya mahaccāpaṃ dṛḍhajyāghoṣaṇabhīṣaṇam  || 7.1,21.34
525

bhadrastamabhidudrāva vikṣipanneva sāyakān  || 7.1,21.34
ākarṇapūrṇamākṛṣṭaṃ dhanurambudasaṃnibham  || 7.1,21.35
nādayāmāsa ca jyāṃ dyāṃ khaṃ ca bhūmiṃ ca sarvaśaḥ  || 7.1,21.35
tamupaśritya sannādaṃ hato 'smītyeva vihvalam  || 7.1,21.36
śaraṇārdhena vakreṇa sa vīro 'dhvarapūruṣam  || 7.1,21.36
mahābhayaskhalatpādaṃ vepantaṃ vigatatviṣam  || 7.1,21.37
mṛgarūpeṇa dhāvantaṃ viśiraskaṃ tadākarot || 7.1,21.37
tamīdṛśamavajñātaṃ dṛṣṭvā vai sūryasaṃbhavam  || 7.1,21.38
viṣṇuḥ paramasaṃkruddho yuddhāyābhavadudyataḥ  || 7.1,21.38
tamuvāha mahāvegātskandhena natasaṃdhinā  || 7.1,21.39
sarveṣāṃ vayasāṃ rājā garuḍaḥ pannagāśanaḥ  || 7.1,21.39
devāśca hataśiṣṭā ye devarājapurogamāḥ  || 7.1,21.40
pracakrustasya sāhāyyaṃ prāṇāṃstyaktumivodyatāḥ  || 7.1,21.40
viṣṇunā sahitāndevānmṛgendraḥ kroṣṭukāniva  || 7.1,21.41
dṛṣṭvā jahāsa bhūtendro mṛgendra iva vivyathaḥ  || 7.1,21.41

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe devadaṃḍavarṇanaṃ nāmaikaviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 22
tasminnavasare vyomni samāvirabhavadrathaḥ  || 7.1,22.1
sahasrasūryasaṃkāśaścārucīravṛṣadhvajaḥ  || 7.1,22.1
aśvaratnadvayodāro rathacakracatuṣṭayaḥ  || 7.1,22.2
sañcitānekadivyāstraśastraratnapariṣkṛtaḥ  || 7.1,22.2
tasyāpi rathavaryasya syātsa eva hi sārathiḥ  || 7.1,22.3
yathā ca traipure yuddhe pūrvaṃ śārvarathe sthitaḥ  || 7.1,22.3
sa taṃ rathavaraṃ brahmā śāsanādeva śūlinaḥ  || 7.1,22.4
haressamīpamānīya kṛtāñjalirabhāṣata  || 7.1,22.4
bhagavanbhadra bhadrāṃga bhagavānindubhūṣaṇaḥ  || 7.1,22.5
ājñāpayati vīrastvāṃ rathamāroḍhumavyayaḥ  || 7.1,22.5
rebhyāśramasamīpasthastryaṃbako 'ṃbikayā saha  || 7.1,22.6
sampaśyate mahābāho dussahaṃ te parākramam  || 7.1,22.6
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā sa vīro gaṇakuñjaraḥ  || 7.1,22.7
āruroha rathaṃ divyamanugṛhya pitāmaham  || 7.1,22.7
tathā rathavare tasminsthite brahmaṇi sārathau  || 7.1,22.8
bhadrasya vavṛdhe lakṣmī rudrasyeva puradviṣaḥ  || 7.1,22.8
tataḥ śaṃkhavaraṃ dīptaṃ pūrṇacaṃdrasamaprabham  || 7.1,22.9
pradadhmau vadane kṛtvā bhānukaṃpo mahābalaḥ  || 7.1,22.9
tasya śaṃkhasya taṃ nādaṃ bhinnasārasasannibham  || 7.1,22.10
śrutvā bhayena devānāṃ jajvāla jaṭharānalaḥ  || 7.1,22.10
yakṣavidyādharāhīndraiḥ siddhairyuddhadidṛkṣubhiḥ  || 7.1,22.11
kṣaṇena nibaḍībhūtāḥ sākāśavivarā diśāḥ  || 7.1,22.11
tataḥ śārṅgeṇa cāpāṅkātsa nārāyaṇanīradaḥ  || 7.1,22.12
mahatā bāṇavarṣeṇa tutoda gaṇagovṛṣam  || 7.1,22.12
taṃ dṛṣṭvā viṣṇumāyāṃtaṃ śatadhā bāṇavarṣiṇam  || 7.1,22.13
sa cādade dhanurjaitraṃ bhadro bāṇasahasramuk || 7.1,22.13
samādāya ca taddivyaṃ dhanussamarabhairavam  || 7.1,22.14
śanairvisphārayāmāsa meruṃ dhanuriveśvaraḥ  || 7.1,22.14
tasya visphāryamāṇasya dhanuṣo 'bhūnmahāsvanaḥ  || 7.1,22.15
tena svanena mahatā pṛthivīṃ samakaṃpayat || 7.1,22.15
tataḥ śaravaraṃ ghoraṃ dīptamāśīviṣopamam  || 7.1,22.16
jagrāha gaṇapaḥ śrīmānsvayamugraparākramaḥ  || 7.1,22.16
bāṇoddhāre bhujo hyasya tūṇīvadanasaṃgataḥ  || 7.1,22.17
pratyadṛśyata valmīkaṃ vivekṣuriva pannagaḥ  || 7.1,22.17
samuddhṛtaḥ kare tasya tatkṣaṇaṃ ruruce śareḥ  || 7.1,22.18
mahābhujaṃgasaṃdaṣṭo yathā bālabhujaṅgamaḥ  || 7.1,22.18
śareṇa ghanatīvreṇa bhadro rudraparākramaḥ  || 7.1,22.19
vivyādha kupito gāḍhaṃ lalāṭe viṣṇumavyayam  || 7.1,22.19
lalāṭe 'bhihito viṣṇuḥ pūrvamevāvamānitaḥ  || 7.1,22.20
cukopa gaṇapeṃdrāya mṛgeṃdrāyeva govṛṣaḥ  || 7.1,22.20
tatastvaśanikalpena krūrāsyena maheṣuṇā  || 7.1,22.21
vivyādha gaṇarājasya bhuje bhujagasannibhe  || 7.1,22.21
so 'pi tasya bhuje bhūyaḥ sūryāyutasamaprabham  || 7.1,22.22
visasarja śaraṃ vegādvīrabhadro mahābalaḥ  || 7.1,22.22
sa ca viṣṇuḥ punarbhadraṃ bhadro viṣṇuṃ tathā punaḥ  || 7.1,22.23
sa ca taṃ sa ca taṃ viprāśśaraistāvanujaghnatuḥ  || 7.1,22.23
tayoḥ parasparaṃ vegāccharānāśu vimuṃcatoḥ  || 7.1,22.24
dvayossamabhavadyuddhaṃ tumulaṃ romaharṣaṇam  || 7.1,22.24
taddṛṣṭvā tumulaṃ yuddhaṃ tayoreva parasparam  || 7.1,22.25
hāhākāro mahānāsīdākāśe khecareritaḥ  || 7.1,22.25
tatastvanalatuṃḍena śareṇādityavarcasā  || 7.1,22.26
vivyādha sudṛḍhaṃ bhadro viṣṇormahati vakṣasi  || 7.1,22.26
sa tu tīvraprapātena śareṇa dṛḍhamāhataḥ  || 7.1,22.27
mahatīṃ rujamāsādya nipapāta vimohitaḥ  || 7.1,22.27
punaḥ kṣaṇādivotthāya labdhasaṃjñastadā hariḥ  || 7.1,22.28
sarvāṇyapi ca divyāstrāṇyathainaṃ pratyavāsṛjat || 7.1,22.28
sa ca viṣṇurdhanurmuktānsarvāñcharvacamūpatiḥ  || 7.1,22.29
sahasā vārayāmāsa ghoraiḥ pratiśaraiḥ śarān  || 7.1,22.29
tato viṣṇussvanāmāṃkaṃ bāṇamavyāhataṃ kvacit || 7.1,22.30
sasarja krodharaktā kṣastamuddiśya gaṇeśvaram  || 7.1,22.30
taṃ bāṇaṃ bāṇavaryeṇa bhadro bhadrāhvayeṇa tu  || 7.1,22.30
aprāptameva bhagavāñciccheda śatadhā pathi  || 7.1,22.31
athaikeneṣuṇā śārṅgaṃ dvābhyāṃ pakṣau garutmataḥ  || 7.1,22.31
nimeṣādeva ciccheda tadadbhutamivābhavat || 7.1,22.32
tato yogabalādviṣṇurdehāddevānsudāruṇān  || 7.1,22.32
śaṃkhacakragadāhastān visasarja sahasraśaḥ  || 7.1,22.33
sarvāṃstānkṣaṇamātreṇa traipurāniva śaṃkaraḥ  || 7.1,22.33
nirdadāha mahābāhurnetrasṛṣṭena vahninā  || 7.1,22.34
tataḥ kruddhataro viṣṇuścakramudyamya satvaraḥ  || 7.1,22.34
tasminvīro samutsraṣṭuṃ tadānīmudyato 'bhavat || 7.1,22.35
taṃ dṛṣṭvā cakramudyamya purataḥ samupasthitam  || 7.1,22.35
smayanniva gaṇeśāno vyaṣṭaṃbhayadayatnataḥ  || 7.1,22.36
staṃbhitāṃgastu taccakraṃ ghoramapratimaṃ kvacit || 7.1,22.36
icchannapi samutsraṣṭuṃ na viṣṇurabhavatkṣamaḥ  || 7.1,22.37
śvasannivaikamuddhṛtya bāhuṃ cakrasamanvitam  || 7.1,22.37
atiṣṭhadalaso bhūtvā pāṣāṇa iva niścalaḥ  || 7.1,22.38
viśarīro yathājīvo viśṛṅgo vā yathā vṛṣaḥ  || 7.1,22.38
vidaṃṣṭraśca yathā siṃhastathā viṣṇuravasthitaḥ  || 7.1,22.39
taṃ dṛṣṭvā durdaśāpannaṃ viṣṇumiṃdrādayaḥ surāḥ  || 7.1,22.39
samunnaddhā gaṇendreṇa mṛgeṃdreṇeva govṛṣāḥ  || 7.1,22.39
pragṛhītāyudhā yauddhuṃkruddhāḥ samupatasthire  || 7.1,22.40
tāndṛṣṭvā samare bhadraḥkṣudrāniva harirmṛgān  || 7.1,22.40
sākṣādrudratanurvīro varavīragaṇāvṛtaḥ  || 7.1,22.41
aṭṭahāsena ghoreṇa vyaṣṭaṃ bhayadaniṃditaḥ  || 7.1,22.41
tathā śatamakhasyāpi savajro dakṣiṇaḥ karaḥ  || 7.1,22.42
sisṛkṣoreva udvajraścitrīkṛta ivābhavat || 7.1,22.42
anyeṣāmapi sarveṣāṃ saraktā api bāhavaḥ  || 7.1,22.43
alasānāmivāraṃbhāstādṛśāḥ pratiyāṃtyuta  || 7.1,22.43
evaṃ bhagavatā tena vyāhatāśeṣavaibhavāt || 7.1,22.44
amarāḥ samare tasya purataḥ sthātumakṣamāḥ  || 7.1,22.44
stabdhairavayavaireva dudruvurbhayavihvalāḥ  || 7.1,22.45
sthitiṃ ca cakrire yuddhe vīratejobhayākulāḥ  || 7.1,22.45
vidrutāṃstridaśānvīrānvīrabhadro mahābhujaḥ  || 7.1,22.46
vivyādha niśitairbāṇairmagho varṣairivācalān  || 7.1,22.46
bahavastasya vīrasya bāhavaḥ parighopamāḥ  || 7.1,22.47
śastraiścakāśire dīptaiḥ sāgnijvālā ivoragāḥ  || 7.1,22.47
astraśastrāṇyanekānisavīro visṛjanbabhau  || 7.1,22.48
visṛjansarvabhūtāni yathādau viśvasaṃbhavaḥ  || 7.1,22.48
yathā raśmibhirādityaḥ pracchādayati medinīm  || 7.1,22.49
tathā vīraḥ kṣaṇādeva śaraiḥ prācchādayaddiśaḥ  || 7.1,22.49
khamaṃḍale gaṇendrasya śarāḥ kanakabhūṣitāḥ  || 7.1,22.50
utpataṃtastaḍidrūpairupamānapadaṃ yayuḥ  || 7.1,22.50
mahāṃtaste suragaṇānmaṃḍūkānivaḍuṃḍubhāḥ  || 7.1,22.51
prāṇairviyojayāmāsuḥ papuśca rudhirāsavam  || 7.1,22.51
nikṛttabāhavaḥ kecitkecillūnavarānanāḥ  || 7.1,22.52
pārśve vidāritāḥ kecinnipeturamarā bhuvi  || 7.1,22.52
viśikhonmathitairgātrairbahubhiśchinnasandhibhiḥ  || 7.1,22.53
vivṛttanayanāḥ kecinnipeturbhūtale mṛtāḥ  || 7.1,22.53
gāṃ praveṣṭumivecchaṃtaḥ khaṃ gaṃtumiva lipsavaḥ  || 7.1,22.53
alabdhātmanirodhānāṃ vyalīyaṃtaḥ parasparam  || 7.1,22.54
bhūmau kecitpraviviśuḥ parvatānāṃ guhāḥ pare  || 7.1,22.54
apare jagmurākāśaṃ pare ca viviśurjalam  || 7.1,22.55
tathā saṃchinnasarvāṃgaissa vīrastridaśairbabhau  || 7.1,22.55
parigrastaprajāvargo bhagavāniva bhairavaḥ  || 7.1,22.56
dagdhatripurasaṃvyūhastripurāriryathābhavat || 7.1,22.56
evaṃ devabalaṃ sarvaṃ dīnaṃ bībhatsadarśanam  || 7.1,22.57
gaṇeśvarasamutpannaṃ kṛpaṇaṃ vapurādade  || 7.1,22.57
tadā tridaśavīrāṇāmasṛksalilavāhinī  || 7.1,22.58
526

prāvartata nadī ghorā prāṇināṃ bhayaśaṃsinī  || 7.1,22.58
rudhireṇa pariklinnā yajñabhūmistadā babhau  || 7.1,22.59
raktārdravasanā śyāmā hataśuṃbheva kaiśikī  || 7.1,22.59
tasminmahati saṃvṛtte samare bhṛśadāruṇe  || 7.1,22.60
bhayeneva paritrastā pracacāla vasundharā  || 7.1,22.60
mahormikalilāvartaścukṣubhe ca mahodadhiḥ  || 7.1,22.61
petuścolkā mahotpātāḥ śākhāśca mumucurdrumāḥ  || 7.1,22.61
aprasannā diśaḥ sarvāḥ pavanaścāśivo vavau  || 7.1,22.62
aho vidhiviparyāsastvaśvamedhoyamadhvaraḥ  || 7.1,22.62
yajamānassvayaṃ dakṣau brahmaputraprajāpatiḥ  || 7.1,22.62
dharmādayassadasyāśca rakṣitā garuḍadhvajaḥ  || 7.1,22.63
bhāgāṃśca pratigṛhṇaṃti sākṣādiṃdrādayaḥ surāḥ  || 7.1,22.63
tathāpi yajamānasya yajñasya ca sahartvijaḥ  || 7.1,22.64
sadya eva śiraśchedassādhu saṃpadyate phalam  || 7.1,22.64
tasmānnāvedanirdiṣṭaṃ na ceśvarabahiṣkṛtam  || 7.1,22.65
nāsatparigṛhītaṃ ca karma kuryātkadācana  || 7.1,22. 65
kṛtvāpi sumahatpuṇyamiṣṭvā yajñaśatairapi  || 7.1,22.66
na tatphalamavāpnoti bhaktihīno maheśvare  || 7.1,22.66
kṛtvāpi sumahatpāpaṃ bhaktyā yajati yaśśivam  || 7.1,22.67
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvairnātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 7.1,22.67
bahunātra kimuktena vṛthā dānaṃ vṛthā tapaḥ  || 7.1,22.68
vṛthā yajño vṛthā homaḥ śivanindāratasya tu  || 7.1,22.68
tataḥ sanārāyaṇakāssarudrāḥ salokapālāssamare suraughāḥ  || 7.1,22.69
gaṇeṃdracāpacyutabāṇaviddhāḥ pradudruvurgāḍharujābhibhūtāḥ  || 7.1,22.69
celuḥ kvacitkecana śīrṇakeśāḥ seduḥ kvacitkecana dīrghagātrāḥ  || 7.1,22.70
petuḥ kvacitkecana bhinnavaktrā neśuḥ kvacitkecana devavīrāḥ  || 7.1,22.70
kecicca tatra tridaśā vipannā visrastavastrābharaṇāstraśastrāḥ  || 7.1,22.71
nipeturudbhāsitadīnamudrā madaṃ ca darpaṃ ca balaṃ ca hitvā  || 7.1,22.71
sasmutpathaprasthitamapradhṛṣyo vikṣipya dakṣādhvaramakṣatāstraiḥ  || 7.1,22.72
babhau gaṇeśassa gaṇeśvarāṇāṃ madhye sthitaḥ siṃha ivarṣabhāṇām  || 7.1,22.72

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrkhaṇḍe dakṣayajñavidhvaṃsavarṇanaṃ nāma dvāviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 23
vāyuruvāca
iti sañchinnabhinnāṃgā devā viṣṇupurogamāḥ  || 7.1,23.1
kṣaṇātkaṣṭāṃ daśāmetya tresuḥ stokāvaśeṣitā  || 7.1,23.1
trastāṃstānsamare vīrān devānanyāṃśca vai gaṇāḥ  || 7.1,23.2
pramathāḥ paramakruddhā vīrabhadrapraṇoditāḥ  || 7.1,23.2
pragṛhya ca tathā doṣaṃ nigaḍairāyasairdṛḍhaiḥ  || 7.1,23.3
babandhuḥ pāṇipādeṣu kaṃdhareṣūdareṣu ca  || 7.1,23.3
tasminnavasare brahmā bhadramadrīndrajānutam  || 7.1,23.4
sārathyāllabdhavātsalyaḥ prārthayan praṇato 'bravīt || 7.1,23.4
527

alaṃ krodhena bhagavannaṣṭāścaite divaukasaḥ  || 7.1,23.5
prasīda kṣamyatāṃ sarvaṃ romajaissaha suvrata  || 7.1,23.5
evaṃ vijñāpitastena brahmaṇā parameṣṭhinā  || 7.1,23.6
śamaṃ jagāma saṃprīto gaṇapastasya gauravāt || 7.1,23.6
devāśca labdhāvasarā devadevasya maṃtriṇaḥ  || 7.1,23.7
dhārayanto 'ñjalīnmūrdhni tuṣṭuvurvividhaiḥ stavaiḥ  || 7.1,23.7
devā ūcuḥ
namaḥ śivāya śāntāya yajñahantre triśūline  || 7.1,23. 8
rudrabhadrāya rudrāṇāṃ pataye rudrabhūtaye  || 7.1,23.8
kālāgnirudrarūpāya kālakāmāṃgahāriṇe  || 7.1,23.9
devatānāṃ śirohantre dakṣasya ca durātmanaḥ  || 7.1,23.9
saṃsargādasya pāpasya dakṣasyākliṣṭakarmaṇaḥ  || 7.1,23.10
śāsitāḥ samare vīra tvayā vayamaninditā  || 7.1,23.10
dagdhāścāmī vayaṃ sarve tvatto bhītāśca bho prabho  || 7.1,23.11
tvameva gatirasmākaṃ trāhi naśśaraṇāgatān  || 7.1,23.11
vāyuruvāca
tuṣṭastvevaṃ stuto devān visṛjya nigaḍātprabhuḥ  || 7.1,23.12
ānayaddevadevasya samīpamamarāniha  || 7.1,23.12
devopi tatra bhagavānantarikṣe sthitaḥ prabhuḥ  || 7.1,23.13
sagaṇaḥ sarvagaḥ śarvassarvalokamaheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,23.13
taṃ dṛṣṭvā parameśānaṃ devā viṣṇupurogamāḥ  || 7.1,23.14
prītā api ca bhītāśca namaścakrurmaheśvaram  || 7.1,23.14
dṛṣṭvā tānamarānbhītānpraṇatārtiharo haraḥ  || 7.1,23.15
idamāha mahādevaḥ prahasan prekṣya pārvatīm  || 7.1,23.15
mahādeva uvāca
mābhaiṣṭa tridaśāssarve yūyaṃ vai māmikāḥ prajāḥ  || 7.1,23.16
anugrahārthameveha dhṛto daṃḍaḥ kṛpālunā  || 7.1,23.16
bhavatāṃ nirjarāṇāṃ hi kṣānto 'smābhirvyatikramaḥ  || 7.1,23.17
kruddheṣvasmāsu yuṣmākaṃ na sthitirna ca jīvitam  || 7.1,23.17
vāyuruvāca
ityuktāstridaśāssarve śarveṇāmitatejasā  || 7.1,23.18
sadyo vigatasandehā nanṛturvibudhā mudā  || 7.1,23.18
prasannamanaso bhūtvānandavihvalamānasāḥ  || 7.1,23.19
stutimārebhire kartuṃ śaṃkarasya divaukasaḥ  || 7.1,23.19
devā ūcuḥ
tvameva devākhilalokakartā pātā ca hartā parameśvaro 'si  || 7.1,23.20
kaviṣṇurudrākhyasvarūpabhedai rajastamassattvadhṛtātmamūrte  || 7.1,23.20
sarvamūrte namaste 'stu viśvabhāvana pāvana  || 7.1,23.21
amūrte bhaktahetorhi gṛhītākṛtisaukhyada  || 7.1,23.21
caṃdro 'gado hi deveśa kṛpātastava śaṃkara  || 7.1,23.22
nimajjanānmṛtaḥ prāpa sukhaṃ mihirajājaliḥ  || 7.1,23.22
sīmantinī hatadhavā tava pūjanataḥ prabho  || 7.1,23.23
saubhāgyamatulaṃ prāpa somavāravratātsutān  || 7.1,23.23
śrīkarāya dadau devaḥ svīyaṃ padamanuttamam  || 7.1,23.24
sudarśanamarakṣastvaṃ nṛpamaṃḍalabhītitaḥ  || 7.1,23.24
meduraṃ tārayāmāsa sadāraṃ ca ghṛṇānidhiḥ  || 7.1,23.25
śāradāṃ vidhavāṃ cakre sadhavāṃ kriyayā bhavān  || 7.1,23.25
bhadrāyuṣo vipattiṃ ca vicchidya tvamadāḥ sukham  || 7.1,23.26
527

sauminī bhavabandhādvai muktā 'bhūttava sevanāt || 7.1,23.26
viṣṇuruvāca
tvaṃ śaṃbho kaharīśāśca rajassattvatamoguṇaiḥ  || 7.1,23.27
kartā pātā tathā hartā janānugrahakāṃkṣayā  || 7.1,23.27
sarvagarvāpahārī ca sarvatejovilāsakaḥ  || 7.1,23.28
sarvavidyādigūḍhaśca sarvānugrahakārakaḥ  || 7.1,23.28
tvattaḥ sarvaṃ ca tvaṃ sarvaṃ tvayi sarvaṃ girīśvara  || 7.1,23.29
trāhi trāhi punastrāhi kṛpāṃ kuru mamopari  || 7.1,23.29
athāsminnantare brahmā praṇipatya kṛtāṃjaliḥ  || 7.1,23.30
evaṃ tvavasaraṃ prāpya vyajñāpayata śūline  || 7.1,23.30
brahmovāca
jaya deva mahādeva praṇatārtivibhaṃjana  || 7.1,23.31
īdṛśeṣvaparādheṣu ko 'nyastvattaḥ prasīdati  || 7.1,23.31
labdhamāno bhaviṣyaṃti ye purā nihitā mṛdhe  || 7.1,23.32
pratyāpattirna kasya syātprasanne parameśvare  || 7.1,23.32
yadidaṃ devadevānāṃ kṛtamantuṣu dūṣaṇam  || 7.1,23.33
tadidaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ manyeta aṃgīkāragauravāt || 7.1,23.33
iti vijñāpyamānastu brahmaṇā parameṣṭhinā  || 7.1,23.34
vilokya vadanaṃ devyā devadevassmayanniva  || 7.1,23.34
putrabhūtasya vātsalyādbrahmaṇaḥ padmajanmanaḥ  || 7.1,23.35
devādīnāṃ yathāpūrvamaṃgāni pradadau prabhuḥ  || 7.1,23.35
prathamādyaiśca yā devyo daṃḍitā devamātaraḥ  || 7.1,23.36
tāsāmapi yathāpūrvāṇyaṃgāni giriśo dadau  || 7.1,23.36
dakṣasya bhagavāneva svayaṃ brahmā pitāmahaḥ  || 7.1,23.37
tatpāpānuguṇaṃ cakre jaracchāgamukhaṃ mukham  || 7.1,23.37
so 'pi saṃjñāṃ tato labdhvā sa dṛṣṭvā jīvitaḥ sudhī  || 7.1,23.38
bhītaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ śaṃbhuṃ tuṣṭāva pralapanbahu  || 7.1,23.38
dakṣa uvāca
jaya deva jagannātha lokānugrahakāraka  || 7.1,23.39
kṛpāṃ kuru maheśānāparādhaṃ me kṣamasva ha  || 7.1,23.39
kartā bhartā ca hartā ca tvameva jagatāṃ prabho  || 7.1,23.40
mayā jñātaṃ viśeṣeṇa viṣṇvādisakaleśvaraḥ  || 7.1,23.40
tvayaiva vitataṃ sarvaṃ vyāptaṃ sṛṣṭaṃ na nāśitam  || 7.1,23.41
na hi tvadadhikāḥ kecidīśāste 'cyutakādayaḥ  || 7.1,23.41
vāyuruvāca
taṃ tathā vyākulaṃ bhītaṃ pralapaṃtaṃ kṛtāgasam  || 7.1,23.42
smayannivāvadatprekṣya mā bhairiti 1 ghṛṇānidhiḥ  || 7.1,23.42
tathoktvā brahmaṇastasya pituḥ priyacikīrṣayā  || 7.1,23.43
gāṇapatyaṃ dadau tasmai dakṣāyākṣayamīśvaraḥ  || 7.1,23.43
tato brahmādayo devā abhivaṃdya kṛta 2 ṃjaliḥ  || 7.1,23.44
tuṣṭuvuḥ praśrayā vācā śaṃkaraṃ girijādhipam  || 7.1,23.44
brahmādaya ūcuḥ
jaya śaṃkara deveśa dīnānātha mahāprabho  || 7.1,23.45

1 sijlopa ārṣaḥ
2 ekavacanamārṣam

528

kṛpāṃ kuru maheśānāparādhaṃ no kṣamasva vai  || 7.1,23.45
makhapāla makhādhīśa makhavidhvaṃsakāraka  || 7.1,23.46
kṛpāṃ kuru maśānāparādhaṃ naḥ kṣamasva vai  || 7.1,23.46
devadeva pareśāna bhaktaprāṇaprapoṣaka  || 7.1,23.47
duṣṭadaṇḍaprada svāminkṛpāṃ kuru namo 'stu te  || 7.1,23.47
tvaṃ prabho garvahartā vai duṣṭānāṃ tvāmajānatām  || 7.1,23.48
rakṣako hi viśeṣeṇa satāṃ tvatsaktacetasām  || 7.1,23.48
adbhutaṃ caritaṃ te hi niścitaṃ kṛpayā tava  || 7.1,23.49
sarvāparādhaḥ kṣaṃtavyo vibhavo dīnavatsalāḥ  || 7.1,23.49
vāyuruvāca
iti stuto mahādevo brahmādyairamaraiḥ prabhuḥ  || 7.1,23.50
sa bhaktavatsalassvāmī tutoṣa karuṇodadhiḥ  || 7.1,23.50
cakārānugrahaṃ teṣāṃ brahmādīnāṃ divaukasām  || 7.1,23.51
dadau narāṃśca suprītyā śaṃkaro dīnavatsalaḥ  || 7.1,23.51
sa ca tatastridaśāñcharaṇāgatān paramakāruṇikaḥ parameśvaraḥ  || 7.1,23.52
anugatasmitalakṣaṇayā girā śamitasarvabhayaḥ samabhāṣata  || 7.1,23.52
śiva uvāca
yadidamāga ihācaritaṃ surairvidhiniyogavaśādiva yantritaiḥ  || 7.1,23.53
śaraṇameva gatānavalokya vastadakhilaṃ kila vismṛtameva naḥ  || 7.1,23.53
tadiha yūyamapi prakṛtaṃ manasyavigaṇayya vimardamapatrapāḥ  || 7.1,23.54
hariviriṃcisurendramukhāssukhaṃ vrajata devapuraṃ prati saṃprati  || 7.1,23.54
iti surānabhidhāya sureśvaro nikṛtadakṣakṛtakraturakratuḥ  || 7.1,23.55
sagirijānucarassaparicchadaḥ sthita ivāmbaratontaradhāddharaḥ  || 7.1,23.55
atha surā api te vigatavyathāḥ kathitabhadrasubhadraparākramāḥ  || 7.1,23.56
sapadi khena sukhena yathāsukhaṃ yayuranekamukhāḥ maghavanmukhāḥ  || 7.1,23.56

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe giriśānunayo nāma trayoviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 24
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
antardhānagato devyā saha sānucaro haraḥ  || 7.1,24.1
kva yātaḥ kutra vāsaḥ kiṃ kṛtvā virarāma ha  || 7.1,24.1
vāyuruvāca
mahīdharavaraḥ śrīmānmaṃdaraścitrakaṃdaraḥ  || 7.1,24.2
dayito devadevasya nivāsastapaso 'bhavat || 7.1,24.2
tapo mahatkṛtaṃ tena voḍhuṃ svaśirasā śivau  || 7.1,24.3
cireṇa labdhaṃ tatpādapaṃkajasparśajaṃ sukham  || 7.1,24.3
tasya śailasya saundaryaṃ sahasravadanairapi  || 7.1,24.4
na śakyaṃ vistarādvaktuṃ varṣakoṭiśatairapi  || 7.1,24.4
śakyamapyasya saundaryaṃ na varṇayitumutsahe  || 7.1,24.5
parvatāntarasaundaryaṃ sādhāraṇavidhāraṇāt || 7.1,24.5
idantu śakyate vaktumasminparvatasundare  || 7.1,24.6
ṛddhyā kayāpi saundaryamīśvarāvāsayogyatā  || 7.1,24.6
ata eva hi devena devyāḥ priyacikīrṣayā  || 7.1,24.7
atīva ramaṇīyoyaṃ girirantaḥpurīkṛtaḥ  || 7.1,24.7
528

mekhalābhūmayastasya vimalopalapādapāḥ  || 7.1,24.8
śivayornityasānnidhyānnyakkurvaṃtyakhilaṃjagat || 7.1,24.8
pitṛbhyāṃ jagato nityaṃ snānapānopayogataḥ  || 7.1,24.9
avāptapuṇyasaṃskāraḥ prasaradbhiritastataḥ  || 7.1,24.9
laghuśītalasaṃsparśairacchācchairnirjharāmbubhiḥ  || 7.1,24.10
adhirājyena cādrīṇāmadrīreṣo 'bhiṣicyate  || 7.1,24.10
niśāsu śikharaprāntarvartinā sa śiloccayaḥ  || 7.1,24.11
caṃdreṇācala sāmrājyacchatreṇeva virājate  || 7.1,24.11
sa śailaścaṃcalībhūtairbālaiścāmarayoṣitām  || 7.1,24.12
sarvaparvatasāmrājyacāmarairiva vījyate  || 7.1,24.12
prātarabhyudite bhānau bhūdharo ratnabhūṣitaḥ  || 7.1,24.13
darpaṇe dehasaubhāgyaṃ draṣṭukāma iva sthitaḥ  || 7.1,24.13
kūjadvihaṃgavācālairvātoddhṛtalatābhujaiḥ  || 7.1,24.14
vimuktapuṣpaiḥ satataṃ vyālambimṛdupallavaiḥ  || 7.1,24.14
latāpratānajaṭilaistarubhistapasairiva  || 7.1,24.15
jayāśiṣā sahābhyarcya niṣevyata ivādrirāṭ || 7.1,24.15
adhomukhairūrdhvamukhaiśśṛṃgaistiryaṅmukhaistathā  || 7.1,24.16
prapatanniva pātāle bhūpṛṣṭhādutpatanniva  || 7.1,24.16
parītaḥ sarvato dikṣu bhramanniva vihāyasi  || 7.1,24.17
paśyanniva jagatsarvaṃ nṛtyanniva nirantaram  || 7.1,24.17
guhāmukhaiḥ pratidinaṃ vyāttāsyo vipulodaraiḥ  || 7.1,24.18
ajīrṇalāvaṇyatayā jṛṃbhamāṇa ivācalaḥ  || 7.1,24.18
grasanniva jagatsarvaṃ pibanniva payonidhim  || 7.1,24.19
vamanniva tamontasthaṃ mādyanniva khamambudaiḥ  || 7.1,24.19
nivāsa bhūmayastāstā darpaṇapratimodarāḥ  || 7.1,24.20
tiraskṛtātapāssnigdhāśramacchāyāmahīruhāḥ  || 7.1,24.20
saritsarastaḍāgādisaṃparkaśiśirānilāḥ  || 7.1,24.21
tatra tatra niṣaṇṇābhyāṃ śivābhyāṃ saphalīkṛtāḥ  || 7.1,24.21
tamimaṃ sarvataḥ śreṣṭhaṃ smṛtvā sāmbastriyambakaḥ  || 7.1,24.22
raibhyāśramasamīpasthaścāntardhānaṃ gato yayau  || 7.1,24.22
tatrodyānamanuprāpya devyā saha maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,24.23
rarāma ramaṇīyāsu devyāntaḥpurabhūmiṣu  || 7.1,24.23
tathā gateṣu kāleṣu pravṛddhāsu prajāsu ca  || 7.1,24.24
daityau śuṃbhaniśuṃbhākhyau bhrātarau saṃbabhūvatuḥ  || 7.1,24.24
tābhyāṃ tapo balāddattaṃ brahmaṇā parameṣṭinā  || 7.1,24.25
avadhyatvaṃ jagatyasminpuruṣairakhilairapi  || 7.1,24.25
ayonijā tu yā kanyā hyaṃbikāṃśasamudbhavā  || 7.1,24.26
ajātapuṃsparśaratiravilaṃghyaparākramā  || 7.1,24.26
tayā tu nau vadhaḥ saṃkhye tasyāṃ kāmābhibhūtayoḥ  || 7.1,24.27
iti cābhyarthito brahmā tābhyāmprāha tathāstviti  || 7.1,24.27
tataḥ prabhṛti śakrādīnvijitya samare surān  || 7.1,24.28
niḥsvādhyāyavaṣaṭkāraṃ jagaccakraturakramāt || 7.1,24.28
tayorvadhāya deveśaṃ brahmābhyarthitavānpunaḥ  || 7.1,24.29
viniṃdyāpi rahasyaṃ vāṃ krodhayitvā yathā tathā  || 7.1,24.29
529

tadvarṇakośajāṃ śaktimakāmāṃ kanyakātmikām  || 7.1,24.30
niśumbhaśuṃbhayorhaṃtrīṃ surebhyo dātumarhasi  || 7.1,24.30
evamabhyarthito dhātrā bhagavānnīlalohitaḥ  || 7.1,24.31
kālītyāha rahasyaṃ vāṃ nindayanniva sasmitaḥ  || 7.1,24.31
tataḥ kruddhā tadā devī suvarṇā varṇakāraṇāt || 7.1,24.32
smayantī cāha bhartāramasamādheyayā girā  || 7.1,24.32
devyuvāca
īdṛśo mama varṇesminna ratirbhavato 'sti cet || 7.1,24.33
evāvantaṃ ciraṃ kālaṃ kathameṣā niyamyate  || 7.1,24.33
aratyā vartamāno 'pi kathaṃ ca ramase mayā  || 7.1,24.34
na hyaśakyaṃ jagatyasminnīśvarasya jagatprabhoḥ  || 7.1,24.34
svātmārāmasya bhavato ratirna sukhasādhanam  || 7.1,24.35
iti hetoḥ smaro yasmātprasabhaṃ bhasmasātkṛtaḥ  || 7.1,24.35
yā ca nābhimatā bharturapi sarvāṃgasundarī  || 7.1,24.36
sā vṛthaiva hi jāyeta sarvairapi guṇāntaraiḥ  || 7.1,24.36
bharturbhogaikaśeṣo hi sarga evaiṣa yoṣitām  || 7.1,24.37
tathāsatyanyathābhūtā nārī kutropayujyate  || 7.1,24.37
tasmādvarṇamimaṃ tyaktvā tvayā rahasi ninditam  || 7.1,24.38
varṇāntaraṃ bhajiṣye vā na bhajiṣyāmi vā svayam  || 7.1,24.38
ityuktvotthāya śayanāddevī sācaṣṭa gadgadam  || 7.1,24.39
yayāce 'numatiṃ bhartustapase kṛtaniścayā  || 7.1,24.39
tathā praṇayabhaṃgena bhīto bhūtapatiḥ svayam  || 7.1,24.40
pādayoḥ praṇamanneva bhavānīṃ pratyabhāṣata  || 7.1,24.40
īśvara uvāca
ajānatī ca krīḍoktiṃ priye kiṃ kupitāsi me  || 7.1,24.41
ratiḥ kuto vā jāyeta tvattaścedaratirmama  || 7.1,24.41
mātā tvamasya jagataḥ pitāhamadhipastathā  || 7.1,24.42
kathaṃ tadutpapadyeta tvatto nābhiratirmama  || 7.1,24.42
āvayorabhikāmo 'pi kimasau kāmakāritaḥ  || 7.1,24.43
yataḥ kāmasamutpattiḥ prāgeva jagadudbhavaḥ  || 7.1,24.43
pṛthagjanānāṃ rataye kāmātmā kalpito mayā  || 7.1,24.44
tataḥ kathamupālabdhaḥ kāmadāhādahaṃ tvayā  || 7.1,24.44
māṃ vai tridaśasāmānyaṃ manyamāno manobhavaḥ  || 7.1,24.45
manākparibhavaṃ kurvanmayā vai bhasmasātkṛtaḥ  || 7.1,24.45
vihāropyāvayorasya jagatastrāṇakāraṇāt || 7.1,24.46
tatastadarthaṃ tvayyadya krīḍoktiṃ kṛtavāhanam  || 7.1,24.46
sa cāyamacirādarthastavaivāviṣkariṣyate  || 7.1,24.47
krodhasya janakaṃ vākyaṃ hṛdi kṛtvedamabravīt || 7.1,24.47
devyuvāca
śrutapūrvaṃ hi bhagavaṃstava cāṭu vaco mayā  || 7.1,24.48
yenaivamatidhīrāhamapi prāgabhivaṃcitā  || 7.1,24.48
prāṇānapyapriyā bharturnārī yā na parityajet || 7.1,24.49
kulāṃganā śubhā sadbhiḥ kutsitaiva hi gamyate  || 7.1,24.49
bhūyasī ca tavāprītiragauramiti me vapuḥ  || 7.1,24.50
krīḍoktirapi kālīti ghaṭate kathamanyathā  || 7.1,24.50
sadbhirvigarhitaṃ tasmāttava kārṣṇyamasaṃmatam  || 7.1,24.51
529

anutsṛjya tapoyogātsthātumeveha notsahe  || 7.1,24.51
śiva uvāca
sa yadyevaṃvidhatāpaste tapasā kiṃ prayojanam  || 7.1,24.52
mamecchayā svecchayā vā varṇāntaravatī bhava  || 7.1,24.52
devyuvāca
necchāmi bhavato varṇaṃ svayaṃ vā kartumanyathā  || 7.1,24.53
brahmāṇaṃ tapasārādhya kṣipraṃ gaurī bhavāmyaham  || 7.1,24.53
īśvara uvāca
matprasādātpurā brahmā brahmatvaṃ prāptavānpurā  || 7.1,24.54
tamāhūya mahādevi tapasā kiṃ kariṣyasi  || 7.1,24.54
devyuvāca
tvatto labdhapadā eva sarve brahmādayaḥ surāḥ  || 7.1,24.55
tathāpyārādhya tapasā brahmāṇaṃ tvanniyogataḥ  || 7.1,24.55
purā kila satī nāmnā dakṣasya duhitā 'bhavam  || 7.1,24.56
jagatāṃ patimevaṃ tvāṃ patiṃ prāptavatī tathā  || 7.1,24.56
evamadyāpi tapasā toṣayitvā dvijaṃ vidhim  || 7.1,24.57
gaurī bhavitumicchāmi ko doṣaḥ kathyatāmiha  || 7.1,24.57
evamukto mahādevyā vāmadevaḥ smayanniva  || 7.1,24.58
na tāṃ nirbaṃdhayāmāsa devakāryacikīrṣayā  || 7.1,24.58
iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe śivamandaragirinivāsakrīḍoktavarṇanaṃ nāma caturviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 25
vāyuruvāca
tataḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya patimambā pativratā  || 7.1,25.1
niyamya ca viyogārtiṃ jagāma himavadgirim  || 7.1,25.1
tapaḥkṛtavatī pūrvaṃ deśe yasminsakhījanaiḥ  || 7.1,25.2
tameva deśamavṛnottapase praṇayātpunaḥ  || 7.1,25.2
tataḥ svapitaraṃ dṛṣṭvā mātaraṃ ca tayorgṛhe  || 7.1,25.3
praṇamya vṛttaṃ vijñāpya tābhyāṃ cānumatā satī  || 7.1,25.3
punastapovanaṃ gatvā bhūṣaṇāni visṛjya ca  || 7.1,25.4
snātvā tapasvino veṣaṃ kṛtvā paramapāvanam  || 7.1,25.4
saṃkalpya ca mahātīvraṃ tapaḥ paramaduścaram  || 7.1,25.5
sadā manasi sandhāya bhartuścaraṇapaṃkajam  || 7.1,25.5
tameva kṣaṇike liṃge dhyātvā bāhyavidhānataḥ  || 7.1,25.6
trisandhyamabhyarcayantī vanyaiḥ puṣpaiḥ phalādibhiḥ  || 7.1,25.6
sa eva brahmaṇo mūrtimāsthāya tapasaḥ phalam  || 7.1,25.7
pradāsyati mametyevaṃ nityaṃ kṛtvā 'karottapaḥ  || 7.1,25.7
tathā tapaścarantīṃ tāṃ kāle bahutithe gate  || 7.1,25.8
dṛṣṭaḥ kaścinmahāvyāghro duṣṭabhāvādupāgamat || 7.1,25.8
tathaivopagatasyāpi tasyātīvadurātmanaḥ  || 7.1,25.9
gātraṃ citrārpitamiva stabdhaṃ tasyāssakāśataḥ  || 7.1,25.9
taṃ dṛṣṭvāpi tathā vyāghraṃ duṣṭabhāvādupāgatam  || 7.1,25.10
na pṛthagjanavaddevī svabhāvena vivicyate  || 7.1,25.10
sa tu viṣṭabdhasarvāṃgo bubhukṣāparipīḍitaḥ  || 7.1,25.11
mamāmiṣaṃ tato nānyaditi matvā nirantaram  || 7.1,25.11
nirīkṣyamāṇaḥ satataṃ devīmeva tadā 'niśam  || 7.1,25.12
atiṣṭhadagratastasyā upāsanamivācarat || 7.1,25.12
530

devyāśca hṛdaye nityaṃ mamaivāyamupāsakaḥ  || 7.1,25.13
trātā ca duṣṭasattvebhya iti pravavṛte kṛpā  || 7.1,25.13
tasyā eva kṛpā yogātsadyonaṣṭamalatrayaḥ  || 7.1,25.14
babhūva sahasā vyāghro devīṃ ca bubudhe tadā  || 7.1,25.14
nyavartata bubhukṣā ca tasyāṃgastambhanaṃ tathā  || 7.1,25.15
daurātmyaṃ janmasiddhaṃ ca tṛptiśca samajāyata  || 7.1,25.15
tadā paramabhāvena jñātvā kārtārthyamātmanaḥ  || 7.1,25.16
sadyopāsaka evaiṣa siṣeve parameśvarīm  || 7.1,25.16
duṣṭānāmapi sattvānāṃ tathānyeṣāndurātmanām  || 7.1,25.17
sa eva drāvako bhūtvā vicacāra tapovane  || 7.1,25.17
tapaśca vavṛdhe devyāstīvraṃ tīvratarātmakam  || 7.1,25.18
devāśca daityanirbandhādbrahmāṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  || 7.1,25.18
cakrurnivedanaṃ devāḥ svaduḥkhasyāripīḍanāt || 7.1,25.19
yathā ca dadatuḥ śumbhaniśumbhau varasammadāt || 7.1,25.19
so 'pi śrutvā vidhirduḥkhaṃ surāṇāṃ kṛpayānvitaḥ  || 7.1,25.20
āsīddaityavadhāyaiva smṛtvā hetvāśrayāṃ kathām  || 7.1,25.20
sāmaraḥ prārthito brahmā yayau devyāstapovanam  || 7.1,25.21
saṃsmaranmanasā devaduḥkhamokṣaṃ svayatnataḥ  || 7.1,25.21
dadarśa ca suraśreṣṭhaḥ śreṣṭhe tapasi niṣṭhitām  || 7.1,25.22
pratiṣṭhāmiva viśvasya bhavānīṃ parameśvarīm  || 7.1,25.22
nanāma cāsya jagato mātaraṃ svasya vai hareḥ  || 7.1,25.23
rudrasya ca piturbhāryāmāryāmadrīśvarātmajām  || 7.1,25.23
brahmāṇamāgataṃ dṛṣṭvā devī devagaṇaiḥ saha  || 7.1,25.24
arghyaṃ tadarhaṃ dattvā 'smai svāgatādyairupācarat || 7.1,25.24
tāṃ ca pratyupacāroktiṃ puraskṛtyābhinaṃdya ca  || 7.1,25.25
papraccha tapaso hetumajānanniva padmajaḥ  || 7.1,25.25
brahmovāca
tīvreṇa tapasānena devyā kimiha sādhyate  || 7.1,25.26
tapaḥphalānāṃ sarveṣāṃ tvadadhīnā hi siddhayaḥ  || 7.1,25.26
yaścaiva jagatāṃ bhartā tameva parameśvaram  || 7.1,25.27
bhartāramātmanā prāpya prāptañca tapasaḥ phalam  || 7.1,25.27
athavā sarvamevaitatkrīḍāvilasitaṃ tava  || 7.1,25.28
idantu citraṃ devasya virahaṃ sahase katham  || 7.1,25.28
devyuvāca
sargādau bhavato devādutpattiḥ śrūyate yadā  || 7.1,25.29
tadā prajānāṃ prathamastvaṃ me prathamajaḥ sutaḥ  || 7.1,25.29
yadā punaḥ prajāvṛddhyai lalāṭādbhavato bhavaḥ  || 7.1,25.30
utpanno 'bhūttadā tvaṃ me guruḥ śvaśurabhāvataḥ  || 7.1,25.30
yadā bhavadgirīndraste putro mama pitā svayam  || 7.1,25.31
tadā pitāmahastvaṃ me jāto lokapitāmaha  || 7.1,25.31
tadīdṛśasya bhavato lokayātrāvidhāyinaḥ  || 7.1,25.32
vṛttavantaḥpure bhartā kathayiṣye kathaṃ punaḥ  || 7.1,25.32
kimatra bahunā dehe yaścāyaṃ mama kālimā  || 7.1,25.33
tyaktvā sattvavidhānena gaurī bhavitumutsahe  || 7.1,25.33
530

brahmovāca
etāvatā kimarthena tīvraṃ devi tapaḥ kṛtam  || 7.1,25.34
svecchaiva kimaparyāptā krīḍeyaṃ hi tavedṛśī  || 7.1,25.34
krīḍā 'pi ca jaganmātastava lokahitāya vai  || 7.1,25.35
ato mameṣṭamanayā phalaṃ kimapi sādhyatām  || 7.1,25.35
niśuṃbhaśuṃbhanāmānau daityau dattavarau mayā  || 7.1,25.36
dṛptau devānprabādhete tvatto labdhastayorvadhaḥ  || 7.1,25.36
alaṃ vilaṃbanenātra tvaṃ kṣaṇena sthirā bhava  || 7.1,25.37
śaktirvisṛjyamānā 'dya tayormṛtyurbhaviṣyati  || 7.1,25.37
brāhmaṇābhyarthitā caiva devī girivarātmajā  || 7.1,25.38
tvakkośaṃ sahasotsṛjya gaurī sā samajāyata  || 7.1,25.38
sā tvakkośātmanotsṛṣṭā kauśikī nāma nāmataḥ  || 7.1,25.39
kālī kālāmbudaprakhyā kanyakā samapadyata  || 7.1,25.39
sā tu māyātmikā śaktiryoganidrā ca vaiṣṇavī  || 7.1,25.40
śaṃkhacakratriśūlādisāyudhāṣṭamahābhujā  || 7.1,25.40
saumyā ghorā ca miśrā ca trinetrā candraśekharā  || 7.1,25.41
ajātapuṃsparśaratiradhṛṣyā cātisundarī  || 7.1,25.41
dattā ca brahmaṇe devyā śaktireṣā sanātanī  || 7.1,25.42
niśuṃbhasya ca śuṃbhasya nihaṃtrī daityasiṃhayoḥ  || 7.1,25.42
brahmaṇāpi prahṛṣṭena tasyai paramaśaktaye  || 7.1,25.43
prabalaḥ kesarī datto vāhanatve samāgataḥ  || 7.1,25.43
vindhye ca vasatiṃ tasyāḥ pūjāmāsavapūrvakaiḥ  || 7.1,25.44
māṃsairmatsyairapūpaiśca nirvartyāsau samādiśat || 7.1,25.44
sā caiva saṃmatā śaktirbrahmaṇo viśvakarmaṇaḥ  || 7.1,25.45
praṇamya mātaraṃ gaurīṃ brahmāṇaṃ cānupūrvaśaḥ  || 7.1,25.45
śaktibhiścāpi tulyābhiḥ svātmajābhiranekaśaḥ  || 7.1,25.46
parītā prayayau vindhyaṃ daityendrau hantumudyatā  || 7.1,25.46
nihatau ca tayā tatra samare daityapuṃgavau  || 7.1,25.47
tadbāṇaiḥ kāmabāṇaiśca cchinnabhinnāṃgamānasau  || 7.1,25.47
tadyuddhavistaraścātra na kṛto 'nyatra varṇanāt || 7.1,25.48
ūhanīyaṃ parasmācca prastutaṃ varṇayāmi vaḥ  || 7.1,25.48

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe devīgauratvabhavanaṃ nāma pañcaviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 26
vāyuruvāca
utpādya kauśikīṃ gaurī brahmaṇe pratipādya tām  || 7.1,26.1
tasya pratyupakārāya pitāmahamathābravīt || 7.1,26.1
devyuvāca
dṛṣṭaḥ kimeṣa bhavatā śārdūlo madupāśrayaḥ  || 7.1,26.2
anena duṣṭasattvebhyo rakṣitaṃ mattapovanam  || 7.1,26.2
mayyarpitamanā eṣa bhajate māmananyadhīḥ  || 7.1,26.3
asya saṃrakṣaṇādanyatpriyaṃ mama na vidyate  || 7.1,26.3
bhavitavyamanenāto mamāntaḥpuracāriṇā  || 7.1,26.4
gaṇeśvarapadaṃ cāsmai prītyā dāsyati śaṃkaraḥ  || 7.1,26.4
enamagresaraṃ kṛtvā sakhībhirgantumutsahe  || 7.1,26.5
pradīyatāmanujñā me prajānāṃ patinā 1 tvayā  || 7.1,26.5

1 dhitvamārṣam

531

ityuktaḥ prahasanbrahmā devīmmugdhāmiva smayan  || 7.1,26.6
tasya tīvraiḥ purāvṛttairdaurātmyaṃ samavarṇayat1  || 7.1,26.6
brahmovāca
paśau devi mṛgāḥ krūrāḥ kva ca te 'nugrahaḥ śubhaḥ  || 7.1,26.7
āśīviṣamukhe sākṣādamṛtaṃ kiṃ niṣicyate  || 7.1,26.7
vyāghramātreṇa sanneṣa duṣṭaḥ ko 'pi niśācaraḥ  || 7.1,26.8
anena bhakṣitā gāvo brāhmaṇāśca tapodhanāḥ  || 7.1,26.8
tarpayaṃstānyathākāmaṃ kāmarūpī caratyasau  || 7.1,26.9
avaśyaṃ khalu bhoktavyaṃ phalaṃ pāpasya karmaṇaḥ  || 7.1,26.9
ataḥ kiṃ kṛpayā kṛtyamīdṛśeṣu durātmasu  || 7.1,26.10
anena devyāḥ kiṃ kṛtyaṃ prakṛtyā kaluṣātmanā  || 7.1,26.10
devyuvāca
yaduktaṃ bhavatā sarvaṃ tathyamastvayamīdṛśaḥ  || 7.1,26.11
tathāpi māṃ prapanno 'bhūnna tyājyo māmupāśritaḥ  || 7.1,26.11
brahmovāca
asya bhaktimavijñāya prāgvṛttaṃ te niveditam  || 7.1,26.12
bhaktiścedasya kiṃ pāpairna te bhaktaḥ praṇaśyati  || 7.1,26.12
puṇyakarmāpi kiṃ kuryāttvadīyājñānapekṣayā  || 7.1,26.13
ajā prajñā purāṇī ca tvameva parameśvarī  || 7.1,26.13
tvadadhīnā hi sarveṣāṃ baṃdhamokṣavyavasthitiḥ  || 7.1,26.14
tvadṛte paramā śaktiḥ saṃsiddhiḥ kasya karmaṇā  || 7.1,26.14
tvameva vividhā śaktiḥ bhavānāmatha vā svayam  || 7.1,26.15
aśaktaḥ karmakaraṇe kartā vā kiṃ kariṣyati  || 7.1,26.15
viṣṇośca mama cānyeṣāṃ devadānavarakṣasām  || 7.1,26.16
tattadaiśvaryasamprāptyai tavaivājñā hi kāraṇam  || 7.1,26.16
atītāḥ khalvasaṃkhyātā brahmāṇo harayo bhavāḥ  || 7.1,26.17
anāgatāstvasaṃkhyātāstvadājñānuvidhāyinaḥ  || 7.1,26.17
tvāmanārādhya deveśi puruṣārthacatuṣṭayam  || 7.1,26.18
labdhuṃ na śakyamasmābhirapi sarvaiḥ surottamaiḥ  || 7.1,26.18
vyatyāso 'pi bhavetsadyo brahmatvasthāvaratvayoḥ  || 7.1,26.19
sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ cāpi tvayeva sthāpitaṃ yataḥ  || 7.1,26.19
tvaṃ hi sarvajagadbhartuśśivasya paramātmanaḥ  || 7.1,26.20
anādimadhyanidhanā śaktirādyā sanātanī  || 7.1,26.20
samastalokayātrārthaṃ mūrtimāviśya kāmapi  || 7.1,26.21
krīḍase 2 vividhairbhāvaiḥ kastvāṃ jānāti tattvataḥ  || 7.1,26.21
ato duṣkṛtakarmāpi vyāghro 'yaṃ tvadanugrahāt || 7.1,26.22
prāpnotu paramāṃ siddhimatra kaḥ pratibandhakaḥ  || 7.1,26.22
ityātmanaḥ paraṃ bhāvaṃ smārayitvānurūpataḥ  || 7.1,26.23
brahmaṇābhyarthitā gaurī tapaso 'pi nyavartata  || 7.1,26.23
tato devīmanujñāpya brahmaṇyantarhite sati  || 7.1,26.24
devīṃ ca mātaraṃ dṛṣṭvā menāṃ himavatā saha  || 7.1,26.24
praṇamyāśvāsya bahudhā pitarau virahāsahau  || 7.1,26.25

1 parasmaipadamārṣam
2 ātmanepadamārṣam

531

tapaḥ praṇayino devī tapovanamahīruhān  || 7.1,26.25
viprayogaśucevāgre puṣpabāṣpaṃ vimuṃcataḥ  || 7.1,26.26
tattucchākhāsamārūḍhavihago dīritai rutaiḥ  || 7.1,26.26
vyākulaṃ bahudhā dīnaṃ vilāpamiva kurvataḥ  || 7.1,26.27
sakhībhyaḥ kathayaṃtyevaṃ sattvarā bhartṛdarśane  || 7.1,26.27
puraskṛtya ca taṃ vyāghraṃ snehātputramivaurasam  || 7.1,26.28
dehasya prabhayā caiva dīpayantī diśo daśa  || 7.1,26.28
prayayau maṃdaraṃ gaurī yatra bhartā maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,26.29
sarveṣāṃ jagatāṃ dhātā kartā pātā vināśakṛt || 7.1,26.29

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe vyāghragativarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaḍviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 27
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
kṛtvā gauraṃ vapurdivyaṃ devī girivarātmajā  || 7.1,27.1
kathaṃ dadarśa bhartāraṃ praviṣṭā manditaṃ satī  || 7.1,27.1
praveśasamaye tasyā bhavanadvāragocaraiḥ  || 7.1,27.2
gaṇeśaiḥ kiṃ kṛtaṃ devastāndṛṣṭvā kintadā 'karot || 7.1,27.2
vāyuruvāca
pravaktumaṃjasā 'śakyaḥ tādṛśaḥ paramo rasaḥ  || 7.1,27.3
yena praṇayagarbheṇa bhāvo bhāvavatāṃ hṛtaḥ  || 7.1,27.3
dvāsthaissasaṃbhramaireva devo devyāgamotsukaḥ  || 7.1,27.4
śaṃkamānā praviṣṭāntastañca sā samapaśyata  || 7.1,27.4
taistaiḥ praṇayabhāvaiśca bhavanāntaravartibhiḥ  || 7.1,27.5
gaṇendrairvanditā vācā praṇanāma triyambakam  || 7.1,27.5
praṇamya notthitā yāvattāvattāṃ parameśvaraḥ  || 7.1,27.6
pragṛhya dorbhyāmāśliṣya paritaḥ parayā mudā  || 7.1,27.6
svāṃke dhartuṃ pravṛtto 'pi sā paryaṃke nyaṣīdata  || 7.1,27.7
paryaṃkato balāddevīṃ soṅkamāropya susmitām  || 7.1,27.7
sasmito vivṛtairnetraistadvaktraṃ prapibanniva  || 7.1,27.8
tayā saṃbhāṣaṇāyeśaḥ pūrvabhāṣitamabravīt || 7.1,27.8
devadeva uvāca
sā daśā ca vyatītā kiṃ tava sarvāṃgasundari  || 7.1,27.9
yasyāmanunayopāyaḥ ko 'pi kopānna labhyate  || 7.1,27.9
svecchayāpi na kālīti nānyavarṇavatīti ca  || 7.1,27.10
tvatsvabhāvāhṛtaṃ cittaṃ subhru ciṃtāvahaṃ mama  || 7.1,27.10
vismṛtaḥ paramo bhāvaḥ kathaṃ svecchāṃgayogataḥ  || 7.1,27.11
na sambhavanti ye tatra cittakāluṣyahetavaḥ  || 7.1,27.11
pṛthagjanavadanyonyaṃ vipriyasyāpi kāraṇam  || 7.1,27.12
āvayorapi yadyasti nāstyevaitaccarācaram  || 7.1,27.12
ahamagniśironiṣṭhastvaṃ somaśirasi sthitā  || 7.1,27.13
agnīṣomātmakaṃ viśvamāvābhyāṃ samadhiṣṭhitam  || 7.1,27.13
jagaddhitāya caratoḥ svecchādhṛtaśarīrayoḥ  || 7.1,27.14
āvayorviprayoge hi syānnirālambanaṃ jagat || 7.1,27.14
asti hetvantaraṃ cātra śāstrayuktiviniścitam  || 7.1,27.15
vāgarthamiva me vaitajjagatsthāvarajaṃgamam  || 7.1,27.15
tvaṃ hi vāgamṛtaṃ sākṣādahamarthāmṛtaṃ param  || 7.1,27.16
dvayamapyamṛtaṃ kasmādviyuktamupapadyate  || 7.1,27.16
532

vidyāpratyāyikā tvaṃ me vedyo 'haṃ pratyayāttava  || 7.1,27.17
vidyāvedyātmanoreva viśleṣaḥ kathamāvayoḥ  || 7.1,27.17
na karmaṇā sṛjāmīdaṃ jagatpratisṛjāmi ca  || 7.1,27.18
sarvasyājñaikalabhyatvādājñātvaṃ hi garīyasī  || 7.1,27.18
ājñaikasāramaiśvaryaṃ yasmātsvātaṃtryalakṣaṇam  || 7.1,27.19
ājñayā viprayuktasya caiśvaryaṃ mama kīdṛśam  || 7.1,27.19
na kadācidavasthānamāvayorviprayuktayoḥ  || 7.1,27.20
devānāṃ kāryamuddiśya līloktiṃ kṛtavānaham  || 7.1,27.20
tvayāpyaviditaṃ nāsti kathaṃ kupitavatyasi  || 7.1,27.21
tatastrilokarakṣārthe kopo mayyapi te kṛtaḥ  || 7.1,27.21
yadanarthāya bhūtānāṃ na tadasti khalu tvayi  || 7.1,27.22
iti priyaṃvade sākṣādīśvare parameśvare  || 7.1,27.22
śṛṃgārabhāvasārāṇāṃ janmabhūmirakṛtrimā  || 7.1,27.23
svabhartrā lalitantathyamuktaṃ matvā smitottaram  || 7.1,27.23
lajjayā na kimapyūce kauśikī varṇanātparam  || 7.1,27.24
tadeva varṇayāmyadya śṛṇu devyāśca varṇanam  || 7.1,27.24
devyuvāca
kiṃ devena na sā dṛṣṭā yā sṛṣṭā kauśikī mayā  || 7.1,27.25
tādṛśī kanyakā loke na bhūtā na bhaviṣyati  || 7.1,27.25
tasyā vīryaṃ balaṃ vindhyanilayaṃ vijayaṃ tathā  || 7.1,27.26
śuṃbhasya ca niśuṃbhasya māraṇe ca raṇe tayoḥ  || 7.1,27.26
pratyakṣaphaladānaṃ ca lokāya bhajate sadā  || 7.1,27.27
lokānāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ śaśvadbrahmā vijñāpayiṣyati  || 7.1,27.27
iti saṃbhāṣamāṇāyā devyā evājñayā tadā  || 7.1,27.28
vyāghraḥ sakhyā samānīya puro 'vasthāpitastadā  || 7.1,27.28
taṃ prekṣyāha punardevī devānītamupāyatam  || 7.1,27.29
vyāghraṃ paśya na cānena sadṛśo madupāsakaḥ  || 7.1,27.29
anena duṣṭasaṃghebhyo rakṣitaṃ mattapovanam  || 7.1,27.30
atīva mama bhaktaśca viśrabdhaśca svarakṣaṇāt || 7.1,27.30
svadeśaṃ ca parityajya prasādārthaṃ samāgataḥ  || 7.1,27.31
yadi prītirabhūnmattaḥ parāṃ prītiṃ karoṣi me  || 7.1,27.31
nityamantaḥpuradvāri niyogānnandinaḥ svayam  || 7.1,27.32
rakṣibhissaha taccihnairvartatāmayamīśvara  || 7.1,27.32
vāyuruvāca
madhuraṃ praṇayodarkaṃ śrutvā devyāḥ śubhaṃ vacaḥ  || 7.1,27.33
prīto 'smītyāha taṃ devassa cādṛśyata tatkṣaṇāt || 7.1,27.33
bibhradvetralatāṃ haimīṃ ratnacitraṃ ca kaṃcukam  || 7.1,27.34
churikāmuragaprakhyāṃ gaṇeśo rakṣaveṣadhṛk || 7.1,27.34
yasmātsomo mahādevo nandī cānena nanditaḥ  || 7.1,27.35
somanandīti vikhyātastasmādeṣa samākhyayā  || 7.1,27.35
itthaṃ devyāḥ priyaṃ kṛtvā devaścardhendubhūṣaṇaḥ  || 7.1,27.36
bhūṣayāmāsa tandivyairbhūṣaṇai ratnabhūṣitaiḥ  || 7.1,27.36
tatassa gaurīṃ giriśo girīndrajāṃ sagauravāṃ sarvamanoharāṃ haraḥ  || 7.1,27.37
paryaṃkamāropya varāṃgabhūṣaṇairvibhūṣayāmāsa śaśāṃkabhūṣaṇaḥ  || 7.1,27.37

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe saptaviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 28
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
devīṃ samādadhānena devenedaṃ kimīritam  || 7.1,28.1
agniṣomātmakaṃ viśvaṃ vāgarthātmakamityapi  || 7.1,28.1
ājñaikasāramaiśvaryamājñā tvamiti coditam  || 7.1,28.2
tadidaṃ śrotumicchāmo yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ  || 7.1,28.2
vāyuruvāca
agnirityucyate raudrī ghorā yā taijasī tanuḥ  || 7.1,28.3
somaḥ śākto 'mṛtamayaḥ śakteḥ śāntikarī tanuḥ  || 7.1,28.3
amṛtaṃ yatpratiṣṭhā sā tejo vidyā kalā svayam  || 7.1,28.4
bhūtasūkṣmeṣu sarveṣu ta eva rasatejasī  || 7.1,28.4
dvividhā tejaso vṛttisūryātmā cānalātmikā  || 7.1,28.5
tathaiva rasavṛttiśca somātmā ca jalātmikā  || 7.1,28.5
vidyudādimayantejo madhurādimayo rasaḥ  || 7.1,28.6
tejorasavibhedaistu dhṛtametaccarācaram  || 7.1,28.6
agneramṛtaniṣpattiramṛtenāgniredhate  || 7.1,28.7
ata eva hi vikrāntamagnīṣomaṃ jagaddhitam  || 7.1,28.7
haviṣe sasyasampattirvṛṣṭiḥ sasyābhivṛddhaye  || 7.1,28.8
vṛṣṭereva havistasmādagnīṣomadhṛtaṃ jagat || 7.1,28.8
agnirūrdhvaṃ jvalatyeṣa yāvatsaumyaṃ parāmṛtam  || 7.1,28.9
yāvadagnyāspadaṃ saumyamamṛtaṃ ca sravatyadhaḥ  || 7.1,28.9
ata eva hi kālāgniradhastācchaktirūrdhvataḥ  || 7.1,28.10
yāvadādahanaṃ cordhvamadhaścāplāvanaṃ bhavet || 7.1,28.10
ādhāraśaktyaiva dhṛtaḥ kālāgnirayamūrdhvagaḥ  || 7.1,28.11
tathaiva nimnagaḥ somaśśivaśaktipadāspadaḥ  || 7.1,28.11
śivaścordhvamadhaśśaktirūrdhvaṃ śaktiradhaḥ śivaḥ  || 7.1,28.12
taditthaṃ śivaśaktibhyānnāvyāptamiha kiñcana  || 7.1,28.12
asakṛccāgninā dagdhaṃ jagadyadbhasmasātkṛtam  || 7.1,28.13
agnervīryamidaṃ cāhustadvīryaṃ bhasma yattataḥ  || 7.1,28.13
yaścetthaṃ bhasmasadbhāvaṃ jñātvā snāti ca bhasmanā  || 7.1,28.14
agnirityādibhirmantrairbaddhaḥ pāśātpramucyate  || 7.1,28.14
agnervīryaṃ tu yadbhasma somenāplāvitampunaḥ  || 7.1,28.15
ayogayuktyā prakṛteradhikārāya kalpate  || 7.1,28.15
yogayuktyā tu tadbhasma plāvyamānaṃ samantataḥ  || 7.1,28.16
śāktenāmṛtavarṣeṇa cādhikārānnivartayet || 7.1,28.16
ato mṛtyuṃjayāyetthamamṛtaplāvanaṃ sadā  || 7.1,28.17
śivaśaktyamṛtasparśe labdhaṃ yena kuto mṛtiḥ  || 7.1,28.17
yo veda dahanaṃ guhyaṃ plāvanaṃ ca yathoditam  || 7.1,28.18
agnīṣomapadaṃ hitvā na sa bhūyo 'bhijāyate  || 7.1,28.18
śivāgninā tanuṃ dagdhvā śaktisaumyā mṛtena yaḥ  || 7.1,28.19
plāvayedyogamārgeṇa so 'mṛtatvāya kalpate  || 7.1,28.19
hṛdi kṛtvemamarthaṃ vai devena samudāhṛtam  || 7.1,28.20
agnīṣomātmakaṃ viśvaṃ jagadityanurūpataḥ  || 7.1,28.20
533


iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe bhasmatattvavarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭāviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 29
vāyuruvāca
nivedayāmi jagato vāgarthātmyaṃ kṛtaṃ yathā  || 7.1,29.1
ṣaḍadhvavedanaṃ samyaksamāsānna tu vistarāt || 7.1,29.1
nāsti kaścidaśabdārtho nāpi śabdo nirarthakaḥ  || 7.1,29.2
tato hi samaye śabdassarvassarvārthabodhakaḥ  || 7.1,29.2
prakṛteḥ pariṇāmo 'yaṃ dvidhā śabdārthabhāvanā  || 7.1,29.3
tāmāhuḥ prākṛtīṃ mūrtiṃ śivayoḥ paramātmanoḥ  || 7.1,29.3
śabdātmikā vibhūtiryā sā tridhā kathyate budhaiḥ  || 7.1,29.4
sthūlā sūkṣmā parā ceti sthūlā yā śrutigocarā  || 7.1,29.4
sūkṣmā cintāmayī proktā ciṃtayā rahitā parā  || 7.1,29.5
yā śaktiḥ sā parā śaktiśśivatattvasamāśrayā  || 7.1,29.5
jñānaśaktisamāyogādicchopodbalikā tathā  || 7.1,29.6
sarvaśaktisamaṣṭyātmā śaktitattvasamākhyayā  || 7.1,29.6
samastakāryajātasya mūlaprakṛtitāṃ gatā  || 7.1,29.7
saiva kuṇḍalinī māyā śuddhādhvaparamā satī  || 7.1,29.7
sā vibhāgasvarūpaiva ṣaḍadhvātmā vijṛṃbhate  || 7.1,29.8
tatra śabdāstrayo 'dhvānastrayaścārthāḥ samīritāḥ  || 7.1,29.8
sarveṣāmapi vai puṃsāṃ naijaśuddhyanurūpataḥ  || 7.1,29.9
layabhogādhikārāssyussarvatattvavibhāgataḥ  || 7.1,29.9
kalābhistāni tattvāni vyāptānyeva yathātatham  || 7.1,29.10
parasyāḥ prakṛterādau pañcadhā pariṇāmataḥ  || 7.1,29.10
kalāśca tā nivṛttyādyāḥ paryāptā iti niścayaḥ  || 7.1,29.11
maṃtrādhvā ca padādhvā ca varṇādhvā ceti śabdataḥ  || 7.1,29.11
bhuvanādhvā ca tattvādhvā kalādhvā cārthataḥ kramāt || 7.1,29.12
atrānyonyaṃ ca sarveṣāṃ vyāpyavyāpakatocyate  || 7.1,29.12
maṃtrāḥ sarvaiḥ padairvyāptā vākyabhāvātpadāni ca  || 7.1,29.13
varṇairvarṇasamūhaṃ hi padamāhurvipaścitaḥ  || 7.1,29.13
varṇāstu bhuvanairvyāptāsteṣāṃ teṣūpalaṃbhanāt || 7.1,29.14
bhuvanānyapi tattvaughairutpattyāṃtarbahiṣkramāt || 7.1,29.14
vyāptāni kāraṇaistattvairārabdhatvādanekaśaḥ  || 7.1,29.15
aṃtarādutthitānīha bhuvanāni tu kānicit || 7.1,29.15
paurāṇikāni cānyāni vijñeyāni śivāgame  || 7.1,29.16
sāṃkhyayogaprasiddhāni tattvānyapi ca kānicit || 7.1,29.16
śivaśāstraprasiddhāni tatonyānyapi kṛtsnaśaḥ  || 7.1,29.17
kalābhistāni tattvāni vyāptānyeva yathātatham  || 7.1,29.17
parasyāḥ prakṛterādau pañcadhā pariṇāmataḥ  || 7.1,29.18
kalāśca tā nivṛttyādyā vyāptāḥ pañca yathottaram  || 7.1,29.18
vyāpikātaḥ parā śaktiravibhaktā ṣaḍadhvanām  || 7.1,29.19
paraprakṛtibhāvasya tatsattvācchivatattvataḥ  || 7.1,29.19
śaktyādi ca pṛthivyantaṃ śivatattvasamudbhavam  || 7.1,29.20
vyāptamekena tenaiva mṛdā kuṃbhādikaṃ yathā  || 7.1,29.20
śaivaṃ tatparamaṃ dhāma yatprāpyaṃ ṣaḍbhiradhvabhiḥ  || 7.1,29.21
533

vyāpikā 'vyāpikā śaktiḥ pañcatattvaviśodhanāt || 7.1,29.21
nivṛttyā rudraparyantaṃ sthitiraṇḍasya śodhyate  || 7.1,29.22
pratiṣṭhayā tadūrdhvaṃ tu yāvadavyaktagocaram  || 7.1,29.22
tadūrdhvaṃ vidyayā madhye yāvadviśveśvarāvadhi  || 7.1,29.23
śāntyā tadūrdhvaṃ madhvānte viśuddhiḥ śāntyatītayā  || 7.1,29.23
yāmāhuḥ paramaṃ vyoma paraprakṛtiyogataḥ  || 7.1,29.24
etāni pañcatattvāni yairvyāptamakhilaṃ jagat || 7.1,29.24
tatraiva sarvamevedaṃ draṣṭavyaṃ khalu sādhakaiḥ  || 7.1,29.25
adhvavyāptimavijñāya śuddhiṃ yaḥ kartumicchati  || 7.1,29.25
sa vipralambhakaḥ śuddhernālamprāpayituṃ phalam  || 7.1,29.26
vṛthā pariśramastasya nirayāyaiva kevalam  || 7.1,29.26
śaktipātasamāyogādṛte tattvāni tattvataḥ  || 7.1,29.27
tadvyāptistadvivṛddhiśca jñātumevaṃ na śakyate  || 7.1,29.27
śaktirājñā parā śaivī cidrūpā marameśvarī  || 7.1,29.28
śivo 'dhitiṣṭhatyakhilaṃ yayā kāraṇabhūtayā  || 7.1,29.28
nātmano naiva māyaiṣā na vikāro vicārataḥ  || 7.1,29.29
na baṃdho nāpi muktiśca baṃdhamuktividhāyinī  || 7.1,29.29
