sarvaiśvaryaparākāṣṭā śivasya vyabhicāriṇī  || 7.1,29.30
samānadharmiṇī tasya taistairbhāvairviśeṣataḥ  || 7.1,29.30
sa tayaiva gṛhī sāpi tenaiva gṛhiṇī sadā  || 7.1,29.31
tayorapatyaṃ yatkāryaṃ paraprakṛtijaṃ jagat || 7.1,29.31
sa kartā kāraṇaṃ seti tayorbhedo vyavasthitaḥ  || 7.1,29.32
eka eva śivaḥ sākṣāddvidhā 'sau samavasthitaḥ  || 7.1,29.32
strīpuṃsabhāvena tayorbheda ityapi kecana  || 7.1,29.33
apare tu parā śaktiḥ śivasya samavāyinī  || 7.1,29.33
prabheva bhānościdrūpā bhinnaiveti vyavasthitaḥ  || 7.1,29.34
tasmācchivaḥ paro hetustasyājñā parameśvarī  || 7.1,29.34
tayaiva preritā śaivī mūlaprakṛtiravyayā  || 7.1,29.35
mahāmāyā ca māyā ca prakṛtistriguṇeti ca  || 7.1,29.35
trividhā kāryavedhena sā prasūte ṣaḍadhvanaḥ  || 7.1,29.36
sa vāgarthamayaścādhvā ṣaḍvidho nikhilaṃ jagat || 7.1,29.36
asyaiva vistaraṃ prāhuḥ śāstrajātamaśeṣataḥ  || 7.1,29.37

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe vāgarthakatattvavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonatriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 30
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
caritāni vicitrāṇi gṛhyāṇi gahanāni ca  || 7.1,30.1
durvijñeyāni devaiśca mohayaṃti manāṃsi naḥ  || 7.1,30.1
śivayostattvasambandhe na doṣa upalabhyate  || 7.1,30.2
caritaiḥ prākṛto bhāvastayorapi vibhāvyate  || 7.1,30.2
brahmādayo 'pi lokānāṃ sṛṣṭisthityantahetavaḥ  || 7.1,30.3
nigrahānugrahau prāpya śivasya vaśavartinaḥ  || 7.1,30.3
śivaḥ punarna kasyāpi nigrahānugrahāspadam  || 7.1,30.4
ato 'nāyattamaiśvaryaṃ tasyaiveti viniścitam  || 7.1,30.4
534

yadyevamīdṛśaiśvaryaṃ tattu svātantryalakṣaṇam  || 7.1,30.5
svabhāvasiddhaṃ caitasya mūrtimattāspadaṃ bhavet || 7.1,30.5
na mūrtiśca svataṃtrasya ghaṭate mūlahetunā  || 7.1,30.6
mūrterapi ca kāryatvāttatsiddhiḥ syādahaitukī  || 7.1,30.6
sarvatra paramo bhāvo 'paramaścānya ucyate  || 7.1,30.7
paramāparamau bhāvau kathamekatra saṃgatau  || 7.1,30.7
niṣphalo hi svabhāvo 'sya paramaḥ paramātmanaḥ  || 7.1,30.8
sa eva sakalaḥ kasmātsvabhāvo hyaviparyayaḥ  || 7.1,30.8
svabhāvo viparītaścetsvataṃtraḥ svecchayā yadi  || 7.1,30.9
na karoti kimīśāno nityānityaviparyayam  || 7.1,30.9
mūrtātmā sakalaḥ kaścitsa cānyo niṣphalaḥ śivaḥ  || 7.1,30.10
śivenādhiṣṭhitaśceti sarvatra laghu kathyate  || 7.1,30.10
mūrtyātmaiva tadā mūrtiḥ śivasyāsya bhavediti  || 7.1,30.11
tasya mūrtau mūrtimatoḥ pārataṃtryaṃ hi niścitam  || 7.1,30.11
anyathā nirapekṣeṇa mūrtiḥ svīkriyate katham  || 7.1,30.12
mūrtisvīkaraṇaṃ tasmānmūrtau sādhyaphalepsayā  || 7.1,30.12
na hi svecchāśarīratvaṃ svātaṃtryāyopapadyate  || 7.1,30.13
svecchaiva tādṛśī puṃsāṃ yasmātkarmānusāriṇī  || 7.1,30.13
svīkartuṃ svecchayā dehaṃ hātuṃ ca prabhavantyuta  || 7.1,30.14
brahmādayaḥ piśācāṃtāḥ kiṃ te karmātivartinaḥ  || 7.1,30.14
icchayā dehanirmāṇamindrajālopamaṃ viduḥ  || 7.1,30.15
aṇimādiguṇaiśvaryavaśīkārānatikramāt || 7.1,30.15
viśvarūpaṃ dadhadviṣṇurdadhīcena maharṣiṇā  || 7.1,30.16
yudhyatā samupālabdhastadrūpaṃ dadhatā svayam  || 7.1,30.16
sarvasmādadhikasyāpi śivasya paramātmanaḥ  || 7.1,30.17
śarīravattayānyātmasādharmyaṃ pratibhāti naḥ  || 7.1,30.17
sarvānugrāhakaṃ prāhuśśivaṃ paramakāraṇam  || 7.1,30.18
sa nirgṛhṇāti devānāṃ sarvānugrāhakaḥ katham  || 7.1,30.18
ciccheda bahuśo devo brahmaṇaḥ pañcamaṃ śiraḥ  || 7.1,30.19
śivanindāṃ prakurvaṃtaṃ putreti kumaterhaṭhāt || 7.1,30.19
viṣṇorapi nṛsiṃhasya rabhasā śarabhākṛtiḥ  || 7.1,30.20
bibheda padbhyāmākramya hṛdayaṃ nakharaiḥ kharaiḥ  || 7.1,30.20
devastrīṣu ca deveṣu dakṣasyādhvarakāraṇāt || 7.1,30.21
vīreṇa vīrabhadreṇa na hi kaścidadaṇḍitaḥ  || 7.1,30.21
puratrayaṃ ca sastrīkaṃ sadaityaṃ saha bālakaiḥ  || 7.1,30.22
kṣaṇenaikena devena netrāgneriṃdhanīkṛtam  || 7.1,30.22
prajānāṃ ratihetuśca kāmo ratipatissvayam  || 7.1,30.23
krośatāmeva devānāṃ huto netrahutāśane  || 7.1,30.23
gāvaśca kaściddugdhaughaṃ sravantyo mūrdhni khecarāḥ  || 7.1,30.24
saruṣā prekṣya devena tatkṣaṇe bhasmasātkṛtaḥ  || 7.1,30.24
jalaṃdharāsuro dīrṇaścakrīkṛtya jalaṃ padā  || 7.1,30.25
baddhvānaṃtena yo viṣṇuṃ cikṣepa śatayojanam  || 7.1,30.25
tameva jalasaṃdhāyī śūlenaiva jaghāna saḥ  || 7.1,30.26
taccakraṃ tapasā labdhvā labdhavīryo harissadā  || 7.1,30.26
534

jighāṃsatāṃ surārīṇāṃ kulaṃ nirghṛṇacetasām  || 7.1,30.27
triśūlenāndhakasyoraḥ śikhinaivopatāpitam  || 7.1,30.27
kaṇṭhātkālāṃganāṃ sṛṣṭvā dārako 'pi nipātitaḥ  || 7.1,30.28
kauśikīṃ janayitvā tu gauryāstvakkośagocarām  || 7.1,30.28
śuṃbhassaha niśuṃbhena prāpito maraṇaṃ raṇe  || 7.1,30.29
śrutaṃ ca mahadākhyānaṃ skānde skandasamāśrayam  || 7.1,30.29
vadhārthe tārakākhyasya daityendrasyendravidviṣaḥ  || 7.1,30.30
brahmaṇābhyarthito devo mandarāntaḥpuraṃ gataḥ  || 7.1,30.30
vihṛtya suciraṃ devyā vihārā 'tiprasaṅgataḥ  || 7.1,30.31
rasāṃ rasātalaṃ nītāmiva kṛtvābhidhāṃ tataḥ  || 7.1,30.31
devīṃ ca vaṃcayaṃstasyāṃ svavīryamatidurvaham  || 7.1,30.32
avisṛjya visṛjyāgnau haviḥ pūtamivāmṛtam  || 7.1,30.32
gaṃgādiṣvapi nikṣipya vahnidvārā tadaṃśataḥ  || 7.1,30.33
tatsamāhṛtya śanakaistokaṃstokamitastataḥ  || 7.1,30.33
svāhayā kṛttikārūpātsvabhartrā ramamāṇayā  || 7.1,30.34
suvarṇībhūtayā nyastaṃ merau śaravaṇe kvacit || 7.1,30.34
saṃdīpayitvā kālena tasya bhāsā diśo daśa  || 7.1,30.35
rañjayitvā girīnsarvānkāṃcanīkṛtya meruṇā  || 7.1,30.35
tataścireṇa kālena saṃjāte tatra tejasi  || 7.1,30.36
kumāre sukumārāṃge kumārāṇāṃ nidarśane  || 7.1,30.36
tacchaiśavaṃ svarūpaṃ ca tasya dṛṣṭvā manoharam  || 7.1,30.37
saha devasurairlokairvismite ca vimohite  || 7.1,30.37
devo 'pi svayamāyātaḥ putradarśanalālasaḥ  || 7.1,30.38
saha devyāṃkamāropya tato 'sya smeramānanam  || 7.1,30.38
pītāmṛtamiva snehavivaśenāntarātmanā  || 7.1,30.39
deveṣvapi ca paśyatsu vītarāgaistapasvibhiḥ  || 7.1,30.39
svasya vakṣaḥsthale svairaṃ nartayitvā kumārakam  || 7.1,30.40
anubhūya ca tatkrīḍāṃ saṃbhāvya ca parasparam  || 7.1,30.40
stanyamājñāpayandevyāḥ pāyayitvāmṛtopamam  || 7.1,30.41
tavāvatāro jagatāṃ hitāyetyanuśāsya ca  || 7.1,30.41
svayandevaśca devī ca na tṛptimupajagmatuḥ  || 7.1,30.42
tataḥ śakreṇa saṃdhāya bibhyatā tārakāsurāt || 7.1,30.42
kārayitvābhiṣekaṃ ca senāpatye divaukasām  || 7.1,30.43
putramantarataḥ kṛtvā devena tripuradviṣā  || 7.1,30.43
svayamaṃtarhitenaiva skandamindrādirakṣitam  || 7.1,30.44
tacchaktyā krauñcabhedinyā yudhi kālāgnikalpayā  || 7.1,30.44
cheditaṃ tārakasyāpi śiraśśakrabhiyā saha  || 7.1,30.45
stutiṃ cakrurviśeṣeṇa haridhātṛmukhāḥ surāḥ  || 7.1,30.45
tathā rakṣodhipaḥ sākṣādrāvaṇo balagarvitaḥ  || 7.1,30.46
uddharansvabhujairdīrghaiḥ kailāsaṃ girimātmanaḥ  || 7.1,30.46
tadāgo 'sahamānasya devadevasya śūlinaḥ  || 7.1,30.47
padāṃguṣṭhaparispandānmamajja mṛdito bhuvi  || 7.1,30.47
baṭoḥ kenacidarthena svāśritasya gatāyuṣaḥ  || 7.1,30.48
535

tvarayāgatya devena pādāṃtaṃ gamitontakaḥ  || 7.1,30.48
svavāhanamavijñāya vṛṣendraṃ vaḍavānalaḥ  || 7.1,30.49
sagalagrahamānītastato 'styekodakaṃ jagat || 7.1,30.49
alokaviditaistaistairvṛttairānandasundaraiḥ  || 7.1,30.50
aṃgahārasvasenedamasakṛccālitaṃ jagat || 7.1,30.50
śānta eva sadā sarvamanugṛhṇāti cecchivaḥ  || 7.1,30.51
sarvāṇi pūrayedeva kathaṃ śaktena mocayet || 7.1,30.51
anādikarma vaicitryamapi nātra niyāmakam  || 7.1,30.52
kāraṇaṃ khalu karmāpi bhavedīśvarakāritam  || 7.1,30.52
kimatra bahunoktena nāstikyaṃ hetukārakam  || 7.1,30.53
yathā hyāśu nivarteta tathā kathaya māruta  || 7.1,30.53

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe śivatattvapraśno nāma triṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 31
vāyuruvāca
sthane saṃśayitaṃ viprā bhavadbhirhetucoditaiḥ  || 7.1,31.1
jijñāsā hi na nāstikyaṃ sādhayetsādhubuddhiṣu  || 7.1,31.1
pramaṇamatra vakṣyāmi satāmmohanivartakam  || 7.1,31.2
asatāṃ tvanyathābhāvaḥ prasādena vinā prabhoḥ  || 7.1,31.2
śivasya paripūrṇasya parānugrahamantarā  || 7.1,31.3
na kiṃcidapi kartavyamiti sādhu viniścitam  || 7.1,31.3
svabhāva eva paryāptaḥ parānugrahakarmaṇi  || 7.1,31.4
anyathā nissvabhavena na kimapyanugṛhyate  || 7.1,31.4
paraṃ sarvamanugrāhyaṃ paśupāśātmakaṃ jagat || 7.1,31.5
parasyānugrahārthaṃ tu patyurājñāsamanvayaḥ  || 7.1,31.5
patirājñāpakaḥ sarvamanugṛhṇāti sarvadā  || 7.1,31.6
tadarthamarthasvīkāre parataṃtraḥ kathaṃ śivaḥ  || 7.1,31.6
anugrāhyanapekṣo 'sti na hi kaścidanugrahaḥ  || 7.1,31.7
ataḥ svātantryaśabdārthānanapekṣatvalakṣaṇaḥ  || 7.1,31.7
etatpunaranugrāhyaṃ parataṃtraṃ tadiṣyate  || 7.1,31.8
anugrahādṛte tasya bhuktimuktyorananvayāt || 7.1,31.8
mūrtātmano 'pyanugrāhyā śivājñānanivartanāt || 7.1,31.9
ajñānādhiṣṭhitaṃ śambhorna kiṃcidiha vidyate  || 7.1,31.9
yenopalabhyate 'smābhissakalenāpi niṣkalaḥ  || 7.1,31.10
sa mūrtyātmā śivaḥ śaivamūrtirityupacaryate  || 7.1,31.10
na hyasau niṣkalaḥ sākṣācchivaḥ paramakāraṇam  || 7.1,31.11
sākāreṇānubhāvena kenāpyanupalakṣitaḥ  || 7.1,31.11
pramāṇagamyatāmātraṃ tatsvabhāvopapādakam  || 7.1,31.12
na tāvatātropekṣādhīrupalakṣaṇamaṃtarā  || 7.1,31.12
ātmopamolvaṇaṃ sākṣānmūrtireva hi kācana  || 7.1,31.13
śivasya mūrtirmūrtyātmā parastasyopalakṣaṇam  || 7.1,31.13
yathā kāṣṭheṣvanārūḍho na vahnirupalabhyate  || 7.1,31.14
evaṃ śivo 'pi mūrtyātmanyanārūḍha iti sthitiḥ  || 7.1,31.14
yathāgnimānayetyukte jvalatkāṣṭhādṛte svayam  || 7.1,31.15
nāgnirānīyate tadvatpūjyo mūrtyātmanā śivaḥ  || 7.1,31.15
ata eva hi pūjādau mūrtyātmaparikalpanam  || 7.1,31.16
mūrtyātmani kṛtaṃ sākṣācchiva eva kṛtaṃ yataḥ  || 7.1,31.16
liṃgādāvapi tatkṛtyamarcāyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  || 7.1,31.17
tattanmūrtyātmabhāvena śivo 'smābhirupāsyate  || 7.1,31.17
yathānugṛhyate so 'pi mūrtyātmā pārameṣṭhinā  || 7.1,31.18
tathā mūrtyātmaniṣṭhena śivena paśavo vayam  || 7.1,31.18
lokānugrahaṇāyaiva śivena parameṣṭhinā  || 7.1,31.19
sadāśivādayassarve mūrtyātmano 'pyadhiṣṭhitāḥ  || 7.1,31.19
ātmanāmeva bhogāya mokṣāya ca viśeṣataḥ  || 7.1,31.20
tattvātattvasvarūpeṣu mūrtyātmasu śivānvayaḥ  || 7.1,31.20
bhogaḥ karmavipākātmā sukhaduḥkhātmako mataḥ  || 7.1,31.21
na ca karma śivo 'stīti tasya bhogaḥ kimātmakaḥ  || 7.1,31.21
sarvaṃ śivo 'nugṛhṇāti na nigṛhṇāti kiṃcana  || 7.1,31.22
nigṛhṇatāṃ tu ye doṣāśśive teṣāmasaṃbhavāt || 7.1,31.22
ye punarnigrahāḥ kecidbrahmādiṣu nidarśitāḥ  || 7.1,31.23
te 'pi lokahitāyaiva kṛtāḥ śrīkaṇṭhamūrtinā  || 7.1,31.23
brahmāṇḍasyādhipatyaṃ hi śrīkaṇṭhasya na saṃśayaḥ  || 7.1,31.24
śrīkaṇṭhākhyāṃ śivo mūrtiṃ krīḍatīmadhitiṣṭhati  || 7.1,31.24
sadoṣā eva devādyā nigṛhītā yathoditam  || 7.1,31.25
tatastepi vipāpmānaḥ prajāścāpi gatajvarāḥ  || 7.1,31.25
nigraho 'pi svarūpeṇa viduṣāṃ na jugupsitaḥ  || 7.1,31.26
ata eva hi daṇḍyeṣu daṇḍo rājñāṃ praśasyate  || 7.1,31.26
yatsiddhirīśvaratvena kāryavargasya kṛtsnaśaḥ  || 7.1,31.27
na sa cedīśatāṃ kuryājjagataḥ kathamīśvaraḥ  || 7.1,31.27
īśecchā ca vidhātṛtvaṃ vidherājñāpanaṃ param  || 7.1,31.28
ājñāvaśyamidaṃ kuryānna kuryāditi śāsanam  || 7.1,31.28
tacchāsanānuvartitvaṃ sādhubhāvasya lakṣaṇam  || 7.1,31.29
viparītasamādhoḥ syānna sarvaṃ tattu dṛśyate  || 7.1,31.29
sādhu saṃrakṣaṇīyaṃ cedvinivartyamasādhu yat || 7.1,31.30
nivartate ca sāmāderaṃte daṇḍo hi sādhanam  || 7.1,31.30
hitārthalakṣaṇaṃ cedaṃ daṇḍāntamanuśāsanam  || 7.1,31.31
ato yadviparītaṃ tadahitaṃ saṃpracakṣate  || 7.1,31.31
hite sadā niṣaṇṇānāmīśvarasya nidarśanam  || 7.1,31.32
sa kathaṃ duṣyate sadbhirasatāmeva nigrahāt || 7.1,31.32
ayuktakāriṇo loke garhaṇīyāvivekitā  || 7.1,31.33
yadudvejayate lokantadayuktaṃ pracakṣate  || 7.1,31.33
sarvo 'pi nigraho loke na ca vidveṣapūrvakaḥ  || 7.1,31.34
na hi dveṣṭi pitā putraṃ yo nigṛhyāti śikṣayet || 7.1,31.34
mādhyasthenāpi nigrāhyānyo nigṛhṇāti mārgataḥ  || 7.1,31.35
tasyāpyavaśyaṃ yatkiṃcinnairghṛṇyamanuvartate  || 7.1,31.35
anyathā na hinastyeva sadoṣānapyasau parān  || 7.1,31.36
hinasti cāyamapyajñānparaṃ mādhyasthyamācaran  || 7.1,31.36
tasmādduḥkhātmikāṃ hiṃsāṃ kurvāṇo yaḥ sanirghṛṇaḥ  || 7.1,31.37
536

iti nirbaṃdhayaṃtyeke niyamo neti cāpare  || 7.1,31.37
nidānajñasya bhiṣajo rugṇo hiṃsāṃ prayuṃjataḥ  || 7.1,31.38
na kiṃcidapi nairghṛṇyaṃ ghṛṇaivātra prayojikā  || 7.1,31.38
ghṛṇāpi na guṇāyaiva hiṃsreṣu pratiyogiṣu  || 7.1,31.39
tādṛśeṣu ghṛṇī bhrāntyā ghṛṇāntaritanirghṛṇaḥ  || 7.1,31.39
upekṣāpīha doṣāha rakṣyeṣu pratiyogiṣu  || 7.1,31.40
śaktau satyāmupekṣāto rakṣyassadyo vipadyate  || 7.1,31.40
sarpasyā"syagatampaśyanyastu rakṣyamupekṣate  || 7.1,31.41
doṣābhāsānsamutprekṣya phalataḥ so 'pi nirghṛṇaḥ  || 7.1,31.41
tasmādghṛṇā guṇāyaiva sarvatheti na saṃmatam  || 7.1,31.42
saṃmataṃ prāptakāmitvaṃ sarvaṃ tvanyadasammatam  || 7.1,31.42
mūrtyātmasvapi rāgādyā doṣāḥ santyeva vastutaḥ  || 7.1,31.43
tathāpi teṣāmevaite na śivasya tu sarvathā  || 7.1,31.43
agnāvapi samāviṣṭaṃ tāmraṃ khalu sakālikam  || 7.1,31.43
iti nāgnirasau duṣyettāmrasaṃsargakāraṇāt || 7.1,31.44
nāgneraśucisaṃsargādaśucitvamapekṣate  || 7.1,31.44
aśucestvagnisaṃyogācchucitvamapi jāyate  || 7.1,31.45
evaṃ śodhyātmasaṃsargānna hyaśuddhaḥ śivo bhavet || 7.1,31.45
śivasaṃsargatastveṣa śodhyātmaiva hi śudhyati  || 7.1,31.46
ayasyagnau samāviṣṭe dāho 'gnereva nāyasaḥ  || 7.1,31.46
mūrtātmanyevamaiśvaryamīśvarasyaiva nātmanām  || 7.1,31.47
na hi kāṣṭhaṃ jvalatyūrdhvamagnireva jvalatyasau  || 7.1,31.47
kāṣṭhasyāṃgāratā nāgnerevamatrāpi yojyatām  || 7.1,31.48
ata eva jagatyasminkāṣṭhapāṣāṇamṛtsvapi  || 7.1,31.48
śivāveśavaśādeva śivatvamupacaryate  || 7.1,31.49
maitryādayo guṇā gauṇāstasmātte bhinnavṛttayaḥ  || 7.1,31.49
tairguṇairuparaktānāṃ doṣāya ca guṇāya ca  || 7.1,31.50
yattu gauṇamagauṇaṃ ca tatsarvamanugṛhṇataḥ  || 7.1,31.50
na guṇāya na doṣāya śivasya guṇavṛttayaḥ  || 7.1,31.50
na cānugrahaśabdārthaṃ gauṇamāhurvipaścitaḥ  || 7.1,31.51
saṃsāramocanaṃ kiṃ tu śaivamājñāmayaṃ hitam  || 7.1,31.51
hitaṃ tadājñākaraṇaṃ yaddhitaṃ tadanugrahaḥ  || 7.1,31.52
sarvaṃ hite niyuñjāvaḥ sarvānugrahakārakaḥ  || 7.1,31.52
yastūpakāraśabdārthastamapyāhuranugraham  || 7.1,31.53
tasyāpi hitarūpatvācchivaḥ sarvopakārakaḥ  || 7.1,31.53
hite sadā niyuktaṃ tu sarvaṃ cidacidātmakam  || 7.1,31.54
svabhāvapratibandhaṃ tatsamaṃ na labhate hitam  || 7.1,31.54
yathā vikāsayatyeva raviḥ padmāni bhānubhiḥ  || 7.1,31.55
samaṃ na vikasantyeva svasvabhāvānurodhataḥ  || 7.1,31.55
svabhāvo 'pi hi bhāvānāṃ bhāvino 'rthasya kāraṇam  || 7.1,31.56
na hi svabhāvo naśyantamarthaṃ kartṛṣu sādhayet || 7.1,31.56
suvarṇameva nāṃgāraṃ drāvayatyagnisaṃgamaḥ  || 7.1,31.57
evaṃ pakvamalāneva mocayenna śivaparān  || 7.1,31.57
536

yadyathā bhavituṃ yogyaṃ tattathā na bhavetsvayam  || 7.1,31.58
vinā bhāvanayā kartā svatantrassantato bhavet || 7.1,31.58
svabhāvavimalo yadvatsarvānugrāhakaśśivaḥ  || 7.1,31.59
svabhāvamalināstadvadātmano jīvasaṃjñitāḥ  || 7.1,31.59
anyathā saṃsarantyete niyamānna śivaḥ katham  || 7.1,31.60
karmamāyānubandhosya saṃsāraḥ kathyate budhaiḥ  || 7.1,31.60
anubandho 'yamasyaiva na śivasyeti hetumān  || 7.1,31.61
sa heturātmanāmeva nijo nāgantuko malaḥ  || 7.1,31.61
āgantukatve kasyāpi bhāvyaṃ kenāpi hetunā  || 7.1,31.62
yo 'yaṃ heturasāvekastvavicitrasvabhāvataḥ  || 7.1,31.62
ātmatāyāḥ samatve 'pi baddhā muktāḥ pare yataḥ  || 7.1,31.63
baddheṣveva punaḥ kecillayabhogādhikārataḥ  || 7.1,31.63
jñānaiśvaryādivaiṣamyaṃ bhajante sottarādharāḥ  || 7.1,31.64
kecinmūrtyātmatāṃ yānti kecidāsannagocarāḥ  || 7.1,31.64
mūrtyātmasu śivāḥ kecidadhvanāṃ mūrdhasu sthitāḥ  || 7.1,31.65
madhye maheśvarā rudrāstvarvācīnapade sthitāḥ  || 7.1,31.65
āsanne 'pi ca māyāyāḥ parasmātkāraṇāttrayam  || 7.1,31.66
tatrāpyātmā sthito 'dhastādantarātmā ca madhyataḥ  || 7.1,31.66
parastātparamātmeti brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ  || 7.1,31.67
vartante vasavaḥ kecitparamātmapadāśrayāḥ  || 7.1,31.67
antarātmapade kecitkecidātmapade tathā  || 7.1,31.68
śāntyatītapade śaivāḥ śānte māheśvare tataḥ  || 7.1,31.68
vidyāyāntu yathā raudrāḥ pratiṣṭhāyāṃ tu vaiṣṇavāḥ  || 7.1,31.69
nivṛttau ca tathātmāno brahmā brahmāṃgayonayaḥ  || 7.1,31.69
devayonyaṣṭakaṃ mukhyaṃ mānuṣyamatha madhyamam  || 7.1,31.70
pakṣyādayo 'dhamāḥ pañcayonayastāścaturdaśa  || 7.1,31.70
uttarādharabhāvo 'pi jñeyassaṃsāriṇo malaḥ  || 7.1,31.71
yathāmabhāvo muktasya pūrvaṃ paścāttu pakvatā  || 7.1,31.71
malo 'pyāmaśca pakvaśca bhavetsaṃsārakāraṇam  || 7.1,31.72
āme tvadharatā puṃsāṃ pakve tūttaratā kramāt || 7.1,31.72
paśvātmānastridhābhinnā ekadvitrimalāḥ kramāt || 7.1,31.73
atrottarā ekamalā dvimalā madhyamā matāḥ  || 7.1,31.73
trimalāstvadhamā jñeyā yathottaramadhiṣṭhitāḥ  || 7.1,31.73
trimalānadhitiṣṭhaṃti dvimalaikamalāḥ kramāt || 7.1,31.74
itthamaupādhiko bhedo viśvasya parikalpitaḥ  || 7.1,31.74
ekadvitrimalānsarvāñchiva eko 'dhitiṣṭhati  || 7.1,31.75
aśivātmakamapyetacchivenādhiṣṭhitaṃ yathā  || 7.1,31.75
arudrātmakamityevaṃ rudrairjagadadhiṣṭhitam  || 7.1,31.76
aṇḍāntā hi mahābhūmiśśatarudrādyadhiṣṭhitā  || 7.1,31.76
māyāntamantarikṣaṃ tu hyamareśādibhiḥ kramāt || 7.1,31.77
aṃguṣṭhamātraparyantaissamaṃtātsaṃtataṃ tatam  || 7.1,31.77
mahāmāyāvasānā dyaurvāyvādyairbhuvanādhipaiḥ  || 7.1,31.78
537

anāśritāntairadhvāntarvartibhissamadhiṣṭhitāḥ  || 7.1,31.78
te hi sākṣāddiviṣadastvantarikṣasadastathā  || 7.1,31.79
pṛthivīpada ityevaṃ devā devavrataiḥ stutā  || 7.1,31.79
evantribhirmalairāmaiḥ pakvaireva pṛthakpṛthak || 7.1,31.80
nidānabhūtaissaṃsārarogaḥ puṃsāṃ pravartate  || 7.1,31.80
asya rogasya bhaiṣajyaṃ jñānameva na cāparam  || 7.1,31.81
bhiṣagājñāpakaḥ śambhuśśivaḥ paramakāraṇam  || 7.1,31.81
aduḥkhenā 'pi śakto 'sau paśūnmocayituṃ śivaḥ  || 7.1,31.82
kathaṃ duḥkhaṃ karotīti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 7.1,31.82
duḥkhameva hi sarvo 'pi saṃsāra iti niścitam  || 7.1,31.83
kathaṃ duḥkhamaduḥkhaṃ syātsvabhāvo hyaviparyayaḥ  || 7.1,31.83
na hi rogī hyarogī syādbhiṣagbhaiṣajyakāraṇāt || 7.1,31.84
rogārtaṃ tu bhiṣagrogādbhaiṣajaissukhamuddharet || 7.1,31.84
evaṃ svabhāvamalinānsvabhāvādduḥkhinaḥ paśūn  || 7.1,31.85
svājñauṣadhavidhānena duḥkhānmocayate śivaḥ  || 7.1,31.85
na bhiṣakkāraṇaṃ roge śivaḥ saṃsārakāraṇam  || 7.1,31.86
ityetadapi vaiṣamyaṃ na doṣāyāsya kalpate  || 7.1,31.86
duḥkhe svabhāvasaṃsiddhe kathantatkāraṇaṃ śivaḥ  || 7.1,31.87
svābhāviko malaḥ puṃsāṃ sa hi saṃsārayatyamūn  || 7.1,31.87
saṃsārakāraṇaṃ yattu malaṃ māyādyacetanam  || 7.1,31.88
tatsvayaṃ na pravarteta śivasānnidhyamantarā  || 7.1,31.88
yathā maṇirayaskāṃtassānnidhyādupakārakaḥ  || 7.1,31.89
ayasaścalatastadvacchivo 'pyasyeti sūrayaḥ  || 7.1,31.89
na nivartayituṃ śakyaṃ sānnidhyaṃ sadakāraṇam  || 7.1,31.90
adhiṣṭhātā tato nityamajñāto jagataśśivaḥ  || 7.1,31.90
na śivena vinā kiṃcitpravṛttamiha vidyate  || 7.1,31.91
tatpreritamidaṃ sarvaṃ tathāpi na sa muhyati  || 7.1,31.91
śaktirājñātmikā tasya niyantrī viśvatomukhī  || 7.1,31.92
tayā tatamidaṃ śaśvattathāpi sa na duṣyati  || 7.1,31.92
anidaṃ prathamaṃ sarvamīśitavyaṃ sa īśvaraḥ  || 7.1,31.93
īśanācca tadīyājñā tathāpi sa na duṣyati  || 7.1,31.93
yo 'nyathā manyate mohātsa vinaṣyati durmatiḥ  || 7.1,31.94
tacchaktivaibhavādeva tathāpi sa na duṣyati  || 7.1,31.94
etasminnaṃtare vyomnaḥ śrutāḥ vāgarīriṇī  || 7.1,31.95
satyamomamṛtaṃ saumyamityāvirabhavatsphuṭam  || 7.1,31.95
tato hṛṣṭatarāḥ sarve vinaṣṭāśeṣasaṃśayāḥ  || 7.1,31.96
munayo vismayāviṣṭāḥ preṇemuḥ pavanaṃ prabhum  || 7.1,31.96
tathā vigatasandehānkṛtvāpi pavano munīn  || 7.1,31.97
naite pratiṣṭhitajñānā iti matvaivamabravīt || 7.1,31.97
vāyuruvācva
parokṣamaparokṣaṃ ca dvividhaṃ jñānamiṣyate  || 7.1,31.98
parokṣamasthiraṃ prāhuraparokṣaṃ tu susthiram  || 7.1,31.98
hetūpadeśagamyaṃ yattatparokṣaṃ pracakṣate  || 7.1,31.99
537

aparokṣaṃ punaḥ śreṣṭhādanuṣṭhānādbhaviṣyati  || 7.1,31.99
nāparokṣādṛte mokṣa iti kṛtvā viniścayam  || 7.1,31.100
śreṣṭhānuṣṭhānasiddhyarthaṃ prayatadhvamatandritāḥ  || 7.1,31.100

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe jñānopadeśo nāmaikatriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 32
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
kiṃ tacchreṣṭamanuṣṭhānaṃ mokṣo yenaparokṣitaḥ  || 7.1,32.1
tattasya sādhanaṃ cādya vaktumarhasi māruta  || 7.1,32.1
vāyuruvāca
śaivo hi paramo dharmaḥ śreṣṭhānuṣṭhānaśabditaḥ  || 7.1,32.2
yatrāparokṣo lakṣyeta sākṣānmokṣapradaḥ śivaḥ  || 7.1,32.2
sa tu pañcavidho jñeyaḥ pañcabhiḥ parvabhiḥ kramāt || 7.1,32.3
kriyātapojapadhyānajñānātmabhiranuttaraiḥ  || 7.1,32.3
taireva sottaraissiddho dharmastu paramo mataḥ  || 7.1,32.4
parokṣamaparokṣaṃ ca jñānaṃ yatra ca mokṣadam  || 7.1,32.4
paramo 'paramaścobhau dharmau hi śruticoditau  || 7.1,32.5
dharmaśabdābhidheyerthe pramāṇaṃ śrutireva naḥ  || 7.1,32.5
paramo yogaparyanto dharmaḥ śrutiśirogataḥ  || 7.1,32.6
dharmastvaparamastadvadadhaḥ śrutimukhotthitaḥ  || 7.1,32.6
apaśvātmādhikāratvādyo dharamaḥ paramo mataḥ  || 7.1,32.7
sādhāraṇastato 'nyastu sarveṣāmadhikārataḥ  || 7.1,32.7
sa cāyaṃ paramo dharmaḥ paradharmasya sādhanam  || 7.1,32.8
dharmaśāstrādibhissamyaksāṃga evopabṛṃhitaḥ  || 7.1,32.8
śaivo yaḥ paramo dharmaḥ śreṣṭhānuṣṭhānaśabditaḥ  || 7.1,32.9
itihāsapurāṇābhyāṃ kathaṃcidupabṛṃhitaḥ  || 7.1,32.9
śaivāgamaistu saṃpannaḥ sahāṃgopāṃvistaraḥ  || 7.1,32.10
tatsaṃskārādhikāraiśca samyagevopabṛṃhitaḥ  || 7.1,32.10
śaivāgamo hi dvividhaḥ śrauto 'śrautaśca saṃskṛtaḥ  || 7.1,32.11
śrutisāramayaḥ śrautassvataṃtra itaro mataḥ  || 7.1,32.11
svataṃtro daśadhā pūrvaṃ tathāṣṭādaśadhā punaḥ  || 7.1,32.12
kāmikādisamākhyābhissiddhaḥ siddhāntasaṃjñitaḥ  || 7.1,32.12
śrutisāramayo yastu śatakoṭipravistaraḥ  || 7.1,32.13
paraṃ pāśupataṃ yatra vrataṃ jñānaṃ ca kathyate  || 7.1,32.13
yugāvarteṣu śiṣyeta yogācāryasvarūpiṇā  || 7.1,32.14
tatratatrāvatīrṇena śivenaiva pravartyate  || 7.1,32.14
saṃkṣipyāsya pravaktāraścatvāraḥ paramarṣaya  || 7.1,32.15
rururdadhīco 'gastyaśca upamanyurmahāyaśāḥ  || 7.1,32.15
te ca pāśupatā jñeyāssaṃhitānāṃ pravartakāḥ  || 7.1,32.16
tatsaṃtatīyā guravaḥ śataśo 'tha sahasraśaḥ  || 7.1,32.16
tatroktaḥ paramo dharmaścaryādyātmā caturvidhaḥ  || 7.1,32.17
teṣu pāśupato yogaḥ śivaṃ pratyakṣayeddṛḍham  || 7.1,32.17
tasmācchreṣṭhamanuṣṭhānaṃ yogaḥ pāśupato mataḥ  || 7.1,32.18
tatrāpyupāyako yukto brahmaṇā sa tu kathyate  || 7.1,32.18
nāmāṣṭakamayo yogaśśivena parikalpitaḥ  || 7.1,32.19
tena yogena sahasā śaivī prajñā prajāyate  || 7.1,32.19
prajñayā paramaṃ jñānamacirāllabhate sthiram  || 7.1,32.20
538

prasīdati śivastasya yasya jñānaṃ pratiṣṭhitam  || 7.1,32.20
prasādātparamo yogo yaḥ śivaṃ cāparokṣayet || 7.1,32.21
śivāparokṣātsaṃsārakāraṇena viyujyate  || 7.1,32.21
tataḥ syānmuktasaṃsāro muktaḥ śivasamo bhavet || 7.1,32.22
brahmaprokta ityupāyaḥ sa eva pṛthagucyate  || 7.1,32.22
śivo maheśvaraścaiva rudro viṣṇuḥ pitāmahaḥ  || 7.1,32.23
saṃsāravaidyaḥ sarvajñaḥ paramātmeti mukhyataḥ  || 7.1,32.23
nāmāṣṭakamidaṃ mukhyaṃ śivasya pratipādakam  || 7.1,32.24
ādyantu pañcakaṃ jñeyaṃ śāntyatītādyanukramāt || 7.1,32.24
saṃjñā sadāśivādīnāṃ pañcopādhiparigrahāt || 7.1,32.25
upādhivinivṛttau tu yathāsvaṃ vinivartate  || 7.1,32.25
padameva hi tannityamanityāḥ padinaḥ smṛtāḥ  || 7.1,32.26
padānāṃ pratikṛttau tu mucyante padino yataḥ  || 7.1,32.26
parivṛttyantare bhūyastatpadaprāptirucyate  || 7.1,32.27
ātmāntarābhidhānaṃ syādyadādyaṃ nāma pañcakam  || 7.1,32.27
anyattu tritayaṃ nāmnāmupādānādiyogataḥ  || 7.1,32.28
trividhopādhivacanācchiva evānuvartate  || 7.1,32.28
anādimalasaṃśleṣaḥ prāgabhāvātsvabhāvataḥ  || 7.1,32.29
atyaṃtaṃ pariśuddhātmetyato 'yaṃ śiva ucyate  || 7.1,32.29
athavāśeṣakalyāṇaguṇaikadhana īśvaraḥ  || 7.1,32.30
śiva ityucyate sadbhiśśivatattvārthavādibhiḥ  || 7.1,32.30
trayoviṃśatitattvebhyaḥ prakṛtirhi parā matā  || 7.1,32.31
prakṛtestu paraṃ prāhuḥ puruṣaṃ pañcaviṃśakam  || 7.1,32.31
yaṃ vedādau svaraṃ prāhurvācyavācakabhāvataḥ  || 7.1,32.32
vedaikavedyayāthātmyādvedānte ca pratiṣṭhitaḥ  || 7.1,32.32
tasya prakṛtilīnasya yaḥ parassa maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,32.33
tadadhīnapravṛttitvātprakṛteḥ puruṣasya ca  || 7.1,32.33
athavā triguṇaṃ tattvamupeyamidamavyayam  || 7.1,32.34
māyāntu prakṛtiṃ vidyānmāyinaṃ tu maheśvaram  || 7.1,32.34
māyāvikṣobhako 'naṃto maheśvarasamanvayāt || 7.1,32.35
kālātmā paramātmādiḥ sthūlaḥ sūkṣmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  || 7.1,32.35
rudduḥkhaṃ duḥkhaheturvā tadrāvayati naḥ prabhuḥ  || 7.1,32.36
rudra ityucyate sadbhiḥ śivaḥ paramakāraṇam  || 7.1,32.36
tattvādibhūtaparyantaṃ śarīrādiṣvatandritaḥ  || 7.1,32.37
vyāpyādhitiṣṭhati śivastato rudra itastataḥ  || 7.1,32.37
jagataḥ pitṛbhūtānāṃ śivo mūrtyātmanāmapi  || 7.1,32.38
pitṛbhāvena sarveṣāṃ pitāmaha udīritaḥ  || 7.1,32.38
nidānajño yathā vaidyo rogasya vinivartakaḥ  || 7.1,32.39
upāyairbheṣajaistadvallayabhogādhikārataḥ  || 7.1,32.39
saṃsārasyeśvaro nityaṃ samūlasya nivartakaḥ  || 7.1,32.40
saṃsāravaidya ityuktaḥ sarvatattvārthavedibhiḥ  || 7.1,32.40
daśārthajñānasiddhyarthamindriyeṣveṣu satsvapi  || 7.1,32.41
trikālabhāvino bhāvānsthūlānsūkṣmānaśeṣataḥ  || 7.1,32.41
aṇavo naiva jānanti māyayaiva malāvṛtāḥ  || 7.1,32.42
asatsvapi ca sarveṣu sarvārthajñānahetuṣu  || 7.1,32.42
538

yadyathāvasthitaṃ vastu tattathaiva sadāśivaḥ  || 7.1,32.43
ayatnenaiva jānāti tasmātsarvajña ucyate  || 7.1,32.43
sarvātmā paramairebhirguṇairnityasamanvayāt || 7.1,32.44
svasmātparātmavirahātparamātmā śivaḥ svayam  || 7.1,32.44
nāmāṣṭakamidaṃ caiva labdhvācāryaprasādataḥ  || 7.1,32.45
nivṛttyādikalāgranthiṃ śivādyaiḥ pañcanāmabhiḥ  || 7.1,32.45
yathāsvaṃ kramaśaśchitvā śodhayitvā yathāguṇam  || 7.1,32.46
guṇitaireva soddhātairaniruddhairathāpi vā  || 7.1,32.46
hṛtkaṇṭhatālubhrūmadhyabrahmarandhrasamanvitām  || 7.1,32.47
chittvā paryaṣṭakākāraṃ svātmānaṃ ca suṣumṇayā  || 7.1,32.47
dvādaśāṃtaḥsthitasyendornītvopari śivaujasi  || 7.1,32.48
saṃhṛtyaṃ vadanaṃ paścādyathāsaṃskaraṇaṃ layāt || 7.1,32.48
śāktenāmṛtavarṣeṇa saṃsiktāyāṃ tanau punaḥ  || 7.1,32.49
avatārya svamātmānamamṛtātmākṛtiṃ hṛdi  || 7.1,32.49
dvādaśāṃtaḥsthitasyendoḥ parastācchvetapaṃkaje  || 7.1,32.50
samāsīnaṃ mahādevaṃ śaṃkarambhaktavatsalam  || 7.1,32.50
ardhanārīśvaraṃ devaṃ nirmalaṃ madhurākṛtim  || 7.1,32.51
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ prasannaṃ śītaladyutim  || 7.1,32.51
dhyātvā hi mānase devaṃ svasthacitto 'tha mānavaḥ  || 7.1,32.52
śivanāmāṣṭakenaiva bhāvapuṣpaissamarcayet || 7.1,32.52
abhyarcanānte tu punaḥ prāṇānāyamya mānavaḥ  || 7.1,32.53
samyakcittaṃ samādhāya śārvaṃ nāmāṣṭakaṃ japet || 7.1,32.53
nābhau cāṣṭāhutīrhutvā pūrṇāhutyā namastataḥ  || 7.1,32.54
aṣṭapuṣpapradānena kṛtvābhyarcanamaṃtimam  || 7.1,32.54
nivedayetsvamātmānaṃ culukodakavartmanā  || 7.1,32.55
evaṃ kṛtvā cirādeva jñānaṃ pāśupataṃ śubham  || 7.1,32.55
labhate tatpratiṣṭhāṃ ca vṛttaṃ cānuttamaṃ tathā  || 7.1,32.56
yogaṃ ca paramaṃ labdhvā mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 7.1,32.56

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe śreṣṭhānuṣṭhānavarṇanaṃ nāma dvātriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 33
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
bhagavañchrotumicchāmo vrataṃ pāśupataṃ param  || 7.1,33.1
brahmādayo 'pi yatkṛtvā sarve pāśupatāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 7.1,33.1
vāyuruvāca
rahasyaṃ vaḥ pravakṣyāmi sarvapāpanikṛntanam  || 7.1,33.2
vrataṃ pāśupataṃ śrautamatharvaśirasi śrutam  || 7.1,33.2
kālaścaitrī paurṇamāsī deśaḥ śivaparigrahaḥ  || 7.1,33.3
kṣetrārāmādyaraṇyaṃ vā praśastaśśubhalakṣaṇaḥ  || 7.1,33.3
tatra pūrvaṃ trayodaśyāṃ susnātaḥ sukṛtāhnikaḥ  || 7.1,33.4
anujñāpya svamācāryaṃ saṃpūjya praṇipatya ca  || 7.1,33.4
pūjāṃ vaiśeṣikīṃ kṛtvā śuklāṃbaradharaḥ svayam  || 7.1,33.5
śuklayajñopavītī ca śuklamālyānulepanaḥ  || 7.1,33.5
darbhāsane samāsīno darbhamuṣṭiṃ pragṛhya ca  || 7.1,33.6
539

prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā prāṅmukho vāpyudaṅmukhaḥ  || 7.1,33.6
dhyātvā devaṃ ca devīṃ ca tadvijñāpanavartmanā  || 7.1,33.6
vratametatkaromīti bhavetsaṃkalpya dīkṣitaḥ  || 7.1,33.7
yāvaccharīrapātaṃ vā dvādaśābdamathāpi vā  || 7.1,33.7
tadardhaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā māsadvādaśakaṃ tu vā  || 7.1,33.8
tadardhaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā māsamekamathāpi vā  || 7.1,33.8
dinadvādaśakaṃ vā 'tha dinaṣaṭkamathāpi vā  || 7.1,33.9
tadardhaṃ dinamekaṃ vā vratasaṃkalpanāvadhi  || 7.1,33.9
agnimādhāya vidhivadvirajāhomakāraṇāt || 7.1,33.10
hutvājyena samidbhiśca caruṇā ca yathākramam  || 7.1,33.10
pūrṇāmāpūrya tāṃ bhūyastattvānāṃ śuddhimuddiśan  || 7.1,33.11
juhuyānmūlamantreṇa taireva samidādibhiḥ  || 7.1,33.11
tattvānyetāni maddehe śuddhyaṃtām 1 tyanusmaran  || 7.1,33.12
pañcabhūtāni tanmātrāḥ pañcakarmendriyāṇi ca  || 7.1,33.12
jñānakarmavibhedena pañcakarmavibhāgaśaḥ  || 7.1,33.13
tvagādidhātavassapta pañca prāṇādivāyavaḥ  || 7.1,33.13
manobuddhirahaṃ khyātirguṇāḥ prakṛtipūruṣau  || 7.1,33.14
rāgo vidyākale caiva niyatiḥ kāla eva ca  || 7.1,33.14
māyā ca śuddhividyā ca maheśvarasadāśivau  || 7.1,33.15
śaktiśca śivatattvaṃ ca tattvāni kramaśo viduḥ  || 7.1,33.15
mantraistu virajairhutvā hotāsau virajā bhavet || 7.1,33.16
śivānugrahamāsādya jñānavānsa hi jāyate  || 7.1,33.16
atha gomayamādāya piṇḍīkṛtyābhimaṃtrya ca  || 7.1,33.17
vinyasyāgnau ca samprokṣya dine tasminhaviṣyabhuk || 7.1,33.17
prabhāte tu caturdaśyāṃ kṛtvā sarvaṃ puroditam  || 7.1,33.18
dine tasminnirāhāraḥ kālaṃ śeṣaṃ samāpayet || 7.1,33.18
prātaḥ parvaṇi cāpyevaṃ kṛtvā homā vasānataḥ  || 7.1,33.19
upasaṃhṛtya rudrāgniṃ gṛhṇīyādbhasma yatnataḥ  || 7.1,33.19
tataśca jaṭilo muṇḍī śikhaikajaṭa eva vā  || 7.1,33.20
bhūtvā snātvā tato vītalajjaścetsyāddigambaraḥ  || 7.1,33.20
api kāṣāyavasanaścarmacīrāmbaro 'tha vā  || 7.1,33.21
ekāmbaro valkalī vā bhaveddaṇḍī ca mekhalī  || 7.1,33.21
prakṣālya caraṇau paścāddvirācamyātmanastanum  || 7.1,33.22
saṃkulīkṛtya tadbhasma virajānalasaṃbhavam  || 7.1,33.22
agnirityādibhirmaṃtraiḥ ṣaḍbhirātharvaṇaiḥ kramāt || 7.1,33.23
vibhṛjyāṃgāni mūrdhādicaraṇāṃtāni taisspṛśet || 7.1,33.23
tatastena krameṇaiva samuddhṛtya ca bhasmanā  || 7.1,33.24
sarvāṃgoddhūlanaṃ kuryātpraṇavena śivena vā  || 7.1,33.24
tatastripuṇḍraṃ racayettriyāyuṣasamāhvayam  || 7.1,33.25
śivabhāvaṃ samāgamya śivayogamathācaret || 7.1,33.25
kuryātstrisandhyamapyevametatpāśupataṃ vratam  || 7.1,33.26
bhuktimuktipradaṃ caitatpaśutvaṃ vinivartayet || 7.1,33.26
tatpaśutvaṃ parityajya kṛtvā pāśupataṃ vratam  || 7.1,33.27
pūjanīyo mahādevo liṃgamūrtissanātanaḥ  || 7.1,33.27
padmamaṣṭadalaṃ haimaṃ navaratnairalaṃkṛtam  || 7.1,33.28
karṇikākeśaropetamāsanaṃ parikalpayet || 7.1,33.28
vibhave tadabhāve tu raktaṃ sitamathāpi vā  || 7.1,33.29
padmaṃ tasyāpyabhāve tu kevalaṃ bhāvanāmayam  || 7.1,33.29
tatpadmakarṇikāmadhye kṛtvā liṃgaṃ kanīyasam  || 7.1,33.30
sphīṭikaṃ pīṭhikopetaṃ pūjayedvidhivatkramāt || 7.1,33.30
pratiṣṭhāpya vidhānena talliṃgaṃ kṛtaśodhanam  || 7.1,33.31
parikalpyāsanaṃ mūrtiṃ pañcavaktraprakārataḥ  || 7.1,33.31
pañcagavyādibhiḥ pūrṇairyathāvibhavasaṃbhṛtaiḥ  || 7.1,33.32
snāpayetkalaśaiḥ pūrṇairaṣṭāpadasamudbhavaiḥ  || 7.1,33.32
gaṃdhadravyaissakarpūraiścandanādyaissakuṃkumaiḥ  || 7.1,33.33
savedikaṃ samālipya liṃgaṃ bhūṣaṇabhūṣitam  || 7.1,33.33
bilvapatraiśca padmaiśca raktaiḥ śvetaistathotpalaiḥ  || 7.1,33.34
nīlotpalaistathānyaiśca puṣpaistaistaissugaṃdhibhiḥ  || 7.1,33.34
puṇyaiḥ praśastaiḥ patraiśca citrairdūrvākṣatādibhiḥ  || 7.1,33.35
samabhyarcya yathālābhaṃ mahāpūjāvidhānataḥ  || 7.1,33.35
dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ tathā cāpi naivedyaṃ ca samādiśet || 7.1,33.36
nivedayitvā vibhave kalyāṇaṃ ca samācaret || 7.1,33.36
iṣṭāni ca viśiṣṭāni nyāyenopārjitāni ca  || 7.1,33.37
sarvadravyāṇi deyāni vrate tasminviśeṣataḥ  || 7.1,33.37
śrīpatrotpalapadmānāṃ saṃkhyā sāhasrikī matā  || 7.1,33.38
pratyekamaparā saṃkhyā śatamaṣṭottaraṃ dvijāḥ  || 7.1,33.38
tatrāpi ca viśeṣeṇa na tyajedbilvapatrakam  || 7.1,33.39
haimamekaṃ paraṃ prāhuḥ padmaṃ padmasahasrakāt || 7.1,33.39
nīlotpalādiṣvapyetatsamānaṃ bilbapatrakaiḥ  || 7.1,33.40
puṣpāntare na niyamo yathālābhaṃ nivedayet || 7.1,33.40
aṣṭāṅgamarghyamutkṛṣṭaṃ dhūpālepau viśeṣataḥ  || 7.1,33.41
candanaṃ vāmadevākhye haritālaṃ ca pauruṣe  || 7.1,33.41
īśāne bhasitaṃ kecidālepanamitīdṛśām  || 7.1,33.42
na dhūpamiti manyante dhūpāntaravidhānataḥ  || 7.1,33.42
sitāgurumaghorākhye mukhe kṛṣṇāguruṃ punaḥ  || 7.1,33.42
pauruṣe guggulaṃ savye saumye saugaṃdhikaṃ mukhe  || 7.1,33.43
īśāne 'pi hyuśīrādi deyāddhūpaṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 7.1,33.43
śarkarāmadhukarpūrakapilāghṛtasaṃyutam  || 7.1,33.44
caṃdanāgurukāṣṭhādyaṃ sāmānyaṃ saṃpracakṣate  || 7.1,33.44
karpūravartirājyāḍhyā deyā dīpāvalistataḥ  || 7.1,33.45
arghyamācamanaṃ deyaṃ prativaktramataḥ param  || 7.1,33.45
prathamāvaraṇe pūjyo kramāddherambaṣaṇmukhau  || 7.1,33.46
brahmāṃgāni tataścaiva prathamāvaraṇercite  || 7.1,33.46
dvitīyāvaraṇe pūjyā vighneśāścakravartinaḥ  || 7.1,33.47
tṛtīyāvaraṇe pūjyā bhavādyā aṣṭamūrtayaḥ  || 7.1,33.47
540

mahādevādayastatra tathaikādaśamūrtayaḥ  || 7.1,33.48
caturthāvaraṇe pūjyāḥ sarva eva gaṇeśvarāḥ  || 7.1,33.48
bahireva tu padmasya pañcamāvaraṇe kramāt || 7.1,33.49
daśadikpatayaḥ pūjyāḥ sāstrāḥ sānucarāstathā  || 7.1,33.49
brahmaṇo mānasāḥ putrāḥ sarve 'pi jyotiṣāṃ gaṇāḥ  || 7.1,33.50
sarvā devyaśca devāśca sarve sarve ca khecarāḥ  || 7.1,33.50
pātālavāsinaścānye sarve munigaṇā api  || 7.1,33.51
yogino hi sakhāssarve pataṃgā mātarastathā  || 7.1,33.51
kṣetrapālāśca sagaṇāḥ sarvaṃ caitaccarācaram  || 7.1,33.52
pūjanīyaṃ śivaprītyā mattvā śaṃbhuvibhūtimat || 7.1,33.52
athāvaraṇapūjāṃte saṃpūjya parameśvaram  || 7.1,33.53
sājyaṃ savyaṃ janaṃ hṛdyaṃ havirbhaktyā nivedayet || 7.1,33.53
mukhavāsādikaṃ dattvā tāmbūlaṃ sopadaṃśakam  || 7.1,33.54
alaṃkṛtya ca bhūyo 'pi nānāpuṣpavibhūṣaṇaiḥ  || 7.1,33.54
nīrājanāṃte vistīrya pūjāśeṣaṃ samāpayet || 7.1,33.55
caṣakaṃ sopakāraṃ ca śayanaṃ ca samarpayet || 7.1,33.55
candrasaṃkāśahāraṃ ca śayanīyaṃ samarpayet || 7.1,33.56
ādyaṃ nṛpocitaṃ hṛdyaṃ tatsarvamanurūpataḥ  || 7.1,33.56
kṛtvā ca kārayitvā ca hitvā ca pratipūjanam  || 7.1,33.57
stotraṃ vyapohanaṃ japtvā vidyāṃ pañcākṣarīṃ japet || 7.1,33.57
pradakṣiṇāṃ praṇāmaṃ ca kṛtvātmānaṃ samarpayet || 7.1,33.58
tataḥ purastāddevasya guruviprau ca pūjayet || 7.1,33.58
dattvārghyamaṣṭau puṣpāṇi devamudvāsya liṃgataḥ  || 7.1,33.59
agneścāgniṃ susaṃyamya hyudvāsya ca tamapyuta  || 7.1,33.59
pratyahaṃ ca janastvevaṃ kuryātsevāṃ puroditām  || 7.1,33.60
tatastatsāmbujaṃ liṃgaṃ sarvopakaraṇānvitam  || 7.1,33.60
samarpayetsvagurave sthāpayedvā śivālaye  || 7.1,33.61
saṃpūjya ca gurūnviprānvratinaśca viśeṣataḥ  || 7.1,33.61
bhaktāndvijāṃśca śaktaśceddīnānāthāṃśca toṣayet || 7.1,33.62
svayaṃ cānaśane śaktaḥ phalamūlāśane 'tha vā  || 7.1,33.62
payovrato vā bhikṣāśī bhavedekāśanastathā  || 7.1,33.63
naktaṃ yuktāśano nityaṃ bhūśayyānirataḥ śuciḥ  || 7.1,33.63
bhasmaśāyī tṛṇeśāyī cīrājinadhṛto 'thavā  || 7.1,33.64
brahmacaryavrato nityaṃ vratametatsamācaret || 7.1,33.64
arkavāre tathārdrāyāṃ pañcadaśyāṃ ca pakṣayoḥ  || 7.1,33.65
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ śaktastūpavasedapi  || 7.1,33.65
pākhaṇḍipatitodakyāssūtakāntyajapūrvakān  || 7.1,33.66
varjayetsarvayatnena manasā karmaṇā girā  || 7.1,33.66
kṣamadānadayāsatyāhiṃsāśīlaḥ sadā bhavet || 7.1,33.67
saṃtuṣṭaśca praśāntaśca japadhyānaratastathā  || 7.1,33.67
kuryāttriṣavaṇasnānaṃ bhasmasnānamathāpi vā  || 7.1,33.68
pūjāṃ vaiśeṣikīṃ caiva manasā vacasā girā  || 7.1,33.68
bahunātra kimuktena nācaredaśivaṃ vratī  || 7.1,33.69
pramādāttu tathācāre nirūpya gurulāghave  || 7.1,33.69
540

ucitāṃ niṣkṛtiṃ kuryātpūjāhomajapādibhiḥ  || 7.1,33.70
āsamāptervratasyaivamācarenna pramādataḥ  || 7.1,33.70
godānaṃ ca vṛṣotsargaṃ kuryātpūjāṃ ca saṃpadā  || 7.1,33.71
bhaktaśca śivaprītyarthaṃ sarvakāmavivarjitaḥ  || 7.1,33.71
sāmānyametatkathitaṃ vratasyāsya samāsataḥ  || 7.1,33.72
pratimāsaṃ viśeṣaṃ ca pravadāmi yathāśrutam  || 7.1,33.72
vaiśākhe vajraliṃgaṃ tu jyeṣṭhe mārakataṃ śubham  || 7.1,33.73
āṣāḍhe mauktikaṃ vidyācchrāvaṇe nīlanirmitam  || 7.1,33.73
māse bhādrapade caiva padmarāgamayaṃ param  || 7.1,33.74
āśvine māsi vidyādvai liṃgaṃ gomedakaṃ varam  || 7.1,33.74
kārtikyāṃ vaidrumaṃ liṃgaṃ vaidūryaṃ mārgaśīrṣake  || 7.1,33.75
puṣparāgamayaṃ pauṣe māghe dyumaṇijantathā  || 7.1,33.75
phālguṇe candrakāntotthaṃ caitre tadvyatyayo 'thavā  || 7.1,33.76
sarvamāseṣu ratnānāmalābhe haimameva vā  || 7.1,33.76
haimābhāve rājataṃ vā tāmrajaṃ śailajantathā  || 7.1,33.77
mṛnmayaṃ vā yathālābhaṃ jātuṣaṃ cānyadeva vā  || 7.1,33.77
sarvagaṃdhamayaṃ vātha liṃgaṃ kuryādyathāruci  || 7.1,33.78
vratāvasānasamaye samācaritanityakaḥ  || 7.1,33.78
kṛtvā vaiśeṣikīṃ pūjāṃ hutvā caiva yathā purā  || 7.1,33.79
saṃpūjya ca tathācāryaṃ vratinaśca viśeṣataḥ  || 7.1,33.79
deśikenāpyanujñātaḥ prāṅmukho vāpyudaṅmukhaḥ  || 7.1,33.80
darbhāsano darbhapāṇiḥ prāṇāpānau niyamya ca  || 7.1,33.80
japitvā śaktito mūlaṃ dhyātvā sāmbaṃ triyambakam  || 7.1,33.81
anujñāpya yathāpūrvaṃ namaskṛtya kṛtāñjaliḥ  || 7.1,33.81
samutsṛjāmi bhagavanvratametattvadājñayā  || 7.1,33.82
ityuktvā liṃgamūlasthāndarbhānuttaratastyajet || 7.1,33.82
tato daṇḍajaṭācīramekhalā api cotsṛjet || 7.1,33.83
punarācamya vidhivatpañcākṣaramudīrayet || 7.1,33.83
yaḥ kṛtvātyaṃtikīṃ dīkṣāmādehāntamanākulaḥ  || 7.1,33.84
vratametatprakurvīta sa tu vai naiṣṭhikaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.1,33.84
so 'tyāśramī ca vijñeyo mahāpāśupatastathā  || 7.1,33.85
sa eva tapatāṃ śreṣṭha sa eva ca mahāvratī  || 7.1,33.85
na tena sadṛśaḥ kaścitkṛtakṛtyo mumukṣuṣu  || 7.1,33.86
yo yatirnaiṣṭhiko jātastamāhurnaiṣṭhikottamam  || 7.1,33.86
yo 'nvahaṃ dvādaśāhaṃ vā vratametatsamācaret || 7.1,33.87
so 'pi naiṣṭhikatulyaḥ syāttīvravratasamanvayāt || 7.1,33.87
ghṛtākto yaścaredetadvrataṃ vrataparāyaṇaḥ  || 7.1,33.88
dvitraikadivasaṃ vāpi sa ca kaścana naiṣṭhikaḥ  || 7.1,33.88
kṛtyamityeva niṣkāmo yaścaredvratamuttamam  || 7.1,33.89
śivārpitātmā satataṃ na tena sadṛśaḥ kvacit || 7.1,33.89
bhasmacchanno dvijo vidvānmahāpātakasaṃbhavaiḥ  || 7.1,33.90
pāpaissudāruṇaissadyo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 7.1,33.90
rudrāgniryatparaṃ vīryantadbhasma parikīrtitam  || 7.1,33.91
tasmātsarveṣu kāleṣu vīryavānbhasmasaṃyutaḥ  || 7.1,33.91
541

bhasmaniṣṭhasya naśyanti deṣā bhasmāgnisaṃgamāt || 7.1,33.92
bhasmasnānaviśuddhātmā bhasmaniṣṭha iti smṛtaḥ  || 7.1,33.92
bhasmanā digdhasarvāṃgo bhasmadīptatripuṃḍrakaḥ  || 7.1,33.93
bhasmasnāyī ca puruṣo bhasmaniṣṭha iti smṛtaḥ  || 7.1,33.93
bhūtapretapiśāsāśca rogāścātīva dussahāḥ  || 7.1,33.94
bhasmaniṣṭhasya sānnidhyādvidravaṃti na saṃśayaḥ  || 7.1,33.94
bhāsanādbhāsitaṃ proktaṃ bhasma kalmaṣabhakṣaṇāt || 7.1,33.95
bhūtibhūtikarī caiva rakṣā rakṣākarī param  || 7.1,33.95
kimanyadiha vaktavyaṃ bhasmamāhātmyakāraṇam  || 7.1,33.96
vratī ca bhasmanā snātassvayaṃ devo maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,33.96
paramāstraṃ ca śaivānāṃ bhasmaitatpārameśvaram  || 7.1,33.97
dhaumyāgrajasya tapasi vyāpado yannivāritāḥ  || 7.1,33.97
tasmātsarvaprayatnena kṛtvā pāśupatavratam  || 7.1,33.98
dhanavadbhasma saṃgṛhya bhasmasnānarato bhavet || 7.1,33.98

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe paśupativratavidhānavarṇanaṃ nāma trayastriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 34
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
dhaumyāgrajena śuśunā kṣīrārthaṃ hi tapaḥ kṛtam  || 7.1,34.1
tasmātkṣīrārṇavo dattastasmai devena śūlinā  || 7.1,34.1
sa kathaṃ śiśuko lebhe śivaśāstrapravaktṛtām  || 7.1,34.2
kathaṃ vā śivasadbhāvaṃ jñātvā tapasi niṣṭhitaḥ  || 7.1,34.2
kathaṃ ca labdhavijñānastapaścaraṇaparvaṇi  || 7.1,34.3
rudrāgneryatparaṃ vīryaṃ labhe bhasma svarakṣakam  || 7.1,34.3
vāyuruvāca
na hyeṣa śiśukaḥ kaścitprākṛtaḥ kṛtavāṃstapaḥ  || 7.1,34.4
munivaryasya tanayo vyāghrapādasya dhīmataḥ  || 7.1,34.4
janmāntareṇa saṃsiddhaḥ kenāpi khalu hetunā  || 7.1,34.5
svapadapracyuto diṣṭyā prāpto munikumāratām  || 7.1,34.5
mahādevaprasādasya bhāgyāpannasya bhāvinaḥ  || 7.1,34.6
dugdhābhilāṣaprabhavadvāratāmagamattapaḥ  || 7.1,34.6
ataḥ sarvagaṇeśatvaṃ kumāratvaṃ ca śāśvatam  || 7.1,34.7
saha dugdhābdhinā tasmai pradadau śaṃkaraḥ svayam  || 7.1,34.7
tasya jñānāgamopyasya prasādādeva śāṃkarāt || 7.1,34.8
kaumāraṃ hi paraṃ sākṣājjñānaṃ śaktimayaṃ viduḥ  || 7.1,34.8
śivaśāstrapravaktṛtvamapi tasya hi tatkṛtam  || 7.1,34.9
kumāro munito labdhajñānābdhiriva nandanaḥ  || 7.1,34.9
dṛṣṭaṃ tu kāraṇaṃ tasya śivajñānasamanvaye  || 7.1,34.10
svamātṛvacanaṃ sākṣācchokajaṃ kṣīrakāraṇāt || 7.1,34.10
kadācitkṣīramatyalpaṃ pītavānmātulāśrame  || 7.1,34.11
īrṣayayā mātulasutaṃ saṃtṛptakṣīramuttamam  || 7.1,34.11
pītvā sthitaṃ yathākāmaṃ dṛṣṭvā vai mātulātmajam  || 7.1,34.12
upamanyurvyāghrapādiḥ prītyā provāca mātaram  || 7.1,34.12
upamanyuruvāca
mātarmātarmahābhāge mama dehi tapasvini  || 7.1,34.13
541

gavyaṃ kṣīramatisvādu nālpamuṣṇaṃ pibāmyaham  || 7.1,34.13
vāyuruvāca
tacchrutvā putravacanaṃ tanmātā ca tapasvinī  || 7.1,34.14
vyāghrapādasya mahiṣī duḥkhamāpattadā ca sā  || 7.1,34.14
upalālyātha suprītyā putramāliṃgya sādaram  || 7.1,34.15
duḥkhitā vilalāpātha smṛtvā nairdhanyamātmanaḥ  || 7.1,34.15
smṛtvāsmṛtvā punaḥ kṣīramupamanyussa bālakaḥ  || 7.1,34.16
dehi dehīti tāmāha rudranbhūyo mahādyutiḥ  || 7.1,34.16
taddhaṭhaṃ sā parijñāya dvijapatnī tapasvinī  || 7.1,34.17
śāntaye taddhaṭhasyātha śubhopāyamarīracat || 7.1,34.17
uñchavṛttyārjitānbījānsvayaṃ dṛṣṭvā ca sā tadā  || 7.1,34.18
bījapiṣṭamathāloḍya toyena kalabhāṣiṇī  || 7.1,34.18
ehyehi mama putreti sāmapūrvaṃ tatassutam  || 7.1,34.19
āliṃgyādāya duḥkhārtā pradadau kṛtrimaṃ payaḥ  || 7.1,34.19
pītvā ca kṛtrimaṃ kṣīraṃ mātrāṃ dattaṃ sa bālakaḥ  || 7.1,34.20
naitatkṣīramiti prāha mātaraṃ cātivihvalaḥ  || 7.1,34.20
duḥkhitā sā tadā prāha saṃprekṣyāghrāya mūrdhani  || 7.1,34.21
samārjya netra putrasya karābhyāṃ kamalāyate  || 7.1,34.21
jananyuvāca
taṭinī ratnapūrṇāstāssvargapātālagocarāḥ  || 7.1,34.22
bhāgyahīnā na paśyanti bhaktihīnāśca ye śive  || 7.1,34.22
rājyaṃ svargaṃ ca mokṣaṃ ca bhojanaṃ kṣīrasaṃbhavam  || 7.1,34.23
na labhante priyāṇyeṣāṃ na tuṣyati yadā śivaḥ  || 7.1,34.23
bhavaprasādajaṃ sarvaṃ nānyaddevaprasādajam  || 7.1,34.24
anyadeveṣu niratā duḥkhārtā vibhramanti ca  || 7.1,34.24
kṣīraṃ tatra kuto 'smākaṃ vane nivasatāṃ sadā  || 7.1,34.25
kva dugdhasādhanaṃ vatsa kva vayaṃ vanavāsinaḥ  || 7.1,34.25
kṛtsnābhāvena dāridryānmayā te bhāgyahīnayā  || 7.1,34.26
mithyādugdhamidaṃ dattampiṣṭamāloḍya vāriṇā  || 7.1,34.26
tvaṃ mātulagṛhe svalpaṃ pītvā svādu payaḥ śṛtam  || 7.1,34.27
jñātvā svādu tvayā pītaṃ tajjātīyamanusmaran  || 7.1,34.27
dattaṃ na paya ityuktvā rudan duḥkhīkaroṣi mām  || 7.1,34.28
prasādena vinā śaṃbho payastava na vidyate  || 7.1,34.28
pādapaṃkajayostasya sāmbasya sagaṇasya ca  || 7.1,34.29
bhaktyā samarpitaṃ yattatkāraṇaṃ sarvasampadām  || 7.1,34.29
adhunā vasudosmābhirmahādevo na pūjitaḥ  || 7.1,34.30
sakāmānāṃ yathākāmaṃ yathoktaphaladāyakaḥ  || 7.1,34.30
dhanānyuddiśya nāsmābhiritaḥ prāgarcitaḥ śivaḥ  || 7.1,34.31
ato daridrāssaṃjātā vayaṃ tasmānna te payaḥ  || 7.1,34.31
pūrvajanmani yaddattaṃ śivamuddiśya vai sutaḥ  || 7.1,34.32
tadeva labhyate nānyadviṣṇumuddiśya vā prabhum  || 7.1,34.32
vāyuruvāca
iti mātṛvacaḥ śrutvā tathyaṃ śokādisūcakam  || 7.1,34.33
bālo 'pyanutapannaṃtaḥ pragalbhamidamabravīt || 7.1,34.33
542

upamanyuruvāca
śokenālamito mataḥ sāṃbo yadyasti śaṃkaraḥ  || 7.1,34.34
tyaja śokaṃ mahābhāge sarvaṃ bhadraṃ bhaviṣyati  || 7.1,34.34
śṛṇu mātarvaco medya mahādevo 'sti cetkvacit || 7.1,34.35
cirādvā hyacirādvāpi kṣīrodaṃ sādhayāmyaham  || 7.1,34.35
vāyuruvāca
iti śrutvā vacastasya bālakasya mahāmateḥ  || 7.1,34.36
praytuvāca tadā mātā suprasannā manasvinī  || 7.1,34.36
mātovāca
śubhaṃ vicāritaṃ tāta tvayā matprītivardhanam  || 7.1,34.37
vilaṃbaṃ mā kathāstvaṃ hi bhaja sāṃbaṃ sadāśivam  || 7.1,34.37
sarvasmādadhiko 'styeva śivaḥ paramakāraṇam  || 7.1,34.38
tatkṛtaṃ hi jagatsarvaṃ brahmādyāstasya kiṃkarāḥ  || 7.1,34.38
tatprasādakṛtaiśvaryā dāsāstasya vayaṃ prabhoḥ  || 7.1,34.39
taṃ vinānyaṃ na jānīmaśśaṃkaraṃ lokaśaṃkaram  || 7.1,34.39
anyāndevānparityajya karmaṇā manasā girā  || 7.1,34.40
tameva sāṃbaṃ sagaṇaṃ bhaja bhāvapurassaram  || 7.1,34.40
tasya devādhidevasya śivasya varadāyinaḥ  || 7.1,34.41
sākṣānnamaśśivāyeti maṃtro 'yaṃ vācakaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.1,34.41
saptakoṭimahāmaṃtrāḥ sarve sapraṇavāḥ pare  || 7.1,34.42
tasminneva vilīyaṃte punastasmādvinirgatāḥ  || 7.1,34.42
saprasādāśca te maṃtrāḥ svādhikārādyapekṣayā  || 7.1,34.43
sarvādhikārastveko 'yaṃ maṃtra eveśvarājñayā  || 7.1,34.43
yathā nikṛṣṭānutkṛṣṭānsarvānapyātmanaḥ śivaḥ  || 7.1,34.44
kṣamate rakṣituṃ tadvanmaṃtro 'yamapi sarvadā  || 7.1,34.44
prabalaśca tathā hyeṣa maṃtro mantrāntarādapi  || 7.1,34.45
sarvarakṣākṣamo 'pyeṣa nāparaḥ kaścidiṣyate  || 7.1,34.45
tasmānmantrāntarāṃstyaktvā pañcākṣaraparo bhava  || 7.1,34.46
tasmiñjihvāṃtaragate na kiṃcidiha durlabham  || 7.1,34.46
adhorāstraṃ ca śaivānāṃ rakṣāheturanuttamam  || 7.1,34.47
tacca tatprabhavaṃ matvā tatparo bhava nānyathā  || 7.1,34.47
bhasmedantu mayā labdhaṃ pitureva tavottamam  || 7.1,34.48
virajānalasaṃsiddhaṃ mahāvyāpannivāraṇam  || 7.1,34.48
maṃtraṃ ca te mayā dattaṃ gṛhāṇa madanujñayā  || 7.1,34.49
anenaivāśu japtena rakṣā tava bhaviṣyati  || 7.1,34.49
vāyuruvāca
evaṃ mātrā samādiśya śivamastvityudīrya ca  || 7.1,34.50
visṛṣṭastadvaco mūrdhni kurvanneva tadā muniḥ  || 7.1,34.50
tāṃ praṇamyaivamuktvā ca tapaḥ kartuṃ pracakrame  || 7.1,34.51
tamāha ca tadā mātā śubhaṃ kurvaṃtu te surāḥ  || 7.1,34.51
anujñātastayā tatra tapastepe sa duścaram  || 7.1,34.52
himavatparvataṃ prāpya vāyubhakṣaḥ samāhitaḥ  || 7.1,34.52
aṣṭeṣṭakābhiḥ prasādaṃ kṛtvā liṃgaṃ ca mṛnmayam  || 7.1,34.53
tatrāvāhya mahādevaṃ sāṃbaṃ sagaṇamavyayam  || 7.1,34.53
542

bhaktyā pañcākṣareṇaiva putraiḥ puṣpairvanodbhavaiḥ  || 7.1,34.54
samabhyarcya ciraṃ kālaṃ cacāra paramaṃ tapaḥ  || 7.1,34.54
tatastapaścarattaṃ taṃ bālamekākinaṃ kṛśam  || 7.1,34.55
upamanyuṃ dvijavaraṃ śivasaṃsaktamānasam  || 7.1,34.55
purā marīcinā śaptāḥ kecinmunipiśācakāḥ  || 7.1,34.56
saṃpīḍya rākṣasairbhāvaistapasovighnamācaran  || 7.1,34.56
sa ca taiḥ pīḍyamāno 'pi tapaḥ kurvankathañcana  || 7.1,34.57
sadā namaḥ śivāyeti krośati smārtanādavat || 7.1,34.57
tannādaśravaṇādeva tapaso vighnakāriṇaḥ  || 7.1,34.58
te taṃ bālaṃ samutsṛjya munayassamupācaran  || 7.1,34.58
tapasā tasya viprasya copamanyormahātmanaḥ  || 7.1,34.59
carācaraṃ ca munayaḥ pradīpitamabhūjjagat || 7.1,34.59
iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe upamanyutapovarṇanaṃ nāma catustriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 35
vāyuruvāca
atha sarve pradīptāṃgā vaikuṇṭhaṃ prayayurdrutam  || 7.1,35.1
praṇamyāhuśca tatsarvaṃ haraye devasattamāḥ  || 7.1,35.1
śrutvā teṣāṃ tadā vākyaṃ bhagavānpuruṣottamaḥ  || 7.1,35.2
kimidantviti saṃcintya jñātvā tatkāraṇaṃ ca saḥ  || 7.1,35.2
jagāma mandaraṃ tūrṇaṃ maheśvaradidṛkṣayā  || 7.1,35.3
dṛṣṭvā devaṃ praṇamyaivaṃ provāca sukṛtāṃjaliḥ  || 7.1,35.3
viṣṇuruvāca
bhagavanbrāhmaṇaḥ kaścidupamanyuriti śrutaḥ  || 7.1,35.4
kṣīrārthamadahatsarvaṃ tapasā tannivāraya  || 7.1,35.4
vāyuruvāca
iti śrutvā vaco viṣṇoḥ prāha devo maheśvaraḥ  || 7.1,35.5
śiśuṃ nivārayiṣyāmi tattvaṃ gaccha svamāśramam  || 7.1,35.5
tacchrutvā śaṃbhuvacanaṃ sa viṣṇurdevavallabhaḥ  || 7.1,35.6
jagāmāśvāsya tānsarvānsvalokamamarādikān  || 7.1,35.6
etasminnaṃtare devaḥ pinākī parameśvaraḥ  || 7.1,35.7
śakrasya rūpamāsthāya gantuṃ cakre matiṃ tataḥ  || 7.1,35.7
atha jagāma munestu tapovanaṃ gajavareṇa sitena sadāśivaḥ  || 7.1,35.8
saha surāsurasiddhamahoragairamararājatanuṃ svayamāsthitaḥ  || 7.1,35.8
sa vāraṇaścāru tadā vibhuṃ taṃ nivījya vālavyajanena divyam  || 7.1,35.9
dadhāra śacyā sahitaṃ sureṃdraṃ kareṇa vāmena śitātapatram  || 7.1,35.9
rarāja bhagavānsomaḥ śakrarūpī sadāśivaḥ  || 7.1,35.10
tenātapatreṇa yathā candrabiṃbena mandaraḥ  || 7.1,35.10
āsthāyaivaṃ hi śakrasya svarūpaṃ parameśvaraḥ  || 7.1,35.11
jagāmānugrahaṃ kartumupamanyostadāśramam  || 7.1,35.11
taṃ dṛṣṭvā parameśānaṃ śakrarūpadharaṃ śivam  || 7.1,35.12
praṇamya śirasā prāha mahāmunivaraḥ svayam  || 7.1,35.12
upamanyuruvāca
pāvitaścāśramasso 'yaṃ mama deveśvara svayam  || 7.1,35.13
prāpto yattvaṃ jagannātha bhagavandevasattama  || 7.1,35.13
vāyuruvāca
evamuktvā sthitaṃ prekṣya kṛtāṃjalipuṭaṃ dvijam  || 7.1,35.14
543

prāha gaṃbhīrayā vācā śakrarūpadharo haraḥ  || 7.1,35.14
śakra uvāca
tuṣṭo 'smi te varaṃ brūhi tapasānena suvrata  || 7.1,35.15
dadāmi cepsitānsarvāndhaumyāgraja mahāmune  || 7.1,35.15
vāyuruvāca
evamuktastadā tena śakreṇa munipuṃgavaḥ  || 7.1,35.16
vārayāmi śive bhaktimityuvāca kṛtāñjaliḥ  || 7.1,35.16
tanniśamya hariḥ 1 prāha māṃ na jānāsi lekhapam  || 7.1,35.17
trailokyādhipatiṃ śakraṃ sarvadevanamaskṛtam  || 7.1,35.17
madbhakto bhava viprarṣe māmevārcaya sarvadā  || 7.1,35.18
dadāmi sarvaṃ bhadraṃ te tyaja rudraṃ ca nirguṇam  || 7.1,35.18
rudreṇa nirguṇenāpi kiṃ te kāryaṃ bhaviṣyati  || 7.1,35.19
devapaṅktibahirbhūto yaḥ piśācatvamāgataḥ  || 7.1,35.19
vāyuruvāca
tacchrutvā prāha sa munirjapanpañcākṣaraṃ manum  || 7.1,35.20
manyamāno dharmavighnaṃ prāha taṃ kartumāgatam  || 7.1,35.20
upamanyuruvāca
tvayaivaṃ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ bhavaniṃdāratena vai  || 7.1,35.21
prasaṃgādeva devasya nirguṇatvaṃ mahātmanaḥ  || 7.1,35.21
tvaṃ na jānāmi vai rudraṃ sarvadeveśvareśvaram  || 7.1,35.22
brahmaviṣṇumaheśānāṃ janaka prakṛteḥ param  || 7.1,35.22
sadasadvyaktamavyaktaṃ yamāhurbrahmavādinaḥ  || 7.1,35.23
nityamekamanekaṃ ca varaṃ tasmādvṛṇomyaham  || 7.1,35.23
hetuvādavinirmuktaṃ sāṃkhyayogārthadamparam  || 7.1,35.24
upāsate yaṃ tattvajñā varaṃ tasmādvṛṇomyaham  || 7.1,35.24
nāsti śaṃbhoḥ paraṃ tattvaṃ sarvakāraṇakāraṇāt || 7.1,35.25
brahmaviṣṇvādidevānāṃ sraṣṭurguṇaparādvibhoḥ  || 7.1,35.25
bahunātra kimuktena mayādyānumitaṃ mahat || 7.1,35.26
bhavāṃtare kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ śrutā nindā bhavasya cet || 7.1,35.26
śrutvā niṃdāṃ bhavasyātha tatkṣaṇādeva santyajet || 7.1,35.27
svadehaṃ tannihatyāśu śivalokaṃ sa gacchati  || 7.1,35.27
āstāṃ tāvanmameccheyaṃ kṣīraṃ prati surādhama  || 7.1,35.28
nihatya tvāṃ śivāstreṇa tyajāmyetaṃ kalevaram  || 7.1,35.28
vāyuruvāca
evamuktvopamanyustaṃ martuṃ vyavasitassvayam  || 7.1,35.29
kṣīre vāñchāmapi tyaktvā nihantuṃ śakramudyataḥ  || 7.1,35.29
bhasmādāya tadā ghoramaghorāstrābhimaṃtritam  || 7.1,35.30
visṛjya śakramuddiśya nanāda sa munistadā  || 7.1,35.30
smṛtvā śaṃbhupadadvaṃdvaṃ svadehaṃ dugdhumudyataḥ  || 7.1,35.31
āgneyīṃ dhāraṇāṃ bibhradupamanyuravasthitaḥ  || 7.1,35.31
evaṃ vyavasite vipre bhagavānbhaganetrahā  || 7.1,35.32
vārayāmāsa saumyena dhāraṇāṃ tasya yoginaḥ  || 7.1,35.32
tadvisṛṣṭamaghorāstraṃ naṃdīśvaraniyogataḥ  || 7.1,35.33
jagṛhe madhyataḥ kṣiptaṃ nandī śaṃkaravallabhaḥ  || 7.1,35.33

1 indraḥ

543

svaṃ rūpameva bhagavānāsthāya parameśvaraḥ  || 7.1,35.34
darśayāmāsa śiprāya bālendukṛtaśekharam  || 7.1,35.34
kṣīrārṇavasahasraṃ ca pīyūṣārṇavameva vā  || 7.1,35.35
dadhyāderarṇavāṃścaiva ghṛtodārṇavameva ca  || 7.1,35.35
phalārṇavaṃ ca bālasya bhakṣya bhojyārṇavaṃ tathā  || 7.1,35.36
apūpānāṃ giriṃ caiva darśayāmāsa sa prabhuḥ  || 7.1,35.36
evaṃ sa dadṛśe devo devyā sārdhaṃ vṛṣopari  || 7.1,35.37
gaṇeśvaraistriśūlādyairdivyāstrairapi saṃvṛtaḥ  || 7.1,35.37
divi duṃdubhayo neduḥ puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ papāta ca  || 7.1,35.38
viṣṇubrahmendrapramukhairdevaiśchannā diśo daśa  || 7.1,35.38
athopamanyurānandasamudrormibhirāvṛtaḥ  || 7.1,35.39
papāta daṇḍavadbhūmau bhaktinamreṇa cetasā  || 7.1,35.39
etasminsamaye tatra sasmito bhagavānbhavaḥ  || 7.1,35.40
ehyehīti tamāhūya mūrdhnyāghrāya dadau varān  || 7.1,35.40
śiva uvāca
bhakṣyabhojyānyathākāmaṃ bāndhavairbhukṣva sarvadā  || 7.1,35.41
sukhī bhava sadā duḥkhānnirmuktā bhaktimānmama  || 7.1,35.41
upamanyo mahābhāga tavāmbaiṣā hi pārvatī  || 7.1,35.42
mayā putrīkṛto hyadya dattaḥ kṣīrodakārṇavaḥ  || 7.1,35.42
madhunaścārṇavaścaiva dadhyannārṇava eva ca  || 7.1,35.43
ājyaudanārṇavaścaiva phalādyarṇava eva ca  || 7.1,35.43
apūpagirayaścaiva bhakṣyabhojyārṇavastathā  || 7.1,35.44
ete dattā mayā te hi tvaṃ gṛhṇīṣva mahāmune  || 7.1,35.44
pitā tava mahādevo mātā vai jagadambikā  || 7.1,35.45
amaratvaṃ mayā dattaṃ gāṇapatyaṃ ca śāśvatam  || 7.1,35.45
varānvaraya suprītyā mano 'bhilaṣitānparān  || 7.1,35.46
prasanno 'haṃ pradāsyāmi nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 7.1,35.46
vāyuruvāca
evamuktvā mahādevaḥ karābhyāmupagṛhyatam  || 7.1,35.47
mūrdhnyāghrāya sutaste 'yamiti devyai nyavedayat || 7.1,35.47
devī ca guhavatprītyā mūrdhni tasya karāmbujam  || 7.1,35.48
vinyasya pradadau tasmai kumārapadamavyayam  || 7.1,35.48
kṣīrābdhirapi sākāraḥ kṣīraṃ svādu kare dadhat || 7.1,35.49
upasthāya dadau piṇḍībhūtaṃ kṣīramanaśvaram  || 7.1,35.49
yogaiśvaryaṃ sadā tuṣṭiṃ brahmavidyāmanaśvarām  || 7.1,35.50
samṛddhiṃ paramāntasmai dadau saṃtuṣṭamānasaḥ  || 7.1,35.50
atha śaṃbhuḥ prasannātmā dṛṣṭvā tasya tapomahaḥ  || 7.1,35.51
punardadau varaṃ divyaṃ munaye hyupamanyave  || 7.1,35.51
vrataṃ pāśupataṃ jñānaṃ vratayogaṃ ca tattvataḥ  || 7.1,35.52
dadau tasmai pravaktṛtvapāṭavaṃ suciraṃ param  || 7.1,35.52
so 'pi labdhvā varāndivyānkumāratvaṃ ca sarvadā  || 7.1,35.53
tasmācchivācca tasyāśca śivāyā mudito 'bhavat || 7.1,35.53
tataḥ prasannacetaskaḥ supraṇamya kṛtāṃjaliḥ  || 7.1,35.54
yayāce sa varaṃ vipro devadevānmaheśvarāt || 7.1,35.54
upamanyuruvāca
prasīda devadeveśa prasīda parameśvara  || 7.1,35.55
svabhaktindehi paramāndivyāmavyabhicāriṇīm  || 7.1,35.55
544

śraddhāndehi mahādeva dvasambandhiṣu me sadā  || 7.1,35.56
svadāsyaṃ paramaṃ snehaṃ sānnidhyaṃ caiva sarvadā  || 7.1,35.56
evamuktvā prasannātmāharṣagadgadayā girā  || 7.1,35.57
satuṣṭāva mahādevamupamanyurdvijottamaḥ  || 7.1,35.57
upamanyuruvāca
devadeva mahādeva śaraṇāgatavatsala  || 7.1,35.58
prasīda karuṇāsiṃdho sāmba śaṃkara sarvadā  || 7.1,35.58
vāyuruvāca
evamukto mahādevaḥ sarveṣāṃ ca varapradaḥ  || 7.1,35.59
pratyuvāca prasannātmopamanyuṃ munisattamam  || 7.1,35.59
śiva uvāca
vatsopamanyo tuṣṭo 'smi sarvaṃ dattaṃ mayā hi te  || 7.1,35.60
dṛḍhabhakto 'si viprarṣe mayā vijñāsito hyasi  || 7.1,35.60
ajaraścāmaraścaiva bhava tvanduḥkhavarjitaḥ  || 7.1,35.61
yaśasvī tejasā yukto divyajñānasamanvitaḥ  || 7.1,35.61
akṣayā bāndhavāścaiva kulaṃ gotraṃ ca te sadā  || 7.1,35.62
bhaviṣyati dvijaśreṣṭha mayi bhaktiśca śāśvatī  || 7.1,35.62
sānnidhyaṃ cāśrame nityaṃ kariṣyāmi dvijottama  || 7.1,35.63
upakaṃṭhaṃ mama tvaṃ vai sānandaṃ vihariṣyasi  || 7.1,35.63
evamuktvā sa bhagavānsūryakoṭisamaprabhaḥ  || 7.1,35.64
īśānassa varāndattvā tatraivāntardadhe haraḥ  || 7.1,35.64
upamanyuḥ prasannātmā prāpya tasmādvarādvarān  || 7.1,35.65
jagāma jananīsthānaṃ sukhaṃ prāpādhikaṃ ca saḥ  || 7.1,35.65

iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe vaiyāsikyāṃ caturviṃśatisāhasryāṃ saṃhitāyāṃ tadantargatāyāṃ saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ pūrvakhaṇḍe upamanyucaritavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcatriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ
samāpto 'yaṃ saptamyā vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāḥ pūrvakhaṇḍaḥ  ||
śrīḥ   ||
544

__________________________________________________________



śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ   ||
śrīgaurīśaṃkarābhyāṃ namaḥ   ||
atha saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍaḥ prārabhyate   ||

adhyāya 1
oṃ
namassamastasaṃsāracakrabhramaṇahetave  || 7.2,1.1
gaurīkucataṭadvandvakuṃkumāṃkitavakṣase  || 7.2,1.1
sūta uvāca
uktvā bhagavato labdhaprasādādupamanyunā  || 7.2,1.2
niyamādutthito vāyurmadhye prāpte divākare  || 7.2,1.2
ṛṣayaścāpi te sarve naimiṣāraṇyavāsinaḥ  || 7.2,1.3
athāyamarthaḥ praṣṭavya iti kṛtvā viniścayam  || 7.2,1.3
kṛtvā yathā svakaṃ kṛtyaṃ pratyahaṃ te yathā purā  || 7.2,1.4
bhagavaṃtamupāyāṃtaṃ samīkṣya samupāviśan  || 7.2,1.4
athāsau niyamasyāṃte bhagavānambarodbhavaḥ  || 7.2,1.5
madhye munisabhāyāstu bheje kḷptaṃ varāsanam  || 7.2,1.5
sukhāsanopaviṣṭaśca vāyurlokanamaskṛtaḥ  || 7.2,1.6
śrīmadvibhūtimīśasya hṛdi kṛtvedamabravīt || 7.2,1.6
taṃ prapadye mahādevaṃ sarvajñamaparājitam  || 7.2,1.7
vibhūtissakalaṃ yasya carācaramidaṃ jagat || 7.2,1.7
ityākarṇya śubhāṃ vāṇīmṛṣayaḥ kṣīṇakalmaṣāḥ  || 7.2,1.8
vibhūtivistaraṃ śrotumūcuste paramaṃ vacaḥ  || 7.2,1.8
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
uktaṃ bhagavatā vṛttamupamanyormahātmanaḥ  || 7.2,1.9
kṣīrārthenāpi tapasā yatprāptaṃ parameśvarāt || 7.2,1.9
dṛṣṭo 'sau vāsudevena kṛṣṇenākliṣṭakarmaṇā  || 7.2,1.10
dhaumyāgrajastatastena kṛtvā pāśupataṃ vratam  || 7.2,1.10
prāptaṃ ca paramaṃ jñānamiti prāgeva śuśruma  || 7.2,1.11
kathaṃ sa labdhavān kṛṣṇo jñānaṃ pāśupataṃ param  || 7.2,1.11
vāyuruvāca
svecchayā hyavatīrṇopi vāsudevassanātanaḥ  || 7.2,1.12
niṃdayanniva mānuṣyaṃ dehaśuddhiṃ cakāra saḥ  || 7.2,1.12
putrārthaṃ hi tapastaptuṃ gatastasya mahāmuneḥ  || 7.2,1.13
āśramaṃ munibhirdṛṣṭaṃ dṛṣṭavāṃstatra vai munim  || 7.2,1.13
bhasmāvadātasarvāṃgaṃ tripuṃḍrāṃkitamastakam  || 7.2,1.14
rudrākṣamālābharaṇaṃ jaṭāmaṃḍalamaṃḍitam  || 7.2,1.14
tacchiṣyabhūtairmunibhiśśāstrairvedamivāvṛtam  || 7.2,1.15
śivadhyānarataṃ śāṃtamupamanyuṃ mahādyutim  || 7.2,1.15
namaścakāra taṃ dṛṣṭvā hṛṣṭasarvatanūruhaḥ  || 7.2,1.16
bahumānena kṛṣṇo 'sau triḥ kṛtvā tu pradakṣiṇām  || 7.2,1.16
stutiṃ cakāra suprītyā nataskaṃdhaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ  || 7.2,1.16
tasyāvalokanādeva muneḥ kṛṣṇasya dhīmataḥ  || 7.2,1.17
naṣṭamāsīnmalaṃ sarvaṃ māyājaṃ kārmameva ca  || 7.2,1.17
tapaḥkṣīṇamalaṃ kṛṣṇamupamanyuryathāvidhiḥ  || 7.2,1.18
bhasmanoddhūlya taṃ mantrairagnirityādibhiḥ kramāt || 7.2,1.18
atha pāśupataṃ sākṣādvrataṃ dvādaśamāsikam  || 7.2,1.19
kārayitvā munistasmai pradadau jñānamuttamam  || 7.2,1.19
tadāprabhṛti taṃ kṛṣṇaṃ munayaśśaṃsitavratāḥ  || 7.2,1.20
divyāḥ pāśupatāḥ sarve parivṛtyopatasthire  || 7.2,1.20
tato guruniyogādvai kṛṣṇaḥ paramaśaktimān  || 7.2,1.21
545

tapaścakāra putrārthaṃ sāṃbamuddiśya śaṃkaram  || 7.2,1.21
tapaso tena varṣāṃte dṛṣṭo 'sau parameśvaraḥ  || 7.2,1.22
śriyā paramayā yuktassāṃbaśca sagaṇaśśivaḥ  || 7.2,1.22
varārthamāvirbhūtasya harasya subhagākṛteḥ  || 7.2,1.23
stutiṃ cakāra natvāsau kṛṣṇaḥ samyakkṛtāṃjaliḥ  || 7.2,1.23
sāṃbaṃ samagaṇavyagro labdhavānputramātmanaḥ  || 7.2,1.24
tapasā tuṣṭacittena dattaṃ viṣṇośśivena vai  || 7.2,1.24
yasmātsāṃbo mahādevaḥ pradadau putramātmanaḥ  || 7.2,1.25
tasmājjāṃbavatīsūnuṃ sāṃbaṃ cakre sa nāmataḥ  || 7.2,1.25
tadetatkathitaṃ sarvaṃ kṛṣṇasyāmitakarmaṇaḥ  || 7.2,1.26
maharṣerjñānalābhaśca putralābhaśca śaṃkarāt || 7.2,1.26
ya idaṃ kīrtayennityaṃ śṛṇuyācchrāvayettathā  || 7.2,1.27
sa viṣṇorjñānamāsādya tenaiva saha modate  || 7.2,1.27
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe kṛṣṇaputraprāptivarṇanaṃ nama prathamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 2
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
kiṃ tatpāśupataṃ jñānaṃ kathaṃ paśupatiśśivaḥ  || 7.2,2.1
kathaṃ dhaumyāgrajaḥ pṛṣṭaḥ kṛṣṇenākliṣṭakarmaṇā  || 7.2,2.1
etatsarvaṃ samācakṣva vāyo śaṃkaravigraha  || 7.2,2.2
tatsamo na hi vaktāsti trailokyeṣvaparaḥ prabhuḥ  || 7.2,2.2
sūta uvāca
ityākarṇya vacasteṣāṃ maharṣīṇāṃ prabhaṃjanaḥ  || 7.2,2.3
saṃsmṛtya śivamīśānaṃ pravaktumupacakrame  || 7.2,2.3
vāyuruvāca
purā sākṣānmaheśena śrīkaṃṭhākhyena mandare  || 7.2,2.4
devyai devena kathitaṃ jñānaṃ pāśupataṃ param  || 7.2,2.4
tadeva pṛṣṭaṃ kṛṣṇena viṣṇunā viśvayoninā  || 7.2,2.5
paśutvaṃ ca surādīnāṃ patitvaṃ ca śivasya ca  || 7.2,2.5
yathopadiṣṭaṃ kṛṣṇāya muninā hyupamanyunā  || 7.2,2.6
tathā samāsato vakṣye tacchṛṇudhvamataṃdritāḥ  || 7.2,2.6
puropamanyumāsīnaṃ viṣṇuḥkṛṣṇavapurdharaḥ  || 7.2,2.7
praṇipatya yathānyāyamidaṃ vacanamabravīt || 7.2,2.7
śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca
bhagavañchrotumicchāmi devyai devena bhāṣitam  || 7.2,2.8
divyaṃ pāśupataṃ jñānaṃ vibhūtiṃ vāsya kṛtsnaśaḥ  || 7.2,2.8
kathaṃ paśupatirdevaḥ paśavaḥ ke prakīrtitāḥ  || 7.2,2.9
kaiḥ pāśaiste nibadhyaṃte vimucyaṃte ca te katham  || 7.2,2.9
iti saṃcoditaḥ śrīmānupamanyurmahātmanā  || 7.2,2.10
praṇamya devaṃ devīṃ ca prāha puṣṭo yathā tathā  || 7.2,2.10
upamanyuruvāca
brahmādyāḥ sthāvarāṃtāśca devadevasya śūlinaḥ  || 7.2,2.11
paśavaḥ parikīrtyaṃte saṃsāravaśavartinaḥ  || 7.2,2.11
teṣāṃ patitvāddeveśaḥ śivaḥ paśupatiḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.2,2.12
malamāyādibhiḥ pāśaiḥ sa badhnāti paśūnpatiḥ  || 7.2,2.12
sa eva mocakasteṣāṃ bhaktyā samyagupāsitaḥ  || 7.2,2.13
caturviṃśatitattvāni māyākarmaguṇā amī  || 7.2,2.13
viṣayā iti kathyante pāśā jīvanibandhanāḥ  || 7.2,2.14
brahmādistambaparyaṃtān paśūnbaddhvā maheśvaraḥ  || 7.2,2.14
545

pāśairetaiḥ patirdevaḥ kāryaṃ kārayati svakam  || 7.2,2.15
tasyājñayā maheśasya prakṛtiḥ puruṣocitām  || 7.2,2.15
buddhiṃ prasūte sā buddhirahaṃkāramahaṃkṛtiḥ  || 7.2,2.16
indriyāṇi daśaikaṃ ca tanmātrāpañcakaṃ tathā  || 7.2,2.16
śāsanāddevadevasya śivasya śivadāyinaḥ  || 7.2,2.17
tanmātrāṇyapi tasyaiva śāsanena mahīyasā  || 7.2,2.17
mahābhūtānyaśeṣāṇi bhāvayaṃtyanupūrvaśaḥ  || 7.2,2.18
brahmādīnāṃ tṛṇāntānāṃ dehināṃ dehasaṃgatim  || 7.2,2.18
mahābhūtānyaśeṣāṇi janayaṃti śivājñayā  || 7.2,2.19
adhyavasyati vai buddhirahaṃkārobhimanyate  || 7.2,2.19
cittaṃ cetayate cāpi manaḥ saṃkalpayatyapi  || 7.2,2.20
śrotrādīni ca gṛhṇanti śabdādīnviṣayān pṛthak || 7.2,2.20
svāneva nānyāndevasya divyenājñābalena vai  || 7.2,2.21
vāgādīnyapi yānyāsaṃstāni karmendriyāṇi ca  || 7.2,2.21
yathā svaṃ karma kurvanti nānyatkiṃcicchivājñayā  || 7.2,2.22
śabdādayopi gṛhyaṃte kriyante vacanādayaḥ  || 7.2,2.22
avilaṃghyā hi sarveṣāmājñā śaṃbhorgarīyasī  || 7.2,2.23
avakāśamaśeṣāṇāṃ bhūtānāṃ saṃprayacchati  || 7.2,2.23
ākāśaḥ parameśasya śāsanādeva sarvagaḥ  || 7.2,2.24
prāṇādyaiśca tathā nāmabhedairaṃtarbahirjagat || 7.2,2.24
bibharti sarvaṃ śarvasya śāsanena prabhañjanaḥ  || 7.2,2.25
havyaṃ vahati devānāṃ kavyaṃ kavyāśināmapi  || 7.2,2.25
pākādyaṃ ca karotyagniḥ parameśvaraśāsanāt || 7.2,2.26
saṃjīvanādyaṃ sarvasya kurvatyāpastadājñayā  || 7.2,2.26
viśvambharā jagannityaṃ dhatte viśveśvarājñayā  || 7.2,2.27
devānpātyasurān haṃti trilokamabhirakṣati  || 7.2,2.27
ājñayā tasya devendraḥ sarvairdevairalaṃghyayā  || 7.2,2.28
ādhipatyamapāṃ nityaṃ kurute varuṇassadā  || 7.2,2.28
pāśairbadhnāti ca yathā daṃḍyāṃstasyaiva śāsanāt || 7.2,2.29
dadāti nityaṃ yakṣendro draviṇaṃ draviṇeśvaraḥ  || 7.2,2.29
puṇyānurūpaṃ bhūtebhyaḥ puruṣasyānuśāsanāt || 7.2,2.30
karoti saṃpadaḥ śaśvajjñānaṃ cāpi sumedhasām  || 7.2,2.30
nigrahaṃ cāpyasādhūnāmīśānaśśivaśāsanāt || 7.2,2.31
dhatte tu dharaṇīṃ mūrdhnā śeṣaḥ śivaniyogataḥ  || 7.2,2.31
yāmāhustāmasīṃ raudrīṃ mūrtimaṃtakarīṃ hareḥ  || 7.2,2.32
sṛjatyaśeṣamīśasya śāsanāccaturānanaḥ  || 7.2,2.32
anyābhirmūrtibhiḥ svābhiḥ pāti cāṃte nihanti ca  || 7.2,2.33
viṣṇuḥ pālayate viśvaṃ kālakālasya śāsanāt || 7.2,2.33
sṛjate trasate cāpi svakābhistanubhistribhiḥ  || 7.2,2.34
haratyaṃte jagatsarvaṃ harastasyaiva śāsanāt || 7.2,2.34
sṛjatyapi ca viśvātmā tridhā bhinnastu rakṣati  || 7.2,2.35
kālaḥ karoti sakalaṃ kālassaṃharati prajāḥ  || 7.2,2.35
kālaḥ pālayate viśvaṃ kālakālasya śāsanāt || 7.2,2.36
546

tribhiraṃśairjagadbibhrattejobhirvṛṣṭimādiśan  || 7.2,2.36
divi varṣatyasau bhānurdevadevasya śāsanāt || 7.2,2.37
puṣṇātyoṣadhijātāni bhūtānyāhlādayatyapi  || 7.2,2.37
devaiśca pīyate caṃdraścandrabhūṣaṇaśāsanāt || 7.2,2.38
ādityā vasavo rudrā aśvinau marutastathā  || 7.2,2.38
khecarā ṛṣayassiddhā bhogino manujā mṛgāḥ  || 7.2,2.39
paśavaḥ pakṣiṇaścaiva kīṭādyāḥ sthāvarāṇi ca  || 7.2,2.39
nadyassamudrā girayaḥ kānanāni sarāṃsi ca  || 7.2,2.40
vedāḥ sāṃgāśca śāstrāṇi maṃtrastomamakhādayaḥ  || 7.2,2.40
kālāgnyādiśivāṃtāni bhuvanāni sahādhipaiḥ  || 7.2,2.41
brahmāṃḍānyapyasaṃkhyāni teṣāmāvaraṇāni ca  || 7.2,2.41
vartamānānyatītāni bhaviṣyantyapi kṛtsnaśaḥ  || 7.2,2.42
diśaśca vidiśaścaiva kālabhedāḥ kalādayaḥ  || 7.2,2.42
yacca kiṃcijjagatyasmin dṛśyate śrūyate 'pi vā  || 7.2,2.43
tatsarvaṃ śaṃkarasyājñā balena samadhiṣṭhitam  || 7.2,2.43
ājñābalāttasya dharā sthiteha dharādharā vāridharāḥ samudrāḥ  || 7.2,2.44
jyotirgaṇāḥ śakramukhāśca devāḥ sthiraṃ ciraṃ vā cidacidyadasti  || 7.2,2.44
upamanyuruvāca
atyāścaryamidaṃ kṛṣṇa śaṃbhoramitakarmaṇaḥ  || 7.2,2.45
ājñākṛtaṃ śṛṇuṣvaitacchrutaṃ śrutimukhe mayā  || 7.2,2.45
purā kila surāḥ seṃdrā vivadaṃtaḥ parasparam  || 7.2,2.46
asurānsamare jitvā jetāhamahamityuta  || 7.2,2.46
tadā maheśvarasteṣāṃ madhyato varaveṣadhṛk || 7.2,2.47
svalakṣaṇairvihīnāṃgaḥ svayaṃ yakṣa ivābhavat || 7.2,2.47
sa tānāha surānekaṃ tṛṇamādāya bhūtale  || 7.2,2.48
ya etadvikṛtaṃ kartuṃ kṣamate sa tu daityajit || 7.2,2.48
yakṣasya vacanaṃ śrutvā vajrapāṇiḥ śacīpatiḥ  || 7.2,2.49
kiṃcitkruddho vihasyainaṃ tṛṇamādātumudyataḥ  || 7.2,2.49
na tattṛṇamupadātuṃ manasāpi ca śakyate  || 7.2,2.50
yathā tathāpi tacchettuṃ vajraṃ vajradharo 'sṛjat || 7.2,2.50
tadvajraṃ nijavajreṇa saṃsṛṣṭamiva sarvataḥ  || 7.2,2.51
tṛṇenābhihataṃ tena tiryagagraṃ papāta ha  || 7.2,2.51
tataścānye susaṃrabdhā lokapālā mahābalāḥ  || 7.2,2.52
sasṛjustṛṇamuddiśya svāyudhāni sahasraśaḥ  || 7.2,2.52
prajajjvāla mahāvahniḥ pracaṃḍaḥ pavano vavau  || 7.2,2.53
pravṛddho 'pāṃpatiryadvatpralaye samupasthite  || 7.2,2.53
evaṃ devaissamārabdhaṃ tṛṇamuddiśya yatnataḥ  || 7.2,2.54
vyarthamāsīdaho kṛṣṇa yakṣasyātmabalena vai  || 7.2,2.54
tadāha yakṣaṃ deveṃdraḥ ko bhavānityamarṣitaḥ  || 7.2,2.55
tatassa paśyatāmeva teṣāmaṃtaradhādatha  || 7.2,2.55
tadaṃtare haimavatī devī divyavibhūṣaṇā  || 7.2,2.56
āvirāsīnnabhoraṃge śobhamānā śucismitā  || 7.2,2.56
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā vismayāviṣṭā devāḥ śakrapurogamāḥ  || 7.2,2.57
546

praṇamya yakṣaṃ papracchuḥ ko 'sau yakṣo vilakṣaṇaḥ  || 7.2,2.57
sā 'bravītsasmitaṃ devī sa yuṣmākamagocaraḥ  || 7.2,2.58
tenedaṃ bhramyate cakraṃ saṃsārākhyaṃ carācaram  || 7.2,2.58
tenādau kriyate viśvaṃ tena saṃhriyate punaḥ  || 7.2,2.59
na tanniyantā kaścitsyāttena sarvaṃ niyamyate  || 7.2,2.59
ityuktvā sā mahādevī tatraivāṃtaradhatta vai  || 7.2,2.60
devāśca vismitāḥ sarve tāṃ praṇamya divaṃ yayuḥ  || 7.2,2.60
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṃḍe dvitīyo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 3
upamanyuruvāca
śṛṇu kṛṣṇa maheśasya śivasya paramātmanaḥ  || 7.2,3.1
mūrtyātmabhistataṃ kṛtsnaṃ jagadetaccarācaram  || 7.2,3.1
sa śivassarvamevedaṃ svakīyābhiśca mūrtibhiḥ  || 7.2,3.2
adhitiṣṭhatyameyātmā hyetatsarvamanusmṛtam  || 7.2,3.2
brahmā viṣṇustathā rudro maheśānassadāśivaḥ  || 7.2,3.3
mūrtayastasya vijñeyā yābhirviśvamidaṃ tatam  || 7.2,3.3
athānyāścāpi tanavaḥ pañca brahmasamāhvayāḥ  || 7.2,3.4
tanūbhistābhirāvyāptamiha kiṃcinna vidyate  || 7.2,3.4
īśānaḥ puruṣo 'ghoro vāmaḥ sadyastathaiva ca  || 7.2,3.5
brahmāṇyetāni devasya mūrtayaḥ pañca viśrutāḥ  || 7.2,3.5
īśānākhyā tu yā tasya mūrtirādyā garīyasī  || 7.2,3.6
bhoktāraṃ prakṛteḥ sākṣātkṣetrajñamadhitiṣṭhati  || 7.2,3.6
sthāṇostatpuruṣākhyā yā mūrtirmūrtimataḥ prabhoḥ  || 7.2,3.7
guṇāśrayātmakaṃ bhogyamavyaktamadhitiṣṭhati  || 7.2,3.7
dharmādyaṣṭāṃgasaṃyuktaṃ buddhitattvaṃ pinākinaḥ  || 7.2,3.8
adhitiṣṭhatyaghorākhyā mūrtiratyaṃtapūjitā  || 7.2,3.8
vāmadevāhvayāṃ mūrtiṃ mahādevasya vedhasaḥ  || 7.2,3.9
ahaṃkṛteradhiṣṭhātrīmāhurāgamavedinaḥ  || 7.2,3.9
sadyo jātāhvayāṃ mūrtiṃ śambhoramitavarcasaḥ  || 7.2,3.10
mānasaḥ samadhiṣṭhātrīṃ matimaṃtaḥ pracakṣate  || 7.2,3.10
śrotrasya vācaḥ śabdasya vibhorvyomnastathaiva ca  || 7.2,3.11
īśvarīmīśvarasyemāmīśākhyāṃ hi vidurbudhāḥ  || 7.2,3.11
tvakpāṇisparśavāyūnāmīśvarīṃ mūrtimaiśvarīm  || 7.2,3.12
puruṣākhyaṃ vidussarve purāṇārthaviśāradāḥ  || 7.2,3.12
cakṣuṣaścaraṇasyāpi rūpasyāgnestathaiva ca  || 7.2,3.13
aghorākhyāmadhiṣṭhātrīṃ mūrtimāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ  || 7.2,3.13
ramanāyāśca pāyośca rasasyāpāṃ tathaiva ca  || 7.2,3.14
īśvarīṃ vāmadevākhyāṃ mūrtiṃ tanniratāṃ viduḥ  || 7.2,3.14
ghrāṇasya caivopasthasya gaṃdhasya ca bhuvastathā  || 7.2,3.15
sadyo jātāhvayāṃ mūrtimīśvarīṃ saṃpracakṣate  || 7.2,3.15
mūrtayaḥ pañca devasya vaṃdanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ  || 7.2,3.16
śreyorthibhirnarairnityaṃ śreyasāmekahetavaḥ  || 7.2,3.16
tasya devādidevasya mūrtyaṣṭakamayaṃ jagat || 7.2,3.17
tasminvyāpya sthitaṃ viśvaṃ sūtre maṇigaṇā iva  || 7.2,3.17
śarvo bhavastathā rudra ugro bhīmaḥ paśoḥ patiḥ 1  || 7.2,3.18

1 aluksamāsa ārṣaḥ

547

īśānaśca mahādevo mūrtayaścāṣṭa viśrutāḥ  || 7.2,3.18
bhūmyaṃbhognimarudvyomakṣetrajñārkaniśākarāḥ  || 7.2,3.19
adhiṣṭhitā maheśasya śarvādyairaṣṭamūrtibhiḥ  || 7.2,3.19
carācarātmakaṃ viśvaṃ dhatte viśvaṃbharātmikā  || 7.2,3.20
śārvīrśivāhvayā mūrtiriti śāstrasya niścayaḥ  || 7.2,3.20
saṃjīvanaṃ samastasya jagatassalilātmikā  || 7.2,3.21
bhāvīti gīyate mūrtibhavasya paramātmanaḥ  || 7.2,3.21
bahiraṃtargatā viśvaṃ vyāpya tejomayī śubhā  || 7.2,3.22
raudrī rudrāvyayā mūrtirāsthitā ghorarūpiṇī  || 7.2,3.22
spaṃdayatyanilātmadaṃ bibharti spaṃdate svayam  || 7.2,3.23
augrīti kathyate sadbhirmūrtirugrasya vedhasaḥ  || 7.2,3.23
sarvāvakāśadā sarvavyāpikā gaganātmikā  || 7.2,3.24
mūrtirbhīmasya bhīmākhyā bhūtavṛṃdasya bhedikā  || 7.2,3.24
sarvātmanāmadhiṣṭhātrī sarvakṣetranivāsinī  || 7.2,3.25
mūrtiḥ paśupaterjñeyā paśupāśanikṛṃtanī  || 7.2,3.25
dīpayaṃtī jagatsarvaṃ divākarasamāhvayā  || 7.2,3.26
īśānākhyamaheśasya mūrtirdivi visarpati  || 7.2,3.26
āpyāyayati yo viśvamamṛtāṃśurniśākaraḥ  || 7.2,3.27
mahādevasya sā mūrtirmahādevasamāhvayā  || 7.2,3.27
ātmā tasyāṣṭamī mūrtiḥ śivasya paramātmanaḥ  || 7.2,3.28
vyāpiketaramūrtīnāṃ viśvaṃ tasmācchivātmakam  || 7.2,3.28
vṛkṣasya mūlasekena śākhāḥ puṣyaṃti vai yathā  || 7.2,3.29
śivasya pūjayā tadvatpuṣyatyasya vapurjagat || 7.2,3.29
sarvābhayapradānaṃ ca sarvānugrahaṇaṃ tathā  || 7.2,3.30
sarvopakārakaraṇaṃ śivasyārādhanaṃ viduḥ  || 7.2,3.30
yatheha putrapautrādeḥ prītyā prīto bhavetpitā  || 7.2,3.31
tathā sarvasya saṃprītyā prīto bhavati śaṃkaraḥ  || 7.2,3.31
dehino yasya kasyāpi kriyate yadi nigrahaḥ  || 7.2,3.32
aniṣṭamaṣṭamūrtestatkṛtameva na saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,3.32
aṣṭamūrtyātmanā viśvamadhiṣṭhāya sthitaṃ śivam  || 7.2,3.33
bhajasva sarvabhāvena rudraḥ paramakāraṇam  || 7.2,3.33

oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe tṛtīyo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 4
kṛṣṇa uvāca
bhagavanparameśasya śarvasyāmitatejasaḥ  || 7.2,4.1
mūrtibhirviśvamevedaṃ yathā vyāptaṃ tathā śrutam  || 7.2,4.1
athaitajjñātumicchāmi yāthātmyaṃ pameśayoḥ  || 7.2,4.2
strīpuṃbhāvātmakaṃ cedaṃ tābhyāṃ kathamadhiṣṭhitam  || 7.2,4.2
upamanyuruvāca
śrīmadvibhūtiṃ śivayoryāthātmyaṃ ca samāsataḥ  || 7.2,4.3
vakṣye tadvistarādvaktuṃ bhavenāpi na śakyate  || 7.2,4.3
śaktiḥ sākṣānmahādevī mahādevaśca śaktimān  || 7.2,4.4
tayorvibhūtileśo vai sarvametaccarācaram  || 7.2,4.4
vastu kiṃcidacidrūpaṃ kiṃcidvastu cidātmakam  || 7.2,4.5
547

dvayaṃ śuddhamaśuddhaṃ ca paraṃ cāparameva ca  || 7.2,4.5
yatsaṃsarati ciccakramaciccakrasamanvitam  || 7.2,4.6
tadevāśuddhamaparamitaraṃ tu paraṃ śubham  || 7.2,4.6
aparaṃ ca paraṃ caiva dvayaṃ cidacidātmakam  || 7.2,4.7
śivasya ca śivāyāśca svāmyaṃ caitatsvabhāvataḥ  || 7.2,4.7
śivayorvai vaśe viśvaṃ na viśvasya vaśe śivau  || 7.2,4.8
īśitavyamidaṃ yasmāttasmādviśveśvarau śivau  || 7.2,4.8
yathā śivastathā devī yathā devī tathā śivaḥ  || 7.2,4.9
nānayoraṃtaraṃ vidyāccaṃdracandrikayoriva  || 7.2,4.9
caṃdro na khalu bhātyeṣa yathā caṃdrikayā vinā  || 7.2,4.10
na bhāti vidyamāno 'pi tathā śaktyā vinā śivaḥ  || 7.2,4.10
prabhayā hi vināyadvadbhānureṣa na vidyate  || 7.2,4.11
prabhā ca bhānunā tena sutarāṃ tadupāśrayā  || 7.2,4.11
evaṃ parasparāpekṣā śaktiśaktimatoḥ sthitā  || 7.2,4.12
na śivena vinā śaktirna śaktyā ca vinā śivaḥ  || 7.2,4.12
śaktauyayā śivo nityaṃ bhaktau muktau ca dehinām  || 7.2,4.13
ādyā saikā parā śaktiścinmayī śivasaṃśrayā  || 7.2,4.13
yāmāhurakhileśasya taistairanuguṇairguṇaiḥ  || 7.2,4.14
samānadharmiṇīmeva śivasya paramātmanaḥ  || 7.2,4.14
saikā parā ca cidrūpā śaktiḥ prasavadharmiṇī  || 7.2,4.15
vibhajya bahudhā viśvaṃ vidadhāti śivecchayā  || 7.2,4.15
sā mūlaprakṛtirmāyā triguṇā ca tridhā smṛtā  || 7.2,4.16
śivayā ca viparyastaṃ yayā tatamidaṃ jagat || 7.2,4.16
ekadhā ca dvidhā caiva tathā śatasahasradhā  || 7.2,4.17
śaktayaḥ khalu bhidyaṃte bahudhā vyavahārataḥ  || 7.2,4.17
śivecchayā parāśaktiḥ śivatattvaikatāṃ gatā  || 7.2,4.18
tataḥ parisphuratyādau sarge tailaṃ tilādiva  || 7.2,4.18
tataḥ kriyākhyayā śaktyā śaktau śaktimadutthayā  || 7.2,4.19
tasyāṃ vikṣobhyamāṇāyāmādau nādaḥ samudbabhau  || 7.2,4.19
nādādviniḥsṛto biṃdurbiṃdodevassadāśivaḥ  || 7.2,4.20
tasmānmaheśvaro jātaḥ śuddhavidyā maheśvarāt || 7.2,4.20
sā vācāmīśvarī śaktirvāgīśākhyā hi śūlinaḥ  || 7.2,4.21
yā sā varṇasvarūpeṇa mātṛkepi vijṛmbhate  || 7.2,4.21
athānaṃtasamāveśānmāyā kālamavāsṛjat || 7.2,4.22
niyatiñca kalāṃ vidyāṃ kalātorāgapūruṣau  || 7.2,4.22
māyātaḥ punarevābhūdavyaktaṃ triguṇātmakam  || 7.2,4.23
triguṇācca tato vyaktādvibhaktāḥ syustrayo guṇāḥ  || 7.2,4.23
sattvaṃ rajastamaśceti yairvyāptamakhilaṃ jagat || 7.2,4.24
guṇebhyaḥ kṣobhyamāṇebhyo guṇeśākhyāstrimūrtayaḥ  || 7.2,4.24
abhavanmahadādīni tattvāni ca yathākramam  || 7.2,4.25
tebhyassyuraṇḍapiṇḍāni tvasaṃkhyāni śivājñayā  || 7.2,4.25
548

adhiṣṭhitānyanantādyairvidyeśaiścakravartibhiḥ  || 7.2,4.25
śarīrāṃtarabhedena śakterbhedāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  || 7.2,4.26
nānārūpāstu vijñeyāḥ sthūlasūkṣmavibhedataḥ  || 7.2,4.26
rudrasya raudrī sā śaktirviṣṇaurvai vaiṣṇavī matā  || 7.2,4.27
brahmāṇī brahmaṇaḥ proktā cendrasyaiṃdrīti kathyate  || 7.2,4.27
kimatra bahunoktena yadviśvamiti kīrtitam  || 7.2,4.28
śakyātmanaiva tadvyāptaṃ yathā dehe 'ṃtarātmanā  || 7.2,4.28
tasmācchaktimayaṃ sarvaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṃgamam  || 7.2,4.29
kalā yā paramā śaktiḥ kathitā paramātmanaḥ  || 7.2,4.29
evameṣā parā śaktirīśvarecchānuyāyinī  || 7.2,4.30
sthiraṃ caraṃ ca yadviśvaṃ sṛjatīti viniścayaḥ  || 7.2,4.30
jñānakriyā cikīrṣābhistisṛbhissvātmaśaktibhiḥ  || 7.2,4.31
śaktimānīśvaraḥ śaśvadviśvaṃ vyāpyādhitiṣṭhati  || 7.2,4.31
idamitthamidaṃ netthaṃ bhavedityevamātmikā  || 7.2,4.32
icchāśaktirmaheśasya nityā kāryaniyāmikā  || 7.2,4.32
jñānaśaktistu tatkāryaṃ karaṇaṃ kāraṇaṃ tathā  || 7.2,4.33
prayojanaṃ ca tattvena buddhirūpādhyavasyati  || 7.2,4.33
yathepsitaṃ kriyāśaktiryathādhyavasitaṃ jagat || 7.2,4.34
kalpayatyakhilaṃ kāryaṃ kṣaṇātsaṃkalparūpiṇī  || 7.2,4.34
yathā śaktitrayotthānaṃ śaktiprasavadharmiṇī  || 7.2,4.35
śaktyā paramayā nunnā prasūte sakalaṃ jagat || 7.2,4.35
evaṃ śaktisamāyogācchaktimānucyate śivaḥ  || 7.2,4.36
śaktiśaktimadutthaṃ tu śāktaṃ śaivamidaṃ jagat || 7.2,4.36
yathā na jāyate putraḥ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ vinā  || 7.2,4.37
tathā bhavaṃ bhavānīṃ ca vinā naitaccarācaram  || 7.2,4.37
strīpuṃsaprabhavaṃ viśvaṃ strīpuṃsātmakameva ca  || 7.2,4.37
strīpuṃsayorvibhūtiśca strīpuṃsābhyāmadhiṣṭhitam  || 7.2,4.38
paramātmā śivaḥ proktaśśivā sā ca prakīrtitā  || 7.2,4.38
śivassadāśivaḥ proktaḥ śivā sā ca manonmanī  || 7.2,4.39
śivo maheśvaro jñeyaḥ śivā māyeti kathyate  || 7.2,4.39
puruṣaḥ parameśānaḥ prakṛtiḥ parameśvarī  || 7.2,4.40
rudro maheśvarassākṣādrudrāṇī rudravallabhā  || 7.2,4.40
viṣṇurviśveśvaro devo lakṣmīrviśveśvarapriyā  || 7.2,4.41
brahmā śivo yadā sraṣṭā brahmāṇī brahmaṇaḥ priyā  || 7.2,4.41
bhāskaro bhagavāñchaṃbhuḥ prabhā bhagavatī śivā  || 7.2,4.42
maheṃdro manmathārātiḥ śacī śailendrakanyakā  || 7.2,4.42
jātavedā mahādevaḥ svāhā śarvārdhadehinī  || 7.2,4.43
yamastriyaṃbako devastatpriyā girikanyakā  || 7.2,4.43
nirṛtirbhagavānīśo nairṛtī naganaṃdanī  || 7.2,4.44
varuṇo bhagavānrudro vāruṇī bhūdharātmajā  || 7.2,4.44
bāleṃduśekharo vāyuḥ śivā śivamanoharā  || 7.2,4.45
yakṣo yajñaśirohartā ṛddhirhimagirīndrajā  || 7.2,4.45
548

caṃdrārdhaśekharaścaṃdro rohiṇī rudravallabhā  || 7.2,4.46
īśānaḥ parameśānastadāryā parameśvarī  || 7.2,4.46
anaṃtavalayo 'naṃto hyanaṃtānaṃtavallabhā  || 7.2,4.47
kālāgnirudraḥ kālāriḥ kālī kālāṃtakapriyā  || 7.2,4.47
puruṣākhyo manuśśaṃbhuḥ śatarūpā śivapriyā  || 7.2,4.48
dakṣassākṣānmahādevaḥ prasūtiḥ parameśvarī  || 7.2,4.48
rucirbhavo bhavānī ca budhairākūtirucyate  || 7.2,4.49
bhṛgurbhagākṣihā devaḥ khyātistrinayanapriyā  || 7.2,4.49
marīcibhagavānrudraḥ saṃbhūtiśśarvavallabhā  || 7.2,4.50
gaṃgādharo 'ṃgirā jñeyaḥ smṛtiḥ sākṣādumā smṛtā  || 7.2,4.50
pulastyaḥ śaśabhṛnmauliḥ prītiḥ kāṃtā pinākinaḥ  || 7.2,4.51
pulahastripuradhvaṃsī tatpriyā tu śivapriyā  || 7.2,4.51
kratudhvaṃsī kratuḥ proktaḥ saṃnatirdayitā vibhoḥ  || 7.2,4.52
trinetro 'trirumā sākṣādanasūyā smṛtā budhaiḥ  || 7.2,4.52
kaśyapaḥ kālahā devo devamātā maheśvarī  || 7.2,4.53
vasiṣṭho manmathārātirdevī sākṣādaruṃdhatī  || 7.2,4.53
śaṃkaraḥ puruṣāssarve striyassarvā maheśvarī  || 7.2,4.54
sarve strīpuruṣāstasmāttayoreva vibhūtayaḥ  || 7.2,4.54
viṣayī bhagavānīśo viṣayaḥ parameśvarī  || 7.2,4.55
śrāvyaṃ sarvamumārūpaṃ śrotā śūlavarāyudhaḥ  || 7.2,4.55
praṣṭavyaṃ vastujātaṃ tu dhatte śaṃkaravallabhā  || 7.2,4.56
praṣṭā sa eva viśvātmā bālacandrāvataṃsakaḥ  || 7.2,4.56
draṣṭavyaṃ vasturūpaṃ tu bibharti vaktavallabhā  || 7.2,4.57
draṣṭā viśveśvaro devaḥ śaśikhaṃḍaśikhāmaṇiḥ  || 7.2,4.57
rasajātaṃ mahādevī devo rasayitā śivaḥ  || 7.2,4.58
preyajātaṃ ca girijā preyāṃścaiva garāśanaḥ  || 7.2,4.58
maṃtavyavastutāṃ dhatte sadā devī maheśvarī  || 7.2,4.59
maṃtā sa eva viśvātmā mahādevo maheśvaraḥ  || 7.2,4.59
boddhavyavasturūpaṃ tu bibharti bhavavallabhā  || 7.2,4.60
devassa eva bhagavānboddhā mugdhenduśekharaḥ  || 7.2,4.60
prāṇaḥ pinākī sarveṣāṃ prāṇināṃ bhagavānprabhuḥ  || 7.2,4.61
prāṇasthitistu sarveṣāmaṃbikā cāṃburūpiṇī  || 7.2,4.61
bibharti kṣetratāṃ devī tripurāṃtakavallabhā  || 7.2,4.62
kṣetrajñatvaṃ tadā dhatte bhagavānaṃtakāṃtakaḥ  || 7.2,4.62
ahaḥ śūlāyudho devaḥ śūlapāṇipriyā niśā  || 7.2,4.63
ākāśaḥ śaṃkaro devaḥ pṛthivī śaṃkarapriyā  || 7.2,4.63
samudro bhagavānīśo velā śailendrakanyakā  || 7.2,4.64
vṛkṣo vṛṣadhvajo devo latā viśveśvarapriyā  || 7.2,4.64
puṃlliṃgamakhilaṃ dhatte bhagavānpuraśāsanaḥ  || 7.2,4.65
striliṃgaṃ cākhilaṃ dhatte devī devamanoramā  || 7.2,4.65
śabdajālamaśeṣaṃ tu dhatte sarvasya vallabhā  || 7.2,4.66
arthasvarūpamakhilaṃ dhatte mugdhenduśekharaḥ  || 7.2,4.66
yasya yasya padārthasya yā yā śaktirudāhṛtā  || 7.2,4.67
sā sā viśveśvarī devī sa sa sarvo maheśvaraḥ  || 7.2,4.67
549

yatparaṃ yatpavitraṃ ca yatpuṇyaṃ yacca maṃgalam  || 7.2,4.68
tattadāha mahābhāgāstayostejovijṛṃbhitam  || 7.2,4.68
yathā dīpasya dīptasya śikhā dīpayate gṛham  || 7.2,4.69
tathā tejastayoretadvyāpya dīpayate jagat || 7.2,4.69
tṛṇādiśivamūrtyaṃtaṃ viśvakhyātiśayakramaḥ  || 7.2,4.70
sannikarṣakramavaśāttayoriti parā śrutiḥ  || 7.2,4.70
sarvākārātmakāvetau sarvaśreyovidhāyinau  || 7.2,4.71
pūjanīyau namaskāryau ciṃtanīyau ca sarvadā  || 7.2,4.71
yathāprajñamidaṃ kṛṣṇa yāthātmyaṃ parameśayoḥ  || 7.2,4.72
kathitaṃ hi mayā te 'dya na tu tāvadiyattayā  || 7.2,4.72
tatkathaṃ śakyate vaktuṃ yāthātmyaṃ parameśayoḥ  || 7.2,4.73
mahatāmapi sarveṣāṃ manaso 'pi bahirgatam  || 7.2,4.73
aṃtargatamananyānāmīśvarārpitacetasām  || 7.2,4.74
anyeṣāṃ buddhyanārūḍhamārūḍhaṃ ca yathaiva tat || 7.2,4.74
yeyamuktā vibhūtirvai prākṛtī sā parā matā  || 7.2,4.75
aprākṛtāṃ parāmanyāṃ guhyāṃ guhyavido viduḥ  || 7.2,4.75
yato vāco nivartaṃte manasā cendriyaissaha  || 7.2,4.76
aprākṛtī parā caiṣā vibhūtiḥ pārameśvarī  || 7.2,4.76
saiveha paramaṃ dhāma saiveha paramā gatiḥ  || 7.2,4.77
saiveha paramā kāṣṭhā vibhūtiḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 7.2,4.77
tāṃ prāptuṃ prayataṃte 'tra jitaśvāsā jiteṃdriyāḥ  || 7.2,4.78
garbhakārā gṛhadvāraṃ niśchidraṃ ghaṭituṃ yathā  || 7.2,4.78
saṃsārāśīviṣālīḍhamṛtasaṃjīvanauṣadham  || 7.2,4.79
vibhūtiṃ śivayorvidvānna bibheti kutaścana  || 7.2,4.79
yaḥ parāmaparāṃ caiva vibhūtiṃ vetti tattvataḥ  || 7.2,4.80
so 'paro bhūtimullaṃghya parāṃ bhūtiṃ samaśnute  || 7.2,4.80
etatte kathitaṃ kṛṣṇa yāthātmyaṃ paramātmanoḥ  || 7.2,4.81
rahasyamapi yogyo 'si bhargabhakto bhavāniti  || 7.2,4.81
nāśiṣyebhyo 'pyaśaivebhyo nābhaktebhyaḥ kadācana  || 7.2,4.82
vyāharedīśayorbhūtimiti vedānuśāsanam  || 7.2,4.82
tasmāttvamatikalyāṇaparebhyaḥ kathayenna hi  || 7.2,4.83
tvādṛśebhyo 'nurūpebhyaḥ kathayaitanna cānyathā  || 7.2,4.83
vibhūtimetāṃ śivayoryogyebhyo yaḥ pradāpayet || 7.2,4.84
saṃsārasāgarānmuktaḥ śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt || 7.2,4.84
kīrtanādasya naśyaṃti mahāntyaḥ pāpakoṭayaḥ  || 7.2,4.85
triścaturdhāsamabhyastairvinaśyaṃti tato 'dhikāḥ  || 7.2,4.85
naśyaṃtyaniṣṭaripavo vardhante suhṛdastathā  || 7.2,4.86
vidyā ca vardhate śaivī matissatye pravartate  || 7.2,4.86
bhaktiḥ parāḥ śive sāmbe sānuge saparicchide  || 7.2,4.87
yadyadiṣṭatamaṃ cānyattattadāpnotyasaṃśayam  || 7.2,4.87
antaḥśuciḥ śive bhakto visrabdhaḥ kīrtayedyadi  || 7.2,4.88
prabalaiḥ karmabhiḥ pūrvaiḥ phalaṃ cetpratibadhyate  || 7.2,4.88
punaḥ punaḥ samabhyasyettasya nāstīha durllabham  || 7.2,4.88
549

oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe gaurīśaṃkaravibhūtiyogo nāma caturtho 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 5
upamanyuruvāca
vigrahaṃ devadevasya viśvametaccarācaram  || 7.2,5.1
tadevaṃ na vijānaṃti paśavaḥ pāśagauravāt || 7.2,5.1
tamekameva bahudhā vadaṃti yadunaṃdana  || 7.2,5.2
ajānantaḥ paraṃ bhāvamavikalpaṃ maharṣayaḥ  || 7.2,5.2
aparaṃ brahmarūpaṃ ca paraṃ brahmātmakaṃ tathā  || 7.2,5.3
kecidāhurmahādevamanādinidhanaṃ param  || 7.2,5.3
bhūteṃdriyāṃtaḥkaraṇapradhānaviṣayātmakam  || 7.2,5.4
aparaṃ brahma nirdiṣṭaṃ paraṃ brahma cidātmakam  || 7.2,5.4
bṛhattvādbṛhaṇatvādvā brahma cetyabhidhīyate  || 7.2,5.5
ubhe te brahmaṇo rūpe brahmaṇo 'dhipateḥ prabhoḥ  || 7.2,5.5
vidyā 'vidyāsvarūpīti kaiścidīśo nigadyate  || 7.2,5.5
vidyāṃ tu cetanāṃ prāhustathāvidyāmacetanām  || 7.2,5.6
vidyā 'vidyātmakaṃ caiva viśvaṃ viśvagurorvibhoḥ  || 7.2,5.6
rūpameva na saṃdeho viśvaṃ tasya vaśe yataḥ  || 7.2,5.7
bhrāṃtirvidyā parā ceti śārvaṃ rūpaṃ paraṃ viduḥ  || 7.2,5.7
ayathābuddhirartheṣu bahudhā bhrāṃtirucyate  || 7.2,5.8
yathārthākārasaṃvittirvidyeti parikīrtyate  || 7.2,5.8
vikalparahitaṃ tattvaṃ paramityabhidhīyate  || 7.2,5.9
vaiparītyādasacchabdaḥ kathyate vedavādibhiḥ  || 7.2,5.9
tayoḥ patitvāttu śivaḥ sadasatpatirucyate  || 7.2,5.10
kṣarākṣarātmakaṃ prāhuḥ kṣarākṣaraparaṃ pare  || 7.2,5.10
kṣarassarvāṇi bhūtāni kūṭastho 'kṣara ucyate  || 7.2,5.11
ubhe te parameśasya rūpe tasya vaśe yataḥ  || 7.2,5.11
tayoḥ paraḥ śivaḥ śāṃtaḥ kṣarākṣarāparassmṛtaḥ  || 7.2,5.12
samaṣṭivyaṣṭhirūpaṃ ca samaṣṭivyaṣṭikāraṇam  || 7.2,5.12
vadaṃti munayaḥ kecicchivaṃ paramakāraṇam  || 7.2,5.13
samaṣṭimāhuravyaktaṃ vyaṣṭiṃ vyaktaṃ tathaiva ca  || 7.2,5.13
te rūpe parameśasya tadicchāyāḥ pravartanāt || 7.2,5.14
tayoḥ kāraṇabhāvena śivaṃ paramakāraṇam  || 7.2,5.14
kāraṇārthavidaḥ prāhuḥ samaṣṭivyaṣṭikāraṇam  || 7.2,5.15
jātivyaktisvarūpīti kathyate kaiścidīśvaraḥ  || 7.2,5.15
yā piṃḍepyanuvarteta sā jātiriti kathyate  || 7.2,5.16
vyaktirvyāvṛttirūpaṃ taṃ piṇḍajāteḥ samāśrayam  || 7.2,5.16
jātayo vyaktayaścaiva tadājñāparipālitāḥ  || 7.2,5.17
yatastato mahādevo jātivyaktivapuḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.2,5.17
pradhānapuruṣavyaktakālātmā kathyate śivaḥ  || 7.2,5.18
pradhānaṃ prakṛtiṃ prāhuḥkṣetrajñaṃ puruṣaṃ tathā  || 7.2,5.18
trayoviṃśatitattvāni vyaktamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ  || 7.2,5.19
kālaḥ kāryaprapañcasya pariṇāmaikakāraṇam  || 7.2,5.19
eṣāmīśo 'dhipo dhātā pravartakanivartakaḥ  || 7.2,5.20
āvirbhāvatirobhāvaheturekaḥ svarāḍajaḥ  || 7.2,5.20
