tasmātpradhānapuruṣavyaktakālasvarūpavān  || 7.2,5.21
550

heturnetādhipasteṣāṃ dhātā coktā maheśvaraḥ  || 7.2,5.21
virāḍḍhiraṇyagarbhātmā kaiścidīśo nigadyate  || 7.2,5.22
hiraṇyagarbho lokānāṃ heturviśvātmako virāṭ || 7.2,5.22
aṃtaryāmī paraśceti kathyate kavibhiśśivaḥ  || 7.2,5.23
prājñastaijasaviśvātmetyapare saṃpracakṣate  || 7.2,5.23
turīyamapare prāhuḥ saumyameva pare viduḥ  || 7.2,5.24
mātā mānaṃ ca meyaṃ ca matiṃ cāhurathāpare  || 7.2,5.24
kartā kriyā ca kāryaṃ ca karaṇaṃ kāraṇaṃ pare  || 7.2,5.25
jāgratsvapnasuṣuptyātmetyapare saṃpracakṣate  || 7.2,5.25
turīyamapare prāhusturyātītamitītare  || 7.2,5.26
tamāhurviguṇaṃ kecidguṇavantaṃ pare viduḥ  || 7.2,5.26
kecitsaṃsāriṇaṃ prāhustamasaṃsāriṇaṃ pare  || 7.2,5.27
svataṃtramapare prāhurasvataṃtraṃ pare viduḥ  || 7.2,5.27
ghoramityapare prāhuḥ saumyameva pare viduḥ  || 7.2,5.28
rāgavaṃtaṃ pare prāhurvītarāgaṃ tathā pare  || 7.2,5.28
niṣkriyaṃ ca pare prāhuḥ sakriyaṃ cetare janāḥ  || 7.2,5.29
niriṃdriyaṃ pare prāhuḥ seṃdriyaṃ ca tathāpare  || 7.2,5.29
dhruvamityapare prāhustamadhruvāmitīrate  || 7.2,5.30
arūpaṃ kecidāhurvai rūpavaṃtaṃ pare viduḥ  || 7.2,5.30
adṛśyamapare prāhurdṛśyamityapare viduḥ  || 7.2,5.31
vācyamityapare prāhuravācyamiti cāpare  || 7.2,5.31
śabdātmakaṃ pare prāhuśśabdātītamathāpare  || 7.2,5.31
keciccintāmayaṃ prāhuścintayā rahitaṃ pare  || 7.2,5.32
jñānātmakaṃ pare prāhurvijñānamiti cāpare  || 7.2,5.32
kecicjñeyamiti prāhurajñeyamiti kecana  || 7.2,5.33
parameke tamevāhuraparaṃ ca tathā pare  || 7.2,5.33
evaṃ vikalpyamānaṃ tu yāthātmyaṃ parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 7.2,5.34
nādhyavasyaṃti munayo nānāpratyayakāraṇāt || 7.2,5.34
ye punassarvabhāvena prapannāḥ parameśvaram  || 7.2,5.35
te hi jānaṃtyayatnena śivaṃ paramakāraṇam  || 7.2,5.35
yāvatpaśurnaiva paśyatyanīśaṃ 1 purāṇaṃ bhuvanasyeśitāram  || 7.2,5.36
tāvadduḥkhe vartate baddhapāśaḥ saṃsāre 'smiñcakranemikrameṇa  || 7.2,5.36
yadā 2 paśyaḥ paśyate rukmavarṇaṃ kartāramīśaṃ puruṣaṃ brahmayonim  || 7.2,5.37
tadāvidvānpuṇyapāpe vidhūya niraṃjanaḥ paramamupaiti sāmyam  || 7.2,5.37
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe paśupatitvajñānayogo nāma pañcamo 'dhyāyaḥ

1 nāstīśo yasmātso 'nīśa iti bahuvrīhiḥ parantu kliṣṭakalpanāpekṣayā yāvatpaśuśceśvaraṃ na prapaśyediti pāṭhe 'rthassugamaḥ
2 yadā paśyo vīkṣate rukmeti pāṭhe tu bhagnaprakramastasmādātmanepadamārṣamityeva samādhiḥ
550
adhyāya 6
upamanyuruvāca
naśivasyāṇavo baṃdhaḥ kāryo māyeya eva vā  || 7.2,6.1
prākṛto vātha boddhā vā hyahaṃkārātmakastathā  || 7.2,6.1
naivāsya mānaso baṃdho na caitto neṃdriyātmakaḥ  || 7.2,6.2
na ca tanmātrabaṃdho 'pi bhūtabaṃdho na kaścana  || 7.2,6.2
na ca kālaḥ kalā caiva na vidyā niyatistathā  || 7.2,6.3
na rāgo na ca vidveṣaḥ śaṃbhoramitatejasaḥ  || 7.2,6.3
na cāstyabhiniveśo 'sya kuśalā 'kuśalānyapi  || 7.2,6.4
karmāṇi tadvipākaśca sukhaduḥkhe ca tatphale  || 7.2,6.4
āśayairnāpi saṃbandhaḥ saṃskāraiḥ karmaṇāmapi  || 7.2,6.5
bhogaiśca bhogasaṃskāraiḥ kālatritayagocaraiḥ  || 7.2,6.5
na tasya kāraṇaṃ kartā nādiraṃtastathāṃtaram  || 7.2,6.6
na karma karaṇaṃ vāpi nākāryaṃ kāryameva ca  || 7.2,6.6
nāsya baṃdhurabaṃdhurvā niyaṃtā prerako 'pi vā  || 7.2,6.7
na patirna gurustrātā nādhiko na samastathā  || 7.2,6.7
na janmamaraṇe tasya na kāṃkṣitamakāṃkṣitam  || 7.2,6.8
na vidhirna niṣedhaśca na muktirna ca bandhanam  || 7.2,6.8
nāsti yadyadakalyāṇaṃ tattadasya kadācana  || 7.2,6.9
kalyāṇaṃ sakalaṃ cāsti paramātmā śivo yataḥ  || 7.2,6.9
sa śivassarvamevedamadhiṣṭhāya svaśaktibhiḥ  || 7.2,6.10
apracyutassvato bhāvaḥ sthitaḥ sthāṇurataḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.2,6.10
śivenādhiṣṭhitaṃ yasmājjagatsthāvarajaṃgamam  || 7.2,6.11
sarvarūpaḥ smṛtaśśarvastathā jñātvā na muhyati  || 7.2,6.11
śarvo rudro namastasmai puruṣaḥ satparo mahān  || 7.2,6.12
hiraṇyabāhurbhagavānhiraṇyapatirīśvaraḥ  || 7.2,6.12
aṃbikāpatirīśānaḥ pinākī vṛṣavāhanaḥ  || 7.2,6.13
eko rudraḥ paraṃ brahma puruṣaḥ kṛṣṇapiṃgalaḥ  || 7.2,6.13
bālāgramātro hṛnmadhye viciṃtyo daharāṃtare  || 7.2,6.14
hiraṇyakeśaḥ padmākṣo hyaruṇastāmra eva ca  || 7.2,6.14
yo 'vasarpatya sau devo nīlagrīvo hiraṇmayaḥ  || 7.2,6.15
saumyo ghorastathā miśraścākṣāraścāmṛto 'vyayaḥ  || 7.2,6.15
sa puṃviśeṣaḥ paramo bhagavānantakāṃtakaḥ  || 7.2,6.16
cetanacetanonmuktaḥ prapañcācca parātparaḥ  || 7.2,6.16
śivenātiśayatvena jñānaiśvarye vilokite  || 7.2,6.17
lokeśātiśayatvena sthitaṃ prāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ  || 7.2,6.17
pratisargaprasūtānāṃ brahmaṇāṃ śāstravistaram  || 7.2,6.18
upadeṣṭā sa evādau kālāvacchedavartinām  || 7.2,6.18
kālāvacchedayuktānāṃ gurūṇāmapyasau guruḥ  || 7.2,6.19
sarveṣāmeva sarveśaḥ kālāvacchedavarjitaḥ  || 7.2,6.19
śuddhā svābhāvikī tasya śaktissarvātiśāyinī  || 7.2,6.20
jñānamapratimaṃ nityaṃ vapuratyantanirmitam  || 7.2,6.20
aiśvaryamapratidvaṃdvaṃ sukhamātyantikaṃ balam  || 7.2,6.21
tejaḥprabhāvo vīryaṃ ca kṣamā kāruṇyameva ca  || 7.2,6.21
paripūrṇasya sargādyairnātmano 'sti prayojanam  || 7.2,6.22
parānugraha evāsya phalaṃ sarvasya karmaṇaḥ  || 7.2,6.22
551

praṇavo vācakastasya śivasya paramātmanaḥ  || 7.2,6.23
śivarudrādiśabdānāṃ praṇavo hi parassmṛtaḥ  || 7.2,6.23
śaṃbho praṇavavācyasya bhavanāttajjapādapi  || 7.2,6.24
yā siddhissā parā prāpyā bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,6.24
tasmādekākṣaraṃ devamāhurāgamapāragāḥ  || 7.2,6.25
vācyavācakayoraikyaṃ manyamānā manasvinaḥ  || 7.2,6.25
asya mātrāḥ samākhyātāścatasro vedamūrdhani  || 7.2,6.26
akāraścāpyukāraśca makāro nāda ityapi  || 7.2,6.26
akāraṃ bahvṛcaṃ prāhurukāro yajurucyate  || 7.2,6.27
makāraḥ sāmanādosya śrutirātharvaṇī smṛtāḥ  || 7.2,6.27
akāraśca mahābījaṃ rajaḥ sraṣṭā caturmukhaḥ  || 7.2,6.28
ukāraḥ prakṛtiryoniḥ sattvaṃ pālayitā hariḥ  || 7.2,6.28
makāraḥ puruṣo bījaṃ tamaḥ saṃhārako haraḥ  || 7.2,6.29
nādaḥ paraḥ pumānīśo nirguṇo niṣkriyaḥ śivaḥ  || 7.2,6.29
sarvaṃ tisṛbhirevedaṃ mātrābhirnikhilaṃ tridhā  || 7.2,6.30
abhidhāya śivātmānaṃ bodhayatyardhamātrayā  || 7.2,6.30
yasmātparaṃ nāparamasti kiṃcidyasmānnāṇīyo na jyāyo 'sti kiṃcit || 7.2,6.31
vṛkṣa iva stabdho divi tiṣṭhatyekastenedaṃ pūrṇaṃ puruṣeṇa sarvam  || 7.2,6.31
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe śivatattvavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṣṭho 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 7
upamanyuruvaca
śaktissvābhavikī tasya vidyā viśvavilakṣaṇā  || 7.2,7.1
ekānekasya rūpeṇa bhāti bhānoriva prabhā  || 7.2,7.1
anaṃtāḥ śaktayo yasyā icchājñānakriyādayaḥ  || 7.2,7.2
māyādyāścābhavanvahnorvisphuliṃgā yathā tathā  || 7.2,7.2
sadāśiveśvarādyā hi vidyā 'vidyeśvarādayaḥ  || 7.2,7.3
abhavanpuruṣāścāsyāḥ prakṛtiśca parātparā  || 7.2,7.3
mahadādiviśeṣāṃtāstvajādyāścāpi mūrtayaḥ  || 7.2,7.4
yaccānyadasti tatsarvaṃ tasyāḥ kāryaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,7.4
sā śaktissarvagā sūkṣmā prabodhānaṃdarūpiṇī  || 7.2,7.5
śaktimānucyate devaśśivaśśītāṃśubhūṣaṇaḥ  || 7.2,7.5
vedyaśśivaśśivā vidyā prajñā caiva śrutiḥ smṛtiḥ  || 7.2,7.6
dhṛtireṣā sthitirniṣṭhā jñānecchākarmaśaktayaḥ  || 7.2,7.6
ājñā caiva paraṃ brahma dve vidye ca parāpare  || 7.2,7.7
śuddhavidyā śuddhakalā sarvaṃ śaktikṛtaṃ yataḥ  || 7.2,7.7
māyā ca prakṛtirjīvo vikāro vikṛtistathā  || 7.2,7.8
asacca sacca yatkiṃcittayā sarvamidaṃ tatam  || 7.2,7.8
sā devī māyayā sarvaṃ brahmāṃḍaṃ sacarācaram  || 7.2,7.9
mohayatyaprayatnena mocayatyapi līlayā  || 7.2,7.9
anayā saha sarveśaḥ saptaviṃśaprakārayā  || 7.2,7.10
viśvaṃ vyāpya sthitastasmānmuktiratra pravartate  || 7.2,7.10
mumukṣavaḥ purā kecinmunayo brahmavādinaḥ  || 7.2,7.11
saṃśayāviṣṭamanaso vismṛśaṃti yathātatham  || 7.2,7.11
551

kiṃ kāraṇaṃ kuto jātā jīvāmaḥ kena vā vayam  || 7.2,7.12
kutrāsmākaṃ saṃpratiṣṭhā kena vādhiṣṭhitā vayam  || 7.2,7.12
kena vartāmahe śaśvatsukheṣvanyeṣu cāniśam  || 7.2,7.13
avilaṃghyā ca viśvasya vyavasthā kena vā kṛtā  || 7.2,7.13
kālasya bhāvo niyatiryadṛcchā nātra yujyate  || 7.2,7.14
bhūtāni yoniḥ puruṣo yogī caiṣāṃ paro 'tha vā  || 7.2,7.14
acetanatvātkālādeścetanatvepi cātmanaḥ  || 7.2,7.15
sukhaduḥkhāni bhūtatvādanīśatvādvicāryate  || 7.2,7.15
taddhyānayogānugatāṃ prapaśyañchaktimaiśvarīm  || 7.2,7.16
pāśavicchedikāṃ sākṣānnigūḍhāṃ svaguṇairbhṛśam  || 7.2,7.16
tayā vicchinnapāśāste sarvakāraṇakāraṇam  || 7.2,7.17
śaktimaṃtaṃ mahādevamapaśyandivyacakṣuṣā  || 7.2,7.17
yaḥ kāraṇānyaśeṣāṇi kālātmasahitāni ca  || 7.2,7.18
aprameyo 'nayā śaktyā sakalaṃ yo 'dhitiṣṭhati  || 7.2,7.18
tataḥ prasādayogena yogena parameṇa ca  || 7.2,7.19
dṛṣṭena bhaktiyogena divyaḥ gatimavāpnuyuḥ  || 7.2,7.19
tasmātsaha tathā śaktyā hṛdi paśyaṃti ye śivam  || 7.2,7.20
teṣāṃ śāśvatikī śāṃtirnaitareṣāmiti śrutiḥ  || 7.2,7.20
na hi śaktimataśśaktyā viprayogo 'sti jātucit || 7.2,7.21
tasmācchakteḥ śaktimatastādātmyānnirvṛtirdvayoḥ  || 7.2,7.21
kramo vivakṣito nūnaṃ vimuktau jñānakarmaṇoḥ  || 7.2,7.22
prasāde sati sā mūrtiryasmātkaratale sthitā  || 7.2,7.22
devo vā dānavo vāpi paśurvā vihago 'pi vā  || 7.2,7.23
kīro vātha kṛmirvāpi mucyate tatprasādataḥ  || 7.2,7.23
garbhastho jāyamāno vā bālo vā taruṇo.pi vā  || 7.2,7.24
vṛddho vā mriyamāṇo vā svargastho vātha nārakī  || 7.2,7.24
patito vāpi dharmātmā paṃḍito mūḍha eva vā  || 7.2,7.25
prasāde tatkṣaṇādeva mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,7.25
ayogyānāṃ ca kāruṇyādbhaktānāṃ parameśvaraḥ  || 7.2,7.26
prasīdati na saṃdeho vigṛhya vividhānmalān  || 7.2,7.26
prasadādeva sā bhaktiḥ prasādo bhaktisaṃbhavaḥ  || 7.2,7.27
avasthābhedamutprekṣya vidvāṃstatra na muhyati  || 7.2,7.27
prasādapūrvikā yeyaṃ bhuktimuktividhāyinī  || 7.2,7.28
naiva sā śakyate prāptuṃ narairekena janmanā  || 7.2,7.28
anekajanmasiddhānāṃ śrautasmārtānuvartinām  || 7.2,7.29
viraktānāṃ prabuddhānāṃ prasīdati maheśvaraḥ  || 7.2,7.29
prasanne sati deveśa paśau tasminpravartate  || 7.2,7.30
asti nātho mametyalpā bhaktirbuddhipurassarā  || 7.2,7.30
tapasā vividhaiśśaivairdharmaissaṃyujyate naraḥ  || 7.2,7.31
tatra yoge tadabhyāsastato bhaktiḥ parā bhavet || 7.2,7.31
parayā ca tayā bhaktyā prasādo labhyate paraḥ  || 7.2,7.32
prasādātsarvapāśebhyo muktirmuktasya nirvṛtiḥ  || 7.2,7.32
552

alpabhāvo 'pi yo martyasso 'pi janmatrayātparam  || 7.2,7.33
nayoniyaṃtrapīḍāyai bhavennaivātra saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,7.33
sāṃgā 'naṃgā ca yā sevā sā bhaktiriti kathyate  || 7.2,7.34
sā punarbhidyate tredhā manovākkāyasādhanaiḥ  || 7.2,7.34
śivarūpādiciṃtā yā sā sevā mānasī smṛtā  || 7.2,7.35
japādirvācikī sevā karmapūjādi kāyikī  || 7.2,7.35
seyaṃ trisādhanā sevā śivadharmaśca kathyate  || 7.2,7.36
sa tu pañcavidhaḥ proktaḥ śivena paramātmanā  || 7.2,7.36
tapaḥ karma japo dhyānaṃ jñānaṃ ceti samāsataḥ  || 7.2,7.37
karmaliṅgārcanādyaṃ ca tapaścāndrāyaṇādikam  || 7.2,7.37
japastridhā śivābhyāsaścintā dhyānaṃ śivasya tu  || 7.2,7.38
śivāgamoktaṃ yajjñānaṃ tadatra jñānamucyate  || 7.2,7.38
śrīkaṃṭhena śivenoktaṃ śivāyai ca śivāgamaḥ  || 7.2,7.39
śivāśritānāṃ kāruṇyācchreyasāmekasādhanam  || 7.2,7.39
tasmādvivardhayedbhaktiṃ śive paramakāraṇe  || 7.2,7.40
tyajecca viṣayāsaṃgaṃ śreyo 'rthī matimānnaraḥ  || 7.2,7.40
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe śivatattvakathanaṃ nāma saptamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 8
kṛṣṇa uvāca
bhagavañchrotumicchāmi śivena paribhāṣitam  || 7.2,8.1
vedasāre śivajñānaṃ svāśritānāṃ vimuktaye  || 7.2,8.1
abhaktānāmabuddhīnāmayuktānāmagocaram  || 7.2,8.2
arthairdaśardhaiḥ saṃyuktaṃ gūḍhamaprājñaniṃditam  || 7.2,8.2
varṇāśramakṛtairdharmairviparītaṃ kvacitsamam  || 7.2,8.3
vedātṣaḍaṃgāduddhṛtya sāṃkhyādyogācca kṛtsnaśaḥ  || 7.2,8.3
śatakoṭipramāṇena vistīrṇaṃ graṃthasaṃkhyayā  || 7.2,8.4
kathitaṃ parameśena tatra pūjā kathaṃ prabhoḥ  || 7.2,8.4
kasyādhikāraḥ pūjādau jñānayogādayaḥ katham  || 7.2,8.5
tatsarvaṃ vistarādeva vaktumarhasi suvrata  || 7.2,8.5
upamanyuruvāca
śaivaṃ saṃkṣipya vedoktaṃ śivena paribhāṣitam  || 7.2,8.6
stutiniṃdādirahitaṃ sadyaḥ pratyayakāraṇam  || 7.2,8.6
guruprasādajaṃ divyamanāyāsena muktidam  || 7.2,8.7
kathayiṣye samāsena tasya śakyo na vistaraḥ  || 7.2,8.7
sisṛkṣayā purāvyaktācchivaḥ sthāṇurmaheśvaraḥ  || 7.2,8.8
satkāryakāraṇopetassvayamāvirabhūtprabhuḥ  || 7.2,8.8
janayāmāsa ca tadā ṛṣirviśvādhikaḥ prabhuḥ  || 7.2,8.9
devānāṃ prathamaṃ devaṃ brahmāṇaṃ brahmaṇaspatim  || 7.2,8.9
brahmāpi pitaraṃ devaṃ jāyamānaṃ nyavaikṣata  || 7.2,8.10
taṃ jāyamānaṃ janako devaḥ prāpaśyadājñayā  || 7.2,8.10
dṛṣṭo rudreṇa devo 'sāvasṛjadviśvamīśvaraḥ  || 7.2,8.11
varṇāśramavyavasthāṃ ca cakāra sa pṛthakpṛthak || 7.2,8.11
somaṃ sasarja yajñārthe somāddyaussamajāyata  || 7.2,8.12
dharā ca vahniḥ sūryaśca yajño viṣṇuśśacīpatiḥ  || 7.2,8.12
552

te cānye ca surā rudraṃ rudrādhyāyena tuṣṭuvuḥ  || 7.2,8.13
prasannavadanastasthau devānāmagrataḥ prabhuḥ  || 7.2,8.13
apahṛtya svalīlārthaṃ teṣāṃ jñānaṃ maheśvaraḥ  || 7.2,8.14
tamapṛcchaṃstato devāḥ ko bhavāniti mohitāḥ  || 7.2,8.14
so 'bravīdbhagavānrudro hyahamekaḥ purātanaḥ  || 7.2,8.15
āsaṃ prathamamevāhaṃ vartāmi 1 ca surottamāḥ  || 7.2,8.15
bhaviṣyāmi ca mattonyo vyatirikto na kaścana  || 7.2,8.16
ahameva jagatsarvaṃ tarpayāmi svatejasā  || 7.2,8.16
matto 'dhikaḥ samo nāsti māṃ yo veda sa mucyate  || 7.2,8.17
ityuktvā bhagavānrudrastatraivāṃtaradhatta sa  || 7.2,8.17
apaśyaṃtastamīśānaṃ stuvaṃtaścaiva sāmabhiḥ  || 7.2,8.17
vrataṃ pāśupataṃ kṛtvā tvatharvaśirasi sthitam  || 7.2,8.18
bhasmasaṃchannasarvāṃgā babhūvuramarāstadā  || 7.2,8.18
atha teṣāṃ prasādārthaṃ paśūnāṃ patirīśvaraḥ  || 7.2,8.19
sagaṇaścomayā sārdhaṃ sānnidhyamakarotprabhuḥ  || 7.2,8.20
yaṃ vinidrā jitaśvāsā yogino dagdhakilbiṣāḥ  || 7.2,8.20
hṛdi paśyaṃti taṃ devaṃ dadṛśurdevapuṃgavāḥ  || 7.2,8.21
yāmāhuḥ paramāṃ śaktimīśvarecchānuvartinīm  || 7.2,8.21
tāmapaśyanmaheśasya vāmato vāmalocanām  || 7.2,8.22
ye vinirdhūtasaṃsārāḥ prāptāḥ śaivaṃ paraṃ padam  || 7.2,8.22
nityasiddhāśca ye vānyaṃ te ca dṛṣṭā gaṇeśvarāḥ  || 7.2,8.23
atha taṃ tuṣṭuvurdevā devyā saha maheśvaram  || 7.2,8.23
stotrairmāheśvarairdivyaiḥ śrotaiḥ paurāṇikairapi  || 7.2,8.24
devo 'pi devānālokya ghṛṇayā vṛṣabhadhvajaḥ  || 7.2,8.24
tuṣṭo 'smītyāha suprītassvabhāvamadhurāṃ giram  || 7.2,8.25
atha suprītamanasaṃ praṇipatya vṛṣadhvajam  || 7.2,8.25
arthamahattamaṃ devāḥ papracchurimamādarāt || 7.2,8.25
devā ūcuḥ
bhagavankena mārgeṇa pūjanīyo 'si bhūtale  || 7.2,8.26
kasyādhikāraḥ pūjāyāṃ vaktumarhasi tattvataḥ  || 7.2,8.26
tataḥ sasmitamālokya devīṃ devavaroharaḥ  || 7.2,8.27
svarūpaṃ darśayāmāsa ghoraṃ sūryātmakaṃ param  || 7.2,8.27
sarvaiśvaryaguṇopetaṃ sarvatejomayaṃ param  || 7.2,8.28
śaktibhirmūrtibhiścāṃgairgrahairdevaiśca saṃvṛtam  || 7.2,8.28
aṣṭabāhuṃ caturvaktramardhanārīkamadbhutam  || 7.2,8.29
dṛṣṭvaivamadbhutākāraṃ devā viṣṇupurogamāḥ  || 7.2,8.29
buddhvā divākaraṃ devaṃ devīṃ caiva niśākaram  || 7.2,8.30
pañcabhūtāni śeṣāṇi tanmayaṃ ca carācaram  || 7.2,8.30
evamuktvā namaścakrustasmai cārghyaṃ pradāya vai  || 7.2,831
siṃdūravarṇāya sumaṇḍalāya suvarṇavarṇābharaṇāya tubhyam  || 7.2,8.32
padmābhanetrāya sapaṃkajāya brahmendranārāyaṇakāraṇāya  || 7.2,8.32
suratnapūrṇaṃ sasuvarṇatoyaṃ sukuṃkumādyaṃ sakuśaṃ sapuṣpam  || 7.2,8.33
pradattamādāya sahemapātraṃ praśastamarghyaṃ bhagavanprasīda  || 7.2,8.33
namaśśivāya śāṃtāya sagaṇāyādihetave  || 7.2,8.34

1 parasmaipadamārṣam

rudrāya viṣṇave tubhyaṃ brahmaṇe sūryamūrtaye  || 7.2,8.34
yaśśivaṃ maṇḍale saure saṃpūjyaiva samāhitaḥ  || 7.2,8.35
prātarmadhyāhnasāyāhne pradadyādarghyamuttamam  || 7.2,8.35
praṇamedvā paṭhedetāñchlokāñchrutimukhānimān  || 7.2,8.36
na tasya durllabhaṃ kiṃcidbhaktaścenmucyate dṛḍham  || 7.2,8.36
tasmādabhyarcayenityaṃ śivamādityarūpiṇam  || 7.2,8.37
dharmakāmārthamuktyarthaṃ manasā karmaṇā girā  || 7.2,8.37
atha devānsamālokya maṇḍalastho maheśvaraḥ  || 7.2,8.38
sarvāgamottaraṃ dattvā śāstramaṃtaradhāddharaḥ  || 7.2,8.38
tatra pūjādhikāro 'yaṃ brahmakṣatraviśāmiti  || 7.2,8.39
jñātvā praṇamya deveśaṃ devā jagmuryathāgatam  || 7.2,8.39
atha kālena mahatā tasmiñchāstre tirohite  || 7.2,8.40
bhartāraṃ paripapraccha tadaṃkasthā maheśvarī  || 7.2,8.40
tayā sa codito devo devyā candravibhūṣaṇaḥ  || 7.2,8.41
avadatkaramuddhṛtya śāstraṃ sarvāgamottaram  || 7.2,8.41
pravartitaṃ ca talloke niyogātparameṣṭhinaḥ  || 7.2,8.42
mayāgastyena guruṇā dadhīcena maharṣiṇā  || 7.2,8.42
svayamapyavatīryorvyāṃ yugāvarteṣu śūladhṛk || 7.2,8.43
svāśritānāṃ vimuktyarthaṃ kurute jñānasaṃtatim  || 7.2,8.43
ṛbhussatyo bhārgavaśca hyaṃgirāḥ savitā dvijāḥ  || 7.2,8.44
mṛtyuḥ śatakraturdhīmānvasiṣṭho munipuṃgavaḥ  || 7.2,8.44
sārasvatastridhāmā ca trivṛto munipuṃgavaḥ  || 7.2,8.45
śatatejāssvayaṃ dharmo nārāyaṇa iti śrutaḥ  || 7.2,8.45
svarakṣaścāruṇirdhīmāṃstathā caiva kṛtaṃjayaḥ  || 7.2,8.46
kṛtaṃjayo bharadvājo gautamaḥ kaviruttamaḥ  || 7.2,8.46
vācaḥsravā munissākṣāttathā sūkṣmāyaṇiḥ śuciḥ  || 7.2,8.47
tṛṇabiṃdurmuniḥ kṛṣṇaḥ śaktiḥ śākteya uttaraḥ  || 7.2,8.47
jātūkarṇyo harissākṣātkṛṣṇadvaipāyano muniḥ  || 7.2,8.48
vyāsāvatārāñchṛṇvaṃtu kalpayogeśvarānkramāt || 7.2,8.48
laiṃge vyāsāvatārā hi dvāparāṃ teṣu suvratāḥ  || 7.2,8.49
yogācāryāvatārāśca tathā śiṣyeṣu śūlinaḥ  || 7.2,8.49
tatra tatra vibhoḥ śiṣyāścatvāraḥ syurmahaujasaḥ  || 7.2,8.50
śiṣyāsteṣāṃ praśiṣyāśca śataśo 'tha sahasraśaḥ  || 7.2,8.50
teṣāṃ saṃbhāvanālloke śaivājñākaraṇādibhiḥ  || 7.2,8.51
bhāgyavaṃto vimucyaṃte bhaktyā cātyaṃtabhāvitāḥ  || 7.2,8.51
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe śivatattvajñāne vyāsāvatāravarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 9
kṛṣṇa uvāca
yugāvarteṣu sarveṣu yogācāryacchalena tu  || 7.2,9.1
avatārānhi śarvasya śiṣyāṃśca bhagavanvada  || 7.2,9.1
upamanyuruvāca
śvetaḥ sutāro madanaḥ suhotraḥ kaṅka eva ca  || 7.2,9.2
laugākṣiśca mahāmāyo jaigīṣavyastathaiva ca  || 7.2,9.2
dadhivāhaśca ṛṣabho munirugro 'trireva ca  || 7.2,9.3
553

supālako gautamaśca tathā vedaśirā muniḥ  || 7.2,9.3
gokarṇaśca guhāvāsī śikhaṇḍī cāparaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.2,9.4
jaṭāmālī cāṭṭahāso dāruko lāṃgulī tathā  || 7.2,9.4
mahākālaśca śūlī ca ḍaṃḍī muṇḍīśa eva ca  || 7.2,9.5
saviṣṇussomaśarmā ca lakulīśvara eva ca  || 7.2,9.5
ete vārāha kalpe 'sminsaptamasyāṃtaro manoḥ  || 7.2,9.6
aṣṭāviṃśatisaṃkhyātā yogācāryā yugakramāt || 7.2,9.6
śiṣyāḥ pratyekameteṣāṃ catvāraśśāṃtacetasaḥ  || 7.2,9.7
śvetādayaśca ruṣyāṃtāṃstānbravīmi yathākramam  || 7.2,9.7
śvetaśśvetaśikhaścaiva śvetāśvaḥ śvetalohitaḥ  || 7.2,9.8
dundubhiśśatarūpaśca ṛcīkaḥ ketumāṃstathā  || 7.2,9.8
vikośaśca vikeśaśca vipāśaḥ pāśanāśanaḥ  || 7.2,9.9
sumukho durmukhaścaiva durgamo duratikramaḥ  || 7.2,9.9
sanatkumārassanakaḥ sanaṃdaśca sanātanaḥ  || 7.2,9.10
sudhāmā virajāścaiva śaṃkhaścāṃḍaja eva ca  || 7.2,9.10
sārasvataśca meghaśca meghavāhassuvāhakaḥ  || 7.2,9.11
kapilaścāsuriḥ pañcaśikho bāṣkala eva ca  || 7.2,9.11
parāśarāśca gargaśca bhārgavaścāṃgirāstathā  || 7.2,9.12
balabandhurnirāmitrāḥ ketuśṛṃgastapodhanaḥ  || 7.2,9.12
laṃbodaraśca laṃbaśca lambātmā laṃbakeśakaḥ  || 7.2,9.13
sarvajñassamabuddhiśca sādhyasiddhistathaiva ca  || 7.2,9.13
sudhāmā kaśyapaścaiva vasiṣṭho virajāstathā  || 7.2,9.14
atrirugro guruśreṣṭhaḥ śravanotha śraviṣṭakaḥ  || 7.2,9.14
kuṇiśca kuṇibāhuśca kuśarīraḥ kunetrakaḥ  || 7.2,9.15
kāśyapo hyuśanāścaiva cyavanaśca bṛhaspatiḥ  || 7.2,9.15
utathyo vāmadevaśca mahākālo mahā 'nilaḥ  || 7.2,9.16
vācaḥśravāḥ suvīraśca śyāvakaśca yatīśvaraḥ  || 7.2,9.16
hiraṇyanābhaḥ kauśalyo lokākṣiḥ kuthumistathā  || 7.2,9.17
sumanturjaiminiścaiva kubandhaḥ kuśakandharaḥ  || 7.2,9.17
plakṣo dārbhāyaṇiścaiva ketumāngautamastathā  || 7.2,9.18
bhallavī madhupiṃgaśca śvetaketustathaiva ca  || 7.2,9.18
uśijo bṛhadaśvaśca devalaḥ kavireva ca  || 7.2,9.19
śālihotraḥ suveṣaśca yuvanāśvaḥ śaradvasuḥ  || 7.2,9.19
akṣapādaḥ kaṇādaśca ulūko vatsa eva ca  || 7.2,9.20
kulikaścaiva gargaśca mitrako ruṣya eva ca  || 7.2,9.20
ete śiṣyā maheśasya yogācāryasvarūpiṇaḥ  || 7.2,9.21
saṃkhyā ca śatameteṣāṃ saha dvādaśasaṃkhyayā  || 7.2,9.21
sarve pāśupatāḥ siddhā bhasmoddhūlitavigrahāḥ  || 7.2,9.22
sarvaśāstrārthatattvajñā vedavedāṃgapāragāḥ  || 7.2,9.22
śivāśramaratāssarve śivajñānaparāyaṇāḥ  || 7.2,9.23
sarve saṃgavinirmuktāḥ śivaikāsaktacetasaḥ  || 7.2,9.23
sarvadvaṃdvasahā dhīrāḥ sarvabhūtahite ratāḥ  || 7.2,9.24
ṛjavo mṛdavaḥ svasthā jitakrodhā jiteṃdriyāḥ  || 7.2,9.24
rudrākṣamālābharaṇāstripuṃḍrāṃkitamastakāḥ  || 7.2,9.25
śikhājaṭāssarvajaṭā ajaṭā muṃḍaśīrṣakāḥ  || 7.2,9.25
phalamūlāśanaprāyāḥ prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇāḥ  || 7.2,9.26
554

śivābhimānasaṃpannāḥ śivadhyānaikatatparāḥ  || 7.2,9.26
samunmathitasaṃsāraviṣavṛkṣāṃkurodgamāḥ  || 7.2,9.27
prayātumeva sannaddhāḥ paraṃ śivapuraṃ prati  || 7.2,9.27
sadeśikānimānmatvā nityaṃ yaśśivamarcayet || 7.2,9.28
sa yāti śivasāyujyaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 7.2,9.28

oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpu saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe śivasya yogāvatāravarṇanaṃ nāma navamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 10
kṛṣṇa uvāca
bhagavansarvayogīṃdra gaṇeśvara munīśvara  || 7.2,10.1
ṣaḍānanasamaprakhya sarvajñānanidhe guro  || 7.2,10.1
prāyastvamavatīryorvyāṃ pāśavicchittaye nṛṇām  || 7.2,10.2
maharṣivapurāsthāya sthito 'si parameśvara  || 7.2,10.2
anyathā hi jagatyasmin devo vā dānavo 'pi vā  || 7.2,10.3
tvattonyaḥ paramaṃ bhāvaṃ ko jānīyācchivātmakam  || 7.2,10.3
tasmāttava mukhodgīrṇaṃ sākṣādiva pinākinaḥ  || 7.2,10.4
śivajñānāmṛtaṃ pītvā na me tṛptamabhūnmanaḥ  || 7.2,10.4
sākṣātsarvajagatkarturbharturaṃkaṃ samāśritā  || 7.2,10.5
bhagavankinnu papraccha bhartāraṃ parameśvarī  || 7.2,10.5
upamanyuruvāca
sthāne pṛṣṭaṃ tvayā kṛṣṇa tadvakṣyāmi yathātatham  || 7.2,10.6
bhavabhaktasya yuktasya tava kalyāṇacetasaḥ  || 7.2,10.6
mahīdharavare divye maṃdare cārukaṃdare  || 7.2,10.7
devyā saha mahādevo divyo dhyānagato 'bhavat || 7.2,10.7
tadā devyāḥ priyasakhī susmitāsyā śubhāvatī  || 7.2,10.8
phullānyatimanojñāni puṣpāṇi samudāharat || 7.2,10.8
tataḥ svamaṃkamāropya devīṃ devavarorahaḥ  || 7.2,10.9
alaṃkṛtya ca taiḥ puṣpairāste hṛṣṭataraḥ svayam  || 7.2,10.9
athāṃtaḥpuracāriṇyo devyo divyavibhūṣaṇāḥ  || 7.2,10.10
aṃtaraṃgā gaṇendrāśca sarvalokamaheśvarīm  || 7.2,10.10
bhartāraṃ paripūrṇaṃ ca sarvalokamaheśvaram  || 7.2,10.11
cāmarāsaktahastāśca devīṃ devaṃ siṣevire  || 7.2,10.11
tataḥ priyāḥ kathā vṛttā vinodāya maheśayoḥ  || 7.2,10.12
trāṇāya ca nṛṇāṃ loke ye śivaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ  || 7.2,10.12
tadāvasaramālokya sarvalokamaheśvarī  || 7.2,10.13
bhartāraṃ paripapraccha sarvalokamaheśvaram  || 7.2,10.13
devyuvāca
kena vaśyo mahādevo martyānāṃ maṃdacetasām  || 7.2,10.14
ātmatattvādyaśaktānāmātmanāmakṛtātmanām  || 7.2,10.14
īśvara uvāca
na karmaṇā na tapasā na japairnāsanādibhiḥ  || 7.2,10.15
na jñānena na cānyena vaśyo 'haṃ śraddhayā vinā  || 7.2,10.15
śraddhā mayyasti cetpuṃsāṃ yena kenāpi hetunā  || 7.2,10.16
vaśyaḥ spṛśyaśca dṛśyaśca pūjyassaṃbhāṣya eva ca  || 7.2,10.16
sādhyā tasmānmayi śaddhā māṃ vaśīkartumicchatā  || 7.2,10.17
śraddhā hetussvadharmasya rakṣaṇaṃ varṇināmiha  || 7.2,10.17
svavarṇāśramadharmeṇa vartate yastu mānavaḥ  || 7.2,10.18
tasyaiva bhavati śraddhā mayi nānyasya kasyacit || 7.2,10.18
554

āmnāyasiddhamakhilaṃ dharmamāśramiṇāmiha  || 7.2,10.19
brahmaṇā kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ mamaivājñāpurassaram  || 7.2,10.19
sa tu paitāmaho dharmo bahuvittakriyānvitaḥ  || 7.2,10.20
nātyanta phalabhūyiṣṭhaḥ kleśāyā sasamanvitaḥ  || 7.2,10.20
tena dharmeṇa mahatāṃ śraddhāṃ prāpya sudurllabhām  || 7.2,10.20
varṇino ye prapadyaṃte māmananyasamāśrayāḥ  || 7.2,10.21
teṣāṃ sukhena mārgeṇa dharmakāmārthamuktayaḥ  || 7.2,10.21
varṇāśramasamācāro mayā bhūyaḥ prakalpitaḥ  || 7.2,10.22
tasminbhaktimatāmeva madīyānāṃ tu varṇinām  || 7.2,10.22
adhikāro na cānyeṣāmityājñā naiṣṭhikī mama  || 7.2,10.23
tadājñaptena mārgeṇa varṇino madupāśrayāḥ  || 7.2,10.23
malamāyādipāśebhyo vimuktā matprasādataḥ  || 7.2,10.24
paraṃ madīyamāsādya punarāvṛttidurlabham  || 7.2,10.24
paramaṃ mama sādharmyaṃ prāpya nirvṛtimāyayuḥ  || 7.2,10.24
tasmāllabdhvāpyalabdhvā vā varṇadharmaṃ mayeritam  || 7.2,10.25
āśritya mama bhaktaścetsvātmanātmānamuddharet || 7.2,10.25
alabdhalābha evaiṣa koṭikoṭiguṇādhikaḥ  || 7.2,10.26
tasmānme mukhato labdhaṃ varṇadharmaṃ samācaret || 7.2,10.26
mamāvatārā hi śubhe yogācāryacchalena tu  || 7.2,10.27
sarvāṃtareṣu santyārye saṃtatiśca sahasraśaḥ  || 7.2,10.27
ayuktānāmabuddhīnāmabhaktānāṃ sureśvari  || 7.2,10.28
durlabhaṃ saṃtatijñānaṃ tato yatnātsamāśrayet || 7.2,10.28
sā hānistanmahacchidraṃ sa mohassāṃdhamūkatā  || 7.2,10.29
yadanyatra śramaṃ kuryānmokṣamārgabahiṣkṛtaḥ  || 7.2,10.29
jñānaṃ kriyā ca caryā ca yogaśceti sureśvari  || 7.2,10.30
catuṣpādaḥ samākhyāto mama dharmassanātanaḥ  || 7.2,10.30
paśupāśapatijñānaṃ jñānamityabhidhīyate  || 7.2,10.31
ṣaḍadhvaśuddhirvidhinā gurvadhīnā kriyocyate  || 7.2,10.31
varṇāśramaprayuktasya mayaiva vihitasya ca  || 7.2,10.32
mamārcanādidharmasya caryā caryeti kathyate  || 7.2,10.32
maduktenaiva mārgeṇa mayyavasthitacetasaḥ  || 7.2,10.33
vṛttyaṃtaranirodho yo yoga ityabhidhīyate  || 7.2,10.33
aśvamedhagaṇācchreṣṭhaṃ devi cittaprasādhanam  || 7.2,10.34
muktidaṃ ca tathā hyetadduṣprāpyaṃ viṣayaiṣiṇām  || 7.2,10.34
vijiteṃdriyavargasya yamena niyamena ca  || 7.2,10.35
pūrvapāpaharo yogo viraktasyaiva kathyate  || 7.2,10.35
vairāgyājjāyate jñānaṃ jñānādyogaḥ pravartate  || 7.2,10.36
yogajñaḥ patito vāpi mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,10.37
dayā kāryātha satatamahiṃsā jñānasaṃgrahaḥ  || 7.2,10.37
satyamasteyamāstikyaṃ śraddhā ceṃdriyanigrahaḥ  || 7.2,10.38
adhyāpanaṃ cādhyayanaṃ yajanaṃ yājanaṃ tathā  || 7.2,10.38
dhyānamīśvarabhāvaśca satataṃ jñānaśīlatā  || 7.2,10.39
ya evaṃ vartate vipro jñānayogasya siddhaye  || 7.2,10.39
acirādeva vijñānaṃ labdhvā yogaṃ ca viṃdati  || 7.2,10.40
555

dagdhvā dehamimaṃ jñānī kṣaṇājjñānāgninā priye  || 7.2,10.40
prasādānmama yogajñaḥ karmabaṃdhaṃ prahāsyati  || 7.2,10.41
puṇyaḥpuṇyātmakaṃ karmamuktestatpratibaṃdhakam  || 7.2,10.41
tasmānniyogato yogī puṇyāpuṇyaṃ vivarjayet || 7.2,10.41
phalakāmanayā karmakaraṇātpratibadhyate  || 7.2,10.42
na karmamātrakaraṇāttasmātkarmaphalaṃ tyajet || 7.2,10.42
prathamaṃ karmayajñena bahiḥ sampūjya māṃ priye  || 7.2,10.43
jñānayogarato bhūtvā paścādyogaṃ samabhyaset || 7.2,10.43
vidite mama yāthātmye karmayajñena dehinaḥ  || 7.2,10.44
na yajaṃti hi māṃ yuktāḥ samaloṣṭāśmakāṃcanāḥ  || 7.2,10.44
nityayukto muniḥ śreṣṭho madbhaktaśca samāhitaḥ  || 7.2,10.45
jñānayogarato yogī mama sāyujyamāpnuyāt || 7.2,10.45
athāviraktacittā ye varṇino madupāśritāḥ  || 7.2,10.46
jñānacaryākriyāsveva te 'dhikuryustadarhakāḥ  || 7.2,10.46
dvidhā matpūjanaṃ jñeyaṃ bāhyamābhyaṃtaraṃ tathā  || 7.2,10.47
vāṅmanaḥkāyabhedācca tridhā madbhajanaṃ viduḥ  || 7.2,10.47
tapaḥ karma japo dhyānaṃ jñānaṃ vetyanupūrvaśaḥ  || 7.2,10.48
pañcadhā kathyate sadbhistadeva bhajanaṃ punaḥ  || 7.2,10.48
anyātmaviditaṃ bāhyamasmadabhyarcanādikam  || 7.2,10.49
tadeva tu svasaṃvedyamābhyaṃtaramudāhṛtam  || 7.2,10.49
manomatpravaṇaṃ cittaṃ na manomātramucyate  || 7.2,10.50
mannāmaniratā vāṇī vāṅmatā khalu netarā  || 7.2,10.50
liṃgairmacchāsanādiṣṭaistripuṃḍrādibhiraṃkitaḥ  || 7.2,10.51
mamopacāranirataḥ kāyaḥ kāyo na cetaraḥ  || 7.2,10.51
madarcākarma vijñeyaṃ bāhye yāgādinocyate  || 7.2,10.52
madarthe dehasaṃśoṣastapaḥ kṛcchrādi no matam  || 7.2,10.52
japaḥ pañcākṣarābhyāsaḥ praṇavābhyāsa eva ca  || 7.2,10.53
rudrādhyāyādikābhyāso na vedādhyayanādikam  || 7.2,10.53
dhyānammadrūpaciṃtādyaṃ nātmādyarthasamādhayaḥ  || 7.2,10.54
mamāgamārthavijñānaṃ jñānaṃ nānyārthavedanam  || 7.2,10.54
bāhye vābhyaṃtare vātha yatra syānmanaso ratiḥ  || 7.2,10.55
prāgvāsanāvaśāddevi tattvaniṣṭhāṃ samācaret || 7.2,10.55
bāhyādābhyaṃtaraṃ śreṣṭhaṃ bhavecchataguṇādhikam  || 7.2,10.56
asaṃkaratvāddoṣāṇāṃ dṛṣṭānāmapyasambhavāt || 7.2,10.56
śaucamābhyaṃtaraṃ vidyānna bāhyaṃ śaucamucyate  || 7.2,10.57
aṃtaḥ śaucavimuktātmā śucirapyaśuciryataḥ  || 7.2,10.57
bāhyamābhyaṃrtaraṃ caiva bhajanaṃ bhavapūrvakam  || 7.2,10.58
na bhāvarahitaṃ devi vipralaṃbhaikakāraṇam  || 7.2,10.58
kṛtakṛtyasya pūtasya mama kiṃ kriyate naraiḥ  || 7.2,10.59
bahirvābhyaṃtaraṃ vātha mayā bhāvo hi gṛhyate  || 7.2,10.59
bhāvaikātmā kriyā devi mama dharmassanātanaḥ  || 7.2,10.60
manasā karmaṇā vācā hyanapekṣya phalaṃ kvacit || 7.2,10.60
phaloddeśena deveśi laghurmama samāśrayaḥ  || 7.2,10.61
phalārthī tadabhāve māṃ parityaktuṃ kṣamo yataḥ  || 7.2,10.61
555

phalārthino 'pi yasyaiva mayi cittaṃ pratiṣṭhitam  || 7.2,10.62
bhāvānurūpaphaladastasyāpyahamanindite  || 7.2,10.62
phalānapekṣayā yeṣāṃ mano matpravaṇaṃ bhavet || 7.2,10.63
prārthayeyuḥ phalaṃ paścādbhaktāste 'pi mama priyāḥ  || 7.2,10.63
prāksaṃskāravaśādeva ye viciṃtya phalāphale  || 7.2,10.64
vivaśā māṃ prapadyaṃte mama priyatamā matāḥ  || 7.2,10.64
mallābhānna paro lābhasteṣāmasti yathātatham  || 7.2,10.65
mamāpi lābhastallābhānnāparaḥ parameśvari  || 7.2,10.65
madanugrahatasteṣāṃ bhāvo mayi samarpitaḥ  || 7.2,10.66
phalaṃ paramanirvāṇaṃ prayacchati balādiva  || 7.2,10.66
mahātmanāmananyānāṃ mayi saṃnyastacetasām  || 7.2,10.67
aṣṭadhā lakṣaṇaṃ prāhurmama dharmādhikāriṇām  || 7.2,10.67
madbhaktajanavātsalyaṃ pūjāyāṃ cānumodanam  || 7.2,10.68
svayamabhyarcanaṃ caiva madarthe cāṃgaceṣṭitam  || 7.2,10.68
matkathāśravaṇe bhaktiḥ svaranetrāṃgavikriyāḥ  || 7.2,10.69
mamānusmaraṇaṃ nityaṃ yaśca māmupajīvati  || 7.2,10.69
evamaṣṭavidhaṃ cihnaṃ yasminmlecche 'pi vartate  || 7.2,10.70
sa viprendro muniḥ śrīmānsa yatissa ca paṃḍitaḥ  || 7.2,10.70
na me priyaścaturvedī madbhakto śvapaco 'pi yaḥ  || 7.2,10.71
tasmai deyaṃ tato grāhyaṃ sa ca pūjyo yathā hyaham  || 7.2,10.71
patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ toyaṃ yo me bhaktyā prayacchati  || 7.2,10.72
tasyāhaṃ na praṇaśyāmi sa ca me na praṇaśyati  || 7.2,10.72
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe śivabhaktivarṇanaṃ nāma daśamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 11
īśvara uvāca
atha vakṣyāmi deveśi bhaktānāmadhikāriṇām  || 7.2,11.1
viduṣāṃ dvijamukhyānāṃ varṇadharmasamāsataḥ  || 7.2,11.1
triḥ snānaṃ cāgnikāryaṃ ca liṃgārcanamanukramam  || 7.2,11.2
dānamīśrarabhāvaśca dayā sarvatra sarvadā  || 7.2,11.2
satyaṃ saṃtoṣamāstikyamahiṃsā sarvajaṃtuṣu  || 7.2,11.3
hrīśraddhādhyayanaṃ yogassadādhyāpanameva ca  || 7.2,11.3
vyākhyānaṃ brahmacaryaṃ ca śravaṇaṃ ca tapaḥ kṣamā  || 7.2,11.4
śaucaṃ śikhopavītaṃ ca uṣṇīṣaṃ cottarīyakam  || 7.2,11.4
niṣiddhāsevanaṃ caiva bhasmarudrākṣadhāraṇam  || 7.2,11.5
parvaṇyabhyarcanaṃ devi caturdaśyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,11.5
pānaṃ ca brahmakūrcasya māsi māsi yathāvidhi  || 7.2,11.6
abhyarcanaṃ viśeṣeṇa tenaiva snāpya māṃ priye  || 7.2,11.6
sarvakriyānna santyāgaḥ śraddhānnasya ca varjanam  || 7.2,11.7
tathā paryuṣitānnasya yāvakasya viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,11.7
madyasya madyagandhasya naivedyasya ca varjanam  || 7.2,11.8
sāmānyaṃ sarvavarṇānāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,11.8
kṣamā śāṃtiśca santoṣassatyamasteyameva ca  || 7.2,11.9
brahmacaryaṃ mama jñānaṃ vairāgyaṃ bhasmasevanam  || 7.2,11.9
556

sarvasaṃganivṛttiśca daśaitāni viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,11.10
liṃgāni yogināṃ bhūyo divā bhikṣāśanaṃ tathā  || 7.2,11.10
vānaprasthāśramasthānāṃ samānamidamiṣyate  || 7.2,11.11
rātrau na bhojanaṃ kāryaṃ sarveṣāṃ brahmacāriṇām  || 7.2,11.11
adhyāpanaṃ yājanaṃ ca kṣatriyasyāpratigrahaḥ  || 7.2,11.12
vaiśyasya ca viśeṣeṇa mayā nātra vidhīyate  || 7.2,11.12
rakṣaṇaṃ sarvavarṇānāṃ yuddhe śatruvadhastathā  || 7.2,11.13
duṣṭapakṣimṛgāṇāṃ ca duṣṭānāṃ śātanaṃ nṛṇām  || 7.2,11.13
aviśvāsaśca sarvatra viśvāso mama yogiṣu  || 7.2,11.14
strīsaṃsargaśca kāleṣu camūrakṣaṇameva ca  || 7.2,11.14
sadā saṃcāritaiścārairlokavṛttāṃtavedanam  || 7.2,11.15
sadāstradhāraṇaṃ caiva bhasmakaṃcukadhāraṇam  || 7.2,11.15
rājñāṃ mamāśramasthānāmeṣa dharmasya saṃgrahaḥ  || 7.2,11.16
gorakṣaṇaṃ ca vāṇijyaṃ kṛṣirvaiśyasya kathyate  || 7.2,11.16
śuśrūṣetaravarṇānāṃ dharmaḥ śūdrasya kathyate  || 7.2,11.17
udyānakaraṇaṃ caiva mama kṣetrasamāśrayaḥ  || 7.2,11.17
dharmapatnyāstu gamanaṃ gṛhasthasya vidhīyate  || 7.2,11.18
brahmacaryaṃ vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ brahmacāriṇām  || 7.2,11.18
strīṇāṃ tu bhartṛśuśrūṣā dharmo nānyassanātanaḥ  || 7.2,11.19
mamārcanaṃ ca kalyāṇi niyogo bharturasti cet || 7.2,11.19
yā nārī bhartṛśuśrūṣāṃ vihāya vratatatparā  || 7.2,11.20
sā nārī narakaṃ yāti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 7.2,11.20
atha bhartṛvihīnāyā vakṣye dharmaṃ sanātanam  || 7.2,11.21
vrataṃ dānaṃ tapaḥ śaucaṃ bhūśayyānaktabhojanam  || 7.2,11.21
brahmacaryaṃ sadā snānaṃ bhasmanā salilena vā  || 7.2,11.22
śāṃtirmaunaṃ kṣamā nityaṃ saṃvibhāgo yathāvidhi  || 7.2,11.22
aṣṭābhyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,11.23
ekādaśyāṃ ca vidhivadupavāsomamārcanam  || 7.2,11.23
iti saṃkṣepataḥ prokto mayāśramaniṣeviṇām  || 7.2,11.24
brahmakṣatraviśāṃ devi yatīnāṃ brahmacāriṇām  || 7.2,11.24
tathaiva vānaprasthānāṃ gṛhasthānāṃ ca sundari  || 7.2,11.25
śūdrāṇāmatha nārīṇāṃ dharma eṣa sanātanaḥ  || 7.2,11.25
dhyeyastvayāhaṃ deveśi sadā jāpyaḥ ṣaḍakṣaraḥ  || 7.2,11.26
vedoktamakhilaṃ dharmamiti dharmārthasaṃgrahaḥ  || 7.2,11.26
atha ye mānavā loke svecchayā dhṛtavigrahāḥ  || 7.2,11.27
bhāvātiśayasaṃpannāḥ pūrvasaṃskārasaṃyutāḥ  || 7.2,11.27
viraktā vānuraktā vā stryādīnāṃ viṣayeṣvapi  || 7.2,11.28
pāpairna te viliṃpaṃte 1 padmapatramivāṃbhasā  || 7.2,11.28
teṣāṃ mamātmavijñānaṃ viśuddhānāṃ vivekinām  || 7.2,11.29
matprasādādviśuddhānāṃ duḥkhamāśramarakṣaṇāt || 7.2,11.29
nāsti kṛtyamakṛtyaṃ ca samādhirvā parāyaṇam  || 7.2,11.30
na vidhirna niṣedhaśca teṣāṃ mama yathā tathā  || 7.2,11.30

1 ātmanepadamārṣam

556

tatheha paripūrṇasya sādhyaṃ mama na vidyate  || 7.2,11.31
tathaiva kṛtakṛtyānāṃ teṣāmapi na saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,11.31
madbhaktānāṃ hitārthāya mānuṣaṃ bhāvamāśritāḥ  || 7.2,11.32
rudralokātparibhraṣṭāste rudrā nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,11.32
mamānuśāsanaṃ yadvadbrahmādīnāṃ pravartakam  || 7.2,11.33
tathā narāṇāmanyeṣāṃ tanniyogaḥ pravartakaḥ  || 7.2,11.33
mamājñādhārabhāvena sadbhāvātiśayena ca  || 7.2,11.34
tadālokanamātreṇa sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet || 7.2,11.34
pratyayāśca pravartaṃte praśastaphalasūcakāḥ  || 7.2,11.35
mayi bhāvavatāṃ puṃsāṃ prāgadṛṣṭārthagocarāḥ  || 7.2,11.35
kaṃpasvedo 'śrupātaśca kaṇṭhe ca svaravikriyā  || 7.2,11.36
ānaṃdādyupalabdhiśca bhavedākasmikī muhuḥ  || 7.2,11.36
sa tairvyastaissamastairvā liṃgairavyabhicāribhiḥ  || 7.2,11.37
maṃdamadhyottamairbhāvairvijñeyāste narottamāḥ  || 7.2,11.37
yathāyognisamāveśānnāyo bhavati kevalam  || 7.2,11.38
sa tathaiva mama sānnidhyānna te kevalamānuṣāḥ  || 7.2,11.38
hastapādādisādharmyādrudrānmartyavapurdharān  || 7.2,11.39
prākṛtāniva manvāno nāvajānīta paṃḍitaḥ  || 7.2,11.39
avajñānaṃ kṛtaṃ teṣu narairvyāmūḍhacetanaiḥ  || 7.2,11.40
āyuḥ śriyaṃ kulaṃ śīlaṃ hitvā nirayamāvahet || 7.2,11.40
brahmaviṣṇusureśānāmapi tūlāyate padam  || 7.2,11.41
mattonyadanapekṣāṇāmuddhṛtānāṃ mahātmanām  || 7.2,11.41
aśuddhaṃ bauddhamaiśvaryaṃ prākṛtaṃ pauruṣaṃ tathā  || 7.2,11.42
guṇeśānāmatastyājyaṃ guṇātītapadaiṣiṇām  || 7.2,11.42
atha kiṃ bahunoktena śreyaḥ prāptyaikasādhanam  || 7.2,11.43
mayi cittasamāsaṃgo yena kenāpi hetunā  || 7.2,11.43
upamanyuruvāca
itthaṃ śrīkaṇṭhanāthena śivena paramātmanā  || 7.2,11.44
hitāya jagatāmukto jñānasārārthasaṃgrahaḥ  || 7.2,11.44
vijñānasaṃgrahasyāsya vedaśāstrāṇi kṛtsnaśaḥ  || 7.2,11.45
setihāsapurāṇāni vidyā vyākhyānavistaraḥ  || 7.2,11.45
jñānaṃ jñeyamanuṣṭheyamadhikāro 'tha sādhanam  || 7.2,11.46
sādhyaṃ ceti ṣaḍarthānāṃ saṃgrahatveṣa saṃgrahaḥ  || 7.2,11.46
guroradhikṛtaṃ jñānaṃ jñeyaṃ pāśaḥ paśuḥ patiḥ  || 7.2,11.47
liṃgārcanādyanuṣṭheyaṃ bhaktastvadhikṛto 'pi yaḥ  || 7.2,11.47
sādhanaṃ śivamaṃtrādyaṃ sādhyaṃ śivasamānatā  || 7.2,11.48
ṣaḍarthasaṃgrahasyāsya jñānātsarvajñatocyate  || 7.2,11.48
prathamaṃ karma yajñāderbhaktyā vittānusārataḥ  || 7.2,11.49
bāhyebhyarcya śivaṃ paścādaṃtaryāgarato bhavet || 7.2,11.49
ratirabhyaṃtare yasya na bāhye puṇyagauravāt || 7.2,11.50
na karma karaṇīyaṃ hi bahistasya mahātmanāḥ  || 7.2,11.50
jñānāmṛtena tṛptasya bhaktyā śaivaśivātmanaḥ  || 7.2,11.51
nāṃtarna ca bahiḥ kṛṣṇa kṛtyamasti kadācana  || 7.2,11.51
tasmātkrameṇa saṃtyajya bāhyamābhyaṃtaraṃ tathā  || 7.2,11.52
jñānena jñeyamālokyājñānaṃ cāpi parityajet || 7.2,11.52
naikāgraṃ cecchive cittaṃ kiṃ kṛtenāpi karmaṇā  || 7.2,11.53
557

ekāgrameva ceccittaṃ kiṃ kṛtenāpi karmaṇā  || 7.2,11.53
tasmātkarmāṇyakṛtvā vā kṛtvā vāṃtarbahiḥkramāt || 7.2,11.54
yena kenāpyupāyena śive cittaṃ niveśayet || 7.2,11.54
śive niviṣṭacittānāṃ pratiṣṭhitadhiyāṃ satām  || 7.2,11.55
paratreha ca sarvatra nirvṛtiḥ paramā bhavet || 7.2,11.55
ihonnamaḥ śivāyeti maṃtreṇānena siddhayaḥ  || 7.2,11.56
sa tasmādadhigaṃtavyaḥ parāvaravibhūtaye  || 7.2,11.56

oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṃḍe śivajñānavarṇanaṃ nāmaikādaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 12
śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca
maharṣivara sarvajña sarvajñānamahodadhe  || 7.2,12.1
pañcākṣarasya māhātmyaṃ śrotumicchāmi tattvataḥ  || 7.2,12.1
upamanyuruvāca
pañcākṣarasya māhātmyaṃ varṣakoṭiśatairapi  || 7.2,12.2
aśakyaṃ vistarādvaktuṃ tasmātsaṃkṣepataḥ śṛṇu  || 7.2,12.2
vede śivāgame cāyamubhayatra ṣaḍakṣareḥ  || 7.2,12.3
sarveṣāṃ śivabhaktānāmaśeṣārthasādhakaḥ  || 7.2,12.3
tadalpākṣaramarthāḍhyaṃ vedasāraṃ vimuktidam  || 7.2,12.4
ājñāsiddhamasaṃdigdhaṃ vākyametacchivātmakam  || 7.2,12.4
nānāsiddhiyutaṃ divyaṃ lokacittānuraṃjakam  || 7.2,12.5
suniścitārthaṃ gaṃbhīraṃ vākyaṃ tatpārameśvaram  || 7.2,12.5
mantraṃ sukhamukoccāryamaśeṣārthaprasiddhaye  || 7.2,12.6
prāhonnamaḥ śivāyeti sarvajñassarvadehinām  || 7.2,12.6
tadbījaṃ sarvavidyānāṃ maṃtramādyaṃ ṣaḍakṣaram  || 7.2,12.7
atisūkṣmaṃ mahārthaṃ ca jñeyaṃ tadvaṭabījavat || 7.2,12.7
devo guṇatrayātītaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvakṛtprabhuḥ  || 7.2,12.8
omityekākṣare mantre sthitaḥ sarvagataḥ śivaḥ  || 7.2,12.8
iśānādyāni sūkṣmāṇi brahmāṇyekākṣarāṇi tu  || 7.2,12.9
maṃtre namaśśivāyeti saṃsthitāni yathākramam  || 7.2,12.9
maṃtre ṣaḍakṣare sūkṣme pañcabrahmatanuḥ śivaḥ  || 7.2,12.9
vācyavācakabhāvena sthitaḥ sākṣātsvabhāvataḥ  || 7.2,12.10
vācyaśśivoprameyatvānmaṃtrastadvācakassmṛtaḥ  || 7.2,12.10
vācyavācakabhāvo 'yamanādisaṃsthitastayoḥ  || 7.2,12.11
yathā 'nādipravṛttoyaṃ ghorasaṃsārasāgaraḥ  || 7.2,12.11
śivo 'pi hi tathānādisaṃsārānmocakaḥ sthitaḥ  || 7.2,12.12
vyādhīnāṃ bheṣajaṃ yadvatpratipakṣaḥ svabhāvataḥ  || 7.2,12.12
tadvatsaṃsāradoṣāṇāṃ pratipakṣaḥ śivassmṛtaḥ  || 7.2,12.13
asatyasmin jagannāthe tamobhūtamidaṃ bhavet || 7.2,12.13
acetanatvātprakṛterajñatvātpuraṣasya ca  || 7.2,12.14
pradhānaparamāṇvādi yāvatkiṃcidacetanam  || 7.2,12.14
na tatkartṛ svayaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ buddhimatkāraṇaṃ vinā  || 7.2,12.15
dharmādharmopadeśaśca baṃdhamokṣau vicāraṇāt || 7.2,12.15
na sarvajñaṃ vinā puṃsāmādisargaḥ prasiddhyati  || 7.2,12.16
vaidyaṃ vinā nirānaṃdāḥ kliśyaṃte rogiṇo yathā  || 7.2,12.16
557

tasmādanādiḥ sarvajñaḥ paripūrṇassadāśivaḥ  || 7.2,12.17
asti nāthaḥ paritrātā puṃsāṃ saṃsārasāgarāt || 7.2,12.17
ādimadhyāṃtanirmuktassvabhāvavimalaḥ prabhuḥ  || 7.2,12.18
sarvajñaḥ paripūrṇaśca śivo jñeyaśśivāgame  || 7.2,12.18
tasyābhidhānamantro 'yamabhidheyaśca sa smṛtaḥ  || 7.2,12.19
abhidhānābhidheyatvānmaṃtrassiddhaḥ paraśśivaḥ  || 7.2,12.19
etāvattu śivajñānametāvatparamaṃ padam  || 7.2,12.20
yadoṃnamaśśivāyeti śivavākyaṃ ṣaḍakṣaram  || 7.2,12.20
vidhivākyamidaṃ śaivaṃ nārthavādaṃ śivātmakam  || 7.2,12.21
yassarvajñassusaṃpūrṇaḥ svabhāvavimalaḥ śivaḥ  || 7.2,12.21
lokānugrahakartā ca sa mṛṣārthaṃ kathaṃ vadet || 7.2,12.22
yadyathāvasthitaṃ vastu guṇadoṣaiḥ svabhāvataḥ  || 7.2,12.22
yāvatphalaṃ ca tatpūrṇaṃ sarvajñastu yathā vadet || 7.2,12.23
rāgājñānādibhirdoṣairgrastatvādanṛtaṃ vadet || 7.2,12.23
te ceśvare na vidyete brūyātsa kathamanyathā  || 7.2,12.24
ajñātāśeṣadoṣeṇa sarvajñeya śivena yat || 7.2,12.24
praṇītamamalaṃ vākyaṃ tatpramāṇaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,12.24
tasmādīśvaravākyāni śraddheyāni vipaścitā  || 7.2,12.25
yathārthapuṇyapāpeṣu tadaśraddho vrajatyadhaḥ  || 7.2,12.25
svargāpavargasiddhyarthaṃ bhāṣitaṃ yatsuśobhanam  || 7.2,12.26
vākyaṃ munivaraiḥ śāṃtaistadvijñeyaṃ subhāṣitam  || 7.2,12.26
rāgadveṣānṛtakrodhakāmatṛṣṇānusāri yat || 7.2,12.27
vākyaṃ nirayahetutvāttaddurbhāṣitamucyate  || 7.2,12.27
saṃskṛtenāpi kiṃ tena mṛdunā lalitena vā  || 7.2,12.28
avidyārāgavākyena saṃsārakleśahetunā  || 7.2,12.28
yacchrutvā jāyate śreyo rāgādīnāṃ ca saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,12.29
virūpamapi tadvākyaṃ vijñeyamiti śobhanam  || 7.2,12.29
bahutvepi hi maṃtrāṇāṃ sarvajñena śivena yaḥ  || 7.2,12.30
praṇīto vimalo mantro na tena sadṛśaḥ kvacit || 7.2,12.30
sāṃgāni vedaśāstrāṇi saṃsthitāni ṣaḍakṣare  || 7.2,12.31
na tena sadṛśastasmānmantro 'pyastyaparaḥ kvacit || 7.2,12.31
saptakoṭimahāmantrairupamantrairanekadhā  || 7.2,12.32
mantraḥ ṣaḍakṣaro bhinnassūtraṃ vṛtyātmanā yathā  || 7.2,12.32
śivajñānāni yāvaṃti vidyāsthānāpi yāni ca  || 7.2,12.33
ṣaḍakṣarasya sūtrasya tāni bhāṣyaṃ samāsataḥ  || 7.2,12.33
kiṃ tasya bahubhirmaṃtraiśśāstrairvā bahuvistaraiḥ  || 7.2,12.34
yasyonnamaḥ śivāyeti mantro 'yaṃ hṛdi saṃsthitaḥ  || 7.2,12.34
tenādhītaṃ śrutaṃ tena kṛtaṃ sarvamanuṣṭhitam  || 7.2,12.35
yenonnamaśśivāyeti maṃtrābhyāsaḥ sthirīkṛtaḥ  || 7.2,12.35
namaskārādisaṃyuktaṃ śivāyetyakṣaratrayam  || 7.2,12.36
jihvāgre vartate yasya saphalaṃ tasya jīvitam  || 7.2,12.36
aṃtyajo vādhamo vāpi mūrkho vā paṃḍito 'pi vā  || 7.2,12.37
pañcākṣarajape niṣṭho mucyate pāpapaṃjarāt || 7.2,12.37
ityuktaṃ parameśena devyā pṛṣṭena śūlinā  || 7.2,12.38
hitāya sarvamartyānāṃ dvijānāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,12.38
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe pañcākṣaramāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma dvādaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 13
devyuvāca
kalau kaluṣite kāle durjaye duratikrame  || 7.2,13.1
apuṇyatamasācchanne loke dharmaparāṅmukhe  || 7.2,13.1
kṣīṇe varṇāśramācāre saṃkaṭe samupasthite  || 7.2,13.2
sarvādhikāre saṃdigdhe niścite vāpi paryaye  || 7.2,13.2
tadopadeśe vihate guruśiṣyakrame gate  || 7.2,13.3
kenopāyena mucyaṃte bhaktāstava maheśvara  || 7.2,13.3
īśvara uvāca
āśritya paramāṃ vidyāṃ hṛdyāṃ pañcākṣarīṃ mama  || 7.2,13.4
bhaktyā ca bhāvitātmāno mucyaṃte kalijā narāḥ  || 7.2,13.4
manovākkāyajairdoṣairvaktuṃ smartumagocaraiḥ  || 7.2,13.5
dūṣitānāṃ kṛtaghnānāṃ niṃdakānāṃ chalātmanām  || 7.2,13.5
lubdhānāṃ vakramanasāmapi matpravaṇātmanām  || 7.2,13.6
mama pañcākṣarī vidyā saṃsārabhayatāriṇī  || 7.2,13.6
mayaivamasakṛddevi pratijñātaṃ dharātale  || 7.2,13.7
patito 'pi vimucyeta madbhakto vidyayānayā  || 7.2,13.7
devyuvāca
karmāyogyo bhavenmartyaḥ patito yadi sarvathā  || 7.2,13.8
karmāyogena yatkarma kṛtaṃ ca narakāya hi  || 7.2,13.8
tataḥ kathaṃ vimucyeta patito vidyayā 'nayā  || 7.2,13.8
īśvara uvāca
tathyametattvayā proktaṃ tathā hi śṛṇu sundari  || 7.2,13.9
rahasyamiti matvaitadgopitaṃ yanmayā purā  || 7.2,13.9
samaṃtrakaṃ māṃ patitaḥ pūjayedyadi mohitaḥ  || 7.2,13.10
nārakī syānna sandeho mama pañcākṣaraṃ vinā  || 7.2,13.10
abbhakṣā vāyubhakṣāśca ye cānye vratakarśitāḥ  || 7.2,13.11
teṣāmetairvratairnāsti mama lokasamāgamaḥ  || 7.2,13.11
bhaktyā pañcākṣareṇaiva yo hi māṃ sakṛdarcayet || 7.2,13.12
so 'pi gacchenmama sthānaṃ mantrasyāsyaiva gauravāt || 7.2,13.12
tasmāttapāṃsi yajñāśca vratāni niyamāstathā  || 7.2,13.13
pañcākṣarārcanasyaite koṭyaṃśenāpi no samaḥ  || 7.2,13.13
baddho vāpyatha mukto vā pāśātpañcākṣareṇa yaḥ  || 7.2,13.14
pūjayenmāṃ sa mucyeta nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 7.2,13.14
arudro vā sarudro vā sakṛtpañcākṣareṇa yaḥ  || 7.2,13.15
pūjayetpatito vāpi mūḍho vā mucyate naraḥ  || 7.2,13.15
ṣaḍakṣareṇa vā devi tathā pañcākṣareṇa vā  || 7.2,13.16
sa brahmāṃgena māṃ bhaktyā pūjayedyadi mucyate  || 7.2,13.16
patito 'patito vāpi mantreṇānena pūjayet || 7.2,13.17
mama bhakto jitakrodho salabdho 'labdha eva vā  || 7.2,13.17
alabdhālabdha eveha koṭikoṭiguṇādhikaḥ  || 7.2,13.18
tasmāllabdhvaiva māṃ devi mantreṇānena pūjayet || 7.2,13.18
labdhvā saṃpūjayedyastu maitryādiguṇasaṃyutaḥ  || 7.2,13.19
brahmacaryarato bhaktyā matsādṛśyamavāpnuyāt || 7.2,13.19
558

kimatra bahunoktena bhaktāssarvedhikāriṇaḥ  || 7.2,13.20
mama pañcākṣare maṃtre tasmācchreṣṭhataro hi saḥ  || 7.2,13.20
pañcākṣaraprabhāveṇa lokavedamaharṣayaḥ  || 7.2,13.21
tiṣṭhaṃti śāśvatā dharmā devāssarvamidaṃ jagat || 7.2,13.21
pralaye samanuprāpte naṣṭe sthāvarajaṃgame  || 7.2,13.22
sarvaṃ prakṛtimāpannaṃ tatra saṃlayameṣyati  || 7.2,13.22
eko 'haṃ saṃsthito devi na dvitīyo 'sti kutracit || 7.2,13.23
tadā vedāśca śāstrāṇi sarve pañcākṣare sthitāḥ  || 7.2,13.23
te nāśaṃ naiva saṃprāptā macchaktyā hyanupālitāḥ  || 7.2,13.24
tatassṛṣṭirabhūnmattaḥ prakṛtyātmaprabhedataḥ  || 7.2,13.24
guṇamūrtyātmanāṃ caiva tatovāṃtarasaṃhṛtiḥ  || 7.2,13.25
tadā nārāyaṇaśśete devo māyāmayīṃ tanum  || 7.2,13.25
āsthāya bhogiparyaṃkaśayane toyamadhyagaḥ  || 7.2,13.26
tannābhipaṃkajājjātaḥ pañcavaktraḥ pitāmahaḥ  || 7.2,13.26
sisṛkṣamāṇo lokāṃstrīnna sakto hyasahāyavān  || 7.2,13.27
munīndaśa sasarjādau mānasānamitaujasaḥ  || 7.2,13.27
teṣāṃ siddhivivṛddhyarthaṃ māṃ provāca pitāmahaḥ  || 7.2,13.28
matputrāṇāṃ mahādeva śaktiṃ dehi maheśvara  || 7.2,13.28
ityevaṃ prārthitastena pañcavaktradharo hyaham  || 7.2,13.29
pañcākṣarāṇi kramaśaḥ proktavānpadmayonaye  || 7.2,13.29
sa pañcavadanaistāni gṛhṇaṃllokapitāmahaḥ  || 7.2,13.30
vācyavācakabhāvena jñātavānmāṃ maheśvaram  || 7.2,13.30
jñātvā prayogaṃ vividhaṃ siddhamaṃtraḥ prajāpatiḥ  || 7.2,13.31
putrebhyaḥ pradadau maṃtraṃ maṃtrārthaṃ ca yathātatham  || 7.2,13.31
te ca labdhvā maṃtraratnaṃ sākṣāllokapitāmahāt || 7.2,13.32
tadājñaptena mārgeṇa madārādhanakāṃkṣiṇaḥ  || 7.2,13.32
merostu śikhare ramye muṃjavānnāma parvataḥ  || 7.2,13.33
matpriyaḥ satataṃ śrīmānmadbhaktai rakṣitassadā  || 7.2,13.33
tasyābhyāśe tapastīvraṃ lokaṃ sraṣṭuṃ samutsukāḥ  || 7.2,13.34
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu vāyubhakṣāssamācaran  || 7.2,13.34
teṣāṃ bhaktimahaṃ dṛṣṭvā sadyaḥ pratyakṣatāmiyām  || 7.2,13.35
ṛṣiṃ chaṃdaśca kīlaṃ ca bījaśaktiṃ ca daivatam  || 7.2,13.35
nyāsaṃ ṣaḍaṃgaṃ digbaṃdhaṃ viniyogamaśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,13.36
proktavānahamāryāṇāṃ jagatsṛṣṭivivṛddhaye  || 7.2,13.36
tataste maṃtramāhātmyādṛṣayastapasedhitāḥ  || 7.2,13.37
sṛṣṭiṃ vitanvate samyaksadevāsuramānuṣīm  || 7.2,13.37
asyāḥ paramavidyāyāssvarūpamadhunocyate  || 7.2,13.38
ādau namaḥ prayoktavyaṃ śivāya tu tataḥ param  || 7.2,13.38
saiṣā pañcākṣarī vidyā sarvaśrutiśirogatā  || 7.2,13.39
sarvajātasya sarvasya bījabhūtā sanātanī  || 7.2,13.39
prathamaṃ manmukhodgīrṇā sā mamaivāsti vācikā  || 7.2,13.40
taptacāmīkaraprakhyā pīnonnatapayodharā  || 7.2,13.40
caturbhujā trinayanā bāleṃdukṛtaśekharā  || 7.2,13.41
padmotpalakarā saumyā varadābhayapāṇikā  || 7.2,13.41
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpannā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā  || 7.2,13.42
sitapadmāsanāsīnā nīlakuṃcitamūrdhajā  || 7.2,13.42
559
asyāḥ pañcavidhā varṇāḥ prasphuradraśmimaṃḍalāḥ  || 7.2,13.43
pītaḥ kṛṣṇastathā dhūmraḥ svarṇābho rakta eva ca  || 7.2,13.43
pṛthakprayojyā yadyete biṃdunādavibhūṣitāḥ  || 7.2,13.44
ardhacandranibho biṃdurnādo dīpaśikhākṛtiḥ  || 7.2,13.44
bījaṃ dvitīyaṃ bījeṣu maṃtrasyāsya varānane  || 7.2,13.45
dīrghapūrvaṃ turīyasya pañcamaṃ śaktimādiśet || 7.2,13.45
vāmadevo nāma ṛṣiḥ paṃktiśchanda udāhṛtam  || 7.2,13.46
devatā śiva evāhaṃ mantrasyāsya varānane  || 7.2,13.46
gautamo 'trirvarārohe viśvāmitrastathāṃgirāḥ  || 7.2,13.47
bharadvājaśca varṇānāṃ kramaśaścarṣayaḥ smṛtāḥ  || 7.2,13.47
gāyatryanuṣṭuptriṣṭupca chaṃdāṃsi bṛhatī virāṭ || 7.2,13.48
indro rudro harirbrahmā skaṃdasteṣāṃ ca devatāḥ  || 7.2,13.48
mama pañcamukhānyāhuḥ sthāne teṣāṃ varānane  || 7.2,13.49
pūrvādeścordhvaparyaṃtaṃ nakārādi yathākramam  || 7.2,13.49
adāttaḥ prathamo varṇaścaturthaśca dvitīyakaḥ  || 7.2,13.50
pañcamaḥ svaritaścaiva tṛtīyo nihataḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.2,13.50
mūlavidyā śivaṃ śaivaṃ sūtraṃ pañcākṣaraṃ tathā  || 7.2,13.51
nāmānyasya vijānīyācchaivaṃ me hṛdayaṃ mahat || 7.2,13.51
nakāraśśira ucyeta makārastu śikhocyate  || 7.2,13.52
śikāraḥ kavacaṃ tadvadvakāro netramucyate  || 7.2,13.52
yakāro 'straṃ namassvāhā vaṣaṭhuṃvauṣaḍityapi  || 7.2,13.53
phaḍityapi ca varṇānāmante 'ṅgatvaṃ yadā tadā  || 7.2,13.53
tatrāpi mūlamaṃtro 'yaṃ kiṃcidbhedasamanvayāt || 7.2,13.54
tatrāpi pañcamo varṇo dvādaśasvarabhūṣitaḥ  || 7.2,13.54
tāsmādanena maṃtreṇa manovākkāyabhedataḥ  || 7.2,13.55
āvayorarcanaṃ kuryājjapahomādikaṃ tathā  || 7.2,13.55
yathāprajñaṃ yathākālaṃ yathāśāstraṃ yathāmati  || 7.2,13.56
yathāśakti yathāsaṃpadyathāyogaṃ yathārati  || 7.2,13.56
yadā kadāpi vā bhaktyā yatra kutrāpi vā kṛtā  || 7.2,13.57
yena kenāpi vā devi pūjā muktiṃ nayiṣyate  || 7.2,13.57
mayyāsaktena manasā yatkṛtaṃ mama sundari  || 7.2,13.58
matpriyaṃ ca śivaṃ caiva krameṇāpyakrameṇa vā  || 7.2,13.58
tathāpi mama bhaktā ye nātyaṃtavivaśāḥ punaḥ  || 7.2,13.59
teṣāṃ sarveṣu śāstreṣu mayeva niyamaḥ kṛtaḥ  || 7.2,13.59
tatrādau saṃpravakṣyāmi mantrasaṃgrahaṇaṃ śubham  || 7.2,13.60
yaṃ vinā niṣphalaṃ jāpyaṃ yena vā saphalaṃ bhavet || 7.2,13.60
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe pañcākṣaramāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma trayodaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 14
īśvara uvāca
ājñāhīnaṃ kriyāhīnaṃ śraddhāhīnaṃ varānane  || 7.2,14.1
ājñārthaṃ dakṣiṇāhīnaṃ sadā japtaṃ ca niṣphalam  || 7.2,14.1
ājñāsiddhaṃ kriyāsiddhaṃ śraddhāsiddhaṃ mamātmakam  || 7.2,14.2
evaṃ ceddakṣiṇāyuktaṃ maṃtrasiddhirmahatphalam  || 7.2,14.2
559

upagamya guruṃ vipramācāryaṃ tattvavedinam  || 7.2,14.3
jāpitaṃ sadguṇopetaṃ dhyānayogaparāyaṇam  || 7.2,14.3
toṣayettaṃ prayatnena bhāvaśuddhisamanvitaḥ  || 7.2,14.4
vācā ca manasā caiva kāyena draviṇena ca  || 7.2,14.4
ācāryaṃ pūjayedvipraḥ sarvadātiprayatnataḥ  || 7.2,14.5
hastyaśvaratharatnāni kṣetrāṇi ca gṛhāṇi ca  || 7.2,14.5
bhūṣaṇāni ca vāsāṃsi dhānyāni ca dhanāni ca  || 7.2,14.6
etāni gurave dadyādbhaktyā ca vibhave sati  || 7.2,14.6
vittaśāṭhyaṃ na kurvīta yadīcchetsiddhimātmanaḥ  || 7.2,14.7
paścānnivedya svātmānaṃ gurave saparicchadam  || 7.2,14.7
evaṃ saṃpūjya vidhivadyathāśaktitvavaṃcayan  || 7.2,14.8
ādadīta gurormaṃtraṃ jñānaṃ caiva krameṇa tu  || 7.2,14.8
evaṃ tuṣṭo guruḥ śiṣyaṃ pūjakaṃ vatsaroṣitam  || 7.2,14.9
śuśrūṣumanahaṃkāraṃ snātaṃ śucimupoṣitam  || 7.2,14.9
snāpayitvā viśuddhyarthaṃ pūrṇakuṃbhaghṛtena vai  || 7.2,14.10
jalena mantraśuddhena puṇyadravyayutena ca  || 7.2,14.10
alaṃkṛtya suveṣaṃ ca gaṃdhasragvastrabhūṣaṇaiḥ  || 7.2,14.11
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā ca brāhmaṇānabhipūjya ca  || 7.2,14.11
samudratīre nadyāṃ ca goṣṭhe devālaye 'pi vā  || 7.2,14.12
śucau deśe gṛhe vāpi kāle siddhikare tithau  || 7.2,14.12
nakṣatre śubhayoge ca sarvadoṣavivarjite  || 7.2,14.13
anugṛhya tato dadyājjñānaṃ mama yathāvidhi  || 7.2,14.13
svareṇoccārayetsamyagekāṃte 'tiprasannadhīḥ  || 7.2,14.14
uccāryoccārayitvā tamāvayormaṃtramuttamam  || 7.2,14.14
śivaṃ cāstu śubhaṃ cāstu śobhano 'stu priyo 'stviti  || 7.2,14.15
evaṃ dadyādgururmaṃtramājñāṃ caiva tataḥ param  || 7.2,14.15
evaṃ labdhvā gurormaṃtramājñāṃ caiva samāhitaḥ  || 7.2,14.16
saṃkalpya ca japennityaṃ puraścaraṇapūrvakam  || 7.2,14.16
yāvajjīvaṃ japennityamaṣṭottarasahasrakam  || 7.2,14.17
ananyastatparo bhūtvā sa yāti paramāṃ gatim  || 7.2,14.17
japedakṣaralakṣaṃ vai caturguṇitamādarāt || 7.2,14.18
naktāśī saṃyamī yassa pauraścaraṇikaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.2,14.18
yaḥ puraścaraṇaṃ kṛtvā nityajāpī bhavetpunaḥ  || 7.2,14.19
tasya nāsti samo loke sa siddhaḥ siddhado bhavet || 7.2,14.19
snānaṃ kṛtvā śucau deśe baddhvā ruciramānasam  || 7.2,14.20
tvayā māṃ hṛdi saṃciṃtya saṃciṃtya svaguruṃ tataḥ  || 7.2,14.20
udaṅmukhaḥ prāṅmukho vā maunī caikāgramānasaḥ  || 7.2,14.21
viśodhya pañcatattvāni dahanaplāvanādibhiḥ  || 7.2,14.21
mantranyāsādikaṃ kṛtvā saphalīkṛtavigrahaḥ  || 7.2,14.22
āvayorvigrahau dhyāyanprāṇāpānau niyamya ca  || 7.2,14.22
vidyāsthānaṃ svakaṃ rūpamṛṣiñchando 'dhidaivatam  || 7.2,14.23
bījaṃ śaktiṃ tathā vākyaṃ smṛtvā pañcākṣarīṃ japet || 7.2,14.23
uttamaṃ mānasaṃ jāpyamupāṃśuṃ caivamadhyamam  || 7.2,14.24
adhamaṃ vācikaṃ prāhurāgamārthaviśāradāḥ  || 7.2,14.24
560

uttamaṃ rudradaivatyaṃ madhyamaṃ viṣṇudaivatam  || 7.2,14.25
adhamaṃ brahmadaivatyamityāhuranupūrvaśaḥ  || 7.2,14.25
yaduccanīcasvaritaiḥspaṣṭāspaṣṭapadākṣaraiḥ  || 7.2,14.26
maṃtramuccārayedvācā vāciko 'yaṃ japassmṛtaḥ  || 7.2,14.26
jihvāmātraparispaṃdādīṣaduccārito 'pi vā  || 7.2,14.27
aparairaśrutaḥ kiṃcicchruto vopāṃśurucyate  || 7.2,14.27
dhiyā yadakṣaraśreṇyā varṇādvarṇaṃ padātpadam  || 7.2,14.28
śabdārthaciṃtanaṃ bhūyaḥ kathyate mānaso japaḥ  || 7.2,14.28
vācikastveka eva syādupāṃśuḥ śatamucyate  || 7.2,14.29
sāhasraṃ mānasaḥ proktaḥ sagarbhastu śatādhikaḥ  || 7.2,14.29
prāṇāyāmasamāyuktassagarbho japa ucyate  || 7.2,14.30
ādyaṃtayoragarbho 'pi prāṇāyāmaḥ praśasyate  || 7.2,14.30
catvāriṃśatsamāvṛttīḥ prāṇānāyamya saṃsmaret || 7.2,14.31
maṃtraṃ maṃtrārthaviddhīmānaśaktaḥ śaktito japet || 7.2,14.31
pañcakaṃ trikamekaṃ vā prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret || 7.2,14.32
agarbhaṃ vā sagarbhaṃ vā sagarbhastatra śasyate  || 7.2,14.32
sagarbhādapi sāhasraṃ sadhyāno japa ucyate  || 7.2,14.33
eṣu pañcavidheṣvekaḥ kartavyaḥ śaktito japaḥ  || 7.2,14.33
aṅgulyā japasaṃkhyānamekamevamudāhṛtam  || 7.2,14.34
rekhayāṣṭaguṇaṃ vidyātputrajīvairdaśādhikam  || 7.2,14.34
śataṃ syācchaṃkhamaṇibhiḥ pravālaistu sahasrakam  || 7.2,14.35
sphaṭikairdaśasāhasraṃ mauktikairlakṣamucyate  || 7.2,14.35
padmākṣairdaśalakṣantu sauvarṇaiḥ koṭirucyate  || 7.2,14.36
kuśagraṃthyā ca rudrākṣairanaṃtaguṇitaṃ bhavet || 7.2,14.36
triṃśadakṣaiḥ kṛtā mālā dhanadā japakarmaṇi  || 7.2,14.37
saptaviṃśatisaṃkhyātairakṣaiḥ puṣṭipradā bhavet || 7.2,14.37
pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyātaiḥ kṛtā muktiṃ prayacchati  || 7.2,14.38
akṣaistu pañcadaśabhirabhicāraphalapradā  || 7.2,14.38
aṃguṣṭhaṃ mokṣadaṃ vidyāttarjanīṃ śatrunāśinīm  || 7.2,14.39
madhyamāṃ dhanadāṃ śāṃtiṃ karotyeṣā hyanāmikā  || 7.2,14.39
aṣṭottaraśataṃ mālā tatra syāduttamottamā  || 7.2,14.40
śatasaṃkhyottamā mālā pañcāśadbhistu madhyamā  || 7.2,14.40
catuḥ pañcāśadakṣaistu hṛcchreṣṭhā hi prakīrtitā  || 7.2,14.41
ityevaṃ mālayā kuryājjapaṃ kasmai na darśayet || 7.2,14.41
kaniṣṭhā kṣariṇī proktā japakarmaṇi śobhanā  || 7.2,14.42
aṃguṣṭhena japejjapyamanyairaṃgulibhissaha  || 7.2,14.42
aṃguṣṭhena vinā japyaṃ kṛtaṃ tadaphalaṃ yataḥ  || 7.2,14.43
gṛhe japaṃ samaṃ vidyādgoṣṭhe śataguṇaṃ viduḥ  || 7.2,14.43
puṇyāraṇye tathārāme sahasraguṇamucyate  || 7.2,14.44
ayutaṃ parvate puṇye nadyāṃ lakṣamudāhṛtam  || 7.2,14.44
koṭiṃ devālaye prāhuranantaṃ mama sannidhau  || 7.2,14.45
sūryasyāgnerguroriṃdordīpasya ca jalasya ca  || 7.2,14.45
viprāṇāṃ ca gavāṃ caiva sannidhau śasyate japaḥ  || 7.2,14.46
560

tatpūrvābhimukhaṃ vaśyaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ cābhicārikam  || 7.2,14.46
paścimaṃ dhanadaṃ vidyādauttaraṃ śātidaṃ bhavet || 7.2,14.47
sūryāgnivipradevānāṃ gurūṇāmapi sannidhau  || 7.2,14.47
anyeṣāṃ ca prasaktānāṃ mantraṃ na vimukho japet || 7.2,14.48
uṣṇīṣī kuṃcukī namro muktakeśo galāvṛtaḥ  || 7.2,14.48
apavitrakaro 'śuddho vilapanna japetkvacit || 7.2,14.49
krodhaṃ madaṃ kṣutaṃ trīṇi niṣṭhīvanavijṛṃbhaṇe  || 7.2,14.49
darśanaṃ ca śvanīcānāṃ varjayejjapakarmaṇi  || 7.2,14.50
ācametsaṃbhave teṣāṃ smaredvā māṃ tvayā saha  || 7.2,14.50
jyotīṃṣi ca prapaśyedvā kuryādvā prāṇasaṃyamam  || 7.2,14.51
anāsanaḥ śayāne vā gacchannutthita eva vā  || 7.2,14.51
rathyāyāmaśive sthāne na japettimirāntare  || 7.2,14.52
prasārya na japetpādau kukkuṭāsana eva vā  || 7.2,14.52
yānaśayyādhirūḍho vā ciṃtāvyākulito 'tha vā  || 7.2,14.53
śaktaścetsarvamevaitadaśaktaḥ śaktito japet || 7.2,14.53
kimatra bahunoktena samāsena vacaḥ śṛṇu  || 7.2,14.54
sadācāro japañchuddhaṃ dhyāyanbhadraṃ samaśnute  || 7.2,14.54
ācāraḥ paramo dharma ācāraḥ paramaṃ dhanaṃ  || 7.2,14.55
ācāraḥ paramā vidyā ācāraḥ paramā gatiḥ  || 7.2,14.55
ācārahīnaḥ puruṣo loke bhavati niṃditaḥ  || 7.2,14.56
paratra ca sukhī na syāttasmādācāravānbhavet || 7.2,14.56
yasya yadvihitaṃ karma vede śāstre ca vaidikaiḥ  || 7.2,14.57
tasya tena samācāraḥ sadācāro na cetaraḥ  || 7.2,14.57
sadbhirācaritatvācca sadācāraḥ sa ucyate  || 7.2,14.58
sadācārasya tasyāhurāstikyaṃ mūlakāraṇam  || 7.2,14.58
āstikaścetpramādādyaiḥ sadācārādavicyutaḥ  || 7.2,14.59
na duṣyati naro nityaṃ tasmādāstikatāṃ vrajet || 7.2,14.59
yathehāsti sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ sukṛtairduṣkṛtairapi  || 7.2,14.60
tathā paratra cāstīti matirāstikyamucyate  || 7.2,14.60
rahasyamanyadvakṣyāmi gopanīyamidaṃ priye  || 7.2,14.61
na vācyaṃ yasya kasyāpi nāstikasyātha vā paśoḥ  || 7.2,14.61
sadācāravihīnasya patitasyāntyajasya ca  || 7.2,14.62
pañcākṣarātparaṃ nāsti paritrāṇaṃ kalau yuge  || 7.2,14.62
gacchatastiṣṭhato vāpi svecchayā karma kurvataḥ  || 7.2,14.63
aśucervā śucervāpi mantro 'yanna ca niṣphalaḥ  || 7.2,14.63
anācāravatāṃ puṃsāmaviśuddhaṣaḍadhvanām  || 7.2,14.64
anādiṣṭo 'pi guruṇā mantro 'yaṃ na ca niṣphalaḥ  || 7.2,14.64
antyajasyāpi mūrkhasya mūḍhasya patitasya ca  || 7.2,14.65
nirmaryādasya nīcasya maṃtro 'yaṃ na ca niṣphalaḥ  || 7.2,14.65
sarvāvasthāṃ gatasyāpi mayi bhaktimataḥ param  || 7.2,14.66
sidhyatyeva na saṃdeho nāparasya tu kasyacit || 7.2,14.66
na lagnatithinakṣatravārayogādayaḥ priye  || 7.2,14.67
asyātyaṃtamavekṣyāḥ syurnaiṣa saptassadoditaḥ  || 7.2,14.67
561

na kadācinna kasyāpi ripureṣa mahāmanuḥ  || 7.2,14.68
susiddho vāpi siddho vā sādhyo vāpi bhaviṣyati  || 7.2,14.68
siddhena guruṇādiṣṭassusiddha iti kathyate  || 7.2,14.69
asiddhenāpi vā dattassiddhasādhyastu kevalaḥ  || 7.2,14.69
asādhitassādhito vā sidhyatvena na saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,14.70
śraddhātiśayayuktasya mayi maṃtre tathā gurau  || 7.2,14.70
tasmānmaṃtrāntarāṃstyaktvā sāpāyān 1 dhikārataḥ  || 7.2,14.71
āśrametparamāṃ vidyāṃ sākṣātpañcākṣarīṃ budhaḥ  || 7.2,14.71
maṃtrāntareṣu siddheṣu maṃtra eṣa na sidhyati  || 7.2,14.72
siddhe tvasminmahāmaṃtre te ca siddhā bhavaṃtyuta  || 7.2,14.72
yathā deveṣvalabdho 'smi labdheṣvapi maheśvari  || 7.2,14.73
mayi labdhe tu te labdhā maṃtreṣveṣu samo vidhiḥ  || 7.2,14.73
ye doṣāssarvamaṃtrāṇāṃ na te 'sminsaṃbhavaṃtyapi  || 7.2,14.74
asya maṃtrasya jātyādīnanapekṣya pravartanāt || 7.2,14.74
tathāpi naiva kṣudreṣu phaleṣu prati yogiṣu  || 7.2,14.75
sahasā viniyuṃjīta tasmādeṣa mahābalaḥ  || 7.2,14.75
upamanyuruvāca
evaṃ sākṣānmahādevyai mahādevena śūlinā  || 7.2,14.76
hitā ya jagatāmuktaḥ pañcākṣaravidhiryathā  || 7.2,14.76
ya idaṃ kīrtayedbhaktyā śṛṇuyādvā samāhitaḥ  || 7.2,14.77
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ prayāti paramāṃ gatim  || 7.2,14.77

1 nāśayuktānityarthaḥ

oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṃḍe pañcākṣaramahimavarṇanaṃ nāma caturdaśodhyāyaḥ


adhyāya 15
śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca
bhagavānmaṃtramāhātmyaṃ bhavatā kathitaṃ prabho  || 7.2,15.1
tatprayogavidhānaṃ ca sākṣācchrutisamaṃ yathā  || 7.2,15.1
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi śivasaṃskāramuttamam  || 7.2,15.2
maṃtrasaṃgrahaṇe kiṃcitsūcitanna tu vismṛtam  || 7.2,15.2
upamanyuruvāca
hanta te kathayiṣyāmi sarvapāpaviśodhanam  || 7.2,15.3
saṃskāraṃ paramaṃ puṇyaṃ śivena patibhāṣitam  || 7.2,15.3
samyakkṛtādhikāraḥ syātpūjādiṣu naro yataḥ  || 7.2,15.4
saṃskāraḥ kathyate tena ṣaḍadhvapariśodhanam  || 7.2,15.4
dīyate yena vijñānaṃ kṣīyate pāśabaṃdhanam  || 7.2,15.5
tasmātsaṃskāra evāyaṃ dīkṣetyapi ca kathyate  || 7.2,15.5
śāṃbhavī caiva śāktī ca māṃtrī caiva śivāgame  || 7.2,15.6
dīkṣopadiśyate tredhā śivena paramātmanā  || 7.2,15.6
gurorālokamātreṇa sparśātsaṃbhāṣaṇādapi  || 7.2,15.7
sadyassaṃjñā bhavejjaṃtoḥ pāśopakṣayakāriṇī  || 7.2,15.7
sā dīkṣā śāṃbhavī proktā sā punarbhidyate dvidhā  || 7.2,15.8
tīvrā tīvratarā ceti pāśo pakṣayabhedataḥ  || 7.2,15.8
yayā syānnirvṛtiḥ sadyassaiva tīvratarā matā  || 7.2,15.9
561

tīvrā tu jīvatotyaṃtaṃ puṃsaḥ pāpaviśodhikā  || 7.2,15.9
śaktī jñānavatī dīkṣā śiṣyadehaṃ praviśya tu  || 7.2,15.10
guruṇā yogamārgeṇa kriyate jñānacakṣuṣā  || 7.2,15.10
māṃtrī kriyāvatī dīkṣā kuṃḍamaṃḍalapūrvikā  || 7.2,15.11
maṃdamaṃdataroddeśātkartavyā guruṇā bahiḥ  || 7.2,15.11
śaktipātānusāreṇa śiṣyo 'nugrahamarhati  || 7.2,15.12
śaivadharmānusārasya tanmūlatvātsamāsataḥ  || 7.2,15.12
yatra śaktirna patitā tatra śuddhirna jāyate  || 7.2,15.13
na vidyā na śivācāro na muktirna ca siddhayaḥ  || 7.2,15.13
tasmālliṃgāni saṃvīkṣya śaktipātasya bhūyasaḥ  || 7.2,15.14
jñānena kriyayā vātha guruśśiṣyaṃ viśodhayet || 7.2,15.14
yo 'nyathā kurute mohātsa vinaśyati durmatiḥ  || 7.2,15.15
tasmātsarvaprakāreṇa guruḥ śiṣyaṃ parīkṣayet || 7.2,15.15
lakṣaṇaṃ śaktipātasya prabodhānaṃdasaṃbhavaḥ  || 7.2,15.16
sā yasmātparamā śaktiḥ prabodhānaṃdarūpiṇī  || 7.2,15.16
ānaṃdabodhayorliṃgamaṃtaḥkaraṇavikriyāḥ  || 7.2,15.17
yathā syātkaṃparomāṃcasvaranetrāṃgavikriyāḥ  || 7.2,15.17
śiṣyopi lakṣaṇairebhiḥ kuryādguruparīkṣaṇam  || 7.2,15.18
tatsaṃparkaiḥ śivārcādau saṃgatairvātha tadgataiḥ  || 7.2,15.18
śiṣyastu śikṣaṇīyatvādgurorgauravakāraṇāt || 7.2,15.19
tasmātsarvaprayatnena gurorgauravamācaret || 7.2,15.19
yo gurussa śivaḥ prokto yaḥ śivaḥ sa guruḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.2,15.20
gururvā śiva evātha vidyākāreṇa saṃsthitaḥ  || 7.2,15.20
yathā śivastathā vidyā yathā vidyā tathā guruḥ  || 7.2,15.21
śivavidyā gurūṇāṃ ca pūjayā sadṛśaṃ phalam  || 7.2,15.21
sarvadevātmakaścāsau sarvamaṃtramayo guruḥ  || 7.2,15.22
tasmātsarvaprayatnena yasyājñāṃ śirasā vahet || 7.2,15.22
śreyo 'rthī yadi gurvājñāṃ manasāpi na laṃghayet || 7.2,15.23
gurvājñāpālako yasmājjñānasaṃpattimaśnute  || 7.2,15.23
gacchaṃstiṣṭhansvapanbhuṃjannānyatkarma samācaret || 7.2,15.24
samakṣaṃ yadi kurvīta sarvaṃ cānujñayā guroḥ  || 7.2,15.24
gurorgṛhe samakṣaṃ vā na yatheṣṭāsano bhavet || 7.2,15.25
gururdevo yataḥ sākṣāttadgṛhaṃ devamandiram  || 7.2,15.25
pāpināṃ ca yathā saṃgāttatpāpātpatito bhavet || 7.2,15.26
yatheha vahnisaṃparkānmalaṃ tyajati kāṃcanam  || 7.2,15.26
tathaiva gurusaṃparkātpāpaṃ tyajati mānavaḥ  || 7.2,15.27
yathā vahnisamīpastho ghṛtakumbho vilīyate  || 7.2,15.27
tathā pāpaṃ vilīyeta hyācāryasya samīpataḥ  || 7.2,15.28
yathā prajvalito vahniḥ śuṣkamārdraṃ ca nirdahet || 7.2,15.28
tathāyamapi saṃtuṣṭo guruḥ pāpaṃ kṣaṇāddahet || 7.2,15.29
manasā karmaṇā vācā guroḥ krodhaṃ na kārayet || 7.2,15.29
tasya krodhena dahyaṃte hyāyuḥśrījñānasatkriyāḥ  || 7.2,15.30
tatkrodhakāriṇo ye syusteṣāṃ yajñāśca niṣphalāḥ  || 7.2,15.30
562

yamaśca niyamāścaiva nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 7.2,15.31
gurorviruddhaṃ yadvākyaṃ na vadejjātucinnaraḥ  || 7.2,15.31
vadedyadi mahāmohādrauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet || 7.2,15.32
manasā karmaṇā vācā gurumuddiśya yatnataḥ  || 7.2,15.32
śreyorthī cennaro dhīmānna mithyācāramācaret || 7.2,15.33
gurorhitaṃ priyaṃ kuryādādiṣṭo vā na vā sadā  || 7.2,15.33
asamakṣaṃ samakṣaṃ vā tasya kāryaṃ samācaret || 7.2,15.34
itthamācāravānbhakto nityamudyuktamānasaḥ  || 7.2,15.34
gurupriyakaraḥ śiṣyaḥ śaivadharmāṃstato 'rhati  || 7.2,15.35
guruścedguṇavānprājñaḥ paramānaṃdabhāsakaḥ  || 7.2,15.35
tattvavicchivasaṃsakto muktido na tu cāparaḥ  || 7.2,15.36
saṃvitsaṃjananaṃ tattvaṃ paramānaṃdasaṃbhavam  || 7.2,15.36
tattattvaṃ viditaṃ yena sa evānaṃdadarśakaḥ  || 7.2,15.37
na punarnāmamātreṇa saṃvidārahitastu yaḥ  || 7.2,15.37
anyonyaṃ tārayennaukā kiṃ śilā tārayecchilām  || 7.2,15.38
etasyā nāmamātreṇa muktirvai nāmamātrikā  || 7.2,15.38
yaiḥ punarviditaṃ tattvaṃ te muktā mocayantyapi  || 7.2,15.39
tattvahīne kuto bodhaḥ kuto hyātmaparigrahaḥ  || 7.2,15.39
parigrahavinirmuktaḥ paśurityabhidhīyate  || 7.2,15.40
paśubhiḥ preritaścāpi paśutvaṃ nātivartate  || 7.2,15.40
tasmāttattvavideveha mukto mocaka iṣyate  || 7.2,15.41
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktaḥ sarvaśāstravidapyayam  || 7.2,15.41
sarvopāyavidhijño 'pi tattvahīnastu niṣphalaḥ  || 7.2,15.42
yasyānubhavaparyaṃtā buddhistattve pravartate  || 7.2,15.42
tasyāvalokanādyaiśca parānando 'bhijāyate  || 7.2,15.43
tasmādyasyaiva saṃparkātprabodhānaṃdasaṃbhavaḥ  || 7.2,15.43
guruṃ tameva vṛṇuyānnāparaṃ matimānnaraḥ  || 7.2,15.44
sa śiṣyairvinayācāracaturairucito guruḥ  || 7.2,15.44
yāvadvijñāyate tāvatsevanīyo mumukṣubhiḥ  || 7.2,15.45
jñāte tasminsthirā bhaktiryāvattattvaṃ samāśrayet || 7.2,15.45
na tu tattvaṃ tyajejjātu nopekṣeta kathaṃcana  || 7.2,15.46
yatrānaṃdaḥ prabodho vā nālpamapyupalabhyate  || 7.2,15.46
vatsarādapi śiṣyeṇa so 'nyaṃ gurumupāśrayet || 7.2,15.47
gurumanyaṃ prapanne 'pi nāvamanyeta paurvikam  || 7.2,15.47
gurorbhrātḥṃstathā putrānbodhakānprerakānapi  || 7.2,15.47
tatrādāvupasaṃgamya brāhmaṇaṃ vedapāragam  || 7.2,15.48
gurumārādhayetprājñaṃ śubhagaṃ priyadarśanam  || 7.2,15.48
sarvābhayapradātāraṃ karuṇākrāṃtamānasam  || 7.2,15.49
toṣayettaṃ prayatnena manasā karmaṇā girā  || 7.2,15.49
tāvadārādhayecchiṣyaḥ prasannosau bhavedyathā  || 7.2,15.50
tasminprasanne śiṣyasya sadyaḥ pāpakṣayo bhavet || 7.2,15.50
tasmāddhanāni ratnāni kṣetrāṇi ca gṛhāṇi ca  || 7.2,15.51
bhūṣaṇāni ca vāsāṃsi yānaśayyāsanāni ca  || 7.2,15.51
etāni gurave dadyādbhaktyā vittānusārataḥ  || 7.2,15.52
562

vittaśāṭhyaṃ na kurvīta yadīcchetparamāṃ gatim  || 7.2,15.52
sa eva janako mātā bhartā bandhurdhanaṃ sukham  || 7.2,15.53
sakhā mitraṃ ca yattasmātsarvaṃ tasmai nivedayet || 7.2,15.53
nivedya paścātsvātmānaṃ sānvayaṃ saparigraham  || 7.2,15.54
samarpya sodakaṃ tasmai nityaṃ tadvaśago bhavet || 7.2,15.54
yadā śivāya svātmānaṃ dattavān deśikātmane  || 7.2,15.55
tadā śaivo bhaveddehī na tato 'sti punarbhavaḥ  || 7.2,15.55
guruśca svāśritaṃ śiṣyaṃ varṣamekaṃ parīkṣayet || 7.2,15.56
brāhmaṇaṃ kṣatriyaṃ vaiśyaṃ dvivarṣaṃ ca trivarṣakam  || 7.2,15.56
prāṇadravyapradānādyairādeśaiśca samāsamaiḥ  || 7.2,15.57
uttamāṃścādhame kṛtvā nīcānuttamakarmaṇi  || 7.2,15.57
ākruṣṭāstāḍitā vāpi ye viṣādaṃ na yāntyapi  || 7.2,15.58
te yogyāḥ saṃyatāḥ śuddhāḥ śivasaṃskārakarmaṇi  || 7.2,15.58
ahiṃsakā dayāvaṃto nityamudyuktacetasaḥ  || 7.2,15.59
amānino buddhimaṃtastyaktaspardhāḥ priyaṃvadāḥ  || 7.2,15.59
ṛjavo mṛdavaḥ svacchā vinītāḥ sthiracetasaḥ  || 7.2,15.60
śaucācārasamāyuktāḥ śivabhaktā dvijātayaḥ  || 7.2,15.60
evaṃ vṛttasamopetā vāṅmanaḥkāyakarmabhiḥ  || 7.2,15.61
śodhyā bodhyā yathānyāyamiti śāstreṣu niścayaḥ  || 7.2,15.61
nādhikāraḥ svato nāryāḥ śivasaṃskārakarmaṇi  || 7.2,15.62
niyogādbharturastyeva bhaktiyuktā yadīśvare  || 7.2,15.62
tathaiva bhartṛhīnāyā putrāderabhyanujñayā  || 7.2,15.63
adhikāro bhavatyeva kanyāyāḥ piturājñayā  || 7.2,15.63
śūdrāṇāṃ martyajātīnāṃ patitānāṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,15.63
tathā saṃkarajātīnāṃ nādhvaśuddhirvidhīyate  || 7.2,15.64
taipyakṛtrimabhāvaścecchive paramakāraṇe  || 7.2,15.64
pādodakapradānādyaiḥ kuryuḥ pāpaviśodhanam  || 7.2,15.65
atrānulomajātā ye yuktā eva dvijātiṣu  || 7.2,15.65
teṣāmadhvaviśuddhyādi kuryānmātṛkulocitam  || 7.2,15.66
yā tu kanyā svapitrādyaiśśivadharme niyojitā  || 7.2,15.66
sā bhaktāya pradātavyā nāparāya virodhine  || 7.2,15.67
dattā cetpratikūlāya pramādādbodhayetpatim  || 7.2,15.67
aśaktā taṃ parityajya manasā dharmamācaret || 7.2,15.68
yathā munivaraṃ tyaktvā patimatriṃ pativratā  || 7.2,15.68
kṛtakṛtyā 'bhavatpūrvaṃ tapasārādhya śaṅkaram  || 7.2,15.69
yathā nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ tapasārādhya pāṃḍavān  || 7.2,15.69
patīṃllabdhavatī dharme gurubhirna niyojitā  || 7.2,15.70
asvātantryakṛto doṣo nehāsti paramārthataḥ  || 7.2,15.70
śivadharme niyuktāyāśśivaśāsanagauravāt || 7.2,15.71
bahunātra kimuktena yo 'pi ko 'pi śivāśrayaḥ  || 7.2,15.71
saṃskāryo gurvadhīnaścetsaṃskriyā na prabhidyate  || 7.2,15.72
gurorālokanādeva sparśātsaṃbhāṣaṇādapi  || 7.2,15.72
563

yasya saṃjāyate prajñā tasya nāsti parājayaḥ  || 7.2,15.73
manasā yastu saṃskāraḥ kriyate yogavartmanā  || 7.2,15.73
sa neha kathito guhyo guruvaktraikagocaraḥ  || 7.2,15.74
kriyāvānyastu saṃskāraḥ kuṃḍamaṃḍapapūrvakaḥ  || 7.2,15.74
sa vakṣyate samāsena tasya śakyo na vistaraḥ  || 7.2,15.74
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe dīkṣāvidhāne gurumāhātmyaṃ nāma pañcadaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 16
upamanyuruvāca
puṇye 'hani śucau deśe bahudoṣavivarjite  || 7.2,16.1
deśikaḥ prathamaṃ kuryātsaṃskāraṃ samayāhvayam  || 7.2,16.1
parīkṣya bhūmiṃ vidhivadgaṃdhavarṇarasādibhiḥ  || 7.2,16.2
śilpiśāstroktamārgeṇa maṇḍapaṃ tatra kalpayet || 7.2,16.2
kṛtvā vediṃ ca tanmadhye kuṇḍāni parikalpayet || 7.2,16.3
aṣṭadikṣu tathā dikṣu tatraiśānyāṃ punaḥ kramāt || 7.2,16.3
pradhānakuṃḍaṃ kurvīta yadvā paścimabhāgataḥ  || 7.2,16.4
pradhānamekamevātha kṛtvā śobhāṃ prakalpayet || 7.2,16.4
vitānadhvajamālābhirvividhābhiranekaśaḥ  || 7.2,16.5
vedimadhye tataḥ kuryānmaṃḍalaṃ śubhalakṣaṇam  || 7.2,16.5
raktahemādibhiścūrṇairīśvarāvāhanocitam  || 7.2,16.6
siṃdūraśālinīvāracūrṇairevātha nirdhanaḥ  || 7.2,16.6
ekahastaṃ dvihastaṃ vā sitaṃ vā raktameva vā  || 7.2,16.7
ekahastasya padmasya karṇikāṣṭāṃgulā matā  || 7.2,16.7
kesarāṇi tadardhāni śeṣaṃ cāṣṭadalādikam  || 7.2,16.8
dvihastasya tu padmasya dviguṇaṃ karṇikādikam  || 7.2,16.8
kṛtvā śobhopaśobhāḍhyamaiśānyāṃ tasya kalpayet || 7.2,16.9
ekahastaṃ tadardhaṃ vā punarvedyaḥ tu maṃḍalam  || 7.2,16.9
vrīhitaṃdulasiddhārthatilapuṣpakuśāstṛte  || 7.2,16.10
tatra lakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ śivakuṃbhaṃ prasādhayet || 7.2,16.10
sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ vāpi tāmrajaṃ mṛnmayaṃ tu vā  || 7.2,16.11
gandhapuṣpākṣatākīrṇaṃ kuśadūrvāṃkurācitam  || 7.2,16.11
sitasūtrāvṛtaṃ kaṃṭhe navavastrayugāvṛtam  || 7.2,16.12
śuddhāmbupūrṇamutkūrcaṃ sadravyaṃ sapidhānakam  || 7.2,16.12
bhṛṅgāraṃ vardhanīṃ cāpi śaṃkhaṃ ca cakrameva vā  || 7.2,16.13
vinā sūtrādikaṃ sarvaṃ padmapatramathāpi vā  || 7.2,16.13
tasyāsanāraviṃdasya kalpayeduttare dale  || 7.2,16.14
agrataścaṃdanāṃbhobhirastrarājasya vardhanīm  || 7.2,16.14
maṇḍalasya tataḥ prācyāṃ maṃtrakuṃbhe ca pūrvavat || 7.2,16.15
kṛtvā vidhivadīśasya mahāpūjāṃ samācaret || 7.2,16.15
athārṇavasya tīre vā nadyāṃ goṣṭhe 'pi vā girau  || 7.2,16.16
devāgare gṛhe vāpi deśe 'nyasminmanohare  || 7.2,16.16
kṛtvā pūrvoditaṃ sarvaṃ vinā vā maṃḍapādikam  || 7.2,16.17
maṃḍalaṃ pūrvavatkṛtvā sthaṃḍilaṃ ca vibhāvasoḥ  || 7.2,16.17
praviśya pūjābhavanaṃ prahṛṣṭavadano guruḥ  || 7.2,16.18
sarvamaṃgalasaṃyuktaḥ samācaritanaityakaḥ  || 7.2,16.18
563

mahāpūjāṃ maheśasya kṛtvā maṇḍalamadhyataḥ  || 7.2,16.19
śivakuṃbhe tathā bhūyaḥ śivamāvāhya pūjayet || 7.2,16.19
paścimābhimukhaṃ dhyātvā yajñarakṣakamīśvaram  || 7.2,16.20
arcayedastravardhanyāmastramīśasya dakṣiṇe  || 7.2,16.20
mantrakumbhe ca vinyasya mantraṃ mantraviśāradaḥ  || 7.2,16.21
kṛtvā mudrādikaṃ sarvaṃ mantrayāgaṃ samācaret || 7.2,16.21
tataśśivānale homaṃ kuryāddeśikasattamaḥ  || 7.2,16.22
pradhānakuṇḍe parito juhuyuścāpare dvijāḥ  || 7.2,16.22
ācāryātpādamardhaṃ vā homasteṣāṃ vidhīyate  || 7.2,16.23
pradhānakuṇḍa evātha juhuyāddeśikottamaḥ  || 7.2,16.23
svādhyāyamapare kuryuḥ stotraṃ maṃgalavācanam  || 7.2,16.24
japaṃ ca vidhivaccānye śivabhaktiparāyaṇāḥ  || 7.2,16.24
nṛtyaṃ gītaṃ ca vādyaṃ ca maṃgalānyaparāṇi ca  || 7.2,16.25
pūjanaṃ ca sadasyānāṃ kṛtvā samyagvidhānataḥ  || 7.2,16.25
puṇyāhaṃ kārayitvātha punaḥ saṃpūjya śaṃkaram  || 7.2,16.26
prārthayeddeśiko devaṃ śiṣyānugrahakāmyayā  || 7.2,16.26
prasīda devadeveśa dehamāviśya māmakam  || 7.2,16.27
vimocayainaṃ viśveśa ghṛṇayā ca ghṛṇānidhe  || 7.2,16.27
atha caivaṃ karomīti labdhānujñastu deśikaḥ  || 7.2,16.28
ānīyopoṣitaṃ śiṣyaṃ haviṣyāśinameva vā  || 7.2,16.28
ekāśanaṃ vā virataṃ snātaṃ prātaḥkṛtakriyam  || 7.2,16.29
japaṃtaṃ praṇavaṃ devaṃ dhyāyaṃtaṃ kṛtamaṃgalam  || 7.2,16.29
dvārasya paścimasyāgramaṇḍale dakṣiṇasya vā  || 7.2,16.30
darbhāsane samāsīnaṃ vidhāyodaṅmukhaṃ śiśum  || 7.2,16.30
svayaṃ prāgvadanastiṣṭhannūrdhvakāyaṃ kṛtāṃjalim  || 7.2,16.31
saṃprokṣya prokṣaṇautoyairmūrdhanyastreṇa mudrayā  || 7.2,16.31
puṣpakṣepeṇa saṃtāḍya badhnīyāllocanaṃ guruḥ  || 7.2,16.32
dukūlārdhena vastreṇa maṃtritena navena ca  || 7.2,16.32
tataḥ praveśayecchiṣyaṃ gururdvāreṇa maṃḍalam  || 7.2,16.33
so 'pi teneritaḥ śaṃbhorācarettriḥ pradakṣiṇam  || 7.2,16.33
tatassuvarṇasaṃmiśraṃ dattvā puṣpāṃjaliṃ prabhoḥ  || 7.2,16.34
prāṅmukhaścodaṅmukho vā praṇameddaṃḍavatkṣito  || 7.2,16.34
tatassaṃprokṣya mūlena śirasyastreṇa pūrvavat || 7.2,16.35
saṃtāḍya deśikastasya mocayennetrabaṃdhanam  || 7.2,16.35
sa dṛṣṭvā maṃḍalaṃ bhūyaḥ praṇametsāñjaliḥ prabhum  || 7.2,16.36
athāsīnaṃ śivācāryo maṃḍalasya tu dakṣiṇe  || 7.2,16.36
upaveśyātmanassavye śiṣyaṃ darbhāsane guruḥ  || 7.2,16.37
ārādhya ca mahādevaṃ śivahastaṃ pravinyaset || 7.2,16.37
śivatejomayaṃ pāṇiṃ śivamaṃtramudīrayet || 7.2,16.38
śivābhimānasaṃpanno nyasecchiṣyasya mastake  || 7.2,16.38
sarvāṃgālaṃbanaṃ caiva kuryāttenaiva deśikaḥ  || 7.2,16.39
śiṣyo 'pi praṇamedbhūmau deśikākṛtamīśvaram  || 7.2,16.39
tataśśivānale devaṃ samabhyarcya yathāvidhi  || 7.2,16.40
hutāhutitrayaṃ śiṣyamupaveśya yathā purā  || 7.2,16.40
564

darbhāgraiḥ saṃspṛśaṃstaṃ ca vidyayātmānamāviśet || 7.2,16.41
namaskṛtya mahādevaṃ nāḍīsaṃdhānamācaret || 7.2,16.41
śivaśāstroktamārgeṇa kṛtvā prāṇasya nirgamam  || 7.2,16.42
śiṣyadehapraveśaṃ ca smṛtvā maṃtrāṃstu tarpayet || 7.2,16.42
saṃtarpaṇāya mūlasya tenaivāhutayo daśa  || 7.2,16.43
deyāstisrastathāṃgānāmaṃgaireva yathākramam  || 7.2,16.43
tataḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ dattvā prāyaścittāya deśikaḥ  || 7.2,16.44
punardaśāhutīnkuryānmūlamaṃtreṇa maṃtravit || 7.2,16.44
punaḥ saṃpūjya deveśaṃ samyagācamya deśikaḥ  || 7.2,16.45
hutvā caiva yathānyāyaṃ svajātyā vaiśyamuddharet || 7.2,16.45
tasyaivaṃ janayetkṣātramuddhāraṃ ca tataḥ punaḥ  || 7.2,16.46
kṛtvā tathaiva vipratvaṃ janayedasya deśikaḥ  || 7.2,16.46
rājanyaṃ caivamuddhṛtya kṛtvā vipraṃ punastayoḥ  || 7.2,16.47
rudratvaṃ janayedvipre rudranāmaiva sādhayet || 7.2,16.47
prokṣaṇaṃ tāḍanaṃ kṛtvā śiśossvātmānamātmani  || 7.2,16.48
śivātmakamanusmṛtya sphuraṃtaṃ visphuliṃgavat || 7.2,16.48
nāḍyā yathoktayā vāyuṃ recayenmaṃtrato guruḥ  || 7.2,16.49
nirgamya praviśennāḍyā śiṣyasya hṛdayaṃ tathā  || 7.2,16.49
praviśya tasya caitanyaṃ nīlabindunibhaṃ smaran  || 7.2,16.50
svatejasāpāstamalaṃ jvalaṃtamanuciṃtayet || 7.2,16.50
tamādāya tayā nāḍyā maṃtrī saṃhāramudrayā  || 7.2,16.51
na pūrakeṇa niveśyainamekībhāvārthamātmanaḥ  || 7.2,16.51
kuṃbhakena tathā nāḍyā recakena yathā purā  || 7.2,16.52
tasmādādāya śiṣyasya hṛdaye tanniveśayet || 7.2,16.52
tamālabhya śivāllabdhaṃ tasmai dattvopavītakam  || 7.2,16.53
hutvā"hutitrayaṃ paścāddadyātpūrṇāhutiṃ tataḥ  || 7.2,16.53
devasya dakṣiṇe śiṣyamupaveśyavarāsane  || 7.2,16.54
kuśapuṣpaparistīrṇe baddhāṃjalirudaṅmukham  || 7.2,16.54
svastikāsanamārūḍhaṃ vidhāya prāṅmukhaḥ svayam  || 7.2,16.55
varāsanasthito maṃtrairmahāmaṃgalaniḥsvanaiḥ  || 7.2,16.55
samādāya ghaṭaṃ pūrṇaṃ pūrṇameva prasāditam  || 7.2,16.56
dhyāyamānaḥ śivaṃ śiṣyamābhiṣiṃceta deśikaḥ  || 7.2,16.56
athāpanudya snānāṃbu paridhāya sitāṃbaram  || 7.2,16.57
ācāntolaṃkṛtaśśiṣyaḥ prāṃjalirmaṃḍapaṃ vrajet || 7.2,16.57
upaveśya yathāpūrvaṃ taṃ gururdarbhaviṣṭare  || 7.2,16.58
saṃpūjya maṃḍalaṃ devaṃ karanyāsaṃ samācaret || 7.2,16.58
tatastu bhasmanā devaṃ dhyāyanmanasi deśikaḥ  || 7.2,16.59
samālabheta pāṇibhyāṃ śiśuṃ śivamudīrayet || 7.2,16.59
atha tasya śivācāryo dahanaplāvanādikam  || 7.2,16.60
sakalīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā mātṛkānyāsavartmanā  || 7.2,16.60
tataḥ śivāsanaṃ dhyātvā śiṣyamūrdhni deśikaḥ  || 7.2,16.61
tatrāvāhya yathānyāyamarcayenmanasā śivam  || 7.2,16.61
prārthayetprāṃjalirdevaṃ nityamatra sthito bhava  || 7.2,16.62
iti vijñāpya taṃ śaṃbhostejasā bhāsuraṃ smaret || 7.2,16.62
564

saṃpūjyātha śivaṃ śaivīmājñāṃ prāpya śivātmikām  || 7.2,16.63
karṇe śiṣyasya śanakaiśśivamantramudīrayet || 7.2,16.63
sa tu baddhāṃjaliḥ śrutvā mantraṃ tadgatamānasaḥ  || 7.2,16.64
śanaistaṃ vyāharecchiṣyaśivācāryasya śāsanāt || 7.2,16.64
tataḥ śāktaṃ ca saṃdiśya mantraṃ mantravicakṣaṇaḥ  || 7.2,16.65
uccārayitvā ca sukhaṃ tasmai maṃgalamādiśet || 7.2,16.65
tatassamāsānmantrārthaṃ vācyavācakayogataḥ  || 7.2,16.66
samadiśyeśvaraṃ rūpaṃ yogamāsanamādiśet || 7.2,16.66
atha gurvājñayā śiṣyaḥ śivāgnigurusannidhau  || 7.2,16.67
bhaktyaivamabhisaṃdhāya dīkṣāvākyamudīrayet || 7.2,16.67
varaṃ prāṇaparityāgaśchedanaṃ śiraso 'pi vā  || 7.2,16.68
na tvanabhyarcya bhuṃjīya bhagavantaṃ trilocanam  || 7.2,16.68
sa eva dadyānniyato yāvanmohaviparyayaḥ  || 7.2,16.69
tāvadārādhayeddevaṃ tanniṣṭhastatparāyaṇaḥ  || 7.2,16.69
tataḥ sa samayo nāma bhaviṣyati śivāśrame  || 7.2,16.70
labdhādhikāro gurvājñāpālakastadvaśo bhavet || 7.2,16.70
ataḥ paraṃ nyastakaro bhasmādāya svahastataḥ  || 7.2,16.71
dadyācchiṣyāya mūlena rudrākṣaṃ cābhimaṃtritam  || 7.2,16.71
pratimā vāpi devasya gūḍhadehamathāpi vā  || 7.2,16.72
pūjāhomajapadhyānasādhanāni ca saṃbhave  || 7.2,16.72
sopi śiṣyaḥ śivācāryāllabdhāni bahumānataḥ  || 7.2,16.73
ādadītājñayā tasya deśikasya na cānyathā  || 7.2,16.73
ācāryādāptamakhilaṃ śirasyādhāya bhaktitaḥ  || 7.2,16.74
rakṣayetpūjayecchaṃbhuṃ maṭhe vā gṛha evavā  || 7.2,16.74
ataḥ paraṃ śivācāramādiśedasya deśikaḥ  || 7.2,16.75
bhaktiśraddhānusāreṇa prajñāyāścānusārataḥ  || 7.2,16.75
yaduktaṃ yatsamājñātaṃ yaccaivānyatprakīrtitam  || 7.2,16.76
śivācāryeṇa samaye tatsarvaṃ śirasā vahet || 7.2,16.76
śivāgamasya grahaṇaṃ vācanaṃ śravaṇaṃ tathā  || 7.2,16.77
deśikadeśataḥ kuryānna svecchāto na cānyataḥ  || 7.2,16.77
iti saṃkṣepataḥ proktaḥ saṃskāraḥ samayāhvayaḥ  || 7.2,16.78
sākṣācchivapuraprāptau nṛṇāṃ paramasādhanam  || 7.2,16.78
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṃḍe śiṣyasaṃskāravarṇanaṃ nāma ṣoḍaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 17
upamanyuruvāca
ataḥ paraṃ samāvekṣya guruḥ śiṣyasya yogyatām  || 7.2,17.1
ṣaḍadhvaśuddhiṃ kurvīta sarvabaṃdhavimuktaye  || 7.2,17.1
kalāṃ tattvaṃ ca bhuvanaṃ varṇaṃ padamataḥ param  || 7.2,17.2
maṃtraśceti samāsena ṣaḍadhvā paripaṭhyate  || 7.2,17.2
nivṛttyādyāḥ kalāḥ pañca kalādhvā kathyate budhaiḥ  || 7.2,17.3
vyāptāḥ kalābhiritare tvadhvānaḥ pañca pañcabhiḥ  || 7.2,17.3
565

śivatattvādibhūmyaṃtaṃ tattvādhvā samudāhṛtaḥ  || 7.2,17.4
ṣaḍviṃśatsaṃkhyayopetaḥ śuddhāśuddhobhayātmakaḥ  || 7.2,17.4
ādhārādyunmanāṃtaśca bhuvanādhvā prakīrtitaḥ  || 7.2,17.5
vinā bhedopabhedābhyāṃ ṣaṣṭisaṃkhyāsamanvitaḥ  || 7.2,17.5
pañcāśadrudrarūpāstu varṇā varṇādhvasaṃjñitāḥ  || 7.2,17.6
anekabhedasaṃpannaḥ padādhvā samudāhṛtaḥ  || 7.2,17.6
sarvopamaṃtrairmaṃtrādhvā vyāptaḥ paramavidyayā  || 7.2,17.7
yathā śivo na tattveṣu gaṇyate tattvanāyakaḥ  || 7.2,17.7
maṃtrādhvani na gaṇyeta tathāsau maṃtranāyakaḥ  || 7.2,17.8
kalādhvano vyāpakatvaṃ vyāpyatvaṃ cetarādhvanām  || 7.2,17.8
na vetti tattvato yasya naivārhatyadhvaśodhanam  || 7.2,17.9
ṣaḍvidhasyādhvano rūpaṃ na yena viditaṃ bhavet || 7.2,17.9
vyāpyavyāpakatā tena jñātumeva na śakyate  || 7.2,17.10
tasmādadhvasvarūpaṃ ca vyāpyavyāpakatāṃ tathā  || 7.2,17.10
yathāvadavagamyaiva kuryādadhvaviśodhanam  || 7.2,17.11
kuṃḍamaṃḍalaparyaṃtaṃ tatra kṛtvā yathā purā  || 7.2,17.11
dvihastamānaṃ kurvīta prācyāṃ kalaśamaṃḍalam  || 7.2,17.12
tataḥ snātaśśivācāryaḥ saśiṣyaḥ kṛtanaityakaḥ  || 7.2,17.12
praviśya maṃḍalaṃ śaṃbhoḥ pūjāṃ pūrvavadācaret || 7.2,17.13
tatrāḍhakāvaraissiddhaṃ taṃdulaiḥ pāyasaṃ prabhoḥ  || 7.2,17.13
ardhaṃ nivedya homārthaṃ śeṣaṃ samupakalpayet || 7.2,17.14
purataḥ kalpite vātha maṃḍale varṇimaṃḍite  || 7.2,17.14
sthāpayetpañcakalaśāndikṣu madhye ca deśikaḥ  || 7.2,17.15
teṣu brahmāṇi mūlārṇairbindunādasamanvitaiḥ  || 7.2,17.15
nama ādyairyakarāṃtaiḥ kalpayetkalpavittamaḥ  || 7.2,17.16
īśānaṃ madhyame kuṃbhe puruṣaṃ purataḥ sthite  || 7.2,17.16
aghoraṃ dakṣiṇe vāme vāmaṃ sadyaṃ ca paścime  || 7.2,17.17
rakṣāṃ vidhāya mudrā ca baddhvā kuṃbhābhimaṃtraṇam  || 7.2,17.17
kṛtvā śivānalairhomaṃ prārabhetyathā purā  || 7.2,17.18
yadardhaṃ pāyasaṃ pūrvaṃ homārthaṃ parikalpitam  || 7.2,17.18
hutvā śiṣyasya taccheṣaṃ bhoktuṃ samupakalpayet || 7.2,17.19
tarpaṇāṃtaṃ ca maṃtrāṇāṃ kṛtvā karma yathā purā  || 7.2,17.19
hutvā pūrṇāhutiṃ teṣāṃ tataḥ kuryātpradīpanam  || 7.2,17.20
oṃkārādanu huṃkāraṃ tato mūlaṃ phaḍaṃtakam  || 7.2,17.20
svāhāṃtaṃ dīpane prāhuraṃgāni ca yathākramam  || 7.2,17.21
teṣāmāhutayastisro deyā dīpanakarmaṇi  || 7.2,17.21
maṃtrairekaikaśastaistu vicintyā dīptamūrtayaḥ  || 7.2,17.22
triguṇaṃ triguṇī kṛtya dvijakanyākṛtaṃ sitam  || 7.2,17.22
sūtraṃ sūtreṇa saṃmaṃtrya śikhāgre baṃdhayecchiśoḥ  || 7.2,17.23
caraṇāṃguṣṭhaparyaṃtamūrdhvakāyasya tiṣṭhataḥ  || 7.2,17.23
laṃbayitvā tu tatsūtraṃ suṣumṇāṃ tatra yojayet || 7.2,17.24
śāṃtayā mudrayādāya mūlamaṃtreṇa maṃtravit || 7.2,17.24
hutvāhutitrayaṃ tasyāssānnidhyamupakalpayet || 7.2,17.25
hṛdi saṃtāḍya śiṣyasya puṣpakṣepeṇa pūrvavat || 7.2,17.25
565

caitanyaṃ samupādāya dvādaśāṃte nivedya ca  || 7.2,17.26
sūtraṃ sūtreṇa saṃyojya saṃrakṣyāstreṇa varmaṇā  || 7.2,17.26
avaguṃṭhyātha tatsūtraṃ śiṣyadehaṃ viciṃtayet || 7.2,17.27
mūlatrayamayaṃ pāśaṃ bhogabhogyatvalakṣaṇam  || 7.2,17.27
viṣayendriyadehādijanakaṃ tasya bhāvayet || 7.2,17.28
vyomādibhūtarūpiṇyaḥ śāṃtyatītādayaḥ kalāḥ  || 7.2,17.28
sūtre svanāmabhiryojyaḥ pūjyaścaiva namoyutaiḥ  || 7.2,17.29
athavā bījabhūtaistatkṛtvā pūrvoditaṃ kramāt || 7.2,17.29
tato malādestattvādau vyāptiṃ samalokayet || 7.2,17.30
kalāvyāptiṃ malādau ca hutvā saṃdīpayetkalāḥ  || 7.2,17.30
śiṣyaṃ śirasi saṃtāḍya sūtraṃ dehe yathākramam  || 7.2,17.31
śāṃtyatītapade sūtraṃ lāñchayenmaṃtramuccaran  || 7.2,17.31
evaṃ kṛtvā nivṛttyantaṃ śāṃtyatītamanukramāt || 7.2,17.32
hutvāhutitrayaṃ paścānmaṇḍale ca śivaṃ yajet || 7.2,17.32
devasya dakṣiṇe śiṣyamupaveśyottarāmukham  || 7.2,17.33
sadarbhe maṇḍale dadyāddhomaśiṣṭaṃ caruṃ guruḥ  || 7.2,17.33
śiṣyastadguruṇā dattaṃ satkṛtya śivapūrvakam  || 7.2,17.34
bhuktvā paścāddvirācamya śivamantramudīrayet || 7.2,17.34
apare maṇḍale dadyātpañcagavyaṃ tathā guruḥ  || 7.2,17.35
so 'pi tacchaktitaḥ pītvā dvirācamya śivaṃ smaret || 7.2,17.35
tṛtīye maṇḍale śiṣyamupaveśya yathā purā  || 7.2,17.36
pradadyāddaṃtapavanaṃ yathāśāstroktalakṣaṇam  || 7.2,17.36
agreṇa tasya mṛdunā prāṅmukho vāpyudaṅmukhaḥ  || 7.2,17.37
vācaṃ niyamya cāsīnaśśiṣyo daṃtānviśodhayet || 7.2,17.37
prakṣālya daṃtapavanaṃ tyaktvācamya śivaṃ smaret || 7.2,17.38
praviśeddeśikādiṣṭaḥ prāṃjaliḥ śivamaṇḍalam  || 7.2,17.38
tyaktaṃ taddantapavanaṃ dṛśyate guruṇā yadi  || 7.2,17.39
prāgudakpaścime vāgre śivamanyacchivetaram  || 7.2,17.39
aśastāśāmukhe tasmingurustaddoṣaśāṃtaye  || 7.2,17.40
śatamardhaṃ tadardhaṃ vājuhuyānmūlamantrataḥ  || 7.2,17.40
tataḥ śiṣyaṃ samālabhya japitvā karṇayoḥ śivam  || 7.2,17.41
devasya dakṣiṇe bhāge taṃ śiṣyamadhivāsayet || 7.2,17.41
ahatāstaraṇāstīrṇe sa darbhaśayane śuciḥ  || 7.2,17.42
maṃtrite 'ntaḥ śivaṃ dhyāyañśayīta prākchirā niśi  || 7.2,17.42
śikhāyāṃ baddhasūtrasya śikhayā tacchikhāṃ guruḥ  || 7.2,17.43
ābadhyāhatavastreṇa tamācchādya ca varmaṇā  || 7.2,17.43
rekhātrayaṃ ca parito bhasmanā tilasarṣapaiḥ  || 7.2,17.44
