kṛtvāstrajaptaistadvāhye digīśānāṃ baliṃ haret || 7.2,17.44
śiṣyo 'pi parato 'naśnankṛtvaivamadhivāsanam  || 7.2,17.45
prabudhyotthāya gurave svapnaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ nivedayet || 7.2,17.45

oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe śivadīkṣāvidhānavarṇanaṃ nāma saptadaśo 'dhyāyaḥ
566


adhyāya 18
upamanyuruvāca
tataḥ snānādikaṃ sarvaṃ samāpyācāryacoditaḥ  || 7.2,18.1
gacchedbaddhāṃjalirdhyāyañchivamaṇḍalapārśvataḥ  || 7.2,18.1
atha pūjāṃ vinā sarvaṃ kṛtvā pūrvadine yathā  || 7.2,18.2
netrabaṃdhanaparyaṃtaṃ darśayenmaṇḍalaṃ guruḥ  || 7.2,18.2
baddhanetreṇa śiṣyeṇa puṣpāvakiraṇe kṛte  || 7.2,18.3
yatrāpataṃti puṣṇāṇi tasya nāmā 'sya saṃdiśet || 7.2,18.3
taṃ copanīya nirmālyamaṇḍale 'sminyathā purā  || 7.2,18.4
pūjayeddevamīśānaṃ juhuyācca śivānale  || 7.2,18.4
śiṣyeṇa yadi duḥsvapno dṛṣṭastaddoṣaśāṃtaye  || 7.2,18.5
śatamardhaṃ tadardhaṃ vā juhuyānmūlavidyayā  || 7.2,18.5
tataḥ sūtraṃ śikhābaddhaṃ laṃbayitvā yathā purā  || 7.2,18.6
ādhārapūjāprabhṛti yannivṛttikalāśrayam  || 7.2,18.6
vāgīśvarīpūjanāṃtaṃ kuryāddhomapurassaram  || 7.2,18.7
atha praṇamya vāgīśaṃ nivṛttervyāpikāṃ satīm  || 7.2,18.7
maṇḍale devamabhyarcya hutvā caivāhutitrayam  || 7.2,18.8
prāpayecca śiśoḥ prāptiṃ yugapatsarvayoniṣu  || 7.2,18.8
sūtradehe 'tha śiṣyasya tāḍanaprokṣaṇādikam  || 7.2,18.9
kṛtvātmānaṃ samādāya dvādaśāṃte nivedya ca  || 7.2,18.9
tato 'pyādāya mūlena mudrayā śāstradṛṣṭayā  || 7.2,18.10
yojayenmanasācāryo yugapatsarvayoniṣu  || 7.2,18.10
devānāṃ jātayaścāṣṭau tiraścāṃ pañca jātayaḥ  || 7.2,18.11
jātyaikayā ca mānuṣyā yonayaśca caturdaśa  || 7.2,18.11
tāsu sarvāsu yugapatpraveśāya śiśordhiyā  || 7.2,18.12
vāgīśānyāṃ yathānyāyaṃ śiṣyātmānaṃ niveśayet || 7.2,18.12
garbhaniṣpattaye devaṃ saṃpūjya praṇipatya ca  || 7.2,18.13
hutvā caiva yathānyāyaṃ niṣpannaṃ tadanusmaret || 7.2,18.13
niṣpannasyaivamutpattimanuvṛttiṃ ca karmaṇā  || 7.2,18.14
ārjavaṃ bhoganiṣpattiḥ kuryātprītiṃ parāṃ tathā  || 7.2,18.14
niṣkṛtyarthaṃ ca jātyāyurbhogasaṃskārasiddhaye  || 7.2,18.15
hutvāhutitrayaṃ devaṃ prārthayeddeśikottamaḥ  || 7.2,18.15
bhoktṛtvaviṣayāsaṃgamalaṃ 1 tatkāyaśodhanam  || 7.2,18.16
kṛtvaivameva śiṣyasya chiṃdyātpāśatrayaṃ tataḥ  || 7.2,18.16
nikṛtyā pari baddhasya pāśasyātyaṃtabhedataḥ  || 7.2,18.17
kṛtvā śiṣyasya caitanyaṃ svacchaṃ manyeta kevalam  || 7.2,18.17
hutvā pūrṇāhutiṃ vahnau brahmāṇaṃ pūjayettataḥ  || 7.2,18.18
hutvāhutitrayaṃ tasmai śivājñāmanusaṃdiśet || 7.2,18.18
pitāmaha tvayā nāsya yātuḥ śaivaṃ paraṃ padam  || 7.2,18.19
pratibandho vidhātavyaḥ śaivājñaiṣā garīyasī  || 7.2,18.19
ityādiśya tamabhyarcya visṛja ca vidhānataḥ  || 7.2,18.20

1 tyājayitveti śeṣaḥ

566

samabhyarcya mahādevaṃ juhuyādāhutitrayam  || 7.2,18.20
nivṛttyā śuddhamuddhṛtya śiṣyātmānaṃ yathā purā  || 7.2,18.21
niveśyātmani sūtre ca vāgīśaṃ pūjayettataḥ  || 7.2,18.21
hutvāhutitrayaṃ tasmai praṇamya ca visṛjya tām  || 7.2,18.22
kuryānnivṛttaḥ saṃdhānaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ kalayā saha  || 7.2,18.22
saṃdhāne yugapatpūjāṃ kṛtvā hutvāhutitrayam  || 7.2,18.23
śiṣyātmanaḥ pratiṣṭhāyāṃ praveśaṃ tvatha bhāvayet || 7.2,18.23
tataḥ pratiṣṭhāmāvāhya kṛtvāśeṣaṃ puroditam  || 7.2,18.24
tadvyāptiṃ vyāpikāṃ tasya vāgīśānīṃ ca bhāvayet || 7.2,18.24
pūrṇedumaṃḍalaprakhyāṃ kṛtvā śeṣaṃ ca pūrvavat || 7.2,18.25
viṣṇave saṃviśedājñāṃ śivasya paramātmanaḥ  || 7.2,18.25
viṣṇorvisarjanādyaṃ ca kṛtvā śeṣaṃ ca vidyayā  || 7.2,18.26
pratiṣṭhāmanusaṃdhāya tasyāṃ cāpi yathā purā  || 7.2,18.26
kṛtvānucintya tadvyāptiṃ vāgīśāṃ ca yathākramam  || 7.2,18.27
dīptāgnau pūrṇahomāntaṃ kṛtvā śeṣaṃ ca pūrvavat || 7.2,18.27
nīlarudramupasthāpya tasmai pūjādikaṃ tathā  || 7.2,18.28
kṛtvā karma śivājñāṃ ca dadyātpūrvoktavartmanā  || 7.2,18.28
tapastamapi codvāsya kṛtvā tasyātha śāṃtaye  || 7.2,18.29
vidyākalāṃ samādhāya tadvyāptiṃ cāvalokayet || 7.2,18.29
svātmano vyāpikāṃ tadvadvāgīśīṃ ca yathā purā  || 7.2,18.30
bālārkasadṛśākārāṃ bhāsayaṃtīṃ diśo daśa  || 7.2,18.30
tataḥ śeṣaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ kṛtvā devaṃ maheśvaram  || 7.2,18.31
āvāhyārādhya hutvāsmai śivājñāṃ manasā diśet || 7.2,18.31
maheśvaraṃ tathotsṛjya kṛtvānyāṃ ca kalāmimām  || 7.2,18.32
śāṃtyatītāṃ kalāṃ nītvā tadvyāptimavalokayet || 7.2,18.32
svātmano vyāpikāṃ tadvadvāgīśāṃ ca viciṃtayet || 7.2,18.33
nabhomaṃḍalasaṃkāśāṃ pūrṇāṃtaṃ cāpi pūrvavat || 7.2,18.33
kṛtvā śeṣavidhānena samabhyarcya sadāśivam  || 7.2,18.34
tasmai samādiśedājñāṃ śaṃbhoramitakarmaṇaḥ  || 7.2,18.34
tatrāpi ca yathāpūrvaṃ śivaṃ śirasi pūrvavat || 7.2,18.35
samabhyarcya ca vāgīśaṃ praṇamya ca visarjayet || 7.2,18.35
tataśśivena samprokṣya śiṣyaṃ śirasi pūrvavat || 7.2,18.36
vilayaṃ śāṃtyatītāyāḥ śaktitattve 'tha ciṃtayet || 7.2,18.36
ṣaḍadhvanaḥ pare pāre sarvādhvavyāpinī parām  || 7.2,18.37
koṭisūryapratīkāśaṃ śaivīṃ śaktiñca cintayet || 7.2,18.37
tadagre śiṣyamānīya śuddhasphaṭikanirmalam  || 7.2,18.38
prakṣālya kartarīṃ paścācchivaśāstroktamārgataḥ  || 7.2,18.38
kuryāttasya śikhācchedaṃ saha sūtreṇa deśikaḥ  || 7.2,18.39
tatastāṃ gomaye nyasya śivāgnau juhuyācchikhām  || 7.2,18.39
vauṣaḍaṃtena mūlena punaḥ prakṣālya kartarīm  || 7.2,18.40
haste śiṣyasya caitanyaṃ taddehe vinivartayet || 7.2,18.40
567

tataḥ snātaṃ samācāṃtaṃ kṛtasvastyayanaṃ śiśum  || 7.2,18.41
praveśya maṃḍalābhyāsaṃ praṇipatya ca daṃḍavat || 7.2,18.41
pūjāṃ kṛtvā yathānyāyaṃ kriyāvaikalyaśuddhaye  || 7.2,18.42
vācakenaiva maṃtreṇa juhuyādāhutitrayam  || 7.2,18.42
upāṃśūccārayogena juhuyādāhutitrayam  || 7.2,18.43
punassaṃpūjya deveśaṃ mantravaikalyaśuddhaye  || 7.2,18.43
mānasoccārayogena juhuyādāhutitrayam  || 7.2,18.44
tatra śaṃbhuṃ samārādhya maṃḍalasthaṃ sahāṃbayā  || 7.2,18.44
hutvāhutitrayaṃ paścātprārthayetprāṃjalirguruḥ  || 7.2,18.44
bhagavaṃstvatprasādena śuddhirasya ṣaḍadhvanaḥ  || 7.2,18.45
kṛtā tasmātparaṃ dhāma gamayainaṃ tavāvyayam  || 7.2,18.45
iti vijñāpya devāya nāḍīsaṃdhānapūrvakam  || 7.2,18.46
pūrṇāṃtaṃ pūrvavatkṛtvā tato bhūtāni śodhayet || 7.2,18.46
sthirāsthire tataḥ śuddhyai śītoṣṇe ca tataḥ pade  || 7.2,18.47
dhyāyedvyāptyaikatākāre bhūtaśodhanakarmaṇi  || 7.2,18.47
bhūtānāṃ graṃthivicchedaṃ kṛtvā tyaktvā sahādhipaiḥ  || 7.2,18.48
bhūtāni sthitayogena yo japetparame śive  || 7.2,18.48
viśodhyāsya tanuṃ dagdhvā plāvayitvā sudhākaṇaiḥ  || 7.2,18.49
sthāpyātmānaṃ tataḥ kuryādviśuddhādhvamayaṃ vapuḥ  || 7.2,18.49
tatrādau śāntyatītāṃ tu vyāpikāṃ svādhvanaḥ kalām  || 7.2,18.50
śuddhāmeva śiśormūrdhni nyasecchāntimukhe tathā  || 7.2,18.50
vidyāṃ galādinābhyaṃtaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ tadadhaḥ kramāt || 7.2,18.51
jānvaṃtaṃ tadadho nyasyennivṛttiṃ cānuciṃtayet || 7.2,18.51
svabījaissūtramaṃtraṃ ca nyasyāṃ gaistaṃ śivātmakam  || 7.2,18.52
buddhvā taṃ hṛdayāṃbhoje devamāvāhya pūjayet || 7.2,18.52
āśāsya nityasāṃnidhyaṃ śivasvātmyaṃ śiśau guruḥ  || 7.2,18.53
śivatejomayasyāsya śiśorāpādayedguṇān  || 7.2,18.53
aṇimādīnprasīdeti pradadyādāhutitrayam  || 7.2,18.54
tathaiva tu guṇāneva punarasyopapādayet || 7.2,18.54
sarvajñātāṃ tathā tṛptiṃ bodhaṃ cādyantavarjitam  || 7.2,18.55
aluptaśaktiṃ svātantryamanaṃtāṃ śaktimeva ca  || 7.2,18.55
tato devamanujñāpya sadyādikalaśaistu tam  || 7.2,18.56
abhiṣiṃceta deveśaṃ dhyāyanhṛdi yathākramam  || 7.2,18.56
athopaveśya taṃ śiṣyaṃ śivamabhyarcya pūrvavat || 7.2,18.57
labdhānujñaḥ śivācchaivīṃ vidyāmasmai samādiśet || 7.2,18.57
oṃkārapūrvikāṃ tatra saṃpuṭāntu namo 'ṃtagām  || 7.2,18.58
śivaśaktiyutāñcaiva śaktividyāṃ ca tādṛśīm  || 7.2,18.58
ṛṣiṃ chandaśca devaṃ ca śivatāṃ śivayostathā  || 7.2,18.59
pūjāṃ sāvaraṇāṃ śambhorāsanāni ca sandiśet || 7.2,18.59
punaḥ saṃpūjya deveśaṃ yanmayā samanuṣṭhitam  || 7.2,18.60
sukṛtaṃ kuru tatsarvamiti vijñāpayecchivam  || 7.2,18.60
sahaśiṣyo gururdevaṃ daṇḍavatkṣitimaṃḍale  || 7.2,18.61
praṇamyodvāsayettasmānmaṃḍalātpāvakādapi  || 7.2,18.61
567

tataḥ sadasikāḥ sarve pūjyāḥ pūjārhakāḥ kramāt || 7.2,18.62
sevyā vittānusāreṇa sadasyāśca sahartvijaḥ  || 7.2,18.63
vittaśāṭhyaṃ na kurvīta yadīcchecchivamātmanaḥ  || 7.2,18.63
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe ṣaḍadhvaśuddhyādikathanaṃ nāmāṣṭādaśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 19
upamanyuruvāca
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi sādhakaṃ nāma nāmataḥ  || 7.2,19.1
saṃskāramantramāhātmyaṃ kathane sūcitaṃ mayā  || 7.2,19.1
saṃpūjya maṃḍale devaṃ sthāpya kumbhe ca pūrvavat || 7.2,19.2
hutvā śiṣyamanuṣṇīṣaṃ prāpayedbhuvi maṃḍale  || 7.2,19.2
pūrvāṃtaṃ pūrvavatkṛtvā hutvāhutiśataṃ tathā  || 7.2,19.3
saṃtarpya mūlamantreṇa kalaśairdeśikottamaḥ  || 7.2,19.3
sandīpya ca yathāpūrvaṃ kṛtvā pūrvoditaṃ kramāt || 7.2,19.4
abhiṣicya yathāpūrvaṃ pradadyānmantramuttamam  || 7.2,19.4
tatra vidyopadeśāṃtaṃ kṛtvā vistaraśaḥ kramāt || 7.2,19.5
puṣpāmbunā śiśoḥ pāṇau vidyāṃ śaivīṃ samarpayet || 7.2,19.5
tavaihikāmuṣmikayoḥ sarvasiddhiphalapradaḥ  || 7.2,19.6
bhavatyeva mahāmantraḥ prasādātparameṣṭhinaḥ  || 7.2,19.6
ityutvā devamabhyarcya labdhānujñaḥ śivādguruḥ  || 7.2,19.7
sādhanaṃ śivayogaṃ ca sādhakāya samādiśet || 7.2,19.7
tacchrutvā gurusaṃdeśaṃ kramaśo maṃtrasādhakaḥ  || 7.2,19.8
purato viniyogasya mantrasādhanamācaret || 7.2,19.8
sādhanaṃ mūlamantrasya puraścaraṇamucyate  || 7.2,19.9
purataścaraṇīyatvādviniyogākhyakarmaṇaḥ  || 7.2,19.9
nātyantaṃ karaṇīyantu mumukṣormantrasādhanam  || 7.2,19.10
kṛtantu tadihānyatra tāsyāpi śubhadaṃ bhavet || 7.2,19.10
śubhe 'hani śubhe deśe kāle vā doṣavarjite  || 7.2,19.11
śukladantanakhaḥ snātaḥ kṛtapūrvāhṇikakriyaḥ  || 7.2,19.11
alaṃkṛtya yathā labdhairgaṃdhamālyavibhūṣaṇaiḥ  || 7.2,19.12
soṣṇīṣaḥ sottarāsaṃgaḥ sarvaśuklasamāhitaḥ  || 7.2,19.12
devālaye gṛhe 'nyasmindeśe vā sumanohare  || 7.2,19.13
sukhenābhyastapūrveṇa tvāsanena kṛtāsanaḥ  || 7.2,19.13
tanuṃ kṛtvātmanaḥ śaivīṃ śivaśāstroktavartmanā  || 7.2,19.14
saṃpūjya devadeveśaṃ nakulīśvaramīśvaram  || 7.2,19.14
nivedya pāyasaṃ tasmai samapyārādhanaṃ kramāt || 7.2,19.15
praṇipatya ca taṃ devaṃ prāptānujñaśca tanmukhāt || 7.2,19.15
koṭivāraṃ tadardhaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā japecchivam  || 7.2,19.16
lakṣaviṃśatikaṃ vāpi daśalakṣamathāpi vā  || 7.2,19.16
tataśca pāyasākṣāralavaṇaikamitāśanaḥ  || 7.2,19.17
ahiṃsakaḥ kṣamī śāṃto dāṃtaścaiva sadā bhavet || 7.2,19.17
alābhe pāyasasyāśnanphalamūlādikāni vā  || 7.2,19.18
vihitāni śivenaiva viśiṣṭānyuttarottaram  || 7.2,19.18
caruṃ bhakṣyamatho saktukaṇānyāvakameva ca  || 7.2,19.19
568

śākaṃ payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ mūlaṃ phalamathodakam  || 7.2,19.19
abhimaṃtrya ca mantreṇa bhakṣyabhojyādikāni ca  || 7.2,19.20
sādhane 'sminviśeṣeṇa nityaṃ bhuñjīta vāgyataḥ  || 7.2,19.20
mantrāṣṭaśatapūtena jalena śucinā vratī  || 7.2,19.21
snāyānnadīnadotthena prokṣayedvātha śaktitaḥ  || 7.2,19.21
tarpayecca tathā nityaṃ juhuyācca śivānale  || 7.2,19.22
saptabhiḥ pañcabhirdravyaistribhirvātha ghṛtena vā  || 7.2,19.22
itthaṃ bhaktyā śivaṃ śaivo yaḥ sādhayati sādhakaḥ  || 7.2,19.23
tasyehāmutra duṣprāpaṃ na kiṃcidapi vidyate  || 7.2,19.23
athavā 'haraharmaṃtraṃ japedekāgramānasaḥ  || 7.2,19.24
anaśnanneva sāhasraṃ vinā mantrasya sādhanam  || 7.2,19.24
na tasya durlabhaṃ kiṃcinna tasyāstyaśubhaṃ kvacit || 7.2,19.25
iha vidyāṃ śriyaṃ saukhyaṃ labdhvā muktiṃ ca viṃdati  || 7.2,19.25
sādhane viniyoge ca nitye naimittike tathā  || 7.2,19.26
japejjalairbhasmanā ca snātvā mantreṇa ca kramāt || 7.2,19.26
śucirbaddhaśikhassūtrī sapavitrakarastathā  || 7.2,19.27
dhṛtatripuṃḍrarudrākṣo vidyāṃ pañcākṣarīṃ japet || 7.2,19.27

oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṃḍe sādhakasaṃskāramantramāhātmyaṃ nāmaikonaviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 20
upamanyuruvāca
athaivaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ śiṣyaṃ kṛtapāśupatavratam  || 7.2,20.1
ācāryatve 'bhiṣiṃceta tadyogatvena cānyathā  || 7.2,20.1
maṇḍalaṃ pūrvavatkṛttvā saṃpūjya parameśvaram  || 7.2,20.2
sthāpayatpañcakalaśāndikṣu madhye ca pūrvavat || 7.2,20.2
nivṛttiṃ purato nyasya pratiṣṭhāṃ paścime ghaṭe  || 7.2,20.3
vidyāṃ dakṣiṇataḥ śāṃtimuttare madhyataḥ parām  || 7.2,20.3
kṛtvā rakṣādikaṃ tatra baddhvā mudrāṃ ca dhainavīm  || 7.2,20.4
abhimaṃtrya ghaṭānhutvā pūrṇāṃtaṃ ca yathā purā  || 7.2,20.4
praveśya maṃḍale śiṣyamanuṣṇīṣaṃ ca deśikaḥ  || 7.2,20.5
tarpaṇādyaṃ tu maṃtrāṇāṃ kuryātpūrvāvasānakam  || 7.2,20.5
tataḥ saṃpūjya deveśamanujñāpya ca pūrvavat || 7.2,20.6
abhiṣekāya taṃ śiṣyamāsanaṃ tvadhirohayet || 7.2,20.6
sakalīkṛtya taṃ paścātkalāpañcakarūpiṇam  || 7.2,20.7
nyastamaṃtratanuṃ baddhvā śivaṃ śiṣyaṃ samarpayet || 7.2,20.7
tato nivṛttikuṃbhādighaṭānuddhṛtya vai kramāt || 7.2,20.8
madhyamāntācchivenaiva śiṣyaṃ tamabhiṣecayat || 7.2,20.8
śivahastaṃ samarpyātha śiśoḥ śirasi deśikaḥ  || 7.2,20.9
śivabhāvasamāpannaḥ śivācāryaṃ tamādiśet || 7.2,20.9
athālaṃkṛtya taṃ devamārādhya śivamaṇḍale  || 7.2,20.10
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ hutvā dadyātpūrṇāhutiṃ tataḥ  || 7.2,20.10
punaḥ sampūjya deveśaṃ praṇamya bhuvi daṃḍavat || 7.2,20.11
śirasyaṃjalimādhāya śivaṃ vijñāpayedguruḥ  || 7.2,20.11
bhagavaṃstvatprasādena deśiko.yaṃ mayā kṛtaḥ  || 7.2,20.12
anugṛhya tvayā deva divyājñāsmai pradīyatām  || 7.2,20.12
evaṃ vijñāpya śiṣyeṇa saha bhūyaḥ praṇamya ca  || 7.2,20.13
568

śivaṃ śivāgamaṃ divyaṃ pūjayecchivavadguruḥ  || 7.2,20.13
punaḥ śivamanujñāpya śivajñānasya pustakam  || 7.2,20.14
ubhābhyāmatha pāṇibhyāṃ dadyācchiṣyāya deśikaḥ  || 7.2,20.14
sa tāmmūrdhni samādhāya vidyāṃ vidyāsanopari  || 7.2,20.15
adhiropya yathānyāyamabhivaṃdya samarcayet || 7.2,20.15
atha tasmai gururdadyādrājopakaraṇānyapi  || 7.2,20.16
ācāryapadavīṃ prāpto rājyaṃ cāpi yato 'rhati  || 7.2,20.16
athānuśāsanaṃ kuryātpūrvairācaritaṃ yathā  || 7.2,20.17
yathā ca śivaśāstroktaṃ yathā lokeṣu pūjyate  || 7.2,20.17
śiṣyānparikṣya yatnena śivaśāstroktalakṣaṇaiḥ  || 7.2,20.18
saṃskṛtya ca śivajñānaṃ tebhyo dadyācca deśikaḥ  || 7.2,20.18
evaṃ sarvamanāyāsaṃ śaucaṃ kṣāṃtiṃ dayāṃ tathā  || 7.2,20.19
aspṛhāmapyasūyāṃ ca yatnena ca vibhāvayet || 7.2,20.19
itthamādiśya taṃ śiṣyaṃ śivamudvāsya maṃḍalāt || 7.2,20.20
śivakuṃbhānalādīṃśca sadasyānapi pūjayet || 7.2,20.20
yugapadvātha saṃskārānkurvīta sagaṇo guruḥ  || 7.2,20.21
tatra yatra dvayaṃ vāpi prayogasyopadiśyate  || 7.2,20.21
tadādāveva kalaśānkalpayedadhvaśuddhivat || 7.2,20.22
kṛtvā samayasaṃskāramabhiṣekaṃ vinākhilam  || 7.2,20.22
samabhyarcya śivaṃ bhūyaḥ kṛtvā cādhvaviśodhanam  || 7.2,20.23
tasminparisamāpte tu punardevaṃ prapūjayet || 7.2,20.23
hutvā maṃtrantu saṃtarpya saṃdīpyāśāsya ceśvaram  || 7.2,20.24
samarpya maṃtraṃ śiṣyasya pāṇau śeṣaṃ samāpayet || 7.2,20.24
athavā maṃtrasaṃskāramanuciṃtyākhilaṃ kramāt || 7.2,20.25
adhvaśuddhiṃ guruḥ kuryādabhiṣekāvasānikam  || 7.2,20.25
tatra yaḥ śāntyatītādikalāsu vihito vidhiḥ  || 7.2,20.26
sa sarvo 'pi vidhātavyastattvatrayaviśodhane  || 7.2,20.26
śivavidyātmatattvākhyaṃ tattvatrayamudāhṛtam  || 7.2,20.27
śaktau śivastato vidyāttasyāstvātmā samudbabhau  || 7.2,20.27
śivena śāṃtyatītādhvā vyāptastadaparaḥ paraḥ  || 7.2,20.28
vidyayā pariśiṣṭo 'dhvā hyātmanā nikhilaḥ kramāt || 7.2,20.28
durlabhaṃ śāṃbhavaṃ matvā maṃtramūlaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ  || 7.2,20.29
śāktaṃ śaṃsīta saṃskāraṃ śivaśāstrārthapāragāḥ  || 7.2,20.29
iti te sarvamākhyātaṃ saṃskārākhyasya karmaṇaḥ  || 7.2,20.30
cāturvidhyamidaṃ kṛṣṇa kiṃ bhūya śrotumicchasi  || 7.2,20.30
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe viśeṣādisaṃskṛtirnāma viṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 21
kṛṣṇa uvāca
bhagavañchrotumicchāmi śivāśramaniṣeviṇām  || 7.2,21.1
śivaśāstroditaṃ karma nityanaimittikaṃ tathā  || 7.2,21.1
upamanyuruvāca
prātarutthāya śayanāddhyātvā devaṃ sahāmbayā  || 7.2,21.2
vicārya kāryaṃ nirgacchedgṛhādabhyudite 'ruṇe  || 7.2,21.2
abādhe vijane deśe kuryādāvaśyakaṃ tataḥ  || 7.2,21.3
569

kṛtvā śaucaṃ vidhānena daṃtadhāvanamācaret || 7.2,21.3
alābhe daṃtakāṣṭhānāmaṣṭamyādidineṣu ca  || 7.2,21.4
apāṃ dvādaśagaṇḍūṣaiḥ kuryādāsyaviśodhanam  || 7.2,21.4
ācamya vidhivatpaścādvāruṇaṃ snānamācaret || 7.2,21.5
nadyāṃ vā devakhāte vā hrade vātha gṛhe 'pi vā  || 7.2,21.5
snānadravyāṇi tattīre sthāpayitvā bahirmalam  || 7.2,21.6
vyāpohya mṛdamālipya snātvā gomayamālipet || 7.2,21.6
snātvā punaḥ punarvastraṃ tyaktvāvātha viśodhya ca  || 7.2,21.7
susnāto nṛpavadbhūyaḥ śuddhaṃ vāso vasīta ca  || 7.2,21.7
malasnānaṃ sugaṃdhādyaiḥ snānaṃ dantaviśodhanam  || 7.2,21.8
na kuryādbrahmacārī ca tapasvī vidhavā tathā  || 7.2,21.8
sopavītaśśikhāṃ baddhā praviśya ca jalāṃtaram  || 7.2,21.9
avagāhya samācāṃto jale nyasyettrimaṃḍalam  || 7.2,21.9
saumye magnaḥ punarmaṃtraṃ japecchaktyā śivaṃ smaret || 7.2,21.10
utthāyācamya tenaiva svātmānamabhiṣecayet || 7.2,21.10
gośṛṃgeṇa sadarbheṇa pālāśena dalena vā  || 7.2,21.11
pādmena vātha pāṇibhyāṃ pañcakṛtvastrireva vā  || 7.2,21.11
udyānādau gṛhe caiva vardhanyā kalaśena vā  || 7.2,21.12
avagāhanakāle 'dbhirmaṃtritairabhiṣecayet || 7.2,21.12
atha cedvāruṇaṃ kartumaśaktaḥ śuddhavāsasā  || 7.2,21.13
ārdreṇa śodhayeddehamāpādatalamastakam  || 7.2,21.13
āgneyaṃ vātha vā māṃtraṃ kuryātsnānaṃ śivena vā  || 7.2,21.14
śivaciṃtāparaṃ snānaṃ yuktasyātmīyamucyate  || 7.2,21.14
svasūtroktavidhānena maṃtrācamanapūrvakam  || 7.2,21.15
ācaredbrahmayajñāṃtaṃ kṛtvā devāditarpaṇam  || 7.2,21.15
maṃḍalasthaṃ mahādevaṃ dhyātvābhyarcya yathāvidhi  || 7.2,21.16
dadyādarghyaṃ tatastasmai śivāyādityarūpiṇe  || 7.2,21.16
atha vaitatsvasūtroktaṃ kṛtvā hastau viśodhayet || 7.2,21.17
karanyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā sakalīkṛtavigrahaḥ  || 7.2,21.17
vāmahastagatāṃbhobhirgaṃdhasiddhārthakānvitaiḥ  || 7.2,21.18
kuśapuṃjena vābhyukṣya mūlamaṃtrasamanvitaiḥ  || 7.2,21.18
āpohiṣṭhādibhirmantraiḥ śeṣamāghrāya vai jalam  || 7.2,21.19
vāmanāsāpuṭenaiva devaṃ saṃbhāvayetsitam  || 7.2,21.19
arghamādāya dehasthaṃ savyanāsāpuṭena ca  || 7.2,21.20
kṛṣṇavarṇena bāhyasthaṃ bhāvayecca śilāgatam  || 7.2,21.20
tarpayedatha devebhya ṛṣibhiśca viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,21.21
bhūtebhyaśca pitṛbhyaśca dadyādarghyaṃ yathāvidhi  || 7.2,21.21
raktacaṃdanatoyena hastamātreṇa maṃḍalam  || 7.2,21.22
suvṛttaṃ kalpayedbhūmau raktacūrṇādyalaṃkṛtam  || 7.2,21.22
tatra saṃpūjayedbhānuṃ svakīyāvaraṇaiḥ saha  || 7.2,21.23
svakholkāyeti maṃtreṇa sāṃgatassukhasiddhaye  || 7.2,21.23
punaśca maṃḍalaṃ kṛtvā tadaṃgaiḥ paripūjya ca  || 7.2,21.24
tatra sthāpya hemapātraṃ māgadhaprasthasaṃmitam  || 7.2,21.24
569

pūrayedgaṃdhatoyena raktacaṃdanayoginā  || 7.2,21.25
raktapuṣpaistilaiścaiva kuśākṣatasamanvitaiḥ  || 7.2,21.25
dūrvāpāmārgagavyaiśca kevalena jalena vā  || 7.2,21.26
jānubhyāṃ dharaṇīṃ gatvā natvā devaṃ ca maṃḍale  || 7.2,21.26
kṛtvā śirasi tatpātraṃ dadyādarghyaṃ śivāya tat || 7.2,21.27
athavāṃjalinā toyaṃ sadarbhaṃ mūlavidyayā  || 7.2,21.27
utkṣipedambarasthāya śivāyādityamūrtaye  || 7.2,21.28
kṛtvā punaḥ karanyāsaṃ karaśodhanapūrvakam  || 7.2,21.28
buddhveśānādisadyāṃtaṃ pañcabrahmamayaṃ śivam  || 7.2,21.29
gṛhītvā bhasitaṃ mantrairvimṛjyāṅgāni saṃspṛśet || 7.2,21.29
yā dināṃtaiśśirovaktrahṛdguhyacaraṇānkramāt || 7.2,21.30
tato mūlena sarvāṃgamālabhya vasanāntaram  || 7.2,21.30
paridhāya dvirācamya prokṣyaikādaśamantritaiḥ  || 7.2,21.31
jalairācchādya vāso 'yaddvirācamya śivaṃ smaret || 7.2,21.31
punarnyastakaro mantrī tripuṃḍraṃ bhasmanā likhet || 7.2,21.32
avakramāya taṃ vyaktaṃ lalāṭe gandhavāriṇā  || 7.2,21.32
vṛttaṃ vā caturasraṃ vā bindumardhendumeva vā  || 7.2,21.33
lalāṭe yādṛśaṃ puṇḍraṃ likhitaṃ bhasmanā punaḥ  || 7.2,21.33
tādṛśaṃ bhujayormūrdhni stanayoraṃtare likhet || 7.2,21.34
sarvāṃgoddhūlanaṃ caiva na samānaṃ tripuṇḍrakaiḥ  || 7.2,21.34
tasmāttripuṇḍramevaikaṃ likheduddhūlanaṃ vinā  || 7.2,21.35
rudrākṣāndhārayedmūrdhni kaṃṭhe śrote kare tathā  || 7.2,21.35
suvarṇavarṇamacchinnaṃ śubhaṃ nānyairdhṛtaṃ śubham  || 7.2,21.36
viprādīnāṃ kramācchreṣṭhaṃ pītaṃ raktamathāsitam  || 7.2,21.36
tadalābhe yathālābhaṃ dhāraṇīyamadūṣitam  || 7.2,21.37
tatrāpi nottaraṃ nīcairdhāryaṃ nīcamathottaraiḥ  || 7.2,21.37
nāśucirdhārayedakṣaṃ sadā kāleṣu dhārayet || 7.2,21.38
itthaṃ trisaṃdhyamathavā dvisaṃdhyaṃ sakṛdeva vā  || 7.2,21.38
kṛtvā snānādikaṃ śaktyā pūjayetparameśvaram  || 7.2,21.39
prajāsthānaṃ samāsādya baddhvā ruciramāsanam  || 7.2,21.39
dhyāyeddevaṃ ca devīṃ ca prāṅmukho vāpyudaṅmukhaḥ  || 7.2,21.40
śvetādīnnakulīśāṃtāṃstacchiṣyānpraṇamedgurum  || 7.2,21.40
punardevaṃ śivaṃ natvā tato nāmāṣṭakaṃ japet || 7.2,21.41
śivo maheśvaraścaiva rudro viṣṇuḥ pitāmahaḥ  || 7.2,21.41
saṃsāravaidyassarvajñaḥ paramātmeti cāṣṭakam  || 7.2,21.42
athavā śivamevaikaṃ japitvaikādaśādhikam  || 7.2,21.42
jihvāgre tejaso rāśiṃ dhyātvāvyādhyādiśāṃtaye  || 7.2,21.43
prakṣālya caraṇau kṛtvā karau caṃdanacarcitau  || 7.2,21.43
prakurvīta karanyāsaṃ karaśodhanapūrvakam  || 7.2,21.43
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe nityanaimittikakarmavarṇanaṃ nāmaikaviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 22
upamanyuruvāca
nyāsastu trividhaḥ proktaḥ sthityutpattilayakramāt || 7.2,22.1
sthitirnyāso gṛhasthānāmutpattirbrahmacāriṇām  || 7.2,22.1
570

yatīnāṃ saṃhṛtinyāso vanasthānāṃ tathaiva ca  || 7.2,22.2
sa eva bhartṛhīnāyāḥ kuṭuṃbinyāḥ sthitirbhavet || 7.2,22.2
kanyāyāḥ punarutpattiṃ vakṣye nyāsasya lakṣaṇam  || 7.2,22.3
aṃguṣṭhādikaniṣṭhāṃtaṃ sthitinyāsa udāhṛtaḥ  || 7.2,22.3
dakṣiṇāṃguṣṭhamārabhya vāmāṃguṣṭhāntameva ca  || 7.2,22.4
utpattinyāsa ākhyāto viparītastu saṃhṛtiḥ  || 7.2,22.4
sabiṃdukānnakārādīnvarṇānnyasyedanukramāt || 7.2,22.5
aṃgulīṣu śivaṃ nyasyettalayorapyanāmayoḥ  || 7.2,22.5
astranyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā daśadikṣvastramaṃtrataḥ  || 7.2,22.6
nivṛttyādikalāḥ pañca pañcabhūtasvarūpiṇīḥ  || 7.2,22.6
pañcabhūtādhipaissārdhaṃ tataccihnasamanvitāḥ  || 7.2,22.7
hṛtkaṇṭatālubhrūmadhyabrahmarandhrasamāśrayāḥ  || 7.2,22.7
tadtadbījena saṃgraṃthīstadtadbījeṣu bhāvayet || 7.2,22.8
tāsāṃ viśodhanārthāya vidyāṃ pañcākṣarīṃ japet || 7.2,22.8
niruddhvā prāṇavāyuṃ ca guṇasaṃkhyānusārataḥ  || 7.2,22.9
bhūtagraṃthiṃ tataśchidyādastreṇaivāstramudrayā  || 7.2,22.9
nāḍyā suṣumnayātmānaṃ preritaṃ prāṇavāyunā  || 7.2,22.10
nirgataṃ brahmarandhreṇa yojayecchivatejasā  || 7.2,22.10
viśoṣya vāyunā paścāddehaṃ kālāgninā dahet || 7.2,22.11
tataścoparibhāvena kalāssaṃhṛtya vāyunā  || 7.2,22.11
dehaṃ saṃhṛtya vai dagdhaṃ kalāsspṛṣṭvā sahābdhinā  || 7.2,22.12
plāvayitvāmṛtairdehaṃ yathāsthānaṃ niveśayet || 7.2,22.12
atha saṃhṛtya vai dagdhaḥ kalāsargaṃ vinaiva tu  || 7.2,22.13
amṛtaplāvanaṃ kuryādbhasmībhūtasya vai tataḥ  || 7.2,22.13
tato vidyāmaye tasmindehe dīpaśikhākṛtim  || 7.2,22.14
śivānnirgatamātmānaṃ brahmaraṃdhreṇa yojayet || 7.2,22.14
dehasyāntaḥ praviṣṭaṃ taṃ dhyātvā hṛdayapaṃkaje  || 7.2,22.15
punaścāmṛtavarṣeṇa siṃcedvidyāmayaṃ vapuḥ  || 7.2,22.15
tataḥ kuryātkaranyāsaṃ karaśodhanapūrvakam  || 7.2,22.16
dehanyāsaṃ tataḥ paścānmahatyā mudrayā caret || 7.2,22.16
aṃganyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā śivoktena tu vartmanā  || 7.2,22.17
varṇanyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryāddhastapādādisaṃdhiṣu  || 7.2,22.17
ṣaḍaṃgāni tato nyasya jātiṣaṭkayutāni ca  || 7.2,22.18
digbaṃdhamācaretpaścādāgneyādi yathākramam  || 7.2,22.18
yadvā mūrdhādipañcāṃgaṃ nyāsameva samācaret || 7.2,22.19
tathā ṣaḍaṃganyāsaṃ ca bhūtaśuddhyādikaṃ vinā  || 7.2,22.19
evaṃ samāsarūpeṇa kṛtvā dehātmaśodhanam  || 7.2,22.20
śivabhāvamupāgamya pūjayetparameśvaram  || 7.2,22.20
atha yasyāstyavasaro nāsti vā mativibhramaḥ  || 7.2,22.21
sa vistīrṇena kalpena nyāsakarma samācaret || 7.2,22.21
tatrādyo mātṛkānyāso brahmanyāsastataḥ paraḥ  || 7.2,22.22
tṛtīyaḥ praṇavanyāso haṃsanyāsastaduttaraḥ  || 7.2,22.22
pañcamaḥ kathyate sadbhirnyāsaḥ pañcākṣarātmakaḥ  || 7.2,22.23
570

eteṣvekamanekaṃ vā kuryātpūjādi karmasu  || 7.2,22.23
akāraṃ mūrdhni vinyasya ākāraṃ ca lalāṭake  || 7.2,22.24
iṃ īṃ ca netrayostadvatuṃ ūṃ śravaṇayostathā  || 7.2,22.24
ṛṃ ḥṃ kapolayoścaiva ḻaṃ ḹṃ nāsāpuṭadvaye  || 7.2,22.25
ememoṣṭhadvayoromauṃ daṃtapaṃktidvayoḥ kramāt || 7.2,22.25
aṃ jihvāyāmatho tālunyaḥ prayojyo yathākramam  || 7.2,22.26
kavargaṃ dakṣiṇe haste nyasetpañcasu saṃdhiṣu  || 7.2,22.26
cavargaṃ ca tathā vāmahastasaṃdhiṣu vinyaset || 7.2,22.27
ṭavargaṃ ca tavargaṃ ca pādayorubhayorapi  || 7.2,22.27
paphau tu pārśvayoḥ pṛṣṭhe nābhau cāpi babhau tataḥ  || 7.2,22.28
nyasenmakāraṃ hṛdaye tvagādiṣu yathākramam  || 7.2,22.28
yakarādisakārāṃtānnyasetsaptasu dhātuṣu  || 7.2,22.29
haṃkāraṃ hṛdayasyāṃtaḥ kṣakāraṃ bhrūyugāṃtare  || 7.2,22.29
evaṃ varṇānpravinyasya pañcāśadrudravartmanā  || 7.2,22.30
aṃgavaktrakalābhedātpañca brahmāṇi vinyaset || 7.2,22.30
karanyāsādyamapi taiḥ kṛtvā vātha na vā kramāt || 7.2,22.31
śirovadanahṛdguhyapādeṣvetāni kalpayet || 7.2,22.31
tataścordhvādivaktrāṇi paścimāṃtāni kalpayet || 7.2,22.32
īśānasya kalāḥ pañca pañcasveteṣu ca kramāt || 7.2,22.32
tataścaturṣu vaktreṣu puruṣasya kalā api  || 7.2,22.33
catasraḥ praṇidhātavyāḥ pūrvādikramayogataḥ  || 7.2,22.33
hṛtkaṃṭhāṃseṣu nābhau ca kukṣau pṛṣṭhe ca vakṣasi  || 7.2,22.34
aghorasya kalāścāṣṭau pādayorapi hastayoḥ  || 7.2,22.34
paścāttrayoḥdaśakalāḥ pāyumeḍhrorujānuṣu  || 7.2,22.35
jaṃghāsphikkaṭipārśveṣu vāmadevasya bhāvayet || 7.2,22.35
ghrāṇe śirasi bāhvośca kalpayetkalpavittamaḥ  || 7.2,22.36
aṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsamevaṃ kṛtvānupūrvaśaḥ  || 7.2,22.36
paścātpraṇavaviddhīmānpraṇavanyāsamācaret || 7.2,22.37
bāhudvaye kūrparayostathā ca maṇibandhayoḥ  || 7.2,22.37
pārśvodarorujaṃgheṣu pādayoḥ pṛṣṭhatastathā  || 7.2,22.38
itthaṃ praṇavavinyāsaṃ kṛtvā nyāsavicakṣaṇaḥ  || 7.2,22.38
haṃsanyāsaṃ prakurvīta śivaśāstre yathoditam  || 7.2,22.39
bījaṃ vibhajya haṃsasya netrayorghrāṇayorapi  || 7.2,22.39
vibhajya bāhunetrāsyalalāṭe ghrāṇayorapi  || 7.2,22.40
kakṣayoḥ skandhayoścaiva pārśvayostanayostathā  || 7.2,22.40
kaṭhyoḥ pāṇyorgulphayośca yadvā pañcāṃgavartmanā  || 7.2,22.41
haṃsanyāsamimaṃ kṛtvā nyasetpañcākṣarīṃ tataḥ  || 7.2,22.41
yathā pūrvoktamārgeṇa śivatvaṃ yena jāyate  || 7.2,22.42
nāśivaḥ śivamabhyasyennāśivaḥ śivamarcayet || 7.2,22.42
nāśivastu śivaṃ dhyāyennāśivamprāpnuyācchivam  || 7.2,22.43
tasmācchaivīṃ tanuṃ kṛtvā tyaktvā ca paśubhāvanām  || 7.2,22.43
śivo 'hamiti saṃcintya śaivaṃ karma samācaret || 7.2,22.44
karmayajñastapoyajño japayajñastaduttaraḥ  || 7.2,22.44
dhyānayajño jñānayajñaḥ pañca yajñāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  || 7.2,22.44
571

karmayajñaratāḥ kecittapoyajñaratāḥ pare  || 7.2,22.45
japayajñaratāścānye dhyānayajñaratāstathā  || 7.2,22.45
jñānayajñaratāścānye viśiṣṭāścottarottaram  || 7.2,22.46
kramayajño dvidhā proktaḥ kāmākāmavibhedataḥ  || 7.2,22.46
kāmānkāmī tato bhuktvā kāmāsaktaḥ punarbhavet || 7.2,22.47
akāme rudrabhavane bhogānbhuktvā tataścyutaḥ  || 7.2,22.47
tapoyajñarato bhūtvā jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,22.48
tapasvī ca punastasminbhogān bhuktvā tataścyutaḥ  || 7.2,22.48
japadhyānarato bhūtvā jāyate bhuvi mānavaḥ  || 7.2,22.49
japadhyānarato martyastadvaiśiṣṭyavaśādiha  || 7.2,22.49
jñānaṃ labdhvācirādeva śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt || 7.2,22.50
tasmānmukto śivājñaptaḥ karmayajño 'pi dehinām  || 7.2,22.50
akāmaḥ kāmasaṃyukto bandhāyaiva bhaviṣyati  || 7.2,22.51
tasmātpañcasu yajñeṣu dhyānajñānaparo bhavet || 7.2,22.51
dhyānaṃ jñānaṃ ca yasyāsti tīrṇastena bhavārṇavaḥ  || 7.2,22.52
hiṃsādidoṣanirmukto viśuddhaścittasādhanaḥ  || 7.2,22.52
dhyānayajñaḥ parastasmādapavargaphalapradaḥ  || 7.2,22.53
bahiḥ karmakarā yadvannātīva phalabhāginaḥ  || 7.2,22.53
dṛṣṭvā narendrabhavane tadvadatrāpi karmiṇaḥ  || 7.2,22.54
dhyānināṃ hi vapuḥ sūkṣmaṃ bhavetpratyakṣamaiśvaram  || 7.2,22.54
yatheha karmaṇāṃ sthūlaṃ mṛtkāṣṭhādyaiḥ prakalpitam  || 7.2,22.55
dhyānayajñaratāstasmāddevānpāṣāṇamṛṇmayān  || 7.2,22.55
nātyaṃtaṃ pratipadyaṃte śivayāthātmyavedanāt || 7.2,22.56
ātmasthaṃ yaḥ śivaṃ tyaktvā bahirabhyarcayennaraḥ  || 7.2,22.56
hastasthaṃ phalamutsṛjya lihetkūrparamātmanaḥ  || 7.2,22.57
jñānāddhyānaṃ bhaveddhyānājjñānaṃ bhūyaḥ pravartate  || 7.2,22.57
tadubhābhyāṃ bhavenmuktistasmāddhyānarato bhavet || 7.2,22.58
dvādaśānte tathā mūrdhni lalāṭe bhrūyugāntare  || 7.2,22.58
nāsāgre vā tathāsye vā kandhare hṛdaye tathā  || 7.2,22.59
nābhau vā śāśvatasthāne śraddhāviddhena cetasā  || 7.2,22.59
bahiryāgopacāreṇa devaṃ devīṃ ca pūjayet || 7.2,22.60
athavā pūjayennityaṃ liṃge vā kṛtakepi vā  || 7.2,22.60
vahnau vā sthaṇḍile vātha bhaktyā vittānusārataḥ  || 7.2,22.61
athavāṃtarbahiścaiva pūjayetparameśvaram  || 7.2,22.61
aṃtaryāgarataḥ pūjāṃ bahiḥ kurvīta vā na vā  || 7.2,22.61
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe śivaśāstroktanityanaimittikakarmavarṇanaṃ nāma dvāviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 23
upamanyuruvāca
vyākhyāṃ pūjāvidhānasya pravadāmi samāsataḥ  || 7.2,23.1
śivaśāstre śivenaiva śivāyai kathitasya tu  || 7.2,23.1
aṃgamabhyaṃtaraṃ yāgamagnikāryāvasānakam  || 7.2,23.2
vidhāya vā na vā paścādbahiryāgaṃ samācaret || 7.2,23.2
tatra dravyāṇi manasā kalpayitvā viśodhya ca  || 7.2,23.3
571

dhyātvā vināyakaṃ devaṃ pūjayitvā vidhānataḥ  || 7.2,23.3
dakṣiṇe cottare caiva naṃdīśaṃ suyaśaṃ tathā  || 7.2,23.4
ārādhya manasā samyagāsanaṃ kalpayedbudhaḥ  || 7.2,23.4
ārādhanādikairyuktassiṃhayogāsanādikam  || 7.2,23.5
padmāsanaṃ vā vimalaṃ tattvatrayasamanvitam  || 7.2,23.5
tasyopari śivaṃ dhyāyetsāṃbaṃ sarvamanoharam  || 7.2,23.6
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpannaṃ sarvāvayavaśobhanam  || 7.2,23.6
sarvātiśayasaṃyuktaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam  || 7.2,23.7
raktāsyapāṇicaraṇaṃ kuṃdacaṃdrasmitānanam  || 7.2,23.7
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ phullapadmatrilocanam  || 7.2,23.8
caturbhujamudārāṅgaṃ cārucaṃdrakalādharam  || 7.2,23.8
varadābhayahastaṃ ca mṛgaṭaṃkadharaṃ haram  || 7.2,23.9
bhujaṃgahāravalayaṃ cārunīlagalāṃtaram  || 7.2,23.9
sarvopamānarahitaṃ sānugaṃ saparicchadam  || 7.2,23.10
tataḥ saṃciṃtayettasya vāmabhāge maheśvarīm  || 7.2,23.10
praphullotpalapatrābhāṃ vistīrṇāyatalocanām  || 7.2,23.11
pūrṇacaṃdrābhavadanāṃ nīlakuṃcitamūrdhajām  || 7.2,23.11
nīlotpaladalaprakhyāṃ candrārdhakṛtaśekharām  || 7.2,23.12
ativṛttaghanottuṃgasnigdhapīnapayodharām  || 7.2,23.12
tanumadhyāṃ pṛthuśroṇīṃ pītasūkṣmavarāmbarām  || 7.2,23.13
sarvābharaṇasaṃpannāṃ lalāṭatilakojjvalām  || 7.2,23.13
vicitrapuṣpasaṃkīrṇakeśapāśopaśobhitām  || 7.2,23.14
sarvato 'nuguṇākārāṃ kiṃcillajjānatānanām  || 7.2,23.14
hemāraviṃdaṃ vilasaddadhānāṃ dakṣiṇe kare  || 7.2,23.15
daṃḍavaccāparaṃ haste nyasyāsīnāṃ mahāsane  || 7.2,23.15
pāśavicchedikāṃ sākṣātsaccidānaṃdarūpiṇīm  || 7.2,23.16
evaṃ devaṃ ca devīṃ ca dhyātvāsanavare śubhe  || 7.2,23.16
sarvopacāravadbhaktyā bhāvapuṣpaissamarcayet || 7.2,23.17
athavā parikalpyaivaṃ mūrtimanyatamāṃ vibhoḥ  || 7.2,23.17
śaivīṃ sadāśivākhyāṃ vā tathā māheśvarīṃ parām  || 7.2,23.18
ṣaḍviṃśakābhidhānāṃ vā śrīkaṃṭhākhyāmathāpi vā  || 7.2,23.18
mantranyāsādikāṃ cāpi kṛtvā svasyāṃ tanau yathā  || 7.2,23.19
asyāṃ mūrtau mūrtimaṃtaṃ śivaṃ sadasataḥ param  || 7.2,23.19
dhyātvā bāhyakrameṇaiva pūjāṃ nirvartayeddhiyā  || 7.2,23.20
samidājyādibhiḥ paścānnābhau homaṃ ca bhāvayet || 7.2,23.20
bhrūmadhye ca śivaṃ dhyāyecchuddhadīpaśikhākṛtim  || 7.2,23.21
itthamaṃge svataṃtre vā yoge dhyānamaye śubhe  || 7.2,23.21
agnikāryāvasānaṃ ca sarvatraiva samo vidhiḥ  || 7.2,23.22
atha ciṃtāmayaṃ sarvaṃ samāpyārādhanakramam  || 7.2,23.22
liṃge ca pūjayeddevaṃ sthaṃḍile vānale 'pi vā  || 7.2,23.23
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe pūjāvidhānavyākhyānavarṇanaṃ nāma trayoviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ
572


adhyāya 24
upamanyuruvāca
prokṣayenmūlamaṃtreṇa pūjāsthānaṃ viśuddhaye  || 7.2,24.1
gandhacandanatoyena puṣpaṃ tatra vinikṣipet || 7.2,24.1
astreṇotsārya vai vighnānavaguṇṭhya ca varmaṇā  || 7.2,24.2
astraṃ dikṣu pravinyasya kalpayedarcanābhuvam  || 7.2,24.2
tatra darbhānparistīrya kṣālayetprokṣaṇādibhiḥ  || 7.2,24.3
saṃśodhya sarvapātrāṇi dravyaśuddhiṃ samācaret || 7.2,24.3
prokṣaṇīmardhyapātraṃ ca pādyapātramataḥ param  || 7.2,24.4
tathaivācamanīyasya pātraṃ ceti catuṣṭayam  || 7.2,24.4
prakṣālya prokṣya vīkṣyātha kṣipetteṣu jalaṃ śivam  || 7.2,24.5
puṇyadravyāṇi sarvāṇi yathālābhaṃ vinikṣipet || 7.2,24.5
ratnāni rajataṃ hema gandhapuṣpākṣatādayaḥ  || 7.2,24.6
phalapallavadarbhāṃśca puṇyadravyāṇyanekadhā  || 7.2,24.6
snānodake sugandhādi pānīye ca viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,24.7
śītalāni manojñānī kusumādīni nikṣipet || 7.2,24.7
uśīraṃ candanaṃ caiva pādye tu parikalpayet || 7.2,24.8
jātikaṃkolakarpūrabahumūlatamālakān  || 7.2,24.8
kṣipedācamanīye ca cūrṇayitvā viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,24.9
elāṃ pātreṣu sarveṣu karpūraṃ candanaṃ tathā  || 7.2,24.9
kuśāgrāṇyakṣatāṃścaiva yavavrīhitilānapi  || 7.2,24.10
ājyasiddhārthapuṣpāṇi bhasitañcārghyapātrake  || 7.2,24.10
kuśapuṣpayavavrīhibahumūlatamālakān  || 7.2,24.11
prakṣipetprokṣaṇīpātre bhasitaṃ ca yathākramam  || 7.2,24.11
sarvatra mantraṃ vinyasya varmaṇāveṣṭya bāhyataḥ  || 7.2,24.12
paścādastreṇa saṃrakṣya dhenumudrāṃ pradarśayet || 7.2,24.12
pūjādravyāṇi sarvāṇi prokṣaṇīpātravāriṇā  || 7.2,24.13
samprokṣya mūlamaṃtreṇa śodhayedvidhivattataḥ  || 7.2,24.13
pātrāṇāṃ prokṣaṇīmekāmalābhe sarvakarmasu  || 7.2,24.14
sādhayedarghyamadbhistatsāmānyaṃ sādhakottamaḥ  || 7.2,24.14
tato vināyakaṃ devaṃ bhakṣyabhojyādibhiḥ kramāt || 7.2,24.15
pūjayitvā vidhānena dvārapārśve 'tha dakṣiṇe  || 7.2,24.15
antaḥpurādhipaṃ sākṣānnandinaṃ samyagarcayet || 7.2,24.16
cāmīkarācalaprakhyaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam  || 7.2,24.16
bālendumukuṭaṃ saumyaṃ trinetraṃ ca caturbhujam  || 7.2,24.17
dīptaśūlamṛgīṭaṃkatigmavetradharaṃ prabhum  || 7.2,24.17
candrabimbābhavadanaṃ harivaktramathāpi vā  || 7.2,24.18
uttare dvārapārśvasya bhāryāṃ ca marutāṃ sutām  || 7.2,24.18
suyaśāṃ suvratāmambāṃ pādamaṇḍanatatparām  || 7.2,24.19
pūjayitvā praviśyāntarbhavanaṃ parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 7.2,24.19
saṃpūjya liṅgaṃ tairdravyairnirmālyamapanodayet || 7.2,24.20
prakṣālya puṣpaṃ śirasi nyasettasya viśuddhaye  || 7.2,24.20
puṣpahasto japecchaktyā mantraṃ mantraviśuddhaye  || 7.2,24.21
aiśānyāṃ caṇdamārādhya nirmālyaṃ tasya dāpayet || 7.2,24.21
kalpayedāsanaṃ paścādādhārādi yathākramam  || 7.2,24.22
ādhāraśaktiṃ kalyāṇīṃ śyāmāṃ dhyāyedadho bhuvi  || 7.2,24.22
572

tasyāḥ purastādutkaṃṭhamanaṃtaṃ kuṇḍalākṛtim  || 7.2,24.23
dhavalaṃ pañcaphaṇinaṃ lelihānamivāmbaram  || 7.2,24.23
tasyoparyāsanaṃ bhadraṃ kaṇṭhīravacatuṣpadam  || 7.2,24.24
dharmo jñānaṃ ca vairāgyamaiśvaryañca padāni vai  || 7.2,24.24
āgneyādiśvetaraktapītaśyāmāni varṇataḥ  || 7.2,24.25
adharmādīni pūrvādīnyuttarāṃtānyanukramāt || 7.2,24.25
rājāvartamaṇiprakhyānnyasya gātrāṇi bhāvayet || 7.2,24.26
asyordhvacchādanaṃ padmamāsanaṃ vimalaṃ sitam  || 7.2,24.26
aṣṭapatrāṇi tasyāhuraṇimādiguṇāṣṭakam  || 7.2,24.27
kesarāṇi ca vāmādyā rudrāvāmādiśaktibhiḥ  || 7.2,24.27
bījānyapi ca tā eva śaktayoṃtarmanonmanīḥ  || 7.2,24.28
karṇikāparavairāgyaṃ nālaṃ jñānaṃ śivātmakam  || 7.2,24.28
kandaśca śivadharmātmā karṇikānte trimaṇḍale  || 7.2,24.29
trimaṇḍaloparyātmādi tattvatritayamāsanam  || 7.2,24.29
sarvāsanopari sukhaṃ vicitrāstaraṇāstṛtam  || 7.2,24.30
āsanaṃ kalpayeddivyaṃ śuddhavidyāsamujjvalam  || 7.2,24.30
āvāhanaṃ sthāpanaṃ ca sannirodhaṃ nirīkṣaṇam  || 7.2,24.31
namaskāraṃ ca kurvīta badhvā mudrāḥ pṛthakpṛthak || 7.2,24.31
pādyamācamanaṃ cārghyaṃ gaṃdhaṃ puṣpaṃ tataḥ param  || 7.2,24.32
dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ ca tāṃbūlaṃ dattvātha svāpayecchivau  || 7.2,24.32
athavā parikalpyaivamāsanaṃ mūrtimeva ca  || 7.2,24.33
sakalīkṛtya mūlena brahmābhiścāparaistathā  || 7.2,24.33
āvāhayettato devyā śivaṃ paramakāraṇam  || 7.2,24.34
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ devaṃ niścalamakṣaram  || 7.2,24.34
kāraṇaṃ sarvalokānāṃ sarvalokamayaṃ param  || 7.2,24.35
aṃtarbahiḥsthitaṃ vyāpya hyaṇoraṇu 1 mahattaram 2  || 7.2,24.35
bhaktānāmaprayatnena dṛśyamīśvaramavyayam  || 7.2,24.36
brahmeṃdraviṣṇurudrādyairapi devairagocaram  || 7.2,24.36
devasāraṃ ca vidvadbhiragocaramiti śrutam  || 7.2,24.37
ādimadhyāntarahitaṃ bheṣajaṃ bhavarogiṇām  || 7.2,24.37
śivatattvamiti khyātaṃ śivārthaṃ jagati sthiram  || 7.2,24.38
pañcopacāravadbhaktyā pūjayelliṃgamuttamam  || 7.2,24.38
liṃgamūrtirmaheśasya śivasya paramātmanaḥ  || 7.2,24.39
snānakāle prakurvīta jayaśabdādimaṃgalam  || 7.2,24.39
pañcagavyaghṛtakṣīradadhimadhvādipūrvakaiḥ  || 7.2,24.40
mūlaiḥ phalānāṃ sāraiśca tilasarṣapasaktubhiḥ  || 7.2,24.40
bījairyavādibhiśśastaiścūrṇairmāṣādisaṃbhavaiḥ  || 7.2,24.41
saṃsnāpyālipya piṣṭādyaiḥ snāpayeduṣṇavāribhiḥ  || 7.2,24.41
gharṣayedvilvapatrādyairlepagaṃdhāpanuttaye  || 7.2,24.42
punaḥ saṃsnāpya salilaiścakravartyupacārataḥ  || 7.2,24.42

1 klībatvamārṣam
2 mahata iti kṣeṣa

573

sugaṃdhāmalakaṃ dadyāddharidrāṃ ca yathākramam  || 7.2,24.43
tataḥ saṃśodhya salilairliṃgaṃ beramathāpi vā  || 7.2,24.43
snāpayedgaṃdhatoyena kuśapuṣpodakena ca  || 7.2,24.44
hiraṇyaratnatoyaiśca maṃtrasiddhairyathākramam  || 7.2,24.44
asaṃbhave tu dravyāṇāṃ yathāsaṃbhavasaṃbhṛtaiḥ  || 7.2,24.45
kevalairmaṃtratoyairvā snāpayecchraddhayā śivam  || 7.2,24.45
kalaśenātha śaṃkhena vardhanyā pāṇinā tathā  || 7.2,24.46
sakuśena sapuṣpeṇa snāpayenmaṃtrapūrvakam  || 7.2,24.46
pavamānena rudreṇa nīlena tvaritena ca  || 7.2,24.47
liṃgasūktādisūktaiśca śirasātharvaṇena ca  || 7.2,24.47
ṛgbhiśca sāmabhiḥ śaivairbrahmabhiścāpi pañcabhiḥ  || 7.2,24.48
snāpayeddevadeveśaṃ śivena praṇavena ca  || 7.2,24.48
yathā devasya devyāśca kuryātsnānādikaṃ tathā  || 7.2,24.49
na tu kaścidviśeṣo 'sti tatra tau sadṛśau yataḥ  || 7.2,24.49
prathamaṃ devamuddiśya kṛtvā snānādikāḥ kriyāḥ  || 7.2,24.50
devyaiḥ praścātprakurvīta devadevasya śāsanāt || 7.2,24.50
ardhanārīśvare pūjye paurvāparyaṃ na vidyate  || 7.2,24.51
tatra tatropacārāṇāṃ liṃge vānyatra vā kvacit || 7.2,24.51
kṛtvā 'bhiṣekaṃ liṃgasya śucinā ca sugaṃdhinā  || 7.2,24.52
saṃmṛjya vāsasā dadyādaṃbaraṃ copavītakam  || 7.2,24.52
pādyamācamanaṃ cārghyaṃ gaṃdhaṃ puṣpaṃ ca bhūṣaṇam  || 7.2,24.53
dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ pānīyaṃ mukhaśodhanam  || 7.2,24.53
punaścācamanīyaṃ ca mukhavāsaṃ tataḥ param  || 7.2,24.54
mukuṭaṃ ca śubhaṃ bhadraṃ sarvaratnairalaṃkṛtam  || 7.2,24.54
bhūṣaṇāni pavitrāṇi mālyāni vividhāni ca  || 7.2,24.55
vyajane cāmare chatraṃ tālavṛṃtaṃ ca darpaṇam  || 7.2,24.55
dattvā nīrājanaṃ kuryātsarvamaṃgalanisvanaiḥ  || 7.2,24.56
gītanṛtyādibhiścaiva jayaśabdasamanvitaḥ  || 7.2,24.56
haime ca rājate tāmre pātre vā mṛnmaye śubhe  || 7.2,24.57
padmakaiśśobhitaiḥ puṣpairbījairdadhyakṣatādibhiḥ  || 7.2,24.57
triśūlaśaṃkhayugmābjanandyāvartaiḥ karīṣajaiḥ  || 7.2,24.58
śrīvatsasvastikādarśavajrairvahnyādicihnitaiḥ  || 7.2,24.58
aṣṭau pradīpānparito vidhāyaikaṃ tu madhyame  || 7.2,24.59
teṣu vāmādikāścintyāḥ pūjyāśca nava śaktayaḥ  || 7.2,24.59
kavacena samācchādya saṃrakṣyāstreṇa sarvataḥ  || 7.2,24.60
dhenumudrāṃ ca saṃdarśya pāṇibhyāṃ pātramuddharet || 7.2,24.60
athavāropayetpātre pañcadīpānyathākramam  || 7.2,24.61
vidikṣvapi ca madhye ca dīpamekamathāpi vā  || 7.2,24.61
tatastatpātramuddhṛtya liṃgāderupari kramāt || 7.2,24.62
triḥ pradakṣiṇayogena bhrāmayenmūlavidyayā  || 7.2,24.62
dadyādarghyaṃ tato mūrdhni bhasitaṃ ca sugaṃdhitam  || 7.2,24.63
kṛtvā puṣpāṃjaliṃ paścādupahārānnivedayet || 7.2,24.63
pānīyaṃ ca tato dadyāddattvā vācamanaṃ punaḥ  || 7.2,24.64
573

pañcasaugaṃdhikopetaṃ tāmbūlaṃ ca nivedayet || 7.2,24.64
prokṣayetprokṣaṇīyāni gānanāṭyāni kārayet || 7.2,24.65
liṃgādau śivayościntāṃ kṛtvā śaktyajapecchivam  || 7.2,24.65
pradakṣiṇaṃ praṇāmaṃ ca stavaṃ cātmasamarpaṇam  || 7.2,24.66
vijñāpanaṃ ca kāryāṇāṃ kuryādvinayapūrvakam  || 7.2,24.66
arghyaṃ puṣpāṃjaliṃ dattvā baddhvā mudrāṃ yathāvidhi  || 7.2,24.67
paścātkṣamāpayeddevamudvāsyātmani ciṃtayet || 7.2,24.67
pādyādimukhavāsāṃtamarghyādyaṃ cātisaṃkaṭe  || 7.2,24.68
puṣpavikṣepamātraṃ vā kuryādbhāvapurassaram  || 7.2,24.68
tāvataiva paro dharmo bhāvane sukṛto bhavet || 7.2,24.69
asaṃpūjya na bhuñjīta śivamāprāṇasaṃcarāt || 7.2,24.69
yadi pāpastu bhuṃjīta svairaṃ taysa na niṣkṛtiḥ  || 7.2,24.70
pramādena tu bhuṃkte cettadudgīrya prayatnataḥ  || 7.2,24.70
snātvā dviguṇamabhyarcya devaṃ devīmupoṣya ca  || 7.2,24.71
śivasyāyutamabhyasyedbrahmacaryapurassaram  || 7.2,24.71
paredyuśśaktito dattvā suvarṇādyaṃ śivāya ca  || 7.2,24.72
śivabhaktāya vā kṛtvā mahāpūjāṃ śucirbhavet || 7.2,24.72

oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe śāstroktaśivapūjanavarṇanaṃ nāma caturviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 25
upamanyuruvāca
anuktaṃ cātra pūjāyāḥ kamalopabhayādiva  || 7.2,25.1
yattadanyatpravakṣyāmi samāsānna tu vistarāt || 7.2,25.1
havirnivedanātpūrvaṃ dīpadānādanantaram  || 7.2,25.2
kuryādāvaraṇābhyarcāṃ prāpte nīrājane 'tha vā  || 7.2,25.2
tatreśānādisadyāṃtaṃ rudrādyastrāṃtameva ca  || 7.2,25.3
śivasya vā śivāyāśca prathamāvaraṇe japet || 7.2,25.3
aiśānyāṃ pūrvabhāge ca dakṣiṇe cottare tathā  || 7.2,25.4
paścime ca tathāgneyyāmaiśānyāṃ nairṛte tathā  || 7.2,25.4
vāyavyāṃ punaraiśānyāṃ caturdikṣu tataḥ param  || 7.2,25.5
garbhāvaraṇamākhyātaṃ mantrasaṃghātameva vā  || 7.2,25.5
hṛdayādyastraparyaṃtamathavāpi samarcayet || 7.2,25.6
tadbahiḥ pūrvataḥ śakraṃ yamaṃ dakṣiṇato yajet || 7.2,25.6
varuṇaṃ vāruṇe bhāge dhanadaṃ cottare budhaḥ  || 7.2,25.7
īśamaiśe 'nalaṃ svīye nairṛte nirṛtiṃ yajet || 7.2,25.7
mārute mārutaṃ viṣṇuṃ nairṛte vidhimaiśvare  || 7.2,25.8
bahiḥpadmasya vajrādyānyabjāṃtānyāyudhānyapi  || 7.2,25.8
prasiddharūpāṇyāśāsu lokeśānāṃ kramādyajet || 7.2,25.9
devaṃ devīṃ ca saṃprekṣya sarvāvaraṇadevatāḥ  || 7.2,25.9
baddhāṃjalipuṭā dhyeyāḥ samāsīnā yathāsukham  || 7.2,25.10
sarvāvaraṇadevānāṃ svābhidhānairnamoyutaiḥ  || 7.2,25.10
puṣpaiḥ saṃpūjanaṃ kuryānnatvā sarvānyathākramam  || 7.2,25.11
garbhāvaraṇamevāpi yajetsvāvaraṇena vā  || 7.2,25.11
yoge dhyāne jape home vāhye vābhyaṃtare 'pi vā  || 7.2,25.12
haviśca ṣaḍvidhaṃ deyaṃ śuddhaṃ mudgānnameva ca  || 7.2,25.12
pāyasaṃ dadhisaṃmiśraṃ gauḍaṃ ca madhunāplutam  || 7.2,25.13
574

eteṣvekamanekaṃ vā nānāvyaṃjanasaṃyutam  || 7.2,25.13
guḍakhaṃḍanvitaṃ dadyānmathitaṃ dadhi cottamam  || 7.2,25.14
bhakṣyāṇyapūpamukhyāni svādumaṃti phalāni ca  || 7.2,25.14
raktacandanapuṣpāḍhyaṃ pānīyaṃ cātiśītalam  || 7.2,25.15
mṛdu elārasāktaṃ ca khaṇḍaṃ pūgaphalasya ca  || 7.2,25.15
dalāni nāgavallyāśca yuktāni khadirādibhiḥ  || 7.2,25.16
gaurāṇi svarṇavarṇāni vihitāni śivāni ca  || 7.2,25.16
śailameva sitaṃ cūrṇaṃ nātirūkṣaṃ na dūṣitam  || 7.2,25.16
karpūraṃ cātha kaṃkolaṃ jātyādi ca navaṃ śubham  || 7.2,25.17
ālepanaṃ candanaṃ syānmūlakāṣṭhaṃrajomayam  || 7.2,25.17
kastūrikā kuṃkumaṃ ca raso mṛgamadātmakaḥ  || 7.2,25.18
puṣpāṇi surabhīṇyeva pavitrāṇi śubhāni ca  || 7.2,25.18
nirgaṃdhānyugragaṃdhāni dūṣitānyuṣitāni ca  || 7.2,25.19
svayameva viśīrṇāni na deyāni śivārcane  || 7.2,25.19
vāsāṃsi ca mṛdūnyeva tapanīyamayāni ca  || 7.2,25.20
vidyudvalayakalpāni bhūṣaṇāni viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,25.20
sarvāṇyetāni karpūraniryāsāgurucandanaiḥ  || 7.2,25.21
ādhūpitāni puṣpaughairvāsitāni samaṃtataḥ  || 7.2,25.21
candanāgurukarpūrakāṣṭhaguggulucūrṇikaiḥ  || 7.2,25.22
ghṛtena madhunā caiva siddho dhūpaḥ praśasyate  || 7.2,25.22
kapilāsambhavenaiva ghṛtenātisugandhinā  || 7.2,25.23
nityaṃ pradīpitā dīpāḥ śastāḥ karpūrasaṃyutāḥ  || 7.2,25.23
pañcagavyaṃ ca madhuraṃ payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ tathā  || 7.2,25.24
kapilāsambhavaṃ śambhoriṣṭaṃ snāne ca pānake  || 7.2,25.24
āsanāni ca bhadrāṇi gajadaṃtamayāni ca  || 7.2,25.25
suvarṇaratnayuktāni citrāṇyāstaraṇāni ca  || 7.2,25.25
mṛdūpadhānayuktāni sūkṣmatūlamayāni ca  || 7.2,25.26
uccāvacāni ramyāṇi śayanāni sukhāni ca  || 7.2,25.26
nadyassamudragāminyā naṭādvāmbhaḥ samāhṛtam  || 7.2,25.27
śītañca vastrapūtaṃ tadviśiṣṭaṃ snānapānayoḥ  || 7.2,25.27
chatraṃ śaśinibhaṃ cāru muktādāmavirājitam  || 7.2,25.28
navaratnacitaṃ divyaṃ hemadaṇḍamanoharam  || 7.2,25.28
cāmare ca site sūkṣme cāmīkarapariṣkṛte  || 7.2,25.29
rājahaṃsadvayākāre ratnadaṃḍopaśobhite  || 7.2,25.29
darpaṇaṃ cāpi susnigdhaṃ divyagandhānulepanam  || 7.2,25.30
samaṃtādratnasañchannaṃ sragvairaiścāpi bhūṣitam  || 7.2,25.30
gambhīraninadaḥ śaṃkho haṃsakuṃdendusannibhaḥ  || 7.2,25.31
āsvapṛṣṭhādideśeṣu ratnacāmīkarācitaḥ  || 7.2,25.31
kāhalāni ca ramyāṇi nānānādakarāṇi ca  || 7.2,25.32
suvarṇanirmitānyeva mauktikālaṃkṛtāni ca  || 7.2,25.32
bherīmṛdaṃgamurajatimicchapaṭahādayaḥ  || 7.2,25.33
samudrakalpasannādāḥ kalpanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ  || 7.2,25.33
bhāṃḍānyapi ca ramyāṇi patrāṇyapi ca kṛtsnaśaḥ  || 7.2,25.34
574

tadādhārāṇi 1 sarvāṇi sauvarṇānyeva sādhayet || 7.2,25.34
ālayaṃ ca maheśasya śivasya paramātmanaḥ  || 7.2,25.35
rājāvasathavatkalpyaṃ śilpaśāstroktalakṣaṇam  || 7.2,25.35
uccaprākārasaṃbhinnaṃ bhūdharākāragopuram  || 7.2,25.36
anekaratnasaṃcchannaṃ hemadvārakapāṭakam  || 7.2,25.36
taptajāṃbūnadamayaṃ ratnastambhaśatāvṛtam  || 7.2,25.37
muktādāmavitānāḍhyaṃ vidrumadvāratoraṇam  || 7.2,25.37
cāmīkaramayairdivyairmukuṭaiḥ kumbhalakṣaṇaiḥ  || 7.2,25.38
alaṃkṛtaśirobhāgamastra 2 ājena cihnitam  || 7.2,25.38
rājanyārhanivāsaiśca rājavīthyādiśobhitaiḥ  || 7.2,25.39
procchritaprāṃśuśikharaiḥ prāsādaiśca samaṃtataḥ  || 7.2,25.39
āsthānasthānavaryaiśca sthitairdikṣu vidikṣu ca  || 7.2,25.40
atyantālaṃkṛtaprāṃtamaṃtarāvaraṇairiva  || 7.2,25.40
uttamastrīsahasraiśca nṛtyageyaviśāradaiḥ  || 7.2,25.41
veṇuvīṇāvidagdhaiśca puruṣairbahubhiryutam  || 7.2,25.41
rakṣitaṃ rakṣibhirvīrairgajavājirathānvitaiḥ  || 7.2,25.42
anekapuṣpavāṭībhiranekaiśca sarovaraiḥ  || 7.2,25.42
dīrghikābhiranekābhirdigvidikṣu virājitam  || 7.2,25.43
vedavedāṃtatattvajñaiśśivaśāstraparāyaṇaiḥ  || 7.2,25.43
śivāśramaratairbhaktaiḥ śivaśāstroktalakṣaṇaiḥ  || 7.2,25.44
śāṃtaiḥ smitamukhaiḥ sphītaiḥ sadācāraparāyaṇaiḥ  || 7.2,25.44
śaivairmāheśvaraiścaiva śrīmadbhissevitadvijaiḥ  || 7.2,25.45
evamaṃtarbahirvāthayathāśaktivinirmitaiḥ  || 7.2,25.45
sthāne śilāmaye dāṃte dārave ceṣṭakāmaye  || 7.2,25.46
kevalaṃ mṛnmaye vāpi puṇyāraṇye 'tha vā girau  || 7.2,25.46
nadyāṃ devālaye 'nyatra deśe vātha gṛhe śubhe  || 7.2,25.47
āḍhyo vātha daridro vā svakāṃ śaktimavaṃcayan  || 7.2,25.47
dravyairnyāyārjitaireva bhaktyā devaṃ samarcayet || 7.2,25.47
athānyāyārjitaiścāpi bhaktyā cecchivamarcayet || 7.2,25.48
na tasya pratyavāyo 'sti bhāvavaśyo yataḥ prabhuḥ  || 7.2,25.48
nyāyārjitairapi dravyairabhaktyā pūjayedyadi  || 7.2,25.49
na tatphalamavāpnoti bhaktirevātra kāraṇam  || 7.2,25.49
bhaktyā vittānusāreṇa śivamuddiśya yatkṛtam  || 7.2,25.50
alpe mahati vā tulyaṃ phalamāḍhyadaridrayoḥ  || 7.2,25.50
bhaktyā pracoditaḥ kuryādalpavittopi mānavaḥ  || 7.2,25.51
mahāvibhavasāropi na kuryādbhaktivarjitaḥ  || 7.2,25.51
sarvasvamapi yo dadyācchive bhaktivivarjitaḥ  || 7.2,25.52
na tena phalabhāksa syādbhaktirevātra kāraṇam  || 7.2,25.52
na tattapobhiratyugrairna ca sarvairmahāmakhaiḥ  || 7.2,25.53
gacchecchivapuraṃ divyaṃ muktvā bhaktiṃ śivātmakam  || 7.2,25.53

1 sambandhamanuvartate iti bhāṣyātkvacidghañāntasyāpi klībatāta eva śeṣaṃ rāmavadityādi saṃgacchate
2 triśūleneti bhāvaḥ

575

guhyādguhyataraṃ kṛṣṇa sarvatra parameśvare  || 7.2,25.54
śive bhaktirna saṃdehastayā bhakto vimucyate  || 7.2,25.54
śivamaṃtrajapo dhyānaṃ homo yajñastapaḥśrutam  || 7.2,25.55
dānamadhyayanaṃ sarve bhāvārthaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,25.55
bhāvahīno narassarvaṃ kṛtvāpi na vimucyate  || 7.2,25.56
bhāvayuktaḥ punassarvamakṛtvāpi vimucyate  || 7.2,25.56
cāṃdrāyaṇasahasraiśca prājāpatyaśataistathā  || 7.2,25.57
māsopavāsaiścānyaiśca śivabhaktasya kiṃ punaḥ  || 7.2,25.57
abhaktā mānavāścāsmiṃlloke giriguhāsu ca  || 7.2,25.58
tapaṃti cālpabhogārthaṃ bhakto bhāvena mucyate  || 7.2,25.58
sāttvikaṃ muktidaṃ karma sattve vai yoginaḥ sthitāḥ  || 7.2,25.59
rājasaṃ siddhidaṃ kuryuḥ karmiṇo rajasāvṛtāḥ  || 7.2,25.59
asurā rākṣasāścaiva tamoguṇasamanvitāḥ  || 7.2,25.60
aihikārthaṃ yajantīśaṃ narāścānye 'pi tādṛśāḥ  || 7.2,25.60
tāmasaṃ rājasaṃ vāpi sāttvikaṃ bhāvameva ca  || 7.2,25.61
āśritya bhaktyā pūjādyaṃ kurvanbhadraṃ samaśnute  || 7.2,25.61
yataḥ pāpārṇavāttrātuṃ bhaktirnauriva nirmitā  || 7.2,25.62
tasmādbhaktyupapannasya rajasā tamasā ca kim  || 7.2,25.62
antyajo vādhamo vāpi mūrkho vā patito 'pi vā  || 7.2,25.63
śivaṃ prapannaścetkṛṣṇa pūjyassarvasurāsuraiḥ  || 7.2,25.63
tasmātsarvaprayatnena bhaktyaiva śivamarcayet || 7.2,25.64
abhuktānāṃ kvacidapi phalaṃ nāsti yatastataḥ  || 7.2,25.64
vakṣyāmyatirahasyaṃ te śṛṇu kṛṣṇa vaco mama  || 7.2,25.65
vedaiśśāstrairvedavidbhirvicārya suviniścitam  || 7.2,25.65

oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe śivaśāstroktapūjanavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcaviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 26
upamanyuruvāca
brahmaghno vā surāpo vā steyīvā gurutalpagaḥ  || 7.2,26.1
mātṛhā pitṛhā vāpi vīrahā bhrūṇahāpi vā  || 7.2,26.1
saṃpūjyāmantrakaṃ bhaktyā śivaṃ paramakāraṇam  || 7.2,26.2
taistaiḥ pāpaiḥ pramucyeta varṣairdvādaśabhiḥ kramāt || 7.2,26.2
tasmātsarvaprayatnena patito 'pi yajecchivam  || 7.2,26.3
bhaktaścennāparaḥ kaścidbhikṣāhāro jiteṃdriyaḥ  || 7.2,26.3
kṛtvāpi sumahatpāpaṃ bhaktyā pañcākṣareṇa tu  || 7.2,26.4
pūjayedyadi deveśaṃ tasmātpāpātpramucyate  || 7.2,26.4
abbhakṣā vāyubhakṣāśca ye cānye vratakarśitāḥ  || 7.2,26.5
teṣāmetairvratairnāsti śivalokasamāgamaḥ  || 7.2,26.5
bhaktyā pañcākṣareṇaiva yaḥ śivaṃ sakṛdarcayet || 7.2,26.6
sopi gacchecchivasthānaṃ śivamantrasya gauravāt || 7.2,26.6
tasmāttapāṃsi yajñāṃśca sarve sarvasvadakṣiṇāḥ  || 7.2,26.7
śivamūrtyarcanasyaite koṭyaṃśenāpi no samāḥ  || 7.2,26.7
baddho vāpyatha mukto vā paścātpañcākṣareṇa cet || 7.2,26.8
pūjayanmucyate bhakto nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 7.2,26.8
arudro vā sarudro vā sūktena śivamarcayet || 7.2,26.9
yaḥ sakṛtpatito vāpimūḍho vā mucyate naraḥ  || 7.2,26.9
575

ṣaḍakṣareṇa vā devaṃ sūktamantreṇa pūjayet || 7.2,26.10
śivabhakto jitakrodho hyalabdho labdha eva ca  || 7.2,26.10
alabdhāllabdha evātra viśiṣṭo nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,26.11
sa brahmāṃgena vā tena sahaṃsena vimucyate  || 7.2,26.11
tasmānnityaṃ śivaṃ bhaktyā sūktamantreṇa pūjayet || 7.2,26.12
ekakālaṃ dvikālaṃ vā trikālaṃ nityameva vā  || 7.2,26.12
ye 'rcayaṃti mahādevaṃ vijñeyāste maheśvarāḥ  || 7.2,26.13
jñānenātmasahāyena nārcito bhagavāñchivaḥ  || 7.2,26.13
sa ciraṃ saṃsaratyasminsaṃsāre duḥkhasāgare  || 7.2,26.14
durllabhaṃ prāpya mānuṣyaṃ mūḍho nārcayate śivam  || 7.2,26.14
niṣphalaṃ tasya tajjanma mokṣāya na bhavedyataḥ  || 7.2,26.15
durllabhaṃ prāpya mānuṣyaṃ ye 'rcayanti pinākinam  || 7.2,26.15
teṣāṃ hi saphalaṃ janma kṛtārthāste narottamāḥ  || 7.2,26.16
bhavabhaktiparā ye ca bhavapraṇatacetasaḥ  || 7.2,26.16
bhavasaṃsmaraṇodyuktā na te duḥkhasya bhāginaḥ  || 7.2,26.17
bhavanāni manojñāni vibhramābharaṇāḥ striyaḥ  || 7.2,26.17
dhanaṃ cātṛptiparyantaṃ śivapūjāvidheḥ phalam  || 7.2,26.18
ye vāñchanti mahābhogānrājyaṃ ca tridaśālaye  || 7.2,26.18
te vāñchanti sadākālaṃ harasya caraṇāmbujam  || 7.2,26.19
saubhāgyaṃ kāntimadrūpaṃ sattvaṃ tyāgārdrabhāvatā  || 7.2,26.19
śauryaṃ vai jagati khyātiśśivamarcayato bhavet || 7.2,26.20
tasmātsarvaṃ parityajya śivaikāhitamānasaḥ  || 7.2,26.20
śivapūjāvidhiṃ kuryādyadīcchecchivamātmanaḥ  || 7.2,26.21
tvaritaṃ jīvitaṃ yāti tvaritaṃ yāti yauvanam  || 7.2,26.21
tvaritaṃ vyādhirabhyeti tasmātpūjyaḥ pinākadhṛk || 7.2,26.22
yāvannāyāti maraṇaṃ yāvannākramate jarā  || 7.2,26.22
yāvannendriyavaikalyaṃ tāvatpūjaya śaṃkaram  || 7.2,26.23
na śivārcanatulyo 'sti dharmo 'nyo bhuvanatraye  || 7.2,26.23
iti vijñāya yatnena pūjanīyassadāśivaḥ  || 7.2,26.24
dvārayāgaṃ javanikāṃ parivārabalikriyām  || 7.2,26.24
nityotsavaṃ ca kurvīta prasāde yadi pūjayet || 7.2,26.25
havirnivedanādūrdhvaṃ svayaṃ cānucaro 'pi vā  || 7.2,26.25
prasādaparivārebhyo baliṃ dadyādyathākramam  || 7.2,26.26
nirgamya saha vāditraistadāśābhimukhaḥ sthitaḥ  || 7.2,26.26
puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ ca dīpañca dadyādannaṃ jalaiḥ saha  || 7.2,26.27
tato dadyānmahāpīṭhe tiṣṭhanbalimudaṅmukhaḥ  || 7.2,26.27
tato niveditaṃ deve yattadannādikaṃ purā  || 7.2,26.28
tatsarvaṃ sāvaśeṣaṃ vā caṇḍāya vinivedayet || 7.2,26.28
hutvā ca vidhivatpaścātpūjāśeṣaṃ samāpayet || 7.2,26.29
kṛtvā prayogaṃ vidhivadyāvanmantraṃ japaṃ tataḥ  || 7.2,26.29
nityotsavaṃ prakurvīta yathoktaṃ śivaśāsane  || 7.2,26.30
576

vipule taijase pātre raktapadmopaśobhite  || 7.2,26.30
astraṃ pāśupataṃ divyaṃ tatrāvāhya samarcayet || 7.2,26.31
śivasyāropyaḥ tatpātraṃ dvijasyālaṃkṛtasya ca  || 7.2,26.31
nyastāstravapuṣā tena dīptayaṣṭidharasya ca  || 7.2,26.32
prāsādaparivārebhyo bahirmaṃgalaniḥsvanaiḥ  || 7.2,26.32
nṛtyageyādibhiścaiva saha dīpadhvajādibhiḥ  || 7.2,26.33
pradakṣiṇatrayaṃ kṛtvā na drutaṃ cāvilambitam  || 7.2,26.33
mahāpīṭhaṃ samāvṛtya triḥpradakṣiṇayogataḥ  || 7.2,26.34
punaḥ praviṣṭo dvārastho yajamānaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ  || 7.2,26.34
ādāyābhyaṃtaraṃ nītvā hyastramudvāsayettataḥ  || 7.2,26.34
pradakṣiṇādikaṃ kṛtvā yathāpūrvoditaṃ kramāt || 7.2,26.35
ādāya cāṣṭapuṣpāṇi pūjāmatha samāpayet || 7.2,26.35
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe sāṃgopāṃgapūjāvidhānavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaḍviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 27
upamanyuruvāca
athāgnikāryaṃ vakṣyāmi kuṇḍe vā sthaṃḍile 'pi vā  || 7.2,27.1
vedyāṃ vā hyāyase pātre mṛnmaye vā nave śubhe  || 7.2,27.1
ādhāyāgniṃ vidhānena saṃskṛtya ca tataḥ param  || 7.2,27.2
tatrārādhya mahādevaṃ homakarma samācaret || 7.2,27.2
kuṇḍaṃ dvihastamānaṃ vā hastamātramathāpi vā  || 7.2,27.3
vṛttaṃ vā caturasraṃ vā kuryādvediṃ ca maṇḍalam  || 7.2,27.3
kuṇḍaṃ vistāravannimnaṃ tanmadhye 'ṣṭadalāmbujam  || 7.2,27.4
caturaṃgulamutsedhaṃ tasya dvyaṃgulameva vā  || 7.2,27.4
vitastidviguṇonnatyā nābhimantaḥ pracakṣate  || 7.2,27.5
madhyaṃ ca madhyamāṃgulyā madhyamottamaparvaṇoḥ  || 7.2,27.5
aṃgulaiḥ kathyate sadbhiścaturviṃśatibhiḥ karaḥ  || 7.2,27.6
mekhalānāṃ trayaṃ vāpi dvayamekamathāpi vā  || 7.2,27.6
yathāśobhaṃ prakurvīta ślakṣṇamiṣṭaṃ mṛdā sthiram  || 7.2,27.7
aśvatthapatravadyoniṃ gajādhāravadeva vā  || 7.2,27.7
mekhalāmadhyataḥ kuryātpaścime dakṣiṇe 'pi vā  || 7.2,27.8
śobhanāmagnitaḥ kiṃcinnimnāmunmīlikāṃ śanaiḥ  || 7.2,27.8
agreṇa kuṇḍābhimukhīṃ kiṃcidutsṛjya mekhalām  || 7.2,27.9
notsedhaniyamo vedyāḥ sā mārdī vātha saikatī  || 7.2,27.9
maṃḍalaṃ gośakṛttoyairmānaṃ pātrasya noditam  || 7.2,27.10
kuṇḍaṃ ca mṛnmayaṃ vedimālipedgomayāṃbunā  || 7.2,27.10
prakṣālya tāpayetpātraṃ prokṣayedanyadaṃbhasā  || 7.2,27.11
svasūtroktaprakāreṇa kuṇḍādau villikhettataḥ  || 7.2,27.11
saṃprokṣya kalpayeddarbhaiḥ puṣpairvā vahniviṣṭaram  || 7.2,27.12
arcanārthaṃ ca homārthaṃ sarvadravyāṇi sādhayet || 7.2,27.12
prakṣālyakṣālanīyāni prokṣaṇyā prokṣya śodhayet || 7.2,27.13
maṇijaṃ kāṣṭhajaṃ vātha śrotriyāgārasambhavam  || 7.2,27.13

1 adantatvamārṣam

576

anyaṃ vābhyarhitaṃ vahniṃ tataḥ sādhāramānayet || 7.2,27.14
triḥ pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya kuṇḍāderupari kramāt || 7.2,27.14
vahnibījaṃ samuccārya tvādadhītāgnimāsane  || 7.2,27.15
yonimārgeṇa vā tadvadātmanaḥ saṃmukhena vā  || 7.2,27.15
niyogaḥ pradeśa sarvaṃ kuṃḍaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ  || 7.2,27.16
svanābhyaṃtaḥsthitaṃ vahniṃ tadraṃdhrādvisphuliṃgavad || 7.2,27.16
nirgamya pāvake bahye līnaṃ biṃbākṛti smaret || 7.2,27.17
ājyasaṃskāraparyaṃtamanvādhānapurassaram  || 7.2,27.17
svasūtroktakramātkuryānmūlamantreṇa mantravit || 7.2,27.18
śivamūrtiṃ samabhyarcya tato dakṣiṇapārśvataḥ  || 7.2,27.18
nyasya mantraṃ ghṛte mudrāṃ darśayeddhenusaṃjñitām  || 7.2,27.19
sruksruvau taijasau grāhyau na kāṃsyāyasasaisakau  || 7.2,27.19
yajñadārumayau vāpi smārtau vā śilpasammatau  || 7.2,27.20
parṇe vā brahmavṛkṣāderacchidre madhya utthite  || 7.2,27.20
saṃsṛjya darbhaistau vahnau saṃtāpya prokṣayetpunaḥ  || 7.2,27.21
pārārṣarcyasvasūtroktakrameṇa śivapūrvakaiḥ  || 7.2,27.21
juhuyādaṣṭabhirbījairagnisaṃskārasiddhaye  || 7.2,27.22
bhruṃstuṃbruśruṃ krameṇaiva puṃḍraṃdramityataḥ param  || 7.2,27.22
bījāni sapta saptānāṃ jihvānāmanupūrvaśaḥ  || 7.2,27.23
triśikhā madhyamā jihvā bahurūpasamāhvayā  || 7.2,27.23
raktāgneyī nairṛtī ca kṛṣṇānyā suprabhā matā  || 7.2,27.24
atiriktā marujjihvā svanāmānuguṇaprabhā  || 7.2,27.24
svabijānantaraṃ vācyā svāhāṃtañca yathākramam  || 7.2,27.25
jihvāmaṃtraistu tairhutvājyaṃ jihvāstvekaikaśa kramāt || 7.2,27.25
raṃ vahnayeti svāheti madhye hutvāhutitrayam  || 7.2,27.26
sarpiṣā vā samidbhirvā pariṣecanamācaret || 7.2,27.26
evaṃ kṛte śivāgniḥ syātsmarettatra śivāsanam  || 7.2,27.27
tatrāvāhya yajeddevamardhanārīśvaraṃ śivam  || 7.2,27.27
dīpāntaṃ pariṣicyātha samiddhomaṃ samācaret || 7.2,27.27
tāḥ pālāśyaḥ parā vāpi yājñiyā dvādaśāṃgulāḥ  || 7.2,27.28
avakrā na svayaṃ śuṣkāssatvaco nirvraṇāḥ samāḥ  || 7.2,27.28
daśāṃgulā vā vihitāḥ kaniṣṭhāṃgulisaṃmitāḥ  || 7.2,27.29
prādeśamātrā vālābhe hotavyāḥ sakalā api  || 7.2,27.29
dūrvāpatrasamākārāṃ caturaṃgulamāyatām  || 7.2,27.30
dadyādājyāhutiṃ paścādannamakṣapramāṇataḥ  || 7.2,27.30
lājāṃstathā sarṣapāṃśca yavāṃścaiva tilāṃstathā  || 7.2,27.31
sarpiṣāktāni bhakṣyāṇi lehyacoṣyāṇi sambhave  || 7.2,27.31
daśaivāhutayastatra pañca vā tritayaṃ ca vā  || 7.2,27.32
hotavyāḥ śaktito dadyādekamevātha vāhutim  || 7.2,27.32
śruveṇājyaṃ samityādyāsrucāśeṣātkareṇa vā  || 7.2,27.33
tatra divyena hotavyaṃ tīrthenārṣeṇa vā tathā  || 7.2,27.33
dravyeṇaikena vā 'lābhe juhuyācchraddhayā punaḥ  || 7.2,27.34
prāyaścittāya juhuyānmaṃtrayitvāhutitrayam  || 7.2,27.34
577

tato homaviśiṣṭena ghṛtenāpūrya vai srucam  || 7.2,27.35
nidhāya puṣpaṃ tasyāgre śruveṇādhomukhena tām  || 7.2,27.35
sadarbhena samācchādya mūlenāṃjalinotthitaḥ  || 7.2,27.36
vauṣaḍaṃtena juhuyāddhārāṃ tu yavasaṃmitām  || 7.2,27.36
itthaṃ pūrṇāhutiṃ kṛtvā pariṣiṃcecca pūrvavat || 7.2,27.37
tata udvāsya deveśaṃ gopayettu hutāśanam  || 7.2,27.37
tamapyudvāsya vā nābhau yajetsaṃdhāya nityaśaḥ  || 7.2,27.38
athavā vahnimānīya śivaśāstroktavartmanā  || 7.2,27.38
vāgīśīgarbhasaṃbhūtaṃ saṃskṛtya vidhivadyajet || 7.2,27.39
anvādhānaṃ punaḥ kṛtvā paridhīn paridhāya ca  || 7.2,27.39
pātrāṇi dvandvarūpeṇa nikṣipyeṣṭvā śivaṃ tataḥ  || 7.2,27.40
saṃśodhya prokṣaṇīpātraṃ prokṣyatāni tadaṃbhasā  || 7.2,27.40
praṇītāpātramaiśānyāṃ vinyasyā pūritaṃ jalaiḥ  || 7.2,27.41
ājyasaṃskāraparyaṃtaṃ kṛtvā saṃśodhya sraksruvau  || 7.2,27.41
garbhādhānaṃ puṃsavanaṃ sīmantonnayanaṃ tataḥ  || 7.2,27.42
kṛtvā pṛthakpṛthagghutvā jātamagniṃ vicintayet || 7.2,27.42
tripādaṃ saptahastaṃ ca catuḥśṛṃgaṃ dviśīrṣakam  || 7.2,27.43
madhupiṃgaṃ trinayanaṃ sakapardenduśekharam  || 7.2,27.43
raktaṃ raktāmbarālepaṃ mālyabhūṣanabhūṣitam  || 7.2,27.44
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpannaṃ sopavītaṃ trimekhalam  || 7.2,27.44
śaktimantaṃ sruksruvau ca dadhānaṃ dakṣiṇe kare  || 7.2,27.45
tomaraṃ tālavṛṃtaṃ ca ghṛtapātraṃ tathetaraiḥ  || 7.2,27.45
jātaṃ dhyātvaivamākāraṃ jātakarma samācaret || 7.2,27.46
nālāpanayanaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ saṃśodhya sūtakam  || 7.2,27.46
śivāgnirucināmāsya kṛtvāhutipurassaram  || 7.2,27.47
pitrorvisarjanaṃ kṛtvā caulopanayanādikam  || 7.2,27.47
aptoryāmāvasānāntaṃ kṛtvā saṃskāramasya tu  || 7.2,27.48
ājyadhārādihomaṃ ca kṛtvā sviṣṭakṛtaṃ tataḥ  || 7.2,27.48
ramityanena bījena pariṣiṃcettataḥ param  || 7.2,27.49
brahmaviṣṇuśiveśānāṃ lokeśānāṃ tathaiva ca  || 7.2,27.49
tadastrāṇāṃ ca paritaḥ kṛtvā pūjāṃ yathākramam  || 7.2,27.50
dhūpadīpādisiddhyarthaṃ vahnimuddhṛtya kṛtyavit || 7.2,27.50
sādhayitvājyapūrvāṇi dravyāṇi punareva ca  || 7.2,27.51
kalpayitvāsanaṃ vahnau tatrāvāhya yathāpurā  || 7.2,27.51
saṃpūjya devaṃ devīṃ ca tataḥ pūrṇāṃtamācaret || 7.2,27.52
atha vā svāśramoktaṃ tu vahnikarma śivārpaṇam  || 7.2,27.52
buddhvā śivāśramī kuryānna ca tatrāparo vidhiḥ  || 7.2,27.53
śivāgnerbhasmasaṃgrāhyamagnihotrodbhavaṃ tu vā  || 7.2,27.53
vaivāhognibhavaṃ vāpi pakvaṃ śuci sugaṃdhi ca  || 7.2,27.54
kapilāyāḥ śakṛcchastaṃ gṛhītaṃ gagane patat || 7.2,27.54
na klinnaṃ nātikaṭhinaṃ na durgandhaṃ na śoṣitam  || 7.2,27.55
uparyadhaḥ parityajya gṛhṇīyātpatitaṃ yadi  || 7.2,27.55
piṃḍīkṛtya śivāgnyādau tatkṣipenmūlamaṃtrataḥ  || 7.2,27.56
577

apakvamatipākvaṃ ca saṃtyajya bhasitaṃ sitam  || 7.2,27.56
ādāya vā samāloḍya bhasmādhāre vinikṣipet || 7.2,27.57
taijasaṃ dāravaṃ vāpi mṛnmayaṃ śailameva ca  || 7.2,27.57
anyadvā śobhanaṃ śuddhaṃ bhasmādhāraṃ prakalpayet || 7.2,27.58
same deśe śubhe śuddhe dhanavadbhasma nikṣipet || 7.2,27.58
na cāyuktakare dadyānnaivāśucitale kṣipet || 7.2,27.59
na saṃspṛśecca nīcāṃgairnopekṣeta na laṃghayet || 7.2,27.59
tasmādbhasitamādāya viniyuṃjīta mantrataḥ  || 7.2,27.60
kāleṣūkteṣu nānyatra nāyogyebhyaḥ pradāpayet || 7.2,27.60
bhasmasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kuryāddeve 'nudvāsite sati  || 7.2,27.61
udvāsane kṛte yasmāccaṇḍabhasma prajāpate  || 7.2,27.61
agnikārye kṛte paścācchivaśāstroktamārgataḥ  || 7.2,27.62
svasūtroktaprakārādvā balikarma samācaret || 7.2,27.62
atha vidyāsanaṃ nyasya supralipte tu maṇdale  || 7.2,27.63
vidyākośaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya yajetpuṣpādibhiḥ kramāt || 7.2,27.63
vidyāyāḥ purataḥ kṛtvā gurorapi ca maṇḍalam  || 7.2,27.64
tatrāsanavaraṃ kṛtvā puṣpādyai gurumarcayet || 7.2,27.64
tatonupūjayetpūjyān bhojayecca bubhukṣitān  || 7.2,27.65
tatassvayaṃ ca bhuṃjīta śuddhamannaṃ yathāsukham  || 7.2,27.65
niveditaṃ ca vā deve taccheṣaṃ cātmaśuddhaye  || 7.2,27.66
śraddadhāno na lobhena na caṇḍāya samarpitam  || 7.2,27.66
gandhamālyādi yaccānyattatrāpyeṣa samo vidhiḥ  || 7.2,27.67
na tu tatra śivosmīti buddhiṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ  || 7.2,27.67
bhuktvācamya śivaṃ dhyātvā hṛdaye mūlamuccaret || 7.2,27.68
kālaśeṣaṃ nayedyogyaiḥ śivaśāstrakathādibhiḥ  || 7.2,27.68
rātrau vyatīte pūrvāṃśe kṛtvā pūjāṃ manoharām  || 7.2,27.69
śivayoḥ śayanaṃ tvekaṃ kalpayedatiśobhanam  || 7.2,27.69
bhakṣyabhojyāṃbarālepapuṣpamālādikaṃ tathā  || 7.2,27.70
manasā karmaṇā vāpi kṛtvā sarvaṃ manoharam  || 7.2,27.70
tato devasya devyāśca pādamūle śucissvapet || 7.2,27.71
gṛhastho bhāryayā sārdhaṃ tadanye 'pi tu kevalāḥ  || 7.2,27.71
pratyūṣasamayaṃ buddhvā mātrāmādyāmudīrayet || 7.2,27.72
praṇamya manasāṃ devaṃ sāṃbaṃ sagaṇamavyayam  || 7.2,27.72
deśakālocitaṃ kṛtvā śaucādyamapi śaktitaḥ  || 7.2,27.73
śaṃkhādininadairdivyairdevaṃ devīṃ ca bodhayet || 7.2,27.73
tatastatsamayonnidraiḥ puṣpairatisugaṃdhibhiḥ  || 7.2,27.74
nirvartya śivayoḥ pūjāṃ prārabheta puroditam  || 7.2,27.74
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe agnikāryavarṇanaṃ nāma saptaviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 28
upamanyuruvāca
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi śivāśramaniṣeviṇām  || 7.2,28.1
śivaśāstroktamārgeṇa naimittikavidhikramam  || 7.2,28.1
sarveṣvapi ca māseṣu pakṣayorubhayorapi  || 7.2,28.2
578

aṣṭābhyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ tathā parvāṇi ca kramāt || 7.2,28.2
ayane viṣuve caiva grahaṇeṣu viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,28.3
kartavyā mahatī pūjā hyadhikā vāpi śaktitaḥ  || 7.2,28.3
māsimāsi yathānyāyaṃ brahmakūrcaṃ prasādhya tu  || 7.2,28.4
snāpayitvā śivaṃ tena pibeccheṣamupoṣitaḥ  || 7.2,28.4
brahmahatyādidoṣāṇāmatīva mahatāmapi  || 7.2,28.5
niṣkṛtirbrahmakūrcasya pānānnānyā viśiṣyate  || 7.2,28.5
pauṣe puṣyanakṣatre kuryānnīrājanaṃ vibhoḥ  || 7.2,28.6
māghe maghākhye nakṣatre pradadyādghṛtakaṃbalam  || 7.2,28.6
phālgune cottarānte vai prārabheta mahotsavam  || 7.2,28.7
caitre citrāpaurṇamāsyāṃ dolāṃ kuryādyathāvidhi  || 7.2,28.7
vaiśākhyāṃ tu viśākhāyāṃ kuryātpuṣpamahālayam  || 7.2,28.8
jyeṣṭhe mūlākhyanakṣatre śītakumbhaṃ pradāpayet || 7.2,28.8
āṣāḍhe cottarāṣāḍhe pavitrāropaṇaṃ tathā  || 7.2,28.9
śrāvaṇe prākṛtānyāpi maṇḍalāni prakalpayet || 7.2,28.9
śraviṣṭhākhye tu nakṣatre prauṣṭhapadyāṃ tataḥ param  || 7.2,28.10
prokṣayecca jalakrīḍāṃ pūrvāṣāḍhāśraye dine  || 7.2,28.10
āśvayujyāṃ tato dadyātpāyasaṃ ca navodanam  || 7.2,28.11
agnikāryaṃ ca tenaiva kuryācchatabhiṣagdine  || 7.2,28.11
kārtikyāṃ kṛtikāyoge dadyāddīpasahasrakam  || 7.2,28.12
mārgaśīrṣe tathārdrāyāṃ ghṛtena snāpayecchivam  || 7.2,28.12
aśaktasteṣu kāleṣu kuryādutsavameva vā  || 7.2,28.13
āsthānaṃ vā mahāpūjāmadhikaṃ vā samarcanam  || 7.2,28.13
āvṛtte 'pi ca kalyāṇe praśasteṣvapi karmasu  || 7.2,28.14
daurmanasye durācāre duḥsvapne duṣṭadarśane  || 7.2,28.14
utpāte vāśubhenyasminroge vā prabale 'tha vā  || 7.2,28.15
snānapūjājapadhyānahomadānādikāḥ kriyaḥ  || 7.2,28.15
nirmitānuguṇāḥ kāryāḥ puraścaraṇapūrvikāḥ  || 7.2,28.16
śivānale ca vihate punassandhānamācaret || 7.2,28.16
ya evaṃ śarvadharmiṣṭho vartate nityamudyataḥ  || 7.2,28.17
tasyaikajanmanā muktiṃ prayacchati maheśvaraḥ  || 7.2,28.17
etadyathottaraṃ kuryānnityanaimittikeṣu yaḥ  || 7.2,28.18
divyaṃ śrīkaṃṭhanāthasya sthānamādyaṃ sa gacchati  || 7.2,28.18
tatra bhuktvā mahābhogānkalpakoṭiśatannaraḥ  || 7.2,28.19
kālāṃtarecyutastasmādaumaṃ kaumārameva ca  || 7.2,28.19
saṃprāpya vaiṣṇavaṃ brāhmaṃ rudralokaṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,28.20
tatroṣitvā ciraṃ kālaṃ bhuktvā bhogānyathoditān  || 7.2,28.20
punaścordhvaṃ gatastasmādatītya sthānapañcakam  || 7.2,28.21
śrīkaṇṭhājjñānamāsādya tasmācchaivapuraṃ vrajet || 7.2,28.21
ardhacaryārataścāpi dvirāvṛttyaivameva tu  || 7.2,28.22
paścājjñānaṃ samāsādya śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt || 7.2,28.22
ardhārdhacarito yastu dehī dehakṣayātparam  || 7.2,28.23
aṃḍāṃtaṃ vordhvamavyaktamatītya bhuvanadvayam  || 7.2,28.23
saṃprāpya pauruṣaṃ raudrasthānamadrīndrajāpateḥ  || 7.2,28.24
578

anekayugasāhasraṃ bhuktvā bhogānanekadhā  || 7.2,28.24
puṇyakṣaye kṣitiṃ prāpya kule mahati jāyate  || 7.2,28.25
tatrāpi pūrvasaṃskāravaśena sa mahādyutiḥ  || 7.2,28.25
paśudharmānparityajya śivadharmarato bhavet || 7.2,28.26
taddharmagauravādeva dhyātvā śivapuraṃ vrajet || 7.2,28.26
bhogāṃśca vividhānbhuktvā vidyeśvarapadaṃ vrajet || 7.2,28.27
tatra vidyeśvaraissārdhaṃ bhuktvā bhogānbahūnkramāt || 7.2,28.27
aṇḍasyāṃtarbahirvātha sakṛdāvartate punaḥ  || 7.2,28.28
tato labdhvā śivajñānaṃ parāṃ bhaktimavāpya ca  || 7.2,28.28
śivasādharmyamāsādya na bhūyo vinivartate  || 7.2,28.29
yaścātīva śive bhakto viṣayāsaktacittavat || 7.2,28.29
śivadarmānaso kurvannakurvanvāpi mucyate  || 7.2,28.30
ekāvṛtto dvirāvṛttastrirāvṛtto nivartakaḥ  || 7.2,28.30
na punaścakravartī syācchivadharmādhikāravān  || 7.2,28.31
tasmāccchivāśrito bhūtvā yena kenāpi hetunā  || 7.2,28.31
śivadharme matiṃ kuryācchreyase cetkṛtodyamaḥ  || 7.2,28.32
nātra nirbaṃdhayiṣyāmo vayaṃ kecana kenacit || 7.2,28.32
nirbandhebhyo 'tivādebhyaḥ prakṛtyaitanna rocate  || 7.2,28.33
rocate vā parebhyastu puṇyasaṃskāragauravāt || 7.2,28.33
saṃsārakāraṇaṃ yeṣāṃ na praroḍhumalaṃ bhavet || 7.2,28.34
prakṛtyanuguṇaṃ tasmādvimṛśyaitadaśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,28.34
śivadharme 'dhikurvīta yadīcchecchivamātmanaḥ  || 7.2,28.35

oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe nityanaimittikavidhivarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭāviṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 29
śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca
bhagavaṃstvanmukhādeva śrutaṃ śrutisamaṃ mayā  || 7.2,29.1
svāśritānāṃ śivaproktaṃ nityanaimittikaṃ tathā  || 7.2,29.1
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi śivadharmādhikāriṇām  || 7.2,29.2
kāmyamapyasti cetkarma vaktumarhasi sāmpratam  || 7.2,29.2
upamanyuruvāca
astyaihikaphalaṃ kiñcidāmuṣmikaphalaṃ tathā  || 7.2,29.3
aihikāmuṣmikañcāpi tacca pañcavidhaṃ punaḥ  || 7.2,29.3
kiṃcitkriyāmayaṃ karma kiṃcitkarma tapo mayam  || 7.2,29.4
japadhyānamayaṃ kiṃcitkiṃcitsarvamayaṃ tathā  || 7.2,29.5
kriyāmayaṃ tathā bhinnaṃ homadānārcanakramāt || 7.2,29.5
sarvaśaktimatāmeva nānyeṣāṃ saphalaṃ bhavet || 7.2,29.6
śaktiścājñā madeśasya śivasya paramātmanaḥ  || 7.2,29.6
tasmātkāmyāni karmāṇi kuryādājñādharodvijaḥ  || 7.2,29.7
atha vakṣyāmi kāmyaṃ hi cehāmutra phalapradam  || 7.2,29.7
śaivairmāheśvaraiścaiva kāryamaṃtarbahiḥ kramāt || 7.2,29.8
śivo maheśvaraśceti nātyaṃtamiha bhidyate  || 7.2,29.8
yathā tathā na bhidyaṃte śaivā māheśvarā api  || 7.2,29.9
śivāśritā hi te śaivā jñānayajñaratā narāḥ  || 7.2,29.9
māheśvarāssamākhyātā karmayajñaratā bhuvi  || 7.2,29.10
579

tasmādābhyantare kuryuḥ śaivā māheśvarā vahiḥ  || 7.2,29.10
na tu prayogo bhidyeta vakṣyamāṇasya karmaṇaḥ  || 7.2,29.11
parīkṣya bhūmiṃ vidhivadgaṃdhavarṇarasādibhiḥ  || 7.2,29.11
manobhilaṣite tatra vitānavitatāṃbare  || 7.2,29.12
supralipte mahīpṛṣṭhe darpaṇodarasaṃnibhe  || 7.2,29.12
prācīmutpādayetpūrvaṃ śāstradṛṣṭena vartmanā  || 7.2,29.13
ekahastaṃ dvihastaṃ vā maṇḍalaṃ parikalpayet || 7.2,29.13
ālikhedvimalaṃ padmamaṣṭapatraṃ sakarṇikam  || 7.2,29.14
ratnahemādibhiścūrṇairyathāsaṃbhavasaṃbhṛtaiḥ  || 7.2,29.14
pañcāvaraṇasaṃyuktaṃ bahuśobhāsamanvitam  || 7.2,29.15
daleṣu siddhayaḥ kalpyāḥ kesareṣu saśaktikāḥ  || 7.2,29.15
rudrā vāmādayastvaṣṭau pūrvādidalataḥ kramāt || 7.2,29.16
karṇikāyāṃ ca vairāgyaṃ bījeṣu nava śaktayaḥ  || 7.2,29.16
skande śivātmako dharmo nāle jñānaṃ śivāśrayam  || 7.2,29.17
karṇikopari cāgneyaṃ maṃḍalaṃ sauramaindavam  || 7.2,29.17
śivavidyātmatattvākhyaṃ tattvatrayamataḥ param  || 7.2,29.18
sarvāsanopari sukhaṃ vicitrakusumānvitam  || 7.2,29.18
pañcāvaraṇasaṃyuktaṃ pūjayedaṃbayā saha  || 7.2,29.19
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ prasannaṃ śītaladyutim  || 7.2,29.19
vidyudvalayasaṃkāśajaṭāmukuṭabhūṣitam  || 7.2,29.20
śārdūlacarmavasanaṃ kiñcitsmitamukhāṃbujam  || 7.2,29.20
raktapadmadalaprakhyapādapāṇitalādharam  || 7.2,29.21
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpannaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam  || 7.2,29.21
divyāyudhavarairyuktaṃ divyagaṃdhānulepanam  || 7.2,29.22
pañcavaktraṃ daśabhujaṃ candrakhaṇḍaśikhāmaṇim  || 7.2,29.22
asya pūrvamukhaṃ saumyaṃ bālārkasadṛśaprabham  || 7.2,29.23
trilocanāraviṃdāḍhyaṃ kṛtabāleṃduśekharam  || 7.2,29.23
dakṣiṇaṃ nīlajīmūtasamānaruciraprabham  || 7.2,29.24
bhrukuṭīkuṭilaṃ ghoraṃ raktavṛttekṣaṇatrayam  || 7.2,29.24
daṃṣṭrākarālaṃ durdharṣaṃ sphuritādharapallavam  || 7.2,29.25
uttaraṃ vidrumaprakhyaṃ nīlālakavibhūṣitam  || 7.2,29.25
savilāsaṃ trinayanaṃ candrābharaṇaśekharam  || 7.2,29.26
paścimaṃ pūrṇacandrābhaṃ locanatritayojjvalam  || 7.2,29.26
candrarekhādharaṃ saumyaṃ maṃdasmitamanoharam  || 7.2,29.27
pañcamaṃ sphaṭikaprakhyamiṃdurekhāsamujjvalam  || 7.2,29.27
atīva saumyamutphullalocanatritayojjvalam  || 7.2,29.28
dakṣiṇe śūlaparaśuvajrakhaḍgānalojjvalam  || 7.2,29.28
savye ca nāganārācaghaṇṭāpāśāṃkuśojjvalam  || 7.2,29.29
nivṛttyājānusaṃbaddhamānābheśca pratiṣṭhayā  || 7.2,29.29
ākaṃṭhaṃ vidyayā tadvadālalāṭaṃ tu śāṃtayā  || 7.2,29.30
tadūrdhvaṃ śāṃtyatītākhyakalayā parayā tathā  || 7.2,29.30
pañcādhvavyāpinaṃ sākṣātkalāpañcakavigraham  || 7.2,29.31
īśānamukuṭaṃ devaṃ puruṣākhyaṃ purātanam  || 7.2,29.31
aghorahṛdayaṃ tadvadvāmaguhyaṃ maheśvaram  || 7.2,29.32
579

sadyapādaṃ ca tanmūrtimaṣṭatriṃśatkalāmayam  || 7.2,29.32
mātṛkāmayamīśānaṃ pañcabrahmamayaṃ tathā  || 7.2,29.33
oṃkārākhyamayaṃ caiva haṃsaśaktyā samanvitam  || 7.2,29.33
tathecchātmikayā śaktyā samārūḍhāṃkamaṃḍalam  || 7.2,29.34
jñānākhyayā dakṣiṇato vāmataśca kriyākhyayā  || 7.2,29.34
tattvatrayamayaṃ sākṣādvidyāmūrtiṃ sadāśivam  || 7.2,29.35
mūrtimūlena saṃkalpya sakalīkṛtya ca kramāt || 7.2,29.35
saṃpūjya ca yathānyāyamarghāntaṃ mūlavidyayā  || 7.2,29.36
mūrtimantaṃ śivaṃ sākṣācchaktyā paramayā saha  || 7.2,29.36
tatrāvāhya mahādevaṃ sadasadvyaktivarjitam  || 7.2,29.37
pañcopakaraṇaṃ kṛtvā pūjayetparameśvaram  || 7.2,29.37
brahmabhiśca ṣaḍaṅgaiśca tato mātṛkayā saha  || 7.2,29.38
praṇavena śivenaiva śaktiyuktena ca kramāt || 7.2,29.38
śāṃtena vā tathānyaiśca vedamantraiśca kṛtsnaśaḥ  || 7.2,29.39
pūjayetparamaṃ devaṃ kevalena śivena vā  || 7.2,29.39
pādyādimukhavāsāṃtaṃ kṛtvā prasthāpanaṃ vinā  || 7.2,29.40
pañcāvaraṇapūjāṃ tu hyārabheta yathākramam  || 7.2,29.40
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe kāmyakarmavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonatriṃśattamo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 30
tatrādau śivayoḥ pārśve dakṣiṇe vāmataḥ kramāt || 7.2,30.1
gaṃdhādyairarcayetpūrvaṃ devau heraṃbaṣaṇmukhau  || 7.2,30.1
tato brahmāṇi parita īśānādi yathākramam  || 7.2,30.2
saśaktikāni sadyāṃtaṃ prathamāvaraṇe yajet || 7.2,30.2
ṣaḍaṃgānyapi tatraiva hṛdayādīnyanukramāt || 7.2,30.3
śivasya ca śivāyāśca vāhneyādi samarcayet || 7.2,30.3
tatra vāmādikānrudrānaṣṭau vāmādiśaktibhiḥ  || 7.2,30.4
arcayedvā na vā paścātpūrvādiparitaḥ kramāt || 7.2,30.4
prathamāvaraṇaṃ proktaṃ mayā te yadunaṃdana  || 7.2,30.5
dvitīyāvaraṇaṃ prītyā procyate śraddhayā śṛṇu  || 7.2,30.5
anaṃtaṃ pūrvādikpatre tacchaktiṃ tasya vāmataḥ  || 7.2,30.6
sūkṣmaṃ dakṣiṇadikpatre saha śaktyā samarcayet || 7.2,30.6
tataḥ paścimadikpatre saha śaktyā śivottamam  || 7.2,30.7
tathaivottaradikpatre caikanetraṃ samarcayet || 7.2,30.7
ekarudraṃ ca tacchaktiṃ paścādīśadale 'rcayet || 7.2,30.8
trimūrtiṃ tasya śaktiṃ ca pūjayedagnidigdale  || 7.2,30.8
śrīkaṇṭhaṃ nairṛte patre tacchaktiṃ tasya vāmataḥ  || 7.2,30.9
tathaiva mārute patre śikhaṃḍīśaṃ samarcayet || 7.2,30.9
dvitīyāvaraṇe cejyāssarvartaścakravartinaḥ  || 7.2,30.10
tṛtīyāvaraṇe pūjyāḥ śaktibhiścāṣṭamūrtayaḥ  || 7.2,30.10
aṣṭasu kramaśo dikṣu pūrvādiparitaḥ kramāt || 7.2,30.11
bhavaḥ śarvastatheśāno rudraḥ paśupatistataḥ  || 7.2,30.11
ugro bhīmo mahādeva ityaṣṭau mūrtayaḥ kramāt || 7.2,30.12
anaṃtaraṃ tataścaiva mahādevādayaḥ kramāt || 7.2,30.12
śaktibhissaha saṃpūjyāstatraikādaśamūrtayaḥ  || 7.2,30.12
580

mahādevaḥ śivo rudraḥ śaṃkaro nīlalohitaḥ  || 7.2,30.13
īśāno vijayo bhīmo devadevo bhavodbhavaḥ  || 7.2,30.13
kapardīśaśca kathyaṃte tathaikādaśaśaktayaḥ  || 7.2,30.14
tatrāṣṭau prathamaṃ pūjyāḥ vāhneyādi yathākramam  || 7.2,30.14
devadevaḥ pūrvapatre īśānaṃ cāgnigocare  || 7.2,30.15
bhavodbhavastayormadhye kapālīśastataḥ param  || 7.2,30.15
tasminnāvaraṇe bhūyo vṛṣendraṃ purato yajet || 7.2,30.16
naṃdinaṃ dakṣiṇe tasya mahākālaṃ tathottare  || 7.2,30.16
śāstāraṃ vahnidikpatre mātḥrdakṣiṇadigdale  || 7.2,30.17
gajāsyaṃ nairṛte patre ṣaṇmukhaṃ vāruṇe punaḥ  || 7.2,30.17
jyeṣṭhāṃ vāyudale gaurīmuttare caṃḍamaiśvare  || 7.2,30.18
śāstṛnandīśayormadhye munīndraṃ vṛṣabhaṃ yajet || 7.2,30.18
mahākālasyottarataḥ piṃgalaṃ tu samarcayet || 7.2,30.19
śāstṛmātṛsamūhasya madhye bhṛṃgīśvaraṃ tataḥ  || 7.2,30.19
mātṛvighneśamadhye tu vīrabhadraṃ samarcayet || 7.2,30.20
skandavighneśayormadhye yajeddevīṃ sarasvatīm  || 7.2,30.20
jyeṣṭhākumārayormadhye śriyaṃ śivapadārcitām  || 7.2,30.21
jyeṣṭhāgaṇāmbayormadhye mahāmoṭīṃ samarcayet || 7.2,30.21
gaṇāmbācaṇḍayormadhye devīṃ durgāṃ prapūjayet || 7.2,30.22
atraivāvaraṇe bhūyaḥ śivānucarasaṃhatim  || 7.2,30.22
rudraprathamabhūtākhyāṃ vividhāṃ ca saśaktikām  || 7.2,30.23
śivāyāśca sakhīvargaṃ japeddhyātvā samāhitaḥ  || 7.2,30.23
evaṃ tṛtīyāvaraṇe vitate pūjite sati  || 7.2,30.24
caturthāvaraṇaṃ dhyātvā bahistasya samarcayet || 7.2,30.24
bhānuḥ pūrvadale pūjyo dakṣiṇe caturānanaḥ  || 7.2,30.25
rudro varuṇadikpatre viṣṇuruttaradigdale  || 7.2,30.25
caturṇāmapi devānāṃ pṛthagāvaraṇānyatha  || 7.2,30.26
tasyāṃgāni ṣaḍevādau dīptādyābhiśca śaktibhiḥ  || 7.2,30.26
dīptā sūkṣmā jayā bhadrā vibhūtirvimalā kramāt || 7.2,30.27
amoghā vidyutā caiva pūrvādi paritaḥ sthitāḥ  || 7.2,30.27
dvitīyāvaraṇe pūjyāścatasro mūrtayaḥ kramāt || 7.2,30.28
pūrvādyuttaraparyaṃtāḥ śaktayaśca tataḥ param  || 7.2,30.28
ādityo bhāskaro bhānū raviścetyanupūrvaśaḥ  || 7.2,30.29
arko brahmā tathā rudro viṣṇuścaite vivasvataḥ  || 7.2,30.29
vistārā pūrvadigbhāge sutarāṃ dakṣiṇe sthitāḥ  || 7.2,30.30
bodhanī paścime bhāge āpyāyinyuttare punaḥ  || 7.2,30.30
uṣāṃ prabhāṃ tathā prājñāṃ sandhyāmapi tataḥ param  || 7.2,30.31
aiśānādiṣu vinyasya dvitīyāvaraṇe yajet || 7.2,30.31
somamaṃgārakaṃ caiva budhaṃ buddhimatāṃ varam  || 7.2,30.32
bṛhaspatiṃ bṛhadbuddhiṃ bhārgavaṃ tejasāṃ nidhim  || 7.2,30.32
śanaiścaraṃ tathā rāhuṃ ketuṃ dhūmraṃ bhayaṃkaram  || 7.2,30.33
samaṃtato yajedetāṃstṛtīyāvaraṇe kramāt || 7.2,30.33
athavā dvādaśādityā dvitīyāvaraṇe yajet || 7.2,30.34
580

tṛtīyāvaraṇe caiva rāśīndvādaśa pūjayet || 7.2,30.34
saptasapta gaṇāṃścaiva bahistasya samaṃtataḥ  || 7.2,30.35
ṛṣīndevāṃśca gaṃdharvānpannagānapsarogaṇān  || 7.2,30.35
grāmaṇyaśca tathā yakṣānyātudhānāṃstathā hayān  || 7.2,30.36
saptacchaṃdomayāṃścaiva vālakhilyāṃśca pūjayet || 7.2,30.36
evaṃ tṛtīyāvaraṇe samabhyarcya divākaram  || 7.2,30.37
brahmāṇamarcayetpaścāttribhirāvaraṇaiḥ sahaḥ  || 7.2,30.37
hiraṇyagarbhaṃ pūrvasyāṃ virājaṃ dakṣiṇe tataḥ  || 7.2,30.38
kālaṃ paścimadigbhāge puruṣaṃ cottare yajet || 7.2,30.38
hiraṇyagarbhaḥ prathamo brahmā kamalasannibhaḥ  || 7.2,30.39
kālo jātyaṃjanaprakhyaḥ puruṣaḥ sphaṭikopamaḥ  || 7.2,30.39
triguṇo rājasaścaiva tāmasaḥ sāttvikastathā  || 7.2,30.40
catvāra ete kramaśaḥ prathamāvaraṇe sthitāḥ  || 7.2,30.40
dvitīyāvaraṇe pūjyāḥ pūrvādiparitaḥ kramāt || 7.2,30.41
sanatkumāraḥ sanakaḥ sanaṃdaśca sanātanaḥ  || 7.2,30.41
tṛtīyāvaraṇe paścādarcayecca prajāpatīn  || 7.2,30.42
aṣṭau pūrvāṃśca pūrvādau trīnprākpaścādanukramāt || 7.2,30.42
dakṣo rucirbhṛguścaiva marīciśca tathāṃgirāḥ  || 7.2,30.43
pulastyaḥ pulahaścaiva kraturatriśca kaśyapaḥ  || 7.2,30.43
vasiṣṭhaśceti vikhyātāḥ prajānāṃ patayastvime  || 7.2,30.44
teṣāṃ bhāryāśca taissārdhaṃ pūjanīyā yathākramam  || 7.2,30.44
prasūtiśca tathā"kūtiḥ khyātiḥ sambhūtireva ca  || 7.2,30.45
dhṛtiḥ smṛtiḥ kṣamā caiva sannatiścānasūyakā  || 7.2,30.45
devamātārundhatī ca sarvāḥ khalu pativratāḥ  || 7.2,30.46
śivārcanarato nityaṃ śrīmatyaḥ priyadarśanāḥ  || 7.2,30.46
prathamāvaraṇe vedāṃścaturo vā prapūjayet || 7.2,30.47
itihāsapurāṇāni dvitīyāvaraṇe punaḥ  || 7.2,30.47
tṛtīyāvaraṇe paścāddharmaśāstrapurassarāḥ  || 7.2,30.48
vaidikyo nikhilā vidyāḥ pūjyā eva samaṃtataḥ  || 7.2,30.48
pūrvādipurato vedāstadanye tu yathāruci  || 7.2,30.49
aṣṭadhā vā caturdhā vā kṛtvā pūjāṃ samaṃtataḥ  || 7.2,30.49
evaṃ brahmāṇamabhyarcya tribhirāvaraṇairyutam  || 7.2,30.50
dakṣiṇe paścime paścādrudraṃ sāvaraṇaṃ yajet || 7.2,30.50
tasya brahmaṣaḍaṃgāni prathamāvaraṇaṃ smṛtam  || 7.2,30.51
dvitīyāvaraṇe caiva vidyeśvaramayaṃ tathā  || 7.2,30.51
tṛtīyāvaraṇe bhedo vidyate sa tu kathyate  || 7.2,30.52
catasro mūrtayastasya pūjyāḥ pūrvāditaḥ kramāt || 7.2,30.52
triguṇāssakalo devaḥ purastācchivasaṃjñakaḥ  || 7.2,30.53
rājaso dakṣiṇe brahmā sṛṣṭikṛtpūjyate bhavaḥ  || 7.2,30.53
tāmasaḥ paścime cāgniḥ pūjyassaṃhārako haraḥ  || 7.2,30.54
sāttvikassukhakṛtsaumye viṣṇurviśvapatirmṛḍaḥ  || 7.2,30.54
evaṃ paścimadigbhāge śambhoḥ ṣaḍviṃśakaṃ śivam  || 7.2,30.55
samabhyarcyottare pārśve tato vaikuṃṭhamarcayet || 7.2,30.55
vāsudevaṃ puraskṛtvā prathamāvaraṇe yajet || 7.2,30.56
581

aniruddhaṃ dakṣiṇataḥ pradyumnaṃ paścime tataḥ  || 7.2,30.56
saumye saṃkarṣaṇaṃ paścādvyatyastau vā yajedimau  || 7.2,30.57
prathamāvaraṇaṃ proktaṃ dvitīyāvaraṇaṃ śubham  || 7.2,30.57
matsyaḥ kūrmo varāhaśca narasiṃhotha vāmanaḥ  || 7.2,30.58
rāmaścānyatamaḥ kṛṣṇo bhavānaśvamukhopi ca  || 7.2,30.58
tṛtīyāvaraṇe cakruḥ pūrvabhāge samarcayet || 7.2,30.59
nārāyaṇākhyāṃ yāmyestraṃ kvacidavyāhataṃ yajet || 7.2,30.59
paścime pāṃcajanyaṃ ca śārṅgaṃdhanurathottare  || 7.2,30.60
evaṃ tryāvaraṇaiḥ sākṣādviśvākhyāṃ paramaṃ harim  || 7.2,30.60
mahāviṣṇuṃ sadāviṣṇuṃ mūrtīkṛtya samarcayet || 7.2,30.61
itthaṃ viṣṇoścaturvyūhakramānmūrticatuṣṭayam  || 7.2,30.61
pūjayitvā ca tacchaktīścatasraḥ pujayetkramāt || 7.2,30.61
prabhāmāgneyadigbhāge nairṛte tu sarasvatīm  || 7.2,30.62
gaṇāṃbikā ca vāyavye lakṣmīṃ raudre samarcayet || 7.2,30.62
evaṃ bhānvādimūrtīnāṃ tacchaktīnāmanaṃtaram  || 7.2,30.63
pūjāṃ vidhāya lokeśāṃstatraivāvaraṇe yajet || 7.2,30.63
indramagniṃ yamaṃ caiva nirṛtiṃ varuṇaṃ tathā  || 7.2,30.64
vāyuṃ somaṃ kuberaṃ ca paścādīśānamarcayet || 7.2,30.64
evaṃ caturthāvaraṇaṃ pūjayitvā vidhānataḥ  || 7.2,30.65
āyudhāni maheśasya paścādbāṃhyaṃ samarcayet || 7.2,30.65
śrīmantriśūlamaiśāne vajraṃ māhendradiṅmukhe  || 7.2,30.66
paraśuṃ vahnidigbhāge yāmye sāyakamarcayet || 7.2,30.66
nairṛte tu yajetkhaḍgaṃ pāśaṃ vāruṇagocare  || 7.2,30.67
aṃkuśaṃ mārute bhāge pinākaṃ cottare yajet || 7.2,30.67
paścimābhimukhaṃ raudraṃ kṣetrapālaṃ samarcayet || 7.2,30.68
pañcamāvaraṇaṃ caiva sampūjyānantaraṃ bahiḥ  || 7.2,30.68
sarvāvaraṇadevānāṃ bahirvā pañcame 'thavā  || 7.2,30.69
pañcame mātṛbhissārdhaṃ mahokṣa purato yajet || 7.2,30.69
tataḥ samaṃtataḥ pūjyāssarvā vai devayonayaḥ  || 7.2,30.70
khecarā ṛṣayassiddhā daityā yakṣāśca rākṣasāḥ  || 7.2,30.70
anaṃtādyāśca nāgeṃdrā nāgaistattatkulodbhavaiḥ  || 7.2,30.71
ḍākinībhūtavetālapretabhairavanāyakāḥ  || 7.2,30.71
pātālavāsinaścānye nānāyonisamudbhavāḥ  || 7.2,30.72
nadyassamudrā girayaḥ kānanāni sarāṃsi ca  || 7.2,30.72
paśavaḥ pakṣiṇo vṛkṣāḥ kīṭādyāḥ kṣudrayonayaḥ  || 7.2,30.73
narāśca vividhākārā mṛgāśca kṣudrayonayaḥ  || 7.2,30.73
bhuvanānyantaraṇḍasya tato brahmāṇḍakoṭayaḥ  || 7.2,30.74
bahiraṃḍānyasaṃkhyāni bhuvanāni sahādhipaiḥ  || 7.2,30.74
brahmāṃḍādhārakā rudrā daśadikṣu vyavasthitāḥ  || 7.2,30.75
yadgauḍa yacca māmeyaṃ yadvā śāktaṃ tataḥ param  || 7.2,30.75
yatkiñcidasti śabdasya vācyaṃ cidacidātmakam  || 7.2,30.76
tatsarvaṃ śivayoḥ pārśve buddhvā sāmānyato yajet || 7.2,30.76
kṛtāṃjalipuṭāḥ sarve 'ciṃtyāḥ smitamukhāstathā  || 7.2,30.77
581

prītyā saṃprekṣamāṇāśca devaṃ devīṃ ca sarvadā  || 7.2,30.77
itthamāvaraṇābhyarcāṃ kṛtvāvikṣepaśāṃtaye  || 7.2,30.78
punarabhyarcya deveśaṃ paktvākṣaramudīrayet || 7.2,30.78
nivedayettataḥ paścācchivayoramṛtopamam  || 7.2,30.79
suvyañjanasamāyuktaṃ śuddhaṃ cāru mahācarum  || 7.2,30.79
dvātriṃśadāḍhakairmukhyamadhamaṃ tvāḍhakāvaram  || 7.2,30.80
sādhayitvā yathāsaṃpacchraddhayā vinivedayet || 7.2,30.80
tato nivedya pānīyaṃ tāṃbūlaṃ copadaṃśakaiḥ  || 7.2,30.81
nīrājanādikaṃ kṛtvā pūjāśeṣaṃ samāpayet || 7.2,30.81
bhogopayogyadravyāṇi viśiṣṭānyeva sādhayet || 7.2,30.82
vittaśāṭhyaṃ na kurvīta bhaktimānvibhave sati  || 7.2,30.82
śaṭhasyopekṣakasyāpi vyaṃgyaṃ caivānutiṣṭhataḥ  || 7.2,30.83
na phalaṃtyeva karmāṇi kāmyānīti satāṃ kathā  || 7.2,30.83
tasmātsamyagupekṣāṃ ca tyaktvā sarvāṃgayogataḥ  || 7.2,30.84
kuryātkāmyāni karmāṇi phalasiddhiṃ yadīcchati  || 7.2,30.84
itthaṃ pūjāṃ samāpyātha devaṃ devīṃ praṇamya ca  || 7.2,30.85
bhaktyā manassamādhāya paścātstotramudīrayet || 7.2,30.85
tataḥ stotramupāsyānte tvaṣṭottaraśatāvarām  || 7.2,30.86
japetpañcākṣarīṃ vidyāṃ sahasrottaramutsukaḥ  || 7.2,30.86
vidyāpūjāṃ guroḥ pūjāṃ kṛtvā paścādyathākramam  || 7.2,30.87
yathodayaṃ yathāśrāddhaṃ sadasyānapi pūjayet || 7.2,30.87
tataḥ udvāsya deveśaṃ sarvairāvaraṇaiḥ saha  || 7.2,30.88
maṇḍalaṃ gurave dadyādyāgopakaraṇaissaha  || 7.2,30.88
śivāśritebhyo vā dadyātsarvamevānupūrvaśaḥ  || 7.2,30.89
athavā tacchivāyaiva śivakṣetre samarpayet || 7.2,30.89
śivāgnau vā yajeddevaṃ homadravyaiśca saptabhiḥ  || 7.2,30.90
samabhyarcya yathānyāyaṃ sarvāvaraṇadevatāḥ  || 7.2,30.90
eṣa yogeśvaro nāma triṣu lokeṣu viśrutaḥ  || 7.2,30.91
na tasmādadhikaḥ kaścidyāgo 'sti bhuvane kvacit || 7.2,30.91
na tadasti jagatyasminnasadhyaṃ yadanena tu  || 7.2,30.92
aihikaṃ vā phalaṃ kiṃcidāmuṣmikaphalaṃ tu vā  || 7.2,30.92
idamasya phalaṃ nedamiti naiva niyamyate  || 7.2,30.93
śreyorūpasya kṛtsnasya tadidaṃ śreṣṭasādhanam  || 7.2,30.93
idaṃ na śakyate vaktuṃ puruṣeṇa yadarcyate  || 7.2,30.94
ciṃtāmaṇerivaitasmāttattena prāpyate phalam  || 7.2,30.94
