tathāpi kṣudramuddiśya phalaṃ naitatprayojayet || 7.2,30.95
laghvarthī mahato yasmātsvayaṃ laghutaro bhavet || 7.2,30.95
mahadvā phalamalpaṃ vā kṛtaṃ cetkarma sidhyati  || 7.2,30.96
mahādevaṃ samuddiśya kṛtaṃ karma prayujyatām  || 7.2,30.96
tasmādananyalabhyeṣu śatrumṛtyuṃjayādiṣu  || 7.2,30.97
phaleṣu dṛṣṭādṛṣṭeṣu kuryādetadvicakṣaṇaḥ  || 7.2,30.97
mahatsvapi ca pāteṣu mahārāgabhayādiṣu  || 7.2,30.98
durbhikṣādiṣu śāṃtyarthaṃ śāṃtiṃ kuryādanena tu  || 7.2,30.98
bahunā kiṃ pralāpena mahāvyāpannivārakam  || 7.2,30.99
ātmīyamastraṃ śaivānāmidamāha maheśvaraḥ  || 7.2,30.99
582

tasmāditaḥ paraṃ nāsti paritrāṇamihātmanaḥ  || 7.2,30.100
iti matvā prayuṃjānaḥ karmedaṃ śubhamaśnute  || 7.2,30.100
stotramātraṃ śucirbhūtvā yaḥ paṭhetsusamāhitaḥ  || 7.2,30.101
sopyabhīṣṭatamādarthādaṣṭāṃśaphalamāpnuyāt || 7.2,30.101
arthaṃ tasyānusandhāya parvaṇyanaśanaḥ paṭhet || 7.2,30.102
aṣṭābhyāṃ vā caturdaśyāṃ phalamardhaṃ samāpnuyāt || 7.2,30.102
yastvarthamanusaṃdhāya parvādiṣu tathā vratī  || 7.2,30.103
māsamekaṃ japetstotraṃ sa kṛtsnaṃ phalamāpnuyāt || 7.2,30.103
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe śaivānāṃ kāmyakavarṇanaṃ nāma triṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 31
upamanyuruvāca
stotraṃ vakṣyāmi te kṛṣṇa pañcāvaraṇamārgataḥ  || 7.2,31.1
yogeśvaramidaṃ puṇyaṃ karma yena samāpyate  || 7.2,31.1
jaya jaya jagadekanātha śaṃbho prakṛtimanohara nityacitsvabhāva  || 7.2,31.2
atigatakaluṣaprapañcavācāmapi manasāṃ padavīmatītatattvam  || 7.2,31.2
svabhāvanirmalābhoga jaya sundaraceṣṭita  || 7.2,31.3
svātmatulyamahāśakte jaya śuddhaguṇārṇava  || 7.2,31.3
anantakāṃtisaṃpanna jayāsadṛśavigraha  || 7.2,31.4
atarkyamahimādhāra jayānākulamaṃgala  || 7.2,31.4

tvadvinātaḥ phalaṃ dātumīśvaropi na śaknuyāt || 7.2,31.19

hiraṇyagarbho lokeśo virāṭkālaśca pūruṣaḥ  || 7.2,31.113
sanatkumāraḥ sanakaḥ sanaṃdaśca sanātanaḥ  || 7.2,31.114
prajānāṃ patayaścaiva dakṣādyā brahmasūnavaḥ  || 7.2,31.114
ekādaśa sapatnīkā dharmassaṃkalpa eva ca  || 7.2,31.115
śivārcanaratāścaite śivabhaktiparāyaṇāḥ  || 7.2,31.115
śivājñāvaśagāssarve diśaṃtu mama maṃgalam  || 7.2,31.116
catvāraśca tathā vedāssetihāsapurāṇakāḥ  || 7.2,31.116
dharmaśāstrāṇi vidyābhirvaidikībhissamanvitāḥ  || 7.2,31.117
parasparaviruddhārthāḥ śivaprakṛtipādakāḥ  || 7.2,31.117
satkṛtya śivayorājñāṃ maṃgalaṃ pradiśaṃtu me  || 7.2,31.118
atha rudro mahādevaḥ śaṃbhormūrtirgarīyasī  || 7.2,31.118
vāhneyamaṇḍalādhīśaḥ pauruṣaiśvaryavānprabhuḥ  || 7.2,31.119
śivābhimānasaṃpanno nirguṇastriguṇātmakaḥ  || 7.2,31.119
kevalaṃ sāttvikaścāpi rājasaścaiva tāmasaḥ  || 7.2,31.120
avikārarataḥ pūrvaṃ tatastu samavikriyaḥ  || 7.2,31.120
asādhāraṇakarmā ca sṛṣṭyādikaraṇātpṛthak || 7.2,31.121
brahmaṇopi śiraśchettā janakastasya tatsutaḥ  || 7.2,31.121
janakastanayaścāpi viṣṇorapi niyāmakaḥ  || 7.2,31.122
bodhakaśca tayornityamanugrahakaraḥ prabhuḥ  || 7.2,31.122
aṃḍasyāṃtarbahirvartī rudro lokadvayādhipaḥ  || 7.2,31.123
śivapriyaḥ śivāsaktaḥ śivapādārcane rataḥ  || 7.2,31.123
śivasyājñāṃ puraskṛtya sa me diśatu maṃgalam  || 7.2,31.124
tasya brahma ṣaḍaṃgāni vidyeśāṃtaṃ tathāṣṭakam  || 7.2,31.124
catvāro mūrtibhedāśca śivapūrvāḥ śivārcakāḥ  || 7.2,31.125
śivo bhavo haraścaiva mṛḍaścaiva tathāparaḥ  || 7.2,31.125
585

śivasyājñāṃ puraskṛtya maṃgalaṃ pradiśaṃtu me  || 7.2,31.125
atha viṣṇurmaheśasya śivasyaiva parā tanuḥ  || 7.2,31.126
vāritattvādhipaḥ sākṣādavyaktapadasaṃsthitaḥ  || 7.2,31.126
nirguṇassattvabahulastathaiva guṇakevalaḥ  || 7.2,31.127
avikārābhimānī ca trisādhāraṇavikriyaḥ  || 7.2,31.127
asādhāraṇakarmā ca sṛṣṭyādikaraṇātpṛthak || 7.2,31.128
dakṣiṇāṃgabhavenāpi spardhamānaḥ svayaṃbhuvā  || 7.2,31.128
ādyena brahmaṇā sākṣātsṛṣṭaḥ sraṣṭā ca tasya tu  || 7.2,31.129
aṃḍasyāṃtarbahirvartī viṣṇurlokadvayādhipaḥ  || 7.2,31.129
asurāṃtakaraścakrī śakrasyāpi tathānujaḥ  || 7.2,31.130
prādurbhūtaśca daśadhā bhṛguśāpacchalādiha  || 7.2,31.130
bhūbhāranigrahārthāya svecchayāvātarakṣitau  || 7.2,31.131
aprameyabalo māyī māyayā mohayañjagat || 7.2,31.131
mūrtiṃ kṛtvā mahāviṣṇuṃ sadāśiṣṇumathāpi vā  || 7.2,31.132
vaiṣṇavaiḥ pūjito nityaṃ mūrtitrayamayāsane  || 7.2,31.132
śivapriyaḥ śivāsaktaḥ śivapādārcane rataḥ  || 7.2,31.133
śivasyājñāṃ puraskṛtya sa me diśatu maṃgalam  || 7.2,31.133
vāsudevo 'niruddhaśca pradyumnaśca tataḥ paraḥ  || 7.2,31.134
saṃkarṣaṇassamākhyātāścatasro mūrtayo hareḥ  || 7.2,31.134
matsyaḥ kūrmo varāhaśca nārasiṃho 'tha vāmanaḥ  || 7.2,31.135
rāmatrayaṃ tathā kṛṣṇo viṣṇusturagavaktrakaḥ  || 7.2,31.135
cakraṃ nārāyaṇasyāstraṃ pāṃcajanyaṃ ca śārṅgakam  || 7.2,31.136
satkṛtya śivayorājñāṃ maṃgalaṃ pradiśaṃtu me  || 7.2,31.136
prabhā sarasvatī gaurī lakṣmīśca śivabhāvitā  || 7.2,31.137
śivayoḥ śāsanādetā maṃgalaṃ pradiśaṃtu me  || 7.2,31.137
indro 'gniśca yamaścaiva nirṛtirvaruṇastathā  || 7.2,31.138
vāyuḥ somaḥ kuberaśca tatheśānastriśūladhṛk || 7.2,31.138
sarve śivārcanaratāḥ śivasadbhāvabhāvitāḥ  || 7.2,31.139
satkṛtya śivayorājñāṃ maṃgalaṃ pradiśaṃtu me  || 7.2,31.139
triśūlamatha vajraṃ ca tathā paraśusāyakau  || 7.2,31.140
khaḍgapāśāṃkuśāścaiva pinākaścāyudhottamaḥ  || 7.2,31.140
divyāyudhāni devasya devyāścaitāni nityaśaḥ  || 7.2,31.141
satkṛtya śivayorājñāṃ rakṣāṃ kurvaṃtu me sadā  || 7.2,31.141
vṛṣarūpadharo devaḥ saurabheyo mahābalaḥ  || 7.2,31.142
vaḍavākhyānalaspardhāṃ pañcagomātṛbhirvṛtaḥ  || 7.2,31.142
vāhanatvamanuprāptastapasā parameśayoḥ  || 7.2,31.143
tayorājñāṃ puraskṛtya sa me kāmaṃ prayacchatu  || 7.2,31.143
naṃdā sunaṃdā surabhiḥ suśīlā sumanāstathā  || 7.2,31.144
pañcagomātarastvetāśśivaloke vyavasthitāḥ  || 7.2,31.144
śivabhaktiparā nityaṃ śivārcanaparāyaṇāḥ  || 7.2,31.145
śivayoḥ śāsanādeva diśaṃtu mama vāṃchitam  || 7.2,31.145
kṣetrapālo mahātejā nīla jīmūtasannibhaḥ  || 7.2,31.146
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanaḥ sphuradraktādharojjvalaḥ  || 7.2,31.146
raktordhvamūrdhajaḥ śrīmānbhrukuṭīkuṭilekṣaṇaḥ  || 7.2,31.147
585

raktavṛttatrinayanaḥ śaśipannagabhūṣaṇaḥ  || 7.2,31.147
nagnastriśūlapāśāsikapālodyatapāṇikaḥ  || 7.2,31.148
bhairavo bhairavaiḥ siddhairyoginībhiśca saṃvṛtaḥ  || 7.2,31.148
kṣetrekṣetre samāsīnaḥ sthito yo rakṣakassatām  || 7.2,31.149
śivapraṇāmaparamaḥ śivasadbhāvabhāvitaḥ  || 7.2,31.149
śivaśritānviśeṣeṇa rakṣanputrānivaurasān  || 7.2,31.150
satkṛtya śivayorājñāṃ sa me diśatu maṅgalam  || 7.2,31.150
tālajaṅghādayastasya prathamāvaraṇercitāḥ  || 7.2,31.151
satkṛtya śivayorājñāṃ catvāraḥ samavantu mām  || 7.2,31.151
bhairavādyāśca ye cānye samaṃtāttasya veṣṭitāḥ  || 7.2,31.152
te 'pi māmanugṛhṇaṃtu śivaśāsanagauravāt || 7.2,31.152
nāradādyāśca munayo divyā devaiśca pūjitāḥ  || 7.2,31.153
sādhyā māgāśca ye devā janalokanivāsinaḥ  || 7.2,31.153
vinivṛttādhikārāśca maharlokanivāsinaḥ  || 7.2,31.154
saptarṣayastathānye vai vaimānikaguṇaissaha  || 7.2,31.154
sarve śivārcanaratāḥ śivājñāvaśavartinaḥ  || 7.2,31.155
śivayorājñayā mahyaṃ diśaṃtu mama kāṃkṣitam 1  || 7.2,31.155
gaṃdharvādyāḥ piśācāṃtāścatasro devayonayaḥ  || 7.2,31.156
siddhā vidyādharādyāśca ye 'pi cānye nabhaścarāḥ  || 7.2,31.156
asurā rākṣasāścaiva pātālatalavāsinaḥ  || 7.2,31.157
anaṃtādyāśca nāgendrā vainateyādayo dvijāḥ  || 7.2,31.157
kūṣmāṃḍāḥ pretavetālā grahā bhūtagaṇāḥ pare  || 7.2,31.158
ḍākinyaścāpi yoginyaḥ śākinyaścāpi tādṛśāḥ  || 7.2,31.158
kṣetrārāmagṛhādīni tīrthānyāyatanāni ca  || 7.2,31.159
dvīpāḥ samudrā nadyaśca nadāścānye sarāṃsi ca  || 7.2,31.159
girayaśca sumervādyāḥ kananāni samaṃtataḥ  || 7.2,31.160
paśavaḥ pakṣiṇo vṛkṣāḥ kṛmikīṭādayo mṛgāḥ  || 7.2,31.160
bhuvanānyapi sarvāṇi bhuvanānāmadhīśvaraḥ  || 7.2,31.161
aṇḍānyāvaraṇaissārdhaṃ māsāśca daśa diggajāḥ  || 7.2,31.161
varṇāḥ padāni maṃtrāśca tattvānyapi sahādhipaiḥ  || 7.2,31.162
brahmāṃḍadhārakā rudrā rudrāścānye saśaktikāḥ  || 7.2,31.162
yacca kiṃcijjagatyasmindṛṣṭaṃ cānumitaṃ śrutam  || 7.2,31.163
sarve kāmaṃ prayacchantu śivayoreva śāsanāt || 7.2,31.163
atha vidyā parā śaivī paśupāśavimocinī  || 7.2,31.164
pañcārthasaṃjñitā divyā paśuvidyābahiṣkṛtā  || 7.2,31.164
śāstraṃ ca śivadharmākhyaṃ dharmākhyaṃ ca taduttaram  || 7.2,31.165
śaivākhyaṃ śivadharmākhyaṃ purāṇaṃ śrutisaṃmitam  || 7.2,31.165
śaivāgamāśca ye cānye kāmikādyāścaturvidhāḥ  || 7.2,31.166
śivābhyāmaviśeṣeṇa satkṛtyeha samarcitāḥ  || 7.2,31.166

1 sarvābhilāṣamityarthaḥ

586

tābhyāmeva samājñātā mamābhipretasiddhaye  || 7.2,31.167
karmedamanumanyaṃtāṃ saphalaṃ sādhvanuṣṭhitam  || 7.2,31.167
śvetādyā nakulīśāṃtāḥ saśiṣyāścāpi deśikāḥ  || 7.2,31.168
tatsaṃtatīyā guravo viśeṣādguravo mama  || 7.2,31.168
śaivā māheśvarāścaiva jñānakarmaparāyaṇāḥ  || 7.2,31.169
karmedamanumanyaṃtāṃ saphalaṃ sādhvanuṣṭhitam  || 7.2,31.169
laukikā brāhmaṇāssarve kṣatriyāśca viśaḥ kramāt || 7.2,31.170
vedavedāṃgatattvajñāḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradāḥ  || 7.2,31.170
sāṃkhyā vaiśeṣikāścaiva yaugā naiyāyikā narāḥ  || 7.2,31.171
saurā brahmāstathā raudrā vaiṣṇavāścāpare narāḥ  || 7.2,31.171
śiṣṭāḥ sarve viśiṣṭā ca śivaśāsanayaṃtritāḥ  || 7.2,31.172
karmedamanumanyaṃtāṃ mamābhipretasādhakam  || 7.2,31.172
śaivāḥ siddhāṃtamārgasthāḥ śaivāḥ pāśupatāstathā  || 7.2,31.173
śaivā mahāvratadharāḥ śaivāḥ kāpālikāḥ pare  || 7.2,31.173
śivājñāpālakāḥ pūjyā mamāpi śivaśāsanāt || 7.2,31.174
sarve mamānugṛhṇaṃtu śaṃsaṃtu saphalakriyām  || 7.2,31.174
dakṣiṇajñānaniṣṭhāśca dakṣiṇottaramārgagāḥ  || 7.2,31.175
avirodhena vartaṃtāṃ maṃtraśreyo 'rthino mama  || 7.2,31.175
nāstikāśca śaṭhāścaiva kṛtaghnāścaiva tāmasāḥ  || 7.2,31.176
pāṣaṃḍāścātipāpāśca vartaṃtāṃ dūrato mama  || 7.2,31.176
bahubhiḥ kiṃ stutairatra ye 'pi ke 'picidāstikāḥ  || 7.2,31.177
sarve māmanugṛhṇaṃtu saṃtaḥ śaṃsaṃtu maṃgalam  || 7.2,31.177
namaśśivāya sāṃbāya sasutāyādihetave  || 7.2,31.178
pañcāvaraṇarūpeṇa prapañcenāvṛtāya te  || 7.2,31.178
ityuktvā daṃḍavadbhūmau praṇipatya śivaṃ śivām  || 7.2,31.179
japetpañcākṣarīṃ vidyāmaṣṭottaraśatāvarām  || 7.2,31.179
tathaiva śaktividyāṃ ca japitvā tatsamarpaṇam  || 7.2,31.180
kṛtvā taṃ kṣamayitveśaṃ pūjāśeṣaṃ samāpayet || 7.2,31.180
etatpuṇyatamaṃ stotraṃ śivayorhṛdayaṃgamam  || 7.2,31.181
sarvābhīṣṭapradaṃ sākṣādbhuktimuktyaikasādhanam  || 7.2,31.181
ya idaṃ kīrtayennityaṃ śṛṇuyādvā samāhitaḥ  || 7.2,31.182
sa vidhūyāśu pāpāni śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt || 7.2,31.182
goghnaścaiva kṛtaghnaśca vīrahā bhrūṇahāpi vā  || 7.2,31.183
śaraṇāgataghātī ca mitraviśraṃbhaghātakaḥ  || 7.2,31.183
duṣṭapāpasamācāro mātṛhā pitṛhāpi vā  || 7.2,31.184
stavenānena japtena tattatpāpātpramucyate  || 7.2,31.184
duḥsvapnādimahānarthasūcakeṣu bhayeṣu ca  || 7.2,31.185
yadi saṃkīrtayedetanna tato nārthabhāgbhavet || 7.2,31.185
āyurārogyamaiśvaryaṃ yaccānyadapi vāñchitam  || 7.2,31.186
stotrasyāsya jape tiṣṭhaṃstatsarvaṃ labhate naraḥ  || 7.2,31.186
asaṃpūjya śivastotraṃ japātphalamudāhṛtam  || 7.2,31.187
saṃpūjya ca jape tasya phalaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyate  || 7.2,31.187
586

āstāmiyaṃ phalāvāptirasminsaṃkīrtite sati  || 7.2,31.188
sārdhamaṃbikayā devaḥ śrutyaivaṃ divi tiṣṭhati  || 7.2,31.188
tasmānnabhasi saṃpūjya devaṃ devaṃ sahomayā  || 7.2,31.189
kṛtāṃjalipuṭastiṣṭhaṃstotrametadudīrayet || 7.2,31.189
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe śivamahāstotravarṇanaṃ nāmaikatriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 32
upamanyuruvāca
etatte kathitaṃ kṛṣṇa karmehāmutra siddhidam  || 7.2,32.1
kriyātapojapadhyānasamuccayamayaṃ param  || 7.2,32.1
atha vakṣyāmi śaivānāmihaiva phaladaṃ nṛṇām  || 7.2,32.2
pūjāhomajapadhyānatapodānamayaṃ mahat || 7.2,32.2
tatra saṃsādhayetpūrvaṃ mantraṃ mantrārthavittamaḥ  || 7.2,32.3
dṛṣṭasiddhikaraṃ karma nānyathā phaladaṃ yataḥ  || 7.2,32.3
siddhamantro 'pyadṛṣṭena prabalena tu kenacit || 7.2,32.4
pratibandhaphalaṃ karma na kuryātsahasā budhaḥ  || 7.2,32.4
tasya tu pratibandhasya kartuṃ śakyeha niṣkṛtiḥ  || 7.2,32.5
parīkṣya śakunādyaistadādau niṣkṛtimācaret || 7.2,32.5
yo 'nyathā kurute mohātkarmaihikaphalaṃ naraḥ  || 7.2,32.6
na tena phalabhāksa syātprāpnuyāccopahāsyatām  || 7.2,32.6
abisrabdho na kurvīta karma dṛṣṭaphalaṃ kvacit || 7.2,32.7
sa khalvaśraddhadhānaḥ syānnāśraddhaḥ phalamṛcchati  || 7.2,32.7
nāparādhosti devasya karmaṇyapi tu niṣphale  || 7.2,32.8
yathoktakāriṇāṃ puṃsāmihaiva phaladarśanāt || 7.2,32.8
sādhakaḥ siddhamaṃtraśca nirastapratibaṃdhakaḥ  || 7.2,32.9
viśvastaḥ śraddhadhānaśca kurvannāpnoti tatphalam  || 7.2,32.9
athavā tatphalāvāptyai brahmacaryarato bhavet || 7.2,32.10
rātrau haviṣyamaśnīyātpāyasaṃ vā phalāni vā  || 7.2,32.10
hiṃsādi yanniṣiddhaṃ syānna kuryānmanasāpi tat || 7.2,32.11
sadā bhasmānuliptāṃ gassuveṣaśca śucirbhavet || 7.2,32.11
itthamācāravānbhūtvā svānukūle śubhe 'hani  || 7.2,32.12
pūrvoktalakṣaṇe deśe puṣpadāmādyalaṃkṛte  || 7.2,32.12
ālipya śakṛtā 1 bhūmiṃ hastamānāvarāṃ yathā  || 7.2,32.13
vilikhetkamale bhadre dīpyamānaṃ svatejasā  || 7.2,32.13
taptajāṃbūnadamayamaṣṭapatraṃ sakesaram  || 7.2,32.14
madhye karṇikayā yuktaṃ sarvaratnairalaṃkṛtam  || 7.2,32.14
svākārasadṛśenaiva nālena ca samanvitam  || 7.2,32.15
tādṛśe svarṇanirmāṇe kaṃde samyagvidhānataḥ  || 7.2,32.15
tatrāṇimādikaṃ sarvaṃ saṃkalpya manasā punaḥ  || 7.2,32.16
ratnajaṃ vātha sauvarṇaṃ sphaṭikaṃ vā salakṣaṇam  || 7.2,32.16
liṅgaṃ savedikaṃ caiva sthāpayitvā vidhānataḥ  || 7.2,32.16
tatrāvāhya yajeddevaṃ sāṃbaṃ sagaṇamavyayam  || 7.2,32.17
tatra māheśvarī kalpyā mūrtirmūrtimataḥ prabhoḥ  || 7.2,32.17

1 goriti śeṣaḥ

587

caturbhujā caturvaktrā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā  || 7.2,32.18
śārdūlacarmavasanā kiṃcidvihasitānanā  || 7.2,32.18
varadābhayahastā ca mṛgaṭaṃkadharā tathā  || 7.2,32.19
atha vāṣṭabhujā ciṃtyā ciṃtakasya yathāruci  || 7.2,32.19
tadā triśūlaparaśukhaḍgavajrāṇi dakṣiṇe  || 7.2,32.20
vāme pāśāṃkuśau tadvatkheṭaṃ nāgaṃ ca bibhratī  || 7.2,32.20
bālārkasadṛśaprakhyā prativaktraṃ trilocanā  || 7.2,32.21
tasyāḥ pūrvamukhaṃ saumyaṃ svākārasadṛśaprabham  || 7.2,32.21
dakṣiṇaṃ nīlajīmūtasadṛśaṃ ghoradarśanam  || 7.2,32.22
uttaraṃ vidrumaprakhyaṃ nīlālakavibhūṣitam  || 7.2,32.22
paścimaṃ pūrṇacaṃdrābhaṃ saumyamiṃdukalādharam  || 7.2,32.23
tadaṃkamaṃḍalārūḍhā śaktirmāheśvarī parā  || 7.2,32.23
mahālakṣmīriti khyātā śyāmā sarvamanoharā  || 7.2,32.24
mūrtiṃ kṛtvaivamākārāṃ sakalīkṛtya ca kramāt || 7.2,32.24
mūrtimaṃtamathāvāhya yajetparamakāraṇam  || 7.2,32.25
snānārthe kalpayettatra pañcagavyaṃ tu kāpilam  || 7.2,32.25
pañcāmṛtaṃ ca pūrṇāni bījāni ca viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,32.26
purastānmaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā ratnacūrṇādyalaṃkṛtam  || 7.2,32.26
karṇikāyāṃ pravinyasyedīśānakalaśaṃ punaḥ  || 7.2,32.27
sadyādikalaśānpaścātparitastasya kalpayet || 7.2,32.27
tato vidyeśakalaśānaṣṭau pūrvādivatkramāt || 7.2,32.28
tīrthāmbupūritānkṛtvā sūtreṇāveṣṭya pūrvavat || 7.2,32.28
puṇyadravyāṇi nikṣipya samantraṃ savidhānakam  || 7.2,32.29
dukūlādyena vastreṇa samācchādya samaṃtataḥ  || 7.2,32.29
sarvatra maṃtraṃ vinyasya tattanmaṃtrapurassaram  || 7.2,32.30
snānakāle tu saṃprāpte sarvamaṅgalanisvanaiḥ  || 7.2,32.30
pañcagavyādibhiścaiva snāpayetparameśvaram  || 7.2,32.31
tataḥ kuśodakādyāni svarṇaratnodakānyapi  || 7.2,32.31
gaṃdhapuṣpādisiddhāni mantrasiddhāni ca kramāt || 7.2,32.32
uddhṛtyoddhṛtya mantreṇa taistaissnāpya maheśvaram  || 7.2,32.32
gaṃdhaṃ puṣpādidīpāṃśca pūjākarma samācaret || 7.2,32.33
palāvaraḥ syādālepa ekādaśapalottaraḥ  || 7.2,32.33
suvarṇaratnapuṣpāṇi śubhāni surabhīṇi ca  || 7.2,32.34
nīlotpalādyutpalāni bilvapatrāṇyanekaśaḥ  || 7.2,32.34
kamalāni ca raktāni śvetānyapi ca śaṃbhave  || 7.2,32.35
kṛṣṇāgurūdbhavo dhūpaḥ sakarpūrājyaguggulaḥ  || 7.2,32.35
kapilāghṛtasaṃsiddhā dīpāḥ karpūravartijāḥ  || 7.2,32.36
pañcabrahmaṣaḍaṃgāni pūjyānyāvaraṇāni ca  || 7.2,32.36
naivedyaḥ payasā siddhaḥ sa guḍājyo mahācaruḥ  || 7.2,32.37
pāṭalotpalapadmādyaiḥ pānīyaṃ ca sugandhitam  || 7.2,32.37
pañcasaugaṃdhikopetaṃ tāṃbūlaṃ ca susaṃskṛtam  || 7.2,32.38
suvarṇaratnasiddhāni bhūṣaṇāni viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,32.38
587

vāsāṃsi ca vicitrāṇi sūkṣmāṇi ca navāni ca  || 7.2,32.39
darśanīyāni deyāni gānavādyādibhissaha  || 7.2,32.39
japaśca mūlamaṃtrasya lakṣaḥ paramasaṃkhyayā  || 7.2,32.40
ekāvarā tryuttarā ca pūjā phalavaśādiha  || 7.2,32.40
daśasaṃkhyāvaro homaḥ pratidravyaṃ śatottaraḥ  || 7.2,32.41
ghorarūpaśśivaściṃtyo māraṇoccāṭanādiṣu  || 7.2,32.41
śivaliṃge śivāgnau ca hyanyāsu pratimāsu ca  || 7.2,32.42
ciṃtyassaumyatanuśśaṃbhuḥ kārye śāṃtikapauṣṭike  || 7.2,32.42
āyasau sruksruvau kāryau māraṇādiṣu karmasu  || 7.2,32.43
tadanyatra tu sauvarṇau śāṃtikādyeṣu kṛtsnaśaḥ  || 7.2,32.43
dūrvayā ghṛtagokṣīramiśrayā madhunā tathā  || 7.2,32.44
caruṇā saghṛtenaiva kevalaṃ payasāpi vā  || 7.2,32.44
juhuyānmṛtyuvijaye tilai rogopaśāṃtaye  || 7.2,32.45
ghṛtena payasā caiva kamalairvātha kevalaiḥ  || 7.2,32.45
samṛddhikāmo juhuyānmahādāridryaśāṃtaye  || 7.2,32.46
jātīpuṣpeṇa vaśyārthī juhuyātsaghṛtena tu  || 7.2,32.46
ghṛtena karavīraiśca kuryādākarṣaṇaṃ dvijaḥ  || 7.2,32.47
tailenoccāṭanaṃ kuryātstaṃbhanaṃ madhunā punaḥ  || 7.2,32.47
staṃbhanaṃ sarṣapeṇāpi laśunena tu pātanam  || 7.2,32.48
tāḍanaṃ rudhireṇa syātkharasyoṣṭrasya cobhayoḥ  || 7.2,32.48
māraṇoccāṭane kuryādrohibījaistilānvitaiḥ  || 7.2,32.49
vidveṣaṇaṃ ca tailena kuryāllāṃgalakasya tu  || 7.2,32.49
baṃdhanaṃ rohibījena senāstaṃbhanameva ca  || 7.2,32.50
raktasarṣapasaṃmiśrairhomadravyairaśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,32.50
hastayaṃtrodbhavaistailairjuhuyādābhicārike  || 7.2,32.51
kaṭukītuṣasaṃyuktaiḥ kārpāsāsthibhireva ca  || 7.2,32.51
sarṣapaistailasaṃmiśrairjuhuyādābhicārike  || 7.2,32.52
jvaropaśāṃtidaṃ kṣīraṃ saubhāgyaphaladaṃ tathā  || 7.2,32.52
sarvasiddhikaro homaḥ kṣaudrājyadadhibhiryutaiḥ  || 7.2,32.53
kṣīreṇa taṃdulaiścaiva caruṇā kevalena vā  || 7.2,32.53
śāṃtikaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ vāpi saptabhiḥ samidādibhiḥ  || 7.2,32.54
dravyairviśeṣato home vaśyamākarṣaṇaṃ tathā  || 7.2,32.54
vaśyamākarṣaṇaṃ caiva śrīpadaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,32.55
bilvapatraistu havanaṃ śatrorvijayadaṃ tathā  || 7.2,32.55
samidhaḥ śāṃtikāryeṣu pālāśakhadirādikāḥ  || 7.2,32.56
karavīrārkajāḥ kraurye kaṇṭakinyaśca vigrahe  || 7.2,32.56
praśāṃtaḥ śāṃtikaṃ kuryātpauṣṭikaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,32.57
nirghṛṇaḥ kruddhacittastu prakuryādābhicārikam  || 7.2,32.57
atīvaduravasthāyāṃ pratīkārāṃtaraṃ na cet || 7.2,32.58
ātatāyinamuddiśya prakuryādābhicārikam  || 7.2,32.58
svarāṣṭrapatimuddiśya na kuryādābhicārikam  || 7.2,32.59
yadyāstikassudharmiṣṭho mānyo vā yo 'pi kopi vā  || 7.2,32.59
tamuddiśyāpi no kuryādātatāyinamapyuta  || 7.2,32.60
manasā karmaṇā vācā yo 'pi kopi śivāśritaḥ  || 7.2,32.60
588

svarāṣṭrapatimuddiśya śivā śritamathāpi vā  || 7.2,32.61
kṛtvābhicārikaṃ karma sadyo vinipatennaraḥ  || 7.2,32.61
svarāṣṭrapālakaṃ tasmācchivabhaktaṃ ca kañcana  || 7.2,32.62
na hiṃsyādabhicārādyairyadīcchetsukhamātmanaḥ  || 7.2,32.62
anyaṃ kamapi coddiśya kṛtvā vai māraṇādikam  || 7.2,32.63
paścāttāpena saṃyuktaḥ prāyaścittaṃ samācaret || 7.2,32.63
bāṇaliṃge 'pi vā kuryānnirdhano dhanavānapi  || 7.2,32.64
svayaṃbhūte 'tha vā liṃge ārṣake vaidike 'pi vā  || 7.2,32.64
abhāve hemaratnānāmaśaktau ca tadarjane  || 7.2,32.65
manasaivācaredetaddravyairvā pratirūpakaiḥ  || 7.2,32.65
kvacidaṃśe tu yaḥ śaktastvaśaktaḥ kvacidaṃśake  || 7.2,32.66
so 'pi śaktyanusāreṇa kurvaṃścetphalamṛcchati  || 7.2,32.66
karmaṇyanuṣṭhite 'pyasminphalaṃ yatra na dṛśyate  || 7.2,32.67
dvistrirvāvartayettatra sarvathā dṛśyate phalam  || 7.2,32.67
pūjopayuktaṃ yaddravyaṃ hemaratnādyanuttamam  || 7.2,32.68
tatsarvaṃ gurave dadyāddakṣiṇāṃ ca tataḥ pṛthak || 7.2,32.68
sa cennecchati tatsarvaṃ śivāya vinivedayet || 7.2,32.69
athavā śivabhaktebhyo nānyebhyastu pradīyate  || 7.2,32.69
yaḥ svayaṃ sādhayecchaktyā gurvādinirapekṣayā  || 7.2,32.70
so 'pyevamācaredatra na gṛhṇīyātsvayaṃ punaḥ  || 7.2,32.70
svayaṃ gṛhṇāti yo lobhātpūjāṃgadravyamuttamam  || 7.2,32.71
kāṃkṣitaṃ na labhenmūḍho nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 7.2,32.71
arcitaṃ yattu talliṃgaṃ gṛhṇīyādvā navā svayam  || 7.2,32.72
gṛhṇīyādyadi tannityaṃ svayaṃ vānyo 'pi vārcayet || 7.2,32.72
yathoktameva karmaitadācaredyo 'napāyataḥ  || 7.2,32.73
phalaṃ vyabhicarennaivamityataḥ kiṃ prarocakam  || 7.2,32.73
tathāpyuddeśato vakṣye karmaṇaḥ siddhimuttamam  || 7.2,32.74
api śatrubhirākrāṃto vyādhibhirvāpyanekaśaḥ  || 7.2,32.74
mṛtyorāsyagataścāpi mucyate nirapāyataḥ  || 7.2,32.75
pūjāyate 'tikṛpaṇo rikto vaiśravaṇāyate  || 7.2,32.75
kāmāyate virūpo 'pi vṛddho 'pi taruṇāyate  || 7.2,32.76
śatrurmitrāyate sadyo virodhī kiṃkarāyate  || 7.2,32.76
viṣāyate yadamṛtaṃ viṣamapyamṛtāyate  || 7.2,32.77
sthalāyate samudro 'pi sthalamapyarṇavāyate  || 7.2,32.77
mahīdharāyate śvabhraṃ sa ca śvabhrāyate giriḥ  || 7.2,32.78
padmākarāyate vahniḥ saro vaiśvānarāyate  || 7.2,32.78
vanāyate yadudyānaṃ tadudyānāyate vanam  || 7.2,32.79
siṃhāyate mṛgaḥ kṣudraḥ siṃhaḥ krīḍāmṛgāyate  || 7.2,32.79
striyo 'bhisārikāyante lakṣmīḥ sucaritāyate  || 7.2,32.80
svairapreṣyāyate vāṇī kīrtistu gaṇikāyate  || 7.2,32.80
svairācārāyate medhā vajrasūcīyate manaḥ  || 7.2,32.81
mahāvātāyate śaktirbalaṃ mattagajāyate  || 7.2,32.81
588

stambhāyate samudyogaiḥ śatrupakṣe sthitā kriyā  || 7.2,32.82
śatrupakṣāyate 'rīṇāṃ sarva eva suhṛjjanaḥ  || 7.2,32.82
śatravaḥ kuṇapāyante jīvantopi sabāṃdhavāḥ  || 7.2,32.83
āpanno 'pi gatāriṣṭaḥ svayaṃ khalvamṛtāyate  || 7.2,32.83
rasāya nāyate nityamapathyamapi sevitam  || 7.2,32.84
aniśaṃ kriyamāṇāpi ratistvabhinavāyate  || 7.2,32.84
anāgatādikaṃ sarvaṃ karasthāmalakāyate  || 7.2,32.85
yādṛcchikaphalāyante siddhayo 'pyaṇimādayaḥ  || 7.2,32.85
bahunātra kimuktena sarvakāmārthasiddhiṣu  || 7.2,32.86
asminkarmaṇi nirvṛtte tvanavāpyaṃ na vidyate  || 7.2,32.86
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe aihikasiddhikarmavarṇanaṃ nāma dvātriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 33
upamanyuruvāca
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi kevalāmuṣmikaṃ vidhim  || 7.2,33.1
naitena sadṛśaṃ kiṃcitkarmāsti bhuvanatraye  || 7.2,33.1
puṇyātiśayasaṃyuktaḥ sarvairdevairanuṣṭhitaḥ  || 7.2,33.2
brahmaṇā viṣṇunā caiva rudreṇa ca viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,33.2
iṃdrādilokapāraiśca sūryādyairnavabhirgrahaiḥ  || 7.2,33.3
viśvāmitravasiṣṭhādyairbrahmavidbhirmaharṣibhiḥ  || 7.2,33.3
śvetāgastyadadhīcādyairasmābhiśca śivāśritaiḥ  || 7.2,33.4
naṃdīśvaramahākālabhṛṃgīśādyairgaṇeśvaraiḥ  || 7.2,33.4
pātālavāsibhirdaityaiḥ śeṣādyaiśca mahoragaiḥ  || 7.2,33.5
siddhairyakṣaiśca gaṃdharvai rakṣobhūtapiśācakaiḥ  || 7.2,33.5
svaṃsvaṃ padamanuprāptaṃ sarvairayamanuṣṭhitaḥ  || 7.2,33.6
anena vidhinā sarve devā devatvamāgatāḥ  || 7.2,33.6
brahmā brahmatvamāpanno viṣṇurviṣṇutvamāgataḥ  || 7.2,33.7
rudro rudratvamāpanna iṃdraścendratvamāgataḥ  || 7.2,33.7
gaṇeśaśca gaṇeśatvamanena vidhinā gataḥ  || 7.2,33.8
sitacaṃdanatoyena liṃgaṃ snāpya śivaṃ śivām  || 7.2,33.8
śvetairvikasitaiḥ padmaiḥ saṃpūjya praṇipatya ca  || 7.2,33.8
tatra padmāsanaṃ ramyaṃ kṛtvā lakṣaṇasaṃyutam  || 7.2,33.9
vibhave sati hemādyai ratnādyairvā svaśaktitaḥ  || 7.2,33.9
madhye kesarajālāsya sthāpya liṃgaṃ kanīyasam  || 7.2,33.10
aṃguṣṭhapratimaṃ ramyaṃ sarvagandhamayaṃ śubham  || 7.2,33.10
dakṣiṇe sthāpayitvā tu bilvapatraiḥ samarcayet || 7.2,33.11
aguruṃ dakṣiṇe pārśve paścime tu manaḥśilām  || 7.2,33.11
uttare caṃdanaṃ dadyāddharitālaṃ tu pūrvataḥ  || 7.2,33.12
sugandhaiḥ kusumai ramyairvicitraiścāpi pūjayet || 7.2,33.12
dhūpaṃ kṛṣṇāguruṃ dadyātsarvataśca saguggulam  || 7.2,33.13
vāsāṃsi cātisūkṣmāṇi vikāśāni nivedayet || 7.2,33.13
pāyasaṃ ghṛtasaṃmiśraṃ ghṛtadīpāṃśca dāpayet || 7.2,33.14
sarvaṃ nivedya mantreṇa tato gacchetpradakṣiṇām  || 7.2,33.14
praṇamya bhaktyā deveśaṃ stutvā cānte kṣamāpayet || 7.2,33.15
sarvopahārasaṃmiśraṃ tato liṃgaṃ nivedayet || 7.2,33.15
śivāya śivamantreṇa dakṣiṇāmūrtimāśritaḥ  || 7.2,33.16
evaṃ yo 'rcayate nityaṃ pañcagandhamayaṃ śubham  || 7.2,33.16
589

sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ śivaloke mahīyate  || 7.2,33.17
etadvratottamaṃ guhyaṃ śivaliṃgamahāvratam  || 7.2,33.17
bhaktasya te samākhyātaṃ na deyaṃ yasya kasyacit || 7.2,33.18
deyaṃ ca śivabhaktebhyaḥ śivena kathitaṃ purā  || 7.2,33.18
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe āmuṣmikakarmavidhivarṇanaṃ nāma trayastriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 34
upamanyuruvāca
nityanaimittikātkāmyādyā siddhiriha kīrtitā  || 7.2,34.1
sā sarvā labhyeta sadyo liṃgaberapratiṣṭhayā  || 7.2,34.1
sarvo liṃgamayo lokassarvaṃ liṃge pratiṣṭhitam  || 7.2,34.2
tasmātpratiṣṭhite liṃge bhavetsarvaṃ patiṣṭhitam  || 7.2,34.2
brahmaṇā viṣṇunā vāpi rudreṇānyena kena vā  || 7.2,34.3
liṃgapratiṣṭhāmutsṛjya kriyate svapadasthitiḥ  || 7.2,34.3
kimanyadiha vaktavyaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ prati kāraṇam  || 7.2,34.4
partiṣṭhitaṃ śivenāpi liṃgaṃ vaiśveśvaraṃ yataḥ  || 7.2,34.4
tasmātsarvaprayatnena paratreha ca śarmaṇe  || 7.2,34.5
sthāpayetparameśasya liṃgaṃ beramathāpi vā  || 7.2,34.5
śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca
kimidaṃ liṃgamākhyātaṃ kathaṃ liṃgī maheśvaraḥ  || 7.2,34.6
kathaṃ ca liṃgabhāvo 'sya kasmādasmiñchivo 'rcyate  || 7.2,34.6
upamanyuruvāca
avyaktaṃ liṃgamākhyātaṃ triguṇaprabhavāpyayam  || 7.2,34.7
anādyanaṃtaṃ viśvasya yadupādānakāraṇam  || 7.2,34.7
tadeva mūlaprakṛtirmāyā ca gaganātmikā  || 7.2,34.8
tata eva samutpannaṃ jagadetaccarācaram  || 7.2,34.8
aśuddhaṃ caiva śuddhaṃ yacchuddhāśuddhaṃ ca tattridhā  || 7.2,34.9
tataḥ śivo maheśaśca rudro viṣṇuḥ pitāmahaḥ  || 7.2,34.9
bhūtāni cendriyairjātā līyante 'tra śivājñayā  || 7.2,34.10
ata eva śivo liṃgo liṃgamājñāpayedyataḥ  || 7.2,34.10
yato na tadanājñātaṃ kāryāya prabhavetsvataḥ  || 7.2,34.11
tato jātasya viśvasya tatraiva vilayo yataḥ  || 7.2,34.11
anena liṃgatāṃ tasya bhavennānyena kenacit || 7.2,34.12
liṃgaṃ ca śivayordehastābhyāṃ yasmādadhiṣṭhitam  || 7.2,34.12
atastatra śivaḥ sāmbo nityameva samarcayet || 7.2,34.13
liṃgavedī mahādevī liṃgaṃ sākṣānmaheśvaraḥ  || 7.2,34.13
tayoḥ saṃpūjanādeva sa ca sā ca samarcitau  || 7.2,34.14
na tayorliṃgadehatvaṃ vidyate paramārthataḥ  || 7.2,34.14
yatastvetau viśuddhau tau dehastadupacārataḥ  || 7.2,34.15
tadeva paramā śaktiḥ śivasya paramātmanaḥ  || 7.2,34.15
śaktirājñāṃ yadādatte prasūte taccarācaram  || 7.2,34.16
na tasya mahimā śakyo vaktuṃ varṣaśatairapi  || 7.2,34.16
yenādau mohitau syātāṃ brahmanārāyaṇāvapi  || 7.2,34.17
purā tribhuvanasyāsya pralaye samupasthite  || 7.2,34.17
vāriśayyāgato viṣṇuḥ suṣvāpānākulaḥ sukham  || 7.2,34.18
589

yadṛcchayā gatastatra brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ  || 7.2,34.18
dadarśa puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ svapantaṃ tamanākulam  || 7.2,34.19
māyayā mohitaḥ śambhorviṣṇumāha pitāmahaḥ  || 7.2,34.19
kastvaṃ vadetyamarṣeṇa prahṛtyotthāpya mādhavam  || 7.2,34.20
sa tu hastaprahāreṇa tīvreṇābhihataḥ kṣaṇāt || 7.2,34.20
prabuddhotthāya śayanāddadarśa parameṣṭhinam  || 7.2,34.21
tamāha cāṃtassaṃkruddhaḥ svayamakruddhavaddhariḥ  || 7.2,34.21
kutastvamāgato vatsa kasmāttvaṃ vyākulo vada  || 7.2,34.22
iti viṣṇuvacaḥ śrutvā prabhutvaguṇasūcakam  || 7.2,34.22
rajasā baddhavairastaṃ brahmā punarabhāṣata  || 7.2,34.23
vatseti māṃ kuto brūṣe guruḥ śiṣyamivātmanaḥ  || 7.2,34.23
māṃ na jānāsi kiṃ nāthaṃ prapañco yasya me kṛtiḥ  || 7.2,34.24
tridhātmānaṃ vibhajyedaṃ sṛṣṭvātha paripālyate  || 7.2,34.24
saṃharāmi name kaścitsraṣṭā jagati vidyate  || 7.2,34.25
ityukte sati so 'pyāha brahmāṇaṃ viṣṇuravyayaḥ  || 7.2,34.25
ahamevādikartāsya hartā ca paripālakaḥ  || 7.2,34.26
bhavānapi mamaivāṃgādavatīrṇaḥ purāvyayāt || 7.2,34.26
manniyogāttvamātmānaṃ tridhā kṛtvā jagattrayam  || 7.2,34.27
sṛjasyavasi cāṃte tatpunaḥ pratisṛjasyapi  || 7.2,34.27
vismṛtosi jagannāthaṃ nārāyaṇamanāmayam  || 7.2,34.28
tavāpi janakaṃ sākṣānmāmevamavamanyase  || 7.2,34.28
tavāparādho nāstyatra bhrāṃtosi mama māyayā  || 7.2,34.29
matprasādādiyaṃ bhrāṃtirapaiṣyati tavācirāt || 7.2,34.29
śṛṇu satyaṃ caturvaktra sarvadeveśvaro hyaham  || 7.2,34.30
kartā bhartā ca hartā ca na mayāsti samo vibhuḥ  || 7.2,34.30
evameva vivādobhūdbrahmaviṣṇvoḥ parasparam  || 7.2,34.31
abhavacca mahāyuddhaṃ bhairavaṃ romaharṣaṇam  || 7.2,34.31
muṣṭibhirnnighnatostīvraṃ rajasā baddhavairayoḥ  || 7.2,34.32
tayordarpāpahārāya prabodhāya ca devayoḥ  || 7.2,34.32
madhye samāvirabhavalliṃgamaiśvaramadbhutam  || 7.2,34.33
jvālāmālāsahasrāḍhyamaprameyamanaupamam  || 7.2,34.33
kṣayavṛddhivinirmuktamādimadhyāṃtavarjitam  || 7.2,34.34
tasya jvālāsahasreṇa brahmaviṣṇū vimohitau  || 7.2,34.34
visṛjya yuddhaṃ kiṃ tvetadityaciṃtayatāṃ tadā  || 7.2,34.35
na tayostasya yāthātmyaṃ prabuddhamabhavadyadā  || 7.2,34.35
tadā samudyatau syātāṃ tasyādyaṃtaṃ parīkṣitum  || 7.2,34.36
tatra haṃsākṛtirbrahmā viśvataḥ pakṣasaṃyutaḥ  || 7.2,34.37
manonilajavo bhūtvā gatastūrdhvaṃ prayatnataḥ  || 7.2,34.37
nārāyaṇopi viśvātmā līlāñjanacayopamam  || 7.2,34.38
vārāhamamitaṃ rūpamasthāya gatavānadhaḥ  || 7.2,34.38
evaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu tvaran viṣṇuradhogataḥ  || 7.2,34.39
nāpaśyadalpamapyasya mūlaṃ liṃgasya sūkaraḥ  || 7.2,34.39
tāvatkālaṃ gataścordhvaṃ tasyāṃtaṃ jñātumicchayā  || 7.2,34.40
śrāṃtotyaṃtamadṛṣṭvāṃtaṃ pāpatādhaḥ pitāmahaḥ  || 7.2,34.40
590

tathaiva bhagavān viṣṇuḥ śrāṃtaḥ saṃvignalocanaḥ  || 7.2,34.41
kleśena mahatā tūrṇamadhastādutthito 'bhavat || 7.2,34.41
samāgatāvathānyonyaṃ vismayasmeravīkṣaṇau  || 7.2,34.42
māyayā mohitau śaṃbhoḥ kṛtyākṛtyaṃ na jagmatuḥ  || 7.2,34.42
pṛṣṭhataḥ pārśvatastasya cāgrataśca sthitāvubhau  || 7.2,34.43
praṇipatya kimātmedamityaciṃtayatāṃ tadā  || 7.2,34.43
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe harividhimohavarṇanaṃ nāma catustriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 35
upamanyuruvāca
athāvirabhavattatra sanādaṃ śabdalakṣaṇam  || 7.2,35.1
omityekākṣaraṃ brahma brahmaṇaḥ pratipādakam  || 7.2,35.1
tadapyaviditaṃ tāvadbrahmaṇā viṣṇunā tathā  || 7.2,35.2
rajasā tamasā cittaṃ tayoryasmāttiraskṛtam  || 7.2,35.2
tadā vibhaktamabhavaccaturdhaikaṃ tadakṣaram  || 7.2,35.3
a u meti trimātrābhiḥ parastāccārdhamātrayā  || 7.2,35.3
tatrākāraḥ śrito bhāge jvalalliṃgasya dakṣiṇe  || 7.2,35.4
ukāraścottare tadvanmakārastasya madhyataḥ  || 7.2,35.4
ardhamātrātmako nādaḥ śrūyate liṃgamūrdhani  || 7.2,35.5
vibhakte 'pi tadā tasminpraṇave paramākṣare  || 7.2,35.5
vibhāvārthaṃ ca tau devau na kiṃcidavajagmatuḥ  || 7.2,35.6
vedātmanā tadāvyaktaḥ praṇavo vikṛtiṃ gataḥ  || 7.2,35.6
tatrākāro ṛgabhavadukāro yajuravyayaḥ  || 7.2,35.7
makārassāma saṃjāto nādastvātharvaṇī śrutiḥ  || 7.2,35.7
ṛgayaṃ sthāpayāmāsa samāsāttvarthamātmanaḥ  || 7.2,35.8
rajoguṇeṣu brahmāṇaṃ mūrtiṣvādyaṃ kriyāsvapi  || 7.2,35.8
sṛṣṭiṃ lokeṣu pṛthivīṃ tattveṣvātmānamavyayam  || 7.2,35.9
kalādhvani nivṛttiṃ ca sadyaṃ brahmasu pañcasu  || 7.2,35.9
liṃgabhāgeṣvadhobhāgaṃ bījākhyaṃ kāraṇatraye  || 7.2,35.10
catuḥṣaṣṭiguṇaiśvaryaṃ bauddhaṃ yadaṇimādiṣu  || 7.2,35.10
taditthamarthairdaśabhirvyāptaṃ viśvamṛcā jagat || 7.2,35.11
athopasthāpayāmāsa svārthaṃ daśavidhaṃ yajuḥ  || 7.2,35.11
sattvaṃ guṇeṣu viṣṇuṃ ca mūrtiṣvādyaṃ kriyāsvapi  || 7.2,35.12
sthitiṃ lokeṣvaṃtarikṣaṃ vidyāṃ tattveṣu ca triṣu  || 7.2,35.12
kalādhvasu pratiṣṭhāṃ ca vāmaṃ brahmasu pañcasu  || 7.2,35.13
madhyaṃ tu liṃgabhāgeṣu yoniṃ ca triṣu hetuṣu  || 7.2,35.13
prākṛtaṃ ca yathaiśvaryaṃ tasmādviśvaṃ yajurmayam  || 7.2,35.14
tatopasthāpayāmāsa sāmārthaṃ daśadhātmanaḥ  || 7.2,35.14
tamoguṇeṣvatho rudraṃ mūrtiṣvādyaṃ kriyāsu ca  || 7.2,35.15
saṃhṛtiṃ triṣu lokeṣu tattveṣu śivamuttamam  || 7.2,35.15
vidyākalāsvaghoraṃ ca tathā brahmasu pañcasu  || 7.2,35.16
liṃgabhāgeṣu pīṭhordhvaṃ bījinaṃ kāraṇatraye  || 7.2,35.16
pauruṣaṃ ca tathaiśvaryamitthaṃ sāmnā tataṃ jagat || 7.2,35.17
athātharvāha nairguṇyamarthaṃ prathamamātmanaḥ  || 7.2,35.17
tato maheśvaraṃ sākṣānmūrtiṣvapi sadāśivam  || 7.2,35.18
590

kriyāsu niṣkriyasyāpi śivasya paramātmanaḥ  || 7.2,35.18
bhūtānugrahaṇaṃ caiva mucyaṃte yena jaṃtavaḥ  || 7.2,35.19
lokeṣvapi yato vāco nivṛttā manasā saha  || 7.2,35.19
tadūrdhvamunmanā lokātsomalokamalaukikam  || 7.2,35.20
somassahomayā yatra nityaṃ nivasatīśvaraḥ  || 7.2,35.20
tadūrdhvamunmanā lokādyaṃ prāpto na nivartate  || 7.2,35.21
śāṃtiṃ ca śāṃtyatītāṃ ca vyāpikāṃ cai kalāsvapi  || 7.2,35.21
tatpūruṣaṃ tatheśānaṃ brahma brahmasu pañcasu  || 7.2,35.22
mūrdhānamapi liṃgasya nādabhāgeṣvanuttamam  || 7.2,35.22
yatrāvāhya samārādhyaḥ kevalo niṣkalaḥ śivaḥ  || 7.2,35.23
tatteṣvapi tadā biṃdornādācchaktestataḥ parāt || 7.2,35.23
tattvādapi paraṃ tattvamatattvaṃ paramārthataḥ  || 7.2,35.24
kāraṇeṣu trayātītānmāyāvikṣobhakāraṇāt || 7.2,35.24
anaṃtācchuddhavidyāyāḥ parastācca maheśvarāt || 7.2,35.25
sarvavidyeśvarādhīśānna parācca sadāśivāt || 7.2,35.25
sarvamaṃtratanordevācchaktitrayasamanvitāt || 7.2,35.26
pañcavaktrāddaśabhujātsākṣātsakalaniṣkalāt || 7.2,35.26
tasmādapi parādbiṃdorardhedośca tataḥ parāt || 7.2,35.27
tataḥ parānniśādhīśānnādākhyācca tataḥ parāt || 7.2,35.27
tataḥ parātsuṣumneśādbrahmaraṃdhreśvarādapi  || 7.2,35.28
tataḥ parasmācchakteśca parastācchivatattvataḥ  || 7.2,35.28
paramaṃ kāraṇaṃ sākṣātsvayaṃ niṣkāraṇaṃ śivam  || 7.2,35.29
kāraṇānāṃ ca dhātāraṃ dhyātārāṃ dhyeyamavyayam  || 7.2,35.29
paramākāśamadhyasthaṃ paramātmopari sthitaṃ  || 7.2,35.30
sarvaiśvaryeṇa saṃpannaṃ sarveśvaramanīśvaram  || 7.2,35.30
aiśvaryāccāpi māyeyādaśuddhānmānuṣādikāt || 7.2,35.31
aparācca parāttyājyādadhiśuddhādhvagocarāt || 7.2,35.31
tatparācchuddhavidyādyādunmanāṃtātparātparāt || 7.2,35.32
paramaṃ paramaiśvaryamunmanādyamanādi ca  || 7.2,35.32
apāramaparādhīnaṃ nirastātiśayaṃ sthiram  || 7.2,35.33
itthamarthairdaśavidhairiyamātharvaṇī śrutiḥ  || 7.2,35.33
yasmādgarīyasī tasmādviśvaṃ vyāptamatharvaṇāt || 7.2,35.34
ṛgvedaḥ punarāhedaṃ jāgradrūpaṃ mayocyate  || 7.2,35.34
yenāhamātmatattvasya nityamasmyabhidhāyakaḥ  || 7.2,35.35
yajurvedo 'vadattadvatsvapnāvasthā mayocyate  || 7.2,35.35
bhogyātmanā pariṇatā vidyāvedyā yato mayi  || 7.2,35.36
sāma cāha suṣuptyākhyamevaṃ sarvaṃ mayocyate  || 7.2,35.36
mamārthena śivenedaṃ tāmasenābhidhīyate  || 7.2,35.37
atharvāha turāyākhyaṃ turīyātītameva ca  || 7.2,35.37
mayābhidhīyate tasmādadhvātītapadosmyaham  || 7.2,35.38
adhvātmakaṃ tu tritayaṃ śivavidyātmasaṃjñitam  || 7.2,35.38
tattraiguṇyaṃ trayīsādhyaṃ saṃśodhyaṃ ca padaiṣiṇā  || 7.2,35.39
591

adhvātītaṃ turīyākhyaṃ nirvāṇaṃ paramaṃ padam  || 7.2,35.39
tadatītaṃ ca nairguṇyādadhvanosya viśodhakam  || 7.2,35.40
dvayoḥ pramāpako nādo nadāṃtaśca madātmakaḥ  || 7.2,35.40
tasmānmamārthasvātaṃtryātpradhānaḥ parameśvaraḥ  || 7.2,35.41
yadasti vastu tatsarvaṃ guṇapradhānyayogataḥ  || 7.2,35.41
samastaṃ vyastamapi ca praṇavārthaṃ pracakṣate  || 7.2,35.42
savārthavācakaṃ tasmādekaṃ brahmaitadakṣaram  || 7.2,35.42
tenomiti jagatkṛtsnaṃ kurute prathamaṃ śivaḥ  || 7.2,35.43
śivo hi praṇavo hyeṣa praṇavo hi śivaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.2,35.43
vācyavācakayorbhedo nātyaṃtaṃ vidyate yataḥ  || 7.2,35.44
ciṃtayā rahito rudro vācoyanmanasā saha  || 7.2,35.44
aprāpya tannivartaṃte vācyastvekākṣareṇa saḥ  || 7.2,35.45
ekākṣarādakārākhyādātmā brahmābhidhīyate  || 7.2,35.45
ekākṣarādukārākhyāddvidhā viṣṇurudīryate  || 7.2,35.46
ekākṣarānmakārākhyācchivo rudra udāhṛtaḥ  || 7.2,35.46
dakṣiṇāṃgānmaheśasya jāto brahmātmasaṃjñikaḥ  || 7.2,35.47
vāmāṃgādabhavadviṣṇustato vidyeti saṃjñitaḥ  || 7.2,35.47
hṛdayānnīlarudro bhūcchivasya śivasaṃjñikaḥ  || 7.2,35.48
sṛṣṭeḥ pravartako brahmā sthiterviṣṇurvimohakaḥ  || 7.2,35.48
saṃhārasya tathā rudrastayornityaṃ niyāmakaḥ  || 7.2,35.49
tasmāttrayaste kathyaṃte jagataḥ kāraṇāni ca  || 7.2,35.50
kāraṇatrayahetuśca śivaḥ paramakāraṇam  || 7.2,35.50
arthametamavijñāya rajasā baddhavairayoḥ  || 7.2,35.51
yuvayoḥ pratibodhāya madhye liṃgamupasthitam  || 7.2,35.51
evamomiti māṃ prāhuryadihoktamatharvaṇā  || 7.2,35.52
ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmāni śākhāścānyāḥ sahasraśaḥ  || 7.2,35.52
vedeṣvevaṃ svayaṃ vaktrairvyaktamityavadatsvapi  || 7.2,35.53
svapnānubhūtamiva tattābhyāṃ nādhyavasīyate  || 7.2,35.53
tayostatra prabodhāya tamopanayanāya ca  || 7.2,35.54
liṃgepi mudritaṃ sarvaṃ yathā vedairudāhṛtam  || 7.2,35.54
taddṛṣṭvā mudritaṃ liṃge prasādālliṃginastadā  || 7.2,35.55
praśāṃtamanasau devau prabuddhau saṃbabhūvatuḥ  || 7.2,35.55
utpattiṃ vilayaṃ caiva yathātmyaṃ ca ṣaḍadhvanām  || 7.2,35.56
tataḥ parataraṃ dhāma dhāmavaṃtaṃ ca pūruṣam  || 7.2,35.56
niruttarataraṃ brahma niṣkalaṃ śivamīśvaram  || 7.2,35.57
paśupāśamayasyāsya prapañcasya sadā patim  || 7.2,35.57
akutobhayamatyaṃtamavṛddhikṣayamavyayam  || 7.2,35.58
vāhyamābhyaṃtaraṃ vyāptaṃ vāhyābhyaṃtaravarjitam  || 7.2,35.58
nirastātiśayaṃ śaśvadviśvalokavilakṣaṇam  || 7.2,35.59
alakṣaṇamanirdeśyamavāṅmanasagocaram  || 7.2,35.59
prakāśaikarasaṃ śāṃtaṃ prasannaṃ satatoditam  || 7.2,35.60
sarvakalyāṇanilayaṃ śaktyā tādṛśayānvitam  || 7.2,35.60
jñātvā devaṃ virūpākṣaṃ brahmanārāyaṇau tadā  || 7.2,35.61
racayitvāṃjaliṃ mūrdhni bhītau tau vācamūcatuḥ  || 7.2,35.61
591

brahmovāca
ajño vāhamabhijño vā tvayādau deva nirmitaḥ  || 7.2,35.62
īdṛśīṃ bhrāṃtimāpanna iti ko 'trāparādhyati  || 7.2,35.62
āstāṃ mamedamajñānaṃ tvayi sannihate prabho  || 7.2,35.63
nirbhayaḥ ko 'bhibhāṣeta kṛtyaṃ svasya parasya vā  || 7.2,35.63
āvayordevadevasya vivādo 'pi hi śobhanaḥ  || 7.2,35.64
pādapraṇāmaphalado nāthasya bhavato yataḥ  || 7.2,35.64
viṣṇuruvāca
stotuṃ deva na vāgasti mahimnaḥ sadṛśī tava  || 7.2,35.65
prabhoragre vidheyānāṃ tūṣṇīṃbhāvo vyatikramaḥ  || 7.2,35.65
kimatra saṃghaṭetkṛtyamityevāvasarocitam  || 7.2,35.66
ajānannapi yatkiṃcitpralapya tvāṃ nato 'smyaham  || 7.2,35.66
kāraṇatvaṃ tvayā dattaṃ vismṛtaṃ tava māyayā  || 7.2,35.67
mohito 'haṃkṛtaścāpi punarevāsmi śāsitaḥ  || 7.2,35.67
vijñāpitaiḥ kiṃ bahubhirbhītosmi bhṛśamīśvara  || 7.2,35.68
yato 'hamaparicchedyaṃ tvāṃ paricchettumudyataḥ  || 7.2,35.68
tvāmuśaṃti mahādevaṃ bhītānāmārtināśanam  || 7.2,35.69
ato vyatikramaṃ me 'dya kṣaṃtumarhasi śaṃkara  || 7.2,35.69
iti vijñāpitastābhyāmīśvarābhyāṃ maheśvaraḥ  || 7.2,35.70
prīto 'nugṛhya tau devau smitapūrvamabhāṣata  || 7.2,35.70
īśvara uvāca
vatsavatsa vidhe viṣṇo māyayā mama mohitau  || 7.2,35.71
yuvāṃ prabhutve 'haṃkṛtya buddhavairo parasparam  || 7.2,35.71
vivādaṃ yuddhaparyaṃtaṃ kṛtvā noparatau kila  || 7.2,35.72
tataścchinnā prajāsṛṣṭirjagatkāraṇabhūtayoḥ  || 7.2,35.72
ajñānamānaprabhavādvaimatyādyuvayorapi  || 7.2,35.73
tannivartayituṃ yuṣmaddarpamohau mayaiva tu  || 7.2,35.73
evaṃ nivāritāvadyaliṃgāvirbhāvalīlayā  || 7.2,35.74
tasmādbhūyo vivādaṃ ca vrīḍāṃ cotsṛjya kṛtsnaśaḥ  || 7.2,35.74
yathāsvaṃ karma kuryātāṃ bhavaṃtau vītamatsarau  || 7.2,35.75
purā mamājñayā sārdhaṃ samastajñānasaṃhitāḥ  || 7.2,35.75
yuvābhyāṃ hi mayā dattā kāraṇatvaprasiddhaye  || 7.2,35.76
maṃtraratnaṃ ca sūtrākhyaṃ pañcākṣaramayaṃ param  || 7.2,35.76
mayopadiṣṭaṃ sarvaṃ tadyuvayoradya vismṛtam  || 7.2,35.77
dadāmi ca punaḥ sarvaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ mamājñayā  || 7.2,35.77
yato vinā yuvāṃ tena na kṣamau sṛṣṭirakṣaṇe  || 7.2,35.78
evamuktvā mahādevo nārāyaṇapitāmahau  || 7.2,35.78
maṃtrarājaṃ dadau tābhyāṃ jñānasaṃhitayā saha  || 7.2,35.79
tau labdhvā mahatīṃ divyāmājñāṃ māheśvarīṃ parām  || 7.2,35.79
mahārthaṃ maṃtraratnaṃ ca tathaiva sakalāḥ kalāḥ  || 7.2,35.80
daṃḍavatpraṇatiṃ kṛtvā devadevasya pādayoḥ  || 7.2,35.80
atiṣṭhatāṃ vītabhayāvānaṃdāstimitau tadā  || 7.2,35.81
etasminnaṃtare citramiṃdrajālavadaiśvaram  || 7.2,35.81
liṃgaṃ kvāpi tirobhūtaṃ na tābhyāmupalabhyate  || 7.2,35.82
tato vilapya hāheti sadyaḥpraṇayabhaṃgataḥ  || 7.2,35.82
kimasatyamidaṃ vṛttamiti coktvā parasparam  || 7.2,35.83
aciṃtyavaibhavaṃ śaṃbhorviciṃtya ca gatavyathau  || 7.2,35.83
592

abhyupetya parāṃ maitrīmāliṃgya ca parasparam  || 7.2,35.84
jagadvyāpāramuddiśya jagmaturdevapuṃgavau  || 7.2,35.84
tataḥ prabhṛti śakrādyāḥ sarva eva surāsurāḥ  || 7.2,35.85
ṛṣayaśca narā nāgā nāryaścāpi vidhānataḥ  || 7.2,35.85
liṃgapratiṣṭhā kurvaṃti liṃge taṃ pūjayaṃti ca  || 7.2,35.85
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe harividhimohanivāraṇaṃ nāma pañcatriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 36
śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca
bhagavañchrotumicchāmi pratiṣṭhāvidhimuttamam  || 7.2,36.1
liṃgasyāpi ca berasya śivena vihitaṃ yathā  || 7.2,36.1
upamanyuruvāca
anātmapratikūle tu divase śuklapakṣake  || 7.2,36.2
śivaśāstroktamārgeṇa kuryālliṃgaṃ pramāṇavat || 7.2,36.2
svīkṛtyātha śubhasthānaṃ bhūparīkṣāṃ vidhāya ca  || 7.2,36.3
daśopacārānkurvīta lakṣaṇoddhārapūrvakān  || 7.2,36.3
teṣāṃ daśopacārāṇāṃ pūrvaṃ pūjya 1 vināyakam  || 7.2,36.4
sthānaśuddhyādikaṃ kṛtvāliṃgaṃ snānālayaṃ nayet || 7.2,36.4
śalākayā kāṃcanayā 2 kuṃkumādirasāktayā  || 7.2,36.5
lakṣitaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ śilpaśāstreṇa vilikhettataḥ  || 7.2,36.5
aṣṭamṛtsalilairvātha pañcamṛtsalilaistathā  || 7.2,36.6
liṅgaṃ piṃḍikayā sārdhaṃ pañcagavyaiśca śodhayet || 7.2,36.6
savedikaṃ samabhyarcya divyādyaṃ tu jalāśayam  || 7.2,36.7
nītvādhivāsayettatra liṃgaṃ piṃḍikayā saha  || 7.2,36.7
adhivāsālaye śuddhe sarvaśobhāsamanvite  || 7.2,36.8
satoraṇe sāvaraṇe darbhamālāsamāvṛte  || 7.2,36.8
diggajāṣṭakasaṃpanne dikpālāṣṭaghaṭānvite  || 7.2,36.9
aṣṭamaṃgalakairyukte kṛtadikpālakārcite  || 7.2,36.9
tejasaṃ dāravaṃ vāpi kṛtvā padmāsanāṃkitam  || 7.2,36.10
vinyasenmadhyatastatra vipulaṃ pīṭhakālayam  || 7.2,36.10
dvārapālānsamabhyarcya bhadrādīṃścaturaḥkramāt || 7.2,36.11
samudraśca vibhadraśca sunaṃdaśca vinaṃdakaḥ  || 7.2,36.11
snāpayitvā samabhyarcya liṃgaṃ vedikayā saha  || 7.2,36.12
sakūrcābhyāṃ tu vastrābhyāṃ samāveṣṭyaṃ samaṃtataḥ  || 7.2,36.12
prāpayya śanakaistoyaṃ pīṭhikopari śāyayet || 7.2,36.13
prākśiraskamadhaḥsūtraṃ piṃḍikāṃ cāsya paścime  || 7.2,36.13
sarvamaṃgalasaṃyuktaṃ liṃgaṃ tatrādhivāsayet || 7.2,36.14
pañcarātraṃ trirātraṃ vāpyekarātramathāpi vā  || 7.2,36.14
visṛjya pūjitaṃ tatra śodhayitvā ca pūrvavat || 7.2,36.15
saṃpūjyotsavamārgeṇa śayanālayamānayet || 7.2,36.15
tatrāpi śayanasthānaṃ kuryānmaṃḍalamadhyataḥ  || 7.2,36.16
śuddhairjalaiḥ snāpayitvā liṃgamabhyarcayetkramāt || 7.2,36.16
aiśānyāṃ padmamālikhya śuddhalipte mahītale  || 7.2,36.17

1 lyabārṣaḥ
2 ḍiḍhḍhaṇañiti ḍībabhāva ārṣaḥ

592

śivakuṃbhaṃ śodhayitvā tatrāvāhya śivaṃ yajet || 7.2,36.17
vedīmadhye sitaṃ padmaṃ parikalpya vidhānataḥ  || 7.2,36.18
tasya paścimataścāpi caṃḍikāpadmamālikhet || 7.2,36.18
kṣaumādyairvāhatairvastraiḥ puṣpairdarbhairathāpi vā  || 7.2,36.19
prakalpya śayanaṃ tasminhemapuṣpaṃ vinikṣipet || 7.2,36.19
tatra liṃgaṃ samānīya sarvamaṃgalaniḥsvanaiḥ  || 7.2,36.20
raktena vastrayugmena sakūrcena samaṃtataḥ  || 7.2,36.20
saha piṃḍikayāveṣṭya śāyayecca yathā purā  || 7.2,36.21
purastātpadmamālikhya taddaleṣu yathākramam  || 7.2,36.21
vidyeśakalaśānnyasyenmadhye śaivīṃ ca vardhanīm  || 7.2,36.22
parītya padmatritayaṃ juhuyurdvijasattamāḥ  || 7.2,36.22
te cāṣṭamūrtayaḥ kalpyāḥ pūrvādiparitaḥ sthitāḥ  || 7.2,36.23
catvāraścātha vā dikṣu svadhyetārassajāpakāḥ  || 7.2,36.23
juhuyuste viraṃcyādyāścatasro mūrtayaḥ smṛtāḥ  || 7.2,36.24
daiśikaḥ prathamaṃ teṣāmaiśānyāṃ paścime 'tha vā  || 7.2,36.24
pradhānahomaṃ kurvīta saptadravyairyathākramam  || 7.2,36.25
ācāryātpādamardhaṃ vā juhuyuścāpare dvijāḥ  || 7.2,36.25
pradhānamekamevātra juhuyādatha vā guruḥ  || 7.2,36.26
pūrvaṃ pūrṇāhutiṃ hutvā ghṛtenāṣṭottaraṃ śatam  || 7.2,36.26
mūrdhni mūlena liṃgasya śivahastaṃ pravinyaset || 7.2,36.27
śatamardhaṃ tadardhaṃ vā kramāddravyaiśca saptabhiḥ  || 7.2,36.27
hutvāhutvā spṛśelliṃgaṃ vedikāṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ  || 7.2,36.28
pūrṇāhutiṃ tato hutvā kramāddadyācca dakṣiṇām  || 7.2,36.28
ācāryātpādamardhaṃ vā hotḥṇāṃ sthapaterapi  || 7.2,36.29
tadardhaṃ deyamanyebhyaḥ sadasyebhyaśca śaktitaḥ  || 7.2,36.29
tataḥ śvabhre vṛṣaṃ haimaṃ kūrcaṃ vāpi niveśya ca  || 7.2,36.30
mṛdaṃbhasā pañcagavyaiḥ punaḥ śuddhajalena ca  || 7.2,36.30
śodhitāṃ caṃdanāliptāṃ śvabhre brahmaśilāṃ kṣipet || 7.2,36.31
karanyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā navabhiḥ śaktināmabhiḥ  || 7.2,36.31
haritālādidhātūṃśca bījagaṃdhauṣadhairapi  || 7.2,36.32
śivaśāstroktavidhinā kṣipedbrahmaśilopari  || 7.2,36.32
pratiliṃgaṃ tu saṃsthāpya kṣīraṃ vṛkṣasamudbhavam  || 7.2,36.33
sthitaṃ buddhvā tadutsṛjya liṃgaṃ brahmaśilopari  || 7.2,36.33
prāgudakpravarāṃ kiṃcitsthāpayenmūlavidyayā  || 7.2,36.34
piṃḍikāṃ cātha saṃyojya śāktaṃ mūlamanusmaran  || 7.2,36.34
bandhanaṃ baṃdhakadravyaiḥ kṛtvā sthānaṃ viśodhya ca  || 7.2,36.35
dattvā cārghyaṃ ca puṣpāṇi kuryuryavanikāṃ punaḥ  || 7.2,36.35
yathāyogyaṃ niṣekādi liṃgasya puratastadā  || 7.2,36.36
ānīya śayanasthānātkalaśānvinyasetkramāt || 7.2,36.36
mahāpūjāmathārabhya saṃpūjya kalaśāndaśa  || 7.2,36.37
śivamaṃtramanusmṛtya śivakuṃbhajalāṃtare  || 7.2,36.37
aṃguṣṭhānāmikāyogādādāya tamudīrayet || 7.2,36.38
nyasedīśānabhāgasya madhye liṃgasya maṃtravit || 7.2,36.38
593

śaktiṃ nyasettathā vidyāṃ vidyeśāṃśca yathākramam  || 7.2,36.39
liṅgamūle śivajalaistato liṃgaṃ niṣecayet || 7.2,36.39
vardhanyāṃ piṃḍikāliṃgaṃ vidyeśakalaśaiḥ punaḥ  || 7.2,36.40
abhiṣicyāsanaṃ paścādādhārādyaṃ prakalpayet || 7.2,36.40
kṛtvā pañcakalānyāsaṃ dīptaṃ liṃgamanusmaret || 7.2,36.41
āvāhayecchivau sākṣātprāñjaliḥ prāgudaṅmukhaḥ  || 7.2,36.41
vṛṣādhirājamāruhya vimānaṃ vā nabhastalāt || 7.2,36.42
alaṃkṛtaṃ sahāyāṃtaṃ devyā devamanusmaran  || 7.2,36.42
sarvābharaṇaśobhāḍhyaṃ sarvamaṃgalanisvanaiḥ  || 7.2,36.42
brahmaviṣṇumaheśārkaśakrādyairdevadānavaiḥ  || 7.2,36.43
ānaṃdaklinnasarvāṃgairvinyastāṃjalimastakaiḥ  || 7.2,36.43
stuvadbhireva nṛtyadbhirnāmadbhirabhito vṛtam  || 7.2,36.44
tataḥ pañcopacārāṃśca kṛtvā pūjāṃ samāpayet || 7.2,36.44
nātaḥ parataraḥ kaścidvidhiḥ pañcopacārakāt || 7.2,36.45
pratiṣṭhāṃ liṃgavatkuryātpratimāsvapi sarvataḥ  || 7.2,36.45
lakṣaṇoddhārasamaye kāryaṃ nayanamocanam  || 7.2,36.46
jalādhivāse śayane śāyayettāntvadhomukhīm  || 7.2,36.46
kumbhodaśāyitāṃ maṃtrairhṛdi tāṃ sanniyojayet || 7.2,36.47
kṛtālayāṃ parāmāhuḥ pratiṣṭhāmakṛtālayāt || 7.2,36.47
śaktaḥ kṛtālayaḥ paścātpratiṣṭhāvidhimācaret || 7.2,36.48
aśaktaścetpratiṣṭhāpya liṃgaṃ beramathāpi vā  || 7.2,36.48
śakteranuguṇaṃ paścātprakurvīta śivālayam  || 7.2,36.49
gṛhārcāṃ ca punarvakṣye pratiṣṭhāvidhimuttamam  || 7.2,36.49
kṛtvā kanīyasaṃberaṃ liṃgaṃ vā lakṣaṇānvitam  || 7.2,36.50
ayane cottare prāpte śuklapakśe śubhe dine  || 7.2,36.50
devīṃ kṛtvā śubhe deśe tatrābjaṃ pūrvavallikhet || 7.2,36.51
vikīrya patrapuṣpādyairmadhye kuṃbhaṃ nidhāya ca  || 7.2,36.51
paritastasya caturaḥ kalaśān dikṣu vinyaset || 7.2,36.52
pañca brahmāṇi tadbījaisteṣu pañcasu pañcabhiḥ  || 7.2,36.52
nyasya saṃpūjya mudrādi darśayitvābhirakṣya ca  || 7.2,36.53
viśodhya liṃgaṃ beraṃ vā mṛttoyādyairyathā purā  || 7.2,36.53
sthāpayetpuṣpasaṃchannamuttarasthe varāsane  || 7.2,36.54
nidhāya puṣpaṃ śirasi prokṣayetprokṣaṇījalaiḥ  || 7.2,36.54
samabhyarcya punaḥ puṣpairjayaśabdādipūrvakam  || 7.2,36.55
kumbhairīśānavidyāṃtaiḥ snāpayenmūlavidyayā  || 7.2,36.55
tataḥ pañcakalānyāsaṃ kṛtvā pūjāṃ ca pūrvavat || 7.2,36.56
nityamārādhayettatra devyā devaṃ trilocanam  || 7.2,36.56
ekamevātha vā kuṃbhaṃ mūrtimantrasamanvitam  || 7.2,36.57
nyasya padmāṃtare sarvaṃ śeṣaṃ pūrvavadācaret || 7.2,36.57
atyaṃtopahataṃ liṃgaṃ viśodhya sthāpayetpunaḥ  || 7.2,36.58
saṃprokṣayedupahatamanāgupahataṃ yajet || 7.2,36.58
liṃgāni bāṇasaṃjñāni sthāpanīyāni vā na vā  || 7.2,36.59
tāni pūrvaṃ śivenaiva saṃskṛtāni yatastataḥ  || 7.2,36.59
śeṣāṇi sthāpanīyāni yāni dṛṣṭāni bāṇavat || 7.2,36.60
svayamudbhūtaliṃge ca divye cārṣe tathaiva ca  || 7.2,36.60
593

apīṭhe pīṭhamāveśya kṛtvā saṃprokṣaṇaṃ vidhim  || 7.2,36.61
yajettatra śivaṃ teṣāṃ pratiṣṭhā na vidhīyate  || 7.2,36.61
dagdhaṃ ślathaṃ kṣatāṃgaṃ ca kṣipelliṃgaṃ jalāśaye  || 7.2,36.62
saṃdhānayogyaṃ saṃdhāya pratiṣṭhāvidhimācaret || 7.2,36.62
berādvā vikalālliṃgāddevapūjāpurassaram  || 7.2,36.63
udvāsya hṛdi saṃdhānaṃ tyāgaṃ vā yuktamācaret || 7.2,36.63
ekāhapūjāvihatau kuryāddviguṇamarcanam  || 7.2,36.64
dvirātre ca mahāpūjāṃ saṃprokṣaṇamataḥ param  || 7.2,36.64
māsādūrdhvamanekāhaṃ pūjā yadi vihanyate  || 7.2,36.65
pratiṣṭhā procyate kaiścitkaiścitsaṃprokṣaṇakramaḥ  || 7.2,36.65
saṃprokṣaṇe tu liṃgāderdevamudvāsya pūrvavat || 7.2,36.66
aṣṭapañcakrameṇaiva snāpayitvā mṛdaṃbhasā  || 7.2,36.66
gavāṃ rasaiśca saṃsnāpya darbhatoyairviśodhya ca  || 7.2,36.67
prokṣayetprokṣaṇītoyairmūlenāṣṭottaraṃ śatam  || 7.2,36.67
sapuṣpaṃ sakuśaṃ pāṇiṃ nyasya liṃgasya mastake  || 7.2,36.68
pañcavāraṃ japenmūlamaṣṭottaraśataṃ tataḥ  || 7.2,36.68
tato mūlena mūrdhādipīṭhāṃtaṃ saṃspṛśedapi  || 7.2,36.69
pūjāṃ ca mahatīṃ kuryāddevamāvāhya pūrvavat || 7.2,36.69
alabdhe sthāpite liṃge śivasthāne jale 'tha vā  || 7.2,36.70
vahnau ravau tathā vyomni bhagavaṃtaṃ śivaṃ yajet || 7.2,36.70

oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe ptatiṣṭhāvidhivarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṭtriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 37
śīkṛṣṇa uvāca
jñāne kriyāyāṃ caryāyāṃ sāramuddhṛtya saṃgrahāt || 7.2,37.1
uktaṃ bhagavatā sarvaṃ śrutaṃ śrutisamaṃ mayā  || 7.2,37.1
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi yogaṃ paramadurlabham  || 7.2,37.2
sādhikāraṃ ca sāṃgaṃ ca savidhiṃ saprayojanam  || 7.2,37.2
yadyasti maraṇaṃ pūrvaṃ yogādyanupamardataḥ  || 7.2,37.3
sadyaḥ sādhayituṃ śakyaṃ yena syānnātmahā naraḥ  || 7.2,37.3
tacca tatkāraṇaṃ caiva tatkālakaraṇāni ca  || 7.2,37.4
tadbhedatāratamyaṃ ca vaktumarhasi tattvataḥ  || 7.2,37.4
upamanyuruvāca
sthāne pṛṣṭaṃ tvayā kṛṣṇa sarvapraśnārthavedinā  || 7.2,37.5
tataḥ krameṇa tatsarvaṃ vakṣye śṛṇu samāhitaḥ  || 7.2,37.5
niruddhavṛttyaṃtarasyaṃ śive cittasya niścalā  || 7.2,37.6
yā vṛttiḥ sa samāsena yogaḥ sa khalu pañcadhā  || 7.2,37.6
maṃtrayogaḥsparśayogo bhāvayogastathāparaḥ  || 7.2,37.7
abhāvayogassarvebhyo mahāyogaḥ paro mataḥ  || 7.2,37.7
maṃtrābhyāsavaśenaiva maṃtravācyārthagocaraḥ  || 7.2,37.8
avyākṣepā manovṛttirmaṃtrayoga udāhṛtaḥ  || 7.2,37.8
prāṇāyāmamukhā saiva sparśe yogobhidhīyate  || 7.2,37.9
sa maṃtrasparśanirmukto bhāvayogaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  || 7.2,37.9
vilīnāvayavaṃ viśvaṃ rūpaṃ saṃbhāvyate yataḥ  || 7.2,37.10
594

abhāvayogaḥ saṃprokto 'nābhāsādvastunaḥ sataḥ  || 7.2,37.10
śivasvabhāva evaikaściṃtyate nirupādhikaḥ  || 7.2,37.11
yathā śaivamanovṛttirmahāyoga ihocyate  || 7.2,37.11
dṛṣṭe tathānuśravike viraktaṃ viṣaye manaḥ  || 7.2,37.12
yasya tasyādhikārosti yoge nānyasya kasyacit || 7.2,37.12
viṣayadvayadoṣāṇāṃ guṇānāmīśvarasya ca  || 7.2,37.13
darśanādeva satataṃ viraktaṃ jāyate manaḥ  || 7.2,37.13
aṣṭāṃgo vā ṣaḍaṃgo vā sarvayogaḥ samāsataḥ  || 7.2,37.14
yamaśca niyamaścaiva svastikādyaṃ tathāsanam  || 7.2,37.14
prāṇāyāmaḥ pratyāhāro dhāraṇā dhyānameva ca  || 7.2,37.15
samādhiriti yogāṃgānyaṣṭāvuktāni sūribhiḥ  || 7.2,37.15
āsanaṃ prāṇasaṃrodhaḥ pratyāhārotha dhāraṇā  || 7.2,37.16
dhyānaṃ samādhiryogasya ṣaḍaṃgāni samāsataḥ  || 7.2,37.16
pṛthaglakṣaṇameteṣāṃ śivaśāstre samīritam  || 7.2,37.17
śivāgameṣu cānyeṣu viśeṣātkāmikādiṣu  || 7.2,37.17
yogaśāstreṣvapi tathā purāṇeṣvapi keṣu ca  || 7.2,37.18
ahiṃsā satyamasteyaṃ brahmacaryāparigrahaḥ  || 7.2,37.18
yama ityucyate sadbhiḥ pañcāvayavayogataḥ  || 7.2,37.18
śaucaṃ tuṣṭistapaścaiva japaḥ praṇidhireva ca  || 7.2,37.19
iti pañcaprabhedassyānniyamaḥ svāṃśabhedataḥ  || 7.2,37.19
svastikaṃ padmamadhyeṃduṃ vīraṃ yogaṃ prasādhitam  || 7.2,37.20
paryaṃkaṃ ca yatheṣṭaṃ ca proktamāsanamaṣṭadhā  || 7.2,37.20
prāṇaḥ svadehajo vāyustasyāyāmo nirodhanam  || 7.2,37.21
tadrocakaṃ pūrakaṃ ca kuṃbhakaṃ ca tridhocyate  || 7.2,37.21
nāsikāpuṭamaṃgulyā pīḍyaikamapareṇa tu  || 7.2,37.22
audaraṃ recayedvāyuṃ tathāyaṃ recakaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.2,37.22
bāhyena marutā dehaṃ dṛtivatparipūrayet || 7.2,37.23
nāsāpuṭenāpareṇa pūraṇātpūrakaṃ matam  || 7.2,37.23
na muṃcati na gṛhṇāti vāyumaṃtarbahiḥ sthitam  || 7.2,37.24
saṃpūrṇaṃ kuṃbhavattiṣṭhedacalaḥ sa tu kuṃbhaka  || 7.2,37.24
recakādyaṃ trayamidaṃ na drutaṃ na vilaṃbitam  || 7.2,37.25
tadyataḥ kramayogena tvabhyasedyogasādhakaḥ  || 7.2,37.25
recakādiṣu yobhyāso nāḍīśodhanapūrvakaḥ  || 7.2,37.26
svecchotkramaṇaparyaṃtaḥ prokto yogānuśāsane  || 7.2,37.26
kanyakādikramavaśātprāṇāyāmanirodhanam  || 7.2,37.27
taccaturdhopadiṣṭaṃ syānmātrāguṇavibhāgataḥ  || 7.2,37.27
kanyakastu caturdhā syātsa ca dvādaśamātrakaḥ  || 7.2,37.28
madhyamastu dviruddhātaścaturviṃśatimātrakaḥ  || 7.2,37.28
uttamastu triruddhātaḥ ṣaḍviṃśanmātrakaḥ paraḥ  || 7.2,37.29
svedakaṃpādijanakaḥ prāṇāyāmastaduttaraḥ  || 7.2,37.29
ānaṃdodbhavaromāṃcanetrāśrūṇāṃ vimocanam  || 7.2,37.30
jalpabhramaṇamūrchādyaṃ jāyate yoginaḥ param  || 7.2,37.30
jānuṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya na drutaṃ na vilaṃbitam  || 7.2,37.31
aṃgulīsphoṭanaṃ kuryātsā mātreti prakīrtitā  || 7.2,37.31
594

mātrākrameṇa vijñeyāścodvātakramayogataḥ  || 7.2,37.32
nāḍīviśuddhipūrvaṃ tu prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret || 7.2,37.32
agarbhaśca sagarbhaśca prāṇāyāmo dvidhā smṛtaḥ  || 7.2,37.33
japaṃ dhyānaṃ vināgarbhaḥ sagarbhastatsamanvayāt || 7.2,37.33
agarbhādgarbhasaṃyuktaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥśatādhikaḥ  || 7.2,37.34
tasmātsagarbhaṃ kurvanti yoginaḥ prāṇasaṃyamam  || 7.2,37.34
prāṇasya vijayādeva jīyaṃte deha 1 āyavaḥ  || 7.2,37.35
prāṇo 'pānaḥ samānaśca hyudāno vyāna eva ca  || 7.2,37.35
nāgaḥ kūrmaśca kṛkalo devadatto dhanaṃjayaḥ  || 7.2,37.36
prayāṇaṃ kurute yasmāttasmātprāṇo 'bhidhīyate  || 7.2,37.36
avāṅnayatyapānākhyo yadāhārādi bhujyate  || 7.2,37.37
vyāno vyānaśayatyaṃgānyaśeṣāṇi vivardhayan  || 7.2,37.37
udvejayati marmāṇītyudāno vāyurīritaḥ  || 7.2,37.38
samaṃ nayati sarvāṃgaṃ samānastena gīyate  || 7.2,37.38
udgāre nāga ākhyātaḥ kūrma unmīlane sthitaḥ  || 7.2,37.39
kṛkalaḥ kṣavathau jñeyo devadatto vijṛṃbhaṇe  || 7.2,37.39
na jahāti mṛtaṃ cāpi sarvavyāpī dhanaṃjayaḥ  || 7.2,37.40
krameṇābhyasyamānoyaṃ prāṇāyāmapramāṇavān  || 7.2,37.40
nirdahatyakhilaṃ doṣaṃ karturdehaṃ ca rakṣati  || 7.2,37.41
prāṇe tu vijite samyaktaccihnānyupalakṣayet || 7.2,37.41
viṇmūtraśleṣmaṇāṃ tāvadalpabhāvaḥ prajāyate  || 7.2,37.42
bahubhojanasāmarthyaṃ cirāducchvāsanaṃ tathā  || 7.2,37.42
laghutvaṃ śīghragāmitvamutsāhaḥ svarasauṣṭhavam  || 7.2,37.43
sarvarogakṣayaścaiva balaṃ tejaḥ surūpatā  || 7.2,37.43
dhṛtirmedhā yuvatvaṃ ca sthiratā ca prasannatā  || 7.2,37.44
tapāṃsi pāpakṣayatā yajñadānavratādayaḥ  || 7.2,37.44
prāṇāyāmasya tasyaite kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  || 7.2,37.45
indriyāṇi prasaktāni yathāsvaṃ viṣayeṣviha  || 7.2,37.45
āhatya yannigṛhṇāti sa pratyāhāra ucyate  || 7.2,37.46
namaḥpūrvāṇīṃdriyāṇi svargaṃ narakameva ca  || 7.2,37.46
nigṛhītanisṛṣṭāni svargāya narakāya ca  || 7.2,37.47
tasmātsukhārthī matimāñjñānavairāgyamāsthitaḥ  || 7.2,37.47
iṃdriyāśvānnigṛhyāśu svātmanātmānamuddharet || 7.2,37.48
dhāraṇā nāma cittasya sthānabandhassamāsataḥ  || 7.2,37.48
sthānaṃ ca śiva evaiko nānyaddoṣatrayaṃ yataḥ  || 7.2,37.49
kālaṃ kaṃcāvadhīkṛtya sthāne 'vasthāpitaṃ manaḥ  || 7.2,37.49
na tu pracyavate lakṣyāddhāraṇā syānna cānyathā  || 7.2,37.50
manasaḥ prathamaṃ sthairyaṃ dhāraṇātaḥ prajāyate  || 7.2,37.50
tasmāddhīraṃ manaḥ kuryāddhāraṇābhyāsayogataḥ  || 7.2,37.51
dhyai ciṃtāyāṃ smṛto dhātuḥ śivaciṃtā muhurmuhuḥ  || 7.2,37.51
avyākṣiptena manasā dhyānaṃ nāma taducyate  || 7.2,37.52
dhyeyāvasthitacittasya sadṛśaḥ pratyayaśca yaḥ  || 7.2,37.52

1 daśa vāyava iti pāṭhāntaram

595
pratyayāntaranirmuktaḥ pravāho dhyānamucyate  || 7.2,37.53
sarvamanyatparityajya śiva eva śivaṃkaraḥ  || 7.2,37.53
paro dhyeyo 'dhideveśaḥ samāptātharvaṇī śrutiḥ  || 7.2,37.54
tathā śivā parā dhyeyā sarvabhūtagatau śivau  || 7.2,37.54
tau śrutau smṛtiśāstrebhyaḥ sarvagau sarvadoditau  || 7.2,37.55
sarvajñau satataṃ dhyeyau nānārūpavibhedataḥ  || 7.2,37.55
vimuktiḥ pratyayaḥ pūrvaḥ pratyayaścāṇimādikam  || 7.2,37.56
ityetaddvividhaṃ jñeyaṃ dhyānasyāsya prayojanam  || 7.2,37.56
dhyātā dhyānaṃ tathā dhyeyaṃ yacca dhyānaprayojanam  || 7.2,37.57
etaccatuṣṭayaṃ jñātvā yogaṃ yuñjīta yogavit || 7.2,37.57
jñānavairāgyasaṃpannaḥ śraddadhānaḥ kṣamānvitaḥ  || 7.2,37.58
nirmamaśca sadotsāhī dhyātetthaṃ puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.2,37.58
japācchrāṃtaḥ punardhyāyeddhyānācchrāṃtaḥ punarjapet || 7.2,37.59
japadhyanābhiyuktasya kṣipraṃ yogaḥ prasiddhyati  || 7.2,37.59
dhāraṇā dvādaśāyāmā dhyānaṃ dvādaśadhāraṇam  || 7.2,37.60
dhyānadvādaśakaṃ yāvatsamādhirabhidhīyate  || 7.2,37.60
samādhirnnāma yogāṃgamantimaṃ parikīrtitam  || 7.2,37.61
samādhinā ca sarvatra prajñālokaḥ pravartate  || 7.2,37.61
yadarthamātranirbhāsaṃ stimito dadhivatsthitam  || 7.2,37.62
svarūpaśūnyavadbhānaṃ samādhirabhidhīyate  || 7.2,37.62
dhyeye manaḥ samāveśya paśyedapi ca susthiram  || 7.2,37.63
nirvāṇānalavadyogī samādhisthaḥ pragīyate  || 7.2,37.63
na śṛṇoti na cāghrāti na jalpati na paśyati  || 7.2,37.64
na ca sparśaṃ vijānāti na saṃkalpayate manaḥ  || 7.2,37.64
navābhimanyate kiṃcidbadhyate na ca kāṣṭavat || 7.2,37.65
evaṃ śive vilīnātmā samādhistha ihocyate  || 7.2,37.65
yathā dīpo nivātasthaḥ spandate na kadācana  || 7.2,37.66
tathā samādhiniṣṭho 'pi tasmānna vicaletsudhīḥ  || 7.2,37.66
evamabhyasataścāraṃ yogino yogamuttamam  || 7.2,37.67
tadantarāyā naśyaṃti vighnāḥ sarve śanaiḥśanaiḥ  || 7.2,37.67
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ vāyunaimiṣeyarṣisaṃvāde uttarakhaṇḍe yogagativarṇanaṃ nāma saptatriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 38
upamanyuruvāca
ālasyaṃ vyādhayastīvrāḥ pramādaḥ sthānasaṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,38.1
anavasthitacittatvamaśraddhā bhrāṃtidarśanam  || 7.2,38.1
duḥkhāni daurmanasyaṃ ca viṣayeṣu ca lolatā  || 7.2,38.2
daśaite yuñjatāṃ puṃsāmantarāyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  || 7.2,38.2
ālasyamalasattvaṃ tu yogināṃ dehacetanoḥ  || 7.2,38.3
dhātuvaiṣamyajā doṣā vyādhayaḥ karmadoṣajāḥ  || 7.2,38.3
pramādo nāma yogasya sādhanā nāma bhāvanā  || 7.2,38.4
idaṃ vetyubhayākrāntaṃ vijñānaṃ sthānasaṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,38.4
apratiṣṭhā hi manasastvanavasthitirucyate  || 7.2,38.5
595

aśraddhā bhāvarahitā vṛttirvai yogavartmani  || 7.2,38.5
viparyastā matiryā sā bhrāṃtirityabhidhīyate  || 7.2,38.6
duḥkhamajñānajaṃ puṃsāṃ cittasyādhyātmikaṃ viduḥ  || 7.2,38.6
ādhibhautikamaṃgotthaṃ yacca duḥkhaṃ purā kṛtaiḥ  || 7.2,38.7
ādhidaivikamākhyātamaśanyastraviṣādikam  || 7.2,38.7
icchāvighātajaṃ mokṣaṃ daurmanasyaṃ pracakṣate  || 7.2,38.8
viṣayeṣu vicitreṣu vibhramastatra lolatā  || 7.2,38.8
śānteṣveteṣu vighneṣu yogāsaktasya yoginaḥ  || 7.2,38.9
upasargāḥ pravartaṃte divyāste siddhisūcakāḥ  || 7.2,38.9
pratibhā śravaṇaṃ vārtā darśanāsvādavedanāḥ  || 7.2,38.10
upasargāḥ ṣaḍityete vyaye yogasya siddhayaḥ  || 7.2,38.10
sūkṣme vyavahite 'tīte viprakṛṣṭe tvanāgate  || 7.2,38.11
pratibhā kathyate yo 'rthe pratibhāso yathātatham  || 7.2,38.11
śravaṇaṃ sarvaśabdānāṃ śravaṇe cāprayatnataḥ  || 7.2,38.12
vārttā vārttāsu vijñānaṃ sarveṣāmeva dehinām  || 7.2,38.12
darśanaṃ nāma divyānāṃ darśanaṃ cāprayatnataḥ  || 7.2,38.13
tathāsvādaśca divyeṣu raseṣvāsvāda ucyate  || 7.2,38.13
sparśanādhigamastadvadvedanā nāma viśrutā  || 7.2,38.14
gandhādīnāṃ ca divyānāmābrahmabhuvanādhipāḥ  || 7.2,38.14
saṃtiṣṭhante ca ratnāni prayacchaṃti bahūni ca  || 7.2,38.15
svacchandamadhurā vāṇī vividhāsyātpravartate  || 7.2,38.15
rasāyanāni sarvāṇi divyāścauṣadhayastathā  || 7.2,38.16
sidhyaṃti praṇipatyainaṃ diśaṃti surayoṣitaḥ  || 7.2,38.16
yogasiddhyaikadeśe 'pi dṛṣṭe mokṣe bhavenmatiḥ  || 7.2,38.17
dṛṣṭametanmayā yadvattadvanmokṣo bhavediti  || 7.2,38.17
kṛśatā sthūlatā bālyaṃ vārdhakyaṃ caiva yauvanam  || 7.2,38.18
nānācātisvarūpaṃ ca caturṇāṃ dehadhāraṇam  || 7.2,38.18
pārthivāṃśaṃ vinā nityaṃ surabhirgandhasaṃgrahaḥ  || 7.2,38.19
evamaṣṭaguṇaṃ prāhuḥ paiśācaṃ pārthivaṃ padam  || 7.2,38.19
jale nivasanaṃ caiva bhūmyāmevaṃ vinirgamaḥ  || 7.2,38.20
icchecchaktaḥ svayaṃ pātuṃ samudramapi nāturaḥ  || 7.2,38.20
yatrecchati jagatyasmiṃstatraiva jaladarśanam  || 7.2,38.21
vinā kumbhādikaṃ pāṇau jalasañcayadhāraṇam  || 7.2,38.21
yadvastu virasañcāpi bhoktumicchati tatkṣaṇāt || 7.2,38.22
rasādikaṃ bhaveccānyattrayāṇāṃ dehadhāraṇam  || 7.2,38.22
nirvraṇatvaṃ śarīrasya pārthivaiśca samanvitam  || 7.2,38.23
tadidaṃ ṣoḍaśaguṇamāpyamaiśvaryamadbhutam  || 7.2,38.23
śarīrādagninirmāṇaṃ tattāpabhayavarjanam  || 7.2,38.24
śaktirjagadidaṃ dagdhuṃ yadīcchedaprayatnataḥ  || 7.2,38.24
sthāpanaṃ vānalasyā 'psu pāṇau pāvakadhāraṇam  || 7.2,38.25
dagdhe sarge yathāpūrvaṃ mukhe cānnādipācanam  || 7.2,38.25
dvābhyāṃ dehavinirmāṇamāpyaiśvaryasamanvitam  || 7.2,38.25
etaccaturviṃśatidhā taijasaṃ paricakṣate  || 7.2,38.26
manojavatvaṃ bhūtānāṃ kṣaṇādantaḥpraveśanam  || 7.2,38.26
596

parvatādimahābhāradhāraṇañcāprayatnataḥ  || 7.2,38.27
gurutvañca laghutvañca pāṇāvaniladhāraṇam  || 7.2,38.27
aṃgulyagranipātādyairbhūmerapi ca kampanam  || 7.2,38.28
ekena dehaniṣpattiryuktaṃ bhogaiśca taijasaiḥ  || 7.2,38.28
dvātriṃśadguṇamaiśvaryaṃ mārutaṃ kavayo viduḥ  || 7.2,38.29
chāyāhīnaviniṣpattirindriyāṇāmadarśanam  || 7.2,38.29
khecaratvaṃ yathākāmamindriyārthasamanvayaḥ  || 7.2,38.30
ākāśalaṃghanaṃ caiva svadehe tanniveśanam  || 7.2,38.30
ākāśapiṇḍīkaraṇamaśarīratvameva ca  || 7.2,38.31
anilaiśvaryasaṃyuktaṃ catvāriṃśadguṇaṃ mahat || 7.2,38.31
aindramaiśvaryamākhyātamāmbaraṃ tatpracakṣate  || 7.2,38.32
yathākāmopalabdhiśca yathākāmavinirgamaḥ  || 7.2,38.32
sarvasyābhibhavaścaiva sarvaguhyārthadarśanam  || 7.2,38.33
karmānurūpanirmāṇaṃ vaśitvaṃ priyadarśanam  || 7.2,38.33
saṃsāradarśanaṃ caiva bhogairaindraissamanvitam  || 7.2,38.34
etaccāṃdramasaiśvaryaṃ mānasaṃ guṇato 'dhikam  || 7.2,38.34
chedanaṃ tāḍanaṃ caiva baṃdhanaṃ mocanaṃ tathā  || 7.2,38.35
grahaṇaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ saṃsāravaśavartinām  || 7.2,38.35
prasādaścāpi sarveṣāṃ mṛtyukālajayastathā  || 7.2,38.36
ābhimānikamaiśvaryaṃ prājāpatyaṃ pracakṣate  || 7.2,38.36
etaccāndramasairbhogaiḥ ṣaṭpañcāśadguṇaṃ mahat || 7.2,38.37
sargaḥ saṃkalpamātreṇa trāṇaṃ saṃharaṇaṃ tathā  || 7.2,38.37
svādhikāraśca sarveṣāṃ bhūtacittapravartanam  || 7.2,38.38
asādṛśyaṃ ca sarvasya nirmāṇaṃ jagataḥ pṛthak || 7.2,38.38
śubhāśubhasya karaṇaṃ prājāpatyaiśca saṃyutam  || 7.2,38.39
catuṣṣaṣṭhiguṇaṃ brāhmamaiśvaryaṃ ca pracakṣate  || 7.2,38.39
bauddhādasmātparaṃ gauṇamaiśvaryaṃ prākṛtaṃ viduḥ  || 7.2,38.40
vaiṣṇavaṃ tatsamākhyātaṃ tasyaiva bhuvanasthitiḥ  || 7.2,38.40
brahmaṇā tatpadaṃ sarvaṃ vaktumanyairna śakyate  || 7.2,38.40
tatpauruṣaṃ ca gauṇaṃ ca gaṇeśaṃ padamaiśvaram  || 7.2,38.41
viṣṇunā tatpadaṃ kiṃcijjñātumanyairna śakyate  || 7.2,38.41
vijñānasiddhayaścaiva sarvā evaupasargikāḥ  || 7.2,38.42
niroddhavyā prayatnena varrāgyeṇa pareṇa tu  || 7.2,38.42
pratibhāseṣvaśuddheṣu guṇeṣvāsaktacetasaḥ  || 7.2,38.43
na sidhyetparamaiśvaryamabhayaṃ sārvakāmikam  || 7.2,38.43
tasmādguṇāṃśca bhogāṃśca devāsuramahībhṛtām  || 7.2,38.44
tṛṇavadyastyajettasya yogasiddhiḥ parā bhavet || 7.2,38.44
athavānugrahecchāyāṃ jagato vicarenmuniḥ  || 7.2,38.45
yathākāmaṃguṇānbhogānbhuktvā muktiṃ prayāsyati  || 7.2,38.45
atha prayogaṃ yogasya vakṣye śṛṇu samāhitaḥ  || 7.2,38.46
śubhe kāle śubhe deśe śivakṣetrādike punaḥ  || 7.2,38.46
vijane jaṃturahite niḥśabde bādhavarjite  || 7.2,38.46
supralipte sthale saumye gandhadhūpādivāsite  || 7.2,38.47
muktapuṣpasamākīrṇe vitānādi vicitrite  || 7.2,38.47
kuśapuṣpasamittoyaphalamūlasamanvite  || 7.2,38.48
nāgnyabhyāśe jalābhyāśe śuṣkaparṇacaye 'pi vā  || 7.2,38.48
596

na daṃśamaśakākīrṇe sarpaśvāpadasaṃkule  || 7.2,38.49
na ca duṣṭamṛgākīrṇe na bhaye durjanāvṛte  || 7.2,38.49
śmaśāne caityavalmīke jīrṇāgāre catuṣpathe  || 7.2,38.50
nadīnadasamudrāṇāṃ tīre rathyāṃtare 'pi vā  || 7.2,38.50
na jīrṇodyānagoṣṭhādau nāniṣṭe na ca niṃdite  || 7.2,38.51
nājīrṇāmlarasodgāre na ca viṇmūtradūṣite  || 7.2,38.51
nacchardyāmātisāre vā nātibhuktau śramānvite  || 7.2,38.52
na cāticiṃtākulito na cātikṣutpipāsitaḥ  || 7.2,38.52
nāpi svagurukarmādau prasakto yogamācaret || 7.2,38.53
yuktāhāravihāraśca yuktaceṣṭaśca karmasu  || 7.2,38.53
yuktanidrāprabodhaśca sarvāyāsavivarjitaḥ  || 7.2,38.54
āsanaṃ mṛdulaṃ ramyaṃ vipulaṃ susamaṃ śuci  || 7.2,38.54
padmakasvastikādīnāmabhyasedāsaneṣu ca  || 7.2,38.55
abhivaṃdya svagurvaṃtānabhivādyānanukramāt || 7.2,38.55
ṛjugrīvaśirovakṣā nātiṣṭhecchiṣṭalocanaḥ  || 7.2,38.56
kiṃcidunnāmitaśirā daṃtairdaṃtānna saṃspṛśet || 7.2,38.56
daṃtāgrasaṃsthitā jihvāmacalāṃ sanniveśya ca  || 7.2,38.57
pārṣṇibhyāṃ vṛṣaṇau rakṣaṃstathā prajananaṃ punaḥ  || 7.2,38.57
ūrvorupari saṃsthāpya bāhū tiryagayatnataḥ  || 7.2,38.58
dakṣiṇaṃ karapṛṣṭhaṃ tu nyasya vāmatalopari  || 7.2,38.58
unnāmya śanakaiḥ pṛṣṭhamuro viṣṭabhya cāgrataḥ  || 7.2,38.59
saṃprekṣya nāsikāgraṃ svaṃ diśaścānavalokayan  || 7.2,38.59
saṃbhṛtaprāṇasaṃcāraḥ pāṣāṇa iva niścalaḥ  || 7.2,38.60
svadehāyatanasyāṃtarviciṃtya śivamaṃbayā  || 7.2,38.60
hṛtpadmapīṭhikāmadhye dhyānayajñena pūjayet || 7.2,38.61
mūle nāsāgrato nābhau kaṃṭhe vā tāluraṃdhrayoḥ  || 7.2,38.61
bhrūmadhye dvāradeśe vā lalāṭe mūrdhni vā smaret || 7.2,38.62
parikalpya yathānyāyaṃ śivayoḥ paramāsanam  || 7.2,38.62
tatra sāvaraṇaṃ vāpi nirāvaraṇameva vā  || 7.2,38.63
dvidaleṣoḍaśāre vā dvādaśāre yathāvidhi  || 7.2,38.63
daśāre vā ṣaḍasre vā caturasre śivaṃ smaret || 7.2,38.64
bhruvoraṃtarataḥ padmaṃ dvidalaṃ taḍidujjvalam  || 7.2,38.64
bhrūmadhyasthāravindasya kramādvai dakṣiṇottare  || 7.2,38.65
vidyutsamānavarṇe ca parṇe varṇāvasānake  || 7.2,38.65
ṣoḍaśārasya patrāṇi svarāḥ ṣoḍaśa tāni vai  || 7.2,38.66
pūrvādīni kramādetatpadmaṃ kandasya mūlataḥ  || 7.2,38.66
kakārādiṭakārāṃtā varṇāḥ parṇānyanukramāt || 7.2,38.67
bhānuvarṇasya padmasya dhyeyaṃ tad1 hṛdayāntare  || 7.2,38.67
gokṣīradhavalasyoktā ḍādiphāntā yathākramam  || 7.2,38.68
adho dalasyāmbujasya etasya 2 ca dalāni ṣaṭ || 7.2,38.68
vidhūmāṃgāravarṇasya varṇā vādyāśca lāntimāḥ  || 7.2,38.69
mūlādhārāraviṃdasya hemābhasya yathākramam  || 7.2,38.69
vakārādisakārāntā varṇāḥ parṇamayāḥ sthitāḥ  || 7.2,38.69
eteṣvathāraviṃdeṣu yatraivābhirataṃ manaḥ  || 7.2,38.70
tatraiva devaṃ devīṃ ca ciṃtayeddhīrayā dhiyā  || 7.2,38.70
aṃguṣṭhamātramamalaṃ dīpyamānaṃ samaṃtataḥ  || 7.2,38.71
śuddhadīpaśikhākāraṃ svaśaktyā pūrṇamaṇḍitam  || 7.2,38.71
indurekhāsamākāraṃ tārārūpamathāpi vā  || 7.2,38.72
nīvāraśūkassadṛśaṃ bisasutrābhameva vā  || 7.2,38.72
kadambagolakākāraṃ tuṣārakaṇikopamam  || 7.2,38.73
kṣityāditattvavijayaṃ dhyātā yadyapi vāñchati  || 7.2,38.73
tattattattvādhipāmeva mūrtiṃ sthūlāṃ viciṃtayet || 7.2,38.74
sadāśivāṃtā brahmādyabhavādyāścāṣṭamūrtayaḥ  || 7.2,38.74
śivasya mūrtayaḥ sthūlāḥ śivaśāstre viniścitāḥ  || 7.2,38.75
ghorā miśrā praśāntāśca mūrtayastā munīśvaraiḥ  || 7.2,38.75
phalābhilāṣarahitaiścintyāścintāviśāradaiḥ  || 7.2,38.76
ghorāścecciṃtitāḥ kuryuḥ pāparogaparikṣayam  || 7.2,38.76
cireṇa miśre saumye tu na sadyo na cirādapi  || 7.2,38.77
saumye muktirviśeṣeṇa śāṃtiḥ prajñā prasidhyati  || 7.2,38.77
sidhyaṃti siddhayaścātra kramaśo nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,38.78
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe yogagatau vighnotpattivarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭatriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 39
upamanyuruvāca
śrīkaṃṭhanāthaṃ smaratāṃ sadyaḥ sarvārthasiddhayaḥ  || 7.2,39.1
prasidhyaṃtīti matvaike taṃ vai dhyāyaṃti yoginaḥ  || 7.2,39.1
sthityarthaṃ manasaḥ kecitsthūladhyānaṃ prakurvate  || 7.2,39.2
sthūlaṃ tu niścalaṃ ceto bhavetsūkṣme tu tatsthiram  || 7.2,39.2
śive tu ciṃtite sākṣātsarvāḥ sidhyanti siddhayaḥ  || 7.2,39.3
mūrtyaṃtareṣu dhyāteṣu śivarūpaṃ viciṃtayet || 7.2,39.3
lakṣayenmanasaḥ sthairyaṃ tattaddhyāyetpunaḥ punaḥ  || 7.2,39.4
dhyānamādau saviṣayaṃ tato nirviṣayaṃ jaguḥ  || 7.2,39.4
tatra nirviṣayaṃ dhyānaṃ nāstītyeva satāṃ matam  || 7.2,39.5
buddherhi santatiḥ kāciddhyānamityabhidhīyate  || 7.2,39.5
tena nirviṣayā buddhiḥ kevaleha pravartate  || 7.2,39.6
tasmātsaviṣayaṃ dhyānaṃ bālārkakiraṇāśrayam  || 7.2,39.6
sūkṣmāśrayaṃ nirviṣayaṃ nāparaṃ paramārthataḥ  || 7.2,39.7
yadvā saviṣayaṃ dhyānaṃ tatsākārasamāśrayam  || 7.2,39.7
nirākārātmasaṃvittirdhyānaṃ nirviṣayaṃ matam  || 7.2,39.8
nirbījaṃ ca sabījaṃ ca tadeva dhyānamucyate  || 7.2,39.8
nirākāraśrayatvena sākārāśrayatastathā  || 7.2,39.9
tasmātsaviṣayaṃ dhyānamādau kṛtvā sabījakam  || 7.2,39.9
aṃte nirviṣayaṃ kuryānnirbījaṃ sarvasiddhaye  || 7.2,39.10
prāṇāyāmena sidhyaṃti devyāḥ śāṃtyādayaḥ kramāt || 7.2,39.10
śāṃtiḥ praśāṃtirdīptiśca prasādaśca tataḥ param  || 7.2,39.11
śamaḥ sarvāpadāṃ caiva śāṃtirityabhidhīyate  || 7.2,39.11
597

tamaso 'ntabahirnāśaḥ praśāntiḥ parigīyate  || 7.2,39.12
bahirantaḥprakāśo yo dīptirityabhidhīyate  || 7.2,39.12
svasthatā yā tu sā buddhaḥ prasādaḥ parikīrtitaḥ  || 7.2,39.13
kāraṇāni ca sarvāṇi sabāhyābhyaṃtarāṇi ca  || 7.2,39.13
buddheḥ prasādataḥ kṣipraṃ prasannāni bhavantyuta  || 7.2,39.14
dhyātā dhyānaṃ tathā dhyeyaṃ yadvā dhyānaprayojanam  || 7.2,39.14
etaccatuṣṭayaṃ jñātvā dhyātā dhyānaṃ samācaret || 7.2,39.14
jñānavairāgyasaṃpanno nityamavyagramānasaḥ  || 7.2,39.15
śraddadhānaḥ prasannātmā dhyātā sadbhirudāhṛtaḥ  || 7.2,39.15
dhyai ciṃtāyāṃ smṛto dhātuḥ śivaciṃtā muhurmuhuḥ  || 7.2,39.16
yogābhyāsastathālpe 'pi yathā pāpaṃ vināśayet || 7.2,39.17
dhyāyataḥ kṣaṇamātraṃ vā śraddhayā parameśvaram  || 7.2,39.17
avyākṣiptena manasā dhyānamityabhidhīyate  || 7.2,39.18
buddhipravāharūpasya dhyānasyāsyāvalaṃbanam  || 7.2,39.19
dhyeyamityucyate sadbhistacca sāṃbaḥ svayaṃ śivaḥ  || 7.2,39.19
vimuktipratyayaṃ pūrṇamaiśvaryaṃ cāṇimādikam  || 7.2,39.20
śivadhyānasya pūrṇasya sākṣāduktaṃ prayojanam  || 7.2,39.20
yasmātsaukhyaṃ ca mokṣaṃ ca dhyānādabhayamāpnuyāt || 7.2,39.21
tasmātsarvaṃ parityajya dhyānayukto bhavennaraḥ  || 7.2,39.21
nāsti dhyānaṃ vinā jñānaṃ nāsti dhyānamayoginaḥ  || 7.2,39.22
dhyānaṃ jñānaṃ ca yasyāsti tīrṇastena bhavārṇavaḥ  || 7.2,39.22
jñānaṃ prasannamekāgramaśeṣopādhivarjitam  || 7.2,39.23
yogābhyāsena yuktasya yoginastveva sidhyati  || 7.2,39.23
prakṣīṇāśeṣapāpānāṃ jñāne dhyāne bhavenmatiḥ  || 7.2,39.24
pāpopahatabuddhīnāṃ tadvārtāpi sudurlabhā  || 7.2,39.24
yathāvahnirmahādīptaḥ śuṣkamārdraṃ ca nirdahet || 7.2,39.25
tathā śubhāśubhaṃ karma dhyānāgnirdahate kṣaṇāt || 7.2,39.25
atyalpo 'pi yathā dīpaḥ sumahannāśayettamaḥ  || 7.2,39.26
yogābhyāsastathālpo 'pi mahāpāpaṃ vināśayet || 7.2,39.26
dhyāyataḥ kṣaṇamātraṃ vā śraddhayā parameśvaram  || 7.2,39.27
yadbhavetsumahacchreyastasyāṃto naiva vidyate  || 7.2,39.27
nāsti dhyānasamaṃ tīrthaṃ nāsti dhyānasamaṃ tapaḥ  || 7.2,39.28
nāsti dhyānasamo yajñastasmāddhyānaṃ samācaret || 7.2,39.28
tīrthāni toyapūrṇāni devānpāṣāṇamṛnmayān  || 7.2,39.29
yogino na prapadyaṃte svātmapratyayakāraṇāt || 7.2,39.29
yogināṃ ca vapuḥ sūkṣmaṃ bhavetpratyakṣamaiśvaram  || 7.2,39.30
yathā sthūlamayuktānāṃ mṛtkāṣṭhādyaiḥ prakalpitam  || 7.2,39.30
yathehāṃtaścarā rājñaḥ priyāḥ syurna bahiścarāḥ  || 7.2,39.31
tathāṃtardhyānaniratāḥ priyāśśaṃbhorna karmiṇaḥ  || 7.2,39.31
bahiskarā yathā loke nātīva phalabhoginaḥ  || 7.2,39.32
598

dṛṣṭvā narendrabhavane tadvadatrāpi karmiṇaḥ  || 7.2,39.32
yadyaṃtarā vipadyaṃte jñānayogārthamudyataḥ  || 7.2,39.33
yogasyodyogamātreṇa rudralokaṃ gamiṣyati  || 7.2,39.33
anubhūya sukhaṃ tatra sa jāto yogināṃ kule  || 7.2,39.34
jñānayogaṃ punarlabdhvā saṃsāramativartate  || 7.2,39.34
jijñāsurapi yogasya yāṃ gatiṃ labhate naraḥ  || 7.2,39.35
na tāṃ gatimavāpnoti sarvairapi mahāmakhaiḥ  || 7.2,39.35
dvijānāṃ vedaviduṣāṃ koṭiṃ saṃpūjya yatphalam  || 7.2,39.36
bhikṣāmātrapradānena tatphalaṃ śivayogine  || 7.2,39.36
yajñāgnihotradānena tīrthahomeṣu yatphalam  || 7.2,39.37
yogināmannadānena tatsamastaṃ phalaṃ labhet || 7.2,39.37
ye cāpavādaṃ kurvaṃti vimūḍhāśśivayoginām  || 7.2,39.38
śrotṛbhiste prapadyante narakeṣvāmahīkṣayāt || 7.2,39.38
sati śrotari vaktāsyādapavādasya yoginām  || 7.2,39.39
tasmācchrotā ca pāpīyāndaṇḍyassumahatāṃ mataḥ  || 7.2,39.39
ye punaḥ satataṃ bhaktyā bhajaṃti śavayoginaḥ  || 7.2,39.39
te vidaṃti mahābhogānaṃte yogaṃ ca śāṃkaram  || 7.2,39.40
bhogārthibhirnaraistasmātsaṃpūjyāḥ śivayoginaḥ  || 7.2,39.40
pratiśrayānnapānādyaiḥ śayyāprāvaraṇādibhiḥ  || 7.2,39.41
yogadharmaḥ sasāratvādabhedyaḥ pāpamudgaraiḥ  || 7.2,39.41
vajrataṃdulavajjñeyaṃ tathā pāpena yoginaḥ  || 7.2,39.42
na lipyaṃte ca tāpaughaiḥ padmapatraṃ yathāṃbhasā  || 7.2,39.42
yasmindeśe vasennityaṃ śivayogarato muniḥ  || 7.2,39.43
so 'pi deśo bhavetpūtaḥ sapūta iti kiṃ punaḥ  || 7.2,39.43
tasmātsarvaṃ parityajya kṛtyamanyadvicakṣaṇaḥ  || 7.2,39.44
sarvaduḥkhaprahāṇāya śivayogaṃ samabhyaset || 7.2,39.44
siddhayogaphalo yogī lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 7.2,39.45
bhogānbhuktvā yathākāmaṃ viharedvātra vartatām  || 7.2,39.45
athavā kṣudramityeva matvā vaiṣayikaṃ sukham  || 7.2,39.46
tyaktvā virāgayogena svecchayā karma mucyatām  || 7.2,39.46
yastvāsannāṃ mṛtiṃ martyo dṛṣṭāriṣṭaṃ ca bhūyasā  || 7.2,39.47
sa yogārambhanirataḥ śivakṣetraṃ samāśrayet || 7.2,39.47
sa tatra nivasanneva yadi dhīramanā naraḥ  || 7.2,39.48
prāṇānvināpi rogādyaiḥ svayameva parityajet || 7.2,39.48
kṛtvāpyanaśanaṃ caiva hutvā cāṃgaṃ śivānale  || 7.2,39.49
kṣiptvā vā śivatīrtheṣu svadehamavagāhanāt || 7.2,39.49
śivaśāstroktavidhivatprāṇānyastu parityajet || 7.2,39.50
sadya eva vimucyeta nātra kāryā vicāraṇā 2  || 7.2,39.50
rogādyairvātha vivaśaḥ śivakṣetraṃ samāśritaḥ  || 7.2,39.51
mriyate yadi sopyevaṃ mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,39.51

1 udyogaṃ kurvata ityarthaḥ
2 na cāsāvātmaghātaka iti pāṭhāntaram

598

yathā hi maraṇaṃ śreṣṭhamuśaṃtyanaśanādibhiḥ  || 7.2,39.52
śāstraviśraṃbhadhīreṇa manasā kriyate yataḥ  || 7.2,39.52
śivanindārataṃ hatvā pīḍitaḥ svayameva vā  || 7.2,39.53
yastyajeddustyajānprāṇānna sa bhūyaḥ prajāyate  || 7.2,39.53
śivanindārataṃ haṃtumaśakto yaḥ svayaṃ mṛtaḥ  || 7.2,39.54
sadya eva pramucyeta triḥ saptakulasaṃyutaḥ  || 7.2,39.54
śivārthe yastyajetprāṇāñchivabhaktārthameva vā  || 7.2,39.55
na tena sadṛśaḥ kaścinmuktimārgasthito naraḥ  || 7.2,39.55
tasmācchīghratarā muktistasya saṃsāramaṃḍalāt || 7.2,39.56
eteṣvanyatamopāyaṃ kathamapyavalambya vā  || 7.2,39.56
ṣaḍadhvaśuddhiṃ vidhivatprāpto vā mriyate yadi  || 7.2,39.57
paśūnāmiva tasyeha na kuryādaurdhvadaihikam  || 7.2,39.57
naivāśaucaṃ prapadyeta tatputrādiviśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,39.58
śivacārārthamathavā śivavidyārthameva vā  || 7.2,39.58
khanedvā bhuvi taddehaṃ dahedvā śucināgninā  || 7.2,39.58
kṣipedvāpsu śivāsveva tyajedvā kāṣṭhaloṣṭavat || 7.2,39.59
athainamapi coddiśya karma cetkartumīpsitam  || 7.2,39.59
kalyāṇameva kurvīta śaktyā bhaktāṃśca tarpayet || 7.2,39.60
dhanaṃ tasya bhajecchaivaḥ śaivī cetasya santatiḥ  || 7.2,39.60
nāsti cettacchive dadyānnadadyātpaśusantatiḥ  || 7.2,39. 60
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe śaivayogavarṇanaṃ nāmaikonacatvāriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 40
śrīsūta uvāca
iti sa vijitamanyoryādavenopamanyoradhigatamabhidhāya jñānayogaṃ munibhyaḥ  || 7.2,40.1
praṇatimupagatebhyastebhya udbhāvitātmā sapadi viyati vāyuḥ sāyamantarhito 'bhūt || 7.2,40.1
tataḥ prabhātasamaye naimiṣīyāstapodhanāḥ  || 7.2,40.2
satrānte 'vabhṛthaṃ kartuṃ sarva eva samudyayuḥ  || 7.2,40.2
tadā brahmasamādeśāddevī sākṣātsarasvatī  || 7.2,40.3
prasannā svādusalilā prāvartata nadīśubhā  || 7.2,40.3
sarasvatīṃ nadīṃ dṛṣṭvā munayo hṛṣṭamānasāḥ  || 7.2,40.4
samāpya satraṃ prārabdhaṃ cakrustatrāvagāhanam  || 7.2,40.4
atha saṃtarpya devādīṃstadīyaiḥ salilaiḥ śivaiḥ  || 7.2,40.5
smarantaḥ pūrvavṛttāntaṃ yayurvārāṇasīṃ prati  || 7.2,40.5
tadā te himavatpādātpaṃtatīṃ dakṣiṇāmukhīm  || 7.2,40.6
dṛṣṭvā bhāgīrathī tatra snātvā tattīrato yayuḥ  || 7.2,40.6
tato vārāṇasīṃ prāpya muditāssarva eva te  || 7.2,40.7
tadottarapravāhāyāṃ gaṃgāyāmavagāhya ca  || 7.2,40.7
avimukteśvaraṃ liṃgaṃ dṛṣṭvābhyarcya vidhānataḥ  || 7.2,40.8
prayātumudyatāstatra dadṛśurdivi bhāsvaram  || 7.2,40.8
sūryakoṭipratīkāśaṃ tejodivyaṃ mahādbhutam  || 7.2,40.9
ātmaprabhāvitānena vyāptasarvadigantaram  || 7.2,40.9
atha pāśupatāḥ siddhāḥ bhasmasañchannavigrahāḥ  || 7.2,40.10
munayo 'bhyetya śataśo līnāḥ syustatra tejasi  || 7.2,40.10
599

tathā vilīyamāneṣu tapasviṣu mahātmasu  || 7.2,40.11
sadyastirodadhe tejastadadbhutamivābhavat || 7.2,40.11
taddṛṣṭvā mahadāścaryaṃ naimiṣīyā maharṣayaḥ  || 7.2,40.12
kimetadityajānanto yayurbrahmavanaṃ prati  || 7.2,40.12
prāgevaiṣāṃ tu gamanātpavano lokapāvanaḥ  || 7.2,40.13
darśanaṃ naimiṣīyāṇāṃ saṃvādastairmahātmanaḥ  || 7.2,40.13
śaddhāṃ buddhiṃ tatasteṣāṃ sāṃbe sānucare śive  || 7.2,40.14
samāptiṃ cāpi satrasya dīrghapūrvasya satriṇām  || 7.2,40.14
vijñāpya jagatāṃ dhātre brahmaṇe brahmayonaye  || 7.2,40.15
svakārye tadanujñāto jagāma svapuraṃ prati  || 7.2,40.15
atha sthānagato brahmā tumburornāradasya ca  || 7.2,40.16
paraspara spardhitayorgāne vivadamānayoḥ  || 7.2,40.16
tadudbhāvitagānottharasairmādhyasthamācaran  || 7.2,40.17
gandharvairapsarobhiśca sukhamāste niṣevitaḥ  || 7.2,40.17
tadānavasarādeva dvāḥsthairdvāri nivāritāḥ  || 7.2,40.18
munayo brahmabhavanādbahiḥ pārśvamupāviśan  || 7.2,40.18
atha tumburuṇā gāne samatāṃ prāpya nāradaḥ  || 7.2,40.19
sāhacaryeṣvanujñāto brahmaṇā parameṣṭhinā  || 7.2,40.19
tyaktvā parasparaspardhāṃ maitrīṃ ca paramāṃ gataḥ  || 7.2,40.20
saha tenāpsarobhiśca gandharvaiśca samāvṛtaḥ  || 7.2,40.20
upavīṇayituṃ devaṃ nakulīśvaramīśvaram  || 7.2,40.21
bhavanānniryayau dhāturjaladādaṃśumāniva  || 7.2,40.21
taṃ dṛṣṭvā ṣaṭkulīyāste nāradaṃ munigovṛṣam  || 7.2,40.22
praṇamyāvasaraṃ śaṃbhoḥ papracchuḥ paramādarāt || 7.2,40.22
sa cāvasara evāyamitoṃtargamyatāmiti  || 7.2,40.23
vadanyayāvanyaparastvarayā parayā yutaḥ  || 7.2,40.23
tato dvāri sthitā ye vai brahmaṇe tānnyavedayan  || 7.2,40.24
tena te viviśurveśma piṃḍībhūyāṃḍajanmanaḥ  || 7.2,40.24
praviśya dūrato devaṃ praṇamya bhuvi daṃḍavat || 7.2,40.25
samīpe tadanujñātāḥ parivṛtyopatasthire  || 7.2,40.25
tāṃstatrāvasthitān pṛṣṭvā kuśalaṃ kamalāsanaḥ  || 7.2,40.26
vṛttāṃtaṃ vo mayā jñātaṃ vāyurevāha no yataḥ  || 7.2,40.26
bhavadbhiḥ kiṃ kṛtaṃ paścānmāruteṃtarhite sati  || 7.2,40.27
ityuktavati deveśe munayo 'vabhṛthātparam  || 7.2,40.27
gaṃgātīrthesya gamanaṃ yātrāṃ vārāṇasīṃ prati  || 7.2,40.28
darśanaṃ tatra liṃgānāṃ sthāpitānāṃ sureśvaraiḥ  || 7.2,40.28
avimukteśvarasyāpi liṃgasyābhyarcanaṃ sakṛt || 7.2,40.29
ākāśe mahatastasya tejorāśeśca darśanam  || 7.2,40.29
munīnāṃ vilayaṃ tatra nirodhaṃ tejasastataḥ  || 7.2,40.30
yāthātmyavedanaṃ tasya ciṃtitasyāpi cātmabhiḥ  || 7.2,40.30
sarvaṃ savistaraṃ tasmai praṇamyāhurmuhurmuhuḥ  || 7.2,40.31
munibhiḥ kathitaṃ śrutvā viśvakarmā caturmukhaḥ  || 7.2,40.31
kaṃpayitvā śiraḥ kiṃcitprāha gaṃbhīrayā girā  || 7.2,40.32
pratyāsīdati yuṣmākaṃ siddhirāmuṣmikī parā  || 7.2,40.32
599

bhavadbhirdīrghasatreṇa ciramārādhitaḥ prabhuḥ  || 7.2,40.33
prasādābhimukho bhūta iti bhutārthasūcitam  || 7.2,40.33
vārāṇasyāṃ tu yuṣmābhiryaddṛṣṭaṃ divi dīptimat || 7.2,40.34
talliṃgasaṃjñitaṃ sākṣāttejo māheśvaraṃ param  || 7.2,40.34
tatra līnāśca munayaḥ śrautapāśupatavratāḥ  || 7.2,40.35
muktā babhūvuḥ svasthāśca naiṣṭhikā dagdhakilbiṣāḥ  || 7.2,40.35
prāpyānena yathā muktiracirādbhavatāmapi  || 7.2,40.36
sa cāyamarthaḥ sūcyeta yuṣmaddṛṣṭena tejasā  || 7.2,40.36
tatra vaḥ kāla evaiṣa daivādupanataḥ svayam  || 7.2,40.37
prayāta dakṣiṇaṃ meroḥ śikharaṃ devasevitam  || 7.2,40.37
sanatkumāro yatrāste mama putraḥ paro muniḥ  || 7.2,40.38
pratīkṣyāgamanaṃ sākṣādbhūtanāthasya naṃdinaḥ  || 7.2,40.38
purā sanatkumāropi dṛṣṭvāpi parameśvaram  || 7.2,40.39
ajñānātsarvayogīndramānī vinayadūṣitaḥ  || 7.2,40.39
abhyutthānādikaṃ yuktamakurvannatinirbhayaḥ  || 7.2,40.40
tato 'parādhātkruddhena mahoṣṭro naṃdinā kṛtaḥ  || 7.2,40.40
atha kālena mahatā tadarthe śocatā mayā  || 7.2,40.41
upāsya devaṃ devīñca naṃdinaṃ cānunīya vai  || 7.2,40.41
kathaṃciduṣṭratā tasya prayatnena nivāritā  || 7.2,40.42
prāpito hi yathāpūrvaṃ sanatpūrvāṃ kumāratām  || 7.2,40.42
tadāha ca mahādevaḥ smayanniva gaṇādhipam  || 7.2,40.43
avajñāya hi māmeva tathāhaṃkṛtavānmuniḥ  || 7.2,40.43
atastvameva yāthātmyaṃ mamāsmai kathayānagha  || 7.2,40.44
brahmaṇaḥ pūrvajaḥ putro māṃ mūḍha iva saṃsmaran  || 7.2,40.44
mayaiva śiṣyate datto mama jñānapravartakaḥ  || 7.2,40.45
dharmādhyakṣābhiṣekaṃ ca tava nirvartayiṣyati  || 7.2,40.45
sa evaṃ vyāhṛto bhūyassarvabhūtagaṇāgraṇīḥ  || 7.2,40.46
yatparājñāpanaṃ mūrdhnā prātaḥ pratigṛhītavān  || 7.2,40.46
tathā sanatkumāro 'pi merau madanuśāsanāt || 7.2,40.47
prasādārthaṃ gaṇasyāsya tapaścarati duścaram  || 7.2,40.47
draṣṭavyaśceti yuṣmābhiḥ prāggaṇeśasamāgamāt || 7.2,40.48
tatprasādārthamacirānnaṃdī tatrāgamiṣyati  || 7.2,40.48
iti satvaramādiśya preṣitā viśvayoginā  || 7.2,40.49
kumāraśikharaṃ merordakṣiṇaṃ munayo yayuḥ  || 7.2,40.49
oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe naimiṣarṣiyātrāvarṇanaṃ nāma catvāriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ

adhyāya 41
sūta uvāca
tatra skaṃdasaro nāma sarassāgarasannibham  || 7.2,41.1
amṛtasvāduśiśirasvacchā gādhalaghūdakam  || 7.2,41.1
samaṃtataḥ saṃghaṭitaṃ sphaṭiko palasaṃcayaiḥ  || 7.2,41.2
sarvartukusumaiḥ phullaiśchāditākhiladiṅmukham  || 7.2,41.2
śaivalairutpalaiḥ padmaiḥ kumudaistārakopamaiḥ  || 7.2,41.3
taraṃgairabhrasaṃkāśairākāśamiva bhūmigam  || 7.2,41.3
sukhāvataraṇārohaiḥ sthalairnīlaśilāmayaiḥ  || 7.2,41.4
sopānamārgau ruciraiśśobhamānāṣṭadiṅmukham  || 7.2,41.4
600

tatrāvatīrṇaiśca yathā tatrottīrṇaśca bhūyasā  || 7.2,41.5
snātaiḥ sitopavītaiśca śuklākaupīnavalkalaiḥ  || 7.2,41.5
jaṭāśikhāyanairmuṃḍaistripuṃḍrakṛtamaṃḍanaiḥ  || 7.2,41.6
virāgavivaśasmeramukhairmunikumārakaiḥ  || 7.2,41.6
ghaṭaiḥ kamalinīpatrapuṭaiśca kalaśaiḥ śivaiḥ  || 7.2,41.7
kamaṇḍalubhiranyaiśca tādṛśaiḥ karakādibhiḥ  || 7.2,41.7
ātmārthaṃ ca parārthaṃ ca devatārthaṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 7.2,41.8
ānīyamānasalilamāttapuṣpaṃ ca nityaśaḥ  || 7.2,41.8
aṃtarjalaśilārūḍhairnīcānāṃ sparśaśaṃkayā  || 7.2,41.9
ācāravadbhirmunibhiḥ kṛtabhasmāṃgadhūsaraiḥ  || 7.2,41.9
itastato 'psu majjadbhiriṣṭaśiṣṭaiḥ śilāgataiḥ  || 7.2,41.10
tilaiśca sākṣataiḥ puṣpaistyaktadarbhapavitrakaiḥ  || 7.2,41.10
devādyamṛṣimadhyaṃ ca nirvartya pitṛtarpaṇam  || 7.2,41.11
nivedayedabhijñebhyo nityasnānagatān dvijān  || 7.2,41.11
sthānesthāne kṛtānekabalipuṣpasamīraṇaiḥ  || 7.2,41.12
saurārghyapūrvaṃ kurvadbhiḥsthaṃḍalebhyarcanādikam  || 7.2,41.12
kvacinnimajjadunmajjatprasrastagajayūthapam  || 7.2,41.13
kvacicca tṛṣayāyātamṛgīmṛgaturaṃgamam  || 7.2,41.13
kvacitpītajanottīrṇamayūravaravāraṇam  || 7.2,41.14
kvacitkṛtataṭāghātavṛṣaprativṛṣojjvalam  || 7.2,41.14
kvacitkāraṃḍavaravaiḥ kvacitsārasakūjitaiḥ  || 7.2,41.15
kvacicca kokaninadaiḥ kvacidbhramaragītibhiḥ  || 7.2,41.15
snānapānādikaraṇaiḥ svasaṃpaddrumajīvibhiḥ  || 7.2,41.16
praṇayātprāṇibhistaistairbhāṣamāṇamivāsakṛt || 7.2,41.16
kūlaśākhiśikhālīnakokilākulakūjitaiḥ  || 7.2,41.17
ātapopahatānsarvānnāmaṃtrayadivāniśam  || 7.2,41.17
uttare tasya sarasastīre kalpataroradhaḥ  || 7.2,41.18
vedyāṃ vajraśilāmayyāṃ mṛdule mṛgacarmaṇi  || 7.2,41.18
sanatkumāramāsīnaṃ śaśvadbālavapurdharam  || 7.2,41.19
