tatkālamātroparataṃ samādheracalātmanaḥ  || 7.2,41.19
upāsyamānaṃ munibhiryogīṃdrairapi pūjitam  || 7.2,41.20
dadṛśurnaimiṣeyāste praṇatāścopatasthire  || 7.2,41.20
yāvatpṛṣṭavate tasmai procuḥ svāgatakāraṇam  || 7.2,41.21
tumulaḥ śuśruve tāvaddivi duṃdubhinisvanaḥ  || 7.2,41.21
dadṛśe tatkṣaṇe tasminvimānaṃ bhānusannibham  || 7.2,41.22
gaṇeśvarairasaṃkhyeyaiḥ saṃvṛtaṃ ca samaṃtataḥ  || 7.2,41.22
apsarogaṇasaṃkīrṇaṃ rudrakanyābhirāvṛtam  || 7.2,41.23
mṛdaṃgamurajodghuṣṭaṃ veṇuvīṇāravānvitam  || 7.2,41.23
citraratnavitānāḍhyaṃ muktādāmavirājitam  || 7.2,41.24
munibhissiddhagaṃdharvairyakṣacāraṇakinnaraiḥ  || 7.2,41.24
nṛtyadbhiścaiva gāyadbhirvādayadbhiśca saṃvṛtam  || 7.2,41.25
vīragovṛṣacihnena vidramadrumayaṣṭinā  || 7.2,41.25
kṛtagopurasatkāraṃ ketunā mānyahetunā  || 7.2,41.26
tasya madhye vimānasya cāmaradvitayāṃtare  || 7.2,41.26
chattrasya maṇidaṃḍasya caṃdrasyeva śuceradhaḥ  || 7.2,41.27
divyasiṃhāsanārūḍhaṃ devyā suyaśayā saha  || 7.2,41.27
600

śriyā ca vapuṣā caiva tribhiścāpi vilocanaiḥ  || 7.2,41.28
prākārairabhikṛtyānāṃ pratyabhijñāpakaṃ prabhoḥ  || 7.2,41.28
avilaṃghya jagatkarturājñāpanamivāgatam  || 7.2,41.29
sarvānugrahaṇaṃ śaṃbhoḥ sākṣādiva puraḥsthitam  || 7.2,41.29
śilādatanayaṃ sākṣācchrīmacchūlavarāyudham  || 7.2,41.30
viśveśvaragaṇādhyakṣaṃ viśveśvaramivāparam  || 7.2,41.30
viśvasyāpi vidhātḥṇāṃ nigrahānugrahakṣamam  || 7.2,41.31
caturbāhumudārāṃgaṃ candrarekhāvibhūṣitam  || 7.2,41.31
kaṃṭhe nāgena maulau ca śaśāṃkenāpyalaṃkṛtam  || 7.2,41.32
savigrahamivaiśvaryaṃ sāmarthyamiva sakriyam  || 7.2,41.32
samāptamiva nirvāṇaṃ sarvajñamiva saṃgatam  || 7.2,41.33
dṛṣṭvā prahṛṣṭavadano brahmaputraḥ saharṣibhiḥ  || 7.2,41.33
tasthau prāñjalirutthāya tasyātmānamivārpayan  || 7.2,41.34
atha tatrāṃtare tasminvimāne cāvaniṃ gate  || 7.2,41.34
praṇamya daṇḍavaddevaṃ stutvā vyajñāpayanmunīm  || 7.2,41.35
ṣaṭkulīyā ime dīrghaṃ naimiṣe satramāsthitāḥ  || 7.2,41.35
āgatā brahmaṇādiṣṭāḥ pūrvamevābhikāṃkṣayā  || 7.2,41.35
śrutvā vākyaṃ brahmaputrasya naṃdīchittvā pāśāndṛṣṭipātena sadyaḥ  || 7.2,41.36
śaivaṃ dharmaṃ caiśvaraṃ jñānayogaṃ dattvā bhūyo devapārśvaṃ jagāma  || 7.2,41.36
sanatkumāreṇa ca tatsamastaṃ vyāsāya sākṣādgurave mamoktam  || 7.2,41.37
vyāsena coktaṃ mahitena mahyaṃ mayā ca tadvaḥ kathitaṃ samāsāt || 7.2,41.37
nāvedavidbhyaḥ kathanīyametatpurāṇaratnaṃ puraśāsanasya  || 7.2,41.38
nābhaktaśiṣyāya ca nāstikebhyo dattaṃ hi mohānnirayaṃ dadāti  || 7.2,41.38
mārgeṇa sevānugatena yaistaddattaṃ gṛhītaṃ paṭhitaṃ śrutaṃ vā  || 7.2,41.39
tebhyaḥ sukhaṃ dharmamukhaṃ trivargaṃ nirvāṇamaṃte niyataṃ dadāti  || 7.2,41.39
parasparasyopakṛtaṃ bhavadbhirmayā ca paurāṇikamārgayogāt || 7.2,41.40
ato gamiṣye 'hamavāptakāmaḥ samastamevāstu śivaṃ sadā naḥ  || 7.2,41.40
sūte kṛtāśiṣi gate munayaḥ suvṛttā yāge ca paryavasite mahati prayoge  || 7.2,41.41
kāle kalau ca viṣayaiḥ kaluṣāyamāṇe vārāṇasīparisare vasatiṃ vinetuḥ  || 7.2,41.41
atha ca te paśupāśamumukṣayākhilatayā kṛtapāśupatavratāḥ  || 7.2,41.42
adhikṛtākhilabodhasamādhayaḥ paramanirvṛtimāpuraniṃditāḥ  || 7.2,41.42
vyāsa uvāca
etacchivapurāṇaṃ hi samāptaṃ hitamādarāt || 7.2,41.43
paṭhitavyaṃ prayatnena śrotavyaṃ ca tathaiva hi  || 7.2,41.43
nāstikāya na vaktavyamaśraddhāya śaṭhāya ca  || 7.2,41.44
abhaktāya maheśasya tathā dharmadhvajāya ca  || 7.2,41.44
etacchrutyā hyekavāraṃ bhavetpāpaṃ hi bhasmasāt || 7.2,41.45
abhakto bhaktimāpnoti bhakto bhaktisamṛddhibhāk || 7.2,41.45
punaḥ śrute ca sadbhaktirmuktissyācca śruteḥ punaḥ  || 7.2,41.46
tasmātpunaḥpunaścaiva śrotavyaṃ hi mumukṣubhiḥ  || 7.2,41.46
601

pañcāvṛttiḥ prakartavyā purāṇasyāsya saddhiyā  || 7.2,41.47
paraṃ phalaṃ samuddiśya tatprāpnoti na saṃśayaḥ  || 7.2,41.47
purātanāśca rājāno viprā vaiśyāśca sattamāḥ  || 7.2,41.48
saptakṛtvastadāvṛttyālabhanta śivadarśanam  || 7.2,41.48
śroṣyatyathāpi yaścedaṃ mānavo bhaktitatparaḥ  || 7.2,41.49
iha bhuktvākhilānbhogānaṃte muktiṃ labhecca saḥ  || 7.2,41.49
etacchivapurāṇaṃ hi śivasyātipriyaṃ param  || 7.2,41.50
bhuktimuktipradaṃ brahmasaṃmitaṃ bhaktivardhanam  || 7.2,41.50
etacchivapurāṇasya vaktuḥ śrotuśca sarvadā  || 7.2,41.51
sagaṇassasutassāṃbaśśaṃ karotu sa śaṃkaraḥ  || 7.2,41.51

oṃ iti śrīśivamahāpurāṇe saptamyāṃ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāmuttarakhaṇḍe vyāsopadeśaśrīśivamahāpurāṇamāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāmaikacatvāriṃśo 'dhyāyaḥ
samāpto 'yaṃ granthaḥ
601
